APARĀRKA
[869]
saṭīkayājñavalkyasmṛtau tṛtīyaḥ
prāyaścittādhyāyaḥ
tatrāśaucaprakaraṇam | (1)
pūrveṇādhyāyadvayena varṇānām āśramiṇoś ca brahmacārigṛhasthayor dharmā uktāḥ | saṃprati gṛhasthasya sveṣu yena nimittenānadhikāras tasmiṃś ca sati yeṣu dharmeṣv adhikāras tadubhayac chedārtham adhyāyāntaram ārabhate | evaṃ sati vakṣyamāṇam arthajātam ekādhyāyārthatāṃ yāti | tathā hi — āśaucaprakaraṇe sapiṇḍajananamaraṇayor nimittayos trirātrādikāliko 'nadhikāraḥ | [mṛtasapiṇḍaśarīrasaṃskārādau karmarāśāv adhikāraḥ |] evamāpannasyāpadvṛttivartitayā hīnavarṇavṛttau vihitāyām āyudhīyakakṛṣṭādāv adhikāraḥ | tathā ca jīvataḥ svavṛttau pratigrahādāv anadhikāraḥ | tathāśramasamuccayasaṃkalpavataḥ pañcaviṃśativatsaraparimitagārhasthyānte gārhasthye 'nadhikāraḥ | vanasthatve cādhikāraḥ | evaṃ tṛtīyam āyuṣo bhāgaṃ vanasthatayā nirvāhya na vanastho bhavet | kiṃ tu parivrajet | pātitye sati dvijātikarmasv anadhikāraḥ | tatprasaṅgād upapātakeṣu satsu karmānadhikārābhāve 'pi prāyaścittam eva vidhīyat iti sarvam evādhyāyārtho bhavati | yady api ca pātityaṃ prāyaścittaṃ ca sarveṣām āśramiṇāṃ tulyam, tathāpi gṛhasthasyāpi tad bhavatīti prasaṅgād atraivādhyāye tatkathanam ucitam |
ūnadvivarṣaṃ nikhanen na kuryād udakaṃ tataḥ |
ā śmaśnam anuvrājya itaro jñātibhir mṛtaḥ || 3.1 ||
yamasūktaṃ tathā gāthā japadbhir laukikāgninā |
sa dagdhavya upetaś ced āhitāgnyāvṛtārthavat || 3.2 ||
catvārīmāni vākyāni, tatra ūne asaṃpūrṇe dve varṣe yasya taṃ mṛtaṃ pitrādiḥ pratyāsanno bāndhavo vā nikhanet | bhuvaṃ khātvā nidhāya pūrayet | atra mānavo viśeṣaḥ |
[870]        ūnadvivārṣikaṃ pretaṃ nidadhyur bāndhavā bahiḥ |
alaṃkṛtya śucau bhūmāv asthisaṃcayanād ṛte || iti |
bāndhavā iti bahuvacanam avivakṣitārtham | bahir grāmād bahir ity arthaḥ | gandhapuṣpādibhiḥ pretam alaṃkṛtya śucāv anupahatāyāṃ bhūmau nidadhyuḥ | asthisaṃcayād ṛte, asthisaṃcayanaṃ na kuryur ity arthaḥ | na punar yatra bhūmāv asthisaṃcayanaṃ na kṛtaṃ tatreti | śucāv ity anenaiva tatsiddheḥ | yamaḥ |
ūnadvivārṣikaṃ pretaṃ ghṛtāktaṃ nikhaned bhuvi |
yamagāthā gāyamāno yamasūktam anusmaran ||
manuḥ |
dakṣiṇena mṛtaṃ śūdraṃ puradvāreṇa nirharet |
paścimottarapūrvais tu yathāvarṇaṃ dvijātayaḥ ||
nirharaṇīyā iti śeṣaḥ | prātilomyena varṇakramaḥ | tathā ca ādipurāṇam |
pūrvāmukhas tu netavyo brāhmaṇo bāndhavair gṛhāt |
uttarābhimukho rājā vaiśyaḥ paścānmukhas tathā ||
dakṣiṇābhimukhā śūdro nirhartavyaḥ svabāndhavaiḥ || iti |
bāndhavābhāve 'nye nirhareyuḥ | tatra mānavo viśeṣaḥ |
na vipraṃ sveṣu tiṣṭhatsu mṛtaṃ śūdreṇa nānayet |
asvargyā hy āhutiḥ sā syāc chūdrasaṃsparśadūṣitā || iti |
sveṣu dvijeṣu | hārītaḥ |
na grāmābhimukhaṃ pretaṃ na kuryād udakaṃ tataḥ || iti |
tato nikhananād anantaram udakaṃ na kuryāt | snānaṃ tu śuddhaye kuryād eva | udakadānaniṣedhaś ca sakalaurdhvadehikapratiṣedhapradarśanārthaḥ | tathā conadvivārṣikakāre manuḥ |
nāsya kāryo 'gnisaṃskāro nāpi kāryodakakriyā |
araṇya kāṣṭhavat tyaktvā kṣipeta tryaham eva tu ||
kāṣṭhavad iti vacanāt sakalaurdhvadaihikanivṛttāv ūnadvivārṣikasyākṛtacūḍasya nikhananam udakaniṣedhaś ca vaikalpikaḥ | yato laugakṣir āha |
tūṣṇīm evodakaṃ kuryāt tūṣṇīṃ saṃskāram eva ca |
sarveṣāṃ kṛtacūḍānām anyatrāpīcchayā dvayam || iti | [871]
anyatrākṛtacūḍānām ity arthaḥ | [evaṃ ca "ūnadvivarṣaṃ nikhanet" ity akṛtacūḍaviṣayaṃ draṣṭavyam |] asti hi prathame 'bde 'pi cūḍākarma | akṛtacūḍasya cāgnisaṃskāravikalpo nāmakaraṇād ūrdhvam eva | yad āha manuḥ |
nātrivarṣasya kartavyā bāndhavair udakakriyā |
jātadantasya vā kuryur nāmni vāpi kṛte sati || iti |
udakakriyāgnisaṃskāraṃ gamayaty avyabhicārāt | āśmaśānam iti | itaraḥ saṃpūrṇadivarṣo mṛto jñātibhiḥ sapiṇḍaiḥ śmaśānasaṃskārabhūmiṃ yāvad anuvrājyaḥ | tato ymadevatyaṃ sūktaṃ "pareyivāṃsam" iti ṣoḍarcaṃ yamadṛṣṭaṃ traiṣṭubhaṃ yamadevatyāś ca gāthāḥ "yo 'sya kauṣṭhya jagataḥ" iti tisro japadbhir laukikenāsaṃskṛtenāgninā dagdhavyaḥ | upanītaś cen mṛtas tadāhitāgner yā saṃskārakriyā tayārthavad aluptārthaṃ yathā bhavati tathā dagdhavyaḥ | luptāthānāṃ pātracamasādīnāṃ lopo 'nyeṣāṃ ca homādīnāṃ padārthānām anuṣṭhānam | yamaḥ |
yasyānayati śūdro 'gniṃ tṛṇaṃ kāṣṭhaṃ havīṃṣi ca |
pretatvaṃ ca sadā tasya sa cādharmeṇa lipyate ||
sa śūdra ity arthaḥ | precetāḥ |
snānaṃ pretasya putrādair vastrādyaiḥ pūjanaṃ tataḥ |
nagnadehaṃ dahen naiva kiṃcid deyaṃ parityajet ||
snānaṃ pūjanaṃ kāryam ity arthaḥ | kiṃcid vastraṃ yal loke devatvena prasiddhaṃ tat parityajet, tatsthebhyaś caṇḍālādibhyaḥ | hārītaḥ |
vihitaṃ hi sapiṇḍasya pretanirharaṇādikam |
doṣaḥ syād asapiṇḍasya tatrānāthakriyāṃ vinā ||
dhaniṣṭhāpañcakamṛte pañcaratnāni tanmukhe |
nyasyāhutitrayaṃ kartā dadyād vaha vapām iti ||
vaha vapāṃ jātavedaḥ pitṛbhyo yatraitān vettha vihitān parāke |
medha(da)saḥ kulyāta upa tāḥ sravantu satyā etā āśiṣaḥ santu kāmaiḥ ||
vahānnaṃ jātavedaḥ pitṛbhyo yatraitān vettha nihitān parāke |
annasya kulyāta upa tāḥ sravantu satyā etā āśiṣaḥ santu kāmaiḥ ||
[872]        vaha mā(māṃ)saṃ jātavedaḥ pitṛbhyo yatraitān vettha nihitān parāke |
māṃsasya kulyāta upa tāḥ sravantu satyā etā āśiṣaḥ santu kāmaiḥ ||
tato nirharaṇaṃ kuryāt taṃ saṃskuryād yathāvidhi |
itaraṃ nikhaned eva jale vā pratipadayet ||
tripādarkṣamṛte tadvad dhiraṇyaśakalaṃ mukhe |
nyasya pitṣṭamayaṃ kuryāt puruṣatritayaṃ tataḥ ||
homaṃ pratimukhaṃ kuryāt tato vaha vapām iti |
āsya eva sakarpāsaṃ kusumbhaṃ pratipādya ca ||
niryāsya sāgniṃ saṃskuryād bhuvy agnau vānyam utsṛjet ||
smṛtyantaram |
kanakaṃ hīrakaṃ nīlaṃ padmarāgaṃ sabhauktikam |
pañcaratnam iti proktam ṛṣibhiḥ sarvadarśibhiḥ ||
ratnānāṃ vāpy abhāve tu karṣaṃ karṣārdham eva vā |
suvarṇaṃ yojayet tatra yathāśakty atha vā budhaḥ ||
suvarṇaśakalārthas tu yatra yatra na vidyate |
tatra tatra nayed ājyaṃ tajo 'sītinidarśanāt ||
kriyāśrayaḥ |
punarvasūttarāṣāḍhā kṛttikottaraphālgunī |
pūrvabhadrā viśākhā ca jñeyam etat tripuṣkaram ||
sārāvaliś ca |
viṣamacaraṇaṃ dhiṣṇyaṃ bhadrā tithir yadi jāyate
śaniravigurukṣmāputrāṇāṃ kathaṃ cana vāsare |
munibhir uditaḥ so 'yaṃ yogas tripuṣkarasaṃjñakas
triguṇaphalado labdhe naṣṭe hṛte 'pi mṛte 'pi vā ||
dvitīyā saptamī ṣaṣṭhī dvādaśī digvasū tathī |
kramād gurubudhau śukrasaurabhaumaniśākarāḥ ||
etair naṣṭaṃ hṛtaṃ labdhaṃ yac cānyat kāraṇaṃ bhavet |
sarvaṃ tat triguṇaṃ proktaṃ tena caitat tripuṣkaram ||
viṣṇuḥ: "mṛtaṃ dvijaṃ na śūdreṇa nirhārayet | na śūdraṃ dvijena | pitaraṃ mātaraṃ ca putrā nirhareyur na dvijaṃ pitaram api śūdraḥ" || kātyāyanaḥ |
hutāyāṃ sāyam āhutyāṃ durbalaś ced gṛhī bhavet |
prātarhomas tadaiva syāj jīvec cec chvaḥ punar na vā ||
[873]        durbalaṃ snāpayitvā tu śuddhacailābhisaṃvṛtam |
dakṣiṇāśirasaṃ bhūmau barhiṣmatyāṃ niveśayet ||
ghṛtenābhyaktam āplāvya śuddhavastropavītinam |
candanokṣitasarvāṅgaṃ sumanobhir vibhūṣitam ||
hiraṇyaśakalānyasya kṣiptvā chidreṣu saptasu |
mukhyeṣv athāpidhāyainaṃ nirhareyuḥ sutādayaḥ ||
āmapātre 'nnam ādāya pretam agnipuraḥsaram |
eko 'nugacchet tasyānnam ardhaṃ pathy utsṛjed bhuvi ||
ardham ādahanaṃ prāpta āsīno dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ |
savyaṃ jānva(nvā?)sya śanakaiḥ satilaṃ piṇḍadānavat ||
tasyānnasyārdham ādāyaikaḥ puruṣaḥ pretam anugacchet | aparas tadardhaṃ bhuvi pathi ca tyajet | ādahanaṃ śmaśānam | vṛddhayājñavalkyaḥ |
āhitāgnir yathānyāyaṃ dagdhavyas tribhir agnibhiḥ |
anāhitāgnir ekena laukikenetaro janaḥ ||
ekena gṛhyeṇety arthaḥ | laukikaś cāgnir jātāraṇyor abhāve grāhyaḥ | tatsadbhāve tu mathita eva | laukikāgnipakṣe viśeṣam āha devalaḥ |
caṇḍālāgnir amedhyāgniḥ sūtikāgniś ca karhicit |
patitāgniś citāgniś ca na śiṣṭagrahaṇe 'rhati || 3.1, 2 ||
upanīta saṃsthite saṃskārāntarakarmāha |
saptamād daśamād vāpi jñātayo 'bhyupayanty apaḥ |
apa naḥ śośucad agham anena pitṛdiṅmukhāḥ || 3.3 ||
jñātayaḥ sapiṇḍāḥ samānodakāś ca sapta vā daśa vā dināni yāvat pitṛdiṅmukhā dakṣiṇābhimukhāś ca snātvā jalaṃ pretāya dātum abhyupeyur upagaccheyuḥ | gatvā cāpa naḥ śośucad agham iti kutsadṛṣṭayāgnidevatyayābdevatyayā vā gāyatryā snātvodakaṃ dadyuḥ || 3.3 ||
ajñātiṣv atideśam āha |
evaṃ mātāmahācāryapretānāṃ codakakriyā |
kāmodakaṃ sakhiprattāsvasrīyaśvaśurartvijām || 3.4 ||
[874]
mātāmahādīnāṃ pretānāṃ dauhitrādibhiḥ pūrvoktaprakāreṇodakakriyā kāryā, sakhiprabhṛtīnāṃ kāmodakaṃ kuryāt | yady udakaṃ dātuṃ kāmayate dadyād ity arthaḥ | prattā kṛtavivāhā duhitrādiḥ | pāraskaraḥ: "kāmodakam ṛtvikśvaśurasakhimātulabhāgineyānām" || 3.4 ||
udakadānadharmavidhim āha |
sakṛt prasiñcanty udakaṃ nāmagotreṇa vāgyatāḥ |
uktasaṃkhyākeṣu pratyahaṃ sakṛd ekavāraṃ jñātyādaya udakaṃ nāmagotrābhyāṃ vācaṃyamāḥ prasiñceyuḥ | kātyāyanaḥ |
athānapekṣam etyāpaḥ sarva eva śavaspṛśaḥ |
snātvā sacailam ācamya dadyur asyodakaṃ sthale ||
gotranāmapadānte ca tarpayāmīty ayaṃ vadan |
dakṣiṇāgrān kuśān kṛtvā satilaṃ tu pṛthak pṛthak ||
viṣṇuḥ: "nirhṛtya bāndhavāḥ pretaṃ saṃskṛtyāpradakṣiṇena citām abhigamyāpsu savāsaso nimajjanaṃ kuryuḥ | pretasyodakanirvāpaṇaṃ kṛtvaikaṃ ca piṇḍaṃ kuśeṣu dadyuḥ" | vasiṣṭhaḥ: "savyottarābhyāṃ pāṇibhyām udakakriyāṃ kurvīrann ayugmā dakṣiṇāmukhāḥ" | baijavāpaḥ: "udakāntaṃ gatvā sakṛd unmṛjyāpsu savyasya pāṇeḥ kaniṣṭhikayāvalikhanti kaniṣṭhaṃ pāpam iti, tasminn ekam udakāñjaliṃ pretāya dadyur amuṣmai svadheti" | hārtītaḥ: "niṣkramya saṃskṛtyāpo gatvāplāvyāsau tṛpyatām ity udakāñjaliṃ ninayanti" | niṣkramya grāmān niṣkramyety arthaḥ | paiṭhīnasiḥ: "pretaṃ manasā dhyāyan dakṣiṇābhimukhas trīn udakāñjalīn ninayet | śāvaṃ prakṛtyaikādaśahe viramet" | pracetāḥ |
dine dine 'ñjalīn pūrṇān pradadyāt pretakāraṇāt |
tāvad vṛddhis tu kartavyā yāvat piṇḍaḥ samāpyate ||
gautamaḥ: "prathamatṛtīyasaptamanavameṣūdakakriyāḥ" | [875] pracetāḥ: "pretasya bāndhavā yathāvṛddham udakam avatīryāpo 'bhyupeyur udakānte prasiñceyur apasavye yajñopavītavasasī kṛtvā dakṣiṇābhimukhā brāhmaṇasyodaṅmukhaprāṅmukhā rājanyavaiśayor yāvad āśaucaṃ tāvat pretasyodakaṃ piṇḍaṃ ca dadyuḥ" | ādipurāṇāt |
ādau tu vastraṃ prakṣālya tenaivācchaditais tataḥ |
kartavyaṃ tu sacailaṃ ca snānaṃ sarvamalāpaham ||
tataḥ pāṣāṇapṛṣṭhe tu sarvair deyaṃ tilodakam |
ekenaikena deyāś tu viprāyāñjalayo daśa ||
kṣatre dvādaś deyās tu vaiśyāya daś pañca ca |
triṃśac chūdrāya deyās tu pretabhūmigatāya ca ||
apasavyaṃ kvacit kṛtvā vastrayajñopavītakaiḥ |
dakṣiṇābhimukhair viprair deyaṃ toyāñjalitrayam ||
vāmāṅguṣṭhapravāhena bhūmāv evātha vā kvacit |
asāv amukagotras tu pretas tṛpyatv idaṃ paṭhan ||
atra viprādibhyo daśādisaṃkhyākajalāñjalipradānam ekaṃ karma, añjalitrayadānaṃ tu karmāntaram | yad vā pratyahaṃ deyo 'sya jalāñjaliḥ sākalyena vā | atra cāsāv amukagotraḥ pretas tṛpyatv iti mantraḥ | śaṅkhaḥ: "pretasya bāndhavā yathāvṛddham udakam avatīryāpa na ity arthayerann apaḥ prasiñceran sakṛt pradakṣiṇābhimukho rājanyavaiśyāv apy evam eva, vāsoyajñopavīte kṛtvāñjalināsāv etad udakam ity uktvā tasmād uttīrya pretasaṃspṛṣṭāni vāsāṃsi parityajya paridadhyur anyāni" | āñjalīnāṃ nāmasaṃkhyāvidhānaṃ pretopakārātiśayāyeti mantavyam | rāmāyaṇe jalapradānavākyaṃ rāma āha |
idaṃ puruṣaśārdūla vimalaṃ divyam akṣayam |
ptiṛlokeṣu pānīyaṃ maddattam upatiṣṭhatām ||
tathā |
pitaraṃ tarpayāmāsa bharataḥ sasuhṛjjanaḥ |
sa ca paurajanaḥ sarvaḥ sāmātyaḥ sapurohitaḥ ||
[876]        tarpayāmāsa rājānaṃ salilena vidhānataḥ || iti |
smṛtyantaram |
snātvā sacailaṃ spṛṣṭvāgniṃ ghṛtaṃ prāśyāyujaḥ (?) pṛthak |
prācīnāvītino nāmagotrābhyāṃ dakṣiṇāmukhāḥ ||
jale pretāya madhyāhne dadyur yāvad aśuddhatā |
atrāpavādam āha |
na brahmacāriṇaḥ kuryur udakaṃ patitās tathā || 3.5 ||
brahmacāriṇaḥ sapiṇḍasodakatve saty api pretāyodakaṃ na dadyuḥ, tathā vakṣyamāṇalakṣaṇāḥ patitāḥ | tatra manur viśeṣam āha |
ādiṣṭī nodakaṃ kuryād ā vratasya samāpanāt |
samāpte tūdakaṃ kṛtvā trirātram aśucir bhavet ||
ādiṣṭī brahmacārī | ā vratasya samāpanād ā samāvartanād ity arthaḥ || 3.5 ||
adhunā keṣāṃcid udakadānakartṛtvasaṃpradānatvayor apavādam āha |
pāṣāṇḍān āśritāḥ stenā bhartṛghnyaḥ kāmagādikāḥ |
surāpya ātmaghātinyo nāśaucodakabhājanāḥ || 3.6 ||
pāṣāṇḍān baudhādīn āśritās taddīkṣāyāṃ praviṣṭāḥ, stenāś caurāḥ, striyaś ca yā bhartṛghātinyaḥ kāmagāḥ svairiṇyaḥ | ādiśabdena svagarbhasya brāhmaṇasya ca hantrīprabṛtikā gṛhyante | surāpya ātmaghātinyaś ca prasiddhāḥ | pāṣāṇḍāśritādayo 'śaucasya trirātrādyapaneyatayā vakṣyamānasyodakadānasya ca bhājanāḥ saṃbandhino na bhavanti | eteṣu mṛteṣu sapiṇḍaiḥ sapiṇḍamaraṇe caitair āśaucādi na kāryam ity arthaḥ | vṛddhamanuḥ |
klībādyā nodakaṃ kuryuḥ stenā vratyā vikarmiṇaḥ |
garbhabhartṛdruhaś caiva surāpyaś caiva yoṣitaḥ ||
surāpīṇāṃ pātityād eva pratiṣiddhodakadānānāṃ tatsaṃpradānatvapratiṣedhārthaṃ punargarhaṇam | maraṇaviśeṣe 'pi nodakaṃ deyam \ yad āha vṛddhayājñavalkyaḥ |
vidyudgonṛpaviprāmbuśṛṅgidaṃṣṭryagnighātitāḥ |
vṛthotpanno mahāklībo vratī naivodakārhakāḥ ||
liṅgarahito mahāklībaḥ | vratī mahāvratyādiḥ pāṣaṇḍī (?) | [877] yamaḥ |
caṇḍālād udakāt sarpād brāhmaṇāḍ vaidyutād api |
daṃṣṭribhyaś ca paśubhyaś ca maraṇaṃ pāpakarmaṇām ||
udakaṃ piṇḍadānaṃ ca pretebhyo yat pradīyate |
nopatiṣṭhati tat sarvam antarikṣe vinaśyati ||
nāśaucaṃ nodakaṃ nāsru na dāhādyaṃ ca karma ca |
brahmadaṇḍahatānāṃ ca na kuryāt kaṭadhāraṇam ||
brahmadaṇḍo brahmaśāpaḥ | kaṭaḥ śavakhaṭvā | āpastambaḥ |
vyāpādayed ihātmānaṃ svayaṃ yo 'gnyudakādibhiḥ |
vihitaṃ tasya nāśaucaṃ nāpi kāryodakakriyā ||
manuḥ |
vṛthā saṃkarajātānāṃ pravrajyāsu ca tiṣṭhatām |
ātmanas tyāgināṃ caiva nivartetodakakriyā ||
svayaṃ grahaṇād rāgataḥ pravṛtto na vidhita iti gamyate | ata eva gautamaḥ: "prāyānaśanaśastrāgniviṣodakodbandhanaprayāṇaiś cec chatām" | icchatām iti viśeṣeṇopādānāt pramādād vidhito vā mṛtānām adoṣa iti | tathā ca aṅgirāḥ |
atha kaścit pramādena mriyetāgnyudakādibhiḥ |
tasyāśaucaṃ vidhātavyaṃ kartavyā codakakriyā ||
pramādo 'navadhānatā | asti ca prāyaścittādau vidhiḥ | tathā ca ādipurāṇam |
duścikitsair mahārogaiḥ pīḍitas tu pumān yadi |
praviśej jvalanaṃ dīptaṃ karoty anaśanaṃ tathā ||
agādhatoyarāśiṃ vā bhṛguprapatanaṃ tathā |
gacchet mahāpathaṃ vāpi tuṣāragirim ādarāt ||
prayāgavaṭaśākhāyāṃ dehatyāgaṃ karoti vā |
svayaṃ dehavināśasya kāle prāpte mahāmatiḥ ||
uttamān prāpnuyāl lokān nātmaghātī bhavet kvacit |
mahāpāpakṣayāt svarge divyān bhogān samaśnute ||
eteṣām adhikāras tu sarveṣāṃ sarvajantuṣu |
narāṇām atha nārīṇāṃ sarvavarṇeṣu sarvadā ||
[878]        īdṛśaṃ mṛtakaṃ teṣāṃ jīvatāṃ kutracid bhavet |
āśaucaṃ syāt tryahaṃ teṣāṃ vajrānalahetuṣu ca ||
vārāṇasyāṃ mriyed yas tu pratyākhyātabhiṣakkriyaḥ |
kāṣṭhapāṣāṇamadhyastho jāhnavījalamadhyagaḥ ||
avimuktonmukhas tasya karṇamūlagato haraḥ |
praṇavaṃ tārakaṃ brūte nānyathā kasyacit kvacit ||
tathā |
vṛddhatīrthasya pūrvārdhe gaṅgāyāḥ paścimena tu |
agnitīrthaṃ tataḥ puṇyaṃ vṛddhatīrthād adhaḥ sthitam ||
indrayopanibhādīnāṃ jvālā yatra pradṛśyate |
tac ca sthānaṃ na sulabhaṃ martyas tac ca na vindate ||
yadi vā vindate kaścit prasādān narapuṃgavaḥ |
svaśarīraṃ nayan mokṣam iti satyaṃ vadāmy aham ||
vṛddho vāpy atha vā bālo yauvanastho 'pi mānavaḥ |
dehatyāgaṃ tu yaḥ kuryāt tatra sarvātmanā vibhīḥ ||
ādityamaṇḍalaṃ bhittvā so 'pi mokṣaṃ gamiṣyati |
avināśam idaṃ tīrthaṃ nityaṃ saṃnihito hariḥ ||
viśokāsaṃnidhau pāpam aṇumātraṃ kṛtaṃ yadi |
nistaren merutulyaṃ vai kṣetramāhātmyasaṃnidheḥ ||
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena pāpaṃ tatra vivarjayet ||
tathā |
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi vṛddhatīrthasya dakṣiṇe |
dehatyāgena vai puṃsāṃ yā gatir bhavīti ca ||
svargadvāraṃ samāsādya śucivastraparivṛtaḥ |
mṛgacihnaśilāṃ gatvā svargadvāreṇa tena ca ||
tataḥ svargaśilāṃ prāpya puṇyakarmā narottamaḥ |
varaṃ prārthayate yatra tyaktātmā dairyam āśritaḥ ||
ādau praṇamate devaṃ tathā tīrthaṃ surārcitam |
oṃ namaste viśvarūpākhye viśoke viśvasaṃbhave ||
[879]        namas te mokṣamārgāya parāya paramātmane |
namo hiraṇyanābhāya hayaśīrṣāya viṣṇave ||
ajatuṅgāya tīrthāya avināśāya pātu mām |
tathā viṣṇur viśokāya yathā tīrtham anāmayam ||
yācñā me varam etat tu śravaṇaṃ dīyatām iti ||
bhīto 'smy ataḥ pitṛpathayānamūrchito mahāpathe daṇḍasahasratāḍitaḥ |
vināśitaṃ triguṇarāgarañjitaṃ kalevaraṃ maraṇabhiyārpayāmi tat ||
yathā puṇyaṃ mahātīrthaṃ yathā naubandhano giriḥ |
tena satyena kāyān me vimuktis tv akṣayā bhavet ||
iti mantrottamaṃ smṛtvā dehaṃ tyaktvā jalāśaye |
smaraṇāt tasya mantrasya tathā naubandhanasya ca |
ādityamaṇḍalaṃ bhittvā parabrahmaṇi līyate || iti |
[vṛddhatīrthādīni kāśmīradeśe naubadhanagirer mūle vijñeyāni |]
tathā |
bhikṣāhāro nirāhāro laghvāśī vigataspṛhaḥ |
sarvadvaṃdvanirodhena na vaset tatra mānavaḥ ||
vimalo virajā vipro vipāpmā vyomasaṃnibhaḥ |
iha loke yaśo'vāptiḥ pretya svargaṃ ca śāśvatam ||
tathā |
mahāprasthānayātrā ca kartavyā tuhinopari |
āśritya sattvaṃ dhairyaṃ ca sadyaḥ svargapradā ca sā ||
yāvat puraṃdaro loke na jātaḥ kāryagauravāt |
tāvat tuṣāramadhye tu kas tanuṃ tyaktum utsahet ||
yāvat tuṣāradagdhas tu muñcat prāṇān vicetanaḥ |
pradakṣiṇāvartaśikhaṃ paśyed dīptaṃ hutāśanam ||
saṃkarṣaṇaṃ vapur viṣṇoś caṇḍāgnibhavadāhakam |
vivasvān |
sarvendriyaviraktasya svavyāpārākṣamasya ca |
prāyaścittam anujñātam agnipāto mahāpathaḥ ||
[880]        dharmārjanāsamarthasy kartuḥ pāpāṅkitasya ca |
brāhmaṇasyāpy anujñātaṃ tīrthe prāṇavimokṣaṇam ||
icchanti jīvitaṃ devā dharmārthaṃ tu dvijātiṣu |
adharmajīvinas tīrthe dehatyāgo vidhīyate ||
brahmagarbhaḥ |
yo 'nuṣṭhātuṃ na śaknoti mahāvyādhyupapīḍitaḥ |
so 'gniṃ vāri mahāyātrāṃ kurvan nāmutra duṣyati ||
vṛddhagārgyaḥ |
vyādhibhir luptaceṣṭānāṃ gṛhasthānāṃ vidhīyate |
mahāprasthānagamanaṃ jvalanāmbupraveśanam ||
bhṛguprapatanaṃ caiva vṛthā necchet tu jīvitum |
bhṛguḥ parvatataṭakaḥ | vasiṣṭhaḥ |
bhṛguprapatanād rājyaṃ nākapṛṣṭham anāśakāt |
vyāsaḥ |
jale sapta sahasrāṇi caturdaśa hutāśane |
anāśakasya rājendra phalasaṃkhyā na vidyate ||
jamadagniḥ |
vaitānaṃ prakṣiped apsu āvasathyaṃ catuṣpathe |
pātrāṇi tu dahed agnau yajamāne vṛthā mṛte ||
smṛtyantaram |
ātmanas tyāgināṃ nāsti patitānāṃ tathā kriyā |
teṣām api tathā gaṅgātoye saṃsthāpanaṃ hitam || 3.6 ||
sāpavādam udakadānam abhidhāya taduttarakarmāha |
kṛtodakān samuttīrṇān mṛduśādvālasaṃsthitān |
snātān apavadeyus tān itihāsaiḥ putrātanaiḥ || 3.7 ||
tān pretasaṃbandhinaḥ putrādīn snātān kṛtodakadānāṃs tīrthād uttīrṇān udgatān mṛdau śādvale haritatṛṇāvṛte predeśe samavasthitān anye vṛddhāḥ sādhavaś ciraṃtanair itihāsaiḥ pūrvavṛttāntakathābhir apavadeyur apagataduḥkhyān kuryuḥ | paiṭhīnasiḥ: "āpo asmān ity uttīrya anyavastrānuveṣṭanam | tac cakṣur ity upasthāya sūryaṃ bālapuraḥsarāḥ" | śādvale 'vatiṣṭherann iti śeṣaḥ | kātyāyanaḥ |
evaṃ kṛtodakān samyaksarvāñ śādvalasaṃsthitān |
āplutya punar ācāntān vadeyus tān amāyinaḥ ||
[881]        mā śokaṃ kurutānitye sarvasmin prāṇidharmaṇi |
dharmaṃ kuruta yatnena yo vaḥ saha gamiṣyati || 3.7 ||
yathāpavadeyus tathāha |
mānuṣye kadalīstambhaniḥsāre sāramārgaṇam |
karoti yaḥ sa saṃmūḍho jalabudbudasaṃnibhe || 3.8 ||
pañcadhā saṃbhṛtaḥ kāyo yadi pañcatvam āgataḥ |
karmabhiḥ svaśarīrotthais tatra kā paridevanā || 3.9 ||
gantrī vasumatī nāśam udadhir daivatāni ca |
phenaprakhyaḥ kathaṃ nāśaṃ martyaloko na yāsyati || 3.10 ||
nigadavyākhyātam etat | śrīrāmāyaṇe |
saṃyogo 'bhimato yeṣāṃ niyogaḥ katham apriyaḥ |
saṃyogo hi viyogena grasta evābhijāyate ||
kiṃ tu khalv asi mūḍhas tvaṃ śocyaḥ kim anuśocasti |
yadā tvām anuśocantaḥ śocyā yāsyanti tāṃ gatim ||
adarśanād āpatitaḥ punaś cādarśanaṃ gataḥ |
gatvāsau veda na tv antam ataḥ kim anuśocasi ||
nāyam atyantasaṃvāsaḥ kasyacit kenacit saha |
api svena śarīreṇa kim utānyaiḥ pṛthagjanaiḥ ||
mahābhārate śalyaparvaṇi hate duryodhane dhṛtarāṣṭrasya vāsudevakātaṃ paridevanam |
tataḥ prāyān mahārāja mādhavo bhagavān rathī |
nāgasāhvayam āsādya praviveśa ca vīryavān ||
praviśya nagaraṃ vīro rathaghoṣeṇa nādayan |
vidito dhṛtarāṣṭrasya so 'vatīrya rathottamāt ||
abhyagacchad adīnātmā dhṛtarāṣṭraniveśanam |
pūrvaṃ cābhigataṃ tatra so 'paśyad ṛṣisattamam ||
pādau prapīḍya vyāsasya rājñaś cāpi janārdanaḥ |
abhyāvādayad avyagro gāndhārīṃ cāpi keśavaḥ ||
[882]        tatas tu yāvad aśreṣṭho dhṛtarāṣṭrasya mādhavaḥ |
pāṇim ālambya rājñaḥ sa sasvaraṃ praruroda ha ||
sa muhūrtam ivotsṛjya bāṣpaṃ śokasamudbhavam |
prakṣālya vāriṇā netre hy ācamya ca yathāvidhi ||
uvāca praśrito vākyaṃ ghṛtarāṣṭram arṃdamaḥ |
sarve kṣayāntā vicayāḥ patanāntāḥ samucchrayāḥ ||
saṃyogā viprayogāntā maraṇāntaṃ hi jīvitam || 3.8, 9, 10 ||
rodane doṣam āha |
śleśmāśru bāndhavair muktaṃ preto bhuṅkte yato 'vaśaḥ |
ato na roditavyaṃ hi kriyāḥ kāryāḥ prayatnataḥ || 3.11 ||
ayam api nigadavyākhyātaḥ ślokaḥ || 3.11 ||
uttarakarmāha |
iti saṃśrutya gaccheyur gṛhaṃ bālapuraḥsarāḥ |
vidaśya nimbapatrāṇi niyatā dvāri veśmanaḥ || 3.12 ||
ācamyāthāgnim udakaṃ gomayaṃ gaurasarṣapān |
praviśeyuḥ samālabhya kṛtvāśmani padaṃ śanaiḥ || 2.13 ||
sādhibhir udīritam itihāsavākyajātaṃ śrutvā bālān puraskṛtya gṛhaṃ gaccheyuḥ | tasya ca dvāri nimbapatrāṇi dantaiḥ khaṇḍayitvā parityajya niyatātmāno 'pa ācamyānantaram agnim udakaṃ [gomayaṃ] gaurasarṣapān spṛṣṭvā pāṣāṇe caraṇaṃ kṛtyā śanaiḥ praviśeyuḥ | śaṅkhaḥ: "dūrvāpravālam agniṃ vṛṣabhaṃ cālabhya gṛhadvāre pretāya piṇḍaṃ datvā paścāt praviśeyuḥ" | paiṭhīnasiḥ |
gṛhaṃ gatvā sthitā dvāri sarpiragnyajagomayān |
praviśeyur gṛhaṃ spṛṣṭvā saṃviśeyuḥ kaṭopari || 3.12, 13 ||
praveśanādikaṃ karma pretasaṃsparśinām api |
icchatāṃ tatkṣaṇāc chuddhir anyeṣāṃ snānasaṃyamān || 3.14 ||
[883]
uktaṃ gṛhapraveśanaṃ tadādi kṛtvā yad upariṣṭād vakṣyate | trirātraṃ daśarātraṃ vety etad uktaṃ karma tad asapiṇḍānāṃ vihitaṃ veditavyam | ye tu necchātaḥ śavasaṃsparśinaḥ kiṃ tu vidhitaḥ, teṣāṃ sadyaḥ snānaprāṇāyāmasamanantaram eva śuddhiḥ | ye śavasvyālaṃkaraṇanirharaṇadāhān kurvanti, te śavaspṛśaḥ | vidhim antareṇa ca śavaspṛśāṃ daśarātreṇa śuddhir ity āha manuḥ |
ahnā caikena rātryā ca trirātrair eva ca tribhiḥ |
śavaspṛśo viśudhyanti tryahāt tūdakadāyinaḥ ||
ahnā caikena rātryā cety ahorātrasyopalakṣaṇārtham | trirātrair eva ca tribhir iti navarātrasya | evaṃ daśarātro 'yaṃ śavaspṛśāṃ kṣatriyādiśavaspṛśāṃ śuddhaye vidhīyate | atra ca daśarātrāc chuddhir brāhmaṇaśavaspṛśām, kṣatriyādiśavaspṛśāṃ tu kālāntarāt | tathā ca viṣṇupurāṇe |
yo 'savarṇaṃ tu mūlyena nītvā caiva dahen naraḥ |
āśaucaṃ tu bhavet tasya pretajātisamaṃ sadā ||
yadā tu na mūlyarāgāt pravṛttis tadā naitat, kiṃ tu snānāt | tad āha manuḥ |
nāsipiṇḍaṃ dvijaṃ pretaṃ vipro nirhṛtya bandhuvat |
viśudhyati trirātreṇa mātur āptāṃś ca bāndhavān ||
etac ca tadgṛhavāsinas tada[nnā]nāśitve sati draṣṭavyam | yad āha sa eva |
yady annam atti teṣāṃ tu daśāhenaiva śudhyati |
anadann annam ahnaiva na cet tasmin gṛhe vaset ||
daśāhagrahaṇaṃ sarvavarṇāśaucopalakṣaṇārtham | dharmārthaṃ tu śavaspṛśo na kaścid doṣa ity āha parāśaraḥ |
anāthaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ pretaṃ ye vahanti dvijātayaḥ |
pade pade yajñaphalam ānupūrvyā labhanti te ||
na teṣām aśubhaṃ kiṃcit pāpaṃ cāśubhakarmaṇi |
jalāvagāhanāt teṣāṃ sadyaḥ śaucaṃ vidhīyate ||
tathā |
pretasaṃsparśasaṃskārair brāhmaṇo naiva duṣyati |
voḍhā cāpy agnidātā ca sadyaḥ snātvā śucir bhavet ||
yat tu hārītenoktam: "pretaspṛśo grāmaṃ na praviśeyur ā nakṣatradarśanāt, rātrau ced ādityasya" iti, [884] tat snehavaśena śavaspṛśām aśavaspṛśāṃ vāsapiṇḍānāṃ tadannam aśnatāṃ tadgṛhe cānivasatāṃ veditavyam || 3.14 ||
nirharaṇādisakalapretakarmapratiṣedhadarśanārthaṃ brahmacāry udakaṃ na kuryād ity uktam | tasya viṣayaviśeṣe 'pavādam āha |
ācāryapitrupādhāyān nirhṛtyāpi vratī vratī |
sa kaṭānnaṃ ca nāśnīyān na ca taiḥ saha saṃvaset || 3.15 ||
ācāryādīnāṃ nirharaṇādi kurvann api vratī brahmacārī vratī bhavati nāvratī | anyāṃs tu nirharann avratī brahmacaryātmakavratānadhikṛto vinaṣṭopanayanasaṃskāra ity ārthiko 'rthaḥ | sa brahmacārī nirharann apy ācāryādīn kaṭānnam aśaucannaṃ nāśnīyāt | tair āśauc(cyā)dibhiḥ saha ca na saṃvaset | manus tu mātur guroś caupacārikasyāpi nirharaṇe doṣābhāvam āha |
ācāryaṃ svam upādhyāyaṃ pitaraṃ mātaraṃ gurum |
nirhṛtyāpi vratī pretān na vratena viyujyate ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ: "brahmacāriṇaḥ śavakarmaṇā vratāvṛttir anyatra mātāpitṛbhyām" | vratāvṛttiḥ punarupanayanam | brahmapurāṇam |
ācāryaṃ vāpy upādhyāyaṃ guruṃ vā pitaraṃ ca vā |
mātaraṃ vā svayaṃ dagdhvā vratasthas tatra bhojanam ||
kṛtvā patati no tasmāt pretānnaṃ tatra bhakṣayet |
anyatra bhojanaṃ kuryān na ca taiḥ saha saṃvaset ||
ekāham aśucir bhūtvā dvitīye 'hani śudhyati |
tathā |
brāhmaṇo na dahec chūdraṃ mitraṃ vāpy anyam eva tu |
mohād dagdhvā tataḥ snātaḥ spṛṣṭvāgniṃ prāśayed ghṛtam ||
upavāsarataḥ paścāt trirātreṇa viśudhyati |
sarveṣāṃ syād ahorātraṃ śavānugamanād api ||
śavasparśavidhānoktaṃ vihitaṃ sārvavarṇikam || 3.15 ||
jñātīnāṃ niyamaviśeṣam āha |
krītalabdhāśanā bhūmau svapeyus te pṛthak pṛthak |
piṇḍayajñāvṛtā deyaṃ pretāyānnaṃ dinatrayam || 3.16 ||
[885]
te jñātayo gṛhaṃ praviśya trirātraṃ yāvat krītam annam aśnīyur ayācitaṃ labdhaṃ vā | tathā pṛthak pṛthag bhuvi śayīran | tathā putrādinā pretāya piṇḍaḥ piṇḍapitṛyajñasyāvṛtā mantravarjitayā prakriyayā deyaḥ | krītādyaśanabhojanaṃ ca tritrātropavāsāsamarthān prati vidhīyate, yad āha vasiṣṭhaḥ: "gṛhān vrajitvāghaprastare tryaham anaśnanta āsīran krītotpannena vā varteran" | aghaprastara āśaucināṃ śayanādyarthaḥ kaṭaḥ | paiṭḥīnasiḥ |
ekāham upavāsaḥ syād aśnīyur labdham eva vā |
gatvāraṇyaṃ caturthe 'hni pūrvāhṇe tv asthisaṃcayaḥ ||
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ |
krītalabdhāśanāś caiva bhaveyuḥ susamāhitāḥ |
na caiva māṃsam aśnīyur vrajeyur na ca yoṣitām ||
manuḥ |
kṣāralavaṇānnāḥ syur nimajjeyuś ca te tryaham |
māṃsāśanaṃ ca nāśnīyuḥ śayīraṃś ca pṛthak kṣitau ||
gautamaḥ: "adhaḥ śayyāsanino brahmacāriṇaś ca sarve samāsīran, māṃsaṃ na bhakṣeur ā pradānāt, prathamatṛtīyasaptamanavameṣūdakakarma, navame vāsasāṃ tyāgaḥ | antye tv antyānām" | pradānm ekādaśāhādikaṃ śrāddham | vāsasāṃ tyāgaḥ prakṣālanārthaṃ rajakārpaṇam | antyam āśaucāntyam ahaḥ | tatrāntyānāṃ vāsasām atyantatyājyānāṃ tyāgaḥ | antyānāṃ śūdrāṇāṃ vā | ante tv antyānām ity api paṭhanti | bṛhaspatiḥ |
prathame 'hni tṛtīye ca pañcame saptame 'pi ca |
navame vāsasāṃ tyāgo nakharomṇāṃ tathāntime ||
saptame 'pi snātavyam iti śeṣaḥ | navamadaśamayos tu vastratyāgo nakhādicchedanaṃ ca snānena samuccīyate | brahmapurāṇam |
snātvā trirātraṃ kurvanti pretāyodakatarpaṇam |
śmaśānadevatāyāgaṃ caturthadivase tataḥ ||
bhavanti pūjitā yasmāt tatrasthāḥ śaṃkarādayaḥ |
snātaiḥ sudhautavastraiś ca śmaśānasya samīpagaiḥ ||
svajātivihitair dravyair yathāśakti samāhitaiḥ |
mṛnmayeṣu tu bhāṇḍeṣu hṛdyeṣu carukeṣu ca ||
[886]        supakvair bhakṣyabhojyaiś ca pāyasaiḥ pānakais tathā |
phalamūlair vanotthaiś ca pūjyāḥ kravyādadevatāḥ ||
kaiścid arghyaṃ pradātavyaṃ kaiścit puṣpaṃ suśobhanam |
dhūpo dīpas tathā mālyaṃ kaiścid deyaṃ tvarānvitaiḥ ||
tatra pātrāṇi pūrṇāni śmaśānāgneḥ samantataḥ |
kaiścid deyāni sarvāṇi yāni hy anyakṛtāni ca ||
nivedayeti vaktavyaṃ taiḥ sarvam anahaṃkṛtaiḥ |
namaḥ kravyādamukhebhyo devebhya iti sarvadā ||
ye 'smiñ śmaśāne devāḥ syur bhagavantaḥ sanātanāḥ |
te 'smāt sakāśād gṛhṇantu balim aṣṭāṅgam akṣayam ||
pretasyāsya śabhāṃl lokān prayacchantv api śāśvatān |
asmākam āyur ārogyaṃ sukhaṃ ca dadatāṃ varāḥ ||
evaṃ kṛtvā balīn sarvān kṣīreṇābhyukṣya vāgyataiḥ |
visarjanaṃ ca devānāṃ kartavyaṃ tu samāhitaiḥ ||
tato yajñiyavṛkṣottāṃ śākhām ādāya vāgyataḥ |
apasavyaṃ kramād vastraṃ kaścikṛtvā (?) sagotrajaḥ ||
pretasyāsthīni gṛhṇāti pradhānāṅgodbhavāni ca |
pañcagavyena susnāpya kṣaumavastreṇa veṣṭya ca ||
prakṣipya mṛnmaye bhāṇḍe nave sācchādane śubhe |
araṇye vṛkṣamūle vā śuddhe saṃsthāpayet kvacit ||
tatsthānāc chanakair nītvā kadācij jāhnavījale |
kaścit kṣipati satputro dauhitro vā sahodaraḥ ||
gṛhītvāsthīni tad bhasma nītvā toye vinikṣipet |
tataḥ saṃmārjanaṃ bhūmeḥ kartavyaṃ gomayāmbubhiḥ ||
pūjā ca puṣpadhūpādyair balibhiḥ pūrvavat kramāt |
bhūmer ācchādanārthaṃ tu vṛkṣaḥ puṣakaro 'tha vā ||
eḍūko vā prakartavyas tatra sarvaiḥ svabandhubhiḥ |
tataḥ sacailaṃ snānaṃ tu kartavyaṃ tair vidhānataḥ ||
tathā |
kvacit tṛtīye viprasya caturthe kṣatriyasya tu |
pañcame vaiśyajātes tu śūdrasya daśame 'hani ||
[887]        asthnāṃ tu saṃcayaḥ prete kiriyate doṣagauravāt |
jīvatām api varṇānāṃ saṃpanne cāpy ayaṃ kramaḥ ||
caturthe brāhmaṇānāṃ tu pañcame 'hani bhūbhṛtām |
navame vaiśyajātīnāṃ śūdrāṇāṃ daśamāt pare ||
kartavyaṃ tu naraiḥ śrāddhaṃ deśakālāvirodhataḥ |
sapiṇḍāś ca sajātīyās tathānye 'pi bubhukṣitāḥ ||
dīnānāthāndhakṛpaṇās tatrānnam api bhuñjate |
tryahāc chauce dvitīye 'hni kartavyas tv asthisaṃcayaḥ ||
tathā |
asthīni mātāpitṛpūrvajānāṃ nayanti gaṅgām api ye kadācit |
sadbhāvakasyāpi dayābhibhutās teṣāṃ tu tīrthāni phalapradāni ||
tathā |
snātvā tataḥ pañcagavyena siktvā hiraṇyamadhyājyatilaiś ca yojya |
tatas tu mṛtpiṇḍāpuṭe nidhāya paśyan diśaṃ pretagaṇopagūḍhām ||
namo 'stu dharmāya vadan praviśya jalaṃ sa me preta iti kṣipec ca |
utthāya bhāsvantam avekṣya sūryaṃ sa dakṣiṇāṃ vipramukhāya dadyāt ||
evaṃ kṛte pretapure sthitasya svarge gatiḥ syāc ca mahendratulyā ||
tathā |
mātuḥ kulaṃ pitṛkulaṃ varjayitvā narādhamaḥ |
asthīny anyakulotthasya nītvā cāndrāyaṇāc chuciḥ ||
marīciḥ |
pretapiṇḍaṃ bahir dadyād darbhamantravivarjitam |
prāgudīcyāṃ caruṃ kṛtvā snātaḥ prayatamānasaḥ ||
pracetāḥ: "asaṃskṛtānāṃ bhūmau piṇḍaṃ dadyāt saṃskṛtānāṃ kuśeṣu" | gṛhyapariśiṣṭāt |
asagotraḥ sagotro vā yadi strī yadi vā pumān |
prathame 'hani yo dadyāt sa daśāhaṃ samāpayet ||
śunaḥpucchaḥ |
phalamūlaiś ca payasā śākena ca guḍena ca |
gilamiśraṃ tu darbheṣu piṇḍaṃ dakṣiṇato haret ||
dvāradeśe pradātavyo devatāyataneṣu vā |
tūṣṇīṃ prasekaṃ puṣpaṃ ca dīpaṃ dhūpaṃ tathaiva ca ||
śālinā saktubhir vāpi śākair vāpy atha nirvapet |
prathame 'hani yad dravyaṃ tad eva syād daśāhikam ||
śaṅkhaḥ: "bhūmau mālyaṃ piṇḍaṃ pānīyam upalepanaṃ ca dadyuḥ" | [888] pāraskaraḥ |
brāhmaṇe daśa piṇḍās tu kṣatriye dvādaś smṛtāḥ |
vaiśye pañcadaśa proktāḥ śūdre triṃśat prakīrtitāḥ ||
uktasaṃkhyāyām asamarthān prati sa eva saṃkhyāntaram āha |
pretebhyaḥ sarvavarṇebhyaḥ piṇḍān dadyur daśaiva tu |
śrāddhakarmaṇi saṃprāpte piṇḍa eko vidhīyate ||
ete ca piṇḍā yājñavalkyamate trirātra eva deyāḥ | anyamate tu pratyahaṃ yāvad āśaucam ekaikaḥ piṇḍo deyaḥ | tathā ca viṣṇuḥ: "yāvad āśaucaṃ pretasyodakaṃ piṇḍam ekaṃ ca dadyuḥ" | vṛddhaprecetāḥ |
navāny ādāya bhāṇḍāni ālikaṃ carukaṃ tathā |
toyārthaṃ tu tato gacched gṛhītvā puruṣaṃ punaḥ ||
gṛhītvā laguḍaṃ yatnāt sarvaduṣṭanivāraṇam |
tato gṛhaṃ saṃpraviśet pretasyānnaṃ tu yat sadā ||
tasya prasṛtim ādāya kartavyā piṇḍakarmaṇi |
dviḥ prakṣālya tu taṃ samyak caruṃ saṃpādayet tataḥ ||
taṃ sabāṣpam athādāya darbheṣu viniveśayet |
dakṣiṇāgrāś ca darbhāḥ syuḥ sa ca vai dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ ||
dvāradeśe pradātavyo devatāyatane kvacit |
piṇḍam uddhṛtya tat sarvaṃ nāmnā gotreṇa cārpayet ||
vāgyataḥ prayataś caiva tiṣṭhet piṇḍasya saṃnidhau |
tato bāṣpe nivṛtte 'sya nadyāṃ ca prakṣipet tu tat ||
dine dine 'ñjaliṇ pūrṇān prakṣipet pretakāraṇāt |
tāvad vṛddhiś ca kartavyā yāvat piṇḍaḥ samāpyate ||
śātātapaḥ |
aśaucasyāpi ca hrāse piṇḍān dadyur daśaiva tu |
piṇḍam ekam atha śrāddhe samyag dadyād yathā vidhiḥ ||
pāraskaraḥ |
prathame divase deyās traya piṇḍāḥ samāhitaiḥ |
dvitīye caturo dadyād asthisaṃcayanaṃ tathā ||
trīṃs tu dadyāt tṛtīye 'hni vastrādi kṣālayet tathā |
ekoddiṣṭaṃ ca kartavyaṃ śrāddhakarma tataḥ param ||
dakṣaḥ |
prathame 'hani tat piṇḍaṃ dvitīye caturas tathā |
tṛtīye pañca vai dadyād eṣa piṇḍavidhiḥ smṛtaḥ || [889]
marīciḥ |
prathame 'hni tṛtīye ca saptame navame tathā |
jñātibhiḥ saha bhoktavyam etat preteṣu durlabham ||
matsyapurāṇe |
pretāya piṇḍadānaṃ tu dvādaśāhaṃ samācaret |
pātheyaṃ tasya tat proktaṃ yataḥ prītikaraṃ mahat ||
yasmāt pretapuraṃ preto dvādaśāhena nīyate |
gṛhaputrakalatraṃ ca dvādaśāhaṃ prapaśyati ||
ṛṣyaśṛṅgaḥ |
prathame 'hani yaḥ piṇḍas tena mūrdhā prajāyate |
cakṣuḥ śrotraṃ nāsikā ca dvitīye 'hani jāyate ||
bhujau vakṣas tathā grīvā tṛtīye 'hani jāyate |
nābhisthānaṃ gudaṃ liṅgaṃ caturthe 'hani jāyate ||
ūrū tu pañcame jñeyau ṣaṣṭhe marmāṇi nirdiśet |
saptame tu śirāḥ sarvā jāyante nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
aṣṭame tu tataḥ piṇḍe sarvalomāny anantaram |
navame vīryasaṃpattir daśame kṣutparikṣayaḥ ||
āśaucānte tataḥ samyak piṇḍadānaṃ samāpyate |
tataḥ śrāddhaṃ pradātavyaṃ sarvavarṇeṣv ayaṃ vidhiḥ ||
evaṃ ca sati saṃnipātāśaucena śuddhau saṃjātāyām api putrādinaikaikasminn eva dina ekaikaśaḥ piṇḍadānādikaṃ karma kartavyam iti gamyate | tasya punar aspṛśyatvam abhojyānnatvaṃ ca bhavaty eva nimittāntaratvāt tayoḥ | yan manuḥ |
sparśaḥ krameṇa varṇānāṃ tricatuḥpañcaṣaḍdinaiḥ |
bhojyānno daśabhir vipraḥ śeṣā dvitriṣaḍuttaraiḥ || iti |
evaṃ ca sati tadīyaṃ nityadānādi śuṣkaṃ na niṣidhyate, tasyāśauce nivṛttismaraṇāt | tathā ca viṣṇuḥ: "aśauce ca homadānapratigrahasvādhyāyā nivartante" iti | na ca tasyāśaucam asti, saṃnipātāśaucena nivāritatvād iti || 3.16 ||
kiṃ ca |
jalam ekāham ākāśe sthāpyaṃ kṣīraṃ ca mṛnmaye |
pretam uddiśya jalaṃ mṛnmaye pātre kṛtvā kṣīraṃ ca mṛnmayāntare kṛtvākāśe bhūmer anyatra śikyādau sthāpanīyam | ekāhaṃ prathame 'hanīty arthaḥ | [890] pāraskaraḥ: "pretātra snāhīti jalamantraḥ piba cedam iti kṣīramantraḥ" | matsyapurāṇam |
tato niveśyam ākāśe dvādaśāhaṃ payas tadā |
sarvadāhopaśāntyarthaṃ prathamaśramavāraṇam ||
vakṣyamāṇasyāśaucasya viṣayaviśeṣe 'pavādam āha |
vaitānopāsanāḥ kāryāḥ kriyāś ca śruticoditāḥ || 3.17 ||
vitānas tretā, tatsaṃbandhinyo vaitānyaḥ, tāś ca tā upāsanāś ca vaitānopāsanāḥ | puṃvadbhāvād vaitānaśabde strīpratyayalopaḥ | upāsanāś ca,
upāsya paścimāṃ saṃdhyāṃ hutvāgnīṃs tān upāsya ca |
iti smṛtivihitāḥ | atra cāgnyupāsanā puruṣārthatvena vidhīyate na homāṅgatvena, pramāṇābhāvāt | na ca ktvāpratyayo homāṅgatvena vidhīyate homāṅgatvena[ca?] pramāṇam iti vācyam | tathā sasi hutvā — upāsyety anvayaḥ syāt | na ca ktvāpratyayāntayor asty anvayaḥ | ākhyātaguṇatvenobhayoḥ samānatvāt | kim arthaṃ tarhīdaṃ vacanam | paurvāparyapratipattyartham iti brūmaḥ | kramavidhiparatve ca vākyasya niścito 'yam arthaḥ saṃpadyate — saṃdhyām upāsya juhuyāt, hutvopāsītetyādiḥ | na cedānīm aṅgāṅgibhāvena prāmāṇyaṃ ktvāśruteḥ śakyam | kramaparatvāvadhāraṇavirodhāt | na ca kramaparatve vākyasyopāsanāvidhiparatvam anupa[pa?]nnam iti vācyam | na hy upāsanāvidhir iha śrautaḥ, kiṃ tv ārthaḥ | na hy avihitāyām upāsanāyāṃ tadviṣayaḥ kramaḥ śakyo vidhātum | na cānyatas tasyā vidhir asti, ato 'traiva tadvidhiḥ kalpyate | tasyāś ca puruṣārthatve siddhe tannirvāhāya pāpakṣayo 'pūrvasiddhir vā phalaṃ kalpanīyam | upāsanā iti bahuvacanaṃ vyaktyabhiprāyam | kriyāś ca śruticoditāḥ, kriyāḥ karmāṇy agnihotrādīni, cakāro vaitānikatvaviśeṣaṇānukarṣaṇārthaḥ | tenātra vaitānya eva kriyā vivakṣitāḥ | tāsām eva viśeṣaṇārthaṃ śruticoditā ity uktam | saṃbhave vyabhicāre ca viśeṣaṇam arthavat | na coktānāṃ kriyāṇāṃ śruticoditatvaṃ vyabhicaratīti viśeṣaṇadoṣaparihārāya śrutipadārthaviśeṣaṇāya pratyakṣatvm adhyāhriyate | tenaitat siddham — yā vaitānāgnyupāsanās tā(yā)ś ca vaitānyaḥ pratyakṣaśrutivihitāḥ kriyāḥ [iti] | brahmayajñādyā āśauce heyā eva na hi tā vaitānyaḥ | tathā vaitānyo 'pi yā na pratyakṣaśrutivihitāḥ yathā —
[891]        eṣām asaṃbhave kuryād iṣṭiṃ vaiśvānarīṃ dvijaḥ |
ityādismṛtivihitāḥ |
atra kecid vyācakṣate — vitāne vede bhavā vaitānāḥ | tathā — upāsane gṛhye bhavā aupāsanāḥ | vaitānāś co(cau)pāsanāś ca vaitānopāsanāḥ kriyā iti | tad etad vyākhyānam anupapannam | vaitānyaupāsanya iti hi tadā śabdaḥ syān na punar vaitānopāsanā iti | pūrvavyākhyāyāṃ tu karmadhārayo 'yaṃ samāsaḥ | tatra puṃvadbhāvavidhānena vaitānopāsanā iti śabdasādhutāsiddhiḥ | tathopāsanāśabdo nāṇpratyayāntaḥ | yena striyāṃ ṅīvantaḥ syāt | yaś (yac) ca taiḥ śruticodanād iti pāṭhaṃ kṛtvā hetuparatvena vyākhyātaṃ tad api na yuktam | evaṃ hi te manyante, yasmāc chrutyā, "yāvajjīvam agnihotraṃ juhuyāt," "yāvajjīvaṃ darśapūrṇamāsābhyāṃ yajeta" ityādikayāvaśyakāryatayā vihitatvān na smṛtyā śakyam āśauce 'pi tannivartanaṃ vidhātum iti | tadanaikāntikam, śrutividhānād iti pātityena śrutivihitam apy āvaśyakam agnihotrādikaṃ smṛtyā pātityadaśāyāṃ nivartata eva, tasmād dhetutvena śrutivihitatvasya vyākhyānam ayuktam | vi(vai)tānaśabdaś ca vedavihitatvenādṛṣṭaprayogatvān na tatparatvena vyākhyeyaḥ | tasmāt pūrvaiva vyākhyā śreyasī | manuḥ |
na vardhayed aghāhāni pratyūhen nāgniṣu kriyāḥ |
na ca tat karma kurvāṇaḥ sanābhyo 'py aśucir bhavet ||
niṣkarmā sukham āsiṣya iti buddhyā nāśaucāhāni vardhayet | śāstroktāṃ dinasaṃkhyāṃ nātikrāmed ity arthaḥ | agniṣu śrauteṣu yāḥ kriyā agnihotrādyā niyatās tā na pratyūhen na tyajet | āśauce saty api kuryād ity arthaḥ | na cāśuceḥ kriyāsv anadhikārāt pratyūha eva yukta ity atrāha — na ca tat karmeti | saty apy āśauce nāsty adhikāraḥ, kiṃ tu karmaviśeṣe vacanasāmarthyād aśucitvam eva nāstīti mantavyam | atra kurvāṇa ity ātmanepadād adhikārī tat karma kurvāṇaḥ [892] sanābhyo 'pi jātamṛtayoḥ pratyāsanno 'pi nāśucir bhavati, kiṃ tu śucir eveti gamyate | tasmāt sanābhyānām ārtvijya bhavaty evāśaucam | vyāghrapāt |
smārtakarmaparityāgo rāhor anyatra sūtake |
śrautakarmaṇi tat kālaṃ snātaḥ śuddhim āpnuyāt ||
rāhoḥ sūtakād anyatra sūtaka āśauca ity arthaḥ | pāraskare tu viśeṣaḥ: "nityāni nivartante vaitānavarjaṃ śālāgnau caike | anya etāni kuryuḥ" iti | śālāgnir gṛhyāgnis tatsaṃbandhikarmāṇi yadi kriyeraṃs tadā sapiṇḍebhyo 'nye kuryuḥ | anyaiḥ kārayed ity arthaḥ | yāgaṃ dakṣiṇādānaṃ ca svayam eva kuryāt | tayoḥ svadravyatyāgātmakatvam(tvenā)nyaiḥ kartum aśakyatvāt | jābālaḥ |
janmahānyor vitānasya karmatyāgo na vidyate |
śālāgnau kevale homaḥ kārya evānyagotrajaiḥ ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
sūtake mṛtake caiva aśaktau śrāddhabhojane |
pravāsādinimitteṣu hāvayen na tu hāpayet ||
jātūkarṇyaḥ |
sūtake tu samutpanne smārtaṃ karma yathā bhavet |
piṇḍayajñaṃ caruṃ homam asagotreṇa kārayet ||
saṃvartaḥ |
homas tatra tu kartavyaḥ śuṣkānnena phalena vā |
pañcayajñavidhānaṃ ca na kuryān mṛtajanmanoḥ ||
śuṣkānnam āmānnaṃ vrīhiyavādi | vaiśvadevahomas tu na kārya iti sa evāha |
vipro daśāham āsīta vaiśvadevavivarjitaḥ |
yan manunoktam,
ubhayatra daśāhāni kulasyānnaṃ na bhujyate |
dānaṃ pratigraho homaḥ svādhyāyaś ca nivartate ||
iti, tat smārtahomaviṣayam | viṣṇupurāṇam |
sarvakālam upāsā tu saṃdhyāyāḥ pārthiveṣyate |
anyatra sūtakāśaucavibhramāturabhītitaḥ ||
paiṭhīnasiḥ: "sūtake sāvitryāñjaliṃ prakṣipya pradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā sūryaṃ dhyāyan namaskuryāt" | [893] pulastyaḥ |
saṃdhyām iṣṭiṃ caruṃ homaṃ yāvajjīvaṃ samācaret |
na tyajet sūtake cāpi tyajan gacched adho dvijaḥ ||
sūtake mṛtake caiva saṃdhyākarma na saṃtyajet |
manasoccārayen mantrān prāṇāyāmam ṛte dvijaḥ ||
evaṃ ca saty aśauce saṃdhyāniṣedhā mantroccāraṇaviśiṣṭasaṃdhyāgocarā ity anusaṃdheyam | añjaliprakṣepas tūccāryamāṇayā sāvityā kāryaḥ || 3.17 ||
evaṃ cāśaucāpagamahetubhūto nikhananādiḥ karmakalāpa uktaḥ, saṃprati tadapagamasamarthaṃ kālaviśeṣam āha |
trirātraṃ daśarātraṃ vā śāvam āśaucam iṣyate |
ūnadvivarṣa ubhayoḥ sūtakaṃ mātur eva hi || 3.18 ||
śāvaṃ śavanimittam aśucitvaṃ samānodakānāṃ trirātram, sapiṇḍānāṃ daśarātram, manvādibhir iṣyate | ūnadvivarṣe tu prete yad aśucitvaṃ tad ubhayor eva mātāpitroḥ | sūtakaṃ prasavanimittam āśaucaṃ mātur eva, sūtikāyā evety arthaḥ | trirātrāśaucaṃ samānodakānām ity āha manuḥ |
ahnā caikena rātryā ca trirārair eva ca tribhiḥ |
śavaspṛśo viśudhyanti tryahāt tūdakadāyinaḥ || iti |
udakadāyinaḥ samānodakāḥ | teṣāṃ ca lakṣaṇaṃ sa evāha |
sapiṇḍatā tu puruṣe saptame vinivartate |
samānodakabhāvastu tu janmnāmnor avedane || iti |
anena cārthād evaṃsaṃjñakā eṣām asmākaṃ ca saṃtānajanmeti yāvaj jñānam anuvartate tāvat teṣāṃ samānodakatvam iti | daśarātrāśaucaṃ sapiṇḍaviṣayam | tad āha manur eva |
daśāhaṃ śāvam āśaucaṃ sapiṇḍeṣu vidhīyate | iti |
tatrāpi pakṣāntartritayaṃ sa evāha |
āvāsaṃ cayanās asthnāṃ tryaham ekāham eva vā |
āvāsaṃ cayanād asthnām iti caturahopalakṣaṇam | tasya caturthe 'hanit vihitatvāt | tathā ca saṃvartaḥ |
caturthe 'hani kartavyaṃ mṛte saṃcayanaṃ dvijaiḥ | [894]
yady api "saṃcayanam ūrdhvaṃ daśamyāḥ kṛṣṇapakṣe" ity āśvalāyanena (ĀśG 4.5.1) kālāntare saṃcayanaṃ vidhīyate, tathāpi caturthe 'hanit tasya vidhir astīti caturaha eva saṃcayanena lakṣyate | daśarātrān nyūnasya kālasyātra vivakṣitatvā | ata eva dakṣaḥ,
sadyaḥ śaucaṃ tathaikāhas tryahaś caturahas tathā |
ity uktavān | evaṃ ca sati daśāhaś caturahas tryaha ekāha iti tatrāśauce catvāraḥ pakṣāḥ | teṣāṃ vyavasthā smṛtyantare vihitā | tatra parāśaraḥ |
ekāhāc chudyate vipro yo 'gnivedasamanvitaḥ |
tryahāt kevalavedas tu nirguṇo daśabhir dinaiḥ ||
dakṣas tu daśa pakṣān āha |
sadyaḥśaucam athaikāhas tryahaś caturahas tathā |
ṣaḍdaśadvādaśāhaś ca pakṣo māsas tathaiva ca ||
maraṇāntaṃ tathā cānyad daśa pakṣās tu sūtake |
upanyāsakrameṇaiva vakṣyāmy aham aśeṣataḥ ||
granthārthato vijānāti vedam aṅgaiḥ samanvitam |
sakalpaṃ sarahasyaṃ ca kriyāvāṃś ca na sūtakī ||
sadyaḥśucir ity arthaḥ | bṛhaspatiḥ |
svādhyāyaḥ kriyate yatra homaś cobhayakālikaḥ |
satataṃ vaiśvadevaś ca tatrāśaucaṃ na vidyate ||
parāśaraḥ |
svādhyāyaḥ kriyate yatra bhārataṃ yatra paṭhyate |
satataṃ vaiśvadevaś ca tatrāśaucaṃ na vidyate ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
ekāhāc chudhyate vipro yo 'gnivedasamanvitaḥ |
hīne hīnatare vāpi tryahaś caturahas tathā ||
agnivedasaṃyuktād dhīnaḥ kevalavedaḥ | yas tv adhītavedo 'pi vedaṃ na dhārayati, sa hīnataraḥ | yad vā śrautāgnirahito hīnaḥ | smārtāgnimān kevalavedādhyāyī hīnataraḥ, śrautasmārtāgnirahitaḥ saṃpūrṇavedādhyāyī ca | bṛhaspatiḥ |
trirātreṇaiv śudhyet tu vipro vedāgnisaṃyutaḥ |
pañcabhir vāgnihīnas tu daśāhād brāhmaṇadhruvaḥ ||
śiloñchāyācitair jīvan sadyaḥ śudhyed dvijottamaḥ |
gāyatrīmātrasāro 'pi tisraḥ saṃdhyā upāsti ca ||
[895]        na karoti śvavṛttiṃ ca ṣaḍahaḥ sūtakaṃ bhavet |
dakṣaḥ |
asnātvā cāpy ahutvā ca adatvāśnaṃs tu yo dvijaḥ |
evaṃvidhasya viprasya sarvadā sūtakaṃ bhavet ||
vyādhitasya kadaryasya ṛṇagrastasya sarvadā |
kriyāhīnasya mūrkhasya strījitasya viśeṣataḥ ||
vyasanāsaktacittasya parādhīnasya nityaśaḥ |
śraddhātyāgavihīnasya bhasmāntaṃ sūtakaṃ bhavet ||
na sūtakaṃ kadācit syād yāvajjīvaṃ tu sūtakam |
evaṃ guṇaviśeṣeṇa sūtakaṃ samudāhṛtam ||
brahmapurāṇe |
anyapūrvā yasya gehe bhāryā syāt tasya nityaśaḥ |
aśaucaṃ sarvakāryeṣu dehe bhavati sarvadā ||
dānaṃ pratigrahaḥ snānaṃ sarvaṃ tasya bhaved vṛthā ||
śātātapaḥ |
janmakarmaparibhraṣṭaḥ saṃdhyopāsanavarjitaḥ |
nāmadhārakavipras tu daśāhena viśudhyati ||
atra ca sūtakaśabdaḥ āśaucamātre vartate | āśaucasaṃkocāś ca dānapratigrahādiviṣayāyām ā[pa]ttau vidhīyante, nāviśeṣeṇa sarvakarmasu | yad āha gautamaḥ: "brāhmaṇasya ca svādhyāyānivṛttyartham" iti | ayam arthaḥ — bahutarasapiṇḍasya durmedhasaḥ saṃpūrṇāśauce kriyamāṇe svādhyāyo nivartate | svādhyāya(yā)nivṛttigrahaṇaṃ ca pradarśanārtham | tena kusūlakumbhadhānyādīnāṃ vṛttisaṃkocitānāṃ saṃpūrṇe 'śaucapakṣe pratigrahanivṛttyā jīvanam eva durlabhaṃ syāt | tena teṣāṃ yathāsaṃbhavaṃ sadyaḥśaucaikāhādividhānaṃ pratigrahaviṣayam eva | viṣayāntare tu tair api saṃpūrṇam evāśaucaṃ kāryam | ūnadvivarṣe prete tūbhayor eva mātāpitror aspṛśyatvalakṣaṇam āśaucam | anadhikāralakṣaṇaṃ tu sarveṣām eva | yad āha vasiṣṭhaḥ: "ūnadvivarṣe prete garbhapatane vā sapiṇḍānāṃ trirātram āśaucam" iti | yat tv atra tenaivoktam "sadyaḥ śaucam iti gautamaḥ" iti, tad aspṛśyatvāśaucābhiprāyam iti | sūtakaṃ mātur eva hīti dṛṣṭāntārtham | yathā sūtake mātur evāspṛśyatā tathonadivarṣamaraṇe pitror eveti | janmamaraṇayoḥ sapiṇḍasamānodakānāṃ karmaviśeṣānadhikāralakṣaṇam aspṛśyatvalakṣaṇam cāśucitvam asti || 3.18 ||
tatra janmany aspṛśyatvaviśeṣam āha | [896]
pitros tu sūtakaṃ mātus tadasṛgdarśanād dhruvam |
tadahar na praduṣyeta pūrveṣāṃ janmakāraṇāt || 3.19 ||
sūtakaṃ prasūtibhavam aspṛśyatvātmakaṃ aśucitvaṃ mātāpitror nānyeṣāṃ sapiṇḍānām | tatra yadi pitā sūtikāyāḥ saṃsparśalakṣaṇaṃ saṃsargaṃ tayā saha na bhajate, tadā mātur evāspṛśyatvaṃ rajodarśanahetukaṃ dhruvam avicalaṃ daśarātraṃ yāvad bhavati | yad āha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
nāśaucaṃ sūtake puṃsaḥ saṃsargaṃ cen na gacchati |
rajas tatrāśuci bhavet tac ca puṃsi na vidyate || iti |
yasminn ahani putrajanma tadahar na duṣyati, na tasminn ahani pitur aśucitvaṃ bhavatīty arthaḥ | atra "pūrveṣāṃ janmakāraṇāt" iti hetuḥ | pūrveṣāṃ pitrādipuruṣāṇāṃ tatra janma bhavatīti hetos tasminn ahany aśuddhir nāstīti | śaṅkhalikhitau |
pitā pitāmahaś caiva tathaiva prapitāmahaḥ |
bhūyas tatraiva jāyante tadahar vedayanti ca ||
tasmāt sa divasaḥ puṇyaḥ pitṝṇām abhivardhanaḥ |
manuḥ |
jāte kumāre tadahaḥ kāmaṃ kuryāt pratigraham |
hiraṇyadhānyagovāsastilānnaguḍasarpiṣām ||
śaṅkhalikhitau: "kumāraprasave nābhyām acchinnāyāṃ guḍatilahiraṇyavastraprāvaraṇagodhānyapratigraheṣv adoṣaḥ | tadahar ity eke" | vṛddhayājñavalkyaḥ |
kumārajanmadivase vipraiḥ kāryaḥ pratigrahaḥ |
hiraṇyabhūgavāśvājavāsaḥśayyāsanādiṣu ||
tatra sarvaṃ pratigrāhyaṃ kṛtānnaṃ tu na bhakṣayet |
bhakṣayitvā tu tan mohād dvijaś cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret ||
atra ca |
sarveṣāṃ śāvam āśaucaṃ mātāpitros tu sūtakam |
sūtakaṃ mātur eva syād upaspṛśya pitā śuciḥ ||
ityevamādīny aspṛśyatālakṣaṇāśaucaviṣayāṇi, na punar adhikāralakṣaṇāśaucaviṣayāṇi, tasya sarvasapiṇḍaviṣayatve,
daśāhaṃ śāvam āśaucaṃ sapiṇḍeṣu vidhīyate |
janane 'py evam eva syān nipuṇāṃ śuddhim icchatām ||
iti manunā vihitatvāt | anena ca vacanena śāvāśaucavat tatkarmāṇi janane 'py atidiśyante | tataś ca tadvad aśucitvaṃ tadvyudāsopāyaś ca labhyate | tatra [897] piṇḍodakadānasaṃcayanādi śucitvāpāyatayātidiṣṭaṃ sadevakāreṇa nivartyate | daśāhacaturahādikaṃ pakṣadaśakam ādiśyata eva | na ca tathā sati sūtikāyā apy ekāhādinā kalpena śuddhiḥ śiṣṭācāraviśuddhā prasajyata iti vācyam | anadhikāralakṣaṇasyāśaucasyāyam atideśo nāspṛśyatāyāḥ | ata evāha vṛddhapracetāḥ |
sūtikā sarvavarṇānāṃ daśāhena viśudhyati |
ṛto ca na pṛthakśaucaṃ sarvavarṇeṣv ayaṃ vidhiḥ ||
karmādhikāre (ro?) 'pi tasyā ekādaśāhādinā nāstīty āha paiṭhīnasiḥ: "sūtikāṃ putravatīṃ viṃśatirātreṇa karmāṇi kārayet, māsena strījananīm" iti | etac ca prakrāntasomayāgavyatiriktakarmaviṣayam | yataḥ somayāgaṃ praty āha kātyāyanaḥ: "patny udakyā dīkṣārūpāṇi vidhāya siktānvāsīnopaśravaṇāt tiṣṭhet saṃdhvelayor vedisamīpe | sū(su)tyāsu trirātrānte gomūtramiśreṇodakena snapayitvā paridhānādi karoti sāṃnipātikam | prasūtāyāś ca daśarātrād ūrdhvaṃ snānādi" | evaṃ tāvat sūtake 'pi daśāhasaṃbandhiny āśauce sāmānyataḥ prāpte tadapavādaviśeṣaṃ vyāsa āha |
sūtikāvāsanilayā janmadā nāma devatāḥ |
tāsāṃ yāganimittaṃ tu śuddhir janmani kīrtitā ||
prathame divase ṣaṣṭhe daśame caiva sarvadā |
triṣv eteṣu na kurvīta sūtakaṃ putrajanmani ||
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ |
rakṣaṇīyā tithiḥ ṣaṣṭhī niśā caiva viśeṣataḥ |
rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ kāryaṃ janmadānāṃ tathā baliḥ ||
puruṣāḥ śastrahatāś ca nṛtyagītaiś ca yoṣitaḥ |
rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ kuryur daśamyāṃ caiva sūtake ||
brahmapurāṇe |
kanyāś catasro rākādyā bālatakṣṇī ca pañcamī |
krīḍanārthā ca bālānāṃ ṣaṣṭhī ca śiśurakṣiṇī ||
khaḍge tu pūjanīyā sā vaiśyavrātyair dvijātibhiḥ |
rākānumatiḥ sinīvālī kuhūr iti catasraḥ kanyāḥ || 3.19 ||
[898]
āśaucasaṃnipātaṃ prary āha |
antarā janmamaraṇe śeṣāhobhir viśudhyati |
pūrvapravṛttāśaucakālasyāntarā madhye janane maraṇe vā jāte tannimittam āśaucaṃ pūrvapravṛttasyaiva daśarātrādeḥ śeṣāhobhir avaśiṣṭair ahorātraiḥ śudhyati, na punar madhyotpannaṃ jananaṃ maraṇaṃ vārabhya daśarātrāntaraṃ śuddhaye kāryam | atra ca yadi pūrvaṃ janmato daśāhamadhye maraṇaṃ tadā maraṇād ārabhya daśāhaḥ kāryaḥ | yad āha aṅgirāḥ |
sūtake mṛtakaṃ cet syān mṛtake tv atha sūtakam |
tatrādhikṛtya mṛtakaṃ śaucaṃ kuryān na sūtakam ||
ṣaṭtriṃśanmatāt |
śāvāśauce samutpanne sūtakaṃ tu yadā bhavet |
śāvena śudhyate sūtir na sūtiḥ śāvaśodhinī ||
samasaṃkhyākadināpanodyāśaucavixayam etat | tad āha baudhāyanaḥ: "jananamaraṇayoḥ saṃnipāte samāno daśarātro 'tho yadi daśarātrāḥ saṃnipā(pa)teyur ādyaṃ daśarātram āśaucam ā navamād divasāt" | atra daśarātragrahaṇam āśaucasya tulyakālyopalakṣaṇārtham | tathā ca śaṅkhaḥ |
samānāśaucasaṃpāte prathamena samāpayet |
asamānaṃ dvitīyena dharmarājavaco yathā ||
yamaḥ |
aghavṛddhimad āśaucaṃ paścimena samāpayet |
yathā trirātre prakrānte daśāhaṃ praviśed yati ||
aśaucaṃ punar āgacchet tatsamāptyā viśudhyati ||
agham āśaucam, tasya pūrvotpannāśaucakālād adhikakālatvaṃ vṛddhimattvam | hārītaḥ |
śāvāntaḥ śāvam āśauce(caṃ) pūrvāśaucena śudhyati |
laghunā laghu śudhyet tu |
laghunaikāhādinaikāhādikaṃ śudhyen na tu pakṣiṇyādikam | prajāpatiḥ |
sūtake tu samutpanne putrajanma yadā bhavet |
kartus tātkālikī śuddhiḥ pūrvāśaucena śudhyati || [899]
kartur jātakarmādikartuḥ | pūrvāśaucena pūrvāśaucakālenety arthaḥ | tathā |
aghānāṃ yaugapadye tu jñeyā śuddhir garīyasī |
maraṇotpattiyoge tu garīyo maraṇaṃ bhavet ||
tataś cāyam arthaḥ siddhaḥ — śāvāśauce vartamāne yadi tena tulyakālāpanodya(dyaṃ) śāvaṃ sūtakaṃ vāśaucaṃ jāyate, tadā pūrveṇaiva śuddhiḥ | yadā tu svalpakālāpanodye śāve vartamāne bahukālāpanodyaṃ sūtakaṃ mṛtakaṃ vāpadyate, yadi vā sūtakaṃ(kena?) vartamānena mṛtakaṃ tuylyakālam evāpadyeta, tadottareṇaiva śuddhiḥ | gautamaḥ: "tac ced antaḥ punar āpatec cheṣeṇa śudhyeran" | atra ca tac ced iti samānakālam āśaucaṃ parāmṛśyate, na punar janane jananaṃ maraṇe maraṇam iti vivakṣitam | śāstrāntaravirodhaprasaṅgāt | yad api,
janane jananaṃ cet syān maraṇe maraṇaṃ tathā |
iti vacanaṃ tad apy anuvādakaṃ na niyāmakam, virodhaprasaṅgād eva | atra ca viśeṣas tenaivoktaḥ |
rātriśeṣe sati dvābhyāṃ prabhāte tisṛbhis tathā | iti |
ayam arthaḥ — rātrir eva śeṣo yasyāśaucasya tasmin sati yady āśaucāntaram āpadyeta, tadā dvābhyām ahorātrābhyāṃ tasya śuddhiḥ | prabhāte tasyāṃ rātrau vyuṣṭāyāṃ purā sūryodayād āśaucasaṃnipāte tribhir ahorātraiḥ śuddhir iti | aṅgirāḥ |
sūtakaṃ yadi tulyaṃ syān mṛtakena kathaṃcana |
aspṛśyaṃ tad bhaved gotraṃ sarvam eva sabāndhavam ||
atra sūtakamṛtakagrahaṇam aśaucasaṃnipātopalakṣaṇārtham | [ā navamād divasāt pūrvāśaucenaiva śuddhir uktā | aviaśiṣṭadaśamāhorātrasya rātrer vibhāge,
rātriśeṣe sati dvābhyāṃ prabhāte tisṛbhis tathā |
ity anena kṛte 'vaśiṣṭaṃ dinarūpaṃ bhāgadvayaṃ tatra kiṃ kāryam ity āha] brahmapurāṇam |
ādyaṃ bhāgadvayaṃ yāvat sūtakasya tu sūtake |
dvitīye patite tv ādyāt sūtakāc chuddhir iṣyate ||
ata ūrdhvaṃ dvitīyāt tu sūtakāntāc chuciḥ smṛtā |
evam eva vicāryaṃ syān mṛtake mṛtakāntare ||
[900]        mṛtakasyāntare yatra sūtakaṃ pratipadyate |
sūtakasyāntare vātha mṛtakaṃ pratipadyate ||
mṛtakānte bhavet tatra śuddhir varṇeṣu sarvaśaḥ |
ayam arthaḥ — āśaucāntimāhorātrasya yadādyaṃ bhāgadvayaṃ dinarūpaṃ tatra pūrvāśaucamadhye 'śaucāntarapāte pūrveṇaiva śuddhiḥ | avaśiṣṭe rātrirūpe bhāgadvaye 'yaṃ vibhāgaḥ |
rātriśeṣe sati dvābhyāṃ prabhāte tisṛbhis tathā | iti |
lekhakaiḥ punar yad etal likhitam — āśaucakālasya tribhāgakaraṇenātra bhāgadvayam | ato 'vihitād bhāgadvayāc chuddhiḥ | etac ca nirguṇaviṣayam | saguṇasya tu punar gautamīyam | evameveti tribhāgakaraṇādinā | [eta ayuktam | etenaiva granthakāreṇa pūrvam ā navamād divasād iti svayam uktatvāt | tathā rātriśeṣe sati dvābhyām ity atra rātrir eva śeṣo yatra kāla ity uktatvāc ca |] śaṅkhaḥ |
mātary ādau parītāyām aśuddhau mriyate pitā |
pituḥ śeṣeṇa śudhyet tu mātuḥ kāryā tu pakṣiṇī ||
asyārthaḥ — mātā ced ādau mriyate tadaśauca eva paścāt pitā mriyate, tadā pitṛsaṃbandhyāśaucasya śeṣeṇaiva putrādiḥ kartā śudhyet | pitā ced ādau mriyate paścād aśauca eva mātā mriyate tadādhikā pakṣiṇy eva kāryā | pakṣiṇī dinadvayam | "kartus tātkālikī śuddhiḥ" itivacanāt prāptāyāṃ śuddhau tad eva (tadaiva), tadvacanaparihārārtham idaṃ vacanam |
garbhasrāve māsatulyā niśāḥ śuddhes tu kāraṇam || 3.20 ||
garbhādhānaprabhṛti yāvat saṃkhyāke māsi garbhaḥ sravati cyavate tāvanty ahorātrāṇi vyatītāni santi strīśuddheḥ kāraṇaṃ bhavati | garbhasrāvo nāmāprāptaprasavakālasya garbhasya nirgamaḥ | atra gautamaḥ: "garbhamāsasamā rātrīḥ sraṃsave garbhasya tryahaṃ vā" | vyavasthitavikalpo 'yam | tataś ca māsatrayaṃ yāvan tryaham, tataḥ paraṃ māsatrayaṃ sāmasamā trātrayo jñeyā iti | [901] ādipurāṇe |
ṣaṇmāsābhyantare yāvad garbhasrāvo bhaved yadi |
tadā māsasamais tāsāṃ divasaiḥ śuddhir iṣyate ||
ata ūrdhvaṃ svajātyuktam āśaucaṃ tāsu vidyate |
sadyaḥ śaucaṃ sapiṇḍānāṃ garbhasya patane sati ||
sadyaḥ śaucavidhānam aspṛśyatālakṣaṇāśaucaviṣayam | anadhikāralakṣaṇaṃ tv āśaucaṃ trirātraṃ yāvat sapiṇḍānāṃ bhavaty eva | yad āha vasiṣṭhaḥ: "ūnadvivarṣe prete garbhapatane vā sapiṇḍānāṃ trirātram āśaucam" | marīciḥ |
garbhasrutyā yathāmāsam ācaren nūtanas tryahaḥ |
rājany eva catūrātraṃ vaiśye pañcāham eva ca ||
aṣṭāhena ca śūdrasya śuddhir eṣā prakīrtitā |
srāve mātus trirātraṃ syāt sapiṇḍāśaucavarjanam ||
pāte mātur yathāmāsaṃ pitrādīnāṃ dinatrayam ||
atra viśeṣam āha vṛddhavasiṣṭhaḥ: "garbhasrāve māsatulyā rātrayaḥ strīṇāṃ snānamātram eva puruṣasyeṣat kaṭhinagarbhasrāve tu trirāram" | srāvapātavivekaś coktaḥ smṛtyantare |
ā caturthāḍ bhavet srāvaḥ pātaḥ pañcaṣaṣṭhayoḥ |
ata ūrdhvaṃ prasūtiḥ syād daśāhaṃ sūtakaṃ bhavet || iti || 3.20 ||
viprādihatasyāśaucasaṃkocam āha |
vipragonṛpahatānām anvakṣaṃ cātmaghātinām |
viprādibhir hatānām avihitātmatyāgakāriṇāṃ coparame 'nvakṣaṃ pratyakṣaṃ dṛśyamāne taccharīre tatsapiṇḍānām āśaucam ity arthaḥ | gautamaḥ: "gobrāhmaṇahatānām anvakṣaṃ rājakrodhāc ca yuddhe prāyānāśakaśastrāgniviṣodakodbandhanaprapātanair icchatāṃ ca" iti | rājñā yadi krodham antareṇa pramādād dhataḥ, yadi vā kruddhena yuddhe hatas tadā tatsapiṇḍānāṃ tatsamavāye nāśaucam | rājakrodhāc ca yuddha iti viśeṣanopādānāt | prāyādibhiś ca pramādān mṛteṣu pūrṇam evāśaucam | icchatām ity upādānāt | [902] śunaḥpucchaḥ |
agnyādinā vipannasya cāturvarṇyasya rogiṇaḥ |
sapiṇḍās tasya śudhyanti trirātreṇa na saṃśayaḥ ||
aṅgirāḥ |
vṛddhaḥ śaucasmṛter luptaḥ pratyākhyātabhiṣakkriyaḥ |
ātmānaṃ ghātayed yas tu bhṛgvagnyanaśanāmbubhiḥ ||
tasya trirātram āśaucaṃ dvitīye tv asthisaṃcayaḥ |
tṛtīye tūdakaṃ datvā caturthe śrāddham ācaret ||
vṛddhaḥ saptatim atikrāntaḥ | tathā ca smṛtiḥ |
ā ṣoḍaśād bhaved bālo yāvat kṣīrapravartakaḥ |
madhyamaḥ saptatiṃ yāvat tatparaṃ vṛddha ucyate ||
tathā |
vyāpādayed athātmānaṃ svayaṃ yo 'gnyudakādibhiḥ |
vihitaṃ tasya nāśaucaṃ nāgnir nāpy udakakriyā ||
atha kaścit pramādena mriyetāgnyudakādibhiḥ |
tasyāśaucaṃ vidhātavyaṃ kāryā syād udakakriyā ||
brahmapurāṇe |
pramādād api niḥśaṅkas tv akasmād vidhicoditaḥ |
śṛṅgisaṃṣṭrinakhivyālaviṣavidyujjalāgnibhiḥ ||
caṇḍālair atha vā caurair nihato yatra kutracit |
tasya dāhādikaṃ kāryaṃ yasmān na patitas tu saḥ ||
śṛṅgidaṃṣṭrinikhivyālaviṣavahnistrigojalaiḥ |
ādarāt parihartavyaḥ kurvan krīḍāṃ mṛtas tu yaḥ ||
nāgānāṃ vipriyaṃ kurvan hataś cāpy atha vidyutā |
nigṛhītas tu yo rājñā cauradoṣeṇa kutracit ||
paradārān gataś caiva dveṣāt tatpatibhir hataḥ |
asamānais tu saṃkīrṇaiś caṇḍālādyaiś ca vigraham ||
kṛtvā tair hi hatās tāṃs tu caṇḍālādīn samāsthitāḥ |
caurāgniviṣadāś caiva pāṣāṇḍāḥ krūrabuddhayaḥ ||
krodhāt prāyaṃ viṣaṃ vahniṃ śastram udbandhanaṃ jalam |
girivṛkṣaprapātaṃ vā ye kurvanti narādhamāḥ ||
kuśilpajīvino ye 'pi sūnālaṃkārakāriṇaḥ |
mukhebhagās tu ye kecit klībaprāyā napuṃsakāḥ ||
[903]        brahmadaṇḍahatā ye ca ye can syur brāhmaṇair hatāḥ |
mahāpātakino ye ca patitās te prakīrtitāḥ ||
mahābhāratam |
pāpāt pāpasamācārād vihīnāc ca narādhipa |
pāpa eva vadhaḥ prokto narakāyeti niścayaḥ ||
tathā |
viṣam udbandhanaṃ dāho dasyuhastāt tathā vadhaḥ |
daṃṣṭribhyaś ca paśubhyaś ca prākṛto vadha ucyate ||
brahmapurāṇam |
patitānāṃ na dāhaḥ syān nāntyeṣṭir nāsthisaṃcayaḥ |
na cāsrupātaḥ piṇḍo vā kāryaṃ śrāddhādi na kvacit ||
etāni patitānāṃ ca yaḥ karoti vimohitaḥ |
taptakṛcchradvayenaiva tasya śuddhir na cānyathā ||
patitasya tu kāruṇyād yas tṛptiṃ kartum icchati |
sa tu dāsīṃ samāhūya sarvagāṃ dattavetanām ||
aśuddhaghaṭahastāṃ tu tathāvṛttāṃ bravīty api |
he dāsi gaccha mūlyena tīrthād ādāya satvaram ||
toyapūrṇaṃ ghaṭaṃ cemaṃ satilaṃ dakṣiṇāmukhī |
upariṣṭāt tu vāmena caraṇena tataḥ kṣipa ||
kīrtayet pātitīṃ saṃjñāṃ taṃ pibeti diśen muhuḥ |
niśamya tasya vākyaṃ syāl labdhamūlyā karoti tat ||
evaṃ kṛte bhavet tṛptiḥ patitānāṃ ca nānyathā |
kriyate patitānāṃ tu gate saṃvatsare kvacit ||
deśadharmapramāṇatvād gayākūpe svabandhubhiḥ |
mārtaṇḍapādamūle vā śrāddhaṃ hariharau smaran ||
mārtaṇḍapadamūlaṃ kāśmīreṣu prasiddham | skandapurāṇam |
adagdhānāṃ apiṇḍānāṃ patitāvratināṃ tathā |
śrāddhaṃ piṇḍapradānaṃ ca naiva kuryād iti śrutiḥ ||
teṣāṃ malimluce māsi sūryakṣetre prayatnataḥ |
sitāsitānāṃ pakṣāṇāṃ yas tithiḥ śrāddhakarmaṇi ||
tasmiṃs tasmin ravikṣetre māse caiva trayodaśe |
śrāddhaṃ piṇḍapradānaṃ ca kuryān mārtaṇḍapādayoḥ || [904]
sumantuḥ: "bhṛgvagnijalasaṅgrāmadeśāntarasaṃsthasaṃnyāsyanaśanimahādhvanikānām udakakriyā kāryā sadyaḥ śaucaṃ bhavati" | kaśyapaḥ: "anaśanagatānām aśanihatānām agnijalapraveśitānāṃ bhṛgusaṅgrāmakāntāragatānāṃ garbhāṇāṃ jātadantānāṃ trirātreṇa sudhyati" | maraṇa iti śeṣaḥ ||
proṣite kālaśeṣaḥ syād aśeṣe tryaham eva ca || 3.21 ||
sarveṣāṃ vatsare pūrṇe prete datvodakaṃ śuciḥ |
proṣite deśāntarasthe sapiṇḍe mṛte daśarātrād āśaucakāle cānatikrānte śrute, daśarātreḥ śeṣaṃ yāvad āśaucaṃ bhavati | aśeṣe 'tikrānte daśarātrādau yadi tanmaraṇe śrutam, tadā trirātram āśaucaṃ sarveṣāṃ sapiṇḍānām | yadi vatsare pūrṇe tacchrutaṃ tadā pretāyodakaṃ datvā śucir bhavati | gautamas tu daśarātrād ūrdhvaṃ deśāntarasthamaraṇe pakṣiṇīṃ rātrim āśaucam āha: "śrutvā cordhvaṃ daśamyāḥ pakṣiṇīm" iti | vasiṣṭhaḥ punar ahotrātram evāśaucam āha: "deśāntarasthe preta ūrdhvaṃ daśāhāc chrutvaikarātram āśaucam" iti | manus tu sadhyaḥśaucam āha |
nirdaśaṃ jñātimaraṇaṃ śrutvā putrasya janma ca |
savāsā jalam āplutya śuddho bhavati mānavaḥ || iti |
nirdaśaṃ nirgatadaśāham | eṣāṃ virodhaparihārārthaṃ vyavasthām āha devalaḥ |
ā tripakṣāt trirātraṃ syāt ṣaṇmāsāt pakṣiṇī tataḥ |
param ekāham ā varṣād ūrdhvaṃ snāto viśudhyati || iti |
ubhayatodivasā rātriḥ pakṣiṇī | anenaivābhiprāyeṇa viṣṇur apy āha |
arvāk tripakṣāt triniśaṃ ṣaṇmāsāt tu divāniśam |
ahaḥ saṃvatsarād arvāg deśāntarmṛteṣv api || iti |
divāśabdenātrāhardvayam ucyate pakṣiṇītvasiddhaye | viṣṇuḥ: "śrutvā deśāntarasthe janmamaraṇe āśaucaśeṣeṇa śudhyet, vyatīte tv āśauce saṃvatsarasyāntas tv ekarātreṇa, ataḥ paraṃ snānena" | deśāntaralakṣaṇam āha vṛddhamanuḥ |
[905]        mahānadyantaraṃ yatra girir vā vyavadhāyakaḥ |
vāco yatra vibhidyante tad deśāntaram ucyate ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
deśāntaraṃ vadanty eke ṣaṣṭiyojanam āyatam |
catvāriṃśad vadanty anye triṃśad anye tathaiva ca ||
smṛtyantaram |
tiryagyavodarāṇy aṣṭau yavārdhaṃ vrīhayas trayaḥ |
pramāṇam aṅgulasyoktaṃ vitastir dvādaśāṅgulā ||
vitaster dviguṇāratnis tataḥ kiṣkus tato dhanuḥ |
dhanuḥsahasre dve krośaś catuṣkrośaṃ tu yojanam ||
kiṣkur aratner dviguṇa eva | tataḥ kiṣkuto dhanur api dviguṇam eva, nāmakośo 'py evam | kiṣku(dhanu)rhastacatuṣṭayam | mātāpitṛviṣayaṃ paiṭhīnasir āha |
pitarau tu mṛtau śrutvā dūrastho 'pi hi putrakaḥ |
jñānāt tu dinam ārabhya daśāhaṃ sūtakaṃ caret ||
sūtakam āśaucam ity arthaḥ | daśarātrād ūrdhvam iti śeṣaḥ | tasya madhye tu śeṣeṇaiva | dakṣaḥ |
mahāgurunipāteṣu tv ārdravastropavītinā |
atīte 'bde 'pi kartavyaṃ pretakāryaṃ yathāvidhi ||
tathā |
pitṛpatnyām atītāyāṃ mātṛvarjaṃ dvijottamaḥ |
daśarātre vyatīte 'pi trirātram aśucir bhavet ||
prasavaviṣaye devala āha |
nāśuddhiḥ prasavasyāsti vyatīteṣu dineṣv api |
manuḥ |
bāle deśāntarasthe ca pṛthakpiṇḍe ca saṃsthite |
savāsā jalam āplutya sadya eva viśudhyati ||
deśāntarasthatvena bālāsapiṇḍau viśi(śe)ṣyete || 3.21 ||
kṣatriyādyaśaucakālam āha |
kṣatrasya dvādaśāhāni viśaḥ pañcadaśaiva ca || 3.22 ||
triṃśad dināni śūdrasya tadardhaṃ nyāyavartinaḥ |
kṣatriyādīnāṃ yathākramaṃ pañcadaś triṃśad ahorātrā jananamaraṇayor āśaucakālaḥ | nyāyavartinaḥ punaḥ śūdrasya pañcadaśāhaḥ | nyāyavartitvaṃ vihitakāritvam | evaṃ ca sati "trirātraṃ daśarātraṃ vā" ity anena pāriśeṣyād brāhmaṇasyāśaucakāla ukta iti mantavyam | yat tu parāśareṇoktam, [906]
kṣatriyas tu daśāhena svadharmanirataḥ śuciḥ |
tathaiva dvādaśāhena vaiśyaḥ śuddhim avāpnuyāt ||
tad vedāgniyuktakṣatriyādiviṣayam | yat tu vasiṣṭhenoktam: "pañcadaśarātreṇa rājanyo viṃśatirātreṇa vaiśyaḥ" taj jātimātropajīvirājanyādiviṣayam | yat punar āṅgirasam,
sarveṣām eva varṇānāṃ sūtake mṛtake tathā |
daśāhāc chuddhir atha vā śātātapavaco yathā ||
iti vākyam, tat sarvavarṇānāṃ brāhmaṇena pitrā sahaikatra vasatām āśaucavidhāyakam | tathā a manuḥ |
sarve tūttamavarṇānām āśaucaṃ kuryur āditaḥ |
tadvarṇavidhidṛṣṭena svaṃ tv āśaucaṃ svayoniṣu ||
ayam arthaḥ — sarve kṣatriyādayo hīnavarṇā brāhmaṇādyuttamavarṇajā uttamavarṇasaṃbandhini janane maraṇe ca sati, tadvarṇavidhidṛṣṭena daśarātrādināśaucaṃ kuryuḥ, svayoniṣu tu jāteṣu mṛteṣu ca svam āśaucaṃ kuryur iti | tathā ca āpastambaḥ |
kṣatraviṭśūdrajātīnāṃ yadā mṛtakasūtake |
teṣāṃ tu paitṛkaṃ śaucaṃ vibhaktānāṃ tu mātṛkam ||
yat tu manunoktam,
śūdrāṇāṃ māsikaṃ kāryaṃ vapanaṃ nyāyavartinām |
vaiśyavac chaucakalpaś ca dvijocchiṣṭaṃ ca bhojanam ||
iti, tatra pañcadaśarātrātmaka eva vaiśyasyāśaucakalpo 'tidiśyate svaśāstre tasyaivoktatvāt | nyāyavṛttiś ca śūdrasya dvijātiśuśrūṣaṇapañcayajñanirvapaṇabhṛtyabharaṇabhāryāratatvādiḥ | kṣatriyādau ca kṛtopanayane saṃsthite dvādaśāhādi vidhīyate | śūdrasya copanayanasthāne vastradvayagrahaṇam | tadā[ha] sa eva |
ā mauñjibandhanād vipraḥ kṣatriyaś ca dhanurgrahāt |
ā pratodagrahād vaiśyaḥ śūdro vastradvayagrahāt || [907]
śaṅkhaḥ |
anūḍhabhāryaḥ śūdras tu ṣoḍaśād vatsarāt param |
mṛtyuṃ samadhigacchet tu māsāt tasyāpi bāndhavāḥ ||
śudhyantīti śeṣaḥ | brahmapurāṇe |
bāndhaveṣu ca viprasya kṣatraviṭśūdrajātiṣu |
mṛteṣu vātha jāteṣu daśāhāc chuddhir iṣyate ||
deśadharmapramāṇatvāt ṣaḍrātre kṣatriyeṣv atha |
trirātram api vaiśyeṣu śūdreṣv ekāham eva ca ||
kṣatriyasyātha vaiśyas tu vaiśyasya vṛṣalas tu vā |
mriyate jāyate bandhus tatrāśaucaṃ svakaṃ bhavet ||
ekarātraṃ trirātraṃ ca ṣaḍrātraṃ māsam eva ca |
śūdre sapiṇḍe varṇānām āśaucaṃ kramaśaḥ smṛtam ||
trirātram atha ṣaḍrātraṃ pakṣas tv ekas tathaiva ca |
vaiśye sapiṇḍe varṇānām āśaucaṃ kramaśaḥ smṛtam ||
sapiṇḍe kṣatriye śuddhiḥ ṣaḍrātrād brāhmaṇasya tu |
varṇānāṃ pariśeṣāṇāṃ dvādaśāhena nirdiśet ||
sapiṇḍe brāhmaṇe varṇāḥ pūrva evāviśeṣataḥ |
daśarātreṇa śudhyanti ity āha bhagavān yamaḥ || 3.22 ||
ahas tv adattakanyāsu bāleṣu ca viśodhanam || 3.23 ||
gurvantevāsyanūcānamātulaśrotriyeṣu ca |
varāyādattāḥ kanyā yadi mriyeraṃs tadā tatsapiṇḍā ahorātreṇa śudhyanti, tāsāṃ ca tripuruṣaṃ sāpiṇḍyam, "aprattānāṃ strīṇāṃ tripuruṣaṃ sāpiṇḍyam" iti vasiṣṭhavacanāt | atra cāprattopādānasāmarthyāt prattānāṃ bhartṛpakṣeṇa saha saptapuruṣaṃ sāpiṇḍyaṃ gamyate | yat tu manunoktam,
strīṇām asaṃskṛtānāṃ tu tyahāc chudhyanti bāndhavāḥ |
yathoktenaiva kalpena śudhyanti ca sanābhayaḥ || iti | [908]
asyārthaḥ — asaṃskṛtānām anūḍhānāṃ strīṇāṃ bāndhavā bhartṛpakṣyās tryaheṇa śudhyanti, sanābhayaḥ pitṛpakṣyās tu pūrvoktena kalpena śudhyantīti | atra ca sanābhiśabdena pitṛpakṣyāṇāṃ pṛthagupādānād bāndhavaśabdo bhartṛpakṣo vartate | na ca sanābhiśabdena pitṛpakṣyāṇāṃ pṛthagupādānād bāndhavaśabdo bhartṛpakṣo vartate | na ca pradānāt prāg bhartṛpakṣaḥ saṃbhavati, ataḥ pitrādinā vāgdattānāṃ strīṇāṃ vivāhakarmaṇā cāsaṃskṛtānāṃ maraṇe vidhir ayam | sanābhayaḥ pitṛsapiṇḍāḥ kumārasya caulād ūrdhvaṃ prāg upanayanān maraṇe yas trirātmakaḥ kalpa uktas tena śudhyanti |
vaivāhiko vidhiḥ strīṇām aupanāyanikaḥ smṛtaḥ |
iti manuvacanadarśanād vivāha upanayanasthānam | tataś cādattāsu kanyāsv ekāhaḥ prattāsu ca tryaha iti vyavasthā | tathā ca vṛddhamanuḥ |
aprauḍhāyāṃ tu kanyāyāṃ sadyaḥ śaucaṃ vidhīyate |
ahas tv adattakanyāsu tathā dattāsu ca tryaham ||
marīciḥ: "kanyānāṃ prāk cūḍākaraṇāt sadyaḥ śaucam | prāg dānād ekāhaḥ | dattānāṃ prāk pariṇayanāt tryaham" | pulastyaḥ |
sadyas tv aprauḍhabālāyāṃ prauḍhāyāṃ vāsarāc chuciḥ |
pradattāyāṃ trirātreṇa dattāyāṃ pakṣiṇī bhavet ||
pradattāyāṃ prakrāntadānāyāṃ vāgdattāyām iti yāvat | viṣṇuḥ: "saṃskṛtāsu strīṣu nāśaucaṃ pitṛpakṣe, tatprasavamaraṇe cet pitṛgṛhe syātāṃ tadaikarātraṃ trirātraṃ vā" | prasava ekarātram anyatra trirātram | paiṭhinasiḥ |
pitṛgotraṃ kumārīṇām ūḍhānāṃ bhartṛgotratā |
jaladāne pramītānām udvāhād ubhayatra tu ||
yamaḥ |
pitror uparame strīṇām aśaucaṃ tu kathaṃ bhavet |
trirātreṇa viśuddhiḥ syād ity āha bhagavān yamaḥ ||
brahmapurāṇam |
dattā nārī putur gehe sūyate mriyate 'tha vā |
tadbandhuvargas tv ekena janakaḥ śudhyate tribhiḥ ||
ājanmanas tu cūḍāntaṃ yatra kanyā vipadyate |
sadyaḥ śaucaṃ bhavet tatra sarvavarṇeṣu nityaśaḥ ||
tato vāgdānaparyantaṃ yāvad ekāham eva tu |
ataḥ paraṃ pravṛddhānāṃ jñeyaṃ tūbhayatas tryaham || [909]
pitur varasya ca tato dattānāṃ bhartur eva hi |
svajātyuktam aśaucaṃ syāt sūtake mṛtake 'pi ca ||
pitrā dattā tu yā kanyā svātantryād anyam āśritā |
yaṃ yaṃ śritavatī bhūyas tasyāśaucaṃ bhavet tryaham ||
mṛtāyāṃ vā prasūtāyāṃ nānyeṣām iti niścayaḥ |
pade tu saptame yatra bālāt kācid vṛtā bhavet ||
svāmigotraṃ bhavet tasyās tac ca bhūyo viśiṣyate |
paitṛkaṃ tu prasūtāyāṃ tataḥ paurvikabhartṛkam ||
kāmād akṣatayoniś ced anyaṃ gatvā vyavasthitā |
tasyānyasya sagotrā syād yaṃ tv āśritavatī svayam ||
evaṃ kanyāviṣayam uktvā bālaviṣayam āha — bāleṣu ca viśodhanam iti | bālāḥ kṛtanāmakaraṇā ajātadantās teṣu ca mṛteṣv ahar eva viśodhanam | tathā gurvādiṣv asapiṇḍeṣu saṃsthiteṣv ahorātreṇaiva viśuddhiaḥ |
prasaṅgād anyad api kiṃcil likhyate, tatra manuḥ |
jīvaṃjāto yadi mriyān mṛto vā sūyate yadi |
sūtakaṃ tatra mātuḥ syāt pitrādīnāṃ trirātrakam ||
saptamāsaprabhṛtiprasava etat, jīvaṃjāta iti liṅgāt | pitrādīnāṃ puṇavatāṃ trirātram | anyeṣāṃ daśāhaḥ | yat tu bṛhadviṣuṇoktam: "jātamṛte mṛtajāte vā kulasya sadyaḥ śaucam" iti, tat samānodakaviṣayaṃ na sapiṇḍaviṣayam | ata eva manuḥ |
bāle deśāntarasthe ca pṛthakpiṇḍe ca saṃsthite |
savāsā jalam āplutya sadya eva viśudhyati ||
pṛthagpiṇḍo 'sapiṇḍaḥ | bālo 'trākṛtanāmā | atraiva viṣaye kātyāyano 'py āha |
anivṛtte daśāhe ca pañcatvaṃ yadi gacchati |
sadya eva viśuddhiḥ syān na pretaṃ na ca sūtakam || [910]
atha vā viṣṇukātyāyanavākye aspṛśyatvaniṣedhapare | ata eva vṛddhamanuḥ |
daśāhābhyantare bālaḥ pramādān mriyate yadi |
śāvāśaucaṃ na kartavyaṃ sūtiśaucena śudhyati ||
hārītaḥ: "jātamṛte mṛtajāte vā sapiṇḍānāṃ daśāhaḥ" | yat tu bṛhanmanuvacanam,
jīvaṃjāto yadi mṛto mṛtaḥ sūyeta eva vā |
sūtakaṃ sakalaṃ mātuḥ pitrādīnāṃ trirātakam ||
iti, tad guṇavattaragotraviṣayam agnihotrānuṣṭhānaviṣayaṃ samānodakaviṣayaṃ vā | muhūrtajīvito jīvaṃjāta ity arthaḥ | śaṅkho 'py agnihotrānuṣṭhānābhiprāyeṇāha |
anivṛtte daśāhe tu pañcatvaṃ yati gacchati |
mātuḥ prāsavikaṃ śaucam upaspṛśya pitā śuciḥ || iti |
kāśyapaḥ: "bālānām ajā(ta)dantānāṃ trirātreṇa śuddhiḥ" | paiṭhīnasiḥ: "bāle cājātadante trirātraṃ śāvam āśaucam" | śaṅkhaḥ |
ajātadante bāle tu sadyaḥ śaucaṃ vidhīyate |
ahorātrāt tathā śuddhir bāle tu kṛtacūḍake ||
tathaivānupanīte tu tryahāc chudhyanti bāndavāḥ ||
brahmaṇaviṣayam etat | mātāpitṛvyatiriktajñātiviṣayaṃ vā | yaḥ punar ajātadante kāśyapīyas trirātraviṣayaḥ sa kṣatriyādiviṣayaḥ pitṛviṣayo vā | atra cājātadantaśabdo 'kṛtanāmaviṣayaḥ | yad āha śaṅkhaḥ: "prāṅ nāmakaraṇāt sadyaḥ śuddhiḥ" iti, yat tv aṅgirasokatam,
anujātasya tāvat syād āśaucaṃ saṃsthitasya tu |
yāvat snānaṃ na kurvanti sacailaṃ bāndhavā bahiḥ ||
iti, tan mātāpitṛvyatiriktaguṇavatsapiṇḍaviṣayam | āśaucād ūrdhvaṃ nāmakaraṇāt prāṅmṛte caitat | [911] yat tu yamenoktam,
ajātadante tanaye śiśau garbhacyute tathā |
sapiṇḍānāṃ tu sarveṣām ahorātreṇa śaucakam ||
iti, tan nāmakaraṇe sati draṣṭavyam | garbhacyute yad ekāhavidhānaṃ tad guṇātiśayavatsu sapiṇḍeṣu grāhyam | itareṣu tryahaḥ | jātadantasya tv agnisaṃskāre cūḍākaraṇābhāve 'pi trirātrāśaucam | yad āha aṅgirāḥ,
yadāpy akṛtacūḍo vai jātadantas tu saṃsthitaḥ |
dāhayitvā tathāpy enam āśaucaṃ tryaham ācaret || iti |
yat punar ūnadvivarṣādhikāre manunoktam,
nāsya kāryo 'gnisaṃskāro nāpi kāryodakakriyā |
araṇye kāṣṭhavat tyaktvā kṣipeta tryaham eva vā ||
tat prathame 'bde yaḥ kṛtacūḍas tadviṣayam | na caitan niyatam | yataḥ ṣaṭtriṃśanmate prathamābde kṛtacūḍasyāgnisaṃskāra uktaḥ |
yadāpy ajātadantaḥ syāt kṛtacūḍas tu saṃsthitaḥ |
tathāpi dāyayed enaṃ tryaham āśaucam ācaret ||
tṛtīyavarṣe tu kṛtacūḍasya saṃsthitasyāgnisaṃskāraḥ | tata udakaṃ ca | nikhananodakaniṣedhayor ūnadvivarṣaviṣayatvāt | tataś caitad vyavasthitam | āśaucamadhye bāloparame sadyaḥ śaucam | nāmna ūrdhvaṃ prāg dantajananāt pitros trirātram | sapiṇḍāntarāṇām ekāhaḥ | prathame 'bde 'kṛtacūḍasyāpi vipattāv agnisaṃskārapakṣe jñātīnām api trirātram | pakṣāntare tv ekāhaḥ | kṛtacūḍasya pakṣadvaye 'pi tryaha eva | tṛtīye 'bde kṛtacūḍasya saṃsthitāv āvaśyakāgnyudakadānaṃ(ne) vā tryaha iti | sarvaṃ caitad brāhmaṇaviṣayam | kṣatriyādiviṣaye tv aṅgirā āha |
vipre nyūne tribhir varṣair mṛte śuddhis tu naiśikī |
tryaheṇa kṣatriye śuddhis tribhir vaiśye mṛte 'pi ca || iti |
akṛtacūḍābhiprāyaṃ caitat | tathā |
nirvṛttacūḍake vipre trirātrāc chuddhir iṣyate |
nirvṛtte kṣatriye ṣaḍbhir vaiśye navabhir iṣyate ||
śūdre trivarṣān nyūne tu mṛtaḥ śuddhis tu pañcabhiḥ |
ata ūrdhvaṃ mṛte śūdre dvādaśāho vidhīyate ||
[912]        ṣaḍvarāṇtam atīto yaḥ śūdras tu mriyate yadi |
māsikaṃ tu bhavec chaucam ityāṅgirasabhāṣitam || iti |
ṛṣyaśṛṅgaḥ |
yatra trirātraṃ viprāṇām āśaucaṃ saṃprapadyate |
tatra śūdre dvādaśāhaṃ ṣaṇnava kṣatravaiśyayoḥ ||
hārītaḥ |
ā mauñjibandhanād vipraḥ kṣatriyaś ca dhanurgrahāt |
ā pratodagrahād vaiśyaḥ śūdro vastradvayagrahāt ||
mṛtaḥ paripūrṇāśaucanimittaṃ bhavatīti śeṣaḥ | mauñjībandhanādeḥ prāk tu,
aviśeṣeṇa sarveṣāṃ varṇānām anupūrvaśaḥ |
trirātrāt tu bhavec chuddhiḥ kanyāsv ahnā vidhīyate ||
pāraskaraḥ: "ūnadvivārṣike prete mātāpitros aśaucaṃ netareṣām" | aspṛśyatvābhiprāyam etat | iti prāsaṅgikam |
gurvantevāsyanūcānamātulaśrotriyeṣu ca |
gurvādiṣv asapiṇḍeṣu saṃsthiteṣv ahorātreṇaiva śuddhiḥ | gurur atropacāriko na mukhyaḥ | tasya sapiṇḍatvāt | yad vā dattāpaviddhādīn prati nivṛtte sāpiṇḍye mukhyasyāpi guror upādānam aviruddham | antevāsī śiṣyaḥ | anūcāna upādhyāyaḥ | mātulaḥ prasiddhaḥ | yady api chandodhyāyimātre śrotriyaśabdaḥ prasiddhas tathāpi sabrahmacāriviśiṣṭa evaitasminn ayaṃ vartate | tathā ca manuḥ |
sabrahmacāriṇy ekāham atīte kṣapaṇaṃ smṛtam | iti |
ekācāryaḥ sahādhyāyī sabrahmacārī | manuḥ |
guroḥ pretasya śiṣyas tu pitṛmedhaṃ samācaran |
pretāhāraiḥ samaṃ tatra daśarātreṇa śudhyati ||
ācāryo 'tra guruḥ | pitṛmedho 'ntyakarma | yatu tu tenaivoktam,
trirātram āhur āśaucam ācārye saṃsthite sati |
tasya putre ca patnyāṃ ca divārātram iti sthitiḥ ||
iti, tat pitṛmedhācaraṇābhāve sati draṣṭavyam | tathā |
śrotriya tūpasaṃpanne trirātram aśucir bhavet |
mātule pakṣiṇīṃ rātriṃ śiṣyartvigbāndhaveṣu ca ||
upasaṃpannatvaṃ mātulādīnām api viśeṣaṇam | tena śrotriyaviṣayasya trirātrasya mātulādiviṣayāyāś ca pakṣiṇyā yājñavalkīyenaikāhenāvirodhaḥ | upasaṃpanno [913] guṇavān | mānave ca, śrotriyaḥ samānagrāmo vivakṣitaḥ | itaratrāsamānagrāmaḥ | yac ca,
aśrotriye tv ahaḥ kṛtsnam anūcāne tathā gurau |
iti, samānagrāmāśrotriyaviṣayam | yac ca,
asapiṇḍaṃ dvijaṃ pretaṃ vipro nirhṛtya bandhuvat |
viśudhyati trirātreṇa mātur āptāṃś ca bāndhavān ||
iti manuvākyam, tad api mātur āptānāṃ bāndhavānāṃ mātulādīnāṃ nirharaṇakartṛviṣayam ity aviruddham | bṛhaspatiḥ |
tryahaṃ mātāmahācāryaśrotriyeṣv aśucir bhavet |
pracetāḥ |
mṛte cartviji yājye ca trirātreṇa viśudhyati |
kulartvigviṣayam etat |
saṃsthite pakṣiṇīṃ rātriṃ dauhitre bhaginīsute |
saṃskṛte tu trirātraṃ syād iti dharmo vyavasthitaḥ ||
saṃskṛta upanītaḥ |
pitror uparame strīṇām ūḍhānāṃ tu kathaṃ bhavet |
trirātreṇaiva śuddhiḥ syāḍ ity āha bhagavān yamaḥ ||
śvaśurayor bhaginyāṃ ca mātulānyāṃ ca mātule |
pitroḥ svasari tadvac ca pakṣiṇīṃ kṣapeyen niśām ||
pitror mātāpitror yā svasā tasyāṃ saṃsthitāyām ity arthaḥ | uśanā |
mātule śvaśure mitre gurau gurvaṅganāsu ca |
āśaucaṃ pakṣiṇīṃ rātriṃ mṛtā mātāmahī yadi ||
gauṇo 'tra guruḥ | gautamaḥ: "pakṣiṇīm asapiṇḍe yonisaṃbaddhe sahādhyāyini" | yonisaṃbaddho mātāpitṛsaṃbaddhaḥ | sa ced asapiṇḍas tadā pakṣiṇīṃ kuryāt | viṣṇuḥ: "asapiṇḍe svaveśmani mṛta ekarātram" | yad aṅgirasoktam,
gṛhe yasya mṛtaḥ kaścid asapiṇḍaḥ kathaṃcana |
tasyāpy aśaucaṃ vijñeyaṃ trirātraṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
iti, tan mṛtasya guṇavattve snehādyupādhinā vā gṛhamaraṇe | dharmopādhinā tv ekāhaḥ | [914] vṛddhayājñavalkyaḥ |
bhaginyāḥ saṃskṛtāyās tu bhrātary api ca saṃskṛte |
mitre jāmātari prete dauhitre bhaginīsute ||
śālake tatsute caiva sadyaḥ snānena śudhyati ||
saṃskṛtā kṛtavivāhā | bhrātuḥ saṃskāra upanayanam | tasminn uparate bhaginyāḥ saṃskṛtāyāḥ sadyaḥ śuddhiḥ | dauhitrabhaginīsutayoḥ sadyaḥśuddhividhānam āvaśyakapañcapajñādikarmānadhikārāpādakāśaucanivṛttyabhiprāyam | māṃsastrīsaṃgamakṣitiśayanamalasnānotsavādivarjaṃ(janaṃ) tu pakṣiṇīpartyantaṃ vacanāntarād avaseyam | yad vānupanītadauhitrabhāgineyaviṣayam etat | tathā |
grāmamadhyagato yāvac chavas tiṣṭhati kasyacit |
grāmasya tāvad āśaucaṃ nirgate śucitām iyāt ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
daśāhena sapiṇḍās tu śudhyanti pretasūtake |
trirātreṇa sakulyās tu snātvā śudhyanti gotrajāḥ ||
sakulyāḥ samānodakabhājaḥ | jābāliḥ |
tryahaṃ samānodakānāṃ gotrajānām ahaḥ smṛtam |
mātṛbandhau gurau mitre maṇḍalādhipatau tathā || 3.23 ||
anauraseṣu putreṣu bhāryāsv anyagatāsu ca || 3.24 ||
aurasavyatiriktakṣetrajādiputreṣu mṛteṣu jāteṣu pariṇītāḥ striyo bhāryās tāsu puruṣāntarāśritāsu ca mṛtāsv ahorātram aśaucam | cakāreṇātra pūrvoktam ahorātram anukṛṣyate | prajāpatiḥ |
anyāśriteṣu dāreṣu parapatnīsuteṣu ca |
gotriṇaḥ snānaśuddhāḥ syus trirāreṇaiva tatpitā ||
saṃnidhau trirātram asannidhāv ekarātraṃ kalpyam | viṣṇuḥ |
anauraseṣu putreṣu jāteṣu ca mṛteṣu ca |
parapūrvāsu bhāryāsu prasūtāsu mṛtāsu ca ||
trirātram atrānuvartate | marīciḥ |
sūtake mṛtake caiva trirātraṃ parapūrvayoḥ |
ekāhas tu sapiṇḍānāṃ trirātraṃ yat tu vai pituḥ ||
paraḥ pūrvaḥ patir yasyā bhāryāyā yasya ca putrasya krītādeḥ paraḥ pitā pūrvas tayoḥ parapūrvayor ity arthaḥ | tayos tu guṇavattve saṃnidhau ca trirātram | anyatraikāhaḥ | nāśaucam ity anuvṛttau śaṅkhalikhitau: [915] "anyapūrvāsu bhāryāsu kṛtakeṣu suteṣu vā tathā nānadhyāyo nodakakriyā" | brahmapurāṇe |
aurasaṃ varjayitvā tu samavarṇeṣu sarvadā |
kṣetrādiṣu putreṣu jāteṣu ca mṛteṣu ca ||
śaucaṃ ca trirātraṃ syāt samānām iti niścayaḥ ||
tathā |
ādāv anyasya dattāyāṃ kutracit putrayor dvayoḥ |
putur yatra trirātraṃ syād ekaṃ tatra sapiṇḍinām ||
ekā mātā dvayor yatra pitarau yatra kutracit |
tayoḥ syāt sūtakād aikyaṃ mṛtakād vā parasparam ||
"tatsamaḥ putrikāsutaḥ" ity aurasān nānyaḥ putrikāsuta iti nātra tasya pṛthag upādānam | hārītaḥ |
parapūrvāsu bhāryāsu putreṣu kṛtakeṣu ca |
mātāmahe trirātraṃ syād ekāhas tv asapiṇḍataḥ || 3.24 ||
nivāsarājani tathā tad ahaḥ śuddhikāraṇam |
yatra viṣaye nivasati sa nivāsaḥ, tasya rājani svāmini mṛte tadviṣayanivāsināṃ yasminn anany asau mṛtas tad evāhaḥ śuddhikāraṇam | atra rātrāv api cen mṛtas tadā saiva rātrir viśuddhikāraṇam | ahaḥśabdo grahopalakṣaṇam | yad āha manuḥ |
prete rājani sajyotir yasya syād viṣaye sthitiḥ | iti |
jyotiṣā saureṇa nākṣatreṇa vā saha vartate yad āśaucaṃ tat sajyotiḥ ||
sapiṇḍajananādāv api kvacid viṣaya āśaucāpavādam āha |
mahīpatīnāṃ nāśaucaṃ hatānāṃ vidyutā tathā || 2.25 ||
gobrāhmaṇārthe saṃgrāme yasya cecchanti pārthivāḥ |
mahyāḥ kṣiter ye maṇḍalātmikāyāḥ karotpattihetavo bhāgās tadviṣayo 'tra mahīśabdaḥ | na punaḥ sakalabhūgolaviṣayaḥ | na hi tasya svāmī kaścit saṃbhavati | na hi girisāgaravananadīnadādisthānāni kasyacid bhavanti, yāvān khalu bhūpasya gamanāgamanādimārgādiṣu bhogas tāvāṃl lokasyāpīti | mahīpatayo 'tra janapaśvarāḥ | [916] teṣāṃ sapiṇḍajananādau nāśaucam | atha vāśaucapratiṣedho janapadapālanopayogidānādiviṣayaḥ | na punaḥ sarvatra |yad āha viṣnuḥ: "na rājñāṃ rājakarmaṇi, na vratināṃ vrate, na satriṇāṃ satre, na kārūṇāṃ kārukarmaṇi" iti | ye ca vidyutā hatā ye ca gobrāhmaṇaparitrāṇaprayojane saṅgrāme hatās teṣāṃ sapiṇḍā yasya ca purohitādeḥ prajāpālanasiddhyarthaṃ śaucaṃ pārthivā icchanti, teṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ nāśaucam | etac copalakṣaṇārtham | ata evāha pracetāḥ |
kāravaḥ śilpino vaidyā dāsīdāsās tathaiva ca |
rājāno rājabhṛtyāś ca sadyaḥśaucāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ||
etac ca yad ātyayikaṃ vaidyādikāryaṃ tatraiva teṣāṃ śaucavidhānam | śātātapaḥ |
mūlyakarmakarāḥ śūdrā dāsīdāsās tathaiva ca |
snāne śarīrasaṃskāre gṛhakarmaṇy adūṣitāḥ ||
āpadviṣayam etat | smṛtyantaram |
sadyaḥspṛśo garbhadāso bhaktadāsas tryahāc chuciḥ |
tathā |
cikitsako yat kurute tad anyena na śakyate |
tasmāc cikitsakaḥ sparśe śuddho bhavati nityaśaḥ ||
devalaḥ |
kṣatriyasyābhiṣiktasya nivṛttasya ca liṅginaḥ |
prādhānyāt tyāgataś caiva nāśuddhāḥ śucayaḥ smṛtāḥ ||
abhiṣiktasya kṣatriyasya tathā nivṛttasya tyaktasaṅgasya liṅgino yates tatsaṃbandhino nāśuddhāḥ kiṃtu śucaya eva, kṣatriyasya prādhānyān maraṇādi sapiṇḍānām āśauce kāraṇaṃ na bhavati | saṃnyāsinaś ca jñātityāgāt | sadyaḥśaucam ity anuvṛttau manuḥ |
ḍimbāhavahatānāṃ ca vidyutā pārthivena ca |
gobrāhmaṇasya caivārthe yasya cecchati pārthivaḥ ||
aśastrakalaho ḍimbāhavaḥ | tathā |
udyatair āhave śastraiḥ kṣatradharmahatasya ca |
sadyaḥ saṃtiṣṭhate yajñas tathā śaucam iti sthitiḥ || [917]
yamaḥ |
ḍimbāhavahatānāṃ ca tathaiva prāṇasatriṇām |
nadīśvāpadadaṃṣṭribhyaḥ sadyaḥśaucaṃ vidhīyate ||
prāṇasatriṇaḥ prāṇadāyinaḥ | nadyādibhyo mṛtānāṃ ca ye sapiṇḍās teṣāṃ sadyaḥśaucam | bṛhaspatiḥ |
ḍimbāhave vidyutā ca rājñā goviprapālane |
sadyaḥśaucaṃ hatasyāhus tryahaṃ cānye maharṣayaḥ ||
saguṇāguṇakṛtātra vyavasthā, saṃnidhānāsaṃnidhānakṛtā vā | parāśaraḥ |
brāhmaṇārthe vipannānāṃ bandhigrāhahateṣu ca |
āhaveṣu vipannānām ekarātram aśaucakam ||
sumantuḥ: "bhṛgvagnijalasaṅgrāmadeśāntarasaṃsthasaṃyāsyanaśanāśanimahādhvanikānām udakakriyā kāryā sadyaḥśaucaṃ ca bhavati" | apratiṣiddhabhṛgvagnyādiviṣayam etat | kaśyapaḥ: "anaśanahatānām aśanihatānām agnijalapraviṣṭānāṃ bhṛgusaṅgrāmadeśāntaragatānāṃ garbhāṇāṃ jātadantānāṃ trirātreṇa śudhyati" | saguṇanirguṇāpekṣayā sadyaḥśaucatrirātrayor vyavasthā || 2.25 ||
anugamanāśaucam āha |
brāhmaṇenānugantavyo na śūdro na dvijaḥ kvacit || 3.26 ||
anugamyaāmbhasi snātvā vahnispṛg ghṛtabhuk śuciḥ |
brāhmaṇena śūdrao dvijaś ca sapiṇḍavyatirikto nānugantavyaḥ | sapiṇḍānugamanasya "anugantavya itaro jñātibhir mṛtaḥ" it vihitatvāt | yady anugacchet tadā nadyādisthe 'mbhasi snātvā vahniṃ spṛṣṭvā ghṛtaṃ prāśya śudhyati | etac ca sajātīyānugamanaviṣayaṃ śuddhividhānam | asajātīyānugamane tu prāyaścittam adhikam | tatra śūdrānugamane parāśaraḥ āha |
pretībhūtaṃ tu yaḥ śūdraṃ brāhmaṇo jñānadurbalaḥ |
nīyamānam anupreyāt sa trirātreṇa śudhyati ||
trirātre tu tataḥ pūrṇe nadīṃ gatvā samudragām |
prāṇāyāmaśataṃ kṛtvā ghṛtaṃ prāśya śucir bhavet ||
[918]        vinivṛttā yadā śūdrā udakāntād avasthitāḥ |
dvijais tadānugantavyā eṣa dharmaḥ sanātanaḥ ||
yat tu vāsiṣṭham: "mānuṣāsthi snigdhaṃ spṛṣṭvā trirātram āśaucam asnigdhe tv ahorātraṃ śavānugamane caivam" iti prāyaścittavidhānam, na tad dhīnavarṇadvijātyanugamanaviṣayam | mānavaṃ ca,
anugamyecchayā pretaṃ jñātim ajñātim eva vā |
snātvā sacailaṃ spṛṣṭvāgniṃ ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati ||
imaṃ pretam anugacchāmīti kāmanayānugacchataḥ śuddhihetur ayam, na tu yathākathaṃcit paścād gacchataḥ | baudhāyanaḥ: "pāraśavopasparśane 'nabhisaṃdhipūrve sacailo 'paḥ spṛṣṭvā śuddho bhavati, abhisaṃdhipūrvakaṃ tu trirātram" | śūdrāyāṃ brāhmaṇāc jātaḥ pāraśavaḥ, tacchavasparśana etat | gautamaḥ: "pretopasparśane daśarātram āśaucam abhisaṃdhāya cet | uktaṃ vaiśyaśūdrayor ārtavīr vā, pūrvayoś tryahaṃ vā" | asyārthaḥ — yadi mūlyam abhisaṃdhāyāpekṣya nirharaṇadāhātmakaṃ brāhmaṇaḥ pretopasparśanaṃ karoti, tadā pañcadaśatriṃśadahorātrātmakam | yad vā — ṛtusaṃkhyākā rātrīḥ, pañca ṣaḍ vā, ṛtavo bhavanti | tatra vaiśye pañca rātrīr āśaucam, śūdre ṣaṭ | etac ca guṇavata āpadi nirharaṇādi kurvato draṣṭavyam | pūrvayoś ca varṇayor ṛtusaṃkhyākā rātrīs tryahaṃ vā | tatra brāhmaṇe pañca kṣatriye ṣaṭ | ubhayatra vā tisraḥ | guṇavattāratamyāpekṣo vikalpaḥ | tathā — avaraś cet pūrvavarṇam upaspṛśet pūrvo vāvaram, tatra śāvoktam āśaucam | śavasya yo varṇas tadīyam āśaucaṃ daśarātradvādaśarātrādikaṃ spṛśato 'pi jānīyād ity arthaḥ || 3.26 ||
uktāśaucāpavādaṃ keṣāṃcid āha |
ṛtvijāṃ dīkṣitānāṃ ca yajñiyaṃ karma kurvatām || 3.27 ||
satrivratibrahmacāridātṛbrahmavidāṃ tathā |
dāne vivāhe yajñe ca saṅgrāme deśaviplave || 3.28 ||
āpady api hi kaṣṭāyāṃ sadyaḥ śaucaṃ vidhīyate |
[919]
ṛtvig ukto manunā |
agnyādheyaṃ pākayajñān agniṣṭomādikān makhān |
yaḥ karoti vṛto yasya sa tasyartvig ihocyate || iti |
kṛtadīkṣaṇīyo 'vabhṛthaṃ yāvad dīkṣitas teṣām ṛtvijāṃ ca yajñasaṃbandhīni karmāṇi pravartayatāṃ sadyaḥśaucaṃ vidhīyate manvādibhiḥ | tathā satriṇāṃ gavāmayanādyadhikāriṇāṃ ye yajamānās ta ṛtvija iti vacanabalād ārtvijeṣu kāryeṣu yajamāneṣu sadyaḥ śaucaṃ vihitam | anena punar ārtvijapadārthaviṣayaṃ satriṇāṃ vidhīyate | "kriyāś ca śruticoditāḥ" ity anena pratyakṣaśrutivihitāsu kriyāsv āśaucābhāva uktaḥ | atra tu ṛtvigdīkṣitasattriṇāṃ svakāryeṣu pratyakṣāpratyakṣaśrutivihiteṣu sadyaḥśaucaṃ vidhīyata iti vivekaḥ | sattrigrahaṇena vā satatānnadānapravṛttasya grahaṇam | tathā vratināṃ vrate sadyaḥśaucam | sadyaḥśaucaṃ vidhīyata iti pratyekam abhisaṃbadhyate | vrataṃ snātakavratam | prāyaścittādi vā | brahmacāry upakurvāṇako naiṣṭhikaś ca | dātā dānaśīlaḥ | brahmavid yatiḥ | eteṣāṃ sadyaḥśaucam | dānaśīlavyatiriktasyāpi dāne vartamāne vivāhe ca | tathā kṣatriyāder āśauce vartamāna ekādaśāhikavṛṣotsargayajñe saṅgrāme prastute rājñaḥ saṃnahanaprayoge deśasya ca viplave rājadaivikopaplave tatparihārakeṣu grahayajñādiṣu tathāpadi kaṣṭāyāṃ nairdhanyādau svasya poṣyavargasya vātyantikaduḥkhahetoḥ sadya eva tu śuddhiḥ | atra parāśaraḥ |
rājñāṃ tu sūtakaṃ nāsti vratināṃ na tu satriṇām |
dīkṣitānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ yasya cecchanti brāhmaṇāḥ ||
dīkṣiteṣv abhiyukteṣu vratatīrthapareṣu ca |
tapodānaprasakteṣu nāśaucaṃ mṛtasūtake ||
smṛtyantaram |
nityam annapradasyāpi kṛcchracāndrāyaṇādiṣu |
nivṛtte kṛcchrahomādau brāhmaṇādiṣu bhojane ||
sadyaḥśaucam iti śeṣaḥ | chandogapariśiṣṭam | kātyāyanaḥ |
na dīkṣaṇāt paraṃ yajñe na kṛcchrādi tapas caran |
pitary api mṛte naiṣāṃ doṣo bhavati karhicit ||
āśaucaṃ karmaṇo 'nte syāt tryahaṃ vā brahmacāriṇām ||
brahmapurāṇe |
