gṛhītamadhuparkasya yajanārthe tv athartvijaḥ |
paścād aśaucaṃ patitaṃ na bhaved iti niścayaḥ ||
[920]        tadvad gṛhītadīkṣasya traividhyasya mahāmakhe |
snānaṃ tv avabhṛthe yāvat tāvat tasya na vidyate ||
yater vedāntaniṣṭhasya praśāntasya na kutracit |
nivṛtte kṛcchrahomādau brāhmaṇādiṣu bhojane ||
gṛhītaniyamasyāpi na syād anyasya kasyacit |
nimantriteṣu vipreṣu prārabdhe śrāddhakarmaṇi ||
nimantraṇādi(mantritasya) viprasya svādhyāyādiviratasya ca |
dehe pitṛṣu tiṣṭhatsu nāśaucaṃ vidyate kvacit ||
naiṣṭhikasyātha vānyasya bhikṣārthaṃ prasthitasya ca |
vānaprasthasya dātṛtve sādhikārasya sarvadā ||
pratigrahādhikārāc ca nivṛttasya na vidyate |
gomaṅgalādau vaiśyānāṃ rakṣākālātyayād api ||
api dātṛgrahītroś ca sūtake mṛtake tathā |
avijñāte na doṣaḥ syāc chrāddhādiṣu kadācana ||
vijñāte bhoktur eva syāt prāyaścittādikaṃ kramāt |
bhojanārdhe tu saṃbhukte viprair dātur vipadyate ||
yadi kaścit tadocchiṣṭaśeṣaṃ bhuktvā samāhitaḥ |
ācamya parakīyeṇa jalena śucayo dvijāḥ ||
kratuḥ |
pūrvasaṃkalpitaṃ dravyaṃ dīyamānaṃ na duṣyati |
brahmapurāṇāt |
vivāhayajñayor madhye sūtake sati cāntarā |
śeṣam annaṃ parair deyaṃ dātā bhoktṝṃś ca na spṛśet ||
jābālaḥ |
brahmacāriṇi bhūpe ca yatau śilpini dīkṣite |
yajñe vivāhe satre ca sūtakaṃ na kadācana ||
viṣṇuḥ: "na devapratiṣṭhotsavavivāheṣu pūrvasaṃkalpiteṣu na deśavibhrame nāpady api ca kaṣṭāyām āśaucam" iti | pūrvasaṃkalpaś ca niyataḥ | yat smṛtyantaram |
dinadaśakaṃ dinasaptakam atha vā dinapañcakaṃ vadanty anye |
tryaham api kecit tajjñāḥ saṃkalpārthe vadanti kālavidaḥ || iti | [921]
patiṭhīnasiḥ |
vivāhadurgayajñeṣu yātrāyāṃ tīrthakarmaṇi |
na tatra sūtakaṃ tadvat karma yajñādi kārayet ||
durgo durbhikṣaḥ || 3.17, 28 ||
prāsaṅgikaṃ śuddhyantaram āha |
udakyāśaucibhiḥ snāyāt saṃspṛṣṭas tair upaspṛśet || 3.29 ||
abliṅgāni japec caiva gāyatrīṃ manasā sakṛt |
udakyā rajasvalā | āśaucāḥ (cinaḥ) sūtakamṛtakābhyām aspṛśyāḥ | etaiḥ saṃspṛṣṭaḥ snāyāc chuddhyartham | tair udakyādispṛṣṭaiḥ spṛṣṭas tūpaspṛśet, ācāmet | udakyādispṛṣṭas tatspṛṣṭaś ca snātvābliṅgāny abdaivatāni japet, gāyatrīṃ ca sakṛn manasā tair iti bahuvacanaṃ vyaktyapekṣam | saṃspṛṣṭa ity ekavacanam avivakṣitārtham | tasya vā(cā)vivakṣitārthatve snāyād ity asyāvivakṣitārthatvam | manuḥ |
divākīrtim udakyāṃ ca patitān sūtikāṃ tathā |
śavaṃ tatspṛṣṭinaṃ caiva spṛṣṭvā snānena śudhyati ||
yady api cātra tatspṛṣṭiśabdena śavaspṛṣṭy evocyate saṃnidhānāt, tathāpi divākīrtyādīnām aśucitvena śavatulyatvāt tatspṛṣṭinam api spṛśataḥ snānam eva syāt ekavargatvāt | tathā ca gautamaḥ: "patitacaṇḍālasūtikodakyāśavaspṛṣṭitatspṛṣṭyupasparśena sacailam udakasparśanāc chudyet" | ata eva smṛtyantaram: "śavacaṇḍālapatitasūtikodakyāspṛṣṭitatspṛṣṭisparśe snānam" iti | tatspṛṣṭisparśe sati pūrve sacailaṃ snānam | asati pūrve tu snānamātram yad āha saṃvartaḥ |
tatspṛṣṭinaṃ spṛśed yas tu snānaṃ tasya vidhīyate |
ūrdhvam ācamanaṃ proktaṃ dravyāṇāṃ prokṣaṇaṃ tathā ||
prasaṅgād anyāny api snānanimittāni pradarśyante, tatra viṣṇuḥ |
udakyāśavacaṇḍālān asnehāsthi narodbhavam |
spṛṣṭvā sacailaḥ snāyīta kravyāde śirasi sthite ||
tathā |
anucchiṣṭas tu saṃspṛṣṭo yenācamya śucir bhavet |
tenaivocchiṣṭasaṃspṛṣṭas trirātreṇa śucir bhavet ||
[922]        anucchiṣṭena saṃspṛṣṭe yena snānaṃ vidhīyate |
tenaivocchiṣṭasaṃspṛṣṭaḥ prājāpatyena śudhyati ||
parāśaraḥ |
duḥsvapne maithune vānte nir(ri)kte kṣurakarmaṇi |
citiyūpe śmaśānāgnisparśane snānam ācaret ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
maithune kaṭadhūme ca sadyaḥ snānaṃ vidhīyate |
anṛtau tu yadā gacchec chaucaṃ mūtrapurīṣavat ||
smṛtyantaram |
aṣṭamyāṃ ca caturdaśyāṃ divā parvaṇi maithunam |
kṛtvā sacailaṃ snātvā tu vāruṇībhiś ca mārjayet ||
manuḥ |
vānto viriktaḥ snātvā ca ghṛtaprāśanam ācaret |
ācāmed eva bhuktvānnaṃ snānaṃ maithuninaḥ smṛtam ||
ṛtāv iti viśeṣaḥ | yamaḥ |
ajīrṇe 'bhyudite vānte tathātyastam ite ravau |
duḥsvapne durjanasparśe snānamātraṃ vidhīyate ||
sūryodayātyastamayavyāpinī nidrābhyudayāstamayau | cyavanaḥ: "śvānaṃ śvapākaṃ pretadhūmaṃ devadravyopajīvinaṃ grāmayājakaṃ somavikrayiṇaṃ yūpaṃ citiṃ citikāṣṭhaṃ madyaṃ madyabhāṇḍaṃ sasnehaṃ mānuṣāsthi śavaspṛśaṃ rajasvalāṃ mahāpātakinaṃ śavaṃ spṛṣṭvā sacailam ambho 'vagāhyottīryāgnim upaspṛśya gāyatryaṣṭaśataṃ japed ghṛtaṃ prāśya punaḥ snātvā trir ācāmet" iti | kāmataḥ sparśa etat | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
patitaṃ sūtikām antyaṃ śavaṃ spṛṣṭvā ca kāmataḥ |
snātvā sacailaṃ spṛṣṭvāgniṃ ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati ||
śavaspṛśaṃ divākīrtiṃ citiṃ yūpaṃ rajasvalām |
spṛṣṭvā tu kāmato vipraḥ snānaṃ kṛtvā viśudhyati ||
evaṃ ca (tac ca) sacailam | kaśyapaḥ: "udayāstamaye skanditvākṣispandane karṇākrośane cityārohaṇe yūpasaṃsparśane ca sacailaṃ snātvā punar mām iti japet | mahāvyāhṛtibhiḥ saptājyāhutīr juhuyāt" | [923] smṛtyantaram |
spṛṣṭvā devalakaṃ caiva savāsā jalam āviśet |
devārcanaparo vipro vittārthī vatsaratrayam ||
asau devalako nāma havyakavyeṣu garhitaḥ |
ṣaṭtriṃśanmatāt |
śvakukkuṭavarāhāṃś ca grāmyān saṃspṛśya mānavaḥ |
sacailaṃ saśiraḥ snātvā tadānīm eva śudhyati ||
bauddhān pāśupatāṃś caiva laukāyatikanāstikān |
vikarmasthān dvijān spṛṣṭvā sacailo jalam āviśet ||
smṛtyantaram |
nagnān pāśupatān bauddhān kālān kaulān diśaścarān |
etān dṛṣṭvā raviṃ paśyet spṛṣṭvā snānaṃ samācaret ||
aṅgirāḥ |
yas tu chāyāṃ śvapākasya brāhmaṇo hy adhirohati |
tatra snānaṃ prakurvīta ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati ||
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe |
abhojyasūtikāṣaṇḍhamārjārāntyaśvakukkuṭān |
patitāviddhacaṇḍālamṛtahārāṃś ca dharmavit ||
saṃspṛśya śudhyati snānād udakyāgrāmasūkarau |
tadvac ca sūtikāśaucadūṣitau puruṣāv api ||
yasya cānudinaṃ hānir gṛhe nityasya karmaṇaḥ |
tathā |
pakṣeṇa karmaṇo hānyā prayāty aspṛśyatāṃ naraḥ |
āviddho jñātibahiṣkṛtaḥ | vṛddhayājñavalkyaḥ |
caṇḍālapukkasamlecchabhillapārisikādikam |
mahāpātakinaś caiva spṛṣṭvā snāyāt sacailakam ||
vyāsaḥ |
bhāsavānaramārjārakharoṣṭrāṇāṃ śunāṃ tathā |
sūkarāṇām amedhyaṃ vai spṛṣṭvā snāyāt sacailakam ||
āpastambaḥ |
yadviṣṭhitaṃ kākabalākakābhyām amedhyaliptaṃ tu bhavec charīram |
śrotre mukhe ca praviśeta samyak snānena lepāpahatasya śuddhiḥ ||
parāśaraḥ |
caityavṛkṣaś citir yūpaś caṇḍālaḥ somavikrayī |
etāṃs tu brāhmaṇaḥ spṛṣṭvā sacailo jalam āviśet ||
citipradeśabhavo vṛkṣaś caityaḥ | devalaḥ |
śvapākaṃ patitaṃ vyaṅgam unmattaṃ śavahārakam |
sūtikāṃ sūyukāṃ nārīṃ rajasābhipariplutām ||
[924]        sacailaṃ saśirāḥ(raḥ) snātvā tadānīm eva śudhyati |
spṛṣṭveti śeṣaḥ |
aśuddhān svayam apy etān aśuddhaś ca yadi spṛśet |
viśuddhyaty upavāsena punaḥ kṛcchreṇa vā punaḥ ||
upaspṛśyāśuciḥ spṛṣṭaṃ tṛtīyaṃ vāpi mānavaḥ |
hastau pādau ca toyena prakṣālyācamya śudhyati ||
tathā |
mānuṣāsthi vasāṃ viṣṭhām ārtavaṃ mūtraretasī |
majjānaṃ śoṇitaṃ vāpi parasya yadi saṃspṛśet ||
snātvopamṛjya lepādīn ācamya sa śucir bhavet |
tāny eva svāni saṃspṛśya pūtaḥ syāt parimārjanāt ||
saṃvartaḥ |
śvavarāhakharān uṣṭrān vṛkagomāyuvānarān |
kākakukkuṭagṛdhrāṃś ca spṛṣṭvā snānaṃ samācaret ||
tathā |
śūdrocchiṣṭaṃ dvijaḥ spṛṣṭvā ucchiṣṭaṃ śūdram eva vā |
śucim apy avagūhyainaṃ savāsāḥ snānam ācaret ||
nīlīṃ nīlīvikārāṃś ca manuṣyāśthy api vā dvijaḥ |
caṇḍālapatitacchāyāṃ spṛṣṭvā snānaṃ samācaret ||
paiṭhīnasiḥ: "kākolūkasparśane sacailaṃ snānam | anudakamūtrapurīṣakaraṇe sacailaṃ snānam, mahāvyāhṛtibhir homaś ca | śvakharoṣṭracaṇḍālasparśane sacailaṃ snānam" | anudakamūtrapurīṣakaraṇe śaucakālavilamba ity arthaḥ | vāyupurāṇe |
udakyāṃ sūtikāṃ caiva śvānam antyāvasāyinam |
nagnāṭān mṛtahārāṃśca spṛṣṭvāśaucaṃ vidhīyate ||
snātvā sacailo mṛdbhis tu śudhyed dvādaśabhir naraḥ |
kālikāpurāṇe |
spṛṣṭvā rudrasya nirmālyaṃ savāsā āplutaḥ śuciḥ |
smṛtyantaram |
guruvākye hy aghore ca svamantre dakṣiṇottare |
calaliṅge tathā tūre paṭacitre 'tha haimake ||
sphāṭikeṣu ca nirmālyaṃ tathā bāṇe svayaṃbhuve ||
tūre śirasi padmarāgamaye vā | manuḥ |
nāraṃ spṛṣṭvāsthi sasnehaṃ snātvā vipro viśudhyati |
ācamyaiva tu niḥsnehaṃ gām ālabhyārkam īkṣya va || [925]
gavālambhārkekṣaṇayoḥ parasparam ācamanena samuccayaḥ | abuddhipūrvasparśaviṣayam etat | buddhipūrve tu vāsiṣṭham: "mānuṣyāsthi snigdhaṃ spṛṣṭvā trirātram āśaucam | asnigdhe tv ahorātram" iti | viṣṇuḥ: "bhakṣyavarjaṃ pañcanakhaśavaṃ tad asthi ca spṛṣṭvā snāto vastraṃ prakṣālitaṃ bibhṛyāt" iti sacailasnānāni | atha snānāni, tatra yamaḥ |
ajīrṇe 'bhyudite vānte śmaśrukarmaṇi maithune |
duḥsvapne durjanasparśe snānam ity abhidhīyate ||
devalaḥ |
mānuṣāsthi vasāṃ viṣṭhām ārtavaṃ mūtraretasī |
majjānaṃ śoṇitaṃ spṛṣṭvā parasya snānam ācaret ||
viṣṇuḥ: "nābher adhastāt prabāhu ca kāyikair malaiḥ surābhir madyair vopahato mṛttoyais tad aṅgaṃ prakṣālyācāntaḥ śudhyet | anyatropahato mṛttoyais tad aṅgaṃ prakṣālya snānenendriyeṣupahatas tūpoṣya snātvā pañcagavyena, daśanacchadopahataś ca" | prabāhuḥ kaphoṇikāvadhivā(rvā)hvor agrabhāgaḥ | parāmedhyaviṣayam etat | aṅgirāḥ |
indriyair na praviṣṭaṃ syād amedhyaṃ yadi karhicit |
mukhe 'pi saṃsparśagataṃ tatra snānaṃ vidhīyate ||
śaṅkhaḥ |
rathyākardamatoyena ṣṭhīvanādyena vā tathā |
nābher ūrdhvaṃ naraḥ spṛṣṭaḥ sadyaḥ snānena śudhyati ||
ṛṣyaśṛṅgaḥ |
madyaviṇmūtravipruḍbhiḥ saṃspṛṣṭaṃ mukhamaṇḍalam |
mṛttikāgomayair lepāt pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
viṣṇuḥ |
caṇḍālodakasaṃspṛṣṭaḥ snānaṃ kṛtvā viśudhyati |
ucchiṣṭena tu saṃspṛṣṭas trirātreṇa viśudhyati ||
āpastambaḥ |
ekāṃ śākhāṃ samārūḍhaś caṇḍālādir yadā bhavet |
brāhmaṇas tatra nivasan snānena śuditām iyāt ||
devalaḥ |
mānuṣāsthi śavaṃ viṣṭhā reto mūtrārtavaṃ vasā |
śleṣmāśru dūṣikā svedo madyaṃ cāmedhyam ucyate ||
ārtavaṃ strīvīryam | dūṣikā netramalam | [926]
mādhūkam aikṣavaṃ ṭāṅkaṃ kaulaṃ khārjūrapānasam |
mārdvīkaṃ kāramārdīkaṃ maireyaṃ nālikarajam ||
amedhyāni hi caitāni madyāni brāhmaṇasya tu |
kṣatriyaś caiva vaiśyaś ca spṛṣṭvaitāni na duṣyataḥ ||
madhūkapuṣpabhavaṃ madyaṃ mādhūkam | ikṣuvikāraviśeṣa aikṣavam | ṭāṅkaṃ ṭaṅkaphalakṛtaṃ madyaviśeṣaṃ | kaulaṃ badaramayam | mārdvīkaṃ drākṣāmayam | karamardamayaṃ kāramārdīkam | prasiddham anyat | bṛhaspatiḥ |
ūrdhvaṃ nābheḥ karau muktvā yad aṅgam upahanyate |
tatra snānam adhastāt tu prakṣālyācamya śudhyati ||
manuḥ |
vānto viriktaḥ snātvā tu ghṛtaprāśanam ācaret |
ācāmed eva bhuktvānnaṃ snānaṃ maithuninaḥ smṛtam ||
bhuktvānnaṃ vānta ācāmed eva | āpastamba: "api vā lepaṃ pakṣālyācamya prokṣaṇam aṅgānāṃ maithune mūtrad retasa utsarge śaucam" | tathā |
aspṛśyasparśane vānte aśrupāte kṣure bhage |
snānaṃ naimittikaṃ kāryaṃ daivapitṛvivarjitam ||
devāditarpaṇarahitam ity arthaḥ, vāgbhaṭasmṛtisaṃgrahāt |
asthisaṃcayanāt pūrvaṃ ruditvā snānam ācaret |
antardaśāhe viprasya ūrdhvam ācamanaṃ smṛtam ||
yamaḥ |
āturasnānasaṃprāptau daśakṛtvas tv anāturaḥ |
snātvā snātvā spṛśed enaṃ tataḥ śudhyet sa āturaḥ ||
āturo rogī | parāśaraḥ |
astaṃ yāte yadā sūrye caṇḍālakuṇapādibhiḥ |
sparśanaṃ tu kathaṃcit syāt kathaṃ śuddhir vidhīyate ||
jātavedaḥ suvarṇaṃ ca somamārgaṃ tathaiva ca |
brāhmaṇānumatenaiva snātvā dṛṣṭvā viśudhyati ||
kuṇapaḥ śavaḥ | śātātapaḥ |
āditye 'stamite rātrāv aspṛśyaṃ saṃspṛśed yadi |
bhagavan kena śuddhiḥ syāt tan me brūhi suniścitam ||
anastam ita āditye saṃgṛhītaṃ tu yaj jalam |
tena sarvātmanā śuddhiḥ śavaspṛṣṭaṃ tu varjayet || [927]
devalaḥ |
divāhṛtair jalaiḥ snānaṃ niśi kuryān nimittataḥ |
prakṣipya ca suvarṇaṃ tu saṃnidhāpya ca pāvakam || 3.29 ||
idānīṃ nānāprakāreṇoktāñ śuddhihetūn saṃgṛhyāha |
kālo 'gniḥ karma mṛd vāyur mano jñānaṃ tapo jalam || 3.30 ||
paścāttāpo nirāhāraḥ sarve 'mī śuddhihetavaḥ ||
kālādayaḥ sarve pratyekaṃ śuddher aduṣṭatāyā hetavas tatra tatroktāḥ | kālas tāvat "trirātraṃ daśarātraṃ ca" ityādau | agniḥ "bhūśuddhir mārjanād dāhāt" ityādau | karma japādi "abliṅgāni japet" ityādau | mṛttikā "salilaṃ bhasma mṛd vāri" ityādau | vāyuḥ "marutārkeṇa" ityādau | manaḥ "manaḥpūtaṃ samācaret" ityādau śāstrāntare | jñānam ādhyātmikam "jñānaṃ buddheḥ śodhanam" ityādau | tapaḥ "vidyātapobhyāṃ bhūtātmā" ityādau | jalam "varṣmaṇo jalam" ityādau | paścāttāpo 'nutāpaḥ "khyāpanenānutāpena" ityādau | nirāhāra āhārābhāvaḥ "tryaham anaśnanta āsīran" ityādau || 3.30 ||
kiṃ ca |
akāryakāriṇāṃ dānaṃ vego nadyās tu śuddhikṛt || 3.31 ||
śodhyasya mṛc ca toyaṃ ca saṃnyāso 'tha dvijanmanām |
tapo vedavidāṃ kṣāntir viduṣāṃ varṣmaṇo jalam || 3.32 ||
japaḥ pracchannapāpānāṃ manasaḥ satyam ucyate |
bhūtātmanas tapovidye bhuddher jñānaṃ viśodhanam |
kṣetrajñasyeśvarajñānād viśuddhiḥ paramā matā || 3.33 ||
akāryaṃ niṣiddhaṃ tatkartṝṇāṃ dānam | nadyā amedhyayuktāyā vegaḥ | vegavatā jalena pūraṇam | buddher jñānaṃ viśodhanam ity asmāt prāk pratyekaṃ śuddhikṛd ucyata iti saṃbadhyate | śodhyasyāmedhyayuktasya mṛjjalaṃ ca | saṃnyāsaḥ pārivrājyaṃ dvijānām | tapaḥ kṛcchrādi vedavidām adhītavedānām | kṣāntiḥ parāparādhasahiṣṇutā viduṣāṃ vedārthavidām | manaso 'ntaḥkaraṇasya satyaṃ yathādṛṣṭārthavivakṣā | bhūtair bhūtasūkṣmair āvṛta ātmā bhūtātmā tasya tapo nityanaimittikaṃ varṇāśramakarma vidyā [928] brahmopāsanam ubhe śuddhikare ucyete | bhūtātmanaś ca śuddhiḥ kāmyaniṣiddhakarmāpūrvasya kṣayaḥ | buddher vyavasāyātmikāyā jñānaṃ yathāvatsthitātmanātmavivekaḥ | kṣetrasyopādhibhedād īśvarād bhinnasya yad īśvara evāham asmītīśvaravijñānaṃ tasmāt paramā muktilakṣaṇā śuddhir manvādīnāṃ saṃmatā | bṛhaspatiḥ |
svādhyāyenānutāpena (?) homena tapasainasaḥ |
dhyānena kṣetravāsena dāneneha parikṣayaḥ ||
parāpavādāśravaṇaṃ parastrīṇām adarśanam |
etac chaucaṃ śrotradṛśor jihvāśuddhir apaiśunam ||
aprāṇivadham asteyaṃ śucitvaṃ pādahastayoḥ |
asaṃśleṣaḥ parastrīṇāṃ śārīraṃ śaucam iṣyate ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
kālo 'gnir manasas tuṣṭir udakāny avalekhanam |
avijñānaṃ ca bhūtānāṃ ṣaḍvidhā śuddhir iṣyate || 3.31, 32, 33 ||
iti śrīvidyādharavaṃśaprabhavaśrīśilāhāranarendrajīmūtavāhanānvayaprasūta-
śrīmadaparādityadevaviracite yājñavalkyadharmāstranibandhe 'parārke
āśaucaprakaraṇam || 1 ||
atha saṭīkayājñavalkyasmṛtau
āpaddharmaprakraṇam | (2)
uktaṃ sadyaḥśaucam āpadi,
āpady api ca kaṣṭāyāṃ sadyaḥśaucaṃ vidhīyate |
ityādiṣu | tatprasaṅgenāpadi vṛttiviśeṣān āha |
kṣātreṇa karmaṇā jīved viśāṃ vāpy āpadi dvijaḥ |
nistīrya tām athātmānaṃ pāvayitvā nyaset pathi || 3.34 ||
svavarṇavihitena sarvavarṇasādhāraṇena ca dhanopāyena tadaprāptir āpat | tasyāṃ satyāṃ dvijo brāhmaṇaḥ kṣātreṇa karmaṇā kṣatriyavṛttyā śatrujayādikayā jīvet | tadabhāve vaiśyavṛttyā kṛṣyādikayā | tām āpadaṃ nistīrya brāhmyā (?) vṛttyā viguṇayāpi poṣyāṇām ātmanaś ca nirvāhasaṃbhave 'thātmānaṃ pāvayitvā prāyaścittaiḥ pariśodhya pathi svajātivihitāyāṃ vṛttau nyased avasthāpayet | atha vā hīnavarṇavṛttyā [929] yad arjitaṃ tat pathi mārge nyaset | atra pakṣe dravyam arjitam ity adhyāhāryam | tatra pramāṇam,
yad garhitenārjayanti karmanā brāhmaṇā dhanam |
tasyotsargeṇa śudhyanti japena tapasaiva ca ||
iti manuvākyam | atra sarvavarṇasādhāraṇadhanopārjanopāyam āha nāradaḥ |
sādhāraṇaṃ syāt trividhaṃ śeṣaṃ navavidhaṃ viduḥ |
kramāgataṃ prītidāyaṃ prāptaṃ ca saha bhāryayā ||
aviśeṣeṇa sarveṣāṃ varṇānāṃ trividhaṃ dhanam |
vaiśeṣikaṃ dhanaṃ vidyād brāhmaṇasya trilakṣaṇam ||
pratigraheṇa yal labdhaṃ yājyataḥ śiṣyatas tathā |
trividhaṃ kṣatriyasyāpi prāhur vaiśeṣikaṃ dhanam ||
yuddhopalabdhaṃ kāraṃ ca daṇḍaś ca vyavahārataḥ |
kāraṃ karādānaprabhavam |
vaiśeṣikaṃ paraṃ jñeyaṃ vaiśyasyāpi trilakṣaṇam |
kṛṣigaurakṣyavāṇijyaiḥ śūdras tebhyas tv anugrahāt ||
sarveṣām eva varṇānām eṣa dharmyo dhanāgamaḥ |
viparyayād adharmaḥ syān na ced āpad garīyasī ||
śaṅkhalikhitau: "tasya svadharma eva śreyān svalpo 'py anuṣṭhito nāvaraṃ seveta | jaghanyatāsevī hi brāhmaṇo jaghanyeṣv avapadyate, tasmāt praśastasamayācāraṃ sarvataḥ pratigṛhṇīyān nātmanas tu tatas tṛptim icchet | kāmaṃ trayāṇāṃ varṇānāṃ yājanaṃ kurvīta, dharmeṇa tebhyo vṛttim ādāya darmakṛtyeṣūpaharet" | āpatkalpo brāhmaṇasyety anuvṛttau gautamaḥ: "yājanādhyāpanapratigrahāḥ sarveṣāṃ pūrvaḥ pūrvo guruḥ | tadalābhe kṣatravṛttiḥ | tadalābhe vaiśyavṛttiḥ" | yājanādayaḥ sarveṣāṃ varṇānāṃ brāhmaṇena kāryā ity arthaḥ | vasiṣṭhaḥ: "kāmaṃ vā svayaṃ kṛṣyotpādya tilān vikrīṇīran na tv evāyājyayājanaṃ pratiṣiddhaṃ pratigraho vāstīti | [930] athāpy udāharanti |
agnaya iva dīpyante tapohomair dvijottamāḥ |
pratigraheṇa śāmyanti udakenāgnayo yathā ||
śaṅkhalikhitau: "āpatkāle mātāpitṛmato bahubhṛtyasyānantarakā vṛttir iti kalpaḥ | tatra brāhmaṇeṣu triguṇasaṃpanneṣu vṛttidaurbalyam ākhyeyam | yad brūyus te sa dharmaḥ | tasyānantarakā vṛttiḥ kṣātro 'bhiniveśaḥ | trayāṇāṃ brāhmaṇakarmaṇāṃ yā vṛttir yājanādhyāpanapratigrahāṇām evam apy ajīvan vaiśyam upajīvet" | vaiśyaṃ vaiśyavṛttim ity arthaḥ | triguṇasaṃpannā ijyādhyayanadānasaṃpannāḥ | vaiśyavṛttyasaṃbhave brāhmaṇasya na śūdravṛttiḥ | tathā ca nāradaḥ |
āpady anantarā vṛttir brāhmaṇasya vidhīyate |
vaiśyavṛttis tathā coktā na jaghanyā kathaṃcana ||
jaghanyā śūdravṛttiḥ |
na kathaṃcana kurvīta brāhmaṇaḥ karma vārṣalam |
vṛṣalaḥ karma vā brāhmaṃ patanīye hi te tayoḥ ||
utkṛṣṭaṃ cāvakṛṣṭaṃ ca tayoḥ karma na vidyate |
madhyame karmaṇī hitvā sarvasādhāraṇe hi te ||
madhyame kṣatriyavaiśyakarmaṇī | kṣatriyo 'py āpadi vaiśyavṛttyānantarayā, vaiśyaś cānantarayā śūdravṛttyā | yad āha vasiṣṭhaḥ: "ajīvantaḥ svadharmeṇānantarāṃ pāpī(yavī)yasīṃ vṛttim ātiṣṭheran na tu kadācij jyāyasīm" iti | śiloñchādyantaṃ svadharmaḥ | tadasaṃbhave kṣatriyavṛttyā vaiśyavṛttyā vā | "āpatkāle sādhubhyaḥ pratigṛhṇīyāt saṃkaraṃ na kuryāt | kāmaṃ varṇasaṃkaraṃ kṣetraphalasiddhyartham" | kṣetraṃ bhāryā, tatphalam apatyam, saṃkarābhyanujñānaṃ varṇānulomyaviṣayam | na pratilomābhiprāyam || 3.34 ||
brāhmaṇasya vaiśyakarmaṇā jīvataḥ paṇyaviśeṣavikrayapratiṣedham āha |
phalopalakṣaumasomamanuṣyāpūpavīrudhaḥ |
tilaudanarasakṣāradadhikṣīraghṛtaṃ jalam || 3.35 ||
[931]        śastrāsavamadhūcchiṣṭamadhulākṣāḥ sabarhiṣaḥ |
mṛccarmapuṣpakutapakeśatakraviṣakṣitīḥ || 3.36 ||
kauśeyanīlīlavaṇamāṃsaikaśaphasīsakān |
śākārdrauṣadhipiṇyākapaśugandhāṃs tathaiva ca || 3.37 ||
vaiśyavṛttyāpi jīvan no vikrīṇīta kadācana |
brāhmaṇo vaiśyavṛttyā jīvan phalādikaṃ kadācana no vikrīṇīta | phalaṃ pūgādi | upalo 'śmā | kṣaumam atasīmayaṃ vastram | somo latāviśeṣaḥ | apūpā uṇḍerakāḥ | vīrudho gulmebhyo hrasīyāṃsaḥ sthāvaraviśeṣāḥ | rasā ikṣurasādayaḥ | śastraṃ khaḍgādi | āsavo madyam | madhūcchiṣṭaṃ sikthakam | madhu kṣaudram | lākṣā jatu | barhiṣaḥ kuśāḥ | mṛn mṛttikā | kutapaḥ kambalaviśeṣaḥ | keśaś cāmarādiḥ | takram udaśvit | kṣitiḥ kṣetrādiḥ | kauśeyaṃ trasarīmayam | nīlī rañjanadravyaviśeṣaḥ | ekaśaphā aśvādayaḥ | sīsakaṃ nāgam | ārdraṃ śṛṅgaveram | oṣadhayo vrīhiyavādyāḥ | piṇyākam uddhṛtasnehatilapiṇḍaḥ | paśavo gavādayaḥ | gandhāś candanādayaḥ | prasiddham anyat | paśavo 'trāraṇyāḥ,
āraṇyāṃś ca paśūn sarvān daṃṣṭriṇaś ca vayāṃsi ca |
iti manusmaraṇāt | brāhmaṇaṃ praty evāyaṃ niṣedhaḥ | yad āha nāradaḥ |
vaiśyavṛttāv avikreyaṃ brāhmaṇasya payodadhi |
atra brāhmaṇānuvṛttau gautamaḥ: "yasyāpaṇyaṃ gandharasakṛtānnatilapiṇyākaśāṇakṣaumājināni raktanirṇikte vāsasī, kṣīraṃ savikāram, mūlaphalapuṣpauṣadhamadhumāṃsatṛṇodakapathyāni, nārīpuruṣavaśākumārīvehataś ca nityaṃ vrīhiyavajātayaś ca vṛṣabhadhenvanaḍuhaś caike" | vaśā vandhyā gauḥ | vehad garbhaghātinī | dhenur navaprasūtā | vasiṣṭhaḥ: "vaiśyajīvikām āsthāya paṇyena jīvatām aśmalavaṇam apaṇyam, śāṇakauśeyakṣaumājināni tāntavasya ca raktam | sarvaṃ ca kṛtānnam | puṣpamūlaphalāni ca gandhā rasā udakaṃ ca | oṣadhīnāṃ rasaḥ somaḥ | viṣaṃ māṃsaṃ ca kṣīraṃ savikāram | [932] ayas trapu jatu sīsaṃ ca, grāmyapaśūnām ekaśaphāḥ keśinaś ca sarve vāraṇyāḥ paśavo vayāṃsi daṃṣṭriṇaś ca" | grāmyāraṇyāḥ paśavo grāmyāraṇyavāsino vā pāribhāṣikā vā | yad gṛhyapariśiṣṭam |
gaur aśvaḥ puruṣo 'jāvīr gardabhāśvatarāv ubhau |
ete grāmyās tu saṃkhyātāḥ paśavaḥ sapta sādhubhiḥ ||
siṃhā varāhā vyāghrāś ca vāraṇā mahiṣās tathā |
ṛkṣāś ca vānarāś caiva saptāraṇyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || iti |
dvyantarās tu grāhmyāraṇyatvād apaṇyam eva |
gomṛgo 'tha vṛkaś caiva antasthaḥ śvātha kukkuṭaḥ |
cātakaḥ kalaviṅkaś ca saptaite dvyantarāḥ smṛtāḥ || iti |
āpastambaḥ: "avihitā brāhmaṇasya vaṇijyā, yad vyavaharet paṇyānām apaṇyāni vyudasyen manuṣyān rasān gandhān annaṃ carma gavāṃ vaśāḥ śleṣmodake tokmakiṇve pippalīmarice dhānyaṃ māṃsam āyudhaṃ sukṛtāsavatilataṇḍulāṃs tv eva dhānyasya viśeṣeṇa na vikrīṇīyāt" | virūḍhadhānyaṃ tokmam, surāsādhanaṃ dravyaṃ kiṇvam, sukṛtaṃ dharmaḥ | chāgaleyaḥ |
na vedavikrayaṃ kuryād agnivikrayam eva vā |
yo dharmavikraye puṃsāṃ narakaḥ so 'tra jāyate ||
tasmān na vikrayo vede agnihotre tathaiva ca |
prādhyāpanaṃ prādhyayanaṃ praśnapūrvaḥ pratigrahaḥ ||
yājanādhyāpane vādaḥ ṣaḍvidho vedavikrayaḥ ||
yamaḥ |
gavāṃ vikrayakārī tu gavi romāṇi yāni tu |
tāvad varṣasahasrāṇi gavyagoṣṭhe kṛmir bhavet || 3.35, 36, 37 ||
tilavikraye pratiprasavam āha |
dharmārthaṃ vikrayaṃ neyās tilā dhānyena tatsamāḥ || 3.38 ||
dhānyaṃ gṛhītvā tattulyamānās tilā dharmārthaṃ vikreyāḥ | etac cāvaśyadharmātiprasaṅge veditavyam | atra manuḥ | [933]
kāmam utpādya kṛṣyāṃ tu svayam eva kṛṣīvalaḥ |
vikrīṇīta tilāñ śuddhān dharmārtham acirasthitān ||
śuddhān dravyāntarāmiśrān | nāradaḥ |
aśakto bheṣajasyārthe yajñahetos tathaiva ca |
yady avaśyaṃ tu vikreyās tilā dhānyena tatsamāḥ ||
manuḥ |
bhojanābhyañjanād dānād yad anyat kurute tilaiḥ |
krimībhūtaḥ śvaviṣṭhāyāṃ pitṛbhiḥ saha sajjati ||
tathā |
rasā rasair nipā(mā)tavyā na tv eva lavaṇaṃ rasaiḥ |
kṛtānnaṃ cākṛtānnena tilā dhānyena tatsamāḥ ||
śaṅkhalikhitau: "śuṣkakāṣṭhabadareṅgudabilvarajjukārpāsikasūtrair avikṛtair dravyam āharet" | hārītaḥ: "pūrtavṛtteś cājīvañ śuṣkakāṣṭhatṛṇavikrayaṃ vā kuryāt, gā eva vā rakṣet" | vasiṣṭhaḥ: "rasā rasaiḥ samato hīnato vā nipā(mā)tavyāḥ | na tv evaṃ lavaṇaṃ rasena tilataṇḍulapakvānnavidyāmanuṣyāś ca vihitāḥ parivartakena" | tilataṇḍulānuvṛttāv āpastambaḥ: "vihitaś caiṣāṃ vinimayo 'nnena cāsya manuṣyāṇāṃ ca manuṣyaiḥ | rasānāṃ ca rasair vidyayā ca vidyānām | akrītapaṇyair vyavaharet | muñjabilvajais tṛṇakāṣṭhair nātyantam anuvased vṛttiṃ prāpya paravṛttito viramet" | ayam arthaḥ — akrītaṃ gṛhotpannaṃ paṇyaṃ vikreyaṃ na tu krītam | na cātyantaṃ vaṇigvṛttir adhyavasātavyā, svakīyāṃ vṛttiṃ prāpya paravṛttito viramed iti | baudhāyanaḥ: "tṛṇakāṣṭham avikrītaṃ vikreyam | athāpy udāharanti |
paśavaś caikatodantā aśmā tv alavaṇoddhṛtaḥ |
etad brāhmaṇato 'paṇyaṃ tantuś cārajanīkṛtaḥ ||"
lavaṇaravadāta (?) uddhṛtaḥ sa na tathā | arajanīkṛto nīrāga ity arthaḥ | nāradaḥ |
brāhmaṇena tu vikreyaṃ śuṣkaṃ dāru tṛṇāni ca |
gandhadravyairakāvetraṃ tūlastūlīkṛtād ṛte ||
[934]        svayaṃ śīrṇaṃ ca vidalaṃ phalānāṃ badareṅgude |
rajjuḥ kārpāsikaṃ sūtraṃ tac ced avikṛtaṃ bhavet ||
ekarā tṛṇaviśeṣaḥ | śaṅkhalikhitau: "āpadi kāmaṃ brāhmaṇo jijīviṣur ekāntaritāṃ vṛttim āsthāya na paṇyaṃ paṇyam iti brūyāt | sakṛd evoktaṃ mūlyam icchet | gā vā rakṣet | tāsu nāpītāsu pibet | na tiṣṭhantīṣūpaviśet | na svayam utthāpayet | śanair ārdraśākhayā sapalāśayā pṛṣṭhato 'bhihanyāt | nātīrthena viṣameṇālpodakena vā tārayet | bālavṛddharogārtāḥ śrāntā upāveśayet | śaktitaḥ pratīkāraṃ kuryāt | gavām eva dharmo 'nyathā viplavaḥ" || 3.38 ||
dravyaviśeṣavikraye pratyavāyaviśeṣam āha |
lākṣālavaṇamāṃsāni patanīyāni vikraye |
payo dadhi ca madyaṃ ca hīnavarṇakarāṇi tu || 3.39 ||
lākṣādīni vikrīyamāṇāni vikretur brāhmaṇasya patanīyāni patananimittāni bhavanti | patanaṃ dvijātikarmānadhikāraḥ | payaḥprabhṛtīni tu hīnavarṇakarāṇi bhavanti | śūdratvaṃ vaiśyatvaṃ vāpādayantīty arthaḥ | atra mānavo viśeṣaḥ |
sadyaḥ patati māṃsena lākṣayā lavaṇena ca |
tryaheṇa śūdro bhavati brāhmaṇaḥ kṣīravikrayāt ||
tathā |
itareṣām apaṇyānāṃ vikrayād iha kāmataḥ |
brāhmaṇaḥ saptarātreṇa vaiśyabhāvaṃ prayacchati ||
etac ca vākyadvayam akṛtaprāyaścittabrāhmaṇaviṣayam | brāhmaṇagrahaṇāt kṣatriyasya lavaṇādi vikrīṇānasya nāyaṃ doṣaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
jīved etena rājanyaḥ sarveṇāpy anayaṃ gataḥ |
na tv eva jyāyasīṃ vṛttim abhimanyeta karhicit ||
brāhmaṇavṛttir jyāyasī | tathā |
vaiśyo 'jīvan svadharmeṇa śūdravṛttyāpi vartayet |
anācann akāryāṇi nivarteta ca śaktimān || 3.39 ||
[935]
āpadi brāhmaṇasya svakīyā vṛttir viguṇāpi śreyasī, na tu saguṇāpi parakīyety āha |
āpadgataḥ saṃpragṛhṇan bhuñjāno 'pi yatas tataḥ |
na lipyetainasā vipro jvalanārkasamo hi saḥ || 3.40 ||
āpadgataḥ kṛcchrajīvī yatas tato hīnavarṇād api saṃpragṛhṇan, tathā bhuñjāno 'bhyavaharan, enasā pāpena na lipyate na yujyate, yato jvalanenārkeṇa vā samo 'sau pavitratayā | atra ca saṃpretyupasargadvayaprayogo yājanādhyāpanapratigrahāṇāṃ svīkārārthaḥ | tena kṣatriyavaiśyāv api yājyāv adhyāpyau ca | pratigrahas tu śūdrād apy anujñātaḥ | atra manuḥ |
varaṃ svadharmo viguṇo na pārakyaḥ svanuṣṭhitaḥ |
paradharmeṇa jīvan hi sadyaḥ patati jātitaḥ ||
vaiśyavṛttim anātiṣṭhan brāhmaṇaḥ svapathi sthitaḥ |
svavṛttikarṣi(śi)taḥ sīdann imaṃ dharmaṃ samācaret ||
sarvataḥ saṃpragṛhṇīyād brāhmaṇas tv anayaṃ gataḥ |
pavitraṃ duṣyatīty etad dharmato nopapadyate ||
jīvatāny(nāny)ayam āpanno yo 'nnam atti yatas tataḥ |
ākāśam iva paṅkena na pāpena sa lipyate ||
nādhyāpanād yājanād vā garhitād vā pratigrahāt |
doṣo bhavati viprāṇāṃ jvalanāmbusamā hi te ||
ajīgartaḥ sutaṃ hantum upāsarpad bubhukṣitaḥ |
na cālipyata doṣeṇa kṣutpratīkāram ācaran ||
śvamāṃsam icchann ārto 'ttuṃ dharmādharmavicakṣaṇaḥ |
prāṇānāṃ parirakṣārthaṃ vāmadevo na liptavān ||
bhāradvājaḥ kṣudhaṃ hantuṃ saputro vijane vane |
bahvīr gāḥ pratijagrāha śuśutī(ta)kṣṇo mahātmanaḥ ||
kṣudhārtaś cāttum abhyāgād viśvāmitraḥ śvajāghanīm |
caṇḍālahastād ādāya dharmādharmavicakṣaṇaḥ ||
pratigrahād yājanād vā tathā vādhyāpanād api |
pratigrahaḥ pratyavaraḥ pretya viprasya garhitaḥ ||
[936]        yājanādhyāpane nityaṃ kriyete saṃskṛtātmanām |
pratigrahas tu kriyate śūdrād apy antyajanmanaḥ ||
japahomair apaityeno yājanādhyāpanāt kṛtam |
pratigrahanimittaṃ tu tyāgena tapasaiva ca ||
evaṃ ca sati yad gautamavacanam: "yājanādhyāpanapratigrahāḥ sarveṣāṃ pūrvaḥ pūrvo guruḥ" iti, tasyāyam arthaḥ — sarveṣāṃ dvijānāṃ saṃbandhino yājanādayo brāhmaṇasyāpatkalpaḥ | sa ca pūrvaḥ pūrvo gurur adoṣaḥ | yat tu manunā yājanādyapekṣayā pratigrahasya gurudoṣatvam amuktam, tac chūdrasaṃbandhipratigrahaviṣayam | atha vā manuvākyavaśād yājanādīnāṃ yathāpūrvaṃ gurutvam abhyudayagauravajanakatvena gautamoktam iti mantavyam || 3.40 ||
aparām apy āpadvṛttim āha |
kṛṣiḥ śilpaṃ bhṛtir vidyā kusīdaṃ śakaṭaṃ giriḥ |
sevānūpo nṛpo bhaikṣam āpattau jīvanāni tu || 3.41 ||
kṛṣyādīny āpadviṣaye jīvanāni bhavanti | jīvyata ebhir iti jīvanāni | kṛṣiḥ prasiddhā | śilpaṃ vastrālaṃkārasūpagānagṛhanirmāṇādikauśalam | śakaṭaṃ prasiddham, tena ca tannimittaṃ bhāṇḍādiprāpaṇaṃ lakṣyate | giriḥ parvataḥ, tenāpi tatsamutthakāṣṭhādivikriyā | sevā prasiddhā | bahuvṛkṣajalo deśo 'nūpaḥ | tena ca tatratyakandamūlaphalāny upajīvanaṃ lakṣyate | nṛpaḥ prasiddhas tena ca tadupakārakatvanimittā vṛttir upalakṣyate | baikṣyaṃ(kṣaṃ) prasiddham | atra kṛṣigrahaṇaṃ pradarśanārtham | tena pāśupālyavāṇijyayor api parigrahaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
vidyā śilpaṃ bhṛtiḥ sevā gaurakṣyaṃ vipaṇiḥ kṛṣiḥ |
girir bhaikṣaṃ kusīdaṃ ca daśa jīvanahetavaḥ ||
yady api,
kṣātreṇa karmaṇā jīved viśāṃ vāpy āpadi dvijaḥ |
ity anenaiva kṛṣyādyanugrahaḥ siddhas tathāpi svayaṃ kṛṣiḥ pratiṣiddhā phalādīnāṃ ca vikraya iti tayor apy atyantāpadi pratiprasavārtham etad vacanam | tatra kṛṣiniṣedho mānava udāhriyate |
vaiśyavṛttyāpi jīvaṃs tu brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyo 'pi vā |
hiṃsāprāyāṃ parādhīnāṃ kṛṣiṃ yatnena varjayet |
[937]        kṛṣiṃ sādhv iti manyante sā vṛttiḥ sadvigarhitā |
bhūmiṃ bhūmiśayāṃś caiva hanti kāṣṭham ayomukham ||
svayaṃkṛtakṛṣiviṣayaś cāyaṃ niṣedhaḥ | yata āhāpaddharmeṣu gautamaḥ: "kṛṣivāṇijye svayaṃkṛte kusīdaṃ ca" iti | tatrātyantāpadi svayaṃkṛṣato niyamam āha hārītaḥ: "āpatsu kṛṣiṃ prāhuḥ kṛṣiś ced āyovānividāyojya (?) bālavṛddhakṛśārtādīn varjayann adīrghāreṇa pratodena śanair vāhayed yaṣṭimuṣṭiloṣṭāratnijānuyoktraproṣaṇākrośanaprabhṛtibhir nātihanyāt | na parvasu saṃdhivelayor vāhayet | vihvalayeta cet parityāgo na mṛdya mokṣaṇam, vṛddhāvasthāyāṃ pānāhārādīn dadyāt | kṛmikīṭakokilītakadaṃśamaśakamakṣikātivātasamīraśītārkātyuṣṇāti-varṣeṣūddharaṇāvaraṇakaṇḍūyanadhūmāgninivātapracchannasamupastīrṇādiṣu gopāyet | sannabhagnavilagnādīnām auṣadhayavasalavaṇasnehādiṣu yathārthaṃ vapanalavaṇamardanapavanābhihāreṣu balihomādīn kuryāt | snātvānaḍuho 'laṃkṛtya brāhmaṇān bhojayet | saurabheyāṇāṃ puṇyanimittaṃ ṣaḍbhāgaṃ rājñe datvā pañcamād brāhmaṇāṃs toṣayet" | ta(a)thāpy udāharanti |
yūpo 'yaṃ vihito madhye bhethīnām eha (iha) kārṣa(ṣi)kaiḥ |
tasmād atandrito dadyād annadhānyārthadakṣiṇāḥ ||
bhūmiṃ bhittvauṣadhīś chittvā hatvā kīṭapipīlakān |
punanti khalayajñena kārṣikā nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
tathā balīvardaniyame 'pi sa evāha |
aṣṭagavaṃ dharmahalaṃ ṣaḍgavaṃ jīvitārthinām |
caturgavaṃ nṛśaṃsānāṃ dvigavaṃ brahmaghātinām ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
rājñe datvā tu ṣaḍbhāgaṃ devatānāṃ tu viṃśakam |
triṃśadbhāgaṃ tu viprāṇāṃ kṛṣiṃ kṛtvā na doṣabhāk ||
[938]        aṣṭavāhaṃ halaṃ dharmyaṃ ṣaḍbhāgaṃ madhayaṃ smṛtam |
caturgavaṃ jaghanyaṃ ca śāstravidbhir udāhṛtam ||
hārītaḥ |
bālānāṃ damanaṃ caiva vāhanaṃ ca na śasyate |
vṛddhānāṃ durbalānāṃ ca prajāpativaco yathā ||
puṃstvopaghātaṃ nasyaṃ vā vāhānāṃ na ca kārayet |
vṛṣaṃ yuge na yuñjīta jīrṇaṃ vyādhitam eva vā || 3.41 ||
uktajīvanair ajīvato jīvanāntaram āha |
bubhukṣitas tryahaṃ sthitvā dhānyam abrāhmaṇād dharet |
pratigṛhya tathākhyeyam abhiyuktena dharmataḥ || 3.42 ||
bubhukṣito 'naśnaṃs tryahaṃ trirātraṃ yāvad āsthāya brāhmaṇavyatiriktasya svāmino dhānyaṃ harec corayet tac ca dhānyaṃ pratigṛhyopādāya kim iti tvayaitad asmadīyaṃ dhānyaṃ gṛhītam ity ākṣiptena dharmatas tathaym eva tasya kathanīyam | mayā trirātram bhuñjānena sthitavatā prāṇadhāraṇārthaṃ bhavatīyaṃ dhānyam apahṛtam iti | manuḥ |
tathaiva saptamaṃ bhaktaṃ bhaktāni ṣaḍ anaśnatā |
aśvastanavidhānena hartavyaṃ hīnakarmaṇaḥ ||
khalāt kṣetrād agārād vā yato vāpy upalabhyate |
ākhyātavyaṃ tu tat tasmai pṛcchate yadi pṛcchati ||
ayam arthaḥ — ṣaḍ bhojanāny akurvatā saptamabhojanāvasāne kṣetrādikād dhānyam aśvastanavidhānenaikadinaparyāptaṃ hīnavarṇād dhīnakarmaṇaś cāpahared iti | atrāpavādaṃ manur evāha |
brāhmaṇasvaṃ na hartavyaṃ kṣatriyeṇa kadācana |
dasyuniṣkriyayoś ca svam ajīvan hartum arhati || 3.42 ||
prasaṅgād rājño vidhyantaram āha |
tasya vṛttaṃ kulaṃ śīlaṃ śrutam adhyayanaṃ tapaḥ |
jñātvā rājā kuṭumbaṃ ca dharmyāṃ vṛttiṃ prakalpayet || 3.43 ||
tasyoktadurgatikasya brāhmaṇasya vṛttam ācāraṃ kulam anvayaṃ śīlaṃ svabhāvaṃ śrutaṃ vedārthagrahaṇam adhyayanaṃ prasiddhaṃ tapaḥ svadharmavartitvaṃ kuṭumbaṃ poṣyavargaṃ ca jñātvā rājā dharmyāṃ dharmād anapetāṃ vṛttiṃ nirvāhasamarthaṃ dhanaṃ prakalpayet | dakṣaḥ proṣyavargam āha | [939]
pitā mātā gurur bhāryā prajā dīnaḥ samāśritaḥ |
abhyāgato 'tithiś cāgniḥ poṣyavarga udāhṛtaḥ ||
jñātir bandhujanaḥ kṣīṇas tathānāthaḥ samāśritaḥ |
anyo 'pi dhanayuktasya poṣyavarga udāhṛtaḥ ||
jñānavidbhyaḥ pradātavyam anyathā narakaṃ vrajet |
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena poṣyavargaṃ sadā vahet ||
bharaṇaṃ poṣyavargasya praśastaṃ svargasādhanam |
narakaḥ pīḍanenāsya tasmād yatnena taṃ bharet || iti || 3.43 ||
iti śrīvidyādharavaṃśaprabhavaśrīśilāhāranarendrajīmūtavāhanānvayaprasūta-
śrīmadaparādityadevaviracite yājñavalkyadharmāstranibandhe 'parārke
āpaddharmaprakaraṇam || 2 ||
atha saṭīkayājñavalkyasmṛtau
vānaprathaprakraṇam | (3)
kṛtabrahmacaryo gṛhasthāśrame 'dhikṛta iti brahmacaryānantaraṃ gṛhasthāśrama ukto vānaprasthas tu gṛhī brahmacārī vā bhūtvā bhavatīti tadubhayābhidhānānantaraṃ vānaprasthāśramam āha |
sutavinyastapatnīkas tathā vānugato vanam |
vānaprastho brahmacārī sāgniḥ sopāsano vrajet || 3.44 ||
vānaprasthaḥ saṃkalpitavānaprasthāśramaḥ suteṣu patnīṃ vinyasya nirvāhyatayā nidhāyātha vā tayānugato 'nvito brahmacārī maithunatyāgī, yadi sapatnīko vrajet tadāsau sāgnir agnisahitaḥ sopāsanaś ca vrajet | patnīsahitasya gṛhapravrajyām evāha chāgaleyaḥ |
putreṣu bhāryāṃ saṃsthāpya vanaṃ gacched yathāvidhi |
apatnīkaḥ samāropya brāhmaṇaḥ pravrajed gṛhāt || iti |
atra samāropyety asya vanaṃ gacched ity anena vyavahitenānvayo yojyaḥ | agnīn iti [940] vādhyāhāraḥ | ekākinā ca vanaṃ gatena vānaprasthena vidhānāntareṇāgnir ādheya ity āha vasiṣṭhaḥ: "śrāvaṇakenāgim ādāyāhitāgnir vṛkṣamūlika ūrdhvaṃ ṣaḍbhyo māsebhyo 'nagnir aniketano dadyād devapitṛmanuṣyebhyaḥ sa gacchet svargam ānantyam" | śrāvaṇakaṃ vaikhānasaśāstraṃ taduktena vidhināgnim ādāya vṛkṣamūlaniketanaḥ ṣaṇmāsān agniṃ paricarya tato 'gnīn ātmani samāropyānagnir aniketano dadyād devādibhyaḥ | evaṃvṛttiḥ kevalakarmā svargaṃ gacchati, jñānī tu mokṣātmakam ānantyam | yamaḥ |
dvitīyam āyuṣo bhāgam uṣitvā tu gṛhe dvijaḥ |
tṛtīyam āyuṣo bhāgaṃ gṛhamedhī vane vaset ||
utpādya dharmataḥ putrān iṣṭvā yajñaiś ca śaktitaḥ |
dṛṣṭvāpatyasya cāpatyaṃ brāhmaṇo 'raṇyam āśrayet ||
brāhmaṇagrahaṇaṃ dvijopalakṣaṇārtham | brahmacaryānantaram api vānaprastho bhaved ity āha vasiṣṭhaḥ: "catvāra āśramāḥ brahmacārigṛhasthavānaprasthaparivrājakāḥ | teṣāṃ vedam adhītya vedau vedān vā viśīrṇabrahmacaryo yam icchet tam āvaset" | gārhasthyād vānaprasthāśramaṃ gacchato viśeṣam āhatuḥ śaṅkhalikhitau: "putrān utpādya saṃskṛtya vedam adhyāpya vṛttiṃ vidhāya dāraiḥ saṃyojya guṇavati putre kuṭumbam āveśya kṛtaprasthānaliṅgo vṛttiviśeṣān anukramet | kramaśo yāyāvarāṇāṃ vṛttim upāsya vanam āśrayed uttarāyaṇe pūrvapakṣe" iti | yamaḥ |
yajñāṅgāny agnihotraṃ ca dhenūr atha paricchadam |
samādāya sadāro vā tadāraṇyaṃ vrajed dvijaḥ || 3.44 ||
vānaprasthasya śrautasmārtakarmasādhanadravyaveśaviśeṣam āha |
aphālakṛṣṭenāgnīṃś ca pitṝn devātithīn api |
bhṛtyāṃś ca tarpayec chmaśrujaṭālomabhṛd ātmavān || 4.45 ||
phālādyakṛṣṭabhūprabhaveṇa sasyena śyāmākanīvārādināgnidevapitratithibhṛtyāṃs tarpayet | agnitarpaṇam agnisādhyakarmānuṣṭhānam, devapitrādidai(de)vatyakriyākaraṇaṃ [941] devāditarpaṇam, bhṛtyatarpaṇaṃ prasiddham, śmaśruprabhṛtīni ca bibhṛyāt | ātmavān vijitendriyaś ca syāt | manuḥ |
vaitānikaṃ ca juhuyād agnihotraṃ yathāvidhi |
darśam akandayan pūrvaṃ pūrṇamāsaṃ ca śaktitaḥ ||
ṛkṣeṣṭyāgrā(gr)yaṇaṃ caiva cāturmasyāni cāharet |
uttarāyaṇaṃ tu kramaśo dakṣasyāyanam eva ca ||
ṛkṣeṣṭayo nakṣatreṣṭayaḥ | uttarāyaṇe dakṣiṇāyana iṣṭyayane |
vāsantaśāradair medhyair munyannaiḥ svayam āhṛtaiḥ |
puroḍāśāṃś carūṃś caiva nirvapec ca pṛthak pṛthak ||
devatābhyas tu tacchrutvā vanyaṃ medhyataraṃ haviḥ |
śeṣam ātmani yuñjīta lavaṇaṃ ca svayaṃkṛtam ||
tathā |
munyannair vividhair medhyaiḥ śākamūlaphalena vā |
etān eva mahāyajñān nirvaped vidhipūrvakam ||
yad bhakṣyaṃ syāt tato dadyād baliṃ bhikṣāṃ ca śaktitaḥ |
ammūlaphalabhikṣābhir arcayed āśramāgatān ||
vasīta carma cīraṃ vā sāyaṃ snāyāt prage tathā |
jaṭāśca bibhṛyān nityaṃ śmaśrulomanakhāni ca ||
yamaḥ |
akṛṣṭānnāṃ mūlaphalaṃ vrīhīn saṃcintya(tya) cāparān |
haviṣyānnaṃ prayacchet tu mahāsattreṣu pañcasu ||
yasyai yasyai yathādṛṣṭaṃ devatāyai havir dvijaḥ |
tasyai tasyai tathā samyag ghaviṣyaṃ kalpayet pṛthak || 3.45 ||
saṃgrāhyadravyaparimāṇaviśeṣam āha |
ahno māsasya ṣaṇṇāṃ vā tathā saṃvatsarasya vā |
arthāya saṃcayaṃ kuryāt kṛtam āśvayuje tyajet || 3.46 ||
ekasyāhnaḥ sādhyadṛṣṭādṛṣṭaprayojanasya karmasamudāyasyārthāya prayojanāya sasyasaṃcayaṃ kuryāt | atha vā māsena ṣaḍbhir vā māsaiḥ saṃvatsareṇa vā parimitānāṃ karmaṇāṃ kṛte sasyaparigrahaḥ kāryaḥ | tatra yad avaśiṣyate tad āśvayuje māsi parityajet | tathā ca manuḥ |
[942]        sadyaḥprakṣālako vā syān māsasaṃcayako 'pi vā |
ṣaṇmāsasaṃcayo vā syāt samānicaya eva vā ||
varjayen madhumāṃsāni bhaumāni kavakāni ca |
bhūstṛṇaṃ śigrukaṃ caiva śleṣmātakaphalāni ca ||
tyajed āśvayuje māsi munyannaṃ pūrvasaṃcitam |
jīrṇāni caiva vāsāṃsi śākamūlaphalāni ca ||
na phālakṛṣṭam aśnīyād utsṛṣṭam api kenacit |
na grāmajātāny ārto 'pi mūlāni ca phalāni ca ||
grāmajātāny āha laugākṣiḥ |
vrīhayo yavagodhūmā ubhau ca tilasarṣapau |
ikṣuḥ priyaṅgavaś caiva grāmyā oṣadhayaḥ smṛtāḥ || 3.46 ||
kiṃ ca |
dāntas triṣavaṇasnāyī nivṛttaś ca pratigrahāt |
svādhyāyavān dānaśīlaḥ sarvasattvahite rataḥ || 3.47 ||
dānto 'niṣiddhodyamo mṛṣāvādādibhya uparataḥ saṃdhyātrayasnāyī pratigrahān nivṛttaḥ | cakārād yājanādhyāpanābhyāṃ ca | svādhyāyādhyayanavān bhikṣādidānaśīlaḥ prāṇihitanirataś ca syāt || 3.47 ||
dantolūkhalikaḥ kālapakvāśī vāśmakuṭṭakaḥ |
śrautasmārtaṃ phalasnehaiḥ karma kuryāt kriyās tathā || 3.48 ||
dantā ulūkhalakāryaṃ tuṣakaṇavimokākhyaṃ yasya kurvanti sa dantolūkhalikaḥ | agniṃ vinā yat kālenaiva pakvarasāsrāvi tadaśananiyamavān vā syāt | yad vā— aśmakuṭṭako bhavet | aśmanā vā kuṭṭayitvā munyannāny aśnīyād ity arthaḥ | tathā ca śrutismṛtivihitam ājyasādhyakarma medhyavṛkṣaphalamūlasaṃbhavena tailena kuryāt | bhojanābhyañjnādilaukikāś ca kriyāḥ | manuḥ |
agnipakvāśano vā syāt kālapakvabhug eva vā |
aśmakuṭṭo bhaved vāpi dantolūkhalikas tathā ||
sthūlodakajaśākāni puṣpamūlaphalāni ca |
medhyavṛkṣodbhavān anyān snehāṃś ca phalasaṃbhavān || 3.48 ||
[943]
cāndrāyaṇair nayet kālaṃ sadā kṛcchraiś ca vartayet |
pakṣe yāte 'nnam aśnīyān māse vāhani vā gate || 3.49 ||
pipīlikāmadhyayavamadhyādibhiś cāndrāyaṇaiḥ kālaṃ nayet | kṛcchrair vā prājāpatyādibhiḥ sadā vartayet | kālam ity anuṣaṅgaḥ | pakṣe māse 'hany ekasmin vātite 'nnam aśnīyāt | pradarśanārthaṃ caitat | tenānye 'pi tapaḥprakārāḥ svīkāryāḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
naktaṃ yāvaṃ samaśnīyād divā vāhṛtya śaktitaḥ |
caturthakāliko vā syāt syād vāpy aṣṭamakālikaḥ ||
śaktyapekṣo vikalpaḥ | aṅgīkṛtapakṣaś ca yāvadāśramasamāpti nirvāhyaḥ || 3.49 ||
śucir bhūmau svaped rātrau divasaṃ prapadair nayet |
sthānāsanavihārair vā yogābhyāsena vā punaḥ || 3.50 ||
śuciḥ prayataḥ | bhūmāv eva na paryaṅkādau svapyāt | divasam ahaḥ prapadaiḥ pādāgrāvayavais tiṣṭhan nayet | sthānena gatinivṛttyāsanenopaveśanena vihāreṇa caṅkramaṇena ca divasaṃ nayet | yad vā yamaniyamādyaṣṭāṅgopetayogābhyāsena nayet | manuḥ |
bhūmau viparivarteta tiṣṭhed vā prapadair dinam |
sthānāsanair vā viharet savaneṣūpayann apaḥ ||
etāś cānyāś ca seveta dīkṣāḥ vipro vane vasan |
vividhāś copaniṣada ātmasaṃsiddhaye śrutīḥ ||
dīkṣā tapaḥsaṃkalpaḥ | saṃsiddhir viniścayaḥ |
ṛṣibhir brāhmaṇaiś caiva gṛhasthaiś copasevitāḥ |
vidyātapovivṛddhyarthaṃ śarīrasya ca śuddhaye || 3.50 ||
grīṣme pañcāgnimadhyastho varṣāsu sthaṇḍilaśayaḥ |
ārdravāsās tu hemante śaktyā vāpi tapaś caret || 3.51 ||
[944]
sūrya upariṣṭād dikcakracatuṣṭaye catvāro 'gnayaḥ | eṣāṃ pañcānām agnīnāṃ madhye grīṣme vyavasthitaḥ syāt | varṣāsu sthaṇḍileśayaḥ syāt | sthaṇḍilaṃ gṛhavṛkṣādyanāvṛtaṃ bhūtalam | śayanagrahaṇād rātrāv api tatra sthānaṃ gamayati | hemante tv ārdravāsāḥ syāt | vāsaḥśabdena cīravalkalādy atra vivakṣitam | ṛtutrayātmakaṃ saṃvatsaram abhipretyaitad ucyate, tena caitrādyāś catvāro māsāḥ grīṣmaḥ | śrāvaṇādyās tāvanta eva varṣāḥ | mārgaśīrṣādyās tāvanta eva hemantaḥ | atha vā prakārāntareṇ yathāśakti tapaś carec charīraśoṣaṇārtham | yathāha śaṅkhaḥ: "kāle śaktitaḥ śarīraṃ śoṣayed yamaniyamamantropavāsādibhiḥ | kuśacīracarmavalkalavāsā jaṭī kṛṣṇājinottarīyo jalaśayanapañcatapābhrāvakāśaniyamaparaḥ" iti | manuḥ |
upaspṛśet triṣavaṇaṃ pitṝn devāṃś ca tarpayet |
tapaś caraṃś cogrataraṃ śoṣayed deham ātmanaḥ || 3.51 ||
kiṃ ca |
yaḥ kaṇṭakair vitudati candanair yo vilimpati |
akruddho 'parituṣṭaś ca samas tasya ca tasya ca || 3.52 ||
yaḥ kaṇṭakavitodanādibhir duḥkhayati, yaś ca candanavilepanādibhiḥ sukhayati, tayor yathāsaṃkhyaṃ krodhaparitoṣau hitvā sama upekṣakaḥ syāt | asmatkṛtaśubhāśubhaparatantrābhyām ābhyāṃ sukhaṃ duḥkhaṃ ca nirmīyat iti samyakparibhāvayato bhavati tadviṣayopekṣā || 3.52 ||
agnīn vāpy ātmasāt kṛtvā vṛkṣāvāso mitāśanaḥ |
vānaprasthagṛheṣv eva yātrārthaṃ bhaikṣam ācaret || 3.53 ||
yaḥ punar āśramaṃ parigṛhya ṣaṇmāsān yāvad agnīn paricarya parastān na śaknoti paricaritum, so 'gnīn ātmasād ātmasamārūḍhān kṛtvā vṛkṣāvāso vṛkṣeṇaiva gṛhakāryakārī niyatabhojanaḥ | yadā tv evaṃvidhaiḥ prakārair dehayātrāṃ nirvartayitu[ma]samarthas tadā śarīrasthityarthaṃ vānaprasthakuṭīṣu bhaikṣam ācaret | manuḥ |
agnīṃs tv ātmani vaitānān samāropya yathāvidhi |
anagnir aniketaḥ syān munir mūlaphalāśanaḥ || [945]
niketo gṛham | munir maunavān | bhaikṣavidhir mūlaphalālābhe draṣṭavyaḥ | ata evānantaraṃ manur evāha |
tāpaseṣu ca vipreṣu yātrikaṃ bhaikṣam ācaret |
gṛhamedhiṣu vānyeṣu dvijeṣu vanavāsiṣu || 3.53 ||
yadā tu vanasthabhikṣayā na śarīravṛttis tatra yat kāryaṃ tad āha |
grāmād āhṛtya vā grāsān aṣṭau bhuñjīta vāgyataḥ |
nigadavyākhyātam etat | āraṇyānnabhojane grāmeṣu ṣoḍaśasaṃkhyā —
aṣṭau grāsā muner bhaikṣaṃ vānaprasthasya ṣoḍaśa |
ityādibhir vidhīyate | iyaṃ tv aṣṭasaṃkhyā grāmyānnagrāseṣv ity avirodhaḥ |
sakalānuṣṭhānāsamarthaṃ praty āha |
vāyubhakṣaḥ prāgudīcīṃ gacched vāvarṣmasaṃkṣayāt || 3.54 ||
ā varṣmaṇaḥ śarīrasya saṃkṣayān nipātāt prāgudīcīm aparājitāṃ diśaṃ vāyubhakṣo bhūtvā gacchet | evaṃ ca vānaprasthasya svadharmānuṣṭānāsamarthasya dehatyāge na pratyavāyaḥ kiṃ tv abhyudaya iti mantavyaṃ vihitatvāt | manuḥ |
aparājitām āsthāya vrajed diśam ajihmagaḥ |
ā nipātāc charīrasya yukto vāryanilāśanaḥ ||
yuktaḥ samāhitaḥ | ayaṃ ca mahāprasthānopadeśaḥ pradarśanārthaḥ | tenāgnijalapraveśādikam api vihitaṃ bhavati, tathā ca smṛtyantaram: "vānaprastho dūrādhvānaṃ jvalanāmbupraveśanaṃ bhṛguprapatanaṃ vānitiṣṭhet" iti | manuḥ |
āsāṃ maharṣivaryāṇāṃ tyaktvānyatamayā tanum |
vītaśokabhayo vipro brahmaloke mahīyate ||
"grīṣme pañcatapās tu syāt" ityādyās tapaścaryā āsām ity anena parāmṛśyante | brahmacāriprakaraṇoktā avirodhino dharmā gṛhasthavansthayatibhir apy anuṣṭheyāḥ | tad āha gautamaḥ: "uttareṣāṃ caitad avirodhi" iti | etad brahmacāriprakaraṇoktaṃ dharmajātam uttareṣāṃ gṛhasthādīnāṃ vakṣyamāṇānāṃ veditavyam | [946] yat teṣāṃ svāśramavihitena dharmenāvirodhīty arthaḥ || 3.54 ||
iti śrīvidyādharavaṃśaprabhavaśrīśilāhāranarendrajīmūtavāhanānvayaprasūta-
śrīmadaparādityadevaviracite yājñavalkyadharmāstranibandhe 'parārke
vānaprasthaprakaraṇam || 3 ||
atha saṭīkayājñavalkyasmṛtau
yatiprakraṇam | (4)
atha pravrjitānāṃ dharmān vaktum upakramate |
vanād gṛhād vā kṛtveṣṭiṃ sarvavedasadakṣiṇām |
prājāpatyāṃ tadante tān agnīn āropya cātmani || 3.55 ||
adhītavedo japakṛt putravān annado 'gnimān |
śaktyā ca yajñakṛn mokṣe manaḥ kuryāc ca nānyathā || 3.56 ||
brahmacarye 'dhītavedaḥ saṃdhyopāsanādirūpanityanaimittikajapakārī gārhasthye yathāvidy utpāditaputro devapitṛmanuṣyādibhyo 'nnadāyy āvaśyakaśrautasmārtāgnisādhyakarmakārī yathāśakti yajñānuṣṭhāyītyādiviśeṣaṇād vānaprasthāśramād gṛhasthāśramād vā prajāpatidevatākāṃ sarvasvadakṣiṇām iṣṭiṃ kṛtvā tasyā ante tān vaitānān agnīn ātmani samāropya mano 'ntaḥkaraṇaṃ mokṣe mokṣopāye pārivrājye kuryāt | pārivrājyaṃ saṃkalpayed ity arthaḥ | yajñaśabdenātra somayāgo vivikṣitaḥ | tasya ca bahusāmagrīsādhyatvād agnimān ity uktvāpi yajñakṛd iti pṛthaṅnirdeśāt | etac ca gārhasthyapūrvake pārivrājye vidhānaṃ draṣṭavyam | brahmacaryamātrapūrvake tu putravattvādi viśeṣaṇam iṣṭiś cāsaṃbhavān nivartate | asti ca brahmacaryānantaraṃ saṃnyāsaḥ | tathā [ca] jābālaśrutiḥ: "brahmacaryaṃ parisamāpya gṛhī bhavet | gṛhī bhūtvā vanī bhaved vanī bhūtvā pravrajed yad vetarathā brahmacaryād eva parivrajed gṛhād vanād vā" iti | [947] śaṅkhalikhitau: "vanavāsād ūrdhvaṃ śāntasya pariṇatavayasaḥ kāmataḥ pravrjyā, tam evāgnim ātmany āropya vyapagatabhayalobhamohakrodhaśokadrohamadamatsaraḥ kālaṃ nānucintayed anavasthitatvān manasaḥ śraddhā hy aniyatā bhavati | tasmān na śvaḥ śvaḥ samupāsīta | tad ahar eva saṃnyaset sarvārambhaparigrahān utsṛjya" |
atra baudhāyanaḥ |
athātaḥ saṃnyāsavidhiṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ | so 'ta eva brahmacaryavān pravrajatīty ekeṣām | atha śālīnayāyāvarāṇām anapatyānāṃ vidhureva (ro vā) prajāṃ svadharme pratiṣṭhāpya vā saptatyā ūrdhvaṃ saṃnyāsam upadiśanti, vānaprasthasya vā karmavirāma eṣa nityo mahimā brāhmaṇasya [na] karmaṇā vardhate no kanīyān | tasyaivātmā padavittaṃ viditvā na karmaṇā lipyate pāpakenety apunarbhāvaṃ nayatīti, nityo mahad enaṃ gamayatīti mahimā | keśaśmaśrunakhāni vāpayitvā snātvopakalpayate, yaṣṭayaḥ śikyaṃ jalapavitraṃ kamaṇḍaluṃ pātram ity etat samādāya grāmānte grāmasīmānte 'gnyagāre vājyaṃ payo dadhīti trivṛt prāśyopavased apo vā | oṃ bhūḥ sāvitrīṃ praviśāmi, tat savitur vareṇyam, oṃ bhuvaḥ sāvitrīṃ praviśāmi, bhargo devasya dhīmahī, svaḥ sāvitrīṃ praviśāmi, dhiyo yo naḥ pracodayād iti paccho 'rdharcaśas tataḥ samastayā cātmānam ātmanāśramād āśramāntaram upanīya brahmabhūto bhavatīti vijñāyate | athāpy udāharanti |
āśramād āśramaṃ gatvā hutahomo jitendriyaḥ |
bhikṣābalipariśrāntaḥ paścād bhavati bhikṣuka iti ||
sa eṣa bhikṣur ānantyāya purādityasyāstamayād gārhapaty[m upasamādhāyānvāhāryapacanam āhṛtya jvalantam āhavanīyam uddhṛtya nyupyopasamādāya gārhapatya] ājyaṃ [948] vilāpyotpūya sruci caturgṛhītaṃ gṛhītvā samiddha(dva)ty āhavanīye pūrṇāhutiṃ juhoty oṃ svāhety etad brahmānvādhānam iti vijñāyate | atha sāyaṃ hute 'gnihotra uttareṇa gārhapatyaṃ tṛṇāni saṃstīrya teṣu dvaṃdvaśo nyañci pātrāṇi sādha(da)yitvā dakṣiṇenāhavanīye brahmāyatane darbhān saṃstīrya kṛṣṇājinaṃ cāntardhāyaitāṃ rātriṃ jāgarti ya evaṃ vidvān brahmarātrim upoṣya brāhmaṇo 'gnīn samāropya vā pramīyate sa samastaṃ pāpmānaṃ tarati tarati brahmahatyām | atha brāhme muhūrta utthāya kālpa(kalya) eva prātar agnihotraṃ juhoty atha pṛthvyāṃ stīrtvāpaḥ praṇīya vaiśvānaraṃ dvādaśakapālaṃ nirvapati sā prasiddheṣṭiḥ saṃtiṣṭhate | āhavanīye gārhapatye 'raṇī bhavataṃ naḥ samanasāv ity athātmany agnīn samāropayati | yā te agne yajñiyā tanūr iti tris trir ekaikam avajighraty athāntarvedi tiṣṭhann oṃ bhūr bhuvaḥ svaḥ saṃnyastaṃ mayeti trir nirūpyopāṃśūktvā trir uccaiḥ, triṣatyā hi devā iti vijñāyate | abhayaṃ sarvabhūtebhyo matta iti cāpāṃ pūrṇāñjaliṃ ninayati | athāpy udāharanti |
abhayaṃ sarvabhūtebhyo datvā yac carate muniḥ |
na tasya sarvabhūtebhyo bhayaṃ vāpīha vidyata iti ||
sa vācaṃyamo 'bhipravrajati | yaṣṭayaḥ śikyaṃ jalapavitrakaṃ kamaṇḍaluṃ pātram ity etat samādāya yatrāpas tatra gatvā snātvāpa ācamya surabhimatyābliṅgābhir hiraṇyavarṇābhiḥ pāvamānībhir mārjayitvāntarjalagato 'ghamarṣaṇena ṣoḍaśa prāṇāyāmān dhārayitvottīrya vāsaḥ pīḍayitvānyat prayato vāsaḥ paridhāyāpa ācamya, oṃ bhūr bhuvaḥ svar iti pavitram ādāya tarpayāmy oṃ bhūs tarpayāmy oṃ bhuvas [949] tarpayāmy oṃ svas tarpayāmy oṃ bhūr bhuvaḥ svar mahar nama iti [devān pitṛbhyo 'ñjalim ādāya tarpayāmi | oṃ bhūḥ svadhā | oṃ bhuvaḥ svadhā | oṃ svaḥ svadhā | oṃ bhūr bhuvaḥ svar mahar nama iti] | athod u tyaṃ citram iti dvābhyām ādityam upatiṣṭhate | om iti brahmom it brāhmaṃ vā | eṣa jyotir ya eṣa tapati, eṣa vedo (devo) ya eṣa tapati vedyam etad ya eṣa tapati, eṣam evaiṣa ātmā tarpayaty ātmane namaḥ karoty ātmā brahmātmā jyotiḥ | oṃ sāvitrīṃ tarpayāmi | oṃ bhus tarpayāmi | oṃ bhuvas tarpayāmi | oṃ svas tarpayāmi | oṃ bhūr bhuvaḥ svas tarpayāmi | sahasrakṛtva āvartayet | śatakṛtvo 'parimitakṛtvo vā | oṃ bhūr bhuvaḥ svar iti pavitram ādāyopagṛhṇāti | na cāta ūrdhvam anuddhṛtābhir aparisrutābhir aparipūtābhir vārbhir (?) ācāmen na cāt ūrdhvaṃ śuklavāso 'bhidhārayet | ekadaṇḍī tridaṇḍī vā | athemāni pratāni bhavanty ahiṃsā satyam astainyaṃ maithunasya ca varjanaṃ tyāga ity eva pañcaivopavratāni bhavanti, akrodho guruśuśrūṣā prasādaḥ śaucam āhāraśuddhiś ca" iti |
vāyupurāṇe |
evaṃ vanāśrame tiṣṭhaṃs tapasā dagdhakilbiṣaḥ |
caturtham āśramaṃ gacchet saṃnyasya vidhinā dvijaḥ ||
dadyād dvijebhyo devebhyaḥ svapitṛbhyaś ca yatnataḥ |
datvā śrāddham ṛṣibhyaś ca manujebhyas tathātmanaḥ ||
iṣṭiṃ vaiśvānarīṃ kṛtvā prājāpatyam athāpi vā |
agnīṃś cātmani saṃsthāpya mantravat pravrajet tataḥ ||
tataḥ prabhṛti putrādyaiḥ snehalobhādi varjayet |
dadyāc ca bhūmāv udakaṃ sarvabhūtābhayaṃkaram ||
daṇḍaṃ ca vaiṇavaṃ saumyaṃ satvacaṃ samaparvakam |
veṣṭitaṃ kṛṣṇagovālarajjvā ca caturaṅgulam ||
granthibhiś ca tribhir yuktaṃ jalapūtena copari |
gṛhṇīyād dakṣiṇe haste mantreṇaiva tu dharmavit ||
[950]        kṣaumaṃ vā kuśasūtraṃ vā sūtraṃ kārpāsikaṃ tu vā |
tair eva grathitaṃ śikyaṃ padmākārasamanvitam ||
ṣaḍbhir vā parvabhir yuktaṃ muṣṭibhiḥ śikyalakṣaṇam |
gṛhṇīyān mantrato vidvān pātraṃ cāpi kamaṇḍalum ||
āsanaṃ dārujaṃ proktaṃ samāśraṃ vartulaṃ samam |
śaucārtham āsanārthaṃ vā munibhis tair anuṣṭhitam ||
kaupīnācchadanaṃ vāsaḥ kanthāṃ śītanivāriṇīm |
pāduke cāpi gṛhṇīyāt kuryān nānyasya saṃgraham ||
etāni tasya liṅgāni yateḥ proktāni dharmataḥ |
saṃgṛhya kṛtasaṃnyāso gatvā tīrtham anuttamam ||
snātvāthācamya vidhivaj jalapūtāṃśukena vai |
vāriṇā tarpayitvā tu mantravad bhāskaraṃ namet ||
āsīnaḥ prāṅmukho maunī prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ caret |
gāyatrīṃ ca yathāśakti japtvā dhyāyet paraṃ padam ||
vaneṣu tu vigṛhyaivaṃ tṛtīyaṃ bhāgam āyuṣaḥ |
caturtham āyuṣo bhāgaṃ tyaktvā saṅgaṃ parityajet ||
āśramād āśramaṃ gacched dhutahomo jitendriyaḥ |
bhikṣābalipariśrāntaḥ pravrajan pretya vardhate ||
ṛṇāni trīṇy apākṛtya mano mokṣe niveśayet |
anapākṛtya mokṣaṃ tu sevamānaḥ pataty adhaḥ ||
adhītya vidhivad vedān putrāṃś cotpādya dharmataḥ |
iṣṭvā ca śaktito yajñair mano mokṣe niveśayet ||
anadhītya dvijo vedān anutpādya tathātmajān |
aniṣṭvā ca tathā yajñair mokṣam icchan vrajaty adhaḥ ||
etac ca pravrajyāyā gārhasthyapūrvakatvapakṣe draṣṭavyam | yas tu brahmacarya eva virakto na tasya dāraparigrahaḥ, strīrāgaprayuktatvāt tasya | adārasya ca prajotpattījyayor anadhikāro 'nadhikṛtasya cā[na]nuṣṭḥāne 'pi na pratyavāyaḥ | tataś ca brahmacāriṇaḥ pravrajato 'dhītavedatvam eva pravrajyādhikāre hetuḥ | na prajādisaṃpattiḥ | na ca "jāyamāno ha vai brāhmaṇaḥ" ityādivacanabalād [951] utpattimātraviśiṣṭasyaivādhyayanaprajejyātmakarṇāpākaraṇe 'dhikāra iti vācyam | asamarthatvāt | na hy utpanna eva śaknoty etat kartum, tasmād adhikārī jāyamāno brāhmaṇādis tribhir ṛṇavāñ jāyata iti vākyārthaḥ | tataś ca brahmacaryamātrapūrvikāyāṃ pravrajyāyāṃ na kaścid virodhaḥ | nanu saptatyā ūrdhvaṃ saṃnyased iti virudhyate, maivam | rāgāt kṛtagārhatyaparigrahasya hi mandavairāgyasya smṛtyaiṣa kāla āhāryaṃ vairāgyaṃ prati vidhīyate | anyathā "yad ahar eva virajet tad ahar eva pravarjet" iti śrutivirodho duṣparihāraḥ syāt | tathā ca nṛsiṃhapurāṇam |
yasyaitāni suguptāni jihvopasthodaraṃ śiraḥ |
saṃnyased akṛtodvāho brāhmaṇo bhaikṣacaryavān ||
agārād abhiniṣkrāntaḥ pavitropacito muniḥ |
samupoḍheṣu kāmeṣu nirapekṣaḥ parivrajet ||
rāgādiviṣayāsaṅgaputradāraśubhāśubham ||
tathā |
veśmadārasutakṣetraṃ saṃnyastaṃ yena duḥkhadam |
vairāgyajñānapūrvaṃ vai loke 'simin nāsti tatsamaḥ ||
mahābhārate |
caturvidhā bhikṣavas tu kuṭīcarabahūdakau |
haṃsaḥ paramahaṃsaś ca yo yaḥ paścāt sa uttamaḥ || 3.56 ||
parivrajya kiṃ kuryād ity ata āha |
sarvabhūtahitaḥ śāntas tridaṇḍī sakamaṇḍaluḥ |
ekārāmaḥ parivrajya bhikṣārthaṃ grāmam āśrayet || 3.57 ||
ukataprakāreṇa kṛtapravrajyo 'pakārakeṣv api bhūteṣu hito 'napakārakaḥ | upakārakeṣv apy uparatasneho 'nanugrāhaka iti yāvat | tathā ca gautamaḥ: "hiṃsānugrahayor anārambhī" iti | tridaṇḍī vaiṇavadaṇḍatrayavān | yat tu manunoktam,
vāgdaṇḍaś ca manodaṇḍaḥ karmadaṇḍo 'tha te trayaḥ |
yasyaite niyatā daṇḍāḥ sa tridaṇḍīti kathyate ||
vāgdaṇḍo maunam ātiṣṭhet karmadaṇḍas tv ahiṃsanam |
mānasasya tu daṇḍasya prāṇāyāmo vidhīyate || [952]
iti, tatra daṇḍaśabdo vāgādisāmānādhikaraṇyād gauṇaḥ | na punar atrāpi gauṇaḥ kāraṇābhāvāt | sakamaṇḍaluḥ śaucācamanādiprayojanasiddhaye kamaṇḍaludhārī ca syāt | ekārāma ekākī | tathā ca dakṣaḥ |
eko bhikṣur yathoktaḥ syād dvau caiva mithunaṃ smṛtam |
trayo grāmaḥ samākhyāta ūrdhvaṃ tu nagarāyate ||
nagaraṃ na hi kartavyaṃ grāmo vā mithunaṃ tathā |
etat trayaṃ tu kurvāṇaḥ svadharmāc cyavate yatiḥ ||
rājādivārtā teṣāṃ tu bhikṣāvārtā parasparam |
snehapaiśunyamātsaryaṃ saṃnikarṣān na saṃśayaḥ ||
lābhapūjānimittaṃ hi vyākhyānaṃ śiṣyasaṃgrahaḥ |
ete cānye ca bahavaḥ prapañcāḥ kutapasvinām ||
dhyānaṃ śaucaṃ tathā bhikṣā nityam ekāntaśīlatā |
bhikṣoḥ karmāṇi catvāri pañcamaṃ nopapadyate ||
bhikṣārtham eva grāmam āśrayet praviśet | na nivāsādyartham | tato grāmād anyatraiva nivaset | ayaṃ ca haṃsaparamahaṃsayor vidhiḥ | kuṛīcarabahūdakayos tu grāme 'py avasthānam asti, tathā ca prajāpatiḥ: "tatra parivrājakā nāma caturvidhā bhavanti,tatra (te ca) kuṭīcaro bahūdako haṃsaḥ paramahaṃsaś ceti | kuṭīcaro nāma — svagṛhe vartamāno vigatakaluṣa āhitāgniṣu bhikṣā bhuñjāno vyapagatakāmakrodhalobhamohāhaṃkāro yad ātmānugrahaṃ kurute sa kuṭīcaro nāma | bahūdako nāma — grāma ekarātraṃ nagare pañcarātraṃ prāptaṃ bhojyaṃ bhuñjānaḥ sa bahūdako nāma | tatra haṃso nāma — parvataguhāśramadevakulārāmavāsī bhikṣārthe grāmaṃ praviśet sa haṃso nāma | tatra paramahaṃso nāma — nadīpulināraṇyapradeśeṣu tṛṇaharitaśādvaleṣu śayāno 'saṃkusukaś cīrakaupīnakaṣāyavān yas tu prāṇān parityajati sa paramahaṃso nāma" iti | asaṃkusuko 'saṃcayavān | dakṣaḥ |
budho hy ābharaṇaṃ bhāraṃ malam ālepanaṃ tathā |
manyate strī ca mūrkhas tu tad eva bahu manyate ||
[953]        sattvotkaṭāḥ surās te 'pi viṣayais tu vaśīkṛtāḥ |
pramādiṣv alpasattveṣu manuṣyeṣu tu kā kathā ||
tasmāt pakvakaṣāyeṇa kartavyaṃ daṇḍadhāraṇam |
itaras tu na śaknoti viṣayair avahīyate ||
tridaṇḍavyapadeśena jīvanti bahavo janāḥ |
yo hi brahma na jānāti na tridaṇḍī bhaved dhi saḥ ||
vāgdaṇḍo 'tha manodaṇḍaḥ karmadaṇḍaś ca te trayaḥ |
yasyaite niyatā daṇḍāś tridaṇḍīti sa ucyate ||
nādhyatavyaṃ na vaktavyaṃ na śrotavyaṃ kadācana |
etaiḥ sarvaiḥ suniṣpanno yatir bhavati netaraḥ |
mekhalājinadaṇḍena brahmacārīti lakṣyate ||
gṛhastho yaṣṭivedābhyāṃ nakharomair vanāśritaḥ |
tridaṇḍena yatiś caiva lakṣaṇāni pṛthak pṛthak ||
yasyaital lakṣaṇaṃ nāsti prāyaścittī sa nāśramī ||
manuḥ |
eka eva caren nityaṃ siddhyartham asahāyavān |
siddhim ekasya saṃpaśyan na jahāti na hīyate ||
siddhiṃ na jahāti, tayā sa na hīyata ity arthaḥ |
anagnir aniketaḥ syād grāmam annārtham āśrayet |
upekṣako 'saṃcayako munir bhāvasamanvitaḥ ||
kapālaṃ vṛkṣamūlāni kucailam asahāyatā |
samatā caiva sarvasminn etan muktasya lakṣanam ||
nābhinandeta maraṇaṃ nābhinandeta jīvitam |
kālam eva pratīkṣeta nirveśaṃ bhṛtako yathā ||
dṛṣṭipūtaṃ nyaset pādaṃ vastrapūtaṃ jalaṃ pibet |
satyapūtāṃ vaded vāṇīṃ manaḥpūtaṃ samācaret ||
nirveśaḥ kālāvadhi |
ativādāṃs titikṣeta nāvamanyeta kaṃcana |
na cemaṃ deham āśritya vairaṃ kurvīta kenacit ||
[954]        na krudhyantaṃ prati krudhyed ākruṣṭaḥ kuśalaṃ vadet |
saptadvārāvakīrṇāṃ ca na vācaṃ samudīrayet ||
kāmakrodhalobhamohamadamātsaryāhaṃkāradūṣitāṃ vācaṃ [na]samudīrayet |
adhyātmaratir āsīta nirapekṣo nireṣaṇaḥ |
ātmanaiva sahāyena sukhārthī vicared iha ||
kḷpt(kṛtta)keśanakhaśmaśruḥ pātrī daṇḍī kusaṃbhavam |
vicaren niyato nityaṃ sarvabhūtāny apīḍayan ||
saṃrakṣaṇārthaṃ jantūnāṃ rātrāv ahani vā sadā |
śarīrasyātyaye caiva samīkṣya vasudhāṃ caret ||
graiṣmhaimantikān māsān aṣṭau prāyeṇa saṃcaret |
dayārthaṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ varṣāsv ekatra saṃvaset ||
alpānnabhyavahāreṇa rahaḥsthānāsanena vā |
hriyamāṇāni viṣayair indriyāṇi nivartayet ||
indriyāṇāṃ nirodhena rāgadveṣakṣayeṇa ca |
ahiṃsayā ca bhūtānām amṛtatvāya kalpate ||
matsyapurāṇe |
aṣṭau māsān vihāraḥ syād yatīnāṃ saṃyatātmanām |
ekatra caturo māsān vārṣikān nivaset punaḥ ||
avimukte praviṣṭānāṃ vihāras tu na vidyate |
na deho bhavitā tatra dṛṣṭaṃ śāstre purātane ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ: "parivrājakaḥ sarvabhūtābhayadakṣiṇāṃ datvā pratiṣṭheta" | lokān vittaṃ putrāṃś ca varjayitvā vrajatīti parivrājakaḥ | bhāvinī caiṣā saṃjñā vrajanasya bhāvitatvāt | sarveṣāṃ carāṇām acarāṇāṃ ca bhūtānām abhayam eva dakṣiṇāṃ kṛteṣṭisaṃbandhinīṃ datvā vidyādyanapekṣayā pratiṣṭheta vrajet | abhayaṃ mattaḥ sarvabhūtebhya ity uktvā pravrajed ity arthaḥ | "athāpy udāharanti,
abhayaṃ sarvabhūtebhyo datvā carati yo muniḥ |
tasyāpi sarvabhūtebhyo na bhayaṃ jātu vidyate ||"
bhūtasaṃpradānakābhayadātus tata evābhayaṃ mokṣo bhavati | anena ca pravrajyāyā muktiphalatvam uktam | parivrajyābhraṃśe doṣam āha | [955]
abhayaṃ sarvabhūtebhyo datvā yas tu nivartate |
hanti jātān ajātāṃś ca pratigṛhṇāti yasya ca || iti |
abhayadānopalakṣaṇāyāḥ pravrajyāyā yo nivartate, sa svavaṃśe jātāñ janiṣyamāṇāṃś ca yasya ca pratigṛhṇāti tadīyāṃś ca hanti, nirayagāmiṇaḥ karotīty arthaḥ |
ekākṣaraṃ paraṃ brahma prāṇāyāmaḥ paraṃ tapaḥ |
upavāsāt paraṃ bhaikṣaṃ dayā dānād viśiṣyate ||
yat ekaṃ praṇavākhyam akṣaraṃ brahmaṇo vedarāśeḥ param utkṛṣṭaṃ, yataś ca prāṇāyāmas tapasaḥ kṛcchracāndrāyaṇādikāt paraḥ śreṣṭhaḥ | yata eva bhikṣāṇāṃ samūha upavāsād anaśanāt para uttamaḥ | yasmāc ca dayā dānād viśiṣyate guṇavattayā, tasmāt praṇavaprāṇāyāmau sabhikṣācaryāṃ dayāṃ ca satatam anutiṣṭhed iti tātparyārthaḥ | tathā tatraiva: "muṇḍo 'mamo 'parigrahaḥ" | muṇḍo 'śikhaḥ | idaṃ ca vaikalpikam, "muṇḍaḥ śikhī vā" iti gautamavacanāt | amamaḥ putrādimamatvarahitaḥ | aparigrahaḥ śāstroktadaṇḍakamaṇḍalvādivyatiriktacchatrādyupakaraṇarahitaś ca syāt | "saptāgārāṇy asaṃkalpitāni cared bhaikṣam | vidhūme sannamusala ekaśāṭīparihito 'jinena vā" | ekaśāṭīm ajinaṃ vā paridhāya dhūmābhāvamusalavyāpārābhāvopalakṣite 'parāhnātmake kāle sapta gṛhāṇy atra bhikṣitavyam iti, bhikṣālobhenāsaṃkalpitāni bhaikṣaṃ caret | "gopralūnais tṛṇair avastṛtaśarīraḥ sthaṇḍilaśāyī" | syād iti śeṣaḥ | gopralūnāni gavārthaṃ chinnāni | avastṛtaśarīra ācchāditaśarīraḥ | sthaṇḍilaśāyī, adhaḥ śāyī | "anityāṃ vasatiṃ vaset | grāmantare śūnyagṛhe devāgāre vṛkṣamūle vā manasā jñānam adhīyānaḥ" | manasaiva jñānaṃ brahmajñānasādhanam upaniṣadgrantham adhīyāno grāmādiṣv ekatrāniyatadeśāṃ vasatim avasthānaṃ vaset kuryāt | "na grāmyapaśūnāṃ saṃdarśane vicaret" | araṇye vicared iti tātparyārthaḥ | [956] "athāpy udāharanti,
araṇyanityasya jitendriyasya sarvendriyaprītinivartakasya |
adhyātmacintāgatamānasasya dhruvā hy anāvṛttir upekṣakasya ||
nityam araṇye vasato vaśīkṛtendriyasya kāmāpannam apīndriyaprītikaraṃ rāgodayaśaṅkayā varjayata ātmānam adhikṛtya pravṛttasya śāstrasyārthacintāṃ prati pravaṇamānasasyopekṣakasya sarvavastuṣu heyopādeyabuddhirahitasyānāvṛttir muktir dhruvā niyatā | "avyaktaliṅgo vyaktācāro 'nunmatta unmattaveśena yuktaḥ" | avyaktaṃ gūḍhaṃ yogasiddhiliṅgaṃ yasya sa tathoktaḥ | vyakācāro yathoktaśaucādyārāraḥ anunmatto 'py unmattaveśena yuktaḥ pūjādibhayāt syāt | "athāpy udāharanti,
na śabdaśāstrābhiratasya mokṣo na cāpi loke(ka)grahaṇe ratasya |
na bhojanācchādanagarvitasya na cātiramyāvasathapriyasya ||"
śabdaśāstrajñānam eva paramaṃ prayojanam ity abhiniveśino lokapaṅktau ratasya ca bhojanādiślāghino ramyaṃ nivāsam icchataś ca na mokṣalābhaḥ | kasya tarhi sa ity ata āha |
na kuṭyāṃ nodake saṅgo na caile na tripuṣkare |
nāgāre nāsane nānne yasya vai mokṣavit tu saḥ || iti |
kuṭyādiṣu yatidravyeṣu yasya na saṅgo na tatparatā, sa mokṣavin mokṣalābhabhāg bhaved ity arthaḥ | bhikṣāṭanāsamarthaṃ praty anugraham āha | "brāhmaṇakule vā yal labheta tad bhuñjīta sāyaṃ prātar madhumāṃsavarjam" | vyādhyādinā bhikṣāyām asamartho brāhmaṇagṛhe yal labheta tan madhumāṃsavarjaṃ sāyaṃ prātar vā bhuñjīta | asamartha ubhayatra | asamarthasyaiva sāyam anugrahaḥ | madhu māṃsaṃ ca śrāddhe 'pi varjayet | anugrahe 'py atiprasaktivāraṇārtham āha: "na ca tṛpyet" | prāṇayātrikamātraṃ bhuñjītety arthaḥ | aparam anugraham āha: "grāme vā vaset" | kiṃ ca "ajihmo 'śaṭho 'saṃkusukaḥ" | syād iti śeṣaḥ | jihmaḥ kuṭilaḥ | śaṭho māyāvī | saṃkusukaḥ saṃcayavān | evaṃrūpo na syād iti śeṣaḥ | "na cendriyasaṃsargaṃ kurvīta kenacit" | [957] indriyaṃ prajananaṃ tatsaṃyogaṃ kenacit saha na kuryāt | api ca: "upekṣakaḥ sarvabhūtānām" | sarvabhūteṣu rāgadveṣādirahito bhaved ity arthaḥ | "pauśunamatsarābhimānanamasyāhaṃkārāśraddhānarjavātmasaṃstaparagarhādambhalobhavarjanaṃ sarvāśramāṇāṃ dharmiṣṭham" | paiśunaṃ doṣasūcakatvam | matsaraḥ parasamṛddhyasahanam | abhimānaḥ stambhaḥ | namasyā nāma praṇāma iti yāvat | ahaṃkāro 'bhijanaprajñādigarvaḥ | aśraddhā paralokādiṣu nāstikatvam | anārjavaṃ māyāvitā | ātmastavaḥ svaguṇakīrtanam | paragarhā parāpavādaḥ | dambhaḥ sopadhaṃ dharmacaraṇam | lobho vidhyatikrameṇa dravyasyādānam apradānaṃ ca | eṣāṃ varjanaṃ pariharaṇaṃ sarveṣām āśramāṇāṃ dharmāntrebhya utkṛṣṭam | "yajñopavīty udakakamaṇḍaluhastaḥ śucir brāhmaṇo vṛṣalānnavarjī" | yajñopavītī parivrājakaḥ syāt | na punaḥ sarvāśramāṇāṃ yajñopavītīti saṃbandho yujyate | na ca sarvāśramaśabdaḥ prathamāntatayā vipariṇamayya saṃbandhanīyaḥ | parivrājaka ity avipariṇatasyaiva sākṣāt prakṛtatvāt | tathā ca yajñopavītam āśramāntarāṇām anena [na] vidheyam, yajñopavītītyādibhiḥ svayam eva vinitatvāt | tathā sarvāśramāṇāṃ yajñopavītavidhānena parivrājakasyāpi yajñopavītitvaṃ labhyate | atah matam "nakān nikṛtya yajñopavītaṃ visṛjet" iti jābālaśrutivihitena yajñopavitavisargeṇa viśeṣavihitena sāmānyavihitaṃ yajñopavītaṃ bādhyata it | satyam, yadi "nakhāni nikṛtya purāṇavastraṃ yajñopavītaṃ kamaṇḍaluṃ ca tyaktvā navāni gṛhītvā grāmaṃ praviśet" iti vacanāntaraṃ na syāt | kiṃ ca ṣaṣṭhībahuvacanāntaḥ sarvāśramaśabdaḥ prathamāntena vipariṇato 'pi bahuvacanānta eva bhaved iti naikavacanāntena parivrājakaśabdena samānādhikaraṇo bhavati, tasmāt prakṛtena prathamaikavacanāntena parivrājakaśabdena yajñopavītīti saṃbadhyate | udakakamaṇḍalur udakapūrṇakamaṇḍalus taddhastaḥ | śuciḥ prātisvikaśaucavān | vṛṣalānnavarjīti brāhmaṇānnalābhe kṣatriyavaiśyānnānugrahārtham | brāhmaṇagrahaṇaṃ dvijapradarśanārtham | [958] pārivrājyaphalam āha: "na hīyate brahmalokāt" | brahmaiva loko brahmalokaḥ | āpastambaḥ: "atha parivrājo 'ta eva brahmacaryavān parivrajati" | eṣaṇātrayaṃ parityajya yo vrajati sa parivrāṭ | tasya dharmā vakṣyante — ata evācāryakulavāsād brahmacaryavān aviplutabrahmacaryaḥ pravrajati | "tasyopadiśanti | anagnir aniketanaḥ syād aśarmāśaraṇo muniḥ svādhyāya evotsṛjamāno vācam" | avidyamāno 'gnir yasyāsvāv anagniḥ | anena ca laukikavaidikāgnikāryaṃ pākahomādikaṃ nivāryate | aniketano niyatasthānarahitaḥ | aśarmā sukhārthodyamarahitaḥ | aśaraṇo nirgṛhaḥ | munir vācaṃyamaḥ syād ity arthaḥ | svādhyāye 'karmārhamantraprayoge vācam utsṛjamānaḥ | brahamayajñādhyayanaṃ tu yater nāśti | "apa upaspṛśya gṛhān eti | tato yat kiṃcid dadāti sā dakṣiṇā" ity arthaḥ | "grāme prāṇavṛttiṃ pratilabhyānīho 'namutraś caret" | prāṇasthitimātraprayojanaṃ bhakṣaṃ grāme pratilabhya niḥspṛhaḥ paralokaphalakarmarahito mahīṃ vicaret | "tasya muktam ācchādanaṃ vihitam" | muktaṃ vastrasvāminā paridhānāyogyatvāt tyaktam | atraikeṣāṃ matam upanyasyati: "sarvataḥ parimokṣam eke" | sarvasya parimokṣaṃ tyāgam eke vādino manyante | tam evāha: "satyānṛte sukhaduḥkhe vedān imaṃ lokam amuṃ ca parityajyātmānam eva budhyed buddhe kṣemaprāpaṇam" | satyaṃ bhrūyāt kvacic ca viṣaye 'nṛtam api brūyād ityādividhivihitaṃ satyam asatyam, sukhaduḥkhopāyau vedavihitāṃś ca sakalān arthān parityajyātmānam eva budhyet (ta) | yatas tasmin buddhe 'vidyānivṛttirūpasya kṣemasya prāptiḥ | tad dūṣayitum āha: "tad etac chāstair vipratiṣiddham" | tad etat paramataṃ śāstraiḥ śrutismṛtivākyair vipratiṣiddhaṃ viruddhaṃ bādhitam iti [959] yāvat | tathā hi — vidyāparaparyāyasyātmabodhasyāvaśyakavarṇāśrama-rahitasya kṣemaprāptir na phalaṃ kiṃ tu pratyavāya ity āha mādhyandinī śrutiḥ:
andhaṃ tamaḥ praviśanti ye vidyām upāsate |
tato bhūya iva te tamo ye 'vidyāyāṃ ratāḥ || iti |
asyārthaḥ — ye mūḍhacetaso vidyām ātmajñānam upekṣyāvidyāyāṃ karmaṇy eva ratās te 'ndhaṃ tamaḥ praviśanti, ye nityanaimittakakarmarahitāyāṃ vidyāyām ātmajñāne ratās te 'pi |
evaṃ kevalāṃ vidyāṃ kevalaṃ ca karma ninditvā tayor anyonyasumuccitayor mokṣopāyatvaṃ vidhatte śrutiḥ |
vidyāṃ cāvidyāṃ ca yas tad vedobhayaṃ saha |
avidyayā mṛtyuṃ tīrtvā vidyayāmṛtam aśnute || iti |
vidyām ātmopāsanām avidyāṃ varṇāśramadharmajātam anyonyasamucchitam etad ubhayaṃ yo mokṣopāyatvena veda jānāti nāsāv andhatamaḥ praviśati | kiṃ vidyākarmaṇoḥ samuccaya ādhānottarakratuvat | kiṃ vāgneyāgnīṣomīyādivat | yad vāṅgapradhānavad iti vimarśe nirṇayārtham uktam: "avidyayā mṛtyuṃ tīrtvā vidayāmṛtam aśnute" iti | vidyayā sākṣād amṛtaṃ mokṣam aśnute prāpnoti | kiṃ tu mṛtyuṃ mṛtyutulyaṃ mokṣavighnaṃ tīrtvā vidyayātmajñānenāmṛtaṃ prāpnoti, na punaḥ satkarmanibandhanaṃ vighnopaśamam antareṇaiva vidyayā mokṣaṃ kaś cid āpnoti | tataś cāmṛtaprāptikāraṇabhūtāyā vidyāyā vighnataraṇarūpam upakāraṃ janayadbhiḥ karmabhiḥ saha vidyāyā aṅgād vilakṣaṇaḥ samuccaya iti siddham | na ca vācyaṃ karmaṇāṃ mṛtyutaraṇa eva hetubhāvo na tu mokṣe, tena tāni vidyāṅgānīti | tathā hi sati avidyayā mṛtyuṃ tarati vidyayāmṛtam aśnuta ity arthadvayaparatve vākyadvayabhedaḥ syād ekaṃ cedaṃ vākyaṃ ktvāpratyayasāmarthyād gamyate | ekakartṛkatāṃ hi mṛtyutaraṇāmṛtaprāptyoḥ sa brūte | na ca nimittanaimittikabhāvam antareṇa sā tayoḥ saṃbhavati | tasmād vidyāṅgatayā karmaṇāṃ mokṣaṃ prati nimittabhāvo 'tra vidhīyate | yāni tu, "na karmaṇā na prajayā dhanena tyāgenaike amṛtatvam ānaśuḥ" [960] ityādīni karmaṇāṃ mokṣaṃ prati nimittabhāvaniṣedhakāni tāni kevalakarmaviṣayāṇīty avirodhaḥ | yata eva mokṣaṃ prati karmaṇāṃ nimittabhāvo 'sty ata eva "ya ātmānam eva kokam upāste | nehāsya karma kṣīyate" iti karmaṇo 'kṣayatvaṃ pratipādayad vākyasya mokṣopāyatvaṃ gamayati | akṣayaphalatvena hi karmaṇo 'kṣayatvaṃ na svabhāvataḥ kṣayitvāt | ata eva "vividiṣanti yajñena dānena tapasānāśakena" iti mokṣopāyabhūtavidyāṅgatāṃ tṛtīyāśrutiḥ pratipādayati | na ca vācyaṃ sanpratyayavācyecchāṅgatvena tṛtīyā karmaṇāṃ viniyoktrīti, yataḥ pratyayārthībhūtecchāṃ pratīpsitakarmatvena sannanteṣu dhātvarthaḥ pratīyata iti sa eva pradhānam | ataḥ sa eva padārthāntaraiḥ saṃbadhyate | yathā — aśvena jigamiṣatīty aśvasya karaṇabhāvo gamane pratīyate, na tadicchāyām | spaṣṭaṃ ca jñānakarmaṇoḥ samuccaye(yaṃ) mokṣaviṣayam āha "tenaiti brahmavit taijasaḥ puṇyakṛc ca" iti |
etāva chrutivākyāni kevalabuddhikṣemaprāpakatvapakṣapratikṣepakāṇy udāhṛtāni | atha smṛtivākyāny udāhriyante, tatra manuḥ |
tapo vidyā ca viprasya niḥśreyasakaraṃ param |
tapasā kalmaṣaṃ hanti vidyayāmṛtam aśnute ||
ahiṃsayendriyāsaṅgair vaidikaiś caiva karmabhiḥ |
tapasaś caraṇaiś cograiḥ sādayantīha tat param ||
yogayājñavalkyaḥ |
svakarmaṇām anuṣṭhānāt samyagātmanidarśanāt |
vedāntānāṃ parijñānād gṛhastho 'pi vimucyate ||
apiśabdād anye 'py āśramiṇa evaṃ vimucyanta iti gamayati | mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe |
vihitākaraṇāt puṃbhir atyantaṃ kriyate tu yaḥ |
saṃyamo muktaye mohāt pratyutādhogatipradaḥ ||
prakṣālayāmīti bhavān vatsātmānaṃ tu manyate ||
vihitākaraṇodbhūtaiḥ pāpais tvaṃ na vidahmase (?) |
avidyāpy upakārāya viṣavaj jāyate nṛṇām ||
anuṣṭhitāpy upāyena bandhāya(yā)nyāyato 'pi vā || [961]
pulastyaḥ |
jñānakarmasamāyogāt paraṃ prāpnoti pūruṣaḥ |
pṛthagbhāvān na sidhyanti ubhe tasmāt samāśrayet ||
jñānaṃ pradhānaṃ na tu karmahīnaṃ karma pradhānaṃna tu buddhihīnam |
tasmād ubhābhyāṃ tu bhavet prasiddhir na hy ekapakṣo vihagaḥ prayāti ||
vyāsaḥ |
yogasaṃnyastakarmāṇaṃ jñānasṃchinnasaṃśayam |
ātmavantaṃ na karmāṇi nibadhnanti dhamaṃjaya ||
brahmaṇyād ā(dhā)ya karmāṇi saṅgaṃ tyaktvā karoti yaḥ |
na sa lipyeta pāpena padmapatram ivāmbhasā |
yuktaḥ karmaphalaṃ tyaktvā śāntim āpnoti naiṣṭhikīm ||
ayuktaḥ kāmakāreṇa phale sakto nibadhyate |
anāśritaḥ karmaphalaṃ kāryaṃ karma karoti yaḥ ||
sa saṃnyāsī ca yogī ca na niragnir na cākriyaḥ |
yat karoṣi yad aśnāsi yaj juhoti dadāsi yat ||
yat tapasyasi kaunteya tat kuruṣva madarpaṇam |
brahmārpaṇaṃ brahma havir brahmāgnau brahmaṇa hutam ||
brahmaiva yena gantavyaṃ brahmakarmasamādhinā |
havir arpyate prakṣipyate srugādinā yena tad arpaṇam | kārakeṣu kriyāyāṃca brahmabuddhikaraṇam anena vidhīyate | brahmātmakaṃ karma yaḥ samādadhāti budhyate sa brahmakarmasamādhiḥ | viṣṇudharme 'śvinau |
dve karmaṇī naraśreṣṭha brahmaṇā samudāhṛte |
pravṛttaṃ ca nivṛttaṃca svargamuktiphale hi te ||
pravṛttam api mokṣāya karma pārthiva jāyate |
karma svarūpato bhraṣṭam anākāṅkṣiphalaṃ smṛtam ||
viṣṇuḥ |
evaṃ jñānavatāṃ karma kurvato 'pi prajādikam |
bhaven muktir dvijaśreṣṭha raumyasya vacanaṃ yathā ||
anirvāto yathā vahnir dhmāyamānaḥ praṇaśyati |
tathā karmavihīnasya jñānāgniḥ saṃpraṇaśyati ||
tasmād āśramibhiḥ sarvair nityaṃ yajñopavītibhiḥ |
nityanaimittikaṃ karma kāle kāryam atandritaiḥ || [962]
hārītaḥ |
yathāśvā rathahīnās tu ratho vāśvair vinā yathā |
evaṃ tapo 'py avidyasya vidyā vāpy atapasvinaḥ ||
yathānnaṃ madhusaṃyuktaṃ madhu cānnena saṃyutam |
evaṃ tapaś ca vidyā ca saṃyuktaṃ bheṣajaṃ mahat ||
evaṃ śāstrapratiṣedha ātmabodhamātrasya kṣemaprāpakatvapakṣe 'bhihita idānīṃ pramāṇāntaravipratiṣedham āha: "buddhe cet kṣemaprapaṇam ihaiva duḥkhaṃ nopalabheta" | ātmani buddha eva cen muktis tarhīhaiva śarīre mriyamāṇe kim api duḥkhaṃ nopalabheta | samuccayapakṣe tu yāvajjīvam anuṣṭhiteṣ karmasu muktir iti na jīvatas tatsaṃbandhaḥ | tathāh baudhāyanaḥ: "parivrājakaḥ parityajya bandhūn aparigrahaḥ parivrajed araṇyaṃ gatvā śikhī muṇḍo vā kaupīnācchādanaḥ kāṣāyavāsāḥ" | kaṣāyo vṛkṣarasas tena raktaṃ kāṣāyam | "vāṅmanaḥkarmadaṇḍair bhūtānām adrohī pavitrabhṛc chaucārtham uddhṛtābhir adbhiḥ kāryaṃ kurvāṇaḥ" | kāryaṃ malakṣālanam | ācamanaṃ tv anuddhṛtābhir eva || 3.57 ||
bhikṣārthaṃ grāmam āśrayed ity uktam, tatra yathā bhikṣārjanaṃ kāryaṃ tad āha |
apramattaś cared bhaikṣaṃ sāyāhne 'nabhilakṣitaḥ |
rahite bhikṣukair grāme yātrāmātram alolupaḥ || 3.58 ||
apramatta ādaravān | anabhilakṣito bhikṣādātṛbhir guṇitvenānabhijñātaḥ | alolupaḥ svādvannalobharahitaḥ | bhikṣukair anyair brahmacāriprabhṛtibhir gṛhītabhikṣair vimukte grāme prāṇadhāraṇaprayojanaṃ bhaikṣyaṃ(kṣaṃ) sāyāhne 'hno 'ntime muhūrtatraye caret | atra vasiṣṭhaḥ: "saptāgārāṇy asaṃkalpitāni cared bhaikṣam ekaśāṭīparihito 'jinena vā vidhūme sannamusale" | vidhūme sannamusala iti sāyaṃkālopalakṣaṇam | sannaṃ musalam acalaṃ yasmin kāle sa tathoktaḥ | [963] manuḥ |
ekakālaṃ cared bhaikṣaṃ na prasajyeta vistare |
bhaikṣaprasakto hi yatir viṣayeṣv api majjati ||
alābhe na viṣādī syāl lābhaś cainaṃ na harṣayet |
prāṇayātrikamātraḥ syān mātrāsaṅgād vinirgataḥ ||
upakaraṇadravyaṃ mātrā |
abhipūjitalābhāṃś ca jugupsetaiva sarvaśaḥ |
abhipūjitalābhāt tu yatir mukto 'pi badhyate ||
abhipūjitāḥ satkārapūrvakā lābhāḥ | tathā |
na cotpātanimittābhyāṃ na nakṣatrāṅgavidyayā |
nānuśāsanavādābhyāṃ bhikṣāṃ lipseta karhicit ||
utpāto 'dbhutadarśanam | nimittaṃ śakunādi | aṅgaṃ vyākaraṇādi | sāmudraṃ hastalakṣaṇādi vā | saṃvartaḥ |
aṣṭau bhikṣāḥ samādāya sa muniḥ sapta pañca vā |
adbhiḥ prakṣālya tat sarvaṃ bhuñjīta susamāhitaḥ ||
araṇye nirjane vipraḥ punar āsīta bhuktavān |
ekākī cintayen nintyaṃ manovākkāyasaṃyutaḥ ||
yamaḥ |
baikṣabhug brahmacārī syān naikānnāśī bhavet kvacit |
etac ca sati sāmarthye | asāmarthye tu "brāhmaṇakule vā yal labheta" ityādikaṃ yad uktaṃ vasiṣṭhena tad draṣṭavyam | brāhmaṇakulālābhe śūdrānnavarjaṃ varṇāntarānnam api bhuñjīta | tad āha sa eva "vṛṣalānnavarjī" iti | hārītaḥ: "āhāraśuddhau sattvaśuddhir ity ācāryāḥ" | sattvam antaḥkaraṇam | baudhāyanaḥ: "atha bhaikṣacaryā, brāhmaṇānāṃ śālīnayāyāvarāṇām anivṛtte vaiśvadeve bhikṣāṃ lipseta bhavatpūrvaṃ pracodayet | godohamātram ākāṅkṣeta bhaikṣacaryād upāvṛttaḥ śucau deśe nyasya hastapādaṃ prakṣālyācamyādityasyāgre nivedayati ud u tyaṃ citram iti | brahmaṇe nivedayati brahma jajñānam iti vijñāyate | ādhānaprabhṛti yajamāna evāgnayo bhavanti tasya prāṇo gārhapatyo 'pāno 'nvāhāryapacano vyāna [964] āhavanīya udānasamānau sabhyāvasthyau pañca vā ete 'gnaya ātmasthā ātmany eva juhoti sa eva ātmayajñaniṣṭha ātmapratiṣṭha ātmānaṃ kṣemaṃ nayatīti vijñāyatre | nakṣatrodayāt pūrvaṃ vibhajya śeṣam adbhiḥ saṃsṛjyauṣadhavat prāśnīyāt | prāśyāpa ācamya jyotiṣmatyādityam upatiṣṭhate, ud vayaṃ tamasas parīti | vāṅma āsanno prāṇa iti japitvā,
ayācitam asaṃkḷptam upapannaṃ yadṛcchayā |
āhāramātraṃbhuñjīta kevalaṃ prāṇayātrikam ||
devalaḥ: "nityaṃ pratyāvṛtta āditye nivṛttam up(sa)loḍy(dya)me praśāntadhūme kāle grāmaṃ praviśya bhaikṣārthaṃ sarvataḥ paryaṭann agrato yugamātrā(ma)valokya pūrvasaṃkalpitāni nivivṛttadvārāṇi paragṛhāṇy avacanenāśaktaḥ praviśet | praviṣṭaḥ saṃjalpapraṇayakuhakavismayaviḍambaparihāsayācanaprekṣitāni pariharet | godohamātrām sthitvā vrajed bhikṣālabdhau an kupyed antyāvasāyiparipanthijñātīśvarapretasūtakabhikṣāṃ nopalabdhā(bdhrā)prahitāṃ gṛhṇīyād ātmanaḥ saṃskṛtāṃ parabādhākarīṃ varjayet | madhumāṃsakubījavirahitāṃ gṛhītvā tad bhaikṣam ekānte tenaiva pātreṇānyena vā tūṣṇīṃ bhūtvā mātrayā bhuñjīta" || 3.58 ||
yatipātrāṇi mṛdveṇudārvalābumayāni ca |
salilaṃ śuddhir eteṣāṃ govālaiś cāvagharṣaṇam || 3.59 ||
yatīnāṃ bhikṣābhojanārthāni pātrāṇi mṛdādimayāni bhavanti | teṣam ucchiṣṭānāṃ salilaṃ govālagharṣaṇaṃ ca śuddhihetuḥ | manuḥ |
ataijasāni pātrāṇi tasya syur nivraṇāni ca |
teṣām adbhiḥ smṛtaṃ śaucaṃ camasānām ivādhvare ||
hārītaḥ: "mṛddāruvidalālābuśīrṇaparṇapātro vā pāṇipātro vā bhikṣārthaṃ grāmaṃ praveśen nocchiṣṭaṃ dadyān notsṛjet na kutsayet | nātimātram aśnīyāt"  [965]| śaṅkhalikhitau: "kāṣṭhālābuvidalapārthivānām ekaṃ bhikṣabhājanam | tasya govālarajjvā sodakaṃ parigharṣaṇaṃ pratyātmakaṃ śaucam | nātrācamen na bhūmau nikṣipen nāśuci saṃspṛśed uktvācāntaḥ punar ācamya dravyāṇy abhyukṣed rajjuṃ yac cānyad aviruddhaṃ syāt" | nṛsiṃhapurāṇe |
sthityartham ātmano nityaṃ bhikṣāṭanam athācaret |
sāyāhnakāle viprāṇāṃ gṛhāṇi vicared yatiḥ ||
udghāṭayec ca kavacaṃ dakṣiṇena kareṇa vai |
pātraṃ vāmakare sthāpya dakṣiṇenāvaśeṣayet ||
syād vṛttir yāvatānnena tāvad bhaikṣyaṃ(kṣaṃ) samācaret |
tato nirvartya(vṛtya) tat pātraṃ saṃsthāpyācamya saṃyamī ||
caturaṅgulaiḥ prakṣāḷya grāsamātraṃ samāhitaḥ |
sarvavyañjanasaṃyuktaṃ pṛthakpātre nivedayet ||
sūryādidevabhūtebhyo datvānnaṃ prokṣya vāriṇā |
bhuñjīta parṇapuṭake pātre vā vāgyato yatiḥ ||
vaṭārkāśvatthaparṇeṣu kumbhītindukaparṇayoḥ |
kovidārakarañjeṣu na bhuñjīta kadācana ||
samalāḥ sarva ucyante yatayaḥ kāṃsyabhojinaḥ |
kāṃsyakasya tu yat pātraṃ gṛhasthasya tathaiva ca ||
kāṃsyabhojī yatiḥ sarvaṃ prāpnuyāt kilbiṣaṃ tayoḥ |
bhuktvā pātraṃ yatir nityaṃ kṣālayen mantrapūrvakam ||
na dūṣyetāsya tat pātraṃ yajñeṣu camasā iva |
athācamya niruddhāsur upatiṣṭheta bhāskaram ||
japadhyānetihāseṣu dinaśeṣaṃ nayed budhaḥ |
kṛtasaṃdhyas tato rātriṃ nayed devagṛhādiṣu ||
hṛtpuṇḍarīkanilaye dhyātvātmānam akalmaṣam |
yatir dharmarataḥ śāntaḥ sarvabhūtasamo vaśī ||
prāpnoti paramaṃ sthānaṃ yat prāpya na nivartate || 3.59 ||
[966]
saṃnirudhyendriyagrāmaṃ rāgadveṣau vihāya ca |
bhayaṃ hitvā ca bhūtānām amṛtī bhavati dvijaḥ || 3.60 ||
indriyavargaṃ saṃnirudhya niṣayāpravaṇaṃ kṛtvā tathendriyāṇāṃ viṣayonmukhatvapravṛttir ahitatve 'pi tadviṣayagocararāgadveṣasadbhāve 'mṛtībhāvānupapatter ity abhipretyoktam "rāgadveṣau vihāya ca" iti | tathā ca śrutiḥ |
yadā sarve pramucyante kāmā ye 'sya hṛdi sthitāḥ |
atha martyo 'mṛto bhavaty atra brahma samaśnute || iti |
tathā: "svahetukaṃ bhūtānāṃ bhayam apahṛtyānutpādya dvijo 'mṛtī bhavaty apavṛjyate" | atra ca dvijagrahaṇaṃ vivakṣitārtham | na brāhmaṇopalakṣaṇārtham | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
ṛṇatrayam apākṛtya nirmamo nirahaṃkṛtiḥ |
brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyo vāpi vaiśyo vā pravrajed gṛhāt || iti || 3.60 ||
kiṃ ca |
kartavyāśayasuddhis tu bhikṣukeṇa viśeṣataḥ |
jñānotpattinimittatāt svātantryakaraṇāya ca || 3.61 ||
āśayasyāntaḥkaraṇasya śuddhir adoṣatā paramātmajñānotpattyartham ātmanaś ca svatantrabhāvāya rāgadveṣamohādhīnatvanirāsarūpā sarvair āśramibhiḥ kāryā | viśeṣatas tu bhikṣukeṇa yatinā | atriḥ |
paraḥ samādhilābho hi nāśuddhair iha labhyate |
viśuddhaye 'taḥ kartavyo bhāvo brahma bubhutsubhiḥ ||
japena sādhayet pūrvaṃ prāṇāyāmaiś ca śaktitaḥ |
tataḥ śuddhātmano brahma prakāśam upagacchati ||
prāṇāyāmais tu saṃśuddho japed brahma prayatnataḥ |
vedābhyāso hi viprasya tapaḥ param ihocyate ||
praṇavavyāhṛtimatīṃ gāyatrīṃ jpatas tathā |
na tasya kṛtyam astīha tasmin sarvaṃ samāpyate ||
abhyasyābdatrayaṃ japyaṃ śuddhabhāvo dvijottamaḥ |
saṃyamya cendriyagrāmam ataḥ praṇavam abhyaset ||
[967]        ānandaṃ sarvagaṃ brahma kūṭasthaṃ kevalaṃ dvijaḥ |
nātaḥ parataraṃ brahma nāśuddhair iha labhyate ||
apy abdaṃ japyam abhyasya tataḥ praṇavam abhyaset |
aśuddhātmā japann enaḥ(enaṃ) pratyavaiti na saṃśayaḥ ||
nāmaitad brahmaṇaḥ sākṣāt sarvasmād vīryavattaram |
anenoccāryamāṇena brahmābhimukhatām iyāt ||
avajñāto yathācāryaḥ śuśrūṣum api nāśayet |
anapāyopacaritas tathaiva paramaḥ pumān ||
tenāviśuddho 'śraddhālur nābhyaset praṇavaṃ dvijaḥ |
aśraddhālur aśuddhaś ca yāty adhogatim abhyasan ||
brahmacaryā tapaḥśuddhā nityam ekāntaśīlatā |
viṣayeṣv aspṛhā sthairyaṃ sarvabhūtadayālutā ||
kṣamā dhṛtiḥ śucitvaṃ ca nirodho manasaś ca yaḥ |
etair upāyaiḥ saṃyuktas tataḥ praṇavam abhyaset ||
yamaḥ |
yathā parvatadhātūnāṃ dhmāsyatāṃ dahyate rajaḥ |
indriyāṇāṃ tathā doṣaṃ prāṇāyāmair vinirdahet || 3.61 ||
saṃsāravairāgyād ātmayāthātmyaṃ vijñānāc ca muktur uktā yogaśāstreṣu | tatra vairāgyopāyaṃ tāvad āha |
avekṣyā garbhavāsaś ca karmajā gatayas tathā |
ādhayo vyādhayaḥ kleśā jarā rūpaviparyayaḥ || 3.62 ||
bhavo jātisahasreṣu priyāpriyaviparyayaḥ |
avekṣyās tattvataḥ paribhāvanīyāḥ | garbhavāse 'tyantasaṃkaṭe mātur jaṭhare mūtrapurīṣapūrṇa ulbajarāyubhyāṃ piṇḍataḥ sarvāvayavasya gāḍhabaddhasya kṣaṇamātram apy asahyāṃ vedanām anubhavato daśa māsān yāvadgarbhavāsaḥ | tathā jananyā svecchayā kriyamāṇair āhāravihāraviśeṣair garbhasya duḥsaho duḥkhanivaho janyata ity anumātavyam | prasavasamayabhāvinā ca duḥkhena maraṇaduḥkhaṃ na viśiṣyate | tathā pāpakarmajā gatayo narakeṣu patanāni śāstrato jñeyāni | ādhayo duḥkhapradā manovyāpārāḥ | vyādhayo jvarādayaḥ | kleśā avidyāsmitārāgadveṣābhiniveśāḥ | jarā [968] vayovaśāc charīrajarjaratā | rūpaviparyayaḥ surūpasya kurūpatā | kutsiteṣu śvasūkarādijātisahasreṣu bhavo janma | priyasyālābho labdhasya nāśo 'priyasya prāptir avināśaś cetyādayo 'rthā avekṣyās tattvataḥ paribhāvanīyāḥ | manuḥ |
avekṣeta gatīr nṝṇāṃ kāmadoṣasamudbhavāḥ |
niraye caiva patanaṃ yātanāś ca yamakṣaye ||
viprayogaṃ priyaiś caiva saṃprayogaṃ tathāpriyaiḥ |
jarayā cābhibhavanaṃ vyādhibhiś copapīḍanam ||
dehād utkramaṇaṃ cāsmāt punar garbhe ca saṃbhavaḥ |
yonikoṭisahasreṣu sṛtīś cāsyāntarātmanaḥ ||
adharmaprabhavaṃ caiva duḥkhayogaṃ śarīriṇām |
dharmārthaprabhavaṃ caiva sukhasaṃyogam akṣayam ||
deheṣu caivopapattim uttameṣv adhameṣu ca |
uccāvaceṣu bhūteṣu durjñānam akṛtātmabhiḥ ||
yamakṣaye yamagṛhe | śaṅkhaḥ |
aśucitvaśucitvasya priyāpriyaviparyayān |
garbhavāse ca vasatiṃ paśyen mucyeta nānyathā ||
paśyed ity anuvṛttau viṣṇuḥ: "janmasamaye tu yonisaṃkaṭanirgamān mahāduḥkhyānubhavanaṃ bālye mohaṃ guruvaśyatām adhyayanādānakleśaṃ yauvane ca viṣayāprāptau | amārgeṇa tadavāptau viṣayasevanān narakapatanam" | mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe |
putra uvāca |
niṣekam ārtave strīṇāṃ bījaprāpte rajaḥsthitim |
vimuktamātro narakāt svargād vāpi prapadyate ||
nābhibhūtaṃ tataḥ sthairyaṃ yāti bījadvayam pitaḥ |
kalakatvaṃ budbudatvaṃ tataḥ pepi(śī)tvam eva ca ||
peśyā yathā tu bījasya aṅkurādeḥ samudbhavaḥ |
aṅgānāṃ tadvad utpattiḥ pañcānām avibhāgaśaḥ ||
upāṅgāny aṅgulīnetranāsāsyaśravaṇāni ca |
prarohaṃ yāti cāṅgebhyas tadvat tebhyo nakhālakam ||
[969]        samaṃ sa vṛddhim āyāti tenaivodbhavakośakaḥ |
nālikerīphalaṃ yadvat sakośaṃ vṛddhim ṛcchati ||
tadvat prayāty asau vṛddhiṃ sa kośo 'dhomukhasthitaḥ |
evaṃ vṛddhiṃ kramād yāti jantuḥ strīgarbhasaṃsthitaḥ ||
tale tu jānupārśvābhyāṃ karau saṃnyasya vardhate |
aṅguṣṭhau copari nyasya jānvor agre tathāṅgulīḥ ||
jānupṛṣṭhe tathā netre jānumadhye ca nāsikām |
sphijai(cau) pārśvadvayasthau ca jānumadhye vyavsthitau ||
evaṃ vṛddhiṃ kramād yāti jantuḥ strīgarbhasaṃsthitaḥ |
adhyāsyāmbodaraṃ jantur yathārūpaṃ yathāsthiti ||
kāṭhinyam agninā yāti bhuktapītena jīvati |
puṇyāpuṇyāśayamayī sthitir jantos tathodare ||
nāḍir āpyayanī nāma nābhyāṃ tasya nibadhyate |
strīṇāṃ tathāsyasuśi(ṣi)re sā nibaddhopajāyate ||
krāmanti bhuktapītāni strīṇāṃ garbhodare tathā |
tathāpyāyitadeho 'sau jantur vṛddhim upaiti vai ||
smṛtiṃ tatra prayānty asya bahvyaḥ saṃsārabhūmayaḥ |
tato nirvedam āyāti pīḍyamānas tatas tataḥ ||
punar naivaṃ kariṣyāmi muktamātra ihodarāt |
tathā tathā kariṣyāmi garbhaṃ nāpsyāmy ahaṃ yathā ||
iti cintayate smṛtvā janmaduḥkhaśatāni vai |
yāni pūrvānubhūtāni devabhūtātmajāni vai ||
tataḥ kālakramāj jantuḥ parivartaty adhomukhaḥ |
navame daśame vāpi māsi saṃjāyate punaḥ ||
niṣkramyamāṇo vātena prājāpatyena pīḍyate |
niṣkramyate ca vilapaṃs tadā duḥkhena pīḍitaḥ ||
niṣkrāntaś codarān mūrchām asahyāṃ pratipadyate |
prāpnoti cetanāṃ cāsau vāyusparśasukhānvitaḥ ||
[970]        tatas taṃ vaiṣṇavī māyā samāskandati mohinī |
tayā vimohitātmāsau jñānabhraṃśam avāpnute || 3.62 ||
vairāgyopāym uktvātmajñānopāyam āha |
dhyānayogena saṃdṛśyaḥ sūkṣma ātmātmani sthitaḥ || 3.63 ||
dhyānaṃ ca yogaś ca dhyānayogam, tenātmā kṣetrajñaḥ sūkṣmo durjñeya ātmani paramātmani tādātmyena vyavasthitaḥ saṃdṛśyaḥ samyag draṣṭavyaḥ | paramātmaivāham ity aparokṣīkāryaḥ | dhyānaṃ paramātmaivāham iti cintāsaṃtatiḥ | yogo viṣayāntarebhyaś cittavṛttinirodhaḥ | dakṣaḥ |
vṛttihīnaṃ manaḥ kṛtvā kṣetrajñaṃ paramātmani |
ekīkṛtya vimucyeta yogo 'yaṃ mukhya ucyate ||
sarvabhāvavinirmuktaṃ kṣetrajñaṃ brahmaṇi nyaset |
etad dhyānaṃ ca yogaś ca śeṣo yaṃ granthavistaraḥ ||
pulastyaḥ |
kutra tiṣṭhati govindo bāhyārthahṛtacetasām |
tasmān niḥsaṅgacittena śakyaś cintayituṃ hariḥ ||
dakṣaḥ |
na maunamantrakuhakair anekaiś ca śrutais tathā |
lokayātrābhiyuktasya yogo bhavati kasyacit ||
abhiyogāt tathābhyāsāt tasminn eva suniścayāt |
punaḥ punaś ca nirvedād yogaḥ sidhyati nānyathā ||
śaṅkhaḥ |
prāgrātrāpararātreṣu yogī nityam atandritaḥ |
dhyāyet tu puruṣaṃ viṣṇuṃ nirguṇaṃ pañcaviṃśakam ||
tathā: "praśāntātmā caturviṃśatyā tattvair vyatītaṃ cintayen nityam atīndriyam aguṇaṃ śabdasparśarūparasagandhātītaṃ sarvstham atisthūlaṃ sarvagatam atisūkṣmam | sarvataḥpāṇipādāntaṃ sarvato'kṣiśiromukham | sarvataḥ sarvendriyaśaktim evaṃ dhyāyet | saṃvatsareṇa yogāvirbhāvo bhavati |
vicāraś ca vivekaś ca vitarkaś copajāyate |
muneḥ samādadhānasya prathamaṃ dhyānam āditaḥ |
kaṇakulmāṣapiṇyākaśākayāvakasaktavaḥ |
tathā mūlaphalaṃ bhaikṣyaṃ(kṣaṃ) paryāyeṇopabhojayet ||
[971]        mahatas tapaso madhye sthitaṃ jvalanasaṃnibham |
viviktacārī laghvāśī tapasvī niyatendriyaḥ ||
jñānadagdhaparikleśaḥ prayogaratir ātmavān |
niṣpracāreṇa manasā paraṃ tad adhigacchati ||
dattātreyaḥ |
nirvāte vijane sthāne śubhe caiva manorame |
nirjantuke nirābādhe śarkarāvālukādibhiḥ ||
