śabdādivarjite sthāne śubhaṃ kṛtvā tu saṃstaram |
baddhvā sukhāsanaṃ tatra prāṅmukho vāpy udaṅmukhaḥ ||
kṛtvā trir unnataṃ dehaṃ suśamaṃ yogavittamaḥ |
namaskṛtya mahādevaṃ mahāyogīśvaraṃ harim ||
śodhayitvā purā nāḍīḥ prāṇāyāṃś caret tataḥ |
prāṇāyāmāṃś caret tāvad yāvac cittaṃ prasīdati || 3.63 ||
kiṃ ca |
nāśramaḥ kāraṇaṃ dharme kriyamāṇo bhaved dhi saḥ |
ato yad ātmano 'pathyaṃ pareṣāṃ na tad ācaret || 3.64 |
parivrājakāśramaṃ prakṛtyendriyajayarāgadveṣaparityāgabhūtābhayadānasaṃsāravairāgya-dhyānādinā jīvaparayos tādātmyadarśanarūpo dharmo 'mṛtībhāvahetur uktaḥ | tatra dharme na caturthāśrama eva kāraṇaṃ yasmād āśramāntarasthair api kriyamāṇaḥ sa dharmo bhaved eva | tato yaḥ kaścid atra dharme pravṛtta ātmano yad apathyaṃ duḥkhahetus tat pareṣāṃ bhūtānāṃ nācaret | anena ca bhūtāni praty abhayaṃkaratvam ucyate, tac ca pradarśanārtham | tenāmṛtībhāvahetava indriyasaṃnirodhādaya eva tad dharmapravṛttena kāryāḥ | manuḥ |
bhūmito 'pi cared dharmaṃ yatra tatrāśrame vasan |
samaḥ sarveṣu bhūteṣu na liṅgaṃ dharmakāraṇam ||
atriḥ |
evaṃ viṃśatitattvajñe yatra tatrāśrame vaset |
samaḥ sarveṣu bhūteṣu mucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
tāni ca tattvāni sa evāha |
pṛthivy āpas tathā tejo vāyur ākāśam eva ca |
pañcaitāni vijānīyān mahābhūtāni paṇḍitaḥ ||
[972]        śrotraṃ tvak cakṣuṣī jihvā nāsikā caiva pañcamī |
buddhīndriyāṇi jānīyāt pañcaivāsmiñ śarīrake ||
śabdaḥ sparśaś ca rūpaṃ ca raso gandhaś ca pañcamaḥ |
indriyārthān vijānīyāt pañcaiva satataṃ budhaḥ ||
hastau pādāv upasthaś ca jihvā pāyus tathaiva ca |
karmendriyāṇi pañcaiva nityam asmiñ śarīrake ||
mano buddhis tathaivātmā hy avyaktaṃ ca tathaiva ca |
indriyebhyaḥ parāṇīha catvāri kathitāni vai ||
caturviṃśatitattvāni etāni kathitāni tu |
tathātmānaṃ tv advitīyaṃ puruṣaṃ pañcaviṃśakam ||
tathā |
daśa manvantarāṇīha tiṣṭhantīndriyacintakāḥ |
bhautikās tu śataṃ pūrṇaṃ sahasraṃ tv ānumānikāḥ ||
mahātmakāḥ sahasrāṇi daśa tiṣṭhanti vijvarāḥ |
pūrṇaṃ śatasahasraṃ tu tiṣṭhanty avyaktacintakāḥ ||
puruṣaṃ nirguṇaṃ prāpya parisaṃkhyā na vidyate || 3.64 ||
sarvāśrameṣu yo dharmo mokṣopāyatayoktas tasya kārtsnyam āha |
satyam asteyam akrodho hrīḥ śaucaṃ dhīr dhṛtir damaḥ |
saṃyatendriyatā vidyā sarvadharma udīritaḥ || 3.65 ||
satyaṃ yathārthavācyam | asteyam acauryam | akrodhaḥ krodhahetāv api tadvarjanam | hrīr anyāyapravṛttau lajjā | śaucaṃ bhāvaśarīrārthaśuddhiḥ | dhīḥ kāryākāryavivekaḥ | dhṛtir akāryaṃ prati pracalitasya manasaḥ śuddhimukhatayā dhāraṇam | damaḥ kṛcchrādibhir manaso damanam | saṃyatendriyatā vaśyendriyatā | vidyātmajñānam | ayaṃ satyādipadārthavargo dharmaḥ sarvaḥ kṛtsna udīrita uktaḥ | ayaṃ ca sarveṣām āśramiṇāṃ brahmacāryādīnāṃ mokṣaṃ icchatāṃ sādhāraṇakāryatayocyate, na prātisvikavarṇāśramadharmabādhakatayā virodhābhāvāt | manuḥ |
ghṛtiḥ kṣamā damo 'steyaṃ śaucam indriyanigrahaḥ |
dhīr vidyā satyam akrodho daśakaṃ dharmalakṣaṇam ||
[973]        daśalakṣaṇakaṃ dharmaṃ ye 'dhīyeran dvijātayaḥ |
adhītya cānuvartante te yānti paramāṃ gatim || 3.65 ||
jīvaḥ paramātmaikyena vyavasthito dṛśya iti tad anupapannam | tathā hi — yadi jīvaparayor abhedaḥ kathaṃ sa eva dṛśyo draṣṭā, na ky ekasyaikasyāṃ kriyāyāṃ kartṛkarmabhāvo yujyate, bhedaś cet katham abhedena śakyo draṣṭum ity āha |
niḥsaranti yathā lohapiṇḍāt taptāt sphuliṅgakāḥ |
sakāśād ātmanas tadvad ātmānaḥ prabhavanti hi || 3.66 ||
yathāyogolakāt taptād agnivarṇāt sakāśāt sphuliṅgakā agnikaṇā niḥsaranti tataś ca lohapiṇḍāt parasparataś ca bhinnā iti gṛhyante, tadvat paramātmanaḥ sakāśād ātmano jīvāḥ prabhavanti | ātmana iti "bhuvaḥ prabhavaḥ" iti sūtravihitā pañcamī | yathā himavato gaṅgā prabhavatīti | tenāyam arthaḥ — paramātmano jīvātmānaḥ prabhavanti prakāśanta iti paramātmanaś ca tatprakāśanimittatvāt | "tasya bhāsā sarvam idaṃ vibhāti" iti śrutisiddham | hiśabdaḥ prasiddhau | prasiddhaṃ khalv etac chrutitaḥ, śrutiś ceyam: "yathāgneḥ kṣudrā visphuliṅgā vyuccaranty evam asmād ātmanaḥ sarve prāṇāḥ sarve lokāḥ sarve devāḥ sarvāṇi bhūtāni vyuccaranti" iti | ayam arthaḥ — yathāgnitvena visphuliṅgavartino lohapiṇḍavartinaś cāgner abhede 'pi lohagolakatadekadeśarūpopādhivaśād bhedabuddhivyavahārau bhavataḥ | evam antaḥkaraṇarūpopādhibhedāt paramātmanaḥ parasparataś ca jīvānāṃ bhedaḥ | svabhāva[ta]s tv agnivad bheda eva | tenaupādhikabhedāśrayo dhyānadhyeyabhāvaḥ | svābhāvikabhedāpekṣam aikyadarśanam ity avirodhaḥ || 3.66 ||
nanu yadi jīvaḥ pareṇābhinnas tarhi katham asau kāmyāni niṣiddhāni ca karmāṇi svabaddhāni ca karmāṇi svabandhahetūni kuruta ity atrāha |
tatrātmā hi svayaṃ kiṃcit karma kiṃcit svabhāvataḥ |
karoti kiṃcid abhyāsād dharmādharmobhayāśrayam || 3.67 ||
tatra paramātmana evopahitasya jīvatve sthite sa jīvatvam āpanna ātmā kiṃcid audāsīnyaṃ pracyutirūpaṃ svayaṃ svāśritaṃ karoti | kiṃcit tu yāgādikaṃ [974] svabhāvataḥ karoti | sva ātmīyaḥ svakīyakarmārjito dehā(ha)bhāvaḥ svabhāvaḥ | manogato hi yāgādikarmaviśeṣa ātmakartṛkaḥ | na cānyasya vyāpāre katham anyasya kartṛtvam iti vācyam | dṛṣṭaṃ hi rathena gacchaty aśvena gacchatīti prayogāt pratipatteś ca | rathāśvasamavete 'pi gamane rathino 'śvavārasya ca kartṛtvam | tatra rathāśvayoḥ karaṇatvam eva tṛtīyānideśāt | na kartṛtvam | yena rathaprabhṛteḥ prayojakakartṛtvam eva, na svatantrakartṛtvam ucyate | tena buddhiśarīrendriyasthe karmaṇy ātmanaḥ svatantraṃ kartṛtvaṃ yujyate | yadīttham ātmanāṃ kartṛtvaṃ kathaṃ teṣāṃ kecid dharmakāriṇaḥ kecid vādharmakāriṇa ity asyottaraṃ karoti kiṃcid abhyāsād iti | yena khalu pūrveṣu janmasu kriyābhyastā, sa uttareṣv api bhaveṣu dharmaṃ karoti | evam adharmam api kaścit | tataś ca saṃskāravaśāt kiṃcid eva karma karoti, yathā kaścid dāne rajyate, kaścid adhyayane kaścit tapasīti | tathā kaścit paiśunye kaścid dhiṃsāyāṃ kaścit pāruṣya iti | tataś cātmanaḥ sarvajñasyāpy anādyupādhiyoganibandhanāvidyāvaśāt kāmyeṣu niṣiddheṣu karmasu kartṛtopapayata iti || 3.67 ||
yadi jīvaparayor abhedaḥ svābhāvikas tarhi jagatkāraṇatvāvyayatvaboddhṛtādayaḥ paramātmadharmā jīve 'pi santi tataś ca katham asau jāta ity ucyata ity atrāha |
nimittam akṣaraḥ kartā boddhā brahma guṇī vaśī |
ajaḥ śarīragrahaṇāt sa jāta iti kīrtyate || 3.68 ||
yady apy ajo janmarahita eva svabhāvatas tathāpi janmavataḥ śarīrasya grahaṇād upādhitvenātmanaḥ svīkārāt tadupahitena rūpeṇa jāta ity ucyate | tasyājatve prameye nimittatvādikaṃ pramāṇatayopanyasya niyato 'sau nimittaṃ kāraṇam aviśeṣāt sarvasya kāryajātasya, tasmād ajaḥ | na ca kāryasya satas tattvāder ivāsya jagannimittatā vācyā | yasmād akṣaro 'saṃbhavadavayavaviśaraṇo niravayavatvāt | nanu niravayavasyāpi kāraṇasya karmaṇo guṇasya ca vināśitvaṃ dṛṣṭam | ataḥ katham akṣara ity atroktaṃ karteti | kartā svatantraḥ | tena paratantrābhyāṃ guṇakarmabhyām anyaḥ svatantrasya ca niravayavatvaṃ nityatvaṃ gamayati | sāṃkhyaparikalpitam avyaktam eva jagatkartṛ na punar ātmeti nirāsāyoktaṃ boddheti | [975] vividhabhoktṛbhogāyatanabhogaprasādhanādiracanayopetaṃ jagat prāksaṃpradhāryāvyaktaṃ na śaknuyāt kartum acetanatvāt | paramātmā tu sakalam eva kāryajātaṃ vimalakaratalalulitāmalakaphalavad ākulayatīti yuktā tasya kartṛtā | na cātyantavitatasya jagataḥ katham ātmā karteti vācyam | yasmād asau brahma bṛhann antata ity arthaḥ | na ca vācyam ekākinaḥ paramātmanaḥ svarūpataḥ sahāyataś ca vilakṣaṇasya katham iva vilakṣaṇakāryajanakatvam iti | yataḥ sa guṇī, guṇo 'tra śaktiḥ | aviśeṣāc ca sarvaprakārā gamyate | tataś ca sarvaprakāraśaktimattvād yujyate tasya vicitrakāryaracanācaturatvam | nanu ca sarvaśaktir apy ayaṃ kāryaṃ kurvāṇaḥ kulālādivat kāraṇādisahakārikāraṇakalāpam apekṣyaiva kartā bhaven na punar advitīyaḥ | na ca jagatsṛṣṭeḥ prāg ayaṃ saddvitīyaḥ | ato 'sya kartṛtvam anupapannam ity asyottaraṃ vāśīti | vaśī svacchando 'nyānadhīna iti yāvat | ayam abhiprāyaḥ  na tasya jagatkartṛtvam anumānagamyam | yat tarkeṇa bādhyate | kiṃ tu śāstraikavedyam, śāstraṃ ca: "tasmād vā etasmād ātmanaḥ ākāśaḥ saṃbhūtaḥ" ityādikam ekākina eva paramātmanaḥ sakalakāryakāritām ācaṣṭe | tasya ca tarkavirodhaḥ śārītakamīmāṃsābhyāsaśālinām aparihāra eva || 3.68 ||
saha dṛṣṭāntena śarīragrahaṇaprakāram apy āha |
sargādau sa yathākāśaṃ vāyuṃ jyotir jalaṃ mahīm |
sṛjaty ekottaraguṇāṃs tathādatte bhavann api || 3.69 ||
yathā jagatsṛṣṭyādau paramātmākāśādīn arthān ekottaraguṇān sṛjati | tatrākāśasya śabda eko guṇaḥ, sa ca sparśaś ca vāyoḥ, tau ca rūpaṃ tejasaḥ, te ca rasaś cāmbhasaḥ, gandhaḥ pañcamaḥ kṣiteḥ | evam ākāśādīnām ekottaraguṇatā | tān yathotpādayati tathā bhavañ jāyamānas tān evākāśādīñ śarīrārambhakatayā svī kurute || 3.69 ||
ākāśādisvīkāraprakāram āha |
āhutyāpyāyate sūryas tasmād vṛṣṭir athauṣadhiḥ |
tad annaṃ rasarūpeṇa śukratvam upagacchati || 3.70 ||
strīpuṃsayoś ca saṃyoge viśuddhe śukraśoṇite |
pañca dhātūn svayaṃ ṣaṣṭhaḥ ādatte yugapat prabhuḥ || 3.71 ||
[976]
āhutyā ca puroṣāśādikayā yathāvidhyanuṣṭḥitayā sūryaḥ sūryamaṇḍalam āpyāyate rasenopacitam kriyate | tasmāt sūryamaṇḍalād vṛṣṭiḥ | vṛṣṭitaś cauṣadhir vrīhyādiḥ | jātyabhiprāyakam ekavacanam | tasyā oṣadheḥ saṃbandhyodanādyannaṃ strīpuṃsābhyāṃ samabhyavahṛtaṃ rasalohitādipariṇatiparamparayā śukratvaṃ bījatvaṃ strīpuṃsayoḥ śarīre pratipadyate | tata ṛtukāle tayoḥ saṃyoge mithunībhāve yad viśuddhaṃ vātapittādyanupahataṃ śukraṃ śoṇitaṃ ca parasparasamāhṛtam ekatvam upagatam | tatra ye nabhaḥprabhṛtayaḥ pañca dhātavas tān yugapad ekadaiva śarīrārthaṃ svayaṃ ṣaṣṭha ādatte | ātmāpi dhātuśabdavācya iti jñāpayituṃ ṣaṣṭhagrahaṇam | prabhur jagatsvāmī | tatsvāmitvaṃ cāsya paramātmāṃśatvāt | tad uktaṃ śrībhagavatā gītāsu |
mamaivāṃśo jīvaloke jīvabhūtaḥ sanātanaḥ | iti || 3.70, 71 ||
kiṃ ca |
indriyāṇi manaḥ prāṇo jñānam āyuḥ sukhaṃ dhṛtiḥ |
dhāraṇā preraṇaṃ duḥkham icchāhaṃkāra eva ca || 3.72 ||
prayatna ākṛtir varṇaḥ svaradveṣau bhavābhavau |
tasyaitad ātmajaṃ sarvam anāder ādim icchataḥ || 3.73 ||
tasya jīvasyānadeḥ śarīrādim icchata indiryādikam ātmajam ātmārjitadharmādharmajanyatvāt | indriyāṇi bāhyakaraṇāni vakṣyamāṇāni | mano 'ntaḥkaraṇam | prāṇaḥ pañcavṛttiḥ śārīro vāyuḥ | jñānam avabodhaḥ | āyuḥ prāṇadhāraṇam | sukham āhlādaḥ | dhṛtiḥ śarīrendriyasthairyam | dhāraṇā smaraṇaśaktiḥ | preraṇam indriyāṇām | duḥkhaṃ pīḍā | icchā rāgaḥ | ahaṃkāro 'bhimānaḥ | prayatna udyamaḥ | ākṛtir jātiḥ, kharanaratiryaktvādiśarīrāvayavasaṃsthānaviśeṣo vā | varṇo brāhmaṇatvādir gauratvādir vā | svaro dhvaniḥ, dveṣo 'prītiḥ, bhavābhāvau vibhavatadviparyayau || 3.72, 73 ||
alpatarasyāpi śukraśoṇitasya mahākāryārambhaupayikakramam āha |
prathame māsi saṃkledabhūto dhātur vimūrchitaḥ |
māsy arbudaṃ dvitīye tu tṛtīye 'ṅgendriyair yutaḥ || 3.74 ||
[977]
pūrvoktaḥ pañcabhūtātmako dhātuḥ prathame garbhamāsi saṃkledabhūtaḥ parityaktātyantadravatvābhimūrchito ghanāvasthāṃ prāptym unmukho bhavati paya ivāmladravyasaṃpṛktam | dvitīye tu māsi tacchukraśoṇitam arbudaṃ bhavati, vivṛddhaṃ bhavatīty arthaḥ | arbudaṃ vivṛddhamāṃsātmako vyādhiviśeṣaḥ | tatsadṛśaṃ bhavatīty arthaḥ | tṛtīye māsy aṅgaiḥ śiraḥprabhṛtibhir indriyair indriyāyatanaiḥ karṇanayananāsikādibhir jihvāpāṇyādibhiś ca yukto bhavati | aṅgendriyāṇām utpattyanukūlasūkṣmatarāvayavasaṃsthityupakramo bhavatīty arthaḥ || 3.74 ||
tṛtīye(ya) māsagatam evāntarakāryāntaram āha |
ākāśāl lāghavaṃ saukṣmyaṃ śabdaśrotrabalādikam |
vāyos tu sparśanaṃ ceṣṭāṃ vyūhanaṃ raukṣyam eva ca || 3.75 ||
pittāt tu darśanaṃ paktim auṣṇyaṃ rūpaṃ parkāśatām |
rasebhyo rasanaṃ śaityaṃ snehaṃ kledaṃ samārdavam || 3.76 ||
bhūmer gandhaṃ tathā ghrāṇaṃ gauravaṃ mūrtim eva ca |
ātmā gṛhṇāty ajaḥ sarvaṃ tṛtīye spandane tataḥ || 3.77 ||
lāghavam agurutvam | saukṣmyaṃ śarīrātaḥ sthitasya svedāder bahir nirgamaṃ praty avirodhakatvam | uccāraṇādau sati śabdasiddhisāmarthyaṃ | śrotraṃ śravaṇendriyam | balaṃ sāmarthyam | ādiśabdād atryā(antrā)disuṣiraparigrahaḥ | etat sarvam ākāśāt tṛtīye māsy ajo 'pi jāyamāne śarīra ātmā gṛhṇāti | evaṃ vāyoḥ sparśanaṃ tvag indriyaṃ ceṣṭāṃ spandanaṃ vyūhanam avayavavibhāgaṃ raukṣyaṃ kharasparśatām | pittāt tejasas tu darśanaṃ cakṣur indriyaṃ paktiṃ bhuktasya pariṇatim auṣṇyaṃ mātrāṇāṃ rūpaṃ gauratvādi prakāśatāṃ kāntimattām | rasebhyo jalebhyas tu rasanaṃ rasopalabdhakam indriyaṃ śaityaṃ gātreṣu | sneham arūkṣatvam | kledam ārdratvam | bhūmes tu gandham, ghrāṇaṃ gandhagrāhakam indriyam | gauravaṃ bhāravattvam | mūrtiḥ kāṭhinyam | etat sarvaṃ tṛtīye māsy ātmā gṛhītvā caturthe māsi spandate gātraṃ kampayatīty arthaḥ | atra garbhopaniṣat: "ākāśāc chabdaḥ śrotraṃ vyaktatā sarvacchidrasamūhaḥ | tathā śauryāmarṣataikṣṇyapaktyauṣṇyabhrājiṣṇutāsaṃtāpavarṇa-rūpendriyāṇi taijasāni" || 3.75, 76, 77 ||
[978]
caturthe māsi garbharakṣārthaṃ yat kāryaṃ tad āha |
dohadasyāpradānena garbho doṣam avāpnuyāt |
vairūpyaṃ maraṇaṃ vāpi tasmāt kāryaṃ priyaṃ striyāḥ || 3.78 ||
garbhavatyāprekṣitaṃ vastu dohadaḥ, tasyāpradānena garbhasy doṣo vyādhyādir virūpatāṅgavikalatā maraṇaṃ vā syāt | etasmād dhetoḥ striyāḥ priyaṃ kāryam | dohado deya ity arthaḥ || 3.78 ||
kiṃ ca |
sthairyaṃ caturthe tv aṅgānāṃ pañcame śoṇitodbhavaḥ |
ṣaṣṭhe balasya varṇasya nakharomṇāṃ ca saṃbhavaḥ || 3.79 ||
caturthe garbhamāse garbhāṅgānāṃ (ṇāṃ) śiraḥprabhṛtīnāṃ dārḍhyaṃ bhavati | pañcame tu śoṇitodbhavaḥ | śoṇitasya raktasyodbhavaḥ | balavarṇanakhatanūruhāṇāṃ ṣaṣṭhe māsi saṃbhavo veditavyaḥ || 3.79 ||
kiṃ ca |
manaścaitanyayukto 'sau nāḍīsnāyuśirāyutaḥ |
saptame vāṣṭame vāpi tvaṅmāṃsasmṛtimān api || 3.80 ||
manasāntaḥkaraṇena caitanyena viṣayaprakāśena nāḍībhir da(dha)manīprabhṛtibhiḥ snāyubhir asthibandhanaiḥ śirābhiḥ sakalaśarīranibandhanībhī rajjutulyābhir yutaḥ saptame māsy asau garbho bhavati | aṣṭame tvacā māṃśena smṛtyā copeto bhavatīti veditavyam || 3.80 ||
api ca |
punar garbhaṃ punar dhātrīm ojas tasya pradhāvati |
aṣṭame māsy ato garbho jātaḥ prāṇair viyujyate || 3.81 ||
garbhasyāṣṭame māsy ojo jīvanahetur balaṃ dhātrīṃ nābhim āpyāyikāṃ garbhaṃ ca prati dhāvati | ata evāṣṭame māsi jātaḥ prāṇīna ciraṃ jīvati || 3.81 ||
navame daśame māsi prabalaiḥ sūtimāturaiḥ |
niḥsāryate bāṇa iva yantracchidreṇa sajvaraḥ || 3.82 ||
[979]
navame daśame māsi prabalair balavattaraiḥ prasavahetubhir vātair yantracchidreṇa yonidvāreṇa niḥsāryate preryate bāṇa ivāsvatantraḥ | tasyāṃ daśāyāṃ jāto jantur jvaravān bhavati, niratiśayaduḥkhātiśayavaśāt || 3.82 ||
atha śarīraṃ nirūpayati |
tasya ṣoḍhā śarīrāṇi ṣaṭ tvacaṃ dhārayanti ca |
ṣaḍaṅgāni tathāsthīni saha ṣaṣṭyā śatatrayam || 3.83 ||
jarāyujamaṇḍajaṃ svedajam udbhijjam ātvāhikaṃ yātanārthīyam iti ṣaṭprakārāṇi tasya jīvasya śarīrāṇi, tāni ṣaḍ api tvacaṃ dhārayanti | ṣaṇṇāṃ tvag eva niyatā, anyad vakṣyamāṇaṃ ṣaḍaṅgtvādikam aniyatam iti jñāpayituṃ punaḥ ṣaḍgrahaṇam | aṇḍajasvedajodbhijjānāṃ ṣaḍaṅgatvādyabhāvāt | ṣaḍaṅgānītyādinā manuṣyaśarīram eva nirūpayati | ata eva vakṣyati,
ity etad asthiraṃ varṣma yasya mokṣāya kṛty asau | iti |
śiraḥ pāṇī pādau madhyakāya iti ṣaḍaṅgāni | asthīni ca ṣaṣṭyadhikaśatatrayasaṃkhyākāni manuṣyaśarīraṃ dhārayatīti bahuvacanāntam api saṃbandhavaśād ekavacanāntatayā vapariṇamanīyam || 3.83 ||
uktām asthisaṃkhyām upapādayitum āha |
sthālaiḥ saha catuḥṣaṣṭir dantā vai viṃśatir nakhāḥ |
pāṇipādaśalākāś ca tāsāṃ sthānacatuṣṭayam || 3.84 ||
dantā dvātriṃśat | dvātriṃśad eva teṣāṃ sthālasaṃjñakāyatanāsthīni | evaṃ sasthālā dantāś catuḥṣaṣṭir bhavanti | sthālair iti vispaṣṭārthaḥ pāṭhaḥ, sthālāsthibhiḥ sahety arthaḥ | nakhāś ca viṃśatiḥ | tāsāṃ ca śalākānāṃ sthānam asthicatuṣṭayam | evam aṣṭottarāsthiśatam || 3.84 ||
ṣaṣṭyaṅgulīnāṃ dve pārṣṇyor gulpheṣu ca catuṣṭayam |
catvāry aratnikāsthīni jaṅghayos tāvad eva tu || 3.85 ||
ekaikasyām aṅgulyām asthitrayaṃ tataś ca sarvāsām aṅgulīnāṃ ṣaṣṭir asthīni | pādayoḥ paścimau bhāgau pārṣṇī, tayor asthidvayam | jaṅghāpārṣṇyoḥ saṃdhipradeśāv antarbahiravasthitau | ekatra pāde gulphau | tataś ca pādayor gulpheṣu catvāry asthīni | [980] aratnir evāratnikaḥ | yady apy aratniśabdo bāhvagra eva vartate, tathāpy atrāsthicatuṣṭayasaṃkhyāsaṃpattyarthaṃ prayujyamānaḥ samagram eva hastam āha | evam aratnikāsthīni catvāri bhavanti | jaṅghāśabdo 'py atra samastapādavacanaḥ | tathā ca jaṅghayor api catvāry evāsthīni | eṣā catuḥsaptatiḥ pūrveṇāṣṭaśatena saha dvāśītam(dvyaśīti)śatam || 3.85 ||
kiṃ ca |
dve dve jānukapoloruphalakāṃsasamudbhave |
akṣatālūṣake śroṇīphalake ca vinirdiśet || 3.86 ||
jānunī jaṅghorusaṃdhī | kapolau gallau | ūrū sakthinī, te ca phalakākāre | aṃsau bāhumūle | tathā — akṣatālūṣake netraprāntāsthinī, śroṇīphalake jaṅghāspaṣṭamadhyadeśau | etatsamudbhave pratyabhidhānaṃ dve dve asthinī | evaṃvidhayā saṃkhyayā saha caturnavatyadhikaṃ śatam || 3.86 ||
kiṃ ca |
bhagāsthy ekaṃ tathā pṛṣṭhe catvāriṃśac ca pañca ca |
grīvā pañcadaśāsthīni jatrv ekaṃ ca tathā hanuḥ || 3.87 ||
bhagāsthi upasthāsthy ekam | pṛṣṭhe pañcacatvāriṃśat | grīvāyāṃ pañcadaśa | jatrūṇi uroṃsayoḥ saṃdhāv ekam | haunś cibukaṃ tad apy ekāsthi | saiṣā triṣaṣṭiḥ pūrvayā saṃkhyayā saha śatadvayaṃ saptapañcāśadadhikam || 3.87 ||
kiṃ ca |
tanmūle dve lalāṭākṣigaṇḍe nāsā ghanāsthikā |
pārśvakā sthālakaiḥ sārdham arbudaiś ca dvisaptatiḥ || 3.88 ||
tanmūle hanumūle dve asthinī, tathā lalāṭāsthy ekam, tathākṣnor dve, gaṇḍayor dve, kapolākṣimadhyapradeśau gaṇḍau | nāsā ghansaṃjñakenāsthnā veditavyā | tenaitad asthy ekam, pārśvakā vaṅkrayas tāḥ sthālakair arbudasaṃjñakaiś cāsthibhiḥ sārdhaṃ dvāsaptatiḥ | pūrvair aṣṭābhiḥ sārdham aśītiḥ | pūrvasaṃkhyayā saha saptatriṃśadadhikaṃ śatatrayam || 3.88 ||
kiṃ ca |
dvau śaṅkhakau kapālāni catvāri śirasas tathā |
uraḥsaptadaśāsthīni puruṣasyāsthisaṃgrahaḥ || 3.89 ||
bhrūkarṇamadhyapradeśakau śaṅkhakau nāmāsthinī śiraḥsaṃbandhīni kapālākārāṇi [981] catvāri | yat tu "navakapālaṃ vai śiraḥ" iti śrutivacanam | tatra śiraḥśabdaḥ samukhe śirasi vartate | atra tu mukharahite, tenāvirodhaḥ | uro vakṣas tasya saptadaśa, tathā trayoviṃśatiḥ pūrvasaṃkhyopetāḥ ṣaṣṭyadhikaṃ śatatrayam | evaṃ puruṣasya manuṣyaśarīrasya śarīrāṇām asthisaṃkhyāsaṃgrahaḥ || 3.89 ||
śarīraṃ nirūpyendriyāṇi nirūpaṇīyāni śarīrāyattatvāt teṣām | tāni cātīndriyāṇīti nāsikādyanvayavyatirekānuvidhāyigandhādyanubhavabalena kalpyāni | ato viṣayanirūpaṇamukhena nāsikādiśabdalakṣaṇīyatayā ghrāṇādīni jñānendriyāṇy āha |
gandharūparasasparśaśabdāś ca viṣayāḥ smṛtāḥ |
nāsikālocane jihvā tvak śrotraṃ cendriyāṇi ca || 3.90 ||
gandhādayo viṣayās tadgrāhakāṇi nāsikādyadhiṣṭḥānāni jñānendriyāṇi pañca veditavyānīti tātparyārthaḥ | viṣayaśabdaprayogāc ca gandhādīnāṃ niyato grāhyabhāva iti gamayati | anya(ananya)trabhāve(vo) hi viṣayaśabdaḥ prayujyate, tena gandham eva yat sākṣātkārayati nāsikādhiṣṭhānaṃ tad ekam indriyam | tathā cānvartho jighaty aneneti vyutpattyā ghrāṇaśabdaḥ | evaṃ guṇānāṃ madhye rūpasyaiva sākṣātkāranimittaṃ nayanāyatanam aparam indriyam | caṣṭe 'neneti vyutpattyā tatra cakṣuḥśabdaḥ prayujyate | tathā rasaṃ pratyekaṃ kāraṇaṃ jihvāyatanaṃ rasanendriyam indriyāntarebhyo 'nyat | evam eva sparśamātropalambhahetus tvagāyatanaṃ sparśanam | tathaiva śabdaikagrāhakaṃ śrotram || 3.90 ||
jātasya śarīriṇaḥ prāg jñānendriyāṇi pravartante, tataḥ karmendriyāṇīti jñānedriyebhyaḥ paścāt karmendriyāṇy āha |
hastau pāyur upasthaś ca jihvā pādau ca pañca ca |
karmendriyāṇi jānīta manaś caivobhayātmakam || 3.91 ||
hastāyadhiṣṭānāni pañca karmendriyāṇi karmasiddhyā kalpyamānāni karmendriyāṇi | tatrādānalakṣaṇasyaiva karmaṇaḥ kāraṇaṃ hastādhiṣṭhānaṃ hastaśabdavācyam evaikam indriyam | aparaṃ tu mūtrapurīṣavisargakarmakaraṇatayonneyaṃ pāyvadhiṣṭhānaṃ tacchabdalakṣaṇīyam | tṛtīyaṃ tu śrukraśoṇitavisargopalakṣitānandaviśeṣakaraṇam [982] upasthaśabdavācyaṃ prajananāyatanam upasthaśabdenaiveti lakṣyate | aparaṃ tu gatirūpakarmakāraṇaṃ pādadvayādhiṣṭhānaṃ pādāv iti lakṣyate | vyāhārakaraṇaṃ tu jihvāyatanatvāj jihvāśabdalakṣyam indriyam | unmeṣanimeṣātmakaṃ karma nendriyaviśeṣakalpakaṃ vāyumātrasādhyatvāt | dṛṣṭaṃ hi vanaspatiparṇādau tathāvidhaṃ karma vāyusādhyam | manaś cāntaḥkaraṇaṃ tac cobhayātmakaṃ jñānakarmendriyātmakam | he munayo jānīta, anyaviṣaye manasi na ghrāṇādikaṃ nāpi hastādikaṃ svakāryaparyāptaṃ bhavatīti | manuḥ |
śrotraṃ tvak cakṣuṣī jihvā nāsikā caiva pañcamī |
pāyūpasthau hastapādau vāk caiva daśamī smṛtā ||
jñānendriyāṇi pañcaiva śrotrādīny anupūrvaśaḥ |
karmendriyāṇi pañcaiva pāyvādīni pracakṣate ||
ekādaśaṃ mano jñeyaṃ svaguṇenobhayātmakam |
yasmiñ jite jitāv etau bhavataḥ pañcakau guṇau || 3.91 ||
indriyāṇām āyatanāny uktāni prāṇasya koṣṭhyasya pañcavṛtter vāyor āyatanāni tajjāyārtham idānīm āha |
nābhir ojo gudaṃ śukraṃ śoṇitaṃ śaṅkhakau tathā |
mūrdhā sakaṇṭhahṛdayaḥ prāṇasyāyatanāni tu || 3.92 ||
nābhyādīni prāṇasyāyatanāni sthānānyeṣv āyatamānaḥ prāṇaḥ svakāryaṃ karoti | tasmāt taṃ vaśīkartum eṣu sthāneṣu dhārayet | ojo nāma hṛdayakamalavarti dravyaṃ kiṃcid dravyam | prasiddham anyat || 3.92 ||
prapañcena jaṭharāvayavān āha |
vapā vasāvahananaṃ nābhiḥ kloma yakṛt plihā |
kṣudrāntraṃ vṛkkako bastiḥ purīṣādhānam eva ca || 3.93 ||
āmāśayo 'tha hṛdayaṃ sthūlāntraṃguda eva ca |
uttarau ca gudau koṣṭhau(ṣṭyau) vistaro 'yam udāhṛtaḥ || 3.94 ||
vapādigaṇo 'rthagaṇaḥ prāṇāyatanavistāratvenodāhṛtaḥ | vapāvase prasiddhe | avahananaṃ yl laukikaiḥ pupphasa iti kīrtyate | kloma savṛkkaṃ yājñikaprasiddham | yakṛtplīhavastaya āyurvede prasiddhāḥ | purīṣādhānaṃ pakvāśayaḥ | [983] apakvasthānam āmāśayaḥ | bāhyād gudamaṇḍalād antarvyavasthitaṃ maṇḍaladvayam uttarau gudau | eṣa koṣṭhyo jāṭharo vistāra uktaḥ || 3.93, 94 ||
adhunā sakalaṃ śarīraṃ prapañcenāha |
kanīnike cākṣikūṭe śaṣulī karṇaputrakau |
karṇau śaṅkhau bhruvau dantāveṣṭāv oṣṭhau kukundare || 3.95 ||
vaṅkṣaṇau vṛṣaṇau vṛkkau śleṣmasaṃghātakau stanau |
upajihvāsphijau(cau) bāhū jaṅghoruṣu a piṇḍikāḥ || 3.96 ||
tālūdaraṃ bastiśīrṣaṃ cibukaṃ galaśuṇḍike |
avaṭuś caivam etāni sthānāny atra śarīrake || 3.97 ||
kanīnike netratāre | akṣikūṭe pakṣmṇī | śaṣkulī karṇaśaṣkulī | karṇaputrakau karṇasaṅkū | dantāveṣṭau dantapālyau | kukundare nitambakūpakau | vaṅkṣaṇau jaghanorusaṃdhī | vṛkkau māṃsapiṇḍau tau ca śleṣmasaṃghātakau | upajihvā prajihvā | sphijau(cau) kaṭī | jaṅghayor ūrvoś ca vyavasthitaḥ piṇḍākārā māṃsapiṇḍikāḥ | udaraṃ koṣṭhasuṣiram | cibukaṃ hanuḥ | galaśuṇḍike kaṇṭhāvayavaviśeṣau | avaṭuḥ śārīraṃ chidram | prasiddham anyat | śarīram eva śarīrakam | tataitāni sthānāni sthitinimittāni || 3.95, 96, 97 ||
akṣivartvacatuṣkaṃ ca paddhastahṛdayāni ca |
nava chidrāṇi tāny eva prāṇasyāyatanāni tu || 3.98 ||
akṣṇor vartmacatuṣkaṃ śukle dve kṛṣṇe dve maṇḍale caivaṃ catuṣkam | pādau hastau ca hṛdayam | yāni ca nava chidrāṇi gudaṃ prajananam āsyaṃ nāsike akṣiṇī karṇāv ity etāni ca prāṇāyatanāni || 3.98 ||
tathā |
śirāḥ śatāni saptaiva nava snāyuśatāni ca |
dhamanīnāṃ śate dve tu pañca peśīśatāni ca || 3.99 ||
[984]
śirā vātapittaśleṣmavahā nāḍyaḥ | tāsāṃ nābhisamudbhūtānāṃ sarvataḥ sakalaśarīravyāpinīnāṃ pradhānabhūtānāṃ sapta śatāni | yās tu palāśaparṇatantuvac chirābhyo 'nyāḥ śirāḥ pravṛttā tā anantās tathā snāyūnām asthisaṃbandhabandhanahetūnāṃ nava śatāni, dhamanīnāṃ tu vāyupravāhinīnāṃ nāḍīnāṃ dve śate | peśīnāṃ kṣudrarūpamāṃsapiṇḍānāṃ pañca śatāni bhavanti || 3.99 ||
sthūladhamanīnām uktā saṃkhyā, idānīṃ kṣudradhamanīnām āha |
ekonatriṃśallakṣāṇi tathā nava śatāni ca |
ṣaṭpañcāśac ca jānīta śirā dhamanisaṃjñitāḥ || 3.100 ||
dhamanisaṃjñitāḥ śirāḥ ṣaṭpañcāśadadhikanavaśatasahitāny ekonatriṃśallkṣāṇi nava śatāni ṣaṭpañcāśac ca dhamanyaḥ || 3.100 ||
kiṃ ca |
trayo lakṣāś ca vijñeyāḥ keśaśmaśru śarīriṇām |
saptottaraṃ marmaśataṃ dve ca saṃdhiśate tathā || 3.101 ||
romṇāṃ koṭyas tu pañcāśac catasraḥ koṭya eva ca |
saptaṣaṣṭis tathā lakṣāḥ sārdhāḥ svedāyanaiḥ saha || 3.102 ||
vāyavīyair vigaṇyante vibhaktāḥ paramāṇavaḥ |
yady apy eko 'nuvedaiṣāṃ bhāvānāṃ caiva saṃsthitim || 3.103 ||
keśāś ca śmaśrūṇi ca keśaśmaśru tasya trayo lakṣā jñeyāḥ | tasyety adhyāhāryam | tathā marmaṇāṃ saptottaraṃ śataṃ veditavyam | yatra hato mriyate sa dehapradeśo marma | asthisaṃdhīnāṃ dve śate jñeye | romṇāṃ svedāyanānāṃ ca militānāṃ catuṣpañcāśatkoṭyo lakṣāṇāṃ ca saptaṣaṣṭir lakṣārdhaṃ ca jñeyam | svedāyanaṃ svedadvāram | etāni svedāyanāni vāyavīyaiḥ paramāṇubhis trasareṇuṣaṣṭhāṃśair vibhaktāḥ śarīraparamāṇava iti pratyekaṃ parimāṇato jñeyāni | paramāṇuparimitam ekaikaṃ svedāyanam ity arthaḥ | tataś cāsmadīyapratyakṣāyogyatvāt tad asmābhir nopalakṣyate na punar asattvāt | eṣāṃ si(śi)rādibhāvānām uktasaṃkhyāsaṃsthitiṃ ydy eko 'pi yogīśvaro yogaprabhāvād anuveda jānāti, na sarvalokaḥ, tathāpy asau mānyā tatsaṃvedanasya [985] pramāṇatvāt | evaṃ ca vadatā mayaitad yogabalena viditam iti mūrkhe pramāṇam āveditaṃ bhavati || 3.101, 102, 103 ||
kiṃ ca |
rasasya nava vijñeyā jalasyāñjalayo daśa |
saptaiva tu purīṣasya raktasyāṣṭau prakīrtitāḥ || 3.104 ||
ṣaṭ śleṣmā pañca pittaṃ ca catvāro mūram eva ca |
vasā trayo dvau tu medo majjaiko 'rdhaṃ tu mastake || 3.105 ||
śleṣmaujasas tāvad eva retasas tāvad eva ca |
bhuktasyānnasya sārarūpaḥ pariṇāmo rasas tasyāñjalayo nava vijñeyāḥ | śarīrāmbha[ka]pārthivadravyānugrāhakasya jalasya daśa | purīṣasya sapta | aṣṭau raktasya | śleṣmaṇaḥ ṣaṭ | pittasya pañca | mūtrasya catvāraḥ | vasāyāḥ śarīrasnehasya trayaḥ | medaso māṃsābhyantaravartino dhātuviśeṣasya dvau | asthimadhyasthadhātuviśeṣasya majjāsaṃjñakasyaikaḥ | ardhāñjalir mastake | śleṣmaṇa ojasas tāvad evāñjalyardham eva | hṛdayavartī jīvanahetur dravyaviśeṣa ojaḥ | retaso 'py añjalyardham eva || 3.104, 105 ||
kṛtasya śarīranirūpaṇasya prayojanam āha |
ity etad asthiraṃ varṣma yasya mokṣāya kṛy asau || 3.106 ||
evam etad varṣma śarīram asthiraṃ yasya puruṣaviśeṣasya mokṣāya saṃpadyate sa kṛtī paṇḍitaḥ | asthireṇāpi śarīreṇa mokṣaḥ sādhyata iti tātparyārthaḥ || 3.106 ||
evaṃ nityātmanaḥ śarīrasya vivekārtham anityatvam uktam | adhunoktāyāṃ paramātmābhinnakṣetrajñopāsanāyām asamarthaṃ prati prakārāntareṇātmopāsanām āha |
dvāsaptatisahasrāṇi hṛdayād abhiniḥsṛtāḥ |
hitā nāma hi tā nāḍyas tāsāṃ madhye śaśiprabham || 3.107 ||
maṇḍalaṃ tasya madhystha ātmā dīpa ivācalaḥ |
sa jñeyas taṃ viditveha punar ājāyate na tu || 3.108 ||
[986]
yā hṛdayakamalapradeśād abhiniḥsṛtā ūrdhvādhastiryakpravṛttās tā hitāsaṃjñitā dvāsaptatisahasrasaṃkhyākā nāḍyaḥ | hi prasiddhau | tāsāṃ nāḍīnāṃ madhye hṛdayākāśe śaśiprabhaṃ candratulyamaṇḍalaṃ tasya madhya ātmā niścalo dīpa iva sthito yaḥ sa jñeyaḥ sākṣātkaryaḥ | dīpa iveti vadato 'yam abhiprāyaḥ— yathā dīpo dīpāntaram antareṇaiva svayaṃ prakāśate prakāśayati ca cakṣurviṣayam evam ātmāpi sarvasya prakāśakaḥ prakāśakāntaram antareṇa svayaṃ prakāśate prakāśayati ca sakalavastujātam iti | evam ātmānaṃ viditvā sākṣātkṛtyeha saṃsāre punar na jāyate | ātyantikaḥ śarīracchedo bhavatīty arthaḥ || 3.107, 108 ||
apavargahetutayokaṃ paramātmajñānaṃ praty upāyam āha |
jñeyaṃ cāraṇyakam ahaṃ yad ādityād avāptavān |
yogaśāstraṃ ca matproktaṃ jñeyaṃ yogam abhīpsatā || 3.109 ||
yad ādityād āraṇyakam adhītavān, yac ca mayā yogaśāstraṃ praṇītam, tad ubhayaṃ yogaṃ prāptukāmenārthato jñeyam | pradarśanārthaṃ caitat | tenānyān apy upaniṣadyogaśāstrāṇi jñeyāny eva | araṇyādhyeyaṃ brahmāraṇyakam, jīvaparamātmanor abhedavijñānaṃ viṣayāntarāsaṃbhinnaṃ yogaḥ | tathā ca viṣṇupurāṇam |
ātmaprayatnasāpekṣā viśiṣṭā yā manogatiḥ |
tasyā brahmaṇi saṃyogo yoga ity abhidhīyate ||
devalaḥ: "viṣayebhyo nivartyābhiprete 'rthe manaso 'vasthāpanaṃ yogaḥ" | dakṣaḥ |
vṛttihīnaṃ manaḥ kṛtvā kṣetrajñaḥ paramātmani |
ekīkṛtya vimucyeta yogo 'yaṃ mukhya ucyate ||
uktaṃ yogaśāstraṃ matproktam ity asyopalakṣakatvam, tatra yāny upalakṣaṇīyāni tāni kānicit kleśato (-cil leśato) nirdiśyante | tatra devalaḥ: "athāto dharmavarjitatvān na tiryagyonyāṃ puruṣārthopadeśaḥ | devamānuṣayor dvividhaḥ puruṣārthaḥ — abhyudayo niḥśreyasam iti | tayor abhyudayaḥ pūrvoktaḥ | dvividhaṃ niḥśreyasam — sāṃkhyayogāv iti | pañcaviṃśatitattvajñānaṃ sāṃkhyam | viṣayebhyo nivartyābhiprete 'rthe manaso 'vasthāpanaṃ yogaḥ | ubhayatrāpavargaḥ [987] phalam | janmamaraṇaduḥkhayor atyantābhāvo 'pavargaḥ | etau sāṃkhyayogau cādhikṛtya yair yuktitaḥ samayataś ca pūrvapraṇītāni viśālāni gambhīrāṇi tanmātrāṇīha saṃkṣipyoddeśato vakṣyante | tatra sāṃkhyānām ekā mūlaprakṛtiḥ | sapta prakṛtivikṛtayaḥ | pañca tanmātrāṇi | ṣoḍaśa vikārāḥ | pañca pañcendriyāṇi | arthāc (āś) ca pañcabhūtaviśeṣāḥ | trayodaśa karaṇāni | trīṇy antaḥkaraṇāni | catasraś catasro mātṛjāḥ pitṛjāś ca kośāḥ | pañca vāyuviśeṣāḥ | trayo guṇāḥ | trividho bandhaḥ trayo bandhahetavaḥ | dvau bandharāgau | tathā dvividhaḥ sargaḥ | tathā viparyayaḥ pañcavidhaḥ | aśaktir aṣṭāviṃśatividhā | tuṣṭir navavidhā | siddhir aṣṭavidheti pratyayabhedāḥ pañcāśat |
astitvam ekatvam athārthavattvaṃ parārtham anyatvam atho nivṛttiḥ |
yogo viyogo bahavaḥ pumāṃsaḥ sthitiḥ śarīrasya ca śeṣavṛttiḥ ||
iti daśa tanmātrāṇīti prakṛtivikṛtayaḥ | śabdatanmātraṃ sparśatanmātraṃ rasatanmātraṃ ṛupatanmātraṃ gandhatanmātram iti tanmātrāṇi | dvividhānīndriyāṇi | bhūtaviśeṣāś ca vikārāḥ | cakṣuḥśrotraghrāṇajihvātvaco buddhīndriyāṇi | rūpaśabdagandharasasparśās teṣām arthāḥ | vākpāṇipādapāyūpasthāḥ karmendriyāṇi | bhāṣaṇaṃ kriyā gamanam utsarga ānanda eṣāṃ karmāṇi | vāyvagnyabākāśapṛthivyo bhūtaviśeṣāḥ | daśendriyāṇi buddhyahakāramanāṃsi ca karaṇāni | teṣu manobuddhyahaṃkāraś cāntaḥkaraṇāni | daśa bahiṣkaraṇānīndriyāṇi ca guṇasāmyalakṣaṇam avyaktaṃ pradhānaṃ prakṛtir vidhānam ity anarthāntaram | adhyavasāyalakṣaṇo [988] mahān buddhir matir upalabdhi ity anarthāntaram | abhimānalakṣaṇo 'haṃkāro vaikāriko 'bhimāna ity anarthāntaram | na pūrvapūrvikā prakṛtiḥ | prakṛter mahān utpadyate, tato 'haṃkāraḥ | ahaṃkārāt tanmātrāṇīndriyāṇi ca, tanmātrebhyo viśeṣā ity utpattikramaḥ | yo yasmād utpadyate sa tasmiṃl līyata iti vāpy ayaṃ kramaḥ" | yamaḥ |
mano buddhir ahaṃkāraḥ ravānilāgnijalāni bhūḥ |
etāḥ prakṛtayas tv aṣṭau vikārāḥ ṣoḍaśāpare ||
śrotrākṣirasanaghrāṇatvacaḥ saṃkalpa eva ca |
śabdarūparasasparśagandhavākpāṇipāyavaḥ ||
pādāv upastha iti te vikārāḥ ṣoḍaś smṛtāḥ |
caturviṃśakam ity etaj jñānam āhur manīṣiṇaḥ ||
pañcaviṃśakam avyaktaṃ ṣaḍviṃśaḥ puruṣottamaḥ |
etaj jñātvā tu mucyante yatayaḥ śāntabuddhayaḥ ||
pañcaviṃśatitattvajño yatra yatrāśrame rataḥ |
prakṛtijño vikārajño yāti viṣṇoḥ paraṃ padam || iti || 3.109 ||
yad dhyeyam uktaṃ tat prayogakathanāyāha |
ananyaviṣayaṃ kṛtvā manobuddhismṛtīndriyam |
dhyeya ātmā sthito yo 'sau hṛdaye dīpavat prabhuḥ || 3.110 ||
antaḥkaraṇasāmagryaikatra spaṣṭīkṛtaḥ saṃkalpādidarśanāt sarvavyāpitve saty api | yathā vahneḥ sakalakāṣṭhavyāpitve sati yatraiva mathanādisāmagrī bhavati, tatra spaṣṭa upalabhyate | dhyeyo 'sāv ātmā pūrvoktaphalasiddhyartham | katham | ananyaviṣayaṃ kṛtvā | kim, manobuddhismṛtīndriyam, viṣayāntaratvaniṣedhenātmaviṣayatvapratipattyartham ananyaviṣayagrahaṇam | anyathātmano viṣayatvaniṣedhena taddhyānānupapattiḥ | na hi nirviṣayaṃ manaḥ pravartate nāpi buddhismṛtī, smṛter anubhūtaviṣayatvāt | na cendriyasyāviṣayatve saṃvedanam | tasmān naivātmani viṣayatā niṣidhyate | tena yad api pā(pa)tañjalinoktam: "yogaś cittavṛttinirodhaḥ" iti, tad apy anenaivābhiprāyeṇetarathāpi cittavṛtter abhāva ity abhilaṣitāsiddhiḥ samucchinnākāratvāt | [989] ata eva "tadā draṣṭuḥ svarūpeṇāvasthānam" iti tenaivoktam:
śanaiḥ śanair uparamed buddhyā dhṛtigṛhītayā |
ātmasaṃsthaṃ manaḥ kṛtvā na kiṃcid api cintayet || iti |
teṣāṃ cendriyāṇāṃ bahirviṣayāvabhāsasaṃkocanānanyaviṣayatvaṃ yadaiva kṛtaṃ tadaivābhyantaraviṣayapravaṇatā bhavati | yathā yadā pradīpasya rasmayo bahirmukhā bhavanti tadā ghaṭādeḥ prakāśaṃ kurvanti | yadā punaḥ śarāvasaṃpuṭaniruddhaprasarās tadātmany eva vyavasthānaṃ kurvanti | evam evāndhakāre vyavasthitasyaikākino bahirmukhāvabhāsanirodhena svacaitanyamātre 'vasthānam | ata evāraṇyake "śāntāyāṃ vāci kiṃjyotiḥ" iti praśne kṛte "ātmajyotiḥ" ity etad uttaram | evam etad ātmajñānaṃ yadviṣayatiraskāreṇātmacaitanyavyavasthānād antyasya cinmātrarūpatvāt | ata eva "tad idam analpam ahrasvam adīrgham arasam agandham arūpam asparśam" ityādyāraṇyake 'bhihitam | nanu sarva eva svaṃ caitanyaṃ samanubhavaty ahaṃ paśyāmīty upadeśānarthakyam, satyam, yady etāvanmātraṃ jñeyaṃ yāvatā viṣayāntaraśūnyasvacaitanyāvasthā niṣprakampā yāvad iccha(ṣṭa)kartṛkaraṇakārakaśūnyā kāryā | ata eva ,
yathā dīpo nivatastho neṅgate sopamā smṛtā | iti |
ataś ca prāṇāyāmadhyānapratyāhāradhāranātarkasamādhibhiḥ kriyate | tatroktalakṣaṇaiḥ prāṇāyānair indriyāṇāṃ doṣā nirākriyante | yathā |
dahyante dhmāyamānānāṃ dhātūnāṃ hi yathā malāḥ |
tathendriyāṇāṃ dahyante doṣāḥ prāṇasya nigrahāt || iti |
tato dhyānam ātmānusmaraṇam, tato manasaś cañcalatvād viṣayaṃ viṣayaṃ prati pravṛttasyāharaṇaṃ prayāhāraḥ | gītāsv api |
yato yato niḥsarati manaś cañcalam asthiram |
tatas tato nivṛhyaitac chanakair vaśam ānayet || iti |
etac ca manaso durgrahatvād indriyamūlatvāc cocyate |
cañcalaṃ hi manaḥ kṛṣṇa pramāthi balavad dṛḍham |
tasyāhaṃ nigrahaṃ manye vāyor api suduṣkaram || iti ||
punaḥ punar āhṛtvātmani dhāryata iti dhāraṇā | ete pṛthakphalāḥ | [990] yathā |
prāṇāyāmair dahed doṣān dhāraṇābhiś ca kilbiṣam |
pratyāhāreṇa saṃsargān dhyānenānīśvarān guṇān || iti |
anīśvarā guṇā rāgadveṣādayaḥ | tato manobuddhiparityāgenātmani vimarśas tarkaḥ |
yadātmavyatiriktena dvitīyaṃ naiva pāśyati |
brahmabhūtas tadā śāntaḥ sukham atyantam aśnute || iti |
tataḥ sakalaviditavedyasyātmanaḥ samyagādhānam aprakampatayā samādhir ucyate | ata eva,
prāṇāyāmas tathā dhyānaṃ pratyāhāro 'tha dhāraṇā |
tarkaś caiva samādhiś ca ṣāḍaṅgo yoga ucyate ||
iti smaryate || 3.110 ||
yasya punar asmin savitarke samādhau nirālambanatayā bahirmukhāvabhāsatiraskāreṇa cittavṛttir nābhiramate tasya śabdabrahmopāsanena brahmajñānābhyāsāt parabrahmādhigamopāyam āha |
yathāvadhānena paṭhan sāma gāyaty avisvaram |
sāvadhānas tathābhyāsāt paraṃ brahmādhigacchati || 3.111 ||
avadhānenaikāgracittatayā sāma paṭhann adhīyāno 'visvaram aviruddhasvaraṃ yathā gāyati tathā sāvadhāno brahmajñānam abhyasyan paraṃ brahma paramātmānam adhigacchati vetti || 3.111 ||
brahmajñānābhyāsopāyaviśeṣam āha |
aparāntikam ullopyaṃ madrakaṃ prakarīṃ tathā |
traiveṇukaṃ surābindam uttaraṃ gītakāni tu || 3.112 ||
ṛggāthāḥ pāṇikā dakṣavihitā brahmagītikāḥ |
gayann etat tadabhyāsakāraṇān mokṣasaṃjñitam || 3.113 ||
[991]
aparāntikādayo bhārataśāstroktā gītaprakāraviśeṣā brahmajñānābhyāsahetor geyāḥ | eteṣu gīyamāneṣu nādasya yata udayo yatra ca layas tadavagantavyam | tad eva brahma | tataś ca tajjñānābhyāsāya te geyā iti yujyate vaktum || 3.112, 113 ||
api ca |
vīṇāvādanatattvajñaḥ śrutijātiviśāradaḥ |
tālajñaś cāprayatnena mokṣamārgaṃ nigacchati || 3.114 ||
vīṇā vādyaviśeṣaḥ | tadvādanatattvajño bhārataśāstroktena mārgeṇa tālaṃ tattvato yo vetti, tathā śrutiṣu gītaśāstroktasvarūpāsu jātiṣu tacchāstrokāsv eva viśāradaḥ pravīṇaḥ | tathā gandharvavedoktamārgeṇa tālaṃ tattvato yo vetti so 'nāyāsena mokṣamārgaṃ mokṣopāyabhūtaṃ manasa aikāgryaṃ brahmajñānahetuṃ nigacchati || 3.114 ||
yas tu vīṇādinādānāṃ yata udayo yatra ca layas (yat) tattrāntarebhyo viviktatayā na samyag vetti taṃ praty āha |
gītajño yadi gītena nāpnoti paramaṃ padam |
rudrasyānucaro bhūtvā tenaiva saha modate || 3.115 ||
yadi daivāpāditamanovikṣepavaśena gītajño gītaṃ kurvāṇo 'pi paraṃ padaṃ nāsādayati tadā rudrasya parameśvarasyānucaro 'nuvartī bhūtvā tenaiva rudreṇaiva sārdhaṃ modate hṛṣyati || 3.115 ||
munīnāṃ praśnam āha |
anādir ātmā kathitas tasyādis tu śarīrakam |
ātmanaś ca jagat sarvaṃ jagataś cātmasaṃbhavaḥ || 3.116 ||
katham etad vimuhyāmaḥ sadevāsuramānavam |
jagadudbhūtam ātmā ca kathaṃ tasmin vadasva naḥ || 3.117 ||
he bhagavan yājñavalkya tvayātmānādiḥ prāgabhāvarahitaḥ kathitaḥ | tasyādivyavahāraḥ śarīrasyādimattveneti coktam, [992]
ajaḥ śarīragrahaṇāt sa jāta iti kīrtyate |
ity atra | tathātmanaś ca sakalaṃ jagad utpadyata iti tvayaivoktam "sargādau sa yathākāma(śa)m" ity atra | tathā jagataḥ sakāśād ātmanaḥ śarīravataḥ saṃbhavo janmeti bravīṣi, tad etad bhagavato vacanaṃ parasparaṃ vyāhatam iti vimuhyāmaḥ | bhagavāṃs tāvad āptataro 'to bhavadvacanaṃ na vyāhataṃ bhavitum arhati, vyāghātaṃ ca paśyāmo 'to vimuhyāmaḥ | vyāghātam eva spaṣṭayati — sadevāsuramānavaṃ jagad ātmana uddbhūtaṃ ced ātmā śarīragrahaṇanirapekṣa eva svakāryaṃ karoti, tataś ca tasmin katham ākāśādau kāraṇībhūte saty ātmanaḥ saṃbhavaḥ śarīragrahaṇaṃ śarīranirapekṣasyaivātmano jagatsṛṣṭyādikāryakāratvāc charīragrahaṇam anarthakaṃ syāt | atha tadgrahaṇasyārthavattvāya śarīravān eva kāryakārī, kathaṃ tarhi sargādāv aśarīra evātmākāśādi sṛjati, ity etan mohanivṛttaye 'smān prati brūhi || 3.116, 117 ||
atrottaram āha |
mohajālam apāsyeha puruṣo dṛśyate hi yaḥ |
sahasrakarapannetraḥ sūryavarcāḥ sahasrakaḥ || 3.118 ||
sa ātmā caiva yajñaś ca visvarūpaḥ prajāpatiḥ |
mohajālaṃ nānāprakāraṃ mithyājñānaṃ satkarmācaraṇavedāntārthāvagāhanavaśād apāsya nirasya yaḥ puruṣaḥ sahasrakaro 'parimitahasta evaṃ sahasrapāt, sahasranetraḥ, sahasrasūryavarcāḥ sūrya iva jyotiḥsahasrakaḥ sahasramūrdhā ca dṛśyate vidvadbhiḥ sa evātmā kṣetrajño jīva iti(vo) vijñānātmeti yāvat | yajño dharmaś ca sa eva vedāntaikavedyaḥ paramaḥ puruṣo viśvarūpo bhoktṛbogyabhogāyatanabhogasādhanabhogarūpa iti yāvat | ekasya cānekarūpatvaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ yathā vṛkṣasya skandhaśākhāviṭapādirūpatā, ato nānupapannaṃ vaiśvarūpyam | sa eva prajānāṃ punar anādirūpāṇāṃ patiḥ svāmī | atra cāmūrtasya niravayasya paramātmano 'saṃkhyakarādiyogaḥ sarvatra sarvadā ca karādikāryakāraṇaśaktimattvād upacāreṇocyate | tataś ca sarvaviṣayācintanīyaśakteḥ paramātmano 'śarīrasyāpi vyomādiviśvasṛṣṭir nānupapannety uktaṃ bhavati | viśvarūpatvābhidhānena ca jagadupādānakāraṇaṃ ca paramātmaiveti bodhayati | yath — ghaṭakaraśarāvodañcanādirūpā mṛttikā tadupādānakāraṇam evaṃ jagadrūpa ātmā [993] tadupādānakāraṇam | viśvaśabdenaiva yajñasyāpi grahaṇe siddhe pṛthagupādānaṃ prādhānyakhyāpanārtham | evaṃ ca paramātmā śarīrānapekṣa eva svakāryakārīty uktam | tataś cārthāc charīrasāpekṣatā kṣetrajñasyety āpadyate | tataś ca sāpekṣatvānapekṣatvayor virodhaḥ || 3.118 ||
aupādhikaś ca tasya paramātmano bhedo 'stīty uktam eva (?) paramātmano mahāsṛṣṭihetutvaṃ sarvaśaktitvenopapādyāvāntarasṛṣṭau taddhetutvam upapādayann idānīm āha |
virāṭ ca so 'nnarūpeṇa yajñatvam upagacchati || 3.119 ||
yo dravyadevatātyāgāt saṃbhūto rasa uttamaḥ |
devān saṃtarpya sa raso yajamānaṃ phalena ca || 3.120 ||
saṃyojya vāyunā somaṃ nīyate raśmibhis tataḥ |
ṛgyajuḥsāmavihitaṃ sauraṃ dhāmopanīyate || 3.121 ||
tanmaṇḍalam asau sūryaḥ sṛjaty amṛtam uttamam |
tajjanma sarvabhūtānām aśanānaśanātmanām || 3.122 ||
tasmād annāt punar yajñaḥ punar annaṃ punaḥ kratuḥ |
evam etad anādyantaṃ cakraṃ samparivartate || 3.123 ||
vividhaṃ niśeṣeṇa vā rājata iti virāṭ sūryaḥ sa eva pūrvoktaḥ paramātmaiva sa ādityo 'nnarūpeṇa yajño bhavati | tad eva prapañcayitum uktaṃ yo dravyadevatetyādi | dravyasya ca puroḍāśāder haviṣas tyājyasya devatāyā uddeśakārakasyāgnyāder yaḥ sambandhī tyāgo yajñas tasmāt saṃbhūta uttamo rasaḥ puṇyadharmādiparyāyo devān amṛtena prīṇayitvā yajamānaṃ ca svargādinā phalena saṃyojya sa dharmo raso 'mṛtaṃ bhavati, so 'mṛtātmā raso vāyunā somamaṇḍalaṃ nīyate | tataḥ somamaṇḍalād raśmibhiḥ sauraṃ dhāma sūryasthānam ṛgyajuḥsāmātmakatayā vihitaṃ vedaiḥ saiṣā trayy eva vidyā tapatītyādibhir avabodhitam upanīyate | yat tu trayīmayatvena vihitaṃ tat sūryasya [994] maṇḍalaṃ veditavyam | sarvabūtānām aśanānaśanātmanāṃ carācarāṇāṃ sthirāṇāṃ janmabījasamau sūryo maṇḍalāntarvartī puruṣo 'nnarūpaṃ rasaṃ visṛjati | tasmād annāc ca puroḍāśādirūpāt tyajyamānāt punar yajñodayaḥ | tataś ca yajñād uktena krameṇa punar annasaṃbhavaḥ | tataś ca kratur bhavati | evam ādyantarahitam etad uktaṃ vastujātaṃ parasparaṃ hetuhetusa(ma)dbhāvena vyavasthitaṃ cakravat parivartate bhrāmyati vyavatiṣṭhata ity arthaḥ | tathāha bhagavān gītāsu |
annād bhavanti bhūtāni parjanyād annasaṃbhavaḥ |
yajñād bhavati parjanyo yajñaḥ karmasamudbhavaḥ ||
karma brahmodbhavaṃ viddhi brahmākṣarasamudbhavam |
tasmāt sarvagataṃ brahma nityaṃ yajñe pratiṣṭhitam ||
evaṃ pravartitaṃ cakraṃ nānuvartayatīha yaḥ |
aghāyur indriyārāmo modhaṃ pārtha sa jīvati ||
yajño 'pūrvam, brahma vedaḥ | akṣaraṃ paramātmā | manuḥ |
agnau prāstāhutiḥ samyag ādityam upatiṣṭhate |
ādityāj jāyate vṛṣṭtir vṛṣṭer annaṃ tataḥ prajāḥ || 3.119, 120, 121, 122, 123 ||
yadi kāryakāraṇabhāvavyavasthitaṃ saṃsāracakraṃ, na tarhy ātmanaḥ kaivalyarūpo mokṣaḥ saṃbhavatīty atrāha |
anādir ātmā saṃbhūtir vidyate nāntarātmanaḥ |
samavāyī tu puruṣo mohecchādveṣakarmajaḥ || 3.124 ||
satyaṃ yady ādimān ātmā syāt tadā vināśy ap syāt tataś ca vināśe mokṣalakṣaṇo 'sya dharmo na syāt | kiṃ tv anādir anutpāttir ātmā, na hy asyāntarātmanaḥ saṃbhūtiḥ kāraṇam asti | na ca nityasyāpi pūrvoktacakreṇa svābhāvikasamavāyayogitvād nirmokṣo vācyaḥ | yato 'yaṃ puruṣo 'ntarātmā yajñādicakrasya samavāyī saṃbandhī mohecchādveṣasaṃbhavāt karmato jāyate na svataḥ | te ca mohādayaś cakrasamavāyopādhayo vidyātapo'bhyāsātiśayād vairagyāc ca nivartante, tannibandhanāni ca karmāṇi || 3.124 ||
mahāsṛṣṭikartṛtve paramātmanaḥ prakāraviśeṣam āha |
sahasrātmā mayā yo va ādideva udāhṛtaḥ |
mukhabāhūrupajjāḥ syus tasya varṇā yathākramam || 3.125 ||
pṛthivī pādatas tasya śirasto dyaur ajāyata |
nastaḥ prāṇā diśaḥ śrotrāt sparśād vāyur mukhāc chikhī || 3.126 ||
manasaś candramā jātaś cakṣuṣaś ca divākaraḥ |
jaghanād antarikṣaṃ ca jagac ca sacarācaram || 2.127 ||
"sahasrakarapannetraḥ sūryavarcāḥ sahasrakaḥ" ity atra yo mayā yuṣmākam ādidevo jagatkāraṇabhūto deva udāhṛta uktaḥ, tasya mukhabāhūrupādebhyo yathāsaṃkhyaṃ brāhmaṇakṣatriyavaiśyaśūdrā jātāḥ | tathāsya pādato bhūḥ śirastaś ca dyaur ajāyata | nasto nāsikātaḥ prāṇāḥ jātāḥ | śrotrād diśaḥ | tvaco vāyuḥ | mukhād agniḥ | manasaś candraḥ | cakṣuṣo ravir jātaḥ | jaghanān nābhito 'ntarikṣaṃ jaṅgamaṃ sthāvaraṃ jagaj jātam || 3.125, 126, 127 ||
munayo 'trākṣepam āhuḥ |
yady evaṃ sa kathaṃ brahman pāpayoniṣu jāyate |
īśvaraḥ sa kathaṃ bhāvair aniṣṭaiḥ saṃprayujyate || 3.128 ||
karaṇair anvitasyāpi pūrvajñānaṃ kathaṃ ca na |
vetti sarvagatāṃ kasmāt sarvago 'pi na vedanām || 3.129 ||
yady ātmaivam uktaprakāreṇa viśvasṛṣṭihetur ataś ca sarvajñaḥ sarvaśaktiḥ sarveśvaraḥ kathaṃ tarhi brahman yājñavalkya paramātmā pāpayoniṣu śvasūkarādiṣu jāyate kutsitaśarīraṃ parigṛhṇātīti yāvat | yatheśvaraḥ svatantro 'pi san katham aniṣṭair mūkāndhabadhiratvādibhir bhāvaiḥ saṃbadhyate | tathā karaṇaiḥ ṣaḍbhir indriyair jñānakāraṇair yuktasyāpi pūrvajanmānubhūtārthaviṣayacakṣurādibhiḥ pratyabhijñānaṃ manasā ca smaraṇaṃ kathaṃ na bhavati, sarvapuruṣagatāṃ ca vedanāṃ duḥkhaṃ sarvagaḥ sarvakṣetrajñātmāpi san kasmān na vetti | vedanāgrahaṇam upalakṣaṇārtham | tena sarvapuruṣasamavetān sukhādīn bhāvān kathaṃ na vettīti codyārthaḥ || 3.128, 129 ||
[996]
atrottaram āha |
antyapakṣisthāvaratāṃ manovākkāyakarmajaiḥ |
doṣaiḥ prayāti jīvo 'yaṃ bhavayoniśateṣu ca || 3.130 ||
naiveśvaro nirupādhiḥ san prātisvikenaiva rūpeṇāntyajādikutsitadehaparigrahaṃ karoti, kiṃ tu jīvaḥ sann antaḥkaraṇopahitaḥ san mānasavācikakāyikair niṣiddhaiḥ karmabhir ye janitā doṣāḥ pāpāni pūrvāṇi tair yathāsaṃkhyam antyajatvaṃ pakṣitvaṃ sthāvaratvaṃ ca bhavaśateṣu kutsitayoniśateṣu ca yāti | atra ca pakṣigrahaṇaṃ tiryaṅmātropalakṣaṇārtham | tathā ca manuḥ |
śarīrajaiḥ karmadoṣair yāti sthāvaratāṃ naraḥ |
vācikaiḥ pakṣimṛgatāṃ mānasair antyajātitām ||
na copahitasya yo dharma yogaḥ sa tasya nirupādher bhavitum arhati, yathā ghaṭopahitasyākāśasya dhūlidhūmādiyoge(go) na mahākāśasya jivabhāvaś cānādir anāditvād upadhiyogasya || 3.130 ||
kiṃ ca |
anantā hi yathā bhāvāḥ śarīreṣu śarīriṇām |
rūpāṇy api tathaiveha sarvayoniṣu dehinām || 3.131 ||
yathā śarīreṣu vartamānānāṃ śarīriṇāṃ jīvānām anantā asaṃkhyātā bhāvāś cittavṛttayaḥ kuśalākuśalapravṛttihetavo bhavanti, tathā rūpāṇy api kāṇakubjāndhabadhiratvādīny aśubhāni sarvayoniṣu vartamānānāṃ dehabhājāṃ bhavanti | anena ceśvaraḥ sa kathaṃ bhāvair aniṣṭaiḥ saṃprayujyata iti codyaṃ parihṛtam | anena ca śokadvayena sūkṣmaśarīropahitasya paramātmano jīvabhūtasya pāpavaśāt kutsitayonikutsitarūpaprāptir na paramātmana iti parihāra uktaḥ | anenaiva ca hetunā bhāvāntarānubhūtānāṃ bhāvānām anavagamo 'nusaṃdheyaḥ | ata eva yogināṃ kṣīṇadoṣāṇāṃ jātismaratvādikaṃ śāstrakārair ucyate || 3.131 ||
na kevalaṃ rūpavaicitrye bhāvavaicitryaṃ kāraṇam | śubhāśubhakarmaṇāṃ caihikāmuṣmikaphalatve 'pi tad eva hetur ity āha |
[997]
vipākaḥ karmaṇāṃ pretya keṣāṃcid iha jāyate |
iha cāmutra caikeṣāṃ bhāvas tatra prayojanam || 3.132 ||
keṣāṃcit karmaṇāṃ vipākaḥ phalaṃ śubham āyurādi viparītaṃ ca tatkṣayādi, keṣāṃcin marmāgraheṇa kṛtānāṃ pretya janmāntare bhavati, keṣāṃcit punar atyantābhiniveśavaśāt punaḥ punaḥ  kṛtānām atraiva janmani vipāko bhavati | aneyṣāṃ tu karmaṇāṃ pracuratarāgrahasaṃpāditānām iha paratra ca janmani phalaṃ bhavati | tatra ca sarvaphalavaicitrye bhāvaś cittavṛttiveśeṣopetaḥ prayojanaṃ prayojakakāraṇam iti yāvat || 3.132 ||
yad uktam "antyapakṣisthāvaratāṃ manovākkāyakarmajaiḥ doṣaiḥ" iti tad eva prapañcayann āha |
paradravyāṇy abhidhyāyaṃs tathāniṣṭāni cintayan |
vitathābhiniveśī ca jāyate 'ntyāsu yoniṣu || 3.133 ||
parakīyāni dhanāny abhidyāyan katham etāni parasya na bhaveyur mama ca bhaveyur ity abhicintayann ity arthaḥ | tathā yaḥ parasyāniṣṭāni pratikūlāni cintayan bhavati, yady ayam aprajā apaśur adhanaḥ syāt tathā sati samyag bhavatīti | tathā yo vitathābhiniveśī vitathe pramāṇarahita bauddhacārvākādisiddhāntādāv abhiniveśayaty antāgrāhī, tādṛśeṣu pratyavāyavān antyāsu caṇḍālādiyoniṣu jāyate | tad etat trividhaṃ mānasaṃ pāpam | tad āha manuḥ |
paradravyeṣv abhidhyānaṃ manasāniṣṭacintanam |
vitathābhiniveśaś ca trividhaṃ karma mānasam || 3.133 ||
vācikāny āha |
puruṣo 'nṛtavādī ca piśunaḥ paruṣas tathā |
anibaddhapralāpī ca mṛgapakṣiṣu jāyate || 3.134 ||
yo 'nṛtam ayathādṛṣṭaṃ vadatīty evaṃśīlo yaś ca piśunaḥ paradoṣasūcako yo vā parodvegakaraṇaśīlo yo 'nibaddhapralāpī purāṇādividyāsthānaprasiddhaviruddhābydhāyī [998] puruṣaḥ sa mṛgayoniṣu pakṣiyoniṣu vā doṣābhyāsaratāratasya (tāratamya) vaśena jāyate tat tanum upādatte | etac caturvidhaṃ vācikaṃ pāpam | tad āha manuḥ |
pāruṣyam anṛtaṃ caiva paiśunyam api sarvataḥ |
anibaddhapralāpaś ca vāṅmayaṃ syāc caturvidham || 3.134 ||
kāyikāny āha |
adattādānanirataḥ paradāropasevakaḥ |
hiṃsakaś cāvidhānena sthāvareṣūpajāyate || 3.135 ||
yaḥ punar adattādāne caurye nirato 'tyantasaktaḥ parabhāryāgāmī vā yaś cāvihitaṃ prāṇivadhaṃ kuryāt sa sthāvareṣv oṣadhivanaspatigucchagulmādiṣu jāyate | manuḥ |
adattānām upādānaṃ hiṃsā caivāvidhānataḥ |
paradāropasevā ca śārīraṃ trividhaṃ smṛtam ||
evaṃ daśavidhaṃ manovākkāyajaṃ pāpaṃ sapratyayaṃ sābhyāsaṃ cācaritam antyādiyoniprāptinimittam uktam iti veditavyam | yat tūktād anyad vāṅmanaḥkāyasādhyam apy abuddhipūrvakaṃ kṛtam anāgrahasevitaṃ syāt tasya phalāntaraṃ boddhavyam | tathā ca hārītaḥ: "sarvābhakṣybhakṣaṇam abhojyabhojanam apeyapānam ayājyayājanam asatpratigrahaḥ paradārābhigamanaṃ paradravyāpaharaṇaṃ prāṇihiṃsā ceti sārīrāṇi, pāruṣyam anṛtaṃ vivādaḥ śrutivikrayaś ceti vācyāni, paropatāpanaparadravyābhidrohau krodhalobhau māno 'haṃkāraś ceti mānasāni, tad etāny aṣṭādaśa samānarūpāṇi karmāṇi | yasmin yasmin vayasi yaḥ karoti śubhāśubhāni tasmiṃs tasmin vayasi śārīravācikamānasāny āpnoti | evaṃ hy āha |
yasyāṃ yasyām avasthāyāṃ yat karoti śubhāśubham |
tasyāṃ tasyām avasthāyāṃ tat tat phalam avāpnuyāt ||
śarīreṇa tu śārīraṃ vāṅmayena tu vāṅmayam |
mānasaṃ manasā caiva svakarmaphalam aśnute || 3.135 ||
utkṛṣṭayoniprāptikāraṇam āha | [999]
ātmajñaḥ śaucavān dāntas tapasvī vijitendriyaḥ |
dharmakṛd vedavid yāti sāttviko devayonitām || 3.136 ||
ātmajña ātmopāsako na tatsākṣātkārī śaucena śamaprakāreṇa copeto dānto damayuktas tapasvī kṛcchādikārī vijitendriyo vaśīkṛtendriyo dharmarato nityanaimittikakārī vedārthajño yaḥ sa sāttvikatvād devayonitāṃ prāpnoti | udriktasattvo yogī sāttvikaḥ | sattvasvarūpam āha manuḥ |
tatra yat prītisaṃyuktaṃ kiṃcid ātmani lakṣayet |
prabhātam iva śuddhābhaṃ sattvaṃ tad upadhārayet ||
prītiḥ sukhaṃ tatkāraṇaṃ prītisaṃyuktam, śuddhābham anavadyavijñānakāraṇaṃ yat tat satvam ity arthaḥ | tathā,
vedābhyāsas tapo jñānaṃ śaucam indriyanigrahaḥ |
dharmakriyātmacintā ca sāttvikaṃ guṇalakṣaṇam ||
guṇalakṣaṇaṃ guṇaliṅgam, sāttvikaṃ sattvasaṃbandhivedābhyāsādikaṃ sattvakāryatvād bhavati sattve liṅgam | tathā |
yat sarveṇecchati jñātuṃ lajjate yan na cācaran |
yena tuṣyati cātmāsya tat sattvaguṇalakṣaṇam ||
sattve hy udrikte puruṣo jñeyaṃ vastu sarveṇa vastunā sādhāraṇāsādhāraṇadarmayoginā jñātumicchati, atas tādṛśīcchā bhavati sattvalakṣaṇam | tathā |
tamaso lakṣaṇaṃ kāmo rajasas tv artha ucyate |
sattvasya lakṣaṇaṃ dharmaḥ śreṣṭham etad yathākramam ||
sattvavipākaś ca prathamamadhyamottamabhedena trividhaḥ | tatra devatvaprāptir madhamas tadvipākaḥ | tad āha manur eva |
tāpasā yatayo viprā ye ca vaimānikā gaṇāḥ |
nakṣatrāṇi ca daityāś ca prathamā sāttvikī gatiḥ ||
yajvāna ṛṣayo devā vedā jyotīṃṣi vatsarāḥ |
pitaraś caiva sādhyāś ca dvitīyā sāttvikī gatiḥ ||
brahmā viśvasṛjo dharmo mahān avyaktam eva ca |
uttamāṃ sāttvikīm etāṃ gatim āhur manīṣiṇaḥ || 3.136 ||
[1000]
asatkāryarato 'dhīra ārambhī viṣayī ca yaḥ |
sa rājaso manuṣyeṣu punar janmādhigacchati || 3.137 ||
satkāryaṃ dharmas tato 'nyad adharma it yāvat | tatra viṣaye 'nanyavyāpāreṇa rataḥ saktas tannirato 'dhīraḥ śītatāpādidvaṃdvāsahiṣṇuḥ | ārambhī kāmyayuddhakārī, viṣayasakto ya evaṃvidhaḥ sa rājasaḥ, sa punar api manuṣya eva bhavati | rajovipākatraividhyaṃ manur āha |
kallā mallā naṭāś caiva puruṣāś ca kuvṛttayaḥ |
dyūtapānaprasaktāś ca prathamā rājasī gatiḥ ||
rājānaḥ kṣatriyāś caiva rājñāṃ caiva purohitāḥ |
dānayuddhapradhānāś ca dvitīyā rājasī gatiḥ ||
gandharvā guhyakāś caiva vividhā bhūcarāś ca ye |
tathaivāpsarasaḥ sarvā rājaseṣūttamā gatiḥ || 3.137 ||
atha tāmasalakṣaṇam āha |
nidrāluḥ krūrakṛl lubdho nāstiko yācakas tathā |
pramādavān bhinnavṛtto bhavet tiryakṣu tāmasaḥ || 3.138 ||
nidrālur nidrāśīlaḥ | krūrakṛt paraduḥkhakārī | lubdhanāśtikau prasiddhau | yācakaḥ sati dravye bhikṣaṇaśīlaḥ | pramādakārya (mādaḥ kāryā)nādaraḥ | bhinnavṛtto durācāraḥ sa tāmasaḥ sa tiryakṣu jāyate | manuḥ |
sthāvarāḥ kṛmikīṭāś ca matsyāḥ sarpāḥ sarīsṛpāḥ |
paśavaś ca śṛgālāś ca jaghanyā tāmasī gatiḥ ||
hastinaś ca turaṅgāś ca śūdrā mlecchā vigarhitāḥ |
siṃhā vyāghrā varāhāś ca madhyamā tāmasī gatiḥ ||
cāraṇāś ca suparṇāś ca puruṣāś caiv dāmbhikāḥ |
rakṣāṃsi ca piśācāś ca tāmaseṣūttamā gatiḥ ||
atra ca karmatāratamyena phalatāratamyam || 3.138 ||
rajasā tamasā caiva samāviṣṭo bhramann iha |
bhāvair aniṣṭaiḥ saṃyuktaḥ saṃsāraṃ pratipadyate || 3.139 ||
[1001]
evam uktaprakāreṇa rajasā tamasā ca samāviṣṭa upahita iha saṃsāre bhramann anekayonīḥ pratipadyamāno 'niṣṭair mūkādibhir bhāvaiḥ saṃbaddhaḥ saṃsāraṃ janmaparamparāṃ pratipadyate | anena ca,
īśvaraḥ sa kathaṃ bhāvair anekaiḥ saṃprayujyate |
ityādi codyaṃ parihṛtam | na hīśvarasya nirupādher mahākāśakalpasyaupādhikāniṣṭabhāvayogo bhavati kiṃ tu rajaḥpramukheno(ṇo)pādhinopahitasya jīvasya na svābhāvikaṃ kiṃ tv aupādhikam | na hy aupādhikāniṣṭabhāvayogo dūṣaṇam | yathādarśopādhikaṃ mukhasya vaktratvaṃ kurūpatvaṃ vā || 3.139 ||
"karaṇair anvitasyāpi" iti codyasya parihāram āha |
malino hi yathādarśo rūpālokasya na kṣamaḥ |
tathāvipakvakaraṇa ātmajñānasya na kṣamaḥ || 3.140 ||
yady apy ātmā janmāntarānubhūtam arthajātaṃ puruṣāntarasamavetaṃ vedanādikaṃ ca svabhāvato jñātuṃ śakto manaḥprabhṛtīni ca karaṇāni tadavabodhasamarthāni, tathā tāny avipakvāni karmabhiḥ pratibaddhaśaktīni na bhāvāntarānubhūtam arthajātam upalabhante | atra dṛṣṭāntaḥ — ādarśasya yathā sahajāyāṃ rūpāvalokanaśaktau malena pratibaddhāyāṃ na rūpālokanakṣamo 'sau bhavati tathātmāpy apakvakaraṇaḥ | yady api sarvāntaḥkaraṇeṣu jalapātrakalpeṣu sūryavad eka evātmāvabhāsate, tataś copādhyantarāvacchinne 'smin sukhaduḥkānubhavo jāyamāno 'nyopādhyupahitena tenaiva pratisaṃdhātavya iti nyāyyaṃ bhavati, tathāpi sukhaduḥkhayor upādhyantarasamavetayor aindriyaktavaṃ paripakve caindriya sukhādigrahanāsambhavāt tadavicchinnānubhavo na śakyaḥ pratisaṃdhātum iti || 3.140 ||
sann apy arthaḥ paripākam antareṇa na prakāśat ity atra dṛṣṭāntam āha |
kaṭūrvārau yathāpakve madhuraḥ san raso 'pi na |
prāpyate hy ātmani tathā nāpakvakaraṇe jñatā || 3.141 ||
apakve kaṭūrvārau cidbhiṭaviśeṣe yathā sann api madhuro raso na prāpyate [1002] nopalabhyate, tathaivāpakvakaraṇe 'ntarātmani jñatā vyavahitaviprakṛṣṭārthaboddhṛtā saty api na prāpyate | kaṭuśabdo 'yaṃ na tasaviśeṣe vartate, kiṃ tu jātiviśeṣe || 3.141 ||
uktam evārthaṃ spaṣṭayitum āha |
sarvāśrayāṃ nije dehe dehī vindati vedanām |
yogayuktaś ca sarvāsāṃ jñātā nāpnoti vedanām || 3.142 ||
dehī dehaviṣayāhaṃpratyayavān sarvāśrayāṃ śiraḥpāṇipādādisakaladehāvayavāyattodayāṃ vedanāṃ sukhaduḥkhātmikāṃ nija eva dehe vindaty upalabhate | yas tu yogayuktaḥ san siddhayoge nivṛttadehaviṣayāhaṃprayayaḥ sarvāsāṃ sarvakṣetravartinīnāṃ vedanānāṃ jñātā veditā bhavati na cāsau sarvapuruṣavartinīṃ vedanāṃ prāpnoti | svābhāvyatvena pratipadyate paraḥ sukhādimān na punar aham iti pratipadyata ity arthaḥ || 3.142 ||
nanu sarvāntaḥkaraṇeṣu yadaika evātmā bhavet tato yoginaḥ sarvavedanāboddhṛtopapadyate, tad evātmaikyam aham iti bhedapratyaye 'nupapannam ity atrāha |
ākāśam ekaṃ hi yathā ghaṭādiṣu pṛthag bhavet |
tathātmaiko hy anekaś ca jalādhāreṣv ivāṃśumān || 3.143 ||
anaupādhikaṃ hi nānātvam ekena virudhyate na tv aupādhikam | dṛśyate hy ekasyāpi vyomno ghaṭādyupādhivaśān nānātvaṃ ghaṭākāśaḥ kusūlākāśa iti | yathā ca bhinneṣu jalādhāreṣv eko 'py aṃśumān nāneti pratīyate, evam eko 'py ātmāntaḥkaraṇarūpopādhibhedād ayam aham iti nānātvenāvabhāsate | tataś ca svābhāvikam ekatvam aupādhikaṃ ca nānātvam ity avirodhaḥ | na tve(cai)katvapratyaye sati nānātvam aupādhikaṃ syāt sa eva ca kuta iti vācyam | āgamād aham iti pratyakṣatvāc caikatvasiddheḥ | bhavati hi sarveṣāṃ jantūnāṃ grāhyāvabhāse vilakṣaṇo grāhako 'him ity avabhāsaḥ || 3.143 ||
nanu grāhako vyomāder bhūtadhātor anyo nāstīty ata āha |
[1003]
brahmakhānilatejāṃsi jalaṃ bhūś ceti dhātavaḥ |
ime lokā eṣa cātmā tasmāc ca sacarācaram || 3.144 ||
brahmādayaḥ ṣaḍ dhātavo dhārayitāro na punar vyomādayo lokāḥ | lokyanta iti lokā grāhyā iti yāvat | na ca grāhyā eva grāhakā bhavitum arhanti | aham idaṃ gṛhṇāmīti grāhyād bhinnatayaiva grāhakasyāvabhāsāt | tasmād eṣa brahmadhātur evātmā sarvasya grāhakaḥ | ata eva "brahma sarvānubhūḥ" ity upaniṣady uktam [BāU 2.5.20] | yady evaṃ grāhyāt sarvasmād acetanād grāhakasya cetanasya bheda eva tataś ca "sarvaṃ khalv idaṃ brahma" ityādiśrutivirodha ity etatparihārāyoktam — tasmāc ca sacarācaram iti | tasmād brahmaṇaś cetanāt sacarācaraṃ jagaj jāyata iti śeṣaḥ | tataś ca "janikartuḥ prakṛtiḥ" ity etallakṣaṇoktaṃ prakṛtir bhinnaiva, tathā sati hi suvarṇakuṇḍalaṃ mṛdghaṭa iti sāmānādhikaraṇyapratyayo nopapadyate | sa hy atyantabhinnebhyo daṇḍadevadattādibhyo vyāvṛtta iti | na hi bhavati daṇḍo devadatta iti, kiṃ tu daṇḍavān devadatta iti | tathātyantābhinnebhyo 'pi nivṛtto na hi bhavati devadatto devadatta iti kiṃ tu devadatta ity eva, tena brahmaivedaṃ sarvam iti sāmānādhikaraṇyapratyayo vākyajanyo brahmaṇā saha jagata ātyantikād bhedād abhedāc ca vilakṣaṇaṃ bhedābhedatādātmyādipadāspadībhūtaṃ prakāraṃ vyavasthāpayati | na ca sarvaṃ khalv idaṃ brahmeti gauṇas tādātmyavyapadeśo gaur bāhīka itivad iti vācyam | pramāṇāntare gobāhīkayor atyantabhedam avagamya vyapadeśyagauṇatvam avasīyate | na ca jagadbrahmaṇo atyantabhedagrāhakaṃ kiṃcana pramāṇam asti | yas tu grāhyagrāhakatayā bhedapratyayaḥ sa bhedaṃ vidhatte nābhedaṃ niṣedhāt | brahmaprapañcayor abhedavidhāyikā śrutir udīritaiva | yasmāt tayos tv abhedo 'pi bheda eva tasmāt sacarācaram ity atra jāyata ity adhyāhāryam | anyathā "sarvaṃ khalv idaṃ brahma" iti śrutivirodho duṣparihāraḥ syāt || 3.144 ||
yathātmā carācaraṃ sṛjati tathāha | [1004]
mṛddaṇḍacakrasaṃyogāt kumbhakāro yathā ghaṭam |
karoti tṛṇamṛtkāṣṭhair gṛhaṃ vā gṛhakārakaḥ || 3.145 ||
hemamātrām upādāya rūpaṃ vā hemakārakaḥ |
nijalālāsamāyogāt kośaṃ vā kośakārakaḥ || 3.146 ||
kāraṇāny evam ādāya tāsu tāsv ih yoniṣu |
sṛjaty ātmānam ātmaiva saṃbhūya karaṇāni ca || 3.147 ||
yathā kumbhakāraḥ kumbhasya prakṛtibhūtāyā mṛdo daṇḍādīnāṃ cāprakṛtibhūtānām anyonyasamavadhānāt kumbhaḥ karoti | yathā vā gṛhakartā gṛhopādānakāraṇais tṛṇamṛtkāṣṭhair anyonyasaṃyuktair gṛhaṃ nirmimīte | yathā hemakārakaḥ suvarṇaikadeśaṃ gṛhītvā rūpānugrāhakaṃ bhūṣaṇaṃ kaṭakādi karoti | yathā ca kośakārakaḥ śastraśarīrasaṃjñakaḥ kīṭo nijalālayaiva kośaṃ janayati, evam ātmānekāsu yoniṣv ātmānam ātmīyaṃ śarīraṃ tatkāraṇāni ca pṛthvyādīni karaṇānīndriyāṇi cādāya sahakāritvena svīkṛtya sa ātmā bhūyaḥ punaḥ punaḥ sṛjati | yady api bhūtādīni sahakārīṇi nātmano bhidyante tathāpi teṣāṃ sahakāritvam upapadyate | yathā kośakārakaṃ prati tallālāyā iti vaktuṃ kośakāradṛṣṭāntaḥ || 3.145, 146, 147 ||
buddhīndriyaśarīravyatirikte hy ātmani prāmāṇike sati śāstreṇa tasya brahmībhāvaḥ "tat tvam asi" ityādinā pratipādyate, ataś ca brahmakhānilatejāṃsīty upapadyata iti kṣetrajñasya prāmāṇikatāṃ tāvad āha |
mahābhūtāni satyāni yathātmāpi tathaiva hi |
ko 'nyathaikena netreṇa dṛṣṭam anyena paśyati || 3.148 ||
vācaṃ vā ko vijānāti punaḥ saṃśrutya saṃśrutām |
atītārthā smṛtiḥ kasya ko vā svapnasya kārakaḥ || 3.149 ||
yathā kāraṇadoṣabādhakapratyayarahitāni vijñānavedyatvā ākāśādīni mahābhūtāni satyāni, tathātmāpy ahaṃpratyaye bhāsamānaḥ saty eva | na cāhaṃpratyaya [1005] indriyālambanaḥ | tathā hy ekena netreṇa dṛṣṭārtho netrāntareṇa na pratyabhijñāyate, grāhakanetrānyatvāt | na hy anyena gṛhītam anyaḥ pratyabhijānīte | jñānamātrālambanena cāhaṃpratyayasyāpūrvavarṇātmikāṃ vācaṃ saṃśrutya punas tāṃ śrutāṃ saiveyaṃ vāg iti ko vijānāti, na kaścid ity arthaḥ | na hi kṣaṇikāni vijñānāny anyonyaṃ pratyabhijānanti, pūrvottarapratyayayor hy ekasmin pratyetari pratyetavye ca pratyabhijñā bhavati na punar anyathā | tasmāt sthāyī buddhyādivyatirikta ātmāstīti niścīyate | tathā sthāyiny ātmany asaty atītārthaviṣayā smṛtir na kasyacit syāt | arthasya cātītatā pradarśaṇārthā, tena jñānendriyātmavādinos tadatyaye smṛtidarśanānupapattiḥ smartur abhāvāt | tasmād api sthāyyātmasiddhiḥ | tathā sthirātmābhāve svapnajñānakartā ko bhavet, na kaścid ity arthaḥ | tasyāṃ daśāyāṃ jñānāntarāṇi na santi | indriyāṇi coparatavyāpārāṇīti naitāni tatkārakāṇi | vijñānavāde cendriyāṇy api na santi, tena manaso 'pi svapnakartṛtvam anāśaṅkanīyam | na ca dehaś cetayate, tadārambhakadravyāṇām acetanatvāt | teṣām api cetanatve tadārabdhānāṃ ghaṭādidravyāṇāṃ caitanyaprāptiḥ | tataś cāsitatantvārabdhaḥ paṭo yathā na sitaḥ, tadvad acetanadravyārbdho deho 'py acetanaḥ || 3.148, 149 ||
kiṃ ca |
jātirūpavayovittavidyādibhir ahaṃkṛtaḥ |
śabdādiviṣaye saktaḥ karmaṇā manasā girā || 3.150 ||
yadi sthāyinam ekam ātmānaṃ loko na pratisaṃdhatte tadā kasya brāhmaṇyādijātyā gauravattvādinā ca rūpeṇa yauvanena ca vayasā vittavidyābhyāṃ cāhaṃkṛto garvitaḥ ko bhavet | na hi jñānānāṃ kṣaṇikānāṃ jātyādinimittāhaṃkārasaṃbhavaḥ | tathā śabdādiviṣayopabhoge vāṅmanaḥkarmāsaktiḥ kasya || 3.150 ||
uktāny ātmani kānicit pramāṇāni, bandhaviśeṣam adhunā tatpramāṇatvenāha |
sa saṃdighamatiḥ karmaphalam asti na veti vā |
saṃplutaḥ siddham ātmānam asiddho 'pi hi manyate || 3.151 ||
[1006]        mama dārāḥ sutāmātyā aham eṣām iti sthitiḥ |
hitāhiteṣu bhāveṣu viparītamatiḥ sadā || 3.152 ||
jñeye ca prakṛtau caiva vikāre cāviśeṣavān |
anāśakānalāpātajalaprapatanodyamī || 3.153 ||
evaṃvṛtto 'vinītātmā vitathābhiniveśavān |
karmaṇā dveṣamohābhyām icchayā caiva badhyate || 3.154 ||
saṃsārātmabandhaḥ khalu buddhīndriyaśarīrāṇāṃ na saṃbhavati, na hi teṣām anekeṣu janmasv anuvṛttir astīti bhavati buddhyādivilakṣaṇātmasadbhāve pramāṇam, tasya āyam upakramaḥ — śāstravihitasya karmaṇaḥ phalaṃ svargādikam asti vā na veti saṃdigdhamatiḥ saṃsārī bhavati | anena ca śāstraprāmāṇye saṃśaya(yi)tvam ucyate | tatra ca saṃśayānaḥ śāstram atikrāmatīti tātparyārthaḥ | atra ca ślokārdhāntagato vāśabdo 'stīty anena yojanīyaḥ | tathā saṃpluto vipluto 'nātmani śarīra ātmapratyayavān iti yāvat | vastuto 'siddham anutpannam apy ātmānaṃ siddham utpannaṃ manyate | atha vātmani pāramārthike kenopaniṣadvedyena rūpeṇāsiddham anavagatam api kartṛtvabhoktṛtvaviśiṣṭena rūpeṇa siddham ātmānaṃ manyate | etāvad evātmano rūpam iti manyata ity arthaḥ | anātmany ātmabuddheś ca mama saṃbandhina ete dārādayo 'ham eteṣāṃ saṃbandhīti sthitiḥ parvasānaṃ bhavati | tathā hitāhiteṣv anukūlapratikūleṣu bhāveṣu viparyastamatir bhavati | hitam ahitatvenāhitaṃ ca hitatvena manyata ity arthaḥ | tathā jñeya ātmayāthātmye prakṛtāv avyākṛtavikāre ca tatkārye mahadahaṃkārādāv aviśeṣavān, santam apy eṣāṃ viśeṣaṃ na vettīty arthaḥ | tathā duḥkharahitam apy ātmānaṃ duḥkhitaṃ manthamānas taddhānāyānaśanāgnipraveśādinā dehatyāgodyamī bhavati | evaṃvṛtta evaṃceṣṭito 'vinītasvatvāvabodhavidhura ātmā yasya sa tathoktaḥ | vitathe mithyābhūte vedāprāmāṇyanairātmyādāv arthe 'bhiniveśavāṃs tatheti pratītimān | karmaṇā kāmyena niṣiddhena ca dveṣeṇāprītyā mohenāvidayā, icchayā rāgeṇa ca badhyate saṃsarya(sriya)ta iti || 3.151, 152, 153, 154 ||
adhunā mokṣaṃ sopāyam ātmani ślokacatuṣṭayenāha | [1007]
ācāryopāsanaṃ vedaśāstrasya ca vivecanam |
tatkarmaṇām anuṣṭhānaṃ saṅgaḥ sadbhir giraḥ śubhāḥ || 3.155 ||
stryālokālambhavigamaḥ sarvabhūtātmadarśanam |
tyāgaḥ parigrahāṇāṃ ca jīrṇakāṣāyadhāraṇam || 3.156 ||
viṣayendriyasaṃrodhas tandrālasyavivarjanam |
śarīraparisaṃkhyānaṃ pravṛttiṣv aghadarśanam || 3.157 ||
nīrajastamatā sattvaśuddhir nispṛhatā śamaḥ |
etair upāyaiḥ saṃśuddhaḥ saṃ ca yogy amṛtī bhavet || 3.158 ||
ācāryaḥ prasiddhas tasyopāsanaṃ paricaryā, vedasyānyeṣāṃ purāṇādīnāṃ ca viviktārthajñatā, śāstroktānāṃ nityanaimittikānāṃ karmaṇām anuṣṭhānam | sadbhir yogibhiḥ saṅgas tattve(ttva) parāmarṣa(śa)prayojanaṃ sahāvasthānam | giraḥ śubhā hitamitasatyarūpāḥ | strīviṣayālokanasparśanayor vigamo varjanam | aikātmyajñānarūpaṃ sarvabhūteṣv ātmanas teṣāṃ vā svātmani darśanam | parigrahāṇāṃ svīkārāṇāṃ tyāgaḥ | jīrṇasya purāṇasya kāṣāyasya vṛkṣaniryāsādiraktasya vāsaso dhāraṇam | viṣayāṇām indriyāṇāṃ ca saṃrodhaḥ saṃnikarṣanirodhaḥ | tandrā nidrātulyo jāḍyaviśeṣaḥ | ālasyam anudyamaḥ | tayos tyāgaḥ | śarīrasya sarvato 'sthiratvāśucitvādinā saṃkhyānaṃ samyaṅnirūpaṇaṃ ca kāryam | sarvāsu cānāvaśyakīṣu pravṛttiṣu pāpadarśanam | āvaśyakam eva laukikaṃ vaidikaṃ ca karma kāryaṃ nānyad ity arthaḥ | rajastamasor udrekaparihāro rājasatāmasāhārādiparihāreṇa kāryaḥ | sattvaśuddhiḥ prāṇāyāmaśuddhāhāratvādinā vidheyā | nispṛhatā nirīhatā dhanādiṣv anīpsā | śamaḥ parāparādhakṣamā | etair uktair upāyaiḥ saṃśuddho vipāpmā sattvodrekayogī sann amṛtī bhaved ity arthaḥ || 3.155, 156, 157, 158 ||
sa cāyaṃ bhaved brahmībhāvo dehādibhyo vyāvṛttas tadvyatiriktātmasadbhāve pramāṇaṃ yogamuteḥ kāraṇam iti tadupāyam āha | [1008]
tattvasmṛter avasthānāt sattvayogāt parikṣayāt |
karmaṇāṃ saṃnikarṣāc ca satāṃ yogaḥ pravartate || 3.159 ||
tattvasya paramātmanaḥ śāstrāvagatarūpasya sātatyena smṛtyudayāc chuddhasattvayogān niṣiddhakāmyakarmaṇāṃ yathāsaṃbhavaṃ brahmajñānaphalopabhogābhyāṃ parikṣayāt satāṃ sādhūnāṃ yogināṃ ca saṃnikarṣāt saṅgato yogaḥ pravartate || 3.159 ||
idānīm asiddhayogaṃ yoginaṃ praty āha |
śarīrasaṃkṣaye yasya manaḥ sattvastham īśvaram |
aviplutasmṛtiḥ samyak sa jātismaratām iyāt || 3.160 ||
śarīrasaṃkṣaye maraṇakāle yasya yogam ārurukṣato manaḥ sattvasthaṃ rajastamasī vihāya sattva eva vartamānam īśvaraṃ maraṇadaśābhāvinā duḥkhenopa(nā)pīḍyamānaṃ yasya so 'viplutaśvarasmṛtir janmāntare sakalapūrvajanmasmṛtiṃ dṛḍhataravairāgyanimittabhūtāṃ prāpnuyāt || 3.160 ||
idānīṃ tv ayoginaṃ praty āha |
yathā hi bharato varṇair varṇayaty ātmanas tanum |
nānārūpāṇi kurvāṇas tathātmā karmajās tanūḥ || 3.161 ||
yathā bharato naṭo nānāvidhāni rāmarāvaṇādirūpāṇi kurvāṇo varṇair varṇakaiḥ sitāsitādibhir ātmanas tanuṃ varṇayati tathātmāpy akṣīṇakarmā karmajās tanūḥ śarīrāṇi suranarādyātmakāni gṛhṇāti | bharatadṛṣṭāntenātmanaḥ śarīrayoga aupādhiko na svābhāvika iti gamayati | karmajā iti vadan karmanāṃ upādhitvaṃ darśayati || 3.161 ||
rūpanānātve 'pi hetvantaram astīty āha |
kālakarmātmabījānāṃ doṣair mātus tathaiva ca |
garbhasya vaikṛtaṃ dṛṣṭam aṅgahīnādi janmanaḥ || 3.162 ||
garbhasyāṅgeṣu śiraḥpāṇyādiṣu śrotranetrādiṣu ca hīnatvātiriktatvādi vaikṛtaṃ vikṛtarūpatvaṃ kālakarmabījamātṝṇāṃ doṣair bhavatīti śāstradṛṣṭam | na caitad garbhakāla [1009] kāla eva kiṃ tu janmana ūrdhvaṃ(dhva)kāle 'pi | kālasya doṣo grahadauḥsthityam | karmadoṣaḥ śraddhādivaidhurya (?) karmāntarasya ca pratiṣiddhatvam | karmātra dharmaḥ | bhūtātmanaś ca doṣo dhātuvaiṣamyam | bījadoṣaḥ pitṛdoṣaḥ | mātṛdoṣo garbhaviruddhaceṣṭākartṛtvam | aṅgavikāre kāṇādidoṣāṇāṃ nimittatvam atroktam | tanūtpattinimittatvaṃ ca karmaṇāṃ pūrvaśloke, tena pūrvāparāvirodhaḥ || 3.162 ||
prathamaśarīrādyutpattiḥ katham iti ca na vācyam anāditvāc charīraparampara(rā)yā ity āha |
ahaṃkāreṇa manasā gatyā karmaphalena ca |
śarīreṇa ca nātmāyaṃ muktapūrvaḥ kathaṃcana || 3.163 ||
dātā satyaḥ kṣamī prājñaḥ śubhakarmā jitendriyaḥ |
taporato yogaśīlo na rogair abhibhūyate || 3.164 ||
gatir dharmātmikā, karmaphalaṃ sukhaduḥkhabhāvānām anubhavaḥ | prasiddham anyat || 3.163, 164 ||
yadi karmabhir dehinaḥ phalopabhogāya deha ārabhyate tarhi tatphalopabhogasamāpteḥ prāṅ na dehena dehino viyogaḥ saṃbhavatīty akālamṛtyunivāraṇāya karmavidyānarthakyam ity atrāha |
vartyādhārasnehayogād yathā dīpasya saṃsthitiḥ |
vikriyāpi ca dṛṣṭaivam akāle deha(prāṇa)saṃkṣayaḥ || 3.165 ||
yathā vartyādīnāṃ yogād dīpasya vartikāvartinyā jvālāyāḥ saṃsthitir avasthānaṃ vartyādiyoge saty api vikriyā prabalapavanābhighātādinā dīpapradhvaṃso 'pi dṛṣṭaḥ, evam akāle dehārambhakakarmaphalopabhogāsamāptāv api tatparipanthikarmaṇā prabandhe prāṇasaṃkṣayo 'pi saṃbhavatīti | tataś ca paripanthikarmanivāraṇārtham akālamṛtyuśāntiphalāḥ karmavidhaya upapannāḥ || 3.165 ||
uktayogavaśād amṛtībhāvas tanmārgam idānīm āha |
anantā raśmayas tasya dīpavad yaḥ sthito hṛdi |
sitāsitā babhrunīlāḥ kapilā nīlalohitāḥ || 3.166 ||
[1010]        ūrdhvam ekaḥ sthitas teṣāṃ yo bhittvā sūryamaṇḍalam |
brahmalokam atikramya tena yāti parāṃ gatim || 3.167 ||
tasya jīvasya sūryasya vānantā asaṃkhyātā raśmayas te ca sitāsitādyanekavarṇās teṣāṃ madhye raśmir ekā(ka) ūrvavāmī sūryamaṇḍalaṃ bhittvā brahmalokaṃ cātikramya sthitaḥ | tena raśminā mārgeṇa parāṃ gatiṃ paraṃ gantavyaṃ brahma yāti | hitāhitasaṃjñakanāḍīcakramadhyagatasya śaśisūryamaṇḍalasyaite raśmayo na tu jīvasya | upacāreṇa hi tasya te 'bhidhīyante | nanu ca: "nāsya prāṇā utkrāmanti" ityādiśrutiviruddhaiṣā smṛtiḥ | maivam, "tayordhvam āyann amṛtattvam eti" itipratyakaśrutimūlatvād asyāḥ smṛteḥ | tena śrutyor evātra parasparaṃ virodho na śrutimṛtyoḥ | na ca śrutyor api paramārthato virodho 'sti | nāsya prāṇā vyutkrāmantīty atra punar utpattaya iti vākyaśeṣaḥ prakalpyate śrutyantarāvirodhāya | yad vā prāṇānāṃ tasya prabhutvād avaśam iva taṃ parityajya na yānti, kiṃ tu svecchayā dehaṃ tyajantīty arthaḥ | tathātraiva samavanīyanta ity atrātraiva janmani samavanīyante na janmāntara iti vyākhyā na punar atraiva śarīra iti || 3.166, 167 ||
kiṃ ca |
yad asyānyad raśmiśatam ūrdhvam eva vyavasthitam |
tena devaśarīrāṇi sadhāmāni prapadyate || 3.168 ||
paramagatimārgabhūtād raśmer anyad raśmiśatam asyātmana ūrdhvāṃ diśaṃ prati vyavasthitam | tenāsmāc charīrād utkrāmann ayaṃ jīvo devajātīyāni śarīrāṇi devānāṃ dhāmnā sthānena saha prapadyate | devaloke devo bhavatīty arthaḥ | śarīrāṇīti bahuvacanam ekasyām eva jātyākyāyām || 3.168 ||
idānīṃ saṃsaraṇamārgam āha |
yenaikarūpās cādhastād raśmayo 'sya mitaprabhāḥ |
iha karmopabhogāya taiḥ saṃsarati so 'vaśaḥ || 3.169 ||
[1011]
asyātmano hṛdayadeśasthitasyādhastān mandaprabhā anekaraśmayas tair itaḥ śarīrān nirgacchann atraiva loke karmaphalabhogāya saṃsarati, dehāntaraṃ karmavaśaḥ saṃpratipadyate || 3.169 ||
pramāṇataḥ sādhitam apy ātmānaṃ dārḍhyārthaṃ punar api pramāṇāntaraiḥ sādhayati |
vedaiḥ śāstraiḥ savijñānair janmanā maraṇena ca |
ārtyā gatyā tathāgatyā satyena hy anṛtena ca || 3.170 ||
śreyasā sukhaduḥkhābhyāṃ karmabhiś ca śubhāśubhaiḥ |
nimittaśākunajñānagrahasaṃyogajaiḥ phalaiḥ || 3.171 ||
tārānakṣatrasaṃcārair jāgaraiḥ svaprajaiḥ phalaiḥ |
ākāśapavanajyotir jalabhūtimirais tathā || 3.172 ||
manvantarair yugaprāptyā mantrauṣadhiphalair api |
vittātmānaṃ vi(ve)dyamānaṃ sarvasya(kāraṇaṃ) jagatas tathā || 3.173 ||
he maharṣayo vedādibhiḥ pramāṇair buddhīndriyaśarīravyatiriktam ātmānaṃ vi(ve)dyamānaṃ vitta jānīta | tatraiva vedaiḥ karmakāṇḍātmakair agnihotrādikartṛgāmisvargādiphalam ācakṣāṇair ātmā prasādhyate | dehādīnām ātmatve kartṛgāmi phalaṃ na syāt | upaniṣadbhāgātmakais tu vedaiḥ: "avināśī vāre'yam ātmānucchittidharmā" ity evamādibhiḥ śrutyaiva dehādyatiriktātmasadbhāvo 'vabodhyate | śāstraiś ca mīmāṃsādibhir ātmā(tma)sādhakayuktibodhakair vijñānasahitair ātmāvabodhyate | tatra śāstrāṇy ātmasādhakayuktiprasādhanāni, vijñānāni śāstrāṇy ātmasākṣātkārakāraṇaprāṇāyāmādipradarśakāni | janmāpi sukhaduḥkhahetubhūtatvena vicitratvopapādakau dharmādharmau kalpayat tadāśrayam ātmānaṃ gamayati | evaṃ maraṇam api tad dhi dṛṣṭe tatkāraṇe samāne 'pi kasyacid eva bhavadadṛṣṭasya vyavasthāpakatvenākṣipadātmānam api tadāśrayam ākṣipati | maraṇasaṃbandhinyārtyā prāṇiṣu stokāstokarūpeṇa vartamānayā svopapādakādharmālpānalpatvakalpanāmukhenātmāpi kalpyate | tathā [1012] tyajyamānād dehāt sukhaduḥkhopabhogāya svargaṃ narakaṃ vā gacchati | svargaṃ narakaṃ vopabhujya vṛṣṭyudakena sahemaṃ lokam āgacchaty ātmeti upaniṣadādyāgamaprasiddhyā gatyā cāgatyā ca dehādivyatirikta ātmā sādhyate | satyaṃ yathārthaṃ vākyam | anṛtam ayathārtham, tābhyām ātmā sādhyate | na khalu tam antareṇa satyatvāsatyatve sidhyataḥ | yato 'yam arthaṃ vākyād avagatavān asmi, tam eva pramāṇāntareṇa pratyemīti pratyabhijānan vākyasya satyatvam adhyavasyati | viparyayeṇa satyatvam uktanītyā ca vākyārthaṃ na buddhyādayaḥ pratyabhijānantīti tadvyatiriktātmasiddhiḥ | śreyaḥ puruṣaprītiḥ | tena ca puruṣaḥ pramīyate | evaṃ sukhaduḥkhābhyām apy ātmā sādhyate | dharmādharmayuktātmasādhyatvāt tayos tulyavyutpattibhis tulyakulotpannaiḥ kartṛbhiḥ kaiścic chubhāni dharmyāṇi karmāṇi kriyante, kaiścit tadviparītāni | tatra janmāntarīyadharmādharmānuṣṭhānābhyāsavāsanābhedo vyavasthāpakatayātmasamavāyī kalpya iti śubhāśubhakarmaṇām ātmani prāmānyam | nimittam upaśrutyādi | śākunaṃ śakunīnāṃ gatisvanādi, tad eva pratyāsannaphalasya śubhāśubhakarmaṇo jñāpakatvāj jñānam | grahā ādityādayaḥ | teṣāṃ ca parasparaṃ rāśibhir vā saṃbandhaḥ saṃyogas tatsūcitāni dharmādharmaphalāny ātmani pramāṇam | nimittasūcitaṃ hy adṛṣṭaṃ janmāntarājitam api saṃbhavati, tac ca dehādyatiriktam ātmānam antareṇa na saṃbhavatīti nimittād evātmani prāmāṇyam | nakṣatrāṇy aśvinyādīni | uḍūni (?) tārās teṣu grahāṇāṃ saṃcāras tathā jāgraddaśābhāvīni naktaṃcaradarśanādīni | svapnajāni ca kharādyārohaṇādidarśanāni | tatsūcitaiś ca phalaiḥ pūrvavad ātmānumānam | ākāśādibhiḥ śarīrendriyaviṣayādirūpeṇa bhogyatayāvastitair bhoktānumīyate | manvantarāṇāṃ svāyaṃbhuvādīnāṃ ca yugānāṃ kṛtatretādīnāṃ ca vilakṣaṇasvabhāvatvenātmasādhakatvam | uktaṃ ca manunā,
arogāḥ sarvasiddhārthāś caturvarṣaśatāyuṣaḥ |
kṛte tretādiṣu tv eṣāṃ vayo hrasati pādaśaḥ ||
catuṣpāt sakalo dharmaḥ satyaṃ caiva kṛte yuge |
caurikānṛtamāyābhir dharmaś cāpaiti pādaśaḥ ||
ityādi | tataś ca kṛtayuge prāṇināṃ dharmopacayaḥ kalāv adharmopacayas teṣām evety anekayugasthāyiny ātmani manvantarādisthitiḥ pramāṇam | evaṃ sa eva mantras tad evauṣadhaṃ kasyacid [1013] abhīṣṭaphaladaṃ kasyacin neti vyavasthāṃ ghaṭayituṃ janmāntarārjitādṛṣṭāśraya ātmānumeya iti mantrauṣadhaphalam apy ukte 'rthe pramāṇam || 3.170, 171, 172, 173 ||
kiṃ ca |
ahaṃkāraḥ smṛtir medhā dveṣo buddhiḥ sukhaṃ dhṛtiḥ |
indriyāntarasaṃcāra icchā dhāraṇajīvite || 3.174 ||
svargaḥ svapnaś ca bhāvānāṃ prereṇaṃ manaso 'gatiḥ |
unmeṣaś cetanā yatna ādānaṃ pāñcabhautikam || 3.175 ||
yata etāni liṅgāni dṛśyante paramātmanaḥ |
tasmād asti paro dehād ātmā sarvajña īśvaraḥ || 3.176 ||
yasmād ahaṃkārādīni liṅgāny ātmano 'numāpakāni dṛśyante tasmād asti dehād anyaḥ parama āntara ātmā | sarvajñaḥ sarvānubhāvitā | īśvaraḥ svāmī | viśeṣānupādānāt sarvasyāhaṃkāro 'haṃpratyaya idaṃpratyayavedye dehe nopapadyate | na hy abhnāntasya (?) premeyaviśeṣe tadanyavastusaṃbandhī pratyayo ghaṭate | tathā mama śarīram iti pratyayaḥ śarīrād anyasyāhaṃbhāve ghaṭate nānyathā | mamātmeti ca vyapadeśo lākṣaṇikaḥ | nātmany ātmaśabdaḥ | anyathā mamāham ity uktaṃ syāt | evam ahaṃkṛtiḥ śarīrād anyasyātmano liṅgam | smṛtiś ca janmāntarānubhūte stanapānādau bhavantī sukhasādhanatvena bhavaty ātmani liṅgam | na hy adhunā tena dehena prāgbhavīyastanapānasyāpekṣitopāyatvam anubhūtaṃ tena sa eva smartā syāt | medhā granthadhāraṇaśaktir janmāntarīyatadgranthaviṣayānubhāvajanitaṃ saṃskārātiśayaṃ kalpayati | dehātirekiṇi saṃskāravaty ātmani liṅgam | dveṣo 'pi saṃsāre kasyacit prāktanārthaparicayāj jāyamāna ātmani liṅgam | sa hi doṣasmaraṇād bhavati | tac ca janmāntarādhīnam iti bhavaty ātmani liṅgam | buddhiḥ prajñā, sāpi janmāntarāyattety ātmani liṅgam | sukham ānandaḥ | nāsau śarīraguṇaḥ | vaiśeṣikaguṇatve saty akāraṇaguṇapūrvaktvāt | kāraṇaguṇapūrvako hi yāvaccharīrabhāvī syāt | tasmān nāyaṃ śarīrākārapariṇatānāṃ bhūtānāṃ guṇaḥ kiṃ tu tato 'nyasyeti bhavaty ātmani liṅgam | evaṃ dvaṃdvasahiṣṇutvalakṣaṇā ghṛtir api | indriyāntarasaṃcāra indriyāntaraṃ prati [1014] gatiḥ | sā dehasya sarvendriyavyāpino nopapadyate | ātmanaḥ punar aṇuparimāṇāntaḥkaraṇopahitasyāṇor indriyasaṃcāro yujyate | ata eva bṛhadāraṇyake: "aṇīyān nīvārataṇḍulād aṇīyāñ śyāmākataṇḍulāt" ity uktam | icchāpi janmāntarānubhūtasmṛtārthaviṣayā paramātmaliṅgam | dhāraṇajīvite api | dhāraṇaṃ śarīrasya na taccharīreṇaiva śakyaṃ kartum | ekaikasyāṃ kriyāyāṃ karmakartṛtvavirodhāt | śarīram eva dhāryaṃ tad eva dhārakam iti nopapadyate | jīvitaṃ prāṇadhāraṇaṃ tad api na śarīrakārthaṃ prayatnasādhyatvāt | prayatnaś ca na śarīraguṇaḥ | ayāvaddravyabhāvitvāt pākāntarānivartyatvāc ca | svarga(go) lokāntarabhogyaṃ sātiśayaṃ sukham | sa dehavyatirikta ātmā tūpapadyate (ātmany upapadyate) | anyathā kṛtanāśākṛtābhyupagamaprasaṅgaḥ syāt | svapnapratyaya ātmaliṅgam | iha janmanya[na]nubhūtasya svapne darśanaṃ janmāntarīyānubhavāhitasaṃskāravaśād bhavatīy ātmaliṅgatā svapnasya | bhāvānāṃ śarīrendriyāṇāṃ svakārye preraṇam ātmaliṅgam | na hy anyas tatprerako 'pi | manaso 'tisūkṣmasyānekadvāre śarīre 'vasthitasyāgatir apatanam ātmāśritādṛṣṭanimittam ātmaliṅgam | unmeṣaś ca prayatnavad ātmakāryatvād ātmaliṅgam | cetanā jñānam acetanārabdhāc charīrād anyaś cetanaṃ (anyaṃ cetanaṃ) gamayati | yatnaś ca śarīrāśrito na bhavatīti svāśrayam ātmānam anumāpayati | dehatvena pañcānāṃ bhūtānāṃ parigrahaḥ svīkartary ātmani liṅgam || 3.174, 175, 176 ||
ātmana uktāṃ sarvajñatām upapādayann āha |
buddhīndriyāṇi sārthāni manaḥ karmendriyāṇi ca |
ahaṃkāraś ca buddhiś ca pṛthivyādīni caiva hi || 3.177 ||
avyaktam ātmā kṣetrajñaḥ kṣetrasyāsya nigadyate |
īśvaraḥ sarvabhūtasthaḥ sann asan sad asac ca yaḥ || 3.178 ||
buddher gandhādisākṣātkārasya karaṇatayā saṃbandhīni ghrāṇādīni pañca buddhīndriyāṇi | tāni cārthair gandhādibhir viṣayaiḥ sahitāni sārthāni | mano 'ntaḥkaraṇam | karmendriyāṇi vāgādīni | buddhyahaṃkārau, pṛthivyādīni pañca bhūtāni, avyaktaṃ pradhānam ity etasya kṣetrasyātmā sarveśvaraḥ sarvabhūtahṛdaye bhāsamāno 'ta eva san sa eva vibhaktayānavabhāsamānatayāsan | sadasadbhāvāvābhāvaprakṛtitvāj jñātā, tasmāt kṣetrajña iti tajjñair ucyate || 3.177, 178 ||
[1015]
buddhyādīnāṃ kāraṇāny āha |
buddher utpattir avyaktāt tato 'haṃkārasaṃbhavaḥ |
tanmātrādīny ahaṃkārād ekottaraguṇāni tu || 3.179 ||
buddher viṣayaniścayahetor antaḥkaraṇasyāvyaktāt pradhānād utpattiḥ | samāni sattvarajastamāṃsi avyaktam, tato buddher ahaṃkārasya jñānakriyākartṛtvābhimānino 'ntaḥkaraṇasya saṃbhavo janma | sūkṣmāḥ śabdasparśarūparasagandhās tanmātrāṇi tāny ādir eṣāṃ bhūtānām indriyāṇāṃ ca tāvanmātrādīni tāny ahaṃkārāj jāyante | bhūtāni tu tanmātravyavadhānena | ahaṃkāraś ca trividhaḥ, sattvikaḥ sa eva taijasa iti purāṇeṣu gīyate | tasmād buddhīndriyāṇām udayo rājato 'paraḥ | tasmād vaikārikasaṃjñāt karmendriyāṇāṃ tāmasas tṛtīyo bhūtādisaṃjñakas tasmāc chabdāditanmātrāṇi tebhyo yathākramaṃ vyomādibhūtāni tāni ca vakṣyamāṇaiḥ śabdādibhir guṇair ekottaraguṇāni | tatra vyomni śabda eko guṇo vāyoḥ sa ca sparśaś ca | tejasi tau ca rūpaṃ ca | ambhasi te ca rasaś ca, bhūmau gandhaś ca pañcamaḥ || 3.179 ||
guṇān āha |
śabdaḥ sparśaś ca rūpaṃ ca raso gandhaś ca tadguṇāḥ |
yo yasmān niḥsṛtaś caiṣāṃ sa tasminn eva līyate || 3.180 ||
ye vyomādīnām ekottaraguṇā uktās te śabdādayo gandhaparyantāḥ | yaś caiṣāṃ buddhyādīnāṃ madhye yasmāt kāraṇād avyaktādeḥ sṛṣṭikāle niḥsṛto 'bhivyaktaḥ sa tasminn eva kāraṇātmani pralayakāle līyate tirohito bhavati | tatra ca niḥsṛto līyata iti vadatā satkāryavādo darśitaḥ || 3.180 ||
prakaraṇārthopasaṃhāram āha |
yathātmānaṃ sṛjaty ātmā tathā vaḥ kathito mayā |
vipākāt triprakārāṇāṃ karmaṇām īśvaro 'pi san || 3.181 ||
īśvaraḥ svatantro 'pi sannātmā yathātmānaṃ dehavantaṃ mānasavācikakāyikatayā sāttvikarājasatāmasatayā trividhasya karmaṇo vipākapūrvaṃ tadupādānavaśāt sṛjati, tathā vo yuṣmabhyaṃ he manayo mayā kathitaḥ || 3.181 ||
[1016]
sattvaṃ rajas tamaś caiva guṇās tasya prakīrtitāḥ |
rajastamobhyām āviṣṭaś cakravad bhrāmyate hi saḥ || 3.182 ||
sattvādayo guṇās tasya kṣetrajñasya tannibandhanabandhamuktibhāvitatvāt, tad eva darśayat — rajastamobhyām ityādinā | arthāc ca kevalasattvodrekeṇa vimucyata ity api darśitam | cakradṛṣṭāntena cānavasthitatvam uktam, tataś ca tamasātmasvarūpāparijñānātmako mohaḥ | rajasā ca phale rāgāt karmasu pravṛttis tataḥ karmaphalasya jātyāyuṣoḥ sukhaduḥkhānubhavasya codaya ity eta cakravac cakram | etasya parivartanād atmāpi parivartata ity upacaryate || 3.182 ||
anādir ādimāṃś caiva sa eva puruṣaḥ paraḥ |
liṅgendriyagrāhyarūpaḥ savikāra udāhṛtaḥ || 3.183 ||
svarūpato 'nādir ādimaccharīrayogitvād ādimān | parama aupaniṣadaḥ puruṣa eva kṣetrajñaḥ samasvarūpato vikārataś ca yathāsaṃkhyaṃ liṅgendriyagrāhyaḥ | tatrāhaṃkārādibhir liṅgaiḥ kṣetrajñātmano rūpaṃ gṛhyate \ tadvikāras tu śabdādibhir indriyair evaṃvidha ukta uttaraślokārthatayānuvādaḥ || 3.183 ||
pitṛyāno 'javīthyāś ca tathā(yada)gastyasy cāntaram |
tenāgnihotriṇo yānti svargakāmā divaṃ prati || 3.184 ||||
ajavīthīm āha gargaḥ: "hastaś citrā viśākhikā ajavīthī" iti | kaśyapas tu śravaṇādinakṣatratrayam ajavīthīm āha: "ajavīthī viṣṇubhādyāḥ(?)" iti | tasyāś cāgastyasya yad antaraṃ sa pitṛyānasaṃjñako mārgaḥ | tena kāmyakarmakāriṇo divaṃ svargaṃ yānti | agnihotragrahaṇaṃ vaitānikakarmapradarśanārtham | duḥkhasaṃbhinnacirakālopabhogyasukhānubhavāspadībhūto deśaviśeṣo dyauḥ || 3.184 ||
kiṃ ca |
ye ca dānaparāḥ samyag aṣṭābhiś ca guṇair yutāḥ |
te 'pi tenaiva (mārgeṇa) satyavrataparāyaṇāḥ || 3.185 ||
ye ca samyagdānādipūrtadharmaniratās tathā dayā sarveṣu bhūteṣu, kṣāntir anasūyā [1017] śaucam anāyāso maṅgalam akārpaṇyam aspṛhety aṣṭābhir ātmaguṇair yuktāḥ || ye ca niyamena satyavādinas te 'pi pitṛyānenaiva mārgeṇa divaṃ yānti || 3.185 ||
tanmārgagāmināṃ(ṇāṃ) punarāvṛttim itihāsamukhenāha |
aṣṭāśītisahasrāṇi munayo gṛhamedhinaḥ |
punarāvṛtti(varti)no bījabhūtā dharmapravartakāḥ || 3.186 ||
aṣṭāśītisahasrasaṃkhyā munayas tāpasāḥ | iṣṭāpūrtadharmakāriṇa iti yāvat | gṛhamedhino gṛhasthāḥ | anena cākāmyakarmakāritvaṃ sūcayati | punarāvartinaḥ punarjanmabhājaḥ | jagatsṛṣṭer dharmaprabhāvāyās te prāganuṣṭhitadharmatvād bījabhūtāḥ | pravṛttyākhyasya dharmasya ca pravartakāḥ || 3.186 ||
saptarṣināgavīthyante devalokaṃ samāśritāḥ |
tāvanta eva munayaḥ sarvārambhavivarjitāḥ || 3.187 ||
tapasā brahmacaryeṇa saṅgatyāgena medhayā |
tatra gatvāvatiṣṭhante yāvad ābhūtasaṃplavam || 3.188 ||
nāgavīthīm āha gargaḥ |
kṛtikā bharaṇī svātir nāgavīthī prakīrtitā | iti |
devalas tu |
aśvinyāditribhāḥ sarvā nāgādyā dahanāntikāḥ |
ayam arthaḥ — pratyekam aśvinyāditrinakṣatrā nāgādyā nava vīthayaḥ | nāgā gajā airāvatī ṛṣabhā gaur jaradgavī mṛgī ajā dahaneti | tasyā nāgavīthyāḥ saptarṣīṇāṃ ca yad antarālaṃ tena mārgeṇa gatvāṣṭāśītisahasrāṇy eva munayaḥ skalaniṣiddhakāmyakarmārambhanivṛttās tapasāvaśyakena varṇadharmeṇa tathā brahmacaryeṇa saṅgatyāgena dharmānuṣṭhānanimittakhyātilābhapūjāparityāgena medhayā medhākāryeṇa vedadhāraṇena yuktā devalokaṃ samāśritya tatra yāvad ābhūtasaṃplavaṃ mahāpralayaṃ yāvad avatiṣṭhante | te 'pi pūrvavat punarāvartino bījabhūtā nivṛttadharmapravartakāś ca || 3.187, 188 ||
evaṃ ślokadvayenātmajñānarahitaṃ kāmyaṃ karmāvaśyakaṃ vā kurvatāṃ punarāvṛttir uktā | "sa hy āśramair vijijñāsyaḥ" ityādau tv ātmajñānasahitasya karmaṇo [1018] 'nāvṛttiphalatvaṃ vakṣyati | tarādhunā tāvaj jñātavyam ātmānaṃ tajjñānopāyaṃ ca ślokadvayeṇāh |
yato vedāḥ purāṇāni vidyopaniṣadas tathā|
ślokāḥ sūtrāṇi bhāṣyāṇi yac cānyad vāṅmayaṃ kvacit || 3.189 ||
vedānuvacanaṃ yajño brahmacaryaṃ tapo damaḥ |
śraddhopavāsaḥ svātantryam ātmano jñānahetavaḥ || 3.190 ||
dvidhā hy ātmā vyavasthito bandhavartitayā tadrahitatayā ca | tasya yan muktasvabhāvaṃ rūpaṃ tat sarveśvaratvasarvajñatvādiguṇayogitvena vedāntair upāsyatayocyate, na punar yad bandhasvabhāvam | prāśate hi svata eva tat sarveṣāṃ hṛdayeṣu tat prakāśādhīno viśvaprakāśaḥ |
tam eva bhāntam anubhāti sarvaṃ tasya bhāsā sarvam idaṃ vibhāti | iti |
