tatra śāstreṇa yad upāsyatayoktaṃ tad upalakṣayitum idam ucyate — yato vedā ityādi | yato jagatkāranāt, vedāḥ śrutayaḥ, purāṇāni prasiddhāni | anyāś ca dharmaśāstrādyā upaniṣado brahmāvabodhakā vedabhāgāḥ | tāsām amṛtībhāvahetuvijñānavidhāyaktavāt prādhānyena vedatve saty api pṛthaggrahaṇam | ye ca (tathā) kecana ślokāḥ: "yad akṣaraṃ pañcavidhaṃ sameti" ity evamādayaḥ | sūtrāṇi vivakṣitārthavācakāni vākyāni | bhāṣyāṇi sūtrādīnāṃ vivaraṇāni | anyad api vāṅmayaṃ yata udbhūtaṃ tasya paramātmano vedānuvacanādayo jñānahetavaḥ | vedānuvacanaṃ vedādhyayanam, guruvacanam anu paścād vacanam anuvacanam | anena ca sarvasya vedādhyayanasya gurvadhyayanapūrvakatvaṃ vadan vedasyāpauruṣeyatvaṃ gamayati | yat tu paramātmasakāśād vedānām udaya ity uktaṃ na tena vedānāṃ pauruṣeyatāpattiḥ | pramāṇāntareṇa hi vākyārthaṃ niścitya tat pratipādanāyedaṃ prathamatayā vākyasyotpāditatvaṃ pauruṣeyatvam | ata eva bṛhadāraṇyake niḥśvasitatulyatvam uktam: "sa yathārdraidhāgner abhyāhitāt pṛthag dhūmā viniścaranty evaṃ vā are 'sya mahato bhūtasya niḥśvasitam etad yad ṛgvedo yajurvedaḥ sāmavedo 'tharvāṅgirasa itihāsaḥ purāṇaṃ vidyā upaniṣadaḥ ślokāḥ sūtrāṇy anuvyākhyānāni vyākhyānāny asyaivaitāni niḥśvasitāni" iti | [1019] brahmacaryaṃ brahmacārikarma | śraddhā bhiktir āstikyaṃ vā | upavāso gurūpāstiḥ | svātantryaṃ vāsyendriyatā | prasiddham anyat || 3.189, 190 ||
pūrvaśloke ye nirdiṣṭā vedānuvacanādayo 'rthās ta eva brahmajñānasvarūpamātrotpādakatayā viniyuktā iti bhrāntinirāsāyāha |
sa hy āśramair vijijñāsyaḥ samagair evem eva tu |
draṣṭavyas tv ath mantavyaḥ śrotavyaś ca dvijātibhiḥ || 3.191 ||
sa khalu paramātmā samastaiś caturbhir apy āśrayair vijijñāsyo viśeṣato jñātum eṣṭavyaḥ | atra paramātmaviṣayavijñānasyāśramān prati sādhyatā pratyetavyā, iṣyamāṇatvāt | na punas tadviṣayecchāyā iṣyamāṇatvaṃ prati guṇatvāt | pradhānaṃ ca padārthāntareṇa saṃbadhyate na guṇaḥ | na ca pratyayārthatvenecchāyāḥ prādhānyaṃ vācyam | sa hi san pratyayena dhātvartham īpsitatamaṃ prati guṇatvenaivocyate, tena pratyayārthatve 'pi tasyā guṇabhāva eva prayojanavān eva ca dhātvartha iṣyate, na sukhāvāptivat svarūpeṇa | tena "brahma veda brahma bhavati | [ na ca (sa) punar āvartate" ityādivacanāvatatau(tā) punarāvṛttiprayojanaṃ vijñānam āśramāṇāṃ sādhyam ity uktaṃ bhavati ] | brahmajñānasya saprayojanatvād eva tadviṣayecchāsiddher necchāyā vidheyatā | aprāptaviṣayatvād vidheḥ | kiṃ punas tajjñānaṃ veditavyam ity apekṣita uktam — draṣṭavya iti | draṣṭavyaḥ sākṣāt kartavya ity arthaḥ | darśanopāyatayoktam  — mantavya iti | śrutasya yuktibhir anusaṃdhānaṃ mananam | tac ca śravaṇam antareṇa na saṃbhavatīty matvoktam — śrotavya iti | upaniṣadvākyebhyo 'vadhāraṇiyaḥ | dvijātibhir iti śūdravyudāsārtham || 3.191 ||
vihitayor jñānakarmaṇoḥ phalam āha |
ya evam enaṃ vindanti ye cāraṇyakam āśritāḥ |
upāsate dvijāḥ satyaṃ śraddhayā parayā yutāḥ || 3.192 ||
kramāt te saṃbhavanty arcir ahaḥ śuklaṃ tathottaram |
ayanaṃ devalokaṃ ca savitāraṃ savaidyutam || 3.193 ||
[1020]        tatas tān puruṣo 'bhyetya mānavo brahmalaukikān |
karoti punar āvṛttis teṣām iha na vidyate || 3.194 ||
ye dvijātaya enaṃ paramātmānam evaṃ śravṇamananābhyāṃ vindanti labhante brahmāham asmīti sākṣāt kurvanti, ye cāraṇyakam āraṇyādhyetavyaṃ brahmavidyātmakaṃ vedabhāgaṃ brahmapramāṇatayā samāśritāḥ santaḥ paramayā bhaktyopetāḥ satyaṃ yathārthabhūtaṃ parameśvaram upāsate dhyāyanti | atra ye cetyupāsakatvena śravaṇamnanṃ(na)kartṛtvena samuccayārthāś cakāro na puruṣavyaktisamuccayārthaḥ | tena śravaṇamananadhyānārcanavanto dehād utkramya vakṣyamāṇārcirādyabhimāninīr devatāḥ saṃbhavanti prāpnuvanti | arcir agniḥ | ahaḥ divasaḥ | śuklaḥ śuklapakṣaḥ | uttaram ayanaṃ sūryasyodagayanam | devalokasavitṛvaidyutāḥ prasiddhāḥ | tato vaidyutāgniṃ prāptāṃs tān mānavaḥ puruṣo 'bhyeti | tato 'sau brahmalaukikān brahmalokanivāsinas tān karoti | brahmalokaṃ prāptānāṃ na punar āvṛttir janma na vidyate || 3.192, 193, 194 \|
kāmyakarmakāriṇaḥ praty āha |
yajñena tapasā dānair ye hi svargajito narāḥ |
dhūmaṃ niśāṃ kṛṣṇapakṣaṃ dakṣināyanam eva ca || 3.195 ||
pitṛlokaṃ candramasaṃ vāyuṃ vṛṣṭiṃ jalaṃ mahīm |
kramāt te saṃbhavantīha punar eva vrajanti ca || 3.196 ||
phalārthaṃ vihitair yajñatapodānādibhir ye svargaṃ jitavantaḥ svargapradaṃ dharmaṃ kṛtavantas te vakṣyamāṇaprakrameṇa dhūmādīn saṃbhavanti | dhūmādyabhimāninīr devatāḥ prāpnuvanti | tatra candraṃ yāvad ārohaṇaṃ vāyvādyavarohaṇaṃ mahīṃ pṛthvīṃ tatratyān oṣadhivanaspatīn saṃbhavantīty arthaḥ | tata oṣadhivanaspatīn bhuktavatoḥ strīpuruṣayor viśuddhe śukraśoṇite pañca dhātūn ityādi pūrvoktam anusaṃdheyam | na punar yajñādy anuṣṭhāya dehapātād ūrdhvam uktakrameṇa yānty āyānti ca | ata evoktaṃ bhagavatā "gatāgataṃ kāmakāmā labhante" iti gītāsu || 3.195, 196 ||
uktaviparyaye doṣam āha | [1021]
etad yo na vijānāti mārgadvitayam ātmanaḥ |
dandaśūkaḥ pataṅgo vā bhavet kīṭo 'tha vā kṛmiḥ || 3.197 ||
uktam ātmano mārgadvayaṃ yo na vijānāti jñātvā ca tanmārgagamanopāyaṃ nānutiṣṭhati, sa dandaśūkaḥ sarpaḥ pataṅgaḥ pakṣī kṛmiḥ kīṭo vā bhavet || 3.197 ||
upāsanetikartavyatām āha |
ūrusthottānacaraṇaḥ savye nyasyetaraṃ karam |
uttānaṃ kiṃcid unnamya mukhaṃ viṣṭabhya corasā || 3.198 ||
nimīlitākṣaḥ sattvastho dantair dantān asaṃspṛśan |
tālusthācalajihvaś ca saṃvṛtāsyaḥ suniścalaḥ || 3.199 ||
saṃniruddhendriyagrāmo nātinicocchritāsanaḥ |
dviguṇaṃ triguṇaṃ vāpi prāṇāyāmam upakramet || 3.200 ||
tato dhyeyaḥ sthito yo 'sau hṛdaye dīpavat prabhuḥ |
dhārayet tatra cātmānaṃ dhāraṇāṃ dhārayan budhaḥ || 3.201 ||
anatinīcocchrita āsana upaviśyottānau caraṇau sthāpayitvā padmāsanaṃ baddhveti yāvat | tad upari savye kare dakṣiṇaṃ karam uttānaṃ nidhāyāsyaṃ kiṃcid unnamya vakṣasā ca kāyaṃ viṣṭabhyākṣiṇī nimīlya rāgadveṣādirajastamaḥkāryavirodhi sattvakāryaṃ manaḥprasādādikam āsthāya dantapaṅktyor anyonyasaṃsparśaṃ pariharaṃs tāluni jihvām acalāṃ kṛtvā niṣkampaḥ san viṣayebhya indriyāṇi pratyāhṛtva dvādaśamātrikāt kanīyasaḥ prāṇāyāmād dviguṇaṃ triguṇaṃ vā prāṇāyāmaṃ kuryāt | tato hṛdvyomni hitasaṃjñakanāḍīsaṃbhedalakṣaṇe dīpavad avasthita ātmā sarveśvaro dhyeyaś cintanīyaḥ | dhyānānantaraṃ dhāraṇāṃ kurvaṃs tatrātmany ātmānam ātmīyaṃ mano dhārayen niyacchet | dattātreyaḥ|
nivāte vijane sthāne śubhe caiva manorame |
nirjantuke nirābādhe śarkarāvālukādibhiḥ ||
śabdāivarjite sthāne śubhaṃ kṛtvā tu saṃstaram |
baddhvā sukhāsanaṃ tatra prāṅmukho vāpy udaṅmukhaḥ ||
[1022]        kṛtvā susaṃnataṃ dehaṃ susamaṃ yogavittamaḥ |
namaskṛtya mahādevaṃ mahāyogīśvaraṃ harim ||
śodhayitvā purā nāḍīḥ prāṇāyāmāṃś caret tataḥ |
prāṇāyāṃāś caret tāvad yāvac cittaṃ prasīdati ||
mahābhārate: "yamaniyama(māsana)prāṇāyāmapratyāhāradhāraṇādhyānasamādhyo 'ṣṭāv aṅgāni yogasya" | brahmapurāṇe |
brahmacaryam ahiṃsā ca satyāstreyāparigrahān |
seveta yogī niṣkāmo yogyatāṃ svamano nayan ||
svādhyāyaśaucasaṃtoṣatapāṃsi niyatātmavān |
kurvīta brahmaṇi tathā parasmin pravaṇaṃ manaḥ ||
ete yamāś ca niyamāḥ pañca pañca prakīrtitāḥ |
viśiṣṭaphaladāḥ kāmyā niṣkāmānāṃ(ṇāṃ) vimuktidāḥ ||
ekaṃ bhadrāsanādīnāṃ samāsthāya guṇair yutaḥ |
yamākhyair niyamākhyaiś ca yuñjīta niyato muniḥ ||
vāyupurāṇe |
padmam ardhāsanaṃ vāpi tathā svastikam āsanam |
āsthāya yogī yuñjīta kṛtvā tu praṇavaṃ hṛdi ||
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe |
prāṇāpānanirodhaś ca prāṇāyāma udāhṛtaḥ ||
viṣṇupurāṇe |
prāṇākhyam anilaṃ vaśyam abhyāsāt kriyate (kurute) hi yaḥ |
prāṇāyāmaḥ sa vijñeyaḥ sabījo 'bīja eva ca ||
sabījaḥ samantrakaḥ | abīja itaraḥ | mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe |
laghumadhyottarīyākhyaḥ prāṇāyāmas tridhoditaḥ |
tasya pramāṇaṃ vakṣyāmi tad alarka śṛṇuṣva me ||
laghur dvādaśamātras tu dviguṇaḥ sa tu madhyamaḥ |
triguṇābhis tu mātrābhir uttamaḥ sa udāhṛtaḥ ||
nimeṣonmeṣaṇe mātrā tālo laghvakṣaras tathā ||
yogayājñavalkyaḥ |
adgulimokṣatritayaṃ jānvoḥ parimārjanaṃ vāpi |
tālatrayam api tajjñā mātrāsaṃjñaṃ praśaṃsanti || [1023]
devalaḥ: " trividhaḥ prāṇāyāmaḥ kumbho recanaṃ pūraṇam iti | niḥśvāsādhmānaṃ pūraṇam iti | sa punar ekadvitribhir uddhātair mṛdvaṃsa(durma)ndas tīkṣṇo vā bhavati | prāṇāpānavyānodānasamānānāṃ sakṛdudgamanaṃ(mya) mūrdhānam āhatya nivṛttir uddhātaḥ" || mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe |
prathamena jayet svedaṃ dvitīyena tu vepathum |
viṣādaṃ tu tṛtīyena jayed doṣam anukramāt ||
mṛdutve sevamānasya siṃhaśārdulakuñjarāḥ |
yathā yānti tathā prāṇo vaśyo bhavati yoginaḥ ||
vaśyaṃ santaṃ yathecchāto nāgaṃ vahati hastipaḥ |
tathaiva yogī chandena prāṇaṃ nayati sādhitam ||
yathā hi sādhitaḥ siṃho mṛgān hanti na mānavān |
tadvan niṣiddhaḥ pavanaḥ kilbiṣaṃ na nṛṇāṃ tanum ||
tasmād yuktaḥ sadā yogī prāṇāyāmaparo bhavet |
itthaṃ yogī yatāhāraḥ prāṇāyāmaparāyaṇaḥ ||
jitāṃ jitāṃ śanair bhūmim āru(ro)het tu yathā gṛham |
doṣān vyādhiṃ tathā moham ākrāntā bhūr anirjitā ||
vivardhayati nārohet tasmād bhūmim anirjitām |
prāṅnābhyāṃ hṛdaye cātha tṛtīyā ca tathorasi ||
kaṇṭhe mukhe nāsikāgre netrabhrūmadhyamūrdhasu |
kiṃcit tasmāt parasmiṃś ca dhāraṇā paramā smṛtā ||
daśaitā dhāraṇāḥ prāpya prāpnoty akṣarasātmyatām |
nādhmātaḥ kṣudhitaḥ śrānto na ca vyākulacetanaḥ ||
yuñjīta yogaṃ rājendra yogī siddhyartham ādṛtaḥ |
nātiśīte na caivoṣṇe dvaṃdve nāmbvanilārtmake ||
kāleṣv eteṣu yuñjīta na yogaṃ dhyānatatparaḥ |
saśabde 'gnijalābhyāśe jīrṇagoṣṭhe catuṣpathe ||
śuṣkaparṇacaye nadyāṃ śmaśāne sasarīsṛṣe(?) |
sabhaye kūpatīre vā caityavalmīkasaṃcaye ||
[1024]        deśeṣv eteṣu tattvajño yogābhyāsaṃ vivarjayet |
sattvasyānupapattau ca deśakālavivarjite ||
na sato darśanaṃ yāgaṃ tasmāt tatparivarjayet |
doṣān etān anādṛtya mūḍhatvād yo yunakti vai ||
vighnāya tasya ye doṣā jāyante tān nibodha me |
bādhiryaṃ jaḍatā lopaḥ smṛter mūkatvam andhatā ||
jvaraś ca jāyate sadyas tadvad ajñānayoginaḥ |
pramādād yogino doṣā yady ete syuś cikitsitam ||
teṣāṃ nāśāya kartavyaṃ yoginas tan nibodha me |
snigdhāṃ yavāgūm atyuṣṇāṃ bhuktvā tatraiva dhārayet ||
vātagulmapraśāntyarthaṃ gulmāvarte tathā dadhi |
yavāgūṃ vāpi pavanaṃ vāyugranthīn prati kṣipet ||
tadvat kampo(mpe) mahāśailaṃ sthitaṃ manasi dhārayet |
vighāte vacaso vāci bādhirye śravaṇendriye ||
tathaivāmlaphalaṃ dhyāyet tṛṣārte rasanendriye |
yasmin yasmin rujā deśe tasmiṃs tadupakāriṇīm ||
dhārayed dhāraṇām uṣṇe śītāṃ śīte vidāhinīm |
kīlaṃ śirasi saṃsthāpy kāṣṭhaṃ kāṣṭhena tāḍayet ||
luptasmṛteḥ smṛtiḥ sadyo yoginas tena jāyate |
yāvat pṛthivyāṃ vāyvagnī vyāpināv api dhārayet ||
amānuṣāt sattvajātād bādhā syāc cec cikitsayet |
amānuṣaṃ sattvam antaryoginaṃ(naḥ) praviśed yati ||
yāyvagnidhāraṇād enaṃ dehasaṃsthaṃ vinirdahet |
evaṃ sarvātmanā kāryā rakṣā yogavidā nṛpa ||
dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāṃ śarīraṃ sādhanaṃ yataḥ |
prāṇāyāmā daśa dvau ca dhāraṇā sābhidhīyate ||
dve dhāraṇe smṛte yoge yogibhis tattvadarśibhiḥ |
tathā vai yogayuktasya yogino niyatātmanaḥ ||
[1025]        sarve doṣāḥ praṇaśyanti svasthaś caivopajāyate |
īkṣate ca paraṃ brahma prākṛtāṃś ca guṇān pṛthak ||
vyomādiparamāṇūṃś ca tathātmānam akalmaṣam ||
tathā |
śabdādibhyo nivṛttāni yadakṣarāṇi yatātmabhiḥ |
pratyāhriyante yogena pratyāhāras tataḥ smṛtaḥ ||
hārītaḥ: "aṇutvāl lāghavāc cāpalād vāyor yogabhraṣṭasya manasaḥ samānīyārthe yojanaṃ pratyāhāraḥ" | viṣṇupurāṇe |
śabdādiṣv anuraktāni samyagakṣāṇi yogavit |
kuryāc cittānukārīṇi pratyāhāraḥ sa ucyate ||
vasyatā paramā teṣāṃ jāyate 'tibalātmanām |
indriyāṇām avaśyais tair na yogī yogasādhanaḥ ||
devalaḥ: "śarīrendriyamanobuddhyātmanāṃ dhāraṇād dhāraṇā" | atrātmāhaṃkāraḥ | śaṅkhaḥ:
manaḥsaṃyamanāt tajjñair dhāraṇeti nigadyate |
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe |
yogayuktaḥ sadā yogī laghvāhāro jitendriyaḥ |
sūkṣmās tu dhāraṇāḥ sapta bhūrādyā mūrdhni dhārayet ||
dharitrīṃ dhārayan yogī gandhasaukṣmyaṃ prapadyate |
ātmānaṃ manyate pṛthvīṃ tadgandhaṃ ca jahāti saḥ ||
tathaivāpsu rasaṃ sūkṣmaṃ tadvad rūpaṃ ca tejasi |
sparśaṃ vāyau tathā tadvad bibhratas tasya dhāraṇām ||
vyomnaḥ sūkṣmapravṛttasya śabdaṃ tadvac jahāti saḥ |
manasā sarvabhūtānāṃ mana āviśate yadā ||
mānasīṃ dhāraṇāṃ bibhran manaḥśaukṣmyaṃ jahāti ca |
tadvad buddhim aśeṣāṇāṃ sattvānām etya yogavit ||
parityajati saṃprāpya buddhisaukṣṃyam anuttamam |
etāsāṃ dhāraṇānāṃ tu saptānāṃ saukṣmyam āptavān ||
[1026]        dṛṣṭvā dṛṣṭvā tataḥ siddhiṃ tyaktvā tyaktvā parāṃ bhajet |
etāny eva ca saṃdhāya sapta sūkṣmāṇi pārthiva ||
bhūrādīnāṃ virāgo 'tra sadbhāvajñasya muktaye ||
viṣṇupurāṇe |
prāṇāyāmena pavanaiḥ pratyāhāreṇa cendriyaiḥ |
vaśīkṛtaṃ tataḥ kuryāt sthitaṃ cetaḥ śubhāśraye ||
tadrūpapratyayāyaikasaṃtatiḥ sānyaniḥspṛhā |
taddhyānaṃ prathamair aṅgaiḥ ṣaḍbhir niṣpādyate nṛpa ||
ayam arthaḥ — brahmarūpapratyayātmikā saṃtatiḥ pravāhaḥ, sā viṣayāntarāsaṃspṛṣṭā satī dhyānam ity ucyata iti | tathā |
tasyaiva kalpanāhīnaṃ svarūpagrahaṇaṃ hi yat |
manasā dhyānaviṣpādyaṃ samādhiḥ so 'bhidhīyate ||
tasya brahmaṇaḥ kalpanāhīnaṃ dhyeyaṃ dhyānaṃ dhyātetibhedapratyayarahitaṃ dhyānajanitasaṃskarasahitena manasā nirvikalpakam ahaṃ brahmāsīti pratyakṣasamādhiḥ | dhriyamāṇaśarīrasya brahmasākṣātkāro 'pi mānasa ity abhiprāyeṇa manasety uktam | muktāv eva svayaṃprakāśo brahmībhāvaḥ | śarīrapātottarakālā ca muktir ity ato vākyād gamyate | viṣṇuḥ: "ūrusthottānacaraṇaḥ savye kare karaṃ nyasya tālusthācalajihvo dantair dantān asaṃspṛśan svaṃ nāsāgraṃ paśyan diśas tv anavalokayan vibhīḥ praśāntātmā caturviṃśatyātmatattvair vyatītaṃ cintayen nityam atīndriyam aguṇaṃ śabdasparśarasarūpagandhātītaṃ sarvastham atisthūlaṃ sarvagam atisūkṣmaṃ sarvataḥpāṇipādāntaṃ sarvato'kṣiśiromukhaṃ sarvataḥśaktim evaṃ dhyāyet | dhyānaniratasya saṃvatsareṇa yogāvirbhāvo bhavati | atha nirākāre lakṣyabandhaṃ kartuṃ na śaknoti, tadā pṛthivyaptejovāyvākāśādimanobuddhyātmāvyaktapuruṣāṇāṃ pūrvaṃ pūrvaṃ dhyātvā tatra labdhalakṣyas taṃ parityajed aparam aparaṃ dhyāyet | evaṃ puruṣo dhyānam ārohet | tatrāpy [1027] asamartho hṛdayapadmasya madhye dīpavat puruṣaṃ dhyāyet | evaṃ puruṣadhyānam apohet | tatrāpy asamartho bhagavantaṃ vāsudevaṃ kuṇḍalinam aṅgadinaṃ vanamālāvibhūṣitoraskaṃ svarūpaṃ caturbhujaṃ śaṅkhacakragadāpadmadharaṃ caraṇamadhyagatavasudhaṃ dhyāyet | yad yad dhyāyati tat tad āpnotīti dhyānaguhyam | tasmāt sarvam eva kṣaraṃ tyaktvākṣaram eva dhyāyet | na puruṣaṃ vinā kiṃcid apy akṣaram asti taṃ prāpya mukto bhavati" | manuḥ |
puram ākramya sakalaṃ śete yasmān mahāprabhuḥ |
tasmāt puruṣa ity eva procyate tattvacintakaiḥ ||
prāgrātrāpararāteṣu yogī nityam atandritaḥ |
dhyāyate puruṣaṃ nityaṃ nirguṇaṃ pañcaviṃśakam ||
etad vo 'bhihitaṃ sarvaṃ niḥśreyasakaraṃ param |
asmād apracyuto vipraḥ prāpnoti paramāṃ gatim ||
evaṃ sa bhagavān devo lokānāṃ hitamāmyayā |
dharmasya paramaṃ guhyaṃ mamedaṃ sarvam uktavān ||
sarvam ātmani saṃpaśyet sac cāsac ca samāhitaḥ |
sarvam ātmani saṃpaśyan nādharme kurute manaḥ ||
ātmaiva devatāḥ sarvāḥ sarvam ātmany avasthitam |
ātmā hi janayaty eṣāṃ karmayogaṃ śarīriṇām ||
khaṃ saṃniveśayet khe tu ceṣṭanasparśane 'nilam |
paṅktidṛṣṭyoḥ paraṃ tejaḥ sneho(he) 'po gāṃ ca mūrtiṣu ||
manasīndraṃ diśaḥ śrotre krānte viṣṇuṃ bale haram |
vācy agniṃ mitram utsarge prajane ca prajāpatim ||
praśāsitāram eteṣām aṇīyāṃsam aṇor api |
rukmābhaṃ svapnadhīgamyaṃ vidyāt taṃ puruṣaṃ param ||
etam eke vadanty agniṃ manum anye prajāpatim |
indram eke pare prāṇam apare brahma śāśvatam ||
eṣa sarvāṇi bhūtāni pañcabhir vyāpya mūrtibhiḥ |
janmavṛddhikṣaye nityaṃ saṃsārayati cakravat ||
[1028]        ya evaṃ sarvabhūteṣu paśyaty ātmānam ātmanā |
sa sarvasamatām etya brahmābhyeti paraṃ padam || 3.198, 199, 200, 201 ||
yogasiddher lakṣaṇam āha |
antardhānaṃ smṛtiḥ kāntir dṛṣṭiḥ śrotrajñatā tathā |
nijaṃ śarīram utsṛjya parakāyapraveśanam || 3.202 ||
arthānāṃ chandataḥ sṛṣṭir yogasiddhes tu lakṣaṇam |
siddhe yoge tyajan deham amṛtatvāya kalpate || 3.203 ||
antardhānam adṛśyaśarīratvam | tac cāṇuparimāṇatve tasya bhavati | tac cāṇimākhyaguṇaprāptyā, smṛtir janmāntarānubhūtārthaviṣayā | kāntiḥ kamanīyatvam, dṛṣṭir atītānāgatavyavahitavipradṛ(kṛ)ṣṭārthe(rtha)viṣayā | śrotrajñatā śrotrendriyasākṣātkāritvam | anena cātmavyatiriktāvyaktabuddhyahaṃkārapañcatanmātraikādaśendriyapañcabhūtānāṃ sākṣātkāritvam upalakṣyate | nijaṃ svakīyaṃ śarīraṃ pūrvakarmārabdham utsṛjya parakāyaṃ praviśya tadantargatatayā tadviṣayopabhoktṛtvam | arthānāṃ pṛthivyādīnāṃ sraṣṭavyānāṃ sveccheyā svātantryeṇotpādanam ity etad yogasiddher lakṣaṇaṃ liṅgam | etac ca lakṣaṇam eva yogasiddher na prayojanam | prayojanaṃ tv amṛtībhāva eva, tad āha — siddhe yoge tyajan deham amṛtatvāya kalpata iti | tyajan deham itivacanasāmarthyāc charīre nāsti muktir iti darśayati | yuktaṃ caitat | muktir hi bandhābhāvaḥ | bandhaś copādhibhūtāntaḥkaraṇasaṃbandhaḥ | śarīraṃ ca badhyate 'ntaḥkaraṇena, śarīrī tenopādhiyata iti vyāhatam | rāgadveṣamohādīnāṃ prakṣayo 'ntardhānādiguṇodayaś ca na mokṣaḥ, pratyuta mokṣāntarāyaḥ | ata eva ca tatrāsaktir yoginā pariharaṇīyeti yatnaḥ śāstrakārair ucyate || 3.202, 203 ||
etat sarvaṃ yatiṃ praty abhihitam | vānaprathaṃ pratīdānīm āha |
atha vāpy abhyasan vedaṃ nyastakarmā vane vasan |
ayācitāśī mitabhuk parāṃ siddhim avāpnuyāt || 3.204 ||
caturṇāṃ vedānām ekaṃ vedam ekāṃ śākhām araṇye vijane | ayācitāśī svayam upanatānnāśī, mitabhuk prāṇadhāranāyaiva bhuñjāno nyastakarmā tyaktakāmyaniṣiddhakriyaḥ | [1-29] atha vā brahmaṇi nikṣiptāvaśyakriyaḥ | parāṃ siddhiṃ muktim avāpnuyāt || 3.204 ||
idānīṃ gṛhasthaṃ praty āha |
nyāyārjitadhanas tattvajñānaniṣṭho 'tithipriyaḥ |
śrāddhakṛt satyavādī ca gṛhastho 'pi vimucyate || 3.205 ||
nyāyenāniṣiddhena karmaṇārjitaṃ svīkṛtaṃ dhanaṃ yena sa nyāyārjitadhanaḥ | tathā tattvajñāne niṣṭhā tātparyaṃ yasya sa tattvajñānaniṣṭhaḥ | atithayaḥ priyā yasya so 'tithipriyaḥ | tatpriyatvena ca tatpūjakatvaṃ lakṣyate | śrāddham āvaśyakaṃ pārvaṇādikaṃ karotīti śrāddhakṛt | bhūtahitārthaṃ vākyaṃ satyaṃ tad vadatīty evaṃśīlaḥ satyavādī | evaṃvidho gṛhastho 'pi vimucyate 'pavṛjyate | apiśabdenānye 'py āśramiṇo gṛhyante | atra ca nyāyārjitadhatvādibhir varṇāśramadharmā  nityanaimittikāś copalakṣitā jñānasahitā muktaye vidhīyante, tad etad vyākhyānaṃ kecin nānumanyante, yata etadarthāyāḥ smṛter asyāḥ śrutivirodhaḥ syāt | tathā hi,
na karmaṇa na prajayā dhanena tyāgenaike amṛtatvam ānaśuḥ |
pareṇa nākaṃ nihitaṃ guhāyāṃ vibhrājate yad yatayo viśanti ||
ityanena gṛhasthadharmāṇāṃ yāgādikarmaprajotpādanādīnām amṛtatvaprāptihetubhāvaṃ pratiṣidhya yatīnām eva tatprāptir vidhīyate | tenātra tyāgaśabdena brahmacaryādyāśramatrayakarmaṇāṃ tryāgād amṛtībhāva iti pratipādyate | tathā yativyatiriktāśramiṇāṃ puṇyalokaprāptir yater evāmṛtaprāptir iti chāndogyopaniṣat: "sarva ete puṇyalokā bhavanti brahmasaṃstho 'mṛtatvam eti" iti | brahmasaṃstho bhikṣuḥ | tathāgnihotrādīny āvaśyakāni karmāṇi duritakṣayāya śrutyā vihitāni | tāni muktaye vidadhānā smṛtiḥ śrutyā virudhyata eva | yad ucyate śrutyaiva: "vividiṣanti yajñena dāne tapasā na cai(nāśa)kena" ityādikayā varṇāśramakarmāṇi muktau viniyuktānīti, tan na brahmajñānecchāyām eva hy eṣā śrutiḥ karmaṇāṃ viniyoktrī, na muktau nāpi tatsādane jñāne | icchāpi ca vidheyapratiyoginī bhavati | yathā bheṣajaviśeṣasya bubhukṣā | nanu ca [1030] pradhāne 'nyatpradhīyate na guṇe, vividiṣantīty atra ca vedanam icchāṃ prati pradhānam | tena vidyāyām evamādyecchāṃ prati karmībhūtāyāṃ karmaṇāṃ viniyogo necchāyām | yathāśvena jigamiṣatīty aśvasya gatau, na punas tadicchāyām ity ucyate | satyaṃ pradhāne 'nyad viniyuyate | pradhānaṃ tu dvividham — śābdham ārthaṃ ca | tatra śābdam anvaya upayujyate nārtham | ata eva rājapuruṣamānayeti puruṣa ānayanena saṃbadhyate, na rājārthataḥ pradhānabhūto 'pi | sa hi śabdena viśeṣa(ṣya)tayā pradhānabhūtaṃ puruṣaṃ prati viśeṣaṇatāṃ nīyate | sannanteṣu ca śabdeṣu icchaiva pratyayārthatvāt pradhānam | tena tatraiva yajñādiviniyogaḥ | aśvasya tu gatyanvayaḥ (?) pramāṇāntaravaśān na śabdataḥ prasiddhahetukāyām api vividiṣāyām, vaidiko yajñādir upāyaḥ paśuprāptāv iva citrādiḥ "citrayā yajeta paśukāmaḥ" ity atra | kiṃ ca karmāṇi kurvatāhaṃ kartā karmaṇāṃ phalabhoktā ceti pratyayadārḍhyam avalambanīyam | tattvajñānaniṣṭhena punar asyaiva pratyayasya mithyātvam āpādyam | tataś ca jñānakaraṇor virodhād asamuccayaḥ ṣoḍaśina iv grahaṇāgrahaṇayoḥ | kiṃ ca paramakāryābhede 'vāntaravyāpārabhede tu kārakaṃ kārakāntareṇa samuccīyate | yathā "aruṇayaikahāyanyā piṅgākṣyā somaṃ krīṇāti" ity atrāruṇyam ekahāyanyā na punar avāntaravyāpāraikye, na hi vrīhayo yavaiḥ samuccīyante | tulyaṃ ca jñānakarṇor duritakṣapaṇam avāntarakāryam | na ca jñānasyāvidyānivṛttir eva kāryaṃ na tu karmakṣaya iti vācyam | na hi karmajanyāyā avidyāyāḥ karmāṇy akṣapayatā jñānena nivṛttiḥ śakyā kartum | tasmān nyāyaviruddham api samuccayasmaraṇaṃ smṛtyantraviruddhaṃ ca | tatra mahābhārate |
śuka uvāca |
yad idaṃ vedavacanaṃ kuru karma tyajeti ca |
kāṃ diśaṃ vidyayā yāti kāṃ ca gacchati karmaṇā ||
ete cānyonyavairūpye vartete pratikūlataḥ |
ity uktaḥ pratyuvācedaṃ parāśarasutaḥ sutam ||
karmavidyāmayāv etau vyākhyāsyāmi kṣarākṣarau |
yāṃ diśaṃ vidyayā yāt yāṃ ca gacchati karmaṇā ||
śṛṇuṣvaikamanāḥ putra gahvaraṃ hy etadantaram |
karmaṇā badhyate jantur vidyayā ca pramucyate ||
[1031]        tasmāt karma na kurvanti yatayaḥ pāradarśinaḥ |
karmaṇā jāyate pretya mūrtimān ṣoḍaśātmakaḥ ||
vidyayā jāyate nityam avyayo hy avyayātmakaḥ |
karma tv eke praśaṃsanti svalpabuddhitarā narāḥ ||
tena te dehajātāni ramayanta upāsate |
ye tu buddhipathaṃ prāptā dharmanaipuṇadarśinaḥ ||
na te karma praśaṃsanti kūpaṃ nadyāṃ pibann iva |
karmaṇā phalam āpnoti sukhaduḥkhe bhavābhavau ||
vidyayā tad avāpnoti yatra gatvā na śocati |
yatra gatvā na mriyate yatra gatvā na jāyate ||
na hīyate yatra gatvā yatra gatvā na vardhate || iti |
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe pitāputrasaṃvāde |
evaṃ saṃśarcakre 'smin bhramatā tāta saṃkaṭe |
jñānam etan mayā proktaṃ mokṣasaṃprāptikāraṇam ||
vijñāte yatra sarvo 'yam ṛgyajuḥsāmasaṃjñitaḥ |
kriyākalāpo viguṇo na samyak pratibhāti me |
tasmād utpannabodhasya vedair me kiṃ prayojanam ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
satyaṃ jñānaṃ tapo dānam etad dharmasya sādhanam |
dharmāt sukhaṃ ca jñānaṃ ca jñānān mokṣo 'dhigamyate || iti |
tasmād uktavirodhaparihārāya smṛtir iyam evaṃ vyākhyātavyā | gṛhastho 'pi vidhivihitakarmakārī muktyanarho 'pi tattvajñānaniṣṭhatvād vimucyate kiṃ punar bhikṣuḥ karmatyāgī tattvajñānaikaniṣṭha iti | tad etad anye nānumanyante | na hi samuccayavidhāyakasmṛteḥ śrutivirodhād aprāmāṇyam asti | yataḥ samuccayavidhāyakaṃ pratyakṣam eva śrutivākyam asyā mūlam asti | tad yathā:
vidyāṃ cāvidyāṃ ca yas tad vedobhayaṃ saha |
avidyayā mṛtyuṃ tīrtvā vidyayāmṛtam aśnute ||
vidyā brahmajñānam | avidyā karma | tad ubhayaṃ saha samuccayaṃ yo vedānuṣṭhānaparyantatayā jānāti sa karmaṇā mṛtyuṃ mṛtyuphaladaṃ duritaṃ tīrtvātikramya [1032] vidyayāmṛtam aśnuta iti prasiddhārtham | atra ca vidyāśabdaṃ devatājñānaparatvena kecana vyācakṣate, tad ayuktam | prakṛtahānyaprakṛtaprakriyāprasaṅgāt | "īśā vāsyam" ityādinā parameśvarasyaiva prakṛtatvāt | evam atra mantre vidyākarmaṇor amṛtībhāve samuccayaḥ | tadupapādakatayā cāvāntaravyāpārabhedo vidyayā mṛtyuṃ tīrtveti kathitaḥ | ktvāpratyayena ca vidyāṃ prati karmaṇo 'ṅgabhāvaḥ | etad eva spaṣṭayati — "vividiṣanti yajñena" ityādiśrutiḥ | yady apy atra vividiṣā pratyayārthatvād chandataḥ pradhānaṃ tathāpi na tatra yajñānvayo vākyaprameyo 'rthasiddhatvāt | tathā hi — na hi vividiṣāsaṃbandham antareṇa vidyārthatā yajñādīnam asti | na hy aniṣyamāṇaṃ pradhānaṃ bhavati, tena yajñādīn prati vidyāyāḥ prādhānyād vividiṣānvayas teṣāṃ gamyamāno na vidhayaḥ, kiṃ tu vidyānvaya evāprāptatvāt | yathā "etayānnādyakāmaṃ yājayet" ity atra yājanānvayam anūdya yāgānvaya eva prāpto 'nnādyakāmasya vidhīyate | ata eva cāśvena jigamiṣatīty atrāśvena gatyanvayaḥ śābda eva | astu vā vividiṣaiva yajñādīn prati pradhānaṃ kiṃ tu vividiṣāprayojanībhūtayā ca vidyayā viśiṣṭecchā vividiṣocyate | yac ca prayojanavato 'ṅgaṃ yat prayojanasyāpi | tathā "agniṃ citvā sautrāmaṇyā yajeta" iti | atra sautrāmaṇyagniṃ pratyaṅgaṃ bhavantī tatprayojanaṃ yāgaṃ prati cāṅgatāṃ yāti | vidyāpi cāpavargeṇa phalena pahavatī satīcchāyāḥ prayojanaṃ bhavati, na kevalā | tataś ca vidyāṅgabhāvaṃ bhajanto 'py avidyādayo 'pavarge 'ṅgatāṃ yānti | vidyāyāś cāpavargaḥ phalam "brahmavid āpnoti param | brahma veda brahma bhavati" ityādivākyaprasiddheḥ | na ca vācyaṃ yathādhānam āhavanīyādisvarūpotpattyaṅgaṃ na tu tatkāryottarakratvarthaṃ tathā yajñādayo 'pi vidyāsvarūpam utpādayanti na tu tatkāryam apavargaṃ kurvantīti | yuktaṃ hy ādhānasyāhavanīyādisvarūpotpattimātraśeṣatvam | na hy ādhānaviniyogadaśāyām "yad āhavanīye juhvati" ityādibhir āhavanīyādīnāṃ prayojanavatām avagamo 'sti | prasiddhe hy āhavanīyādau tatprayojanāvagamo bhavati | tatprasiddhiś cādhānaviniyogāyattā | tena pūrvam ādhānam agniṣu viniyojyaṃ paścāt kratuṣv agnayaḥ | tenāgatyā viniyokṣyamāṇātīndriyāhavanīyādisvarūpaśeṣatādhānasyāśrīyate, na tu gatisaṃbhave | saṃbhavati cātra gatiḥ | yajñādiviniyotāt prāg eva vidyāyā apavargatājñānāt | vidyāsvarūpasya caindriyakatvāt | [1033] na ca yathā paśvādīnāṃ pramāṇāntarād viditapuruṣārthabhāvānāṃ śāstreṇa citrādir upāyo vidhīyate tathā vidyāyāṃ yajñādir iti vācyam | tasya pramāṇāntarataḥ puruṣārthatvāvedanāt | tasmāc chāstrāvagamyāpavargahetubhūtāyāṃ vidyāyāṃ sākṣād icchādvāreṇa vā yajñādayo viniyujyanta iti siddham | tataś ca sarvāṇy evāvaśyakāni varṇāśramakarmāṇi mokṣe sādhye brahmajñānena samuccīyante | "na karmaṇā" ityādayas tu karmaṇāṃ mokṣopāyatvaniṣedhāj jñānarahitakarmaviṣayāḥ | anyathā samuccayavidhinirodhaḥ syāt | ata eva mantreṇāvidyāśabdavācyaṃ karma kevalaṃ ninditvā vidyāpi kevalā nindyate:
andhaṃ tamaḥ praviśanti ye vidyām upāsate |
tato bhūya iva te tamo yad avidyāyāṃ ratāḥ || iti |
yad api ca "sarva ete puṇyalokā bhavanti" iti vākyaṃ tad api kevalakarmaviṣayam | "brahmasaṃstho 'mṛtatvam eti" ity asyāyam arthaḥ — prakṛtānām āśramiṇāṃ madhye yo brahmasaṃstho brahmapravaṇo brahmajñānāṅgabhūtaṃ karma karotīti yāvat, so 'mṛtatvam etīti | na punar brahmasaṃsthaśabdena bhikṣur ākhyāyate | tasya tatsaṃjñatve pramāṇābhāvāt | yo 'pi vidyākarmaṇor virodha uktaḥ so 'pi nopāsanātmikayā vidyayā kiṃ tu sākṣātkārātmikayā | tathā coktam |
bhidyate hṛdayagranthiś chidyante sarvasaṃśayāḥ |
kṣīyante cāsya karmāṇi tasmin dṛṣṭe parāvare || iti |
tasmād gṛhastho 'pi vimucyata iti smṛtir na bhaṅgena vyākhyeyā | gṛhasthasya tu muktir uktā chāndogye: "kuṭumbī śucau deśe vedam adhīyāno dhārmikān vidadhad ātmani sarvendriyāṇi saṃpratiṣṭhāpyāhiṃsan sarvabhūtāni anyatra tīrthebhyaḥ sa khalv evaṃ vartayan yāvadāyuṣaṃ  brahmalokam abhisaṃpadyate na punar āvartate" iti | vihitā hiṃsā tīrthāni(rtheṣu) | brahmaiva loko brahmalokaḥ | muktau jñānakarmasamuccayaṃ smṛtikārāś cāhuḥ | tatra manuḥ |
sukhābhyudayikaṃ caitan naiḥśreyasikam eva ca |
pravṛttaṃ ca nivṛttaṃ ca dvividhaṃ karma vaidikam ||
iha vāmutra vā kāmyaṃ pravṛttaṃ karma kīrtyate |
jñānapūrvaṃ tu vidvadbhir nivṛttam upadiśyate ||
pravṛttaṃ karma saṃsevya devānām eti sātmyatām |
nivṛttaṃ sevamānas tu bhūtān paśyati pañca vai || [1034]
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
yathānnaṃ madhusaṃyuktaṃ madhu cānnena saṃyutam |
evaṃ tapaś ca vidyā ca saṃyuktaṃ bheṣajaṃ mahat ||
yogayājñavalkyaḥ |
parijñānād bhaven muktir etad ālasyalakṣaṇam |
kāyakleśabhayāc caiva karma necchanti paṇḍitāḥ ||
jñānakarmasamāyogāt param āpnoti pūruṣaḥ |
pṛthagbhāvo na sidhyeta ubhe tasmāt samāśrayet ||
ityādi | tac ca nyāyārjitadhana ityādismṛtir mukhyatayaiva vyākhyātavyeti | tad anayor matayor yan nyāyyaṃ tad grāhyam || 3.205 ||
iti śrīvidyādharavaṃśaprabhavaśrīśilāhāranarendrajīmūtavāhanānvayaprasūta-
śrīmadaparādityadevaviracite yājñavalkyadharmāstranibandhe 'parārke
mokṣa(yati)prakaraṇam || 4 ||
atha saṭīkayājñavalkyasmṛtau
prāyaścittaprakraṇam | (5)
pāpakṣayāya prāyaścittāni vidhāsyante, duḥkhaphalatvena ca pāpasya tatkṣayārthatā prāyaścittānuṣṭhānasya prayujyata iti tatphalatvaṃ pāpasya tāvad āha |
mahāpātakajān ghorān narakān prāpya garhitān |
karmakṣayāt prajāyante mahāpātakinas tv iha || 3.206 ||
brahmahatyādimahāpātakaprabhṛtipāpanimittān narakāṃs tāmisrādīn ghorān suduḥsahān gharhitān atyantaheyān prāpyānubhūya narakadeśopabhogyaduḥkhaviśeṣaphalasya karmaṇaḥ kṣayān mahāpātakaśeṣavanta iha loke kutsitayoniṣu vakṣyamāṇāsu jāyante | narakabhogatīvrataraduḥkhyapradānarūpeṇa karmaṇaḥ kṣayaḥ svalpaduḥkhapradānākāreṇāvasthānam iti karmaṇaḥ kṣayo mahāpātakitvaṃ ceti dvayam aviruddham || 3.206 ||
sāmānyata uktam arthaṃ viśeṣata āha |
mṛgāśvasūkaroṣṭrāṇāṃ brahmahā yonim ṛcchati |
kharapukkasavenānāṃ surāpo 'pi na saṃśayaḥ || 3.207 ||
mṛgādijātīyaṃ śarīraṃ brahmahā, kharādijātīyaṃ surāpaḥ propnoti | pukkasavenau pratilomajaviśeṣau || 3.207 ||
kiṃ ca |
krimikīṭapataṅgatvaṃ svarṇahārī samāpnuyāt |
tṛṇagulmalatātvaṃ ca kramaśo gurutalpagaḥ || 3.208 ||
krimikīṭādir viprahemahārī, gurutalpagas tu tṛṇagulmādiḥ kramaśaḥ syāt || 3.208 ||
kuyoniprāpakatvaṃ pāpasyābhidhāya kuśarīraprāpakatvam idānīm āha |
brahmahā kṣayarogī syāt surāpaḥ śvāvadantakaḥ |
svarṇahārī tu kunakhī duścarmā gurutalpagaḥ || 3.209 ||
yo yean saṃvasty eṣāṃ sa talliṅgo 'bhijāyate |
kṣayarogī rājayakṣmī kuṣṭhī vā, śyāvadantakaḥ svabhāvāt kṛṣṇadantaḥ | kunakhī kṛṣṇanakhaḥ | duścarmā kutsitacarmā paṇḍo (?) vā, brahmahādisaṃsargī pāparogādiliṅgaḥ || 3.209 ||
kiṃ ca |
annahartāmayāvī syān mūkho vāgapahārakaḥ || 3.210 ||
dhānyamiśro 'tiriktāṅgaḥ piśunaḥ pūtināsikaḥ |
tilahṛ tailapāyī syāt pūtivaktraś ca sūcakaḥ || 3.211 ||
annahartṛprabhṛtir āmayāvyādhi(di)r bhavati | āmayāvy atra mandāgniḥ | mūko vāgdhīnaḥ | vāgapahāro 'nanujñātādhyayanaṃ pustakacauryaṃ parakṛtagranthasyātmīyatayā khyāpanaṃ vā | dhānyamiśro vrīhyādeḥ kodravādinā miśrakārī | atiriktāṅgaḥ ṣaḍaṅgulikaḥ sthūlaikacaraṇa ityādi | piśunaḥ pararandhraprakāśakaḥ | [1036] pūtināsiko durgandhi(dha)ghrāṇaḥ | tailapāyī prāṇiviśeṣaḥ | sūcakaḥ paradoṣavādī | piśunas tu netravaktrādiceṣṭayā paradoṣakhyāpaka ity apaunaruktyam || 3.210, 211 ||
api ca |
parasya yoṣitaṃ gatvā brahmasvam apahṛtya ca |
araṇye nirjane deśe bhavati brahmarākṣasaḥ || 3.212 ||
nigadavyākhyātam idam || 3.212 ||
hīnajātau prajāyante ratnānām apahārakāḥ |
pattraśākaṃ śikhī hṛtvā gandhāñ chucchundariḥ śubhān || 3.213 ||
mūṣako dhānyahārī syād yānam uṣṭraḥ phalaṃ kapiḥ |
jalaṃ plavaḥ payaḥ kāko gṛhakārī hy upaskaram || 3.214 ||
madhu daṃśaḥ palaṃ gṛdhro gāṃ godhāgniṃ bakas tathā |
śvitrī vastraṃ śvā rasaṃ tu cīrī lavaṇahārakaḥ || 3.215 ||
hīnajātiḥ śūdrādiyoniḥ tatra ratnānām apahārakā jāyante | ratnāni padmarāgādīni | pattraśākahāriprabhṛtiḥ śikhyādiḥ syāt | chucchundarir gandhamūṣakaḥ | yānam aśvādiḥ | plavaḥ pakṣiviśeṣaḥ | gṛhakārī prāṇiviśeṣaḥ | upaskaro gṛhopaskaraṇam | palaṃ māṃsaṃ, godhā bileśayaḥ śiśumārasadṛśaḥ prāṇiviśeṣaḥ | śvitrī śvetacarmā, rasaḥ kṣīrādiḥ | cīrī prāṇiviśeṣaḥ | prasiddham anyat || 3.213, 214, 215 ||
uktasya tātparyaṃ svayam evāha |
pradarśanārtham etat tu mayoktaṃ steyakarmaṇi |
dravyaprakārā hi yathā tathaiva prāṇijātayaḥ || 3.216 ||
yad uktam etad apahāraka evaṃvidho bhavatīti tan na niyamārthaṃ kiṃ tu pradarśanārtham | yato yathāpahāryadravyāṇy anantāni tathā tadapahārakasya prāptavyajātiviśeṣā apy anantāḥ | ato naite kārtsnyena śakyā vaktum || 3.216 ||
[1037]
pāpasya narakakuyonikuśarīraprāptiḥ phalam uktam, tasya samuccitasya phalam idānīm āha |
yathākarma phalaṃ prāpya tiryaktvaṃ kālaparyayāt |
jāyante lakṣaṇabhraṣṭā daridrāḥ puruṣādhamāḥ || 3.217 ||
yathākarma karmānatikrameṇa phalaṃ narakaṃ prāpya tad anu tiryaktvaṃ mṛgatvādi kālaparyayāt kālakrameṇānubhūya bhraṣṭaśubhalakṣaṇā dāridryādidoṣopetā adhamāḥ puruṣā jāyante || 3.217 ||
tato niṣkalpaṣībhūtāḥ kule mahati bhoginaḥ |
jāyante vidyayopetā dhanadhānyasamanvitāḥ || 3.218 ||
tata uktaphalopabhogāt kṣīṇapāpā dharmaśeṣeṇa guṇavati kule vidyayā purāṇanyāyamīmāṃsādikayā dharma(mā)viruddhaiś ca viṣayopabhogais tatsādhanaiś ca dhanadhānyādibhir upetā jāyante || 3.218 ||
pāpasya kāryam uktam idānīm kāraṇam āha |
vihitasyānanuṣṭhānān ninditasya ca sevanāt |
anigrahāc cendriyāṇāṃ naraḥ patanam ṛcchati || 3.219 ||
vihitasyāvaśyakasyānanuṣṭhānāt tadanyānuṣṭhānāt | ninditasya niṣiddhasya sevanād anuṣṭhānāt | aniṣiddheṣv api viṣayopabhogeṣv indriyaprasaṅganivāraṇāt | nara upanīto 'nupanīto vā patanaṃ pāpam ṛcchati prāpnoti | asmād eva vākyān nityakarmaṇām akaraṇe pratyavāyodaya iti gamyate | tatpratyavāyaprāgabhāvārthaṃ vāvaśyakeṣu karmasu puṃsāṃ pravṛttiḥ | anutpādyo 'pi pratyavāyaprāgabhāvaḥ śaṅkitanivṛttir bhavati | prayojanaṃ (?) yathāśaṅkitanivṛttir vyādhiprāgabhāvo bheṣajakriyāyāḥ | atha vopāttaduritakṣaya evāvaśyakānāṃ karmaṇām astu bhāvyaś ca prayojanaṃ ca | atra ca pakṣe "vihitasyānanuṣṭhānān naraḥ patanam ṛcchati" iti vākye 'nupāttaduritasyāvināśa eva pātakodayatvena kathaṃcil lakṣyate | evaṃ ca sati naimittikānāṃ karmaṇām akaraṇanimittapratyavāyaparihāra upāttaduritakṣayo vā prayojanam iti siddham | tad uktaṃ bhaviṣyatpurāṇe | [1038]
phalaṃ vināpy anuṣṭhānaṃ nityānām iṣyate sphuṭam |
kāmyānāṃ svaphalārthaṃ tu doṣaghātārtham eva tat ||
naimittikānāṃ karaṇaṃ trividhaṃ karmaṇāṃ phalam |
kṣetraṃ kecid upāttasya duritasya pracakṣate ||
anutpattiṃ tathā cānye pratyavāyasya manyate (?) |
nityakriyāṃ tathā cānye anuṣaṅgaphalaśrutim ||
asyārthaḥ — nityanaimittikakāmyāni trividhāni karmāni | tatra kadācit karmaṇāṃ rāgaviṣayīkṛtaṃ phalam uddiśya kāmyānām anuṣṭhānaṃ na tu nityānām | naimittikānāṃ tu brahmavadhādau nimitte vihitānāṃ doṣopaghātaḥ phalam | nityānāṃ tarhi kiṃ phalam ity apekṣite tad uktam upāttaduritakṣayaḥ pratyavāyānudayo vā tatphalam iti | yat tu nityakarmaṇām ānuṣaṅgikaḥ svargaḥ phalam iti tad ānuṣaṅgikatvād eva prayojanam iti || 3.219 ||
tasmāt teneha kartavyaṃ prāyaścittaṃ viśuddhaye |
evam asyāntarātmā ca lokaś caiva prasīdati || 3.220 ||
yasmād duḥkhaphalaṃ pāpaṃ tasmāt tasya viśuddhaye | vigatāmayena vihitākaraṇādimatā puṃsā vakṣyamāṇaṃ prāyaścittaṃ kāryam | kṛte he tasmin pāpakāriṇo 'ntarātmā lokaś ca prasīdati na bībhatsate duḥkhopabhogabhayaṃ lokāsaṃvyavahāraś ca nivartata ity arthaḥ | uktaprāyaścittasya viśuddhyarthā(thatā) | viśuddhiḥ pāpakṣayo vyavahārayogyatā ca || 3.220 ||
vyavasthām āha |
[caritavyam at nityaṃ prāyaścittaṃ viśuddhaye |
evam asyāntarātmā ca lokaś caiva prasīdati || ]
prāyaścittair apaity eno yad ajñānakṛtaṃ bhavet |
kāmato vyavahāryas tu vacanād iha jāyate || 2.221 ||
ajānatā yat kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ yathā suptena paryaṅkāt patatā kaścid dhataḥ | yathā vā [1039] jalabuddhyā surā pītā, tat prāyaścittair apaiti naśyati | yas tu kāmato rāgān martyāpakārī syān na tasya prāyaścittaiḥ pāpakṣayaḥ kiṃ tu vacanāt prāyaścittavacanāt saṃvyavahāryatayā janayogyatā bhavati | etac ca mahāpātakeṣv eva | upapātakādikaṃ tu pāpaṃ kāmakṛtam api prāyaścittaiḥ kṣīyate | yad āha chāgaleyaḥ |
prāyaścittam akāmānāṃ kāmāvāptau na vidyate |
upapātaka eva syāt tathā cātmopaghātine ||
ayam arthaḥ — kāmāvāptau kāmapūrvake pāpe prāyaścittaṃ pāpakṣayaparyantatayā na vidyate | upapātakātmahatyayos tu kāmapūrvake vidyata iti | yāni punar maraṇāntikaprāyaścittāni taiḥ pāpakṣayo bhavaty eva | tathā ca āpastambaḥ: "guruṃ hatvā śrotriyaṃ vā karma samāptam etenaiva vidhinocchvāsāṃś caret | nāsyāsmiṃl loke pratyāpattir vidyate kalmaṣaṃ tu nihanyate" iti | yamo 'pi |
mahāpātakakartāraś catvāro 'py aviśeṣataḥ |
agniṃ praviśya śudhyanti snātvā mahati vā kratau || iti |
yuktaṃ caitat | anyathā prāṇāntikavratavidhir anarthakaḥ syāt | gautamaḥ: "atha khalv asau puruṣo 'yājyena karmā lipyate | yathaitad ayājyayājanam abhakṣyabhakṣaṇam avadyavadanaṃ śiṣṭasyākriyā pratiṣiddhasevanam iti | tatra prāyaścittaṃ kuryān na kuryād iti mīmāṃsyate | na kuryād ity āhuḥ | na hi karma kṣīyata iti | kuryād ity apare | punaḥstomeneṣṭvā punaḥ samavāyantīti vijñāyate | vrātyastomaiś ceṣṭvā tarati sarvaṃ pāpmānaṃ tarati tarati brahmahatyāṃ yo 'śvamedhena yajate" | agnim iṣṭvābhiśasyamānaṃ yājayed iti ca | na hi karma kṣīyate | phalam adattveti śeṣaḥ | kuryād ity aparaṃ matam | siddhānta ity arthaḥ | tatra prāyaścittasya saṃvyavahārpāpakṣayau phalam ity atra śruteḥ(tiṃ) pramāṇatayodāharati — punastomenetyādi | anenāyāvyayatvalakṣaṇaṃ(?) saṃvyavahāryatāṃ prāpnuvantīti darśayati | vrātyastomaiḥ svakālānupanītaṃ prati vihitaiḥ prāyaścittakratubhiḥ sarvaṃ pāpmānaṃ vrātyatālakṣaṇaṃ taratīti vadatopapātakaprāyaścittānāṃ pāpakṣayaphalatā nidarśitā | na kevalaṃ vrātyatādikam upapātakam eva prāyaścittaiḥ kṣīyate | api tu mahāpātakam apīti vaktum udāharaṇāntaram āha: "tarati brahmahatyāṃ [1040] yo 'śvamedhena yajate" iti | atra ca rātrisattranyāyenārthavādikayoḥ saṃvyavahārapāpakṣayayoḥ svīkāraḥ | atra ca pāyaścittair apaity ena ity upakrāntaṃ vākyam, jñānato vyavahāryas tv ity upasaṃhartavyam | tadupakramavaśāj jñānata iti matvā kāmata ity uktavān | tataś cāyam artho bhavati — jñānataḥ kāmataś cainasvī prāyaścittair vyavahāryaḥ kriyate | yas tu jñānato na kāmataḥ pāpakārī syāt sa prāyaścittair apāpo bhavaty eva | yathā mlecchair haṭḥāt surāṃ pāyitaḥ | kāmād yad bhavati tad bhrāntipūrvakāt pramāṇapūrvakāc ca bhavati | tatra kāmato vyavahārya ity anena pramāṇapūrvakāt kāmata ity evaṃparaṃ veditavyam | bhrāntipūrvakakāmakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ prāyaścittāpaneyam eva | yathā dūrasthe brāhmaṇe sthāṇubhrāntyā hate | yata eva bhrāntipūrvakarāgakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ prāyaścittaheyam, ata evāha manuḥ |
akāmataḥ kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ vedābhyāsena naśyati |
kāmatas tu kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ prāyaścittaiḥ pṛthagvidhaiḥ || iti |
anjñānaṃ saṃśayo viparyayaś ca mohaḥ || 3.221 ||
mahāpātakajān ghorān ity atra narakaduḥkhopabhogaḥ pāpaphalatayoktaḥ | tam anūdya tāmisrādisaṃjñakān ekaviṃśatinarakān āha |
prāyaścittam akurvāṇāḥ pāpeṣu niratā narāḥ |
apaścāttāpino yānti narakān atidāruṇān || 3.222 ||
tāmisraṃ lohaśaṅkuṃ ca mahārauravaśālmalim |
rauravaṃ kuḍmalaṃ pūtimṛttikaṃ kālasūtrakam || 3.223 ||
saṃghātaṃ lohatodaṃ ca ṛbīsaṃ saṃpratāpanam |
mahānarakakākolaṃ saṃjīvananadīpatham || 3.224 ||
avīcim andhatāmisraṃ kumbhīpākaṃ tathaiva ca |
asipatravanaṃ caiva tāpanaṃ caikaviṃśatim || 3.225 ||
nigadavyākhyātāḥ ślokāḥ || 3.222, 223, 224, 225 ||
na kevalaṃ mahāpātakino narakayāyinaḥ | api tūpapātakino 'pīty āha | [1041]
mahāpātakajair ghorair upapātakajais tathā |
anvitā yānty acaritaprāyaścittā narādhamāḥ || 3.226 ||
yathā mahāpātakair anvitā akṛtaprāyaścittāḥ kāpuruṣā narakān yānti evaṃ vakṣyamāṇopapātakāpūrvānvitā api narādhamā narakān yānti || 3.226 ||
adhunā mahāpātakisaṃjñām āha |
brahmahā madyapaḥ stenas tathaiva gurutalpagaḥ |
ete mahāpātakino yaś ca taiḥ saṃpibet samām || 3.227 ||
brahmahā, surāpo, brāhmaṇasuvarṇasteyī, pitṛdāragāmī, ete pratyekaṃ mahāpātakisaṃjñā jñeyāḥ | yaś caiṣām anyatamena saha samāṃ saṃvatsaraṃ yāvat saṃpibet saṃvyavaharet so 'pi mahāpātakī | yo brāhmaṇaprāṇaviyogaphalavyāpārakartā sa brahmahā | kartā ca svatantras tatprayojakaś ca | tatra svatantro nāma dvārabhūtaṃ kartrantaram anapekṣya kriyāpravartakaḥ | yas tu svatantrasya kartur anukūlam ācaraṃs tatsādhyāṃ kriyāṃ sādhayati sa prayojakaḥ | tatra svātantryeṇa brahmavadhakartāraṃ brahmaheti śabdo 'ntaraṅgatvād evācaṣṭe na tu prayojakaṃ bahiragṅgatvād iti na vācyam | yad āha āpastambaḥ: "prayojayitānumantā kartā ceti svarganarakaphaleṣu karmasu phalabhāginaḥ | yo bhūya ārabhate tasmin phalaviśeṣaḥ" iti | na cānumantuḥ kathaṃ kartṛtvam akartuś ca kathaṃ svarganarakaphalayoga iti vācyam | prayojakatayā tasyāpi kartṛtvāt | prabale hi daṇḍapraṇetari rājādau tenānumataḥ saty api brahmavadharāge na taṃ śaknuyāt kaścit kartum | gobalīvardanyāyena prayojakakartur anumatas tu śaknuyād iti | vadhādau bhavaty anumantuḥ prayojakakartṛtā | gobalīvardanyāyena prayojakakartuḥ pṛthag anumantopāttaḥ | pradarśanārthaṃ caitat | tataś ca yena vinā hantā svakriyām api na śaknuyāt kartuṃ sa sarvo 'pi hananasiddhaye vyāpriyamāṇo 'pi bhavati ghātakaḥ | ata evāha paiṭhīnasiḥ:
hantānumantāpy upadeśakartā protsāhakaḥ saṃpratipādakaś ca |
ārambhakṛt sahāyaś ca tathā mārgānudeśakaḥ ||
[1042]        āśrayaḥ śastradātā ca bhaktadātā vikarmiṇām |
yuddhopadeśakaś caiva tadvināśapradarśakaḥ ||
upekṣākārakaś caiva doṣavaktānumodakaḥ |
aniṣeddhā kṣamo yaś ca sarve tatkāryakāriṇaḥ ||
yathāśaktyanurūpaṃ vai daṇḍam eṣāṃ prakalpayet ||
bhaktadāyakādayaḥ paramaraṇam abhisaṃdhāya yadi bhaktādi prayacchanti tadā vadhahetavo na bhaktādidānamātreṇa | yadi tanmātreṇāpi hetutā syāt tadā pitror api putraṃ janayatos tatkartṛkāsu kuśalākuśalakriyāsu hetutā syāt | dharmārthanirmitakūpādau ca pramādapātibrāhmaṇamṛtyuhetutā kūpakartur āpadyeta | na caivam asti kāraṇakāraṇatvenākāraṇatvāt | vadham anuddiśyāpi pareṇa kṛto vyāpāro yadi vadham ācaratā kartṛviśeṣaviśiṣṭatvenāpekṣyate tadā bhavaty eva tasya hetur yathāham anenākruṣṭhas tāḍito nirdhano vā kṛta ity ātmānaṃ hanmīty abhisaṃdhāyātmani vyāpādite bhavaty ākrośādikartus tadvyāpattau hetunā na punaḥ kūpādāv evam, na hi tatra bhavati yasmād devadattenāyaṃ kūpo nirmitas tasmād aham atra patāmīty abhisaṃdhiḥ | nāpi yasmād ābhyāṃ pitṛbhyām aham utpāditas tasmād imāṃ kriyāṃ karomīti putrasya | ākrośādikartur brahmavadhanimittatām āha viṣṇuḥ |
ākruṣṭas tāḍito vāpi dhamair vā viprayojitaḥ |
yad uddiśya tyajet prāṇāṃs tam āhur brahmaghātakam ||
yatra tv ākrośatāḍanādisaṃbandham antareṇa bhrāntyādivaśena param uddiśya brāhmaṇa ātmānaṃ hanti tatra hantur eva doṣo noddeśa(śya)syety āha sumantuḥ |
asaṃbandhena yaḥ kaścid dvijaḥ prāṇān samutsṛjet |
tasyaiva tad bhavet pāpaṃ na tu yaṃ parikīrtayet ||
ātatāyinaṃ tu brāhmaṇam api hatavato na brahmahatvam iti vyāsa āha |
ātatāyinam āyāntam api vedāntapāragam |
jighāṃsantaṃ jighāṃseta na tena brahmahā bhavet ||
bhrūṇahety api pāṭhaḥ | tatra bhrūṇahā brahmahety arthaḥ | [1043] manuḥ |
guruṃ vā bālavṛddhau vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā bahuśrutam |
ātatāyinam āyāntaṃ hanyād evāvicārayan ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
svādhāyinaṃ kule jātaṃ yo hanyād ātatāyinam |
na tena brahmahā bhavati manyus taṃ manyum ṛcchati ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
ākruṣṭas tu samākrośaṃs tāḍitaḥ pratitāḍayan |
vināśārthinam āyāntaṃ ghātayan nāparādhnuyāt ||
viṣṇuḥ |
paradāraratāś caurā doṣaprāptāś ca ye dvijāḥ |
anyonyaṃ vā hatā yuddhe na tena brahmahā bhavet ||
manuḥ |
śastraṃ dvijātibhir grāhyaṃ dharmo yatroparudhyate |
ātmanaś ca paritrāṇe dakṣiṇānāṃ ca saṃgare ||
baudhāyanaḥ: "ṣaṭsv anabhicaran patati" | ṣaṭsv ātatāyiṣu | tān āha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
agnido garadaś caiva śastrapāṇir dhanāpahaḥ |
kṣetradāraharaś caiva ṣaḍ ete hy ātatāyinaḥ ||
garado viṣadaḥ | matsyapurāṇam |
gṛhakṣetrādihartāraṃ tathā patnyabhigāminam |
agnidaṃ garadaṃ caiva tathā cāpy udyatāyudham ||
abhicāraṃ ca kurvāṇaṃ rājagāmica paiśunam |
ete hi kathitā loke dharmajñair ātatāyinaḥ ||
vyāsaḥ |
udyatādiviṣāgniṃ ca śāpodyatakaraṃ tathā |
ātharvaṇena hantāraṃ piśunaṃ caiva rājani ||
bhāryāpahāriṇaṃ caiva ṣaḍāhur hy ātatāyinaḥ ||
atra ca vartamānāgnidānādivyāpāra evātatāyina ucyante | tadvyāpāranivāraṇaṃ ca yatra vadham antareṇa na saṃbhavati tatraiva tadvadhānujñā | yatra tu daṇḍaśastrādiprahāramātreṇaiva śakyo nivārayituṃ tatra tu tadvadho doṣanimittam eva | ata evoktaṃ bṛhaspatinā |
ākruṣṭas tu samākrośaṃs tāḍitaḥ pratitāḍayan |
ityādi | etad eva matvā sumantunāpy uktam: "nātatāyivadhe doṣo 'nyatra gobrāhmaṇāt" iti | evaṃ ca sati yatra gobrāhmaṇavadhād ṛte tatprahāramātrādinā [1044] śakya ātmā trātuṃ tatra tadvadhakārī brahmahatyādipātakavān bhavaty eva | tataś ca yā ātatāyivadhe doṣasmṛtayaḥ prāyaścittasmṛtayaś ca tāḥ sarvāḥ pūrvoktaviṣayā jñeyāḥ | tasmād evaṃvidho brahmahā | madyaśabdo 'tra surāvacano grāhyaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
brahmahatyā surāpānaṃ steyaṃ gurvaṅganāgamam |
mahānti pātakāny āhuḥ saṃsargaṃ caiva taiḥ saha ||
surā ca paiṣṭy eva | yad āha manuḥ |
gauḍī paiṣṭī ca mādhvī ca vijñeyā trividhā surā |
yathā caikā tathā sarvā na pātavyā dvijottamaiḥ ||
ekāṃ paiṣṭīṃ dṛṣṭāntīkṛtya sarvasyā brahmaṇaṃ prati pānaniṣedhaṃ kurvann ekā mukhyā gauṇītareti darśayati | evaṃ ca brāhmaṇasya tisro 'py apeyāḥ, kṣatriyavaiśyayos tu paiṣṭy eva | yad āha sa eva |
surā vai malam annānāṃ pāpmā ca malam ucyate |
tasmād brāhmaṇarājanyau vaiśyaś ca na surāṃ pibet ||
annamalatvaṃ ca vrīhyādipiṣṭimayyāḥ | evaṃ ca na saṃbhavati gauḍīmādhvyoḥ | na hi guḍamadhunī annam | annaṃ tu vrīhyādy eva | tataś ca paiṣṭīpānaṃ dvijānāṃ pratiṣiddham | paiṣṭyā itarayoś ca brāhmaṇasyeti mantavyam | ata evāha viśvāmitraḥ |
brāhmaṇo na ca hantavyaḥ surā peyā na ca dvijaiḥ |
brāhmaṇasvarṇaharaṇaṃ kartavyaṃ na kadācana ||
gurupatnīṃ na gacchec ca saṃsargaṃ taiś ca nācaret |
mahāpātakisaṃjñā tu nirdiṣṭaiṣā manīṣibhiḥ ||
stenaśabdo 'tra brāhmaṇasuvarṇasteyavacano draṣṭavyaḥ | pañca mahāpātakāny ācakṣate — "gurutalpam, surāpānam, brahmahatyām, brāhmaṇasuvarṇaharaṇam, patitasaṃyogaṃ ca" iti | dvayam api ceti (ca) vakṣyati — "brahmaṇasvarṇahārī tu" iti | guruḥ pitā tasya talpo bhāryā, tadgāmī gurutalpagaḥ | etair (ta) itinirdeśāc caturṇām evānyatamena saṃsargī mahāpātakī, na tu tenāpi yaḥ saṃsargī | mahattvena ca viśeṣaṇaṃ patitāntarebhya eṣāṃ doṣamattvakhyāpanārtham | tena patitasyodakaṃ kāryam ityādikam eṣām eva patitāntarāṇām iti gamyate | ata evāha devalaḥ: "pañcaitāni mahāpātakāni kṛtvā brāhmaṇaḥ [1045] sadbhir nābhibhāṣyo nānugrāhyo 'bhiśastaḥ sarvakarmaparivarjitaḥ patatasamo bhavati" iti | brāhmaṇagrahaṇam upalakṣaṇārtham | anye 'pi ca patitāḥ santi | yad āha gautamaḥ: "brahmahasurāpagurutalpagamātṛpitṛyonisaṃbaddhāga-
stenanāstikaninditakarmābhyāsyapatitatyāgipatita(tā)tyāginaḥ patitāḥ pātakasaṃyojakāś ca taiś cābdaṃ samācaran" | asyārthaḥ — mātṛsaṃbandhā(baddhā) mātṛbhaginī tadduhitrādis tadgāmī | pitṛsaṃbaddhā pitṛbhaginī tadduhitrādis tadgāmī | yonisaṃbaddhā bhaginī tadduhitrādis tadgāmī | steno brāhmaṇasuvarṇasya nāstiko bauddhādisiddhāntāvaṣṭambhena śrautasmārtavidhyatikrāmī | ninditakarmābhyāsī cirakālaṃ niṣiddhakarmakārī | apatitatyāgī putrādīnām apatitānāṃ tyāgī, teṣām eva putrādīnāṃ patitānām atyāgī | ete patitāḥ | ye ca pātakeṣu tatkartṝn prayojayanti te 'pi patitāḥ | brahmahādiśabdair evopādātuṃ śakyānāṃ pātakasaṃyojakānāṃ pṛthagupādānaṃ prāyaścittānyatvapratipādanārtham | na cedam atrāśaṅkanīyaṃ pātakasaṃyojakavad dharmasaṃyojakānām ṛtvijāṃ yāgaphalāvāptir api syād iti | yato yo vidhipratiyogī san vidhiviṣaye prayuṅkte ssa vai dharmaphalabhāgī | na cartvijo yāgavidhipratiyoginaḥ | ato na te tatphalabhāginaḥ | pratyavāyarūpaṃ tu phalaṃ rāgataḥ kriyāyāṃ kartṛtāyāṃ satyāṃ śāstreṇāvabodhyata iti yuktaṃ pātakasaṃyojakāś ca patitā iti vacanam | kiṃ punaḥ patitatvam ity apekṣita āha — "dvijātikarmabhyo hāniḥ patanam | paratra cāsiddhiḥ" iti | dvijātīnāṃ svakarmasv adhyayanādiṣv anadhikāraḥ patanam | atra ca dvijātigrahaṇaṃ pradarśanārtham | tena yasya yat karma vihitaṃ tasya tatrānadhikāraḥ patanam iti sūtrārthaḥ | anyathā strīṇāṃ na patitatvam ucyetādvijatvāt | na copanayanasthānīyo vivāhas tāsāṃ dvijatvasaṃpādaka iti vācyam | tathā hi sati vedādhyayanam api tāḥ kuryuḥ | na caivaṃ tasmād advijānām api śūdrādīnām asti patanam | prāk ca pātityāt kṛtānāṃ śubhakarmaṇāṃ paratra lokāntare phale bhogāsiddhiḥ pātityam | kiṃ ca — "tam eke narakaṃ bruvate" | eka ācāryāḥ paratrāsiddhirūpaṃ duḥkhopabhogaviśeṣaṃ narakam āhuḥ | gautamas tu naivaṃ manyate | patitasya hi narakabhogo na punaḥ sa eva patanam iti hi tadabhiprāyaḥ | mātrādisaṃbaddhāgaḥ patita uktaḥ | tatra pākṣikam apavādam āha — "na strīṣv agurutalpaṃ patatīty eke" | sakṛdabuddhiviṣayaṃ caitat paramatam | agurutalpam iti pitṛbhāryāparyudāsaḥ | caṇḍālādistrīṇām apy atra paryudāso draṣṭavyaḥ | [1046] ata evāha manuḥ |
caṇḍālāntyastriyo gatvā bhuktvā ca pratigṛhya ca |
pataty ajñānato vipro jñānāt sāmyaṃ tu gacchati ||
tataś ca pitṛpatnyādivarjitāsu gacchan na patati śrautasmārtakarasv anadhikārī na bhavati | "ayājanānadhyāpanād ṛtvigācāryau patanīyasevāyāṃ heyāv anyatra hānāt patati" | [ṛtvig ayājanāt | ācāryo 'nadhyāpanāt | tathobhāv api patanīyakarmakāriṇau heyau | anyathā hānāt patati] | "tasya ca parityajata ity eke" | tasya vinā śāstrīyaṃ kāraṇam ṛtvigācāryau parityajata ity arthaḥ | patitātyāgī patatīty uktam, tadapavādam āha "na karhicin mātāpitror avṛttiḥ" | na karhicit kadācit patitatvadaśāyām apy avṛttiḥ, aśuśrūṣaṇam apoṣaṇaṃ vā | vasiṣṭhas tu patitasya pitus tyāgam āha: "patitaḥ pitā parityājyo mātā tu putraṃ prati na patati" iti | tatra pitur mahāpātakinas tyāgo na tu prakārāntareṇa patitasyety avirodhaḥ | atrāpy apavādāntaram āha: "dāyaṃ tu na bhajetan" iti | dāyaṃ pitror dhanam |
palāṇḍuṃ viḍvarāhaṃ ca chatrākaṃ grāmakukkuṭam |
laśulaṃ gṛñjanaṃ caiva matyā jagdhvā pated dvijaḥ ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ: "patitenotpannaḥ patito bhavatīty āhur anyatra striyāḥ" | sā hi paragāminī | tathā |
sadyaḥ patati māṃsena lākṣayā lavaṇena ca |
tryaheṇ śūdro bhavati brāhmaṇaḥ kṣīravikrayāt ||
baudhāyanaḥ: "samudrayānabrāhmaṇanyāsāpaharaṇam iti patanīye" | āpastambaḥ: "na doṣaṃ buddhvāpi pūrvaṃ parebhyaḥ samākhyāpayet | samākhyātā hi patitaḥ syāt" | devalaḥ: "gobhartṛviśvasitānnadapravrajitabandhumitraghātakā mātṛpitṛputradāratyāgino yajñopahartā vṛṣalīpatiḥ somavikrayī vrātyo niṣkrayaś ceti patitāḥ" | [1047] yāny atropapātakamadhyapatitāni nirdiśyante teṣām anubandhapratyayābhyāsavaśena pātityahetutvam aviruddham || 3.227 ||
gurūṇām adhyadhikṣepo vedanindā suhṛdvadhaḥ |
brahmahatyāsamaṃ jñeyam adhītasya ca nāśanam || 3.228 ||
gurūṇāṃ mukhyāmukhyānām upary upary ākṣepo 'nṛtena pāpābhiśaṃsanam | vedānāṃ nindanam aprāmāṇyābhidhānam | suhṛdo mitrasya brāhmaṇavyatirikta[sya] vadhaḥ | adhītasya vedasya baudhādisiddhāntāvaṣṭambhena nāśaṃ(śaṇaṃ) tyāgo 'nabhyāsaḥ | etat pratyekaṃ brahmahatyāsamaṃ veditavyam | manunā,
brahmojjhaṃ vedanindā ca kūṭasākṣyaṃ suhṛdvadhaḥ |
garhitānādyayor jagdhiḥ surāpānasamāni ṣaṭ ||
iti surāpānasamatvena vedasya tyāganinde suhṛdvadhaś ca nirdiṣṭaḥ | prāyaścittavikalpārthaṃ samatvābhidhānān nyūnatety anusaṃdheyam ṛṣisamo brāhmaṇa iti yāvat | garhitaṃ prasiddham anādyaṃ durgandhatvādinā bhakṣaṇānarham | manuḥ |
anṛtaṃ ca samutkarśe rājagāmi ca paiśunam |
guroś cālīkanirbandhaḥ samāni brahmahatyayā ||
samutkarṣe guṇavattāyāṃ spardhā guṇavattāparīkṣā vā | tatrobhayatra parīkṣakasyānṛtavacanaṃ brahmahatyāsamam | alīkanirbandho 'nṛtābhiśaṃsanam | tathā ca gautamaḥ: "kauṭasākṣyaṃ rājagāmi paiśunaṃ guror anṛtābhiśaṃsanaṃ pātakasamāni" iti | pracetāḥ |
gogarbhiṇībālavadho brahmahatyāsamāni ca || 3.228 ||
niṣiddhabhakṣaṇaṃ jaihmyam utkarṣe ca vaco 'nṛtam |
rajasvalāmukhāsvādaḥ surāpānasamāni tu || 3.229 ||
niṣiddhasya luśunāder bhakṣaṇam, jaihmyaṃ vāṅmanaḥkāyakarmaṇām anārjavam | utkarṣe 'nṛtavacanaṃ vyākhyātam | rajasvalāmukhacumbanam ity etāni pratyekaṃ surāpānatulyāni | abhakṣyabhakṣaṇam it pāṭhe, abhakṣyaśabdenātrātyantagarhitaṃ palāṇḍvādi gṛhyate || 2.229||
aśvaratnamanuṣyastrībhūdhenugrahaṇam tathā |
nikṣepasya ca sarvaṃ hi suvarṇasteyasaṃmitam || 3.230 ||
[1048]
brāhmaṇasaṃbandhinām aśvādīnāṃ pratyekam apahāraḥ suvarṇasteyasaṃmitaḥ | manuṣyaḥ puruṣaḥ | strī puruṣī, manuṣyasāhacaryāt | gobalīvardanyāyena ca paunaruktyaparihāraḥ | dhenur navaprasūtā gauḥ | nikṣepaḥ svarṇavyatiriktaḥ | viṣṇuḥ: "brāhmaṇasya bhūmiharaṇaṃ nikṣepaharaṇaṃ ca suvarṇasteyasamam" | manuḥ |
nikṣepasyāpaharaṇaṃ narāśvarajatasya ca |
bhūmivajramaṇīnāṃ ca rukmasteyasamaṃ smṛtam || 3.230 ||
sakhibhāryākumārīṣu svayoniṣv antyajāsu ca |
sagotrāsu sutastrīṣu gurutalpasamaṃ smṛtam || 3.231 ||
sakhibhāryādiṣu retaḥsecanaṃ gurutalpasamam ṛṣibhiḥ smṛtam | sagotratvenaiva sutastrīniṣedhe siddhe tasyāḥ pṛthagupādānaṃ tadgāmino doṣagauravakhyāpanārtham | tathā ca hārītaḥ: "mātṛduhitṛsnuṣāgamanam ity atipātakāni |
atipātakinas tv ete praviśeyur hutāśanam |
na hy anyā niṣkṛtis teṣāṃ vidyate hi kadācana ||
svayonir bhaginī, antyajā pratilomotpannā | manuḥ |
retaḥsekaḥ svayonīṣu kumārīṣv antyajāsu ca |
sakhyuḥ putrasya ca strīṣu gurutalpasamaṃ viduḥ ||
retaḥsekagrahaṇāt prāktano nivṛttau na tato gurutalpasamatvam | gautamaḥ: "sakhisayonisagotraśiṣyabhāryāsnuṣāyāṃ ca gavi ca talpasamo 'vakā(ka)ra ity eke" | talpo gurutalpaḥ | avakaro retaḥsekaḥ || 3.231 ||
gurutalpanirdeśam āha |
pituḥ svasāraṃ mātuś ca mātulānīṃ snuṣām api |
mātuḥ sapatnīṃ bhaginīm ācāryatanayāṃ tathā || 3.232 ||
ācāryapatnīṃ svasutāṃ gacchaṃś ca gurutalpagaḥ |
liṅgaṃ chittvā vadhas tasya sakāmāyāḥ striyās tathā || 3.233 ||
pitṛbhaginyādīnām anyatamāṃ gacchan gurutalpago bhavati | gurutalpagaprāyaścittabhāg bhavatīty arthaḥ | yasa ca bhrāhmaṇavyatiriktasya liṅgaṃ prajananaṃ chittvā rājñā vadhaḥ kāryaḥ | uktānāṃ yoṣitāṃ madhye yā yoṣidutkaṭakāmā satī puruṣaṃ [1049] pravartayati, tasyā api liṅgacchedaḥ pūrvokto vadha eva daṇḍaḥ | evaṃ daṇḍitasya pāpakṣayo bhavati | yad āha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
rājabhir dhṛtadaṇḍāś ca kṛtvā pāpāni mānavāḥ |
nirmalāḥ svargam āyānti santaḥ sukṛtino yathā ||
nāradaḥ |
mātā mātṛṣvasā śvaśrūr mātulāṇī pitṛṣvasā |
pitṛvyasakhiśiṣyastrī bhaginī tatsakhī snuṣā ||
duhitācāryabhāryā ca sagotrā śaraṇāgatā |
rājñī pravrajitā dhātrī sādhvī varṇottamāpi ca ||
āsām anyatamāṃ gacchan gurutalpaga ucyate |
śiśnasyotkartanāt tatra nānyo daṇḍo vidhīyate ||
atra mātur upādānaṃ mātṛvad itarāsāṃ gamane doṣagauravakhyāpanārtham | na tu doṣasāmyapratipādanāya | ata eva saṃvartaḥ |
mātaraḥ yo 'bhigacchet tu svasāraṃ puruṣādhamaḥ |
na tasya niṣkṛtiṃ vidyāt svāṃ caiva tanayāṃ tathā ||
tathā |
mātaraṃ gurupatnīṃ ca svasāraṃ tanayāṃ tathā |
gatvā tu praviśed agniṃ nānyā śuddhir vidhīyate ||
gurupatnī pitṛbhāryā, ācāryabhāryā vā | marīciḥ: "yady ete nopanetāraḥ pitṛvyabhrātṛmātulāḥ | gurutalpavrataṃ caret" | pitṛvyādīnām anupanetṝṇāṃ yo bhāryāgāmi na tasya gurutalpagatvam iti marīcivākyād gamyate | ata evoktaṃ vasiṣṭhena: "boddhavyaṃ gurutalpasya bhartā hy āpatsu devaraḥ" iti |
mātṛṣvasā mātṛsakhī duhitā ca pitṛṣvasā |
mātulānī svasā śvaśrūr gatvā sadyaḥ pated dvijaḥ || 3.232, 233 ||
upapātakāny āha |
govadho vrātyatā steyam ṛṇānāṃ cānapa(pā)kriyā |
anāhitāgnitāpaṇyavikrayaḥ parivedanam || 3.234 ||
[1050]        bhṛtād adhyayanādānaṃ bhṛtakādhyāpanaṃ tathā |
pāradāryaṃ pārivittyaṃ vārdhuṣyaṃ lavaṇakriyā || 3.235 ||
strīśūdraviṭkṣatravadho ninditārthopajīvanam |
nāstikyaṃ vratalopaś ca sutānāṃ caiva vikrayaḥ || 3.236 ||
dhānyakupyapaśusteyam ayājyānāṃ ca yājanam |
pitṛmātṛsutatyāgas taḍāgārāmavikrayaḥ || 3.237 ||
kanyāsaṃdūṣaṇaṃ caiva parivindakayājanam |
kanyāpradānaṃ tasyaiva kauṭilyaṃ vratalopanam || 3.238 ||
ātmano 'rtha kriyārambho madyapastrīniṣevaṇam |
svādhyāyāgnisutatyāgo bāndhavatyāga eva ca || 2.239 ||
indhanārthaṃ drumacchedaḥ strīhiṃsauṣadhijīvanam |
hiṃsrayantravidhānaṃ ca vyasanāny ātmavikrayaḥ || 3.240 ||
śūdrapreṣyaṃ hīnasakhyaṃ hīnayoniviṣevaṇam |
tathaivānāśrame vāsaḥ parānnaparipuṣṭatā || 3.241 ||
asacchrāstrādhigamanam ākareṣv adhikāritā |
bhāryāyā vikrayaś caiṣām ekaikam upapātakam || 3.242 ||
eṣāṃ govadhādīnām ekaikam upapātakasaṃjñaṃ veditavyam | vrātyatā sāvitrīpatitatvam, steyaṃ cauryam | tac ca suvarṇacauryatatsamavyatiriktam | ṛṇānām anapa(pā)kriyā yajñaprajotpādanasvādhyāyādhyayanānām akaraṇam | atha vottamavarṇād gṛhītānāṃ dhanānām apratidānam | saty adhikāre 'nāhitāgnitā | apaṇyānāṃ pratiṣiddhavikrayāṇāṃ dravyāṇāṃ vikrayaḥ | dārāgniparigrahayogyaṃ jyeṣṭham atikramya kaniṣṭhasya parigrahaḥ parivedanam | bhṛtāt svādhyāyasya grahaṇam | tasya cādhyāpanam | pāradāryaṃ gurudāratatsamādhyām anyat | parivitter bhāvaḥ pārivittyam | parivittim āha manuḥ |
dārāgnihotrasaṃyogaṃ kurute yo 'graje sthite |
partivettā sa vijñeyaḥ parivittis tu pūrvajaḥ || iti || [1051]
vārdhuṣyaṃ niṣiddhavṛddhyupjīvanam | lavaṇakriyā lavaṇotpādanam | etac ca vānaprasthavyatiriktaviṣayam | tasya ca kevalaṃ svayaṃkṛtam iti lavaṇakriyā vihitā manunā | ātreyīvyatiriktāyāḥ striyāḥ savanasthakṣatriyavaiśyayoḥ śūdrasya ca vadhaḥ | aniṣiddhavikrayasyāpi paṇyasya pratiṣiddhārthopajīvanam | nāsti daivam iti matimān nāstikaḥ | tasya bhāvao nāstikyam | vrataṃ brahmacaryam | brahmahastyādiprāyaścittaṃ vā, tasya prakrāntasya lopas tyāgaḥ | sutānām apatyānāṃ vikrayaḥ | dhānyaṃ vrīhiḥ | kupyaṃ trapusīsādiḥ, paśur gavādiḥ | vrātyatā steyam ity anenaiva steyasyopapātakatvasiddhau dhānyādisteyagrahaṇam agryasteyasya pākṣikopapātakatvajñāpanārtham | atha vā dhānyādisteyasya gauravakhyāpanārtham | tasya prāyaścittagauravasiddhiḥ | tyāgakāraṇaṃ vinā pitrādityāgaḥ | tyāgakāraṇaṃ gautamenoktam: "tyajet pitaraṃ rājaghātakaṃ śūdrayājakaṃ śūdrārthayājakaṃ vedaviplāvakaṃ bhrūṇāhanam | yac cāntyāvasāyibhiḥ saha saṃvaset" iti | taḍāgādivikrayas taḍāgādidharmavikrayaḥ | kanyāyā dūṣaṇam aṅgulyādinā kṣatayonikaraṇam | taddoṣakhyāpanaṃ vā | parivindakayājanaṃ parivettṛtvanimitte vivāhe brahmatvaṃ tasya parivedanapravṛttasya kanyādānam | kauṭilyam anārjavam | vratasya saṃkalpasya snātakavratādirūpasya lopanaṃ mithyākaraṇam | kriyārambhaḥ pākakriyārambhaḥ,
aghaṃ sa kevalaṃ bhuṅkte yaḥ pacaty ātmakāraṇāt |
ityādau tannindāśravaṇāt | svakīyāyā api madyapastriyāḥ sevanaṃ niṣevaṇam, svādhyāyasya kāryāntaravyagratayā tyāgaḥ | na tu tadaprāmāṇyādhavasāyitayā | sa hi brahmahatyāsāmyenoktaḥ | agneḥ śrautasya smārtasya vā tyāgaḥ | sutasyārakṣaṇam atra tyāgaḥ, svādhyāyāgnityāgasāhacaryāt | pitṛmātṛsutatyāga ity atra tu jātakarmādisaṃskārākaraṇaṃ sutasya tyāgaḥ | bāndhavānāṃ pitṛvyamātulādīnāṃ śāstrīyatyāgakāraṇābhāve saṃbhojanasaṃbhāṣaṇādivyavahāratyāgaḥ | pākādiprayojanāgnisamindhanārtham aśuṣkavṛkṣabhañjanaṃ drumacchedaḥ | strījīvanaṃ bhāryāyā veśyātvakaraṇena jīvanam | hiṃsājīvanaṃ prāṇivadhena jīvanam | hiṃsrayatnaṃ tilekṣuprabhṛtipīḍanayatnam, vyasanāni mṛgayādīny aṣṭādaśa, ātmavikrayo dravyaṃ gṛhītvātmano dāsatvakaraṇam | śūdrapreṣyam iti bhāvapradhāno nirdeśaḥ | tena śūdraṃ praty ātmanaḥ preṣyatvakaraṇam ity arthaḥ | hīnasakhyaṃ jātito guṇataś ca hīnamaitram | hīnayoniḥ [1052] śūdrā, tanniṣevaṇaṃ tadekabhāryatvam | anāśrame vāso brahmacaryādyāśramarāhityam | parānnaparipuṣṭatā bāhulyena parānnopajīvanam | asacchāstrāṇāṃ vedavirodhinām adhigamanaṃ śravaṇacintanādikam, asacchāsrāṇāṃ cānuttamayugadharmahāsāviṣkaraṇacikīrṣayaiveśvareṇoktānām utpattiḥ śāstreṣv evam eva prasiddheḥ | tathā ca varāhapurāṇe pūrva itihāse 'gastyaṃ prati rudraḥ |
kṛte nārāyaṇaḥ śuddhaḥ sūkṣmamūrtir upāsate |
tretāyāṃ yajñarūpeṇa pañcarātrais tu dvāpare ||
kalau satkṛtamārgeṇa bahurūpeṇa tāmasaiḥ |
ijyate dveṣabuddhyāsau paramātmā janārdanaḥ ||
na tastmāt paramo devo bhavitā na bhaviṣyati ||
bhūyaś tatraiva |
evam uktas tato devo mām uvāca janārdanaḥ |
yugāni trīṇi bahavo mām upaiṣyanti jantavaḥ ||
antye yuge praviralā bhaviṣyanti madāśrayāḥ |
eṣa mohaṃ sṛjāmy āśu yo janaṃ mohayiṣyati(?) ||
tvaṃ ca rudra mahābāho mohaśāstrāṇi kāraya |
alpāyāsān darśayitvā phalaṃ dīrghaṃ pradarśaya ||
kuhakānīndrajālāni viruddhācaraṇāni ca |
darśayitvā janaṃ sarvaṃ mohayāśu maheśvara ||
evam uktas tadā tena devena parameṣṭhinā |
ātmānucoditaḥ sadyaḥ prakāśo 'haṃkṛtas tataḥ ||
tasmād ārabhya kālāt tu matpraṇīteṣu sattama |
śāstreṣv abhirato loko bāhulyena na veda mām || iti |
evaṃ tāyi(?)prabhṛtipraṇītāmnāyādīnām anusaṃdheyam | sarveṣv ākareṣu hemaratnādyutpattisthāneṣv adhikāritā rājaniyogād ādhipatyam | bhāryāyā vikrayaḥ | caśabdād anuktānāṃ hiṃsauṣadhīnāṃ stryājīvo 'bhicāro mūlakarma ceti manvādiśāstreṣu vaśīkaraṇaprabhṛtīnāṃ samuccayārthaḥ || 3.234, 235, 236, 237, 238, 239, 240, 241, 242 ||
[1053]
taddeśakramānurodhena brahmahatyāprāyaścittāni tāvad āha |
śiraḥkapālī dhvajavān bhikṣāśī karma vedayan |
brahmahā dvādaśābdāni mitabhukśuddhim āpnuyāt || 3.243 ||
brahmahā brahmavadhakārī dvādaśa varṣāṇi śiraḥkapālī śirosthyekadeśadhārī, dhvajavān khṭvāṅgī, bhikṣāśī bhikṣābhojananiyamavān | karma svakṛtaṃ brahmavadhākhyaṃ vedayan kathayaṃl lokānāṃ śudhyati brahmavadhapāpān mucyate | śiraḥkapālaṃ ca svayaṃhatasya brāhmaṇasya grāhyam | yad āha śātātapaḥ: "brāhmaṇo brāhmaṇaṃ ghātayitvā vā tasya śiraḥkapālam ādāya tīrthāny anusaṃcaret" iti | kapālaṃ ca pāṇinā dharyam, yad āha gautamaḥ: "khaṭvāṅgakapālapāṇiḥ" iti | khṭvā cātra śavanirharaṇārtha, tadaṅgam eva dhvajaśabdena vivakṣitam | dhvajāgrāropitakapālena bhavitavyam, tathāha manuḥ |
brahmahā dvādaśābdāni kuṭīṃ kṛtvā vane vaset |
bhaikṣāśy ātmaviśuddhyarthaṃ kṛtvā śavaśirodhvajam || iti |
bhaikṣāśane viśeṣam āha saṃvartaḥ |
brahmahā tu vanaṃ gatvā vālavāsā jaṭī dhvajī |
vanyāny eva phalāny aśnan sarvakāmavivarjitaḥ ||
bhikṣāśī vicared eva vanyair yadi na jīvati |
cāturvarṇyaṃ cared bhakṣaṃ khaṭvāṅgī niyataḥ pumān |
bhikṣās tv evaṃ samādāya vanaṃ gachet tataḥ punaḥ ||
vālā gopucchavartinaḥ keśāḥ | atraiva viśeṣam āha vasiṣṭhaḥ: "saptāgārāṇy asaṃkalpitāni vared bhaikṣam" iti | "alabdhopavāsaḥ" (Āp 1.24.17) ity āpastambīyo 'trāparo viśeṣaḥ | jaṭādhāraṇaṃ ca vapanād ūrdhvaṃ kāryam,
kṛtavāso vased goṣṭhe grāmānte govraje 'pi vā |
āśrame vṛkṣamūle vā sarvabhūtahite rataḥ ||
iti manuvacanāt | atra cāraṇyakuṭyasaṃbhave nivāsāntaravidhir draṣṭavyaḥ | grāmānto grāmāraṇyayoḥ saṃdhiḥ | āśramo maharṣīṇāṃ nivāsaḥ | atra ca sarvaśākhāpratyayam ekaṃ karmeti nyāyena smṛtyantaroktā aviruddhā dharmāḥ samuccayena kartavyāḥ | viruddhās tu vikalpena | atra śaṅkhaḥ |
nityaṃ triṣavaṇasnāyī kṛtvā parṇakuṭīṃ vane |
adhaḥśāyī jaṭādhārī parṇamūlaphalāśanaḥ ||
[1054]        grāmaṃ viśec ca bhikṣārthī svakarma parikīrtayan |
ekakālaṃ samaśnāno varṣe tu dvādaśe gate ||
rukmasteyī surāpaś ca brahmahā gurutalpagaḥ |
vratenaitena śudhyante mahāpātakinas tv ime ||
gautamaḥ: "khaṭvāṅgakapālapāṇir dvādaśa saṃvatsarān brahmacārī baikṣāya grāmaṃ praviśet karmācakṣāṇaḥ patho 'pakrāmet saṃdarśanād āryasya sthānāsanābhyāṃ viharan savaneṣūdakopasparśī viśudhyet" | brahmacāripadaṃ cātra varjayen madhumāṃsagandhamālyadivāsvapnāñjanābhyañjanopānacchatrakāmakrodhalobhamohaharṣa-nṛttagītaparivādabhayānīty aviruddhānāṃ brahmacāridharmāṇāṃ prāptyartham | patho 'pakrāmet saṃsarśanād āryasyeti | āryasya traivarṇikasya samyagdarśanapatho mārgād apakrāmed ity arthaḥ | vasiṣṭhaḥ: "dvādaśa varṣāṇy ātmanoddiśya na grāme nāraṇye vaset kapālapāṇiḥ khaṭvāṅgī saptāgārāṇy asaṃkalpitāni cared bhakṣaṃ bhrūṇahanaṃ bhikṣāṃ dehīti svakarmāvedyamāno vijñāya(ye)teti niruktam enaḥ kanīyo bhavati" iti | ātmanoddiśyātmanā śāstrārthaṃ nivā(nirdhā)rya prāyaścittam uddiśya saṃkalpya na grāme na vāraṇye vaset kiṃ tu tayoḥ saṃdhau | asaṃkalpitāni atra praveṣṭavyam atra neti bhikṣālobhenāsaṃpradhāritāni | "niruktam enaḥ kanīyo bhavati" | ākhyātaṃ pāpaṃ kṣīṇaṃ bhavatīty arthaḥ | yamaḥ |
atha vā brahmahatyāyāṃ vasitvā gardabhājinam |
mṛnmayena kapālena svakarmākhyāpayaṃś caret ||
goṣṭhe vaset tapoyukto grāmānte sthaṇḍile 'pi vā |
vṛkṣamūle śmaśāne vā śmaśrulomanakhī jaṭī ||
kuṭīṃ kṛtvā vaset tatra gobrāhmaṇahite rataḥ |
ambhasaḥ patanād agneś cauravyādhyādito bhayāt ||
go brāhmaṇaṃ mocayitvā brahmahatyāṃ vyapohati |
brāhmaṇāvasthān sarvān agnyāgārāṃś ca varjayet ||
śocan nindaṃs tathātmānaṃ saṃsaran brāhmaṇaṃ ca tam |
evaṃ dṛḍhavrato nityaṃ satyavādī jitendriyaḥ ||
saptāgārāṇy apūrvāṇi yāny asaṃkalpitāni ca |
saṃsmaret tāni śanakair vidhūme bhuktavajjane || [1055]
bhrūṇaghne dehi me bhikṣām eno vijñāpya saṃcaret |
ekakālaṃ cared bhaikṣam alabdhāv udakaṃ pibet ||
evaṃ saṃcaramāṇas tu brahmahatyāṃ bruvan sadā |
pūrṇe tu dvādaśe varṣe brahmahatyāṃ vyapohati ||
brāhmaṇāvasathān varjayen na praviśed dūre sthitvā bhikṣāṃ yācet devāṃś ca tadagārebhyo dūre sthitvā namaskuryāt |
brahmacārī mṛdur dānto dharmārāmaḥ śuciḥ kṣamī |
snānavrataparo nityaṃ cīravastro 'jinī jaṭī ||
kutapakṣaumaśāṇīyavalkavāsā malācitaḥ |
kṛśāṅgo lomaśaḥ śāntaḥ sukhaduḥkhavivarjitaḥ ||
madyatailakṣurasvādusaṅgavarjī jitendriyaḥ |
devāgāraguhākuñjacaityavṛkṣādikaṃ tataḥ ||
cared brahmahaṇaḥ sarvaṃ vrataṃ dvādaśavārṣikam |
samāpte tu śataṃ dadyād gavām asmin vrate śubhe ||
śatārdhaṃ vā tadardhaṃ vā daśa vā bhaikṣasaṃbhṛtāḥ |
viprāṇāṃ bhojanaṃ vastraṃ yathāśakti nivedayet ||
mṛdur akaṭhoraḥ | dāntaḥ śītādisahiṣṇuḥ | dharmārāmaḥ svaprāyaścittarataḥ | cīraṃ vastrakhaṇḍam | kutapaḥ kambalaḥ | kṣau(kṣu)mātasī, śāṇaḥ prasiddhaḥ | tayor valko valkalaṃ tvag iti yāvat | tanmayavastracīrādīni vastrāṇi vikalpyante | kuñjo gahvaram, caityavṛkṣaḥ śmaśānavṛkṣaḥ | bhaikṣasaṃbhṛtā gā bhaikṣalabdhāḥ | evamādyā viruddhadharmāḥ smṛtyantarebhyo boddhavyāḥ | viruddhās tu viṣayavyavasthayānuṣṭheyāḥ | tatra dvādaśābdikaṃ vratam akāmato brāhmaṇavadhe draṣṭavyam | etadviṣayam eva mānavaṃ vāsiṣṭhaṃ ca | ata evoktaṃ bhaviṣyatpurāṇe guhaṃ pratīśvareṇa |
pāpānāṃ mahatāṃ vīra prāthamyaṃ munibhir guha |
teṣāṃ ca brahmahatyāyāḥ prathamaṃ grahaṇaṃ kṛtam ||
tatrāpi brāhmaṇavadhe prāyaścittāni suvrata |
manunā kīrtitānīha trayodaśa yathākramam ||
vyavasthāpyāni sarvāṇi tāni vai viṣayeṇa tu |
anyathā tu laghūnāṃ hi upadeśān mahābala || [1056]
gurūṇām upadeśasya na yogyatvaṃ bhaved iha |
smṛtyantarāṇy athāloḍya(cya?) purāṇāni ca kṛtsnaśaḥ ||
evaṃ nirūpyo viṣayo guruhatyādyapekṣayā ||
tatra |
sāmānyato yadā hatvā brāhmaṇaṃ surasattama |
prāyaścittaṃ tu vai kṛtvā vidhivad dvādaśābdikam ||
prāpnoti śuddhiṃ jīvānte svargatvaṃ svajanaiḥ saha || iti |
yat punar āpastambenoktam: "araṇye kuṭīṃ kṛtvā vāgyataḥ śavaśirodhvajo 'rdhaśāṇīpakṣam adho nābhyupari jānv ācchyādya tasya panthāś cāntarā vartmaṇī(nī) dṛṣṭvā cānyam utkrāmet | khaṇḍena lohitena śarāveṇa grāmaṃ pratiṣṭheta | ko 'bhiśaptāya bhikṣāṃ dadātīti saptāgārāṇi caret sā vṛttir alabdhopavāsena vidhinā ā uttamād ucchvāsāc caren nāsyāsmiṃl loke pratyāpattir vidyate kalmaṣaṃ tu nihanyate" iti tan mātāpitṛguruprabhṛtīnāṃ vadhe draṣṭavyam | ata eva bhaviṣyatpurāṇe,
mātaraṃ pitaraṃ hatvā sodaryaṃ bhrātaraṃ tathā |
guruṃ hatvā śrotriyaṃ vā āhitāgnim athāpi vā ||
anena vidhinā pāpī kīrtayan pāpam ātmanaḥ |
pṛthivīm aṭate sarvāṃ śṛṇuṣva gadato vidhim ||
ity uktvā, "araṇye tu kuṭīṃ kṛtvā" ityādikam āpastabīyam evokam | aṅgirāḥ |
ekakālaṃ tu bhuñjānaś cared bhakṣaṃ svakarmaṇā |
kapālapāṇiḥ khaṭvāṅgī brahmacārī sadotthitaḥ ||
anasūyur adhaḥśāyī karma loke prakāśayan |
gate tu dvādaśe varṣe brahmahā pūyate naraḥ ||
yugapat kāmato vākamāto vānekabrāhmaṇavadhe viśeṣa ukto bhaviṣyatpurāṇe |
ekakālaṃ yadā hanyād yugapat suranandana |
nimittam ekam āśritya hanty anekena vā vibho || [1057]
brāhmaṇaṃ brāhmaṇau vīra brāhmaṇān subahūn api |
nihatya yugapad dīra (vīra?) ekaṃ prāṇāntakaṃ varet ||
kiṃ tv atra suraśārdūla viśeṣaṃ gadataḥ śṛṇu |
buddhipūrvābuddhipūrvaṃ viśeṣaṃ śṛṇu putraka ||
akāmato yadā hanyād brāhmaṇān mānavo guha |
cared vane tadā ghore yāvat prāṇaparikṣayaḥ ||
kāmataś ca yadā hanyād brāhmaṇān surasattama |
tadātmānaṃ dahed agnau vidhinā yena tac chṛṇu ||
bhūtvā niṣkālako vīra veṣṭayitvā ca vāsasā |
ghṛtāktena mahābāho datvā sarvasvam eva hi ||
hitvā pādau karīṣāgnau dahed ātmānam ādarāt |
ā uttamāṅgād vidhivad atrasyan surasattama ||
sarvam atra mahābāho nirdeśaḥ parikīrtitaḥ || iti |
tataś cāyam arthaḥ siddhaḥ — yady akāmato yugapad anekabrāhmaṇavadhas tatra yad dvādaśābdikaṃ tapa uktam, tad eva yāvajjīvaṃ karyam | yadi tu kāmatas tadoktaprakāreṇāgnipraveśa iti || 3.243 ||
kiṃ ca |
brāhmaṇasya paritrāṇād gavāṃ dvādaśakasya vā |
tathāśvamedhāvabhṛthasnānād vā śuddhim āpnuyāt || 3.244 ||
sarpavyāghrādinā hanyamānasya brāhmaṇasy parirakṣaṇād brahmahatyāśuddhim āpnoti | prakrāntaṃ vrataṃ kāryābhāvāt tad evotsraṣṭavyam | atha vā gavāṃ dvādaśasaṃkhyānāṃ vadhaparitrāṇāc ca śudhyati | yad vāśvamedhāvabhṛthasnānāt | aśvamedhāvabhṛte ca viśeṣam āha manuḥ |
śiṣṭvā vā bhūmidevānāṃ naradevasamāgame |
svam eno 'vabhṛte snātvā hayamedhe vimucyate ||
bhūmidevā brāhmaṇāḥ | abhiṣiktaḥ kṣatriyo naradevaḥ | teṣām anyonyasamāgame svam eno brahmahatyāṃ śiṣṭvā vikhyāpyāvabhṛthasnātaḥ śudhyatīty arthaḥ | etac ca prakrāntadvādaśābdikasyaiva | yad āha śaṅkhaḥ: "antarā vā brāhmaṇaṃ maraṇān mocayitvā gavāṃ dvādaśānaṃ paritrāṇāt sadya evāvabhṛtasnānena pūto bhavati" | antarā [1058] dvādaśābdikavratamadhya ity arthaḥ | atra ca gobrāhmaṇaparitrāṇam aśvamedhāvabhṛthasnānaṃ ca pāpakṣayahetutvena vidhīyate, na punar vratakartur maraṇam api, aśravaṇāt | prakrāntavratamadhye maraṇaṃ yadi syāt tadā tāvataiva pāpakṣayaḥ | yad āha yamaḥ |
prāyaścitte vyavasite kartā yadi vipadyate |
śuddhas tad ahar evāsāv iha loke paratra ca || iti |
aṅgirāś ca |
yo yad arthaṃ cared dharmam aprāpya mriyate tu tat |
sa tatpuṇyaphalaṃ pretya prāpnuyān manur avravīt ||
sarvadharmaviṣayaṃ caitat | yat tu manunoktam,
brāhmaṇārthe gavārthe vā samyak prāṇān parityajet |
mucyate brahmahatyāyā goptā gobrāhmaṇasya ca ||
iti, tad upakrānte dvādaśābdike draṣṭavyam | gobrāhmaṇasyāparitrāṇe 'pi trāṇārthaṃ prāṇaparityāgāc chudhyati | yat (yas) tu prāṇaparityāgaparyantaḥ(nta) prayatnaḥ sagobrāhmaṇagoptā sa jīvann apīti tasyārthaḥ || 3.244 ||
brāhmaṇasya paritrāṇād iti yad uktaṃ tad eva prapañcayann āha |
dīrghatīvrāmayagrastaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ gām athāpi vā |
dṛṣṭvā pathi nirātaṅkaṃ kṛtvā vā brahmahā śuciḥ || 3.245 ||
dīrghakālena tīvravedanenāmayena vyādhinā grastam atyantapīḍitaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ gāṃ vā pathi mārge dṛṣṭvā nirātaṅkam arogaṃ kṛtvā brahmahā śucir apāpaḥ syāt | pathi dṛṣṭvety etad anāthatāsūcanārtham | gavāṃ dvādaśakasyety uktatvād gām ity ekatvam avivakṣitam | āmayagrahaṇaṃ cātra pradarśanārtham | ata evāha yamaḥ |
ambhasaḥ patanād agneś cauravyāghrādito bhayāt |
gobrāhmaṇaṃ mocayitvā brahmahatyāṃ vyapohati || 3.245 ||
amaram api prakrāntasya pāpakṣayopāyam āha |
ānīya viprasarvasvaṃ hṛtaṃ ghātita eva vā |
tannimittaṃ kṣataḥ śastrair jīvann api viśudhyati || 3.246 ||
yo viprasvaṃ caurāpahṛtaṃ tatsvāmine dātuṃ sarvam ānayati, yo vā tannimittaṃ caurair hanyate vyāpādyate, yaś ca tadvaśāc caurair vā śastraiḥ kṣato mṛtakalpaḥ parityajyate, sa sarvo brahmahatyāyā mucyate | etac ca nirguṇaṃ brāhmaṇam amatipūrvakam akāmataś ca hatavataḥ kṣatriyasya guṇavato draṣṭavyam || 3.246 ||
[1059]
uktam akāmato brāhmaṇavadhe prāyaścittam | kāmata idānīm āha |
lomabhyaḥ svāheti hi vā lomaprabhṛti vai tanum |
majjāntaṃ juhuyād vāpi mantrair ebhir yathākramam || 3.247 ||
lomabhyaḥ svāhā, tvace svāhā, lohitāya svāhā, māṃsāya svāhā, snāyubhyaḥ svāhā, medase svāhā, asthibhyaḥ svāhā, majjābhyaḥ svāhā, ebhir mantrair yathākramaṃ vājasaneyapāṭhakramānatikrameṇa lomaprabhṛti majjāntaṃ yathā bhavati tathā taccharīraṃ juhuyāt | atra lomādīnāṃ mantrasāmarthād devatātvam, teṣām eva lomaprabhṛti vai tanuṃ juhuyād iti vacanasāmarthyād dhaviṣṭvam api | prathamo 'tra vāśabdo vāsiṣṭhaśāstrokenaiṣāṃ mantrāṇāṃ vikalpārthaḥ | dvitīyas tu dvādaśavārṣikeṇa tatra homasya | atraiva mānavo viśeṣaḥ |
prāsyed ātmānam agnau vā samiddhe trir avākśirāḥ | iti |
kṛtopavāsenaitat kāryam | yad āha gautamaḥ: "prāyaścittam agnau saktir brahmaghnas triravacchātasya" iti | avacchāto bubhukṣitaḥ | tathā ca kāṭhakaśrutiḥ: "anaśanena karṣi(śi)to 'gnim ārohet" iti | idaṃ ca kāmakāraviṣayam, maranāntikatvāt | tathā ca aṅgirāḥ |
prāṇāntikaṃ ca yat proktaṃ prāyaścittaṃ manīṣibhiḥ |
tat kāmakāraviṣayam etan nāsty atra saṃśayaḥ ||
tathā |
yaḥ kāmato mahāpāpaṃ naraḥ kuryāt kathaṃcana |
na tasya śuddhir nirdiṣṭā bhṛgvagnipatanād ṛte || iti |
maraṇāntikaṃ ca prāyaścittaṃ kṣatriyasya | tathā ca bhaviṣyatpurāṇam |
hatvā tu kṣatriyo vipraṃ guṇādhyam api kāmataḥ |
prāyaścittaṃ cared dhīro vidhivat tv ātmaśuddhaye ||
lakṣyaṃ śastrabhṛtāṃ vā syād viduṣām icchayātmanaḥ |
prāsyed ātmānam agnau vā prasiddhe trir avākśirāḥ ||
yajeta vāśvamedhena kṣatriyo vipraghātakaḥ |
prāyaścittatrayaṃ hy etat kṣatriyasya prakīrtitam || iti |
viprasya tu kāmato brahmavadhe dviguṇaṃ vratam,
vihitaṃ yad akāmānāṃ kāmāt tu dviguṇaṃ bhavet |
iti devalasmaraṇāt || 3.247 ||
[1060]
aparam api maraṇāntikaṃ prāyaścittam āha |
saṃgrāme va hato lakṣyabhūtaḥ śuddhim avāpnuyāt |
mṛtakalpaḥ prahārārto jīvann api viśudhyati || 3.248 ||
atha vā rājñām anyonyasaṃgrāme senādvayapreritabāṇasāpātadeśe lakṣyabhūto hataḥ paralokagato mṛtakalpo vā prahārapīḍito jīvann api viśūdhyati | manuḥ |
lakṣyaṃ śastrabhṛtāṃ vā syād viduṣām icchayātmanaḥ |
viduṣāṃ prāyaścittopadeśakartṝṇām icchayā tadabhāve svecchayā yudhyamānānāṃ lakṣyaṃ bhaved ity arthaḥ | asya ca viṣayaḥ pūrvam eva vyavasthāpitaḥ || 3.248 ||
araṇye niyato japtvā trir vai vedasya saṃhitām |
śudhyaty atha mitāśī vā pratisrotaḥ sarasvatīm || 3.249 ||
araṇye vane niyato niyatāhāraḥ | ata evāha manuḥ |
japed vā niyatāhāras trir vai vedasya saṃhitām | iti |
āhārasya niyatatvaṃ haviṣyatvam alpatvaṃ vā | vedasya saṃhitāṃ trir japitvā śudhyati | atha vā pūrvavan mitāśī bhūtvā sarasvatīṃ pratisroto 'ntād ārabhyodgamaṃ yāvad api gatvā śudhyati | manuḥ |
haviṣyabhug vānusaret pratisrotaḥ sarasvatīm |
sumantunoktam atha vā prāyaścittaṃ nibodha me ||
atra bhaviṣyatpurāṇam |
hantā yadā vedahīno dhanena ca bhaved guha |
tad etat kalpayet tasya prāyaścittaṃ nibodha me ||
brahmahā saṃvatsaraṃ kṛcchraṃ cared adhaḥśāyī triṣavaṇo bhaikṣāhāro divyanadīpulinasaṃgamāśramagoṣṭhaparvataprasravaṇatapovanavihārī syāt | sthānavīrāsanī saṃvatsare pūrṇe hiraṇyamaṇigovṛṣānnatilabhūsarpīṃṣi brāhmaṇebhyo dadyāt pūto bhavati | aśvamedhāvabhṛthasnānena vā || 3.249 ||
api ca |
pātre dhanaṃ vā paryāptaṃ datvā śuddhim avāpnuyāt |
ādātuś ca viśuddhyartham iṣṭir vaiśvānarī smṛtā || 3.250 ||
[1061]
atha vā vidyātapovratavate kulīnāya yāvajjīvaṃ nirvāhakṣamaṃ dhanaṃ datvā śuddhim āpnoti | yac caitad dhanaṃ pratigṛhṇāti tenātmaśuddhaye vaiśvānarīṣṭiḥ kāryā | manuḥ |
sarvasvaṃ vā vedavide brāhmaṇāyopapādayet |
dhanaṃ vā jīvanāyālaṃ gṛhaṃ vā saparicchadam ||
jīvanaparyāptadhanadānāsamartho gṛhaṃ saparicchadam, tadasamarthaḥ sarvaṃ dadyāt | paricchado gṛhopakaraṇajātam | atra aṅgirāḥ |
sāśītipaṇasāhasrād ūrdhvaṃ sarvasvam ucyate |
nirdhano brahmahā mucyet sukhādhikyān nareśvaraḥ ||
bhaviṣyatpurāṇe brāhmaṇasyāpy etatd bhavatīty uktam |
jātimātraṃ yadā hanyād brāhmaṇo brāhmaṇaṃ guha |
vedābhyāsavihīno vai dhanavān agnivarjitaḥ ||
prāyaścittaṃ tadā kuryād idaṃ pāpaviśuddhaye |
dhanaṃ vā jīvanāyālaṃ gṛhaṃ vā saparicchadam ||
yad vā parāśaroktena prāyaścittena śudhyati ||
tad yathā |
cāturvidyopapanne tu vidhane brahmaghātake |
samudrasetugamanaṃ prāyaścittaṃ vinirdiśet ||
setubandhaṃ tu saṃpaśyel laṅkāmārgaṃ mahodadheḥ |
daśayojanavistīrṇaṃ śatayojanam āyatam ||
setubandhapathe bhikṣāṃ cāturvarṇyāt samāharet |
varjayitvā vikarmasthāṃś chatropānadvivarjitaḥ ||
ahaṃ duṣkṛtakarmā ca mahāpātakakārakaḥ |
veśmadvāreṣu tiṣṭhāmi bhikṣārthī brahmaghātakaḥ ||
gokuleṣu ca goṣṭheṣu grāmeṣu nagareṣu ca |
tapovaneṣu tīrtheṣu nadīprasravaṇeṣu ca ||
eteṣu khyāpayann enaḥ puṇyaṃ gatvā tu sāgaram |
brahmahā vipramucyeta snātvā tatra mahodadhau ||
tataḥ pūto gṛhaṃ prāpya kṛtvā brāhmaṇabhojanam |
japtvā ca supavitrāṇi pūtātmā praviśed gṛham ||
gavāṃ vāpi śataṃ dadyāc cāturvidyāya dakṣiṇām |
evaṃ śuddhim avāpnoti cāturvidyānumoditaḥ || [1062]
vindhyād uttarato yasya nivāsaḥ parikīrtitaḥ |
parāśaramataṃ tasya setubandhasya darśanam ||
rāmabhadrasamādeśānnāsaṃcaysaṃcitam |
setuṃ dṛṣṭvā mahāpuṇyaṃ brahmahā parimucyate || iti |
bhaviṣyatpurāṇe |
jātimātre hate vipre hantur vai nirdhanasya ca |
prāyaścittaṃ tv āṅgirasaṃ traivārṣikam udāhṛtam ||
tad yathā |
ata ūrdhvaṃ pravakṣyāmi kṛcchraṃ śakrādipūjitam |
yady adhītaṃ śrutaṃ vāpi pāpān mocayate naram ||
yuktas triṣavaṇasnāyī snāyān maunavrate sthitaḥ |
prātaḥ snātvā samārambhaṃ kuryāj japyasya yatnataḥ ||
sāvitrīṃ vyāhṛtīs tisro japed aṣṭasahasrakṛt |
oṃkāram āditaḥ kṛtvā rūpe rūpe tathāntataḥ ||
śalyaviddha ivāsīnaḥ pibed gavyaṃ payaḥ śakṛt |
gavyasya payaso 'lābhe gavyam evaṃ paraṃ dadhi ||
tadabhāve pibet takraṃ takrābhāve tu yāvakam |
eṣām anyatamaṃ yad yad upapadyeta tad bhavet ||
gomūtreṇa samāyuktaṃ yāvakaṃ vāpi yojayet |
kṛcchram aikāntikaṃ cedaṃ dṛṣṭam āṅgirasā svayam ||
sarvapāpaharaṃ divyaṃ nāmnā vajra iti smṛtam |
kālasya tu yathoktasya brāhmaṇa eva kāraṇam || iti || 3.250 ||
brahmahatyāvratādeśam āha |
yāgasthakṣatraviḍḍhantā cared brahmahaṇi vratam |
garbhahā ca yathāvarṇaṃ tathātreyīniṣūdakaḥ || 3.251 ||
yāgasthayor yāge 'dhikārapūrvakakartṛtvena sthitayoḥ kṣatriyavaiśyayor hantā yad brahmaṇi nānāprakāraṃ vrataṃ vihitaṃ tac caret | yāgo 'tra somayāgo vivakṣitaḥ, "tat kṣatraṃ nidhāya svāny āyudhāni brāhmaṇa evāyudhair brāhmaṇo rūpeṇa brahmabhūtvā yajñam upāvartate" iti śrutivākyena kṣatriyasya yajamānasya somayāgaṃ prati brāhmaṇatvam ity abhidhānāt | evaṃ ca dīkṣaṇīyāyā ūrdhvaṃ prāg avabhṛthāntād vartamānayoḥ [1063] kṣatriyaviśor hantur vratam etad atidiśyata iti mantavyam | yad api ca vasiṣṭhenoktam "brāhmaṇīṃ cātreyīṃ striyaṃ hatvā bhrūṇahatyāprāyaścittam, savanagatau rājanyavaiśyau" iti, tatrāpi kṛtadīkṣaṇīyāv eva savanagāv iti vivakṣitam | yaś ca yasya varṇasya garbham ātreyī vahati, sa tadvarṇavadhaprāyaścittaṃ kuryāt | manus tu nimittāntareṣv api brahmahatyāvratātideśam āha |
hatvā garbham avijñātam etad eva vrataṃ caret |
rajanyavaiśyāv ījānāv ātreyīm eva ca striyam ||
uktvā caivānṛtaṃ sākṣye pratirabhya guruṃ tathā |
apahṛtya ca nikṣepaṃ kṛtvā ca strīsuhṛdvadham || iti |
garbham avijñātaṃ strīpuruṣabhāvenety arthaḥ | garbhaś cāyaṃ brāhmaṇasyaiva | tathā ca āpastambaḥ: "garbhaṃ ca tasyāvijñātam" iti | tasya brāhmaṇasyety arthaḥ | vijñāte tu garbhe yathāliṅgaṃ prāyaścittam | strīliṅge garbhe hata upapātakaprāyaścittam | itaratra tu brahmahaṇo vratam | etad eva vratam ity anantaroktaṃ nānāprakāraṃ brahmahavrataṃ parāmṛśati, na tu dvādaśābdikam eva viśeṣahetvabhāvāt |  yat tv āpastambena brāhmaṇasya garbham ātreyīṃ ca ghnato dvādaśābdikam uktam, tat pradarśanārtham, na niyamārtham | tathā hi sati sakṛdabhyāsena pratyayāpratyayānubandhatāratamyādikṛte hanane dvādaśavārṣikam eva vrataṃ kāryam iti viṣamasamīkaraṇam anyāyyam āpadyeta | vratagrahaṇāc ca prāyaścittātideśaḥ | tenātidiṣṭaprāyaścitto 'patitatvād dvijātikarmabhir (sv) adhikriyata eva | pratirambhaḥ prātikūlyam | kṛtvā tu strīsuhṛdvadham ity atra na strīmātraṃ vivakṣitam, ātreyīgrahaṇasyānarthakyaprasaṅgāt | ata eva tadviśeṣam āha aṅgirāḥ |
āhitāgner dvijāgryasya hatvā patnīm aninditām |
brahmahatyāvrataṃ kuryād ātreyīghnas tathaiva ca ||
parāśaras tv anyaṃ viśeṣam āha |
savasthāṃ striyaṃ hatvā cared brahmahaṇi vratam | iti |
viṣṇuḥ: "etan mahāvrataṃ brāhmaṇaṃ hatvā dvādaśa saṃvatsarān kuryād atrigotrāṃ vā nārīṃ vā mitraṃ vā" | anena cātrigotraivātreyītyuktaṃ bhavati | vasiṣṭhaḥ punar anyad āha (thaiva): "rajasvalām ṛtusnātām ātreyīm āhuḥ" iti vadan rajodarśanaprabhṛti ṣoḍaśāhorātrāṇi yāvad ātreyī bhavatīti gamayati | atra hy etad apatyaṃ [1064] bhavatīti vyutpattipradarśanaṃ vandhyāvyudāsārtham | anyathānarthakaṃ syād iti kecit | vyutpattimātraparam etad ity apare | anātreyīvadhe vratāntarātideśam āha sa eva: "anātreyīṃ brāhmaṇīm hatvā rājanyahiṃsāyāṃ rājanyāṃ vaiśyahiṃsāyāṃ vaiśyāṃ śūdrahiṃsāyāṃ śūdrāṃ hatvā saṃvatsaram" iti | ayam arthaḥ — anātreyīṃ brāhmaṇīṃ hatvā saṃvatsaraṃ rājanyahiṃsāyāṃ yad vratam uktaṃ tat kuryād iti | evam uttaratrāpi | śūdrāṃ hatvā saṃvatsaraṃ prāyaścittaṃ kāryam | yat tu strīgarbhasyopapātakatvavacanaṃ tadabhivyaktastrītvasya garbhasya vadhe draṣṭavyam | viṣṇuḥ: "samutkarṣānṛte guroś cālīkanirbandhe tadākṣāraṇe māsaṃ payasā vartate" | alpāparādhaviṣayam etat | mahāparādhe brahmahatyāprāyaścittam eva | ata eva bhagavān yājñavalkyo brahmahatyādisamatvenābhitānāṃ gurvadhikṣepādīnāṃ pṛthak prāyaścittaṃ noktavān || 3.251 ||
kiṃ ca |
cared vratam ahatvāpi ghātārthaṃ cet samāgataḥ |
yadi brahmavadhe pravṛtto vighnavaśān na hanti, na tu śāstrabalāt tadāpi brahmahatyāprāyaścittaṃ caret | na ca vadhaṃ kṛtavato 'kṛtavataś caikam eva vrataṃ yuktam | tenāghnataḥ pādonaṃ kalpyam | evaṃ kṣatriyādivadhe 'pi | yat tu gautamenoktam: "spṛṣṭaś ced brāhmaṇavadhe 'hatvāpi" iti, tatra brāhmaṇagrahaṇaṃ pradarśanārtham | āpastambaḥ: "dharmārthasaṃnipāte 'rthagrāhiṇa etad eva prāyaścittam" | brahmahavratam evety arthaḥ |
kiṃ ca |
dviguṇaṃ savanasthe ca brāhmaṇe vratam ācaret || 3.252 ||
somayāgāya kṛtadīkṣasya brāhmaṇasya vadhe brahmahatyāvrataṃ dviguṇam ācaret | evaṃ tadvadhe pravṛttasyāghnato 'pi dviguṇam eva pādonam | evaṃ tāvad bhagavatā yājñavalkyena kānicid brahmavadhavratāny uktāni | anyāny api kānicin maharṣaya āhuḥ | tatra jamadagniḥ |
yad vā vārāṇasīṃ gacchet setuṃ śrīparvatādi vā |
anyāny api ca tīrthāni gāyatrīṃ ca japet tathā || [1065]
atriḥ |
brahmahābdatrayaṃ vipro gāyatrīm abhyaset tadā |
prāṇāyāmaśataṃ kuryāt pratyahaṃ niyataḥ śuciḥ ||
etad eva vrataṃ stenaḥ pādanyūnaṃ samācaret |
gatvaitad eva kurvīta gurutalpam akāmataḥ ||
brahmahatyādisameṣv etat prayojakakartṛṣu vālpatvāt
ṣaḍbhir varṣaiḥ kṛcchracārī brahmahā pūyate naraḥ |
māse māse parākeṇa tribhir varṣair vyapohati ||
saṃvatsareṇa māsāśī pūyate nātra saṃśayaḥ |
gavāṃ sahasraṃ vidhivat pātrebhyaḥ pratipādayet ||
brahmahā vipramucyeta sarvapāpebhya eva ca ||
māse vyatīte 'śnātīti māsāśī | devalaḥ: "tīrtheṣu puṇyatameṣu yathāvad dehasaṃnyāsād brāhmaṇo mahāpātakāt pramucyate | dharmayuddhe gograhaṇagrāmaghātādiṣu prāṇatyāgāt pūyate kṣatriyaḥ" | viṣṇuḥ |
aśvamedhena śudhyeyur mahāpātakinas tv iha |
pṛthivyāṃ sarvatīrthānāṃ tathānusaraṇena vā ||
mahāpātakānuvṛttau kaśyapaḥ |
vatsaratritayaṃ kuryān naraḥ kṛcchraṃ viśuddhaye |
ātmatulyaṃ suvarṇaṃ vai dadyād vā vipratuṣṭidam ||
bhaviṣyatpurāṇam |
matipūrvaṃ yadā hanyād brāhmaṇaṃ jātimātrakam |
gosahasraṃ tadā dadyād atha vā svarjitā yajet ||
aṅgirāḥ |
pariṣad yā brāhmaṇānāṃ sā rājñāṃ dviguṇā bhavet |
vaiśyānāṃ triguṇā caiva parṣad yac ca vrataṃ smṛtam ||
atra bhaviṣyatpurāṇam |
kṣatriyo brāhmaṇaṃ hatvā prāyaścittadvayaṃ caret |
yajeta vāśvamedhena vaiśye taddviguṇaṃ smṛtam ||
prāyaścitadvayaṃ dvādaśavārṣikam ity arthaḥ | patitasya vaitānāgnityāgo nāstīty aśvamedhādikratuvidhānād gamyate | [1066] ata eva kātyāyanaḥ |
mahāpātakasaṃyukto daivāt syād agnimān yadi |
putrādiḥ pālayed agnīn yukta ā doṣasaṃkṣayāt ||
prāyaścittaṃ na kuryāc cet kurvan vā mriyate yadi |
gṛhaṃ nirvāpayet sarvam udasyet saparicchadam ||
śāmayed ubhayaṃ vādbhir adbhyo 'gnir abhavad yataḥ |
pātrāṇi dadyād viprāya dahed apsv atha vā kṣipet ||
ā doṣasaṃkṣayād ā prāyaścittāpavargād ity arthaḥ | evaṃ ca sati,
vaitānaṃ prakṣiped apsu yajñapātrāṇi nirdahet |
ityādikaṃ vacanaṃ prāyaścittam akurvāṇe draṣṭavyam | bhaviṣyatpurāṇam |
prāyaścittatrayaṃ vaiśyo hatvā vipraṃ surādhipa |
catvāri dvādaśābdāni bhikṣāśī vanagocaraḥ ||
japann anyatamaṃ vedaṃ yojanānāṃ śataṃ vrajet |
