brahmahatyāpanodāya mitabhuṅ niyatendriyaḥ ||
kṛtavāpano vā nivased grāmānte govraje 'pi vā |
āśrame vṛkṣamūle vā gobrāhmaṇahite rataḥ ||
śūdro vā brāhmaṇaṃ hatvā prāyaścittacatuṣṭayam |
kuryād ātmaviśuddhyarthaṃ yathāvac chṛṇu suvrata ||
datvā vipre vedavidi sarvasvaṃ prathamaṃ guha |
haviṣyabhuk caret paścāt pratisrotaḥ sarasvatīm ||
setubandhaṃ tato gacched bhikṣāśī kṛtavāpanaḥ |
gavārthe brāḥmaṇārthe vā samyak prāṇān parityajet ||
śaṅkhaḥ: "akāmakṛte brahmahā pariṣado 'numate khaṭvāṅgī gardabhājinī mṛnmayapātrapatitaśiṣṭānnabhojī kapālacihnadhvajī svakarma khyāpayaṃś cared bhaikṣam ekakālāhāraḥ śūnyāgāraparvataguhānadīvṛkṣamūlaniketanaḥ | sa evaṃ gosahasraṃ datvā dvādaśavarśe śuddhim āpnoti | antarā vā brāhmaṇaṃ maraṇān mocayitvā gavāṃ dvādaśānāṃ paritrāṇāt sadya evāśvamedhāvabhṛte snātvā pūto bhavati" | [1067] atha bhaviṣyatpurāṇe viṣaya uktaḥ | tad yathā |
śaṅkhenoktaṃ tu yad vīra prāyaścittaṃ mahātmanā |
tad yajñagataviprasya vināśe parikīrtitam || iti |
vasiṣṭhaḥ: "athāparaṃ bhrūṇahatyāyā dvādaśarātram abbhakṣo dvādaśarātram evopavaset" | asyāpi viṣayo bhaviṣyatpurāṇa evoktaḥ |
yadātyarthaṃ guṇair hīno gāyatryāpi vivarjitaḥ |
nihataḥ kamataś caiva tadā syāt tan nibodha me ||
hantā dānāsamarthaś ca tadaivaṃ parikalpayet |
vasiṣṭhena samākhyātaṃ brahmahatyāvyapohanam ||
dvādaśarātram abbhakṣo dvādaśarāram upavaset || iti |
śaṅkhalikhitau: "patitaśiṣṭānnabhojī dvādaśa varṣāṇi gavāṃ sahasraṃ datvā dvādaśe varṣe śuddhim āpnoti" | patitaśiṣṭaḥ patitavyatiriktaḥ | etad api savanasthabrāhmaṇavadhaviṣayam | bṛhaspatiḥ |
gaṅgāyamunayor vāpi saṃgame lokaviśrute |
śudhyet triṣavaṇasnāyī trirātropoṣito dvijaḥ ||
evamādīni gurūṇi laghūni ca prāyaścittāni santi teṣāṃ deśakālasāmarthyavayojātiguṇādyapekṣayā viṣayavyavasthā kalpanīyā | anyathā gurukalpopadeśo 'narthakaḥ syāt | bhaviṣyatpurāṇe |
guha uvāva —
yam uddiśya dvijaṃ hanyād brāhmaṇaḥ svayam eva hi |
ātmānaṃ sahasā krodhāt tasya kiṃ nu bhaved vibho ||
īśvara uvāca —
keśaśmaśrunakhādīnāṃ kṛtvā vai vapanaṃ guha |
brahmacaryaṃ cared vīra varṣeṇaikena tadvrataḥ ||
sasaṃbandhaṃ yadā vipro hatvātmānaṃ nṛpo guha |
nirguṇaḥ sahasā krodhād gṛhakṣetrādito vibho ||
traivārṣikaṃ vrataṃ kuryād brahmahatyāṃ caran vane |
evaṃ śuddhim avāpnoti hatvātmānaṃ mṛto yadi ||
[1068]        tiraskṛto yadā vipro nirguṇo mriyate 'nagha |
sanimittaṃ tathā putra tad eva suviśuddhaye ||
traivārṣikaṃ brahmacaryaṃ kṛtvā śudhyeta viprahā |
pratilomaṃ ca gacched vā niyatāśī sarasvatīm ||
atyarthaṃ nirguṇo vipro hy atyarthaṃ guṇinopari (?) |
saṃbandhena vinā putra mriyate paribhartsitaḥ ||
prāyaścittam idaṃ kuryāt tadā papaviśuddhaye |
vatsaratritayaṃ kuryān naraḥ kṛcchraṃ viśuddhaye ||
ātmatulyaṃ suvarṇaṃ vā dadyād vai vipratuṣṭidam |
vācoktvā cārpite śastre hatvā vipraḥ kṣayaṃgataḥ ||
ya evaṃ mriyamāṇaṃ tu brāhmaṇaṃ samupekṣate |
tasya te niṣkṛtiṃ vacmi nirguṇasya viśeṣataḥ ||
saśikhaṃ vapanaṃ kṛtvā chāto 'bhyaktas tathā guha |
ātmānaṃ nirdahed agnāv āpādatalamastakam ||
chāto 'naśanaḥ |
yady ekaṃ bahavo viprā ghnanti vipram anāgasam |
tadaiṣāṃ niṣkṛtiṃ vacmi śṛṇuṣvaikamanā guha ||
teṣāṃ yasya prahāreṇa sa vipro nidhanaṃ gataḥ |
sarasvatīṃ pratisrotaḥ sa caret pāpaśuddhaye ||
anye kuryur idaṃ putra prāyaścittaṃ viśuddhaye |
kuryuḥ kṛcchraṃ dviṣaṇmāsāṃś cāturvidyapate guha ||
siṃhādayas tathā sarpā rājā viprakadambakam |
naite doṣeṇa lipyante ghnanto 'pi sakalaṃ jagat ||
gurulaghutāṃ prati yadātmatuṣṭir na jāyate |
pratipūraṇaṃ vai tatra japādibhir udāhṛtam ||
japādīnāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ dharmaśāstreṣu sarvaśaḥ |
prāyaścittaviśeṣeṇa sarveṣām idam ucyate ||
śodhanaṃ devaśārdūla japahomādikaṃ mahat |
japas tapas tathā homa upavāso 'ghamarṣaṇam || [1069]
jñānājñānakṛtānāṃ ca pāpānāṃ śodhanaṃ param |
anyaśāstroditānāṃ ca niṣkṛtīnāṃ surādhipa ||
utprekṣyo viṣayo hy evaṃ satataṃ gurulāghave || 3.252 ||
iti brahmahatyāprāyaścittaprakaraṇam ||
uddeśakramaprāptāni surāpānaprāyaścittāny āha |
surāṃbughṛtagomūtrapayasām agnisaṃnibham |
surāpo 'nyatamaṃ pītvā maraṇāc chuddhim āpnuyāt || 3.253 ||
surādīnām anyatamam ekaṃ saṃtāpanād agnisaṃnibham uṣṇatvenāgnitulyam, yasmin pīte pātur mṛtyur bhavati, tathāvidhaṃ surāpaḥ pītvā maraṇāc chuddho bhavati | matipūrvakasurāpānaviṣayam etat | tathā ca manuḥ |
matipūrvam anirdeśyaṃ prāṇāntikam iti sthitiḥ | iti |
matipūrvam iti kāmapūrvam | anirdeśyam iti bruvan prāṇāntikaprāyaścittaṃ parṣadānuktaṃ svayam eva śāstrato viditvā kuryād iti gamayati | atra bhaviṣyapurāṇam |
gauḍī paiṣṭī ca mādhvī ca vijñeyā trividhā surā |
yathaivaikā tathā sarvā na pātavyā dvijottamaiḥ ||
surā hi paiṣṭī mukhyoktā na tasyās tv itare same ||
netarayor mukhyaḥ surāśabda ity arthaḥ | atra ca dvijottamagrahaṇaṃ vivakṣitam, tena kṣatriyavaiśyayoḥ paiṣṭy eva pratiṣiddhā netare, madhyāntarāṇy api brāhmaṇasyaiva pratiṣiddhāni netarayoḥ | yad āha manuḥ |
yakṣarakṣaḥpiśācānnaṃ madyaṃ māṃsaṃ surāsavam |
tad brāhmaṇena nāttavyaṃ devānām aśnatāṃ haviḥ || iti |
tataś copanayanāt prāg api dvijātibhiḥ surā varjyā, brāhmaṇena tu gauḍīmādhvyādimadyam api | rājanyavaiśyābhyāṃ tu brahmacāribhyām eva | yat tu gautamenoktam "prāg upanayanāt kāmacāravādabhakṣaḥ" iti, tad upaviṣṭenācamya bhakṣaṇīyam ityādiniyamanivṛttyartham, na punar abhakṣyabhakṣaṇānugrahārtham | ata eva jātūkarṇyaḥ |
anupetas tu yo bālo madyaṃ mohāt pibed yadi |
tasya kṛcchratrayaṃ kuryān mātā bhrātā tathā pitā || iti | [1070]
surāpāne tu ṣaṭ kṛcchrāḥ kalpyā iti | madyapānāt surāpānaprāyaścittasya dvaiguṇyadarśanāt | etac ca pañcamād ūrdhvam, pūrvaṃ tu doṣābhāvaḥ | yad āha kumāraḥ |
madyamūtrapurīṣāṇāṃ bhakṣaṇe nasti kaścana |
doṣas tv ā pañcamād varṣād ūrdhvaṃ pitroḥ suhṛd guroḥ || iti |
evaṃ ca pañcamavarṣaprabhṛty eva dvijānāṃ surāpānapratiṣedho veditavyaḥ | bālasya ca kāmato 'pi na maraṇāntikam | yato maraṇāntikaṃ prāyaścittaṃ na parṣadādeśyam | kiṃ tu svayam eva viditvā kāryam | tatra bālo 'pi vyutpannaś cet tarhi maraṇāntikam eva prāyaścittam | tadviṣayāṇi cānugrahavacanāni | yad āha viṣṇuḥ |
aśītir yasya varṣāṇi bālo vāpy ūnaṣoḍaśaḥ |
prāyaścittārdham arhanti striyo rogiṇa eva ca || iti |
pulastyaḥ |
strīṇām ardhaṃ pradātavyaṃ vṛddhānāṃ rogiṇāṃ tathā |
pādo bāleṣu dātavyaḥ sarvapāpeṣv ayaṃ vidhiḥ ||
tathā |
asaṃskṛto nirutsāho rogī navatijīvakaḥ |
yathāśakti prayuñjīta vrataṃ hy eṣu na lupyate ||
aṅgirāḥ |
ūnaikādaśavarṣasya pañcavarṣāt parasya ca |
cared guruḥ suhṛd vāpi prāyaścittaṃ viśuddhaye ||
na ca maraṇāntikasyārdhapādakalpanā yujyate | madyaṃ punar gauḍīmādhvībhyām apy anyad asti, tadbhedād āha bṛhadviṣṇuḥ |
mādhvīkam aikṣavaṃ mairaṃ tālaṃ khārjurapānasam |
madhūtthaṃ caiva mārdvīkaṃ (?) maireyaṃ nārikelajam ||
amedhyāni daśaitāni madyāni brāhmaṇasya tu ||
agastyaḥ punar ekādaśavidhaṃ tad āha |
pānasaṃ drākṣamādhūkaṃ khārjūraṃ tālam aikṣavam |
madhūtthaṃ maireyam āriṣṭaṃ maireyaṃ nārikelajam ||
samānāni vijānīyān madyāny ekādaśaiva tu |
dvādaśaṃ tu surāmadyaṃ sarveṣām adhamaṃ smṛtam ||
[yata eva vyatiriktam anekavidhaṃ madyam, surātmakaṃ tu tadantyaṃ tannikṛṣṭam] ata evāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: [1071] "madyapāne tv asurāyāḥ surāyāś cājñāne kṛcchrātikṛcchrau ghṛtaprāśaḥ punaḥsaṃskāraś ca" | idam atyantāśaktasya bālatarasyāvidyamānapitrādikasya pratiṣiddham etat pānam ity aviduṣo haṭhātkāritapānasya draṣṭavyam | bhaviṣyatpurāṇe |
paiṣṭīpāne tu naitāsāṃ prāyaścittaṃ nibodha me |
manunoktaṃ mahābāho samāsavyāsayogataḥ ||
etāsām iti surāṇām ity arthaḥ | yamaḥ |
surāṃ pītvā dvijo mohād agnivarṇāṃ surāṃ pibet |
tathāsya kāye nirdagdhe mucyate kilbiṣāt tataḥ ||
gomūtram agnivarṇaṃ vā pibed udakam eva vā |
payo ghṛtaṃ suvarṇaṃ vā gośakṛdrasam eva vā ||
asakṛjjñānataḥ pītvā vāruṇīṃ patati dvijaḥ |
maraṇaṃ pūrvanirdiṣṭaṃ prāyaścittaṃ vidhīyate ||
āyase bhājane taptāṃ brāhmaṇo vāruṇīṃ pibet |
agnivarṇāṃ pāpaharāṃ tataḥ pāpād vimucyate ||
jīva(vi)tasyāntakāle tu śmaśāne vihito vidhiḥ ||
gauḍīmādhvīviṣayam etat | uśanā: "govālacīravāsāḥ surāpaḥ surām agnivarṇāṃ pītvā pūto bhavati" | bṛhaspatiḥ |
surāpāne kāmakṛte jvalantī iva tāṃ mukhe |
kṣiptvā tayā sa nirdagdho mṛtaḥ śuddhim avāpnuyāt ||
kuryād vānaśanaṃ tāvad yāvat prāṇair viyujyate ||
devalaḥ: "surāpāne brāhmaṇo rūpyatāmratrapusīsānām anyatamam agnikalpaṃ pītvā śarīraparityāgāt pūyate" | hārītaḥ: "surāpo 'gnivarṇāṃ surāṃ pītvā ghṛtam apaḥ payo vā hiraṇyaṃ vā vilāpya mṛtynā pūto bhavati" | surāpānaṃ sakṛt kṛtvety anuvṛttāv aṅgirāḥ |
bhṛguprapāte patanaṃ jvalanaṃ vā samāviśet |
mahāprasthānam ātiṣṭhen mucyate sarvakilbiṣaiḥ || 3.253 ||
prāyaścitāntaram āha | [1072]
vālavāsā jṭī vāpi cared brahmahaṇi vratam |
piṇyākaṃ vā kaṇān vāpi bhakṣayet tu samāṃ niśi || 3.254 ||
vālādhiṣṭhāḥ keśās tanmayaṃ vasanaṃ jaṭāś ca bibhrāṇo brahmahaṇi yad uktaṃ dvādaśābdikaṃ vrataṃ tad vā caret | atra ca vālavāsā ityādigrahaṇaṃ dvādaśābdikabrahmahavrataprāptiniyamārtham | anyathā vratāntaram api tadīyaṃ syāt | yadi tu vālavasanajaṭitvarūpadharmadvayaṃ niyamārtham idaṃ syān na tarhi manur atra dhvajitvagrahaṇaṃ kuryāt | kṛtaṃ ca tat tena |
kaṇān vā bhakṣayed abdaṃ piṇyākaṃ vā sakṛn niśi |
surāpānāpanuttyarthaṃ vālavāsā jaṭī dhvajī || iti |
piṇyākam uddhṛtasnehaṃ tilakalkam, kaṇāṃs taṇḍulān vā sakṛd rātrau vatsaraṃ yāvad aśnīyāt | kecid atra samāśabdaṃ bahuvacanāntaṃ varṣatrayaparatvena vyācakṣate, teṣāṃ kaṇān vā bhakṣayed abdam iti manuvākyavirodhaḥ | asmād eva ca surāpānāpanuttyartham iti vacanāt kṛtasurāpānasyaitad vratam iti gamyate, na punas tālumātrasaṃyuktasurasyeti | tādṛśasaṃyogaphalavyāpāre pānaśabdāprayogāt | brahmavratam atidiṣṭam api dvādaśābdikam eva surāpaḥ kuryāt | yad āha vṛddhahārītaḥ: "dvādaśabhir varṣair mahāpātakinaḥ pūyante" iti | piṇyākādibhakṣaṇaṃ ca tapastvād bhojanāntaranivartakam | brahmahatyāvratādeśaś cākāmataḥ sakṛt pītāyāṃ samanantaram eva pracchandi(rdi)tāyāṃ veditavyaḥ | ata eva brahmahatyānuvṛttau vyāsaḥ |
etad eva vrataṃ kuryān madyapacchardane kṛte |
pañcagavyaṃ tu tasyoktaṃ pratyahaṃ kāyaśodhanam || iti |
piṇyākādibhakṣaṇena ca saṃvyavahāryataiva bhavati na punaḥ pāpakṣayaḥ | ata eva hārītaḥ: "brāhmaṇānumatād vā saṃvatsaraṃ gomūtrapurīṣabhakṣo gā anugacchan punaḥ saṃskṛtaḥ saṃvyavahāryo bhavati" | yad vā dvādaśavārṣikāsamarthaṃ prati piṇyākādibhakṣaṇaṃ pāpakṣayakaram api bhavatīti surāpānāpanuttyartham ity aviśeṣavacanāc chakyaṃ kalpayitum | surāpānaṃ sakṛt kṛvety anuvṛttāv aṅgirāḥ | [1073]
bṛhaspatisaveneṣṭvā surāpo brāhmaṇaḥ punaḥ |
samatvaṃ brahmaṇo gacched ity eṣā vaidikī śrutiḥ ||
bhūmipradānaṃ yaḥ kuryāt surāṃ pītvā dvijottamaḥ |
punar na ca pibej jātu saṃskṛtaḥ sa viśudhyati ||
saṃskṛta upanītaḥ | viṣṇuḥ |
surāpāne hi vijñānāt pramādāt saṅgato 'pi vā |
śaktyā kṛcchradvayaṃ kāryaṃ vatsaraṃ saṃmitaṃ tathā ||
prathame taptakṛcchraṃ tu punaḥsaṃskārakarma ca |
dvitīyaṃ janma tasya syāt saṃmitaṃ tadanantaram ||
prājāpatyaṃ tataḥ kāryaṃ parākas tu samāpane |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ trir abhyasya tato mucyeta kilbiṣāt ||
śaktyā hiraṇyaṃ rajataṃ gāś ca bhūmiṃ ca dāpayet ||
sumantuḥ: "brāhmaṇasya surāpasya ṣaṇmāsān uddhṛtasamudrodakasnānam | sāvitryaṣṭasahasraṃ juhuyāt pratyahaṃ trirātram upavāsās taptakṛcchreṇa ca pūto bhavaty aśvamedhāvabhṛthasnānena vā" | bṛhaspatiḥ |
gauḍīṃ paiṣṭīṃ tathā mādhvīṃ pītvā vipraḥ samācaret |
taptakṛcchraṃ parākaṃ ca cāndrāyaṇam anukramāt ||
sakṛd amatyākāmataḥ pītāsu praccharditāsu vratāntarāsamarthasyaitat | evamādīni gurulaghūni vratāni nimittābhyāsānabhyāsānusāreṇa kuryur anubandhatāratamyavaśena deśakālaguṇajātisāmarthyavaśena ca pariṣadā vyavasthāpanīyāni || 3.254 ||
kiṃ ca |
ajñānād vāruṇīṃ pītvā reto viṇmūtram eva vā |
punaḥ saṃskāram arhanti trayo varṇā dvijātayaḥ || 3.255 ||
ajñānād akāmataḥ surāṃ sakṛt pītvā reto viṇmūtraṃ vā prāśya saṃskāram upanayanaṃ dvijātayo brāhmaṇakṣatriyavaiśyāḥ kartum arhanti | anena ca surāpānanimittaṃ prāyaścittaṃ kṛtvā punaḥsaṃskārād vā śuddhiḥ | na tu saṃskāraśuddhimātrāt prāyaścittanimittā śuddhir iṣyate | anenaivābhiprāyeṇa manunāpy uktam | [1074]
ajñānād vāruṇīṃ pītvā punaḥ saṃskāram arhati |
matipūrvam anirdeśyaṃ prāṇāntikam iti sthitiḥ ||
matipūrvasurāpānam anirdeśyaprāyaścittaṃ parṣadānirdiṣṭam api nimittavatā tat svayam eva kāryam | anyathā tadupadeśo 'narthakaḥ syāt | tathā |
ajñānāt prāśya viṇmūtraṃ surāsaṃspṛṣṭam eva vā |
punaḥ saṃskāram arhanti trayo varṇā dvijātayaḥ ||
surāsaṃspṛṣṭaṃ tirohitasurārasam iti vijñeyam | yathā śuṣkasurābhāṇḍasthitam udakam itarasya tu prāśaḥ surāpānam eva | tatrāpi ca prāyaścittapūrvakam eva punaḥ saṃskāraḥ | tatprāyaścittam āha śātātapaḥ: "surābhāṇḍodakapāne chardanaṃ ghṛtasya prāśanam ahorātropavāsaś ca" iti | surābhāṇḍaparyuṣitajalapāne baudhāyana āha |
surāpānasya yo bhāṇḍeṣv apaḥ paryuṣitaḥ pibet |
śaṅkhapuṣpīvipakvaṃ tu kṣīraṃ sa tu pibet tryaham ||
kṣīraṃ ca stokam eva pātavyaṃ tapastvāt | matipūrvake madyabhāṇḍodakapānābhyāse cāmatipūrvake manur āha |
apaḥ surābhājanasthā madyabhāṇḍasthitās tathā |
pañcarātraṃ pibet pītvā śaṅkhapuṣpīśṛtaṃ payaḥ ||
payo 'tra kṣīram | abuddhipūrvake tu surābhāṇḍodakapāne viṣṇuḥ: "apaḥ surābhājanasthāḥ pītvā saptarātraṃ śaṅkhapuṣpīśṛtaṃ payaḥ pibet" | asminn eva viṣaye sābhyāse bṛhadyama āha |
surābhāṇḍasthitaṃ toyaṃ yadi kaścid pibed dvijaḥ |
sa dvādaśāhaṃ kṣīreṇa pibed brāhmīṃ suvarcalām ||
brāhmī suvarcalā, lavaṇikā brāhmī | yat (yas) tu surāpā[nā]nuvṛttau gautamena, "amatyā pāne payo ghṛtam udakaṃ vāyuḥ pratitryahaṃ taptāni sakṛcchras tato 'sya saṃskāro mūtrapurīṣaretasāṃ prāśane ca" iti taptakṛcchrapūrvakatvena saṃskāro vihitas tatrāpi taptakṛcchro 'lpāparādhaviṣaya eva, viṣayāntare tu prāyaścittāntaraṃ draṣṭavyam | ata eva baudhāyanaḥ: "amatyā surāpāne kṛcchrābdapādam" | kṛcchreṇābdapādaṃ trīn māsān ity arthaḥ | [1075] vasiṣṭhaḥ: "madyabhāṇḍasthitāś cāpo kaścid dvijaḥ pibet, padmoḍa(du)mbarapo (?) bilvapalāśakuśānām udakaṃ pītvā trirātreṇaiva śudhyati, tataḥ punar upanayanam, vapanādīnāṃ ca nivṛttiḥ" | mānavaṃ cātra ślokam udāharanti |
vapanaṃ mekhalā daṇḍo bhaikṣacaryā vratāni ca |
nivartante dvijātīnāṃ punaḥ saṃskārakarmaṇi || iti |
madyaṃ ca surāyā anyat | tad āha pulastyaḥ |
pānasaṃ drākṣamādhūkaṃ khārjūraṃ kaulam aikṣavam |
madhūtthamairam āriṣṭamaireyaṃ nārīkelajam ||
samānāni vijānīyān madyāny ekādaśaiva tu |
dvādaśaṃ tu surāmadyaṃ sarveṣām adhamaṃ smṛtam ||
drākṣekṣuṭaṅkakharjūrapanasādes tu yo rasaḥ |
sadyojātas tu taṃ pītvā tryahāc chudhyed dvijottamaḥ ||
bhaviṣyatpurāṇam |
prāyeṇ dharmaśāstreṣu sarveṣv eva surādhipa |
matipūrve surāpāne prāṇāntikam udāhṛtam ||
paiṣṭīpāne tu munibhir netarasyāṃ kadācana |
vasiṣṭhena tathoktaṃ vai prāyaścittaṃ surādhipa ||
kāmato madyapāne tu na surāyāḥ kadācana \|
madyapāne tv asurāyāḥ surāyāś cājñāne kṛcchātikṛcchrau ghṛtaprāśaḥ punaḥ saṃskāraś ca | abhyāse tu surāyā agnivarṇāṃ surāṃ pibet | kāmato gauḍīmādhvīpāna etat |
kāmato madyapānaṃ tu sakṛt kṛtvā surādhipa |
kṛcchrātikṛcchrau kṛtvā vai punaḥ saṃskārataḥ śuciḥ ||
madyaṃ surāvyatiriktam |
akāmataḥ surāṃ pītvā paiṣṭīṃ satkulanandana |
kṛcchrātikṛcchrau kṛtvā vai punaḥsaṃskārataḥ śuciḥ ||
atyantāśaktabālataraviṣayam etat |
caturthakālaṃ bhuñjānaḥ surāpo brāhmaṇo guha |
varṣatrayaṃ hi vidhivat tiṣṭhed ātmaviśuddhaye || [1076]
paiṣṭīpāne 'kāmataḥ sakṛt kāmato vābhyāse gauḍīmādhvīpāna etat |
pramādān madyam asuraṃ sakṛt pītvā dvijottamaḥ |
gomūtrayāvakāhāro daśarātreṇa śudhyati ||
ajñānān madyapānaṃ tu sakṛt kṛtvā dvijottamaḥ |
taptakṛcchraṃ samātiṣṭhed ghṛtaṃ prāśya tataḥ śuciḥ ||
atrāpi surāvyatiriktam eva madyaṃ vivakṣitam |
pratiṣedhaḥ surāpāne madyasya ca surādhipa |
dvijottamānām evoktaḥ satataṃ gautamādibhiḥ ||
taptakṛcchābhidhānaṃ hi yad uktaṃ gautamādibhḥ |
abuddhipūrve pāne tu gauḍīmādhvyos tad ucyate ||
gauḍīmādhvyos tathā pāne matipūrvaṃ kṛte guha |
vāsiṣṭhaṃ tatra vijñeyaṃ sarvapāpaharaṃ śubham ||
vāsiṣṭhaṃ "madyapāne tu asurāyāḥ" ityādikam |
asurāmadyapāne tu kṛte vipras tu kāmataḥ |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ samabhyasyec chuddhikāmaḥ suśuddhaye ||
yad vāsminn eva viṣaye mānavīyaṃ prakalpayet |
"kaṇān vā bhakṣayed abdam" ityādikam |
gauḍīmādhvyos tathābhyāse prāṇāntikam udāhṛtam |
mādhvīṃ pītvā pramādena sakṛd vipraḥ surādhipa ||
gomūtrayāvakāhāro daśarātreṇa śudhyati |
sakṛt pītvā tathā gauḍīm ajñānāt surasattama ||
kṛcchrātikṛcchrau vihitau ghṛtapānakam eva ca |
gauḍīm ajñānataḥ pītvā brāhmaṇo brāhmaṇapriya ||
taptakṛcchraṃ tu vai kṛtvā punaḥsaṃskārataḥ śuciḥ |
matipūrvaṃ yadā gauḍīṃ pibed vipraḥ surādhipa |
tadā cāndrāyaṇaṃ kuryād gāṃ ca dadyād dvijottame ||
maraṇāntikaprāyaścittānuvṛttau punar bhaviṣyatpurāṇam |
etāni paiṣṭyāḥ pāne tu kāmataḥ saṃpravartate |
prāyaścittāni coktāni gauḍīpāne nibodha me || [1077]
bṛhaspatisaveneṣṭvā madyapo brāhmaṇaḥ punaḥ |
samatvaṃ brāhmaṇair gacched ity eṣā vaidikī śrutiḥ || 3.255 ||
kiṃ ca |
patilokaṃ na sā yāti brāhmaṇī yā surāṃ pibet |
ihaiva sā śunī gṛdhrī sūkarī copajāyate || 3.256 ||
satkarmārjitam api patilokaṃ svargādikaṃ surāpī brāhmaṇī na yāti | dampatyoḥ sahādhikārād yo yāgādhikāriṇaḥ patyur lokaḥ sa eva patnyā apīty abhiprāyeṇoktam — patilokaṃ na sā yātīti | na kevalaṃ svargādilokaṃ sā na yāti kiṃ tu kutsitāṃ śvādiyoniṃ prāpnoti | tasmād brāhmaṇyāpi surā na peyā, pītā cet prāyaścittaṃ kāryam ity arthaḥ |
tasmād brāhmaṇarājanyau vaiśyaś ca na surāṃ pibet |
itivākyagataṃ liṅgaṃ vivakṣitaṃ manyamānaḥ strīṇāṃ surāpānapratiṣedhaṃ pṛthag āha | smṛtikārāś cānupādeyasyāpi viśeṣaṇaṃ vivakṣitaṃ manyante | ata eva "brāhmaṇo na hantavyaḥ" ity eṣa pratiṣedho brāhmaṇīvadhaviṣayo na bhavati | ata evātreyyādivadhakāriṇo brahmahatyāprāyaścittātideśaḥ | evaṃ sati brāhmaṇasuvarṇaharaṇaṃ mahāpātakam ity ukte brāhmaṇīsuvarṇaharaṇaṃ na mahāpātakam iti sidhyati | atra brāhmaṇīgrahaṇaṃ kṣatriyādistrīṇāṃ vyāvṛttyartham | ata eva vasiṣṭhaḥ: "yā brāhmaṇī surāpī na tāṃ devāḥ patilokaṃ nayanti | ihaiva sā bhramati kṣīṇapuṇyāpsu lug bhavati | śuktikā veti brāhmaṇī vā" ity uktavān | śaṅkho 'pi: "surālaśunapalāṇḍugṛñjanakamāṃsāny abhakṣyāṇy āha rasamayaṃ hi śarīraṃ tanmayatvād brāhmaṇaḥ saṃkīrthate, mātur aśitapītād dhi garbhaḥ saṃbhavati" iti || 3.256 ||
iti surāpānaprāyaścittaprakaraṇam ||
uddeśakramānusāreṇa suvarṇasteyaprāyaścittāny adhunāha |
brāhmaṇasvarṇahārī tu rājñe musalam arpayet |
svakarma khyapayaṃs tena hato mukto 'pi vā śuciḥ || 3.257 ||
[1078]
brāhmaṇasvabhūtaṃ suvarṇaṃ hṛtvā yena kenāpi prakāreṇācchidyābhiṣiktāya kṣatriyāya svakīyaṃ brāhmaṇasuvarṇasteyākhyaṃ karma khyāpayan musalam arpayet | tena rājñā tena musalena hataḥ śucir bhavati, yadi rājā na hanti kiṃ tu muñcati, tadāsau tenāhataḥ śucir bhavati | yady api suvarṇaśabdo 'tra hemajātivacana eva, tathāpy atra parimāṇaviśeṣaviśiṣṭasyaiva hemno 'pahāre prāyaścittam idaṃ draṣṭavyam, maraṇāṇtikatvāt | ata evoktaṃ ṣaṭtriṃśanmate |
suvarṇasyāpaharaṇe māṣakeṇāpi pātakam |
niṣkamātrāpahāreṇa na vidmaḥ kiṃ bhaviṣyati ||
vālāgramātre 'pahṛte prāṇāyāmaṃ samācaret |
likṣāmātre 'pi ca tathā prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ budhaḥ ||
rājasarṣapamātre tu prāṇāyāmacatuṣṭayam |
gāyatryaṣṭasahasraṃ tu japet pāpaviśuddhaye ||
gaurasarṣapamātre tu gāyatīṃ vai dinaṃ japet |
yavamātre suvarṇasya prāyaścittaṃ dinadvayam ||
suvarṇakṛṣṇalaṃ hy ekam apahṛtya dvijottamaḥ |
kuryāt sāṃtapanaṃ kṛcchraṃ tatpāpasyāpanuttaye ||
apahṛtya suvarṇasya māṣamātraṃ dvijādhamaḥ |
gomutrayāvakāhāras tribhir māsair viśudhyati ||
suvarṇasyāpahāreṇa vatsaraṃ yāvakī bhavet |
ūrdhvaṃ prāṇāntikaṃ jñeyam atha vā brahmahavratam || iti |
manuḥ |
rājā stenena gantavyo muktakeśena dhāvatā |
skandhenādāya musu(sa)laṃ laguḍaṃ vāpi khādiram ||
abhriṃ vobhayatas tīkṣṇam āyasaṃ daṇḍam eva vā |
śāsanād vāpi mokṣād vā stenaḥ steyād vimucyate ||
aśāsitvā tu taṃ rājā caurasyāpnoti kilbiṣam |
brāhmaṇo 'pi ca vadham arhatīty āha sa eva |
vadhena śudhyati steno brāhmaṇas tapasaiva vā || iti || [1079]
vyavasthāpanam etad vākyam iti vyavasthā [na] yujyate, vāśabdānarthakyaprasaṅgāt | yat tu manunaivoktam:
na jātu brāhmaṇaṃ hanyāt sarvapāpeṣv avasthitam |
iti, tad daṇḍaviṣayam | idaṃ tu prāyaścittaviṣayam | ata eva prāyaścittaprakaraṇe sa evāha |
suvarṇasteyakṛd vipro rājānam abhigamya tu |
gṛhītvā musu(sa)laṃ brūyān māṃ bhavān anuśāstv iti ||
gṛhītvā musu(sa)laṃ rājā sakṛd dhanyāt tu taṃ svayam ||
atra ca vipragrahaṇaṃ na varṇāntaravyudāsārtham | kiṃ tu "brāhmaṇo na hantavyaḥ" itiniṣedhanirāsārtham | "brāhmaṇo na hantavyaḥ" iti niṣedho rāgaprāptabrāhmaṇavadhaviṣayaḥ | suvarṇāpahartus tu brāhmaṇasya svaghātāya rājānaṃ pratyāgatasya vadhaḥ |
gṛhītvā musu(sa)laṃ rājā sakṛd dhanyāt tu taṃ svayam |
iti vihitatvād rājño dharma eva | tadakaraṇe ca pratyavāyaḥ | ata evāha gautamaḥ: "aghnann enasvī rājā" iti | yadā tu hato 'pi jīvati tadā tāvataiva śudhyati | yad āha saṃvartaḥ |
tato musu(sa)lam ādāya sakṛd dhanyāt tu taṃ svayam |
yadi jīvati sa stenas tadā steyād vimucyate ||
vasiṣṭho 'nyathāha: "brāhmaṇasuvarṇaharaṇe prakīrṇakeśo rājānam abhidhāvet | steno 'smi bhoḥ śāstu māṃ bhavān iti | tasmai rājauḍa(du)mbaraṃ śastraṃ dadyāt tenātmānaṃ pramāpayen maraṇāt pūto bhavati" iti | dṛḍhaprahārāsamarthe rājany etat | auḍa(du)mbaraṃ tāmramayam | "niṣkālako vā ghṛtākto gomayāgninā pādaprabhṛty ātmānaṃ dāhayet | maraṇāt pūto bhavatīti vijñāyate" | nirgataṃ kālaṃ kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ keśādikaṃ yasmāt sa niṣkālakaḥ | evamādīni maraṇāntikaprāyaścittāni kāmakārakṛte steye veditavyāni | tad āha aṅgirāḥ |
maraṇāntaṃ hi tat proktaṃ prayaścittaṃ manīṣibhiḥ |
tat tu kāmakṛte pāpe vijñeyaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ || iti || 3.257 ||
prāyaścittāntaram āha | [1080]
anivedya nṛpe śudhyet surāpavratam ācaran |
ātmatulyaṃ suvarṇaṃ vā dadyād vai vipratuṣṭidam || 3.258 ||
rājñe svakīyaṃ steyam anivedyāpi surāpavratam ācarañ śudhyet | sa bahudhanaś ced apahartā tadā svaśarīragauravatulyagurutvaṃ suvarṇaṃ viprāya dadyāt | alpadhanaś ced yāvajjīvaṃ kuṭumbanirvāhakṣamatayā tuṣṭikaraṃ suvarṇaṃ viprāya dadyāt | manuḥ |
tapasāpanunutsus tu suvarṇasteyajaṃ malam |
cīravāsā dvujo 'raṇye cared brahmahaṇo vratam ||
atra ca kapāladhāraṇādi nivartate | anenaivābhiprāyeṇa bhagavatā yājñavalkyenoktam "surāpavratam ācaran" iti | etac ca nirguṇabrāhmaṇasaṃbandhinaḥ suvarṇasyāpaharaṇa āpatkāle kuṭumbārthaṃ guṇavadbrāhmaṇakartṛke draṣṭavyam | nirguṇasya tu guṇavatsaṃbandhi bahutaraṃ dyūtādyartham apaharataḥ prāṇāntikam eva prāyaścittam | stena ity anuvṛttāv āpastambaḥ: "agniṃ vā praviśet tīkṣṇaṃ vā tapa āyacched bhaktāpacayenātmānaṃ vā samāpayet kṛcchraṃ saṃvatsaraṃ vā caret" | bhaktāpacayo 'naśanam | sumantuḥ: "suvarṇasteyī māsaṃ gāyatryaṣṭaśatam ājyāhutīr juhuyāt pratyahaṃ trirātram upavāsaḥ | taptakṛcchreṇa ca pūto bhavati | aśvamedhāvabhṛthasnānena vā" | laghuviṣṇuḥ |
steye brahmasvabhūte tu suvarṇaharaṇe kṛte |
tasmāt tenaiva dātavyaṃ tasmā ekādaśādhikam ||
ṛṇasaṃśuddhibhāvāya tataś cāndrāyaṇatrayam |
saṃvatsareṇa kartavyaṃ nipuṇāṃ śuddhim icchatā ||
abhāve kāñcanasya syād vratam etac caturguṇam |
cared yatātmā niḥsaṅgaḥ pūrṇaṃ varṣacatuṣṭayam ||
samāpte kāñcanaṃ gāś ca (gāśvo) rajataṃ cāpi śaktitaḥ |
deyam annaṃ dvijāgryebhyaḥ śubhaṃ pāpāpanuttaye ||
ekādaśādhikam ekādaśaguṇam | taddānasya carṇasaṃśuddhaye vihitatvād apūrvaprāyaścittakalpaśeṣabhūtacāndrāyaṇatrayaṃ yavamadhyapipīlikāmadhyayaticāndrāyaṇātmakaṃ [1081] tat saṃvatsaraṃ tāvad abhyaset | nirdhanatayā tv ekādaśaguṇadānāsamarthas tad eva varṣacatuṣṭayam abhyaset | brahmahatyāprāyaścittānuvṛttau vyāsaḥ |
etad eva vrataṃ stenaḥ pādanyūnaṃ samācaret |
atriḥ |
ṣaḍabdaṃ vā caret kṛcchraṃ yajed vā kratunā dvijaḥ |
tīrthāni ca bhraman vidvāṃs tatas steyād (?) vimucyate ||
ṛtur brahmahatyāprāyaścittaprakaraṇe draṣṭavyaḥ | ekavacanaṃ jātyabhiprāyam | sumantuḥ: "suvarṇasteyī dvādaśarātraṃ vāyubhakṣo bhavati" iti | evaṃ gurūṇi laghūni ca prāyaścittāni śāstreṣu vihitāni | strībālavṛddhāturāṇāṃ vihitam eṣāṃ cārdhaprāyaścittam | caturviṃśatimatam |
hṛtvā daśaguṇaṃ datvā prāyaścittaṃ samācaret |
rūpyaṃ hṛtvā dvijo mohāc carec cāndrāyaṇaṃ vratam ||
va(ga)dyāna(ṇa)daśakād ūrdhvam ā śatād dviguṇaṃ caret |
ā sahasrāt tu triguṇam ūrdhvaṃ hemavidhiḥ smṛtaḥ |
sarveṣāṃ dhātulohānāṃ parākaṃ tu samācaret ||
ṣaṭtriṃśanmatāt |
balād ye kāmakāreṇa haranti tu narādhamāḥ |
teṣāṃ tu balahartṝṇāṃ prāṇāntikam ihocyate ||
bhaviṣyatpurāṇam |
mahāpātakanirdeśe tṛtīyaṃ steyam ucyate |
na cāpy abuddhipūrvaṃ tat kriyate kenacit kvacit ||
pātakatvaṃ cāviśiṣṭaṃ vadanti munayo guha |
kṣatriyādir yadā hartā dyūtād yena prayojanam ||
sānubandhopahāraś ca tadaivaṃ śuddhaye caret ||
niṣkālako vā ghṛtākto gomayāgninā padbhyāṃ prabhṛty ātmānaṃ nirdahen maraṇāt pūto bhavatīti vijñāyate |
hṛtvā suvarṇaṃ rājanyo brāhmaṇasyāgnihotriṇaḥ |
romahīno ghṛtāktas tu ātmānaṃ pāvake dahet ||
viśiṣṭabrāhmaṇadravyaharaṇe pṛtanāpate |
doṣo bahutaraḥ syād vai kṣatriyādiṣu hartṛṣu || [1082]
tathāsminn eva viṣaye caurye yāti dvijottamaḥ |
alpānubandhe hi kṛte kṣatriyeṇetareṇa vā ||
mūrdhābhiṣikto rājā syād guṇavān kṣatriyas tathā |
hṛtaviprasuvarṇaś ca prāyaścittam idaṃ caret ||
brāhmaṇasuvarṇaharaṇe prakīrya keśān rājānam abhigacchet | steno 'smi bhoḥ śāstu māṃ bhavān iti | tasmai rājauḍa(du)mbaraṃ śastraṃ dadyāt tenātmānaṃ pramāpayet | maraṇāt pūto bhavatīti vijñāyate ||
tathāsminn eva viṣaya ityāder ayam arthaḥ — agnihotribrāhmaṇasuvarṇāpahāre brāhmaṇo 'tyantasānubandhadyūtādisiddhaye hartā kṣatriyādir vālpānubandhaḥ | tatra yady abhiṣekādiguṇavān kṣatriyo nṛpatir asti, tadā vasiṣṭhoktaṃ brāhmaṇasuvarṇaharaṇe prakīrya keśān rājānam abhigacched ityādyuktaṃ kuryād iti |
apahṛtya suvarṇaṃ ca brāhmaṇasya narādhipa |
paścān niṣkapramāṇaṃ tu rājānaṃ kṣatriyo vrajet ||
tasmād auḍa(du)mbaraṃ śastraṃ prāpyātmānaṃ hared guha |
prayātyave (pratyavāye?) na saṃdeho yadi jīvati suvrata ||
yad etad vacanaṃ vīra brāhmaṇas tapasaiva vā |
tatraiva kāraṇād vidvān brāhmaṇasya surādhipa ||
tapasaivety anenaivakāraṇena (kāreṇa) viduṣo brāhmaṇasya prāṇāntikaṃ niṣidhyate | aviduṣas tv atra viṣaye prāṇāntikam eva | anyathā "suvarṇasteyakṛd vipraḥ" ityādimanuvākyavirodhaḥ syāt || 3.258 ||
iti suvarṇasteyaprāyaścittaprakaraṇam ||
atha gurutalpaprāyaścittāny āha |
tapte 'yaḥśayane su(sva)pyād āyasyā yoṣitā saha |
gṛhītvotkṛtya vṛṣaṇau nairṛtyāṃ votsṛjet tanum || 3.259 ||
prājāpatyaṃ caret kṛcchraṃ samāṃ vā gurutalpagaḥ |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ vā trīn māsān abhyased vedasaṃhitām || 3.260 ||
[1083]
uktalakṣaṇo gurutalpagas tapte 'gnivarṇe lohamaye śayane mṛtyave su(sva)pyat saṃviśet | na kevalam ekākī, āyasyā yoṣitā saheti yāvat | yad vā vṛṣaṇau svakīyau svayam evotkṛtya chittvāñjalinā gṛhītvā nairṛtyāṃ diśi gatvā tatra tanuṃ śarīram utsṛjet | ā śarīranipātād gacched ity arthaḥ | evamādīni prāyaścittāny akāmato mātaraṃ uttamavarṇāṃ ca tatsapatnīṃ tadanyāṃ tu kāmato gacchato draṣṭavyāni | uktaṃ ca ṣaṭtriṃśanmate |
pitṛbhāryāṃ tu vijñāya savarṇāṃ yo 'dhigacchati |
jananīṃ vāpy avijñāya nāmṛtaḥ śuddhim āpnuyāt || iti |
amatipūrvāsamānavarṇapuṃścalīgurustrīgamane prāg retovisargān nivṛttau ca samāṃ saṃvatsaram ekaṃ vā prājāpatyaṃ kṛcchraṃ cared abhyaset | yad vā vakṣyamāṇalakṣaṇaṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ svādhyāyasaṃhitām abhyasan māsatrayaṃ tāvad ācaret | maraṇāntikaṃ ca prāyaścittaṃ tatra svakṛtaṃ gurutalpagamanam abhibhāṣya kāryam | yad āha manuḥ |
gurutalpo 'bhibhāṣyainas tapte su(sva)pyād ayomaye |
sūrmīṃ jvalantīṃ vā śliṣyen mṛtyunā sa viśudhyati ||
svayaṃ vā śiśnavṛṣaṇāv utkṛtyādāya cāñjalau |
nairṛtīṃ diśam ātiṣṭhed ā nipātād ajihmagaḥ ||
gurustrīgamanaṃ niyamenābhibhāṣya kathayitvety arthaḥ | sūrvī lohādimayī strīpratikṛtiḥ | hārītaḥ: "gurutalpago mṛnamaīm āyasīṃ vā striyāḥ pratikṛtim agnivarṇāṃ kṛtvā tām āliṅgya pūto bhavati" | vasiṣṭhaḥ: "niṣkālako ghṛtābhyaktas taptāṃ sūrmīṃ pariṣvajya maraṇāt pūto bhavatīti vijñāyate" | niṣkālako 'kṛtalomaśmaśrukeśavapanaḥ | prājapatyakṛcchrābhyāse ca viśeṣam āha |
khaṭvāṅgī cīravāsā vā śmaśrulo vijane vane |
prāyaścittaṃ caret kṛcchram abdam ekaṃ samāhitaḥ ||
cāndrāyaṇaṃ ca trīn māsān abhyasen niyatavrataḥ |
haviṣyeṇa yavāgvā vā gurutalpāpanuttaye || [1084]
yamaḥ |
atha vā śiśnavṛṣaṇāv utkṛtya pratigṛhya ca |
guror abhimukhas tiṣṭhan pūto bhavati kilbiṣāt ||
kāle 'ṣṭame vā bhuñjāno brahmacārī sadā vratī |
sthānāsanābhyāṃ viharaṃs trirahno 'bhyupayann apaḥ ||
adhaḥśāyī tribhir varṣais tad apohata pātakam ||
aṅgirāḥ |
gurutalpī śilāṃ taptām āyasīm āśu saṃviśet |
utkṛtya vṛṣaṇau vāpi dhārayed añjalau svayam ||
maraṇāyātha vā padbhyāṃ vrajed diśam uttarām |
śarīrasya vimokṣeṇa karmaṇo mucyate 'śubhāt ||
mahāvrataṃ cared vāpi dadyāt sarvasvam eva vā |
gurvarthe vā hato yuddhe mucyate gurutalpagaḥ ||
mahāvrataṃ dvādaśābdikaṃ brahmahavratam | śaṅkhenāpy uktam |
adhaḥśāyī jaṭādhārī parṇamūlaphalāśanaḥ |
ekakālaṃ samaśnan vai varṣe tu dvādaśe gate ||
rukmasteyī surāpaś ca brahmahā gurutalpagaḥ |
vratenānena śudhyanti mahāpātakinas tv ime ||
iti, tac cāmatipūrvasavarṇagurudāragamanaviṣayam | yat tu "mātuḥ sapatnīṃ bhaginīm" ityādinā gurutalpavratam atidiṣṭatvāt pādonam uktaṃ tad asavarṇagurutalpagamanaviṣayam | tatrāpi brāhmaṇīputrasya kṣatriyāṃ pitṛbhāryāṃ gacchato navavārṣikam, vaiśyāṃ ṣaḍvārṣikam, śūdrāṃ traivārṣikam, gurutalpavrataṃ bhavatīty eke manyante | anye tv ātideśikaṃ sarvaṃ saṃpūrṇam eva kāryam ity āhuḥ | akhaṇḍaprāptatvād atideśasya pādonatvādikalpanāyāṃ ca pramāṇābhāvāt | tataś ca yājñavalkīyāṅgirasavākyayoḥ samaviṣayataiva | tatsamaprāyaścittānāṃ tv anyūnatve samaśabdaprayoga eva pramāṇam | rājasamo mantrītyādāv anyūnaviṣayasya samaśabdaprayogasya darśanāt | brahmahatyānuvṛttau vyāsaḥ |
gatvaitad eva kurvīta gurutalpam akāmataḥ |
kāmato dviguṇaṃ proktaṃ pūrveṣu tu yad ucyate ||
pūrveṣu brahmahatyādiṣu dvādaśavārṣikaṃ vratam ucyate, tad api kāmataḥ kṛte pāpe dviguṇaṃ bhavatīty arthaḥ | [1085] sumantuḥ: "gurudāragāmī saṃvatsaraṃ kaṇṭakiśākhāṃ pariṣvajyādhaḥśāyī triṣavaṇī svakarmācakṣāṇo bhikṣāhāraḥ pūto bhavaty aśvamedhāvabhṛthasnānena vā" | brāhmaṇīputrasya vaiśyātmakeṣv agurudāreṣv etat | āpastambaḥ: "gurudāravyatikrāmī kharājinaṃ bahirloma paridhāya gurudāravyatikrāmiṇe me bhikṣāṃ dehīti saptāgārāṇi caret sā vṛttiḥ ṣaṇmāsān" | brāhmaṇīputrasya śūdrātmakeṣu gurudāreṣv etat | viṣṇuḥ: "mātṛgamanaṃ duhitṛgamanaṃ snuṣāgamanam ity atipātakāni |
atipātakinas tv ete praviśeyur hutāśanam |
na hy anyā niṣkṛtis teṣāṃ vidyate tu kadācana" ||
saṃvartaḥ |
mātaraṃ yadi gacchet tu snuṣāṃ vā puruṣādhamaḥ |
na tasya niṣkṛtiṃ vidyāt svakāṃ duhitaraṃ tathā ||
jīvato niṣkṛtir nāstīty arthaḥ | uśanā: "gurutalpābhigāmī saṃvatsaraṃ brahmahavrataṃ ṣaṇmāsān vā taptakṛcchraṃ caret" | etac ca kṣatriyāputrasya śūdrātmikāṃ gurustriyaṃ gacchataḥ | vṛddhamanuḥ |
gamane gurubhāryāyā gurubhāryāgame tathā |
abdatrayam akāmāt tu kṛcchraṃ nityaṃ samācaret ||
saṃvartaḥ |
śūdras tu viprāgamane gurutalpavrataṃ caret |
ajñānāj jñānato gatvā prāṇāntikam iti sthitiḥ ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ: "śūdraś ced brāhmaṇīm abhigacched vīrudbhir veṣṭayitvā śūdram agnau prāsyed brāhmaṇyāḥ śirasi vapanaṃ kārayitvā sarpiṣābhyajya nagnāṃ kṛṣṇakharam āropya mahāpathagamanaṃ vrājayet | pūtā bhavatīti vijñāyate" | anekaśikhāvapanaprāptaye śirasīty uktam | "vaiśyaś ced brāhmaṇīm abhigacchel lohitadarbhair veṣṭayitvā vaiśyam agnau prāsyet | brāhmaṇyāḥ śirasi vapanaṃ kārayitvā sarpiṣābhyajya nagnāṃ gaurakharam āropya mahapathaṃ vrājayet pūtā bhavatīti vijñāyate | rājayaś ced brāhmaṇīm abhigacchec charapatrair veṣṭayitvā rājanyam agnau pāsyed brāhmaṇyāḥ [1086] śirasi vapanaṃ kārayitvā sarpiṣābhyajya nagnāṃ śvetakharam āropya mahāpathagamanaṃ vrājayet pūtā bhavatīti vijñāyate" | mahāpatho rājapathaḥ | pūtā bhavati prāyaścittayogyā bhavatīty arthaḥ | yamaḥ |
retaḥsekaṃ kumārīṣu cāṇḍālīṣv antyajāsu ca |
sapiṇḍāpatyadāreṣu prāṇatyāgo vidhīyate ||
kumāryādiṣu kāmato 'bhyāse 'patyadāreṣv akāmataḥ sakṛd gamana etat | gurutalpaprāyaścittānuvṛttau hārītaḥ: "evam eva gurupitṛvyastrīgamanakanyāsagotrāsvasrīyā-gamane cāndrāyaṇaṃ vā sābhyāse matipūrvake ca pitṛvyādistrīgamane gurutalpagaprāyaścittam | anyathā tu cāndrāyaṇam" | gurutalpaprāyaścittam abhidhāyāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: "ācāryaputraśiṣyabhāryāsu caivam" | kratuḥ |
retaḥsekaḥ svayonyāsu goṣu śiṣyāṅganāsu ca |
sakhibhyāryāsutastrīṣu gamanād gurutalpagaḥ || 3.259, 260 ||
iti gurutalpaprāyaścittam |
athoddeśakramaprāptabrahmahādipatitacatuṣṭayasaṃsargiṇaḥ prāyaścittavidhim āha |
ebhis tu saṃvased yo vai vatsaraṃ so 'pi tatsamaḥ |
eṣāṃ tu brahmahādīnāṃ madhye yaḥ saṃvatsaraṃ yāvat saṃvaset saṃvyavaharet so 'pi prāyaścittatas tatsamo yaḥ prāyaścittaviśeṣas tasya sa eva tatsaṃsargiṇo 'pi bhavati | pātityena tatsamatvaṃ prāg evoktam | jātataś ca saṃvatsaraṃ yāvad atyantasaṃsarge tatprāyaścittaprāptiḥ | yathāha devalaḥ |
patitena sahoṣitvā jānan saṃvatsaraṃ naraḥ |
miśritas tena so 'bdānte svayaṃ ca patito bhavet ||
saṃsargahetūn āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
ekaśayyāsanaṃ paṅktir bhāṇḍapakvānnamiśraṇam |
yājanādhyāpane yānaṃ tathā ca saha bhojanam || [1087]
navadhā saṃkaraḥ prokto na kartavyo 'dhamaiḥ saha ||
devalaḥ |
saṃlāpasparśaniḥśvāsasahayānāsanāśanāt |
yājanādhyāpanād yaunāt pāpaṃ saṃkramate nṛṇām ||
yājanaṃ yonisaṃbandhaṃ svādhyāyaṃ sahabhojanam |
kṛtvā sadyaḥ pataty eva patitena na saṃśayaḥ ||
atra yājanādy ekaikaṃ pātityahetur asamāsanirdeśāt | saṃlāpādi tu samuditam | ata eva saṃlāpasaṃsparśaniḥśvāsasahayānāsanāśanād iti dvaṃdvanirdeśaḥ | yājanam atra somayāgaviṣayam | patitāya kanyādānaṃ tasmāc ca kanyāyā ādānaṃ yonisaṃbandhaḥ | svādhyāyagrahaṇena patitasyādhyayanam adhyāpakatvaṃ cātra vivakṣitam | tathā sahabhojanam [ekasminn amatre yugapadbhojanam] | etat pratyekaṃ sadya eva saṃsargiṇaḥ pātitye hetuḥ | manuḥ |
saṃvatsareṇa patati patitena sahācaran |
yājanādhyāpanād yaunān na tu yānāsanāśanāt ||
asyārthaḥ — patitena jñātena saṃvatsaraṃ yāvat kriyāṃ saha kuvan patati | tatsamo bhavatīty arthaḥ | tatra kā kriyā kriyāntaranirapekṣā pātityahetuḥ kā vā sāpekṣety apekṣāyām uktam: "yājanādhyāpanād yaunāt" iti | yājanādayaḥ pratyekam anyonyanirapekṣāḥ pātityahetavaḥ | na tu yānāsanāśanāt | yānādayaḥ parasparanirapekṣāḥ pātityahetavo na bhavanti kiṃ tu sāpekṣā eveti | yaunasya ca saṃvatsaraṃ yāvat kriyā tatsaṃbandhanimittabhojanādirūpāḥ | anenaivābhiprāyeṇa bṛhaspatir āha |
ṣāṇmāsike tu saṃyoge yājanādhyāpanādinā |
ekatrāsanaśayyābhiḥ prāyaścittārdham ācaret ||
amatipūrvaka eva svalpatare patitasaṃyoge ca vasiṣṭha āha: "patitasaṃyogena brāhmeṇa vā srauveṇa yaunena vā yās tebhyaḥ sakāśān mātrā upalabdhās tāsāṃ ca parityāgaḥ | taiś ca na saṃvased udīcīṃ gatvānaśnan saṃhitādhyayanam adhīyānaḥ pūto bhavatīti vijñāyate" | [1088] brāhmo 'dhyayanādhyāpanātmā, srauvo yājanam, mātrā dhanam | amatipūrvaka eva saṃsarge parāśara āha |
saṃsargam ācaran vipraḥ patitādiṣv akāmataḥ |
pañcāhaṃ vā daśāhaṃ vā dvādaśāham athāpi vā ||
abdārtham abdam ekaṃ vā bhaved ūrdhvaṃ tu tatsamaḥ |
trirātraṃ prathame pakṣe dvitīye kṛcchram ācaret ||
caret sāṃtapanaṃ kṛcchraṃ tṛtīye pakṣa eva tu |
caturthe daśarātraṃ syāt parākaḥ pañcame mataḥ ||
ṣaṣṭhe cāndrāyaṇaṃ kuryāt saptame caindavadvayam |
aṣṭame tu tathā pakṣe ṣaṇmāsān kṛcchram ācaret ||
matipūrvakasaṃsargaviṣaye tu smṛtyantaram |
pañcāhe tu caret kṛcchraṃ daśāhe taptakṛcchrakam |
parākas tv ardhamāse syān māse cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret ||
sāmatraye tu kurvīta kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇottaram |
ṣāṇmāsike tu saṃsarge kṛcchraṃ tv abdārdham ācaret ||
saṃsarge tv ābdike kuryād abdaṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ naraḥ ||
saṃvartaḥ |
ebhiḥ saṃsargam āyāti yaḥ kaścit pāpapūruṣaḥ |
ṣaṇmāsān abdam ekaṃ vā pūrvoktānāṃ vrataṃ caret ||
ebhir brāhmaṇahantṛprabhṛtibhir gurutalpagāntair matipūrvakaṃ ṣaṇmāsān saṃsargaṃ ya āyāti yaś cāmatipūrvam abdam āyāti sa teṣām eva vrataṃ cared ity arthaḥ | yat tu vyāsenoktam,
yo yena saṃpibed varṣaṃ so 'pi tatsamatām iyāt |
pādanyūnaṃ caret so 'pi tasya tasya vrataṃ dvijaḥ ||
iti, tan mahāpātakivyatiriktena yaḥ saṃspṛṣṭas tadviṣayam | bṛhaspatiḥ |
tathā vatsarasaṃsarge yoniyukte viśeṣataḥ |
pūrvoktena vidhānena patitavratam ācaret ||
yoniḥ prajananam | tadyukte pātitye patitayā vā maithunayukta ity arthaḥ | yat tu sumantunoktam: "sāhacarye vā saṃvatsaraṃ prājāpatyaṃ caret" iti, tan mahāpātakisamena saṃspṛṣṭasya veditavyam | sāhacaryaṃ saṃsargaḥ | viṣṇuḥ: "stokasaṃsargeṣv annapānādivarjiteṣu viśodhanaṃ taptakṛcchraṃ caritvā śuśucāndrāyaṇaṃ caret" | [1089] śātātapaḥ |
patitena sahoṣitvā taptakṛcchreṇa śudhyati |
etad amatipūrvakālpatarasaṃsargaviṣayam | pracurasaṃsargaviṣaye śaṅkha āha: "patitasaṃspṛṣṭo hy ātmānam uddharan saṃvatsaraṃ taptakṛcchraṃ caret | tathā patitasaṃvyavahārī mitradhruk śaraṇāgataghātī pratirūpakavṛttir jalaśayanapañcatapoyāvakāśana ātiṣṭhed dhemantagrīṣmavarṣāsu" | atra mārgaśīrṣamāsādimāsacatuṣṭayaṃ hemantaḥ | caitrādimāsacatuṣṭayaṃ grīṣmaḥ | śrāvaṇādimāsacatuṣṭayaṃ varṣāḥ | pratirūpakavṛttir anāśramī sann āśramaliṅgadhārī | tathā māsaṃ gomūtrayāvakaṃ pibet | evaṃ śudhyati |
kiṃ ca |
kanyāṃ samudvahed eṣāṃ sopavāsām akiṃcanām || 3.261 ||
eṣāṃ brahmahādīnāṃ patitānāṃ saṃbandhinīṃ kanyāṃ tadīyadhanarahitāṃ kṛtopavāsām udvahet, vivahet | patitotpannāpi sā na patitā yad āha vasiṣṭhaḥ: "patitenotpannaḥ patita ity āhur anyatra striyāḥ | sā hi paragāminī | tām atiriktām (arikthām) upādeyāt" iti vadaṃs tenādattāṃ svayam upanatāṃ pariṇayed iti gamayati | tathā ca hārītaḥ: "patitasya tu kumārīṃ vivastrām ahorātram upoṣitāṃ prātaḥ śuklenāhatena vāsasācchāditāṃ nāham eteṣāṃ mama naita iti trir uccair abhidhānāṃ tīrthasaṃkaṭahetor udvahet" iti | kanyāyāś ca patitenotpannāyāḥ prāyaścittam āha baudhāyanaḥ: "aśuciśukrotpannānām icchatāṃ prāyaścittāni patanīyānāṃ tṛtīyāṃśaḥ strīṇām aṃśāt tṛtīyam" iti | ayam arthaḥ — janakasya patitasya yāni patanīyāni karmāṇi tatprāyaścittānāṃ tṛtīyāṃśaḥ patitotpannānāṃ puṃsāṃ prāyaścittam | tasyāpi tṛtīyo bhāgaḥ patitotpannānāṃ strīṇām iti || 3.261 ||
kiṃ ca |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret sarvān avakṛṣṭān nihatya tu |
sarvān eva varṇebhyo 'vakṛṣṭān varṇabāhyān antyajān puruṣān nihatya pratyekaṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret | yat tv aṅgirasoktam, [1090]
sarvān tyajānāṃ gamane bhojane saṃpramāpaṇe |
parākeṇa viśuddhiḥ syād ity āṅgirasabhāṣitam ||
iti, tad amatipūrvakāvakṛṣṭavadhaviṣayam | cāndrāyaṇaṃ tu matipūrvakāvakṛṣṭavadhaviṣayam ity avirodhaḥ |
nanu cāndrāyaṇaparākāditapo gāyatryādimantrajapayuktaṃ kathaṃ tatra śūdrāder anadhītavedasyādhikāra ity āha (ity ata āha) |
śūdro 'dhikārahīno 'pi kālenānena śudhyati || 3.262 ||
japahomādyadhikārahīno 'pi śūdro yathoktaśārīrakarmakārī kālenānenoktena māsasaṃvatsarādinā śudhyati, vipāpo bhavati | nanv evaṃ sati paṅgor viṣṇukramarahite śūdrasya ca mantrādihīne darśapūrṇamāsādikarmaṇ¥ adhikāraḥ syāt | maivaṃ vādīḥ | na hi tatra sākṣāc chūdraḥ kartṛtvena kvacid vākyena nirdiṣṭo 'sti, yena tasya mantrādirahite darśādikarmaṇy adhikāraḥ parikalpyeta | santi ca mantrādisakalāṅgopetakarmaprayogasamarthā dvijāyato 'dhikāriṇa iti tatraiva svargakāmādiśruter upasaṃhāraḥ | prāyaścitte tu yathā sthapatīṣṭau mantrādirahito 'pi sākṣācchrutyā niṣādaḥ kartṛtayā gṛhītas tathā śūdraḥ prāyaścitteṣu | tathā ca aṅgirāḥ |
tasmāc chūdraṃ samāsādya sadā dharmapathe sthitam |
prāyaścittaṃ pradātavyaṃ japahomavivarjitam ||
tathā |
śūdraḥ kālena śudhyet tu gobrāhmaṇahite rataḥ |
dānair vāpy upavāsair vā dvijaśuśrūṣayā tathā ||
jābālo 'py āha |
akāmakṛtapānānāṃ vadanti brāhmaṇā vratam |
kāmakārakṛte 'py eke dvijānāṃ vṛṣalasya ca ||
śūdraṃ kṛtainasaṃ prāpya sadā dharmapuraḥsaram |
prāyaścittaṃ pradātavyaṃ vedamantravivarjitam ||
yad uktaṃ mārkaṇdeyena,
kṛcchrāny etāni kāryāṇi sadā varṇatrayeṇa tu |
kṛcchreṣv eteṣu śūdrasya nādhikāro vidhīyate ||
iti, tad viśiṣṭakṛcchrapratiṣedhakaṃ mantravarjaṃ kṛcchraṃ kuryād eva | yat tu manunoktam,
na śūdrāya matiṃ dadyān nocchiṣṭaṃ na haviṣkṛtam |
na cāsyopadiśed dharmaṃ na cāsya vratam ādiśet || [1091]
iti, tad antarā brāhmaṇam akurvatāṃ prāyaścittātmakavratātideśaniṣedhaparam | ata eva śaṅkho 'ntarā brāhmaṇaṃ kṛtvā vratādeśaḥ kārya ity āha |
nyāyato mārgatah kṣipraṃ kṣatriyādeḥ kṛtainasaḥ |
antarā brāhmaṇaḥ kṛtvā vrataṃ sarvaṃ samādiśet ||
āyato brāhmaṇānāṃ yo vṛttiṃ yaś ca prayacchati |
brāhmaṇānāṃ ca yo bhaktaḥ sa tāryaḥ sarvayatnataḥ || iti ||
iti mahāpātakaprāyaścittāni |
atha sarvamahāpātakaprāyaścittāny ucyate, tatra viṣṇuḥ |
aśvamedhena śudhyeyur mahāpātakinas tv ime |
pṛthivyāṃ sarvatīrthānāṃ tathānusaraṇena vā ||
aśvamedhādikraturūpaṃ prāyaścittam āhitāgnim iṣṭaprathamayajñaṃ prati vidhīyate | ādhānāder dvijātikarmatvena pratitānanuṣṭheyatvāt | evam adhītavedasyaiva saṃhitājapādi vidhīyate | aśvamedhaś ca rājakratuḥ | tena yadi rājākāmato guṇavantaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ hanti, tathaiva paiṣṭīṃ surāṃ vā pibati, guṇavato brāhmaṇasya suvarṇaṃ vāpaharati, kāmata eva vā gurudārān gacchati tadāsau pratimahāpātakaṃ yajet | āpastambaḥ: "steyaṃ kṛtvā surāṃ pītvā gurudārāṃś ca gatvā brahmahatyām akṛtvā caturthakālamitabhojino 'po 'bhyupeyuḥ | savanānukalpaṃ sthānāsanābhyāṃ viharanta ete tribhir varṣaiḥ pāpaṃ nudanti" | pañcānāṃ mahāpātakānāṃ madhye brahmahatyāyā naitat prāyaścittam iti vaktum uktaṃ brahmahatyām akṛtveti | caturthe bhojanakāle bhojanaṃ yeṣāṃ te caturthakālāḥ | mitabhojino 'tṛptir annasya vrataṃ yeṣāṃ te, tathāpo 'bhyupeyur bhūmigatāsv apsu snānam ācareyuḥ | savanānukalpaṃ prātar madhyaṃdine sāyam iti | sthānāsanābhyāṃ divā sthāne rātrāv āsane yuktā bhaveyuḥ | viharanto vihareyuḥ | yathāśakty ahar ahar anyam anyaṃ deśaṃ gaccheyuḥ | etad guṇavataḥ kuryur nirguṇaviprasvarṇāpahāre gauḍīmādhvyoś cāmatipūrvake sakṛt pāne gurvaṅganāgame pratyayarahite sakṛd gamane ca veditavyam | yac ca aṅgirasā vajrākhyaṃ vratam uktam |
yuktas triṣavaṇasnāyī saṃyato maunam āsthitaḥ |
prātaḥ snātvā samārambhaṃ kuryāj jāpasya niścalaḥ || [1092]
sāvitrīṃ vyāhṛtīś caiva japed aṣṭasahasrakṛt |
oṃkāram āditaḥ kṛtvā rūpe rūpe tathāntataḥ ||
bhūmau vīrāsane yuktaḥ kuryāj japyaṃ susaṃyataḥ |
tatrāsīnaḥ kāṃsyapātre pibed gavyaṃ payaḥ sakṛt ||
gavyasya payaso 'lābhe gavyam eva pibed dadhi |
dadhyabhāve pibed takraṃ takrābhāve tu yāvakam ||
eṣām anyatamaṃ yad yad upapadyeta tat pibet |
gomūtreṇa samāyuktaṃ yāvakaṃ vopayojayet ||
ekāhenaiva kṛcchro 'yam uktas tv aṅgirasā svayam |
sarvapāpaharo divyo nāmnā vajra iti smṛtaḥ ||
etat pātakayuktānām upapātakināṃ tathā |
mahadbhiś cābhiyuktānāṃ prāyaścittaṃ vinirdiśet ||
mahāpātakasaṃyuktā varṣaiḥ śudhyanti vai tribhiḥ ||
idam api mahāpātakeṣu | āpastambīyena samānaviṣayaṃ tulyaprāyakleśatvāt | pātakayukte māsikaḥ prayogaḥ | upapātayukte tu traimāsikaḥ | jābālaḥ |
apahāre suvarṇasya kṛcchrābdena viśudhyati |
gurvaṅganāgame caiva surāpāne tathaiva ca ||
bahudhananirguṇahīnavarṇasuvarṇam alpataram āpadi guṇavato 'paharato guroś ca hīnavarṇām akāmataḥ sakṛd aṅganāṃ gacchataḥ surāṃ cāmukhyāṃ sakṛd amatyā pītvā charditavataś caitat || 3.262 ||
iti sādhāraṇāni prāyaścittāni |
kiṃ ca |
mithyābhiśaṃsino doṣo dviḥ samo bhūtavādinaḥ |
mithyābhiśastadoṣaṃ ca samādatte mṛṣā vadan || 3.263 ||
mithyābhiśaṃsino nirdoṣaṃ sadoṣatayā pratipādayato dvirdoṣo bhavati, mithyāvākyaprayogāc cābhiśāpāc ca | yas tu bhūtaṃ santam eva doṣaṃ vadati, tasya samaḥ kīrtyamānadoṣatulyo doṣo bhavati | kiṃ ca mithyābhiśaptasya yaḥ kaścid doṣas taṃ mithyābhiśaṃsī samādatte | abhiśastapuruṣasamavetadoṣeṇa tulyaṃ doṣaṃ [1093] prāpnotīty asya tātparyam | na punaḥ puruṣāntaradoṣasya puruṣāntarasaṃkramaṇe pramāṇam asti | apekṣitavyaṃ ca pramāṇāntaraṃ puruṣavacasā | na caivaṃvidhe 'rthe śrutiḥ pramāṇaṃ kiṃ tu kārye | manuḥ |
patitaṃ patitety uktvā cauraṃ caureti vā punaḥ |
vacanāt tulyadoṣaḥ syān mithyā dvirdoṣatāṃ vrajet ||
āpastambaḥ: "doṣaṃ buddhvā na pūrvaṃ parasya samākhyātā syāt pariharec cainaṃ dharmeṣu" | svayaṃ viditam api doṣaṃ pareṇāvijñātaṃ na prakāśayed ity arthaḥ || 3.263 ||
saṃpraty avasaraprāptāny upapātakaprāyaścittāni vakṣyāms taduddeśakramavaśena govadhaprāyaścittāni tāvad āha |
pañcagavyaṃ piban goghno māsam āsīta saṃyataḥ |
goṣṭheśayo go'nugāmī gopradānena śudhyati || 3.264 ||
prājāpatyaṃ vātikṛcchraṃ cared vāpi samāhitaḥ |
dadyāt trirātraṃ vopoṣya vṛṣabhaikādaśās tu gāḥ || 3.265 ||
goghno govadhakārī goḥ kṣīraṃ dadhi sarpiś ca śakṛn mūtram iti pañcagavyaṃ pibann āhārāntaraṃ varjayan rātrau goṣṭhe gosthānake śayānaḥ svapan divā go'nugāmī niyamena gacchantīś cānugacchan saṃyato māsaṃ yāvad asīta, kāryāntaraṃ praty udāsīno bhavet | vratānte gāṃ datva śudhyati | atha vā kṛcchraṃ prājāpatyaṃ caret | kiyantaṃ kālaṃ kiṃ nu kurvann ity apekṣāyāṃ pūrvaślokoktāni padāny atrānuṣajyante | pañcagavyaṃ pibann ity ekaṃ kṛcchravidhānavirodhān nivartate, tataś ca goṣṭheśayo go'nugāmī māsaṃ yāvat prājāpatyaṃ kṛcchraṃ caret | vratānte gopradānena śudhyatīti vākyārthaḥ siddho bhavati | atha vā māsaṃ yāvad atikṛcchraṃ pūrvavac caret | atha vā trirātram upoṣya vṛṣabha ekādaśo yāsāṃ vā vṛṣabhaikādaśā gāś ca dadyāt | atra jābālaḥ |
prājāpatyaṃ caren māsaṃ gohantā ced akāmataḥ |
gohito go'nugāmī ca gopradānena śudhyati ||
etatsāhacaryāt "pañcagavyaṃ piban māsam(ngoghnaḥ)" ityādīny api yājñavalkyoktāny akāmavadhaviṣayāṇi | eṣāṃ ca gauravalāghavaṃ deśakālavayaḥsāmarthyāpekṣayā [1094] vyavasthāpanīyam | sarvatra gosvāmino dravyeṇa parituṣṭiḥ kāryā | yad āha manuḥ |
hiṃsyād dravyāṇi yo yasya jñānato 'jñānato 'pi vā |
sa tasyotpādayet tuṣṭiṃ rājñe dadyāc ca tatsamam || iti |
eṣāṃ prathamena prāyaścittakalpena tulyaviṣayaṃ prāyaścittakalpāntaraṃ śātātapa āha |
pañcagavyena goghātī māsenaikena śudhyati |
gomatīṃ ca japed vidyāṃ gavāṃ goṣṭhe ca saṃviśet ||
kecid anyathā paṭhanti: "goghas trīn kṛcchān prājāpatyān kuryāt | gamatīṃ ca japed vidyām" iti | asminn eva viṣaye hantā ced guṇavāṃs tadā saṃvartoktam |
goghnaḥ kurvīta saṃsthānaṃ goṣṭhe gorūpasaṃnidhau |
tatraiva śayanaṃ cāsya māsārdhaṃ kṣitiśāyinaḥ ||
saktuyāvakabhakṣyāṇi payo dadhi śaraḥ śakṛt |
śaraḥ kṣīrāder uparitano bhāgaḥ |
etāni kramaśo 'śnīyān naras tatpāpamokṣa(ca)kaḥ ||
śudhyeta so 'rdhamāsena nakharomavivarjitaḥ |
snānaṃ triṣavaṇaṃ tasya sa bhaven nityatendriyaḥ ||
etat samāhitaḥ kuryān naro vigatamatsaraḥ |
gāyatrīṃ ca japen nityaṃ pavitrāṇi ca śaktitaḥ ||
pūrṇe caivārdhamāse tu sa viprān bhojayed dvijaḥ |
bhuktavatsu ca vipreṣu gāś ca dadyād vicakṣaṇaḥ ||
gavām alābhe dātavyaṃ gomūlyaṃ na tu saṃśayaḥ |
anena ca vidhānena goghno mucyeta kilbiṣāt || iti |
asminn eva viṣaye śaṅkho 'py āha: "goghnaḥ pañcagavyāhāraḥ pañcaviṃśatirātram upavaset saśikhaṃ vapanaṃ kṛtvā gocarmaprāvṛto gā anugacched goṣṭhe vased gopradānaṃ ca" iti | paiṭīnasiḥ: "yavāgūprasṛtiṃ taṇḍulamiśritāṃ māsaṃ bhuñjāno gobhyaḥ priyaṃ kurvāṇo goghnaḥ śudhyati" | [1095] "pañcagavyaṃ piben māsam" ity anena samānaviṣayam idam | vyādhivaśād āsannamaraṇām abuddhipūrvaṃ vyāpāditavantaṃ prati sumantur āha: "goghnasya gopradānaṃ dvā(da)śarātraṃ pañcagavyaprāsanaṃ udakatarpaṇopasparśane gavām anugamanaṃ ca" iti | atha vā trāsādibalād utpannavyādhibalād gomaraṇanirvāsanādikartuḥ saumantavaṃ draṣṭavyam | parāśaraḥ |
gavāṃ bandhanayoktrais tu bhaven mṛtyur akāmataḥ |
akāmataḥ kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ brāhmaṇebhyo nivedayet ||
vedavedāṅgavid virpo dharmaśāstrabahuśrutaḥ |
akāmakṛtapāpasya prāyaścittaṃ prakalpayet ||
dhuryeṣu vāhyamāneṣu daṇḍenābhihateṣu ca |
kāṣṭhena lo(le)ṣṭunā vāpi pāṣāṇena ca tāḍitaḥ ||
mūrchitaḥ patataś caiva mṛto vā sady eva ca |
evaṃ gatānāṃ dhuryāṇāṃ pravakṣyāmi yathā vidhim ||
utthitas tu padaṃ gacchet pañca sapta daśāpi vā |
grāsaṃ vā yadi gṛhṇāti toyaṃ vā pibati svayam ||
sarvavyādhivinaṣṭānāṃ prāyaścittaṃ na vidyate |
anyatra prāyaścittaṃ bhavaty eva |
rodhane tu bhavet pādaṃ bandhane cārdham eva tu |
yojane pādahīnaṃ syāt prājāpatyaṃ ca ghātane ||
śītānalahatā caiva udbandhanamṛtāpi vā |
śūnyāgārādyupekṣāyāṃ prājāpatyaṃ vinirdiśet ||
tathā |
apālanāt praṇaśyet tu gauś carantī kathaṃcana |
jalapalvalamagnā vā meghavidyuddhatāpi vā ||
śvabhre vā patitākasmāc chvāpadair bakṣitāpi vā |
prājāpatyaṃ caret kṛcchraṃ gosvāmī vratam uttamam ||
saśikhaṃ vapanaṃ kāryaṃ trisaṃdhyam avagāhanam |
śṛṅgair vāpi khurair yuktaṃ lāṅgūlaśravaṇādibhiḥ ||
ārdram eva hi tac carma paridhāya samaṃ vrajet |
tāsāṃ madhye vased rātrau divā tābhiḥ samaṃ vrajet || [1096]
brāhmaṇasya viśeṣeṇa tathā rājanyavaiśyayoḥ |
prāyaścitte tataś cīrṇe kuryād brāhmaṇabhojanam ||
anaḍutsahitāṃ gāṃ ca dadyād viprāya dakṣiṇām || iti |
asyāsmād eva viṣayo 'vagataḥ | anenābhinnaviṣayam āha aṅgirāḥ |
aṅguṣṭhamātraḥ sthaulyena bāhumātraḥ pramāṇataḥ |
ārdraś ca sapalāśaś ca daṇḍa ity abhidhīyate ||
asmād ūrdhvaṃ pramāṇena yadi gāṃ vinipātayet |
dviguṇaṃ tu caret tatra prāyaścittam iti sthitiḥ ||
rodhane bandhane vāpi yojane vā gavāṃ rujām |
utpādya maraṇaṃ vāpi nimittī tatra lipyate ||
pādam ekaṃ cared rodhe dvau pādau bandhane caret |
yojane pādahīnaṃ syāc caret sarvaṃ nipātane ||
śṛṅgabhaṅge 'sthibhaṅge ca carmanirmocane 'pi vā |
daśarātraṃ pibed vajraṃ svasthā sā yadi gaur bhavet ||
anyatrāṅkanalakṣmabhyāṃ bahir nirmocane 'pi vā |
sāyaṃ saṃgopanārthaṃ vā na duṣyed rodhabandhayoḥ || iti |
yadānubandhanādinimitto 'kāmato vināśas tadā pārāsaryam uktaṃ veditavyam | asminn eva viṣaye dānaśaktau kāśyapoktam | tad yathā: "gobalīvardavadhe kāmataḥ prāyaścittam | kāmakārakṛte 'py eke | dogdhrīdamanabandhanavahanadāmapāśasaṃghaṭṭanaghaṇṭābharaṇa-yojanabhojanatailapānauṣadhivikriye(kraya)viniyogeṣu vipattau prāyaścittam | brāhmaṇebhyo nivedya saśikhaṃ vapanaṃ kṛtvā prājāpatyaṃ kṛcchram ācaret | cīrṇānte dakṣiṇāṃ gāṃ viprāya dadyāt | tiladhenuṃ ca" iti | etac cānādareṇauṣadhadānādikarmanimittāyāṃ vipattau satyāṃ draṣṭavyam | yata āha saṃvartaḥ |
yantraṇe gocikitsārthe mūḍhagarbhavimocane |
yatne kṛte vipattiḥ syāt prāyaścittaṃ na vidyate || iti | [1097]
mūḍhagarbho 'ntarmṛtagarbhaḥ | yad vā gūḍhagarbhaḥ | yat tu tenaivoktam,
vyāpannānāṃ bahūnāṃ tu rodhane bandhane 'pi vā |
bhiṣag mithyopacāre tu dviguṇaṃ govrataṃ caret ||
iti, tat pratinimittaṃ naimittikāvṛttinivāraṇāya | atra ca matyamatyādivaśena yat prāptaṃ tasya vaiguṇyaṃ vidhīyate | yac ca tenaivoktam,
ekā ced bahubhiḥ kācid daivād vyāpāditā kvacit |
pādaṃ pādaṃ tu hatyāyāś careyus te pṛthak pṛthak ||
iti, tad api vyāpādakaṃ nyāyato vratasākalyaprāptau vratapādavidhānārtham | amatipūrvakavyāpādana etat | daivād iti vacanāt | matipūrvake tu pratyekaṃ saṃpūrṇavratayogaḥ pratyekaṃ hantṛtvāt | vacanasya cāpavādakasyābhāvāt | ata eva ca,
ekāṃ ghnatāṃ bahūnāṃ tu yathoktād dviguṇo damaḥ |
iti daṇḍadvaiguṇyam upapannaṃ bhavati | āpastambaḥ |
na nālikereṇa na vālarajjvā na cāpi mauñjena na gātrabandhaiḥ |
etais tu gāvo na hi bandhanīyā baddhvā tu tiṣṭhet paraśuṃ gṛhītvā ||
kuśakāśais tu badhnīyāt sthāne 'pāyavivarjite |
gṛhalagnāgnidāheṣu prāyaścittaṃ na vidyate ||
khadāyāṃ patane kṛcchraṃ pāṣāṇe tu dvayaṃ caret |
ardhakṛcchraṃ tu vāpyāṃ syāt pādahīnaṃ tu kūpake ||
kāśyapaḥ |
caret sāṃtapanaṃ kāṣṭhaiḥ prājāpatyaṃ tu loṣṭakaiḥ |
taptakṛcchraṃ tu pāṣāṇair atikṛcchraṃ tathāyasaiḥ ||
kāṣṭhādibhiḥ prahṛtya sāṃtapanaṃ kāryam ity arthaḥ | prahāramātra etat | na tu māraṇe |
śālāyām avaruddhānām agnidāhavipattiṣu |
varṣamārutasaṃpāte maraṇād govadho bhavet ||
kāntāreṣv atha vā durge gehadāhe khaleṣu ca |
yadi tatra vipattiḥ syāt pāda eko vidhīyate ||
pādaś caturo bhāgaḥ | sa cātra prāyaścittasya, na pājāpatyasya tasyāprakṛtatvāt | [1098]
kṛcchratrayaṃ śastraghātī yaṣṭighātī dvayaṃ caret |
mṛtpiṇḍaṃ cātikṛcchreṇa prājāpatyena gomaye ||
yojane pādahīnaṃ syāt snāpane kujalena ca |
taptakṛcchreṇa godhartā parākeṇa nadītarāt ||
ardhakṛcchreṇa muṣṭvā syāt sparśane pādakṛcchrataḥ |
śakaṭena tu kṛcchreṇa ardhakṛcchreṇa vāriṇā ||
gavāṃ nipātane caiva garbhanāśo bhaved yadi |
ekaikasya caret kṛcchraṃ yathā pūrvaṃ tathā param ||
pādam utpannamātre tu dvau pādau dṛḍhatāṃ gate |
pādonaṃ vratam uddiṣṭaṃ hatvā garbham acetanam ||
aṅgapratyaṅgasaṃbhūte garbhe cetaḥsamanvite |
dviguṇaṃ govrataṃ kuryād eṣā goghnasya niṣkṛtiḥ ||
vṛddhaprecetāḥ |
ekavarṣe hate vatse kṛcchrapādo vidhīyate |
abuddhipūrve puṃsaḥ syād dvipādas tu dvihāyane ||
trihāyane tripādaḥ syāt prājāpatyam ataḥ param ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
ativṛddhām atikṛśām atibālāṃ ca rogiṇīm |
hatvā pūrvavidhānena cared abdavrataṃ dvijaḥ ||
hemānnatiladānena naraḥ pāpāt pramucyate ||
parāśaraḥ |
kṣatam utpāditaṃ yena kuryāt sa vraṇaropaṇam |
mṛtyur bhavati cet tena govadhyā tasya niṣkalā ||
śṛṅgabhede trirātraṃ tu dviguṇaṃ pādabhedane |
caturthakāle bhuñjīta gavām utpādya śoṇitam ||
pādena hatvā bhuñjīta rātrau datvā gavāhnikam (?) ||
cyavanaḥ |
goghnaḥ saumyatrayaṃ kuryāt kṛcchraṃ vā navamāsikam |
gomūtrayāvakāśī vā tribhir māsair viśudhyati ||
guṇavatīṃ guṇavatsvāmikām amatipūrvakaṃ nirguṇasya hatavata etat | saumyaḥ kṛcchraviśeṣaḥ | saṃvartaḥ |
garbhiṇīṃ kapilāṃ dogdhrīṃ hemadhenuṃ ca suvratām |
rodhādinā ghātayitvā dviguṇaṃ vratam ācaret || [1099]
yat tu bṛhaspatinoktam,
dvādaśāhena goghātī tasmāt pāpāt pramucyate |
gāyatryāḥ śatasāhasraṃ japed vā yāvakāśanaḥ ||
nirmuṇḍaṃ vapanaṃ kṛtvā mauñjīkaupīnavastradhṛt |
gavāṃ madhye vased goṣṭhe divā cainā anuvrajet |
cīrtvā vrataṃ dvijāgryebhyo dadyād gāḥ svarṇam eva ca ||
iti, tat prāṇahāriṇo rogotpādakabhīṣaṇādikāriṇo veditavyam | gāyatrījapasamarthasya śatasāhasriko japo 'parādhātiśaye sati draṣṭavyaḥ | asminn eva viṣaye yad uktaṃ tenaiva
kṣīrāhāro bhaved vāpi kṣīradadhyaśano 'pi vā |
iti, tad dvādaśāhabhojanāsamarthaviṣayam | yad api bṛhaspatimataṃ kecit paṭhanti: "dvādaśarātraṃ pañcagavyāhāraḥ ṣaḍrātraṃ yāvakāhāro goṣṭhe vased deśakālaśarīrānurūpaṃ keśaśmaśrunakhādīnāṃ vapanaṃ kṛtvā brāhmaṇān praṇipatyānujñātaś cīrṇānte gāṃ dadyād brahmaṇān bhojayitvā śuddhir ity āha bṛhaspatiḥ" | tad api pūrveṇa samānaviṣayam | āpastambaḥ |
kāntāreṣv atha durgeṣu gṛhadāhe 'vaṭeṣu ca |
yadi tatra vipattiḥ syāt pāda eko vidhīyate ||
ghaṇṭābharaṇadoṣeṇa gaus tu yatra vipadyate |
vared eva vrataṃ tatra bhūṣaṇārthaṃ kṛtaṃ hi tat ||
damane dāmane caiva yojane 'tyantasaṃkulam |
pāṃsubhiś ca vipanne vā gavi pādonam ācaret ||
atidohyātivāhyeṣu nāsikābhedanena vā |
nadīparvatasaṃrodhaṃ mṛte pādonam ācaret ||
hale ca śakaṭe caiva durbalaṃ yo niyojayet |
pratyavāye samutpanne tatra prapnoti govadham ||
asthibhaṅgaṃ gavāṃ kṛtvā lāṅgūlasya ca chedanam |
pātanaṃ caiva śṛṅgāṇāṃ māsārdhaṃ yāvakaṃ pibet || [1100]
dātavyo yavasas tāvad yāvad roheta tad vraṇam |
tadvarṇāṃ dakṣiṇāṃ datvā tataḥ pāpāt pramucyate ||
evaṃ yājñavalkīyena samānaviṣayāṇi saprāsaṅgikāni smṛtyantaroktaprāyaścittāny aśeṣataḥ pradarśitāni | yatra punar hantā nirguṇo guṇavatīṃ gāṃ guṇavato brāhmaṇasya svabhūtāṃ kāmato matipūrvakaṃ ca khaḍgādinā vyāpādayati tatra gautama āha: "gāṃ ca vaiśyavat" iti | vaiśyavad vaiśyahatyāprāyaścittavad gāṃ datvā prāyaścittaṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ | vaiśyahatyāyāṃ vratatrayaṃ tenaivopadiṣṭam | "vaiśyaṃ hatvā traivārṣikaṃ brahmahatyāprāyaścittātmakaṃ (?) brahmacaryaṃ caret" ity ekaṃ vratam upadiśyate | "vṛṣabhaikādaśā gā dadyāt" ity aparam | traivārṣikaṃ gośatadānasahitaṃ tṛtīyam | atraiva gāś (gāṃ) ceti cakāreṇāparaṃ prāyaścittatrayam atidiśyate | yadi hi vaiśyavratātideśa evābhimataḥ syāt tadā vaiśyaṃ gāṃ vā hatvety ekam eva sūtraṃ syāt | sutrāntaram ārabhamāṇo jñapayati vratāntaram apy atrātidiśyata iti | tadatideśaś cetthaṃ vyākaraṇīyaḥ | gāṃ vaiśyavac ceti kramānyatvena cakāro yojanīyaḥ | tataś ca śūdravad iti cakārāl labhyate | anantaraṃ hi śūdrahatyāyāṃ prakṛtaṃ brahmacaryaṃ sāṃvatsarikam ṛṣabhaikādaśānāṃ gavāṃ ca dānam | tasya ca pūrvavat traividhyam | tad evaṃ ṣaḍvratāny anenātidiśyante | tatra brāhmaṇasya kuṭumbina āhitāgner gaur agnihotrādike 'ṅgabhūtā durbhikṣe kuṭumbinirvāhikā bahukṣīrā bālāpatyā kapilā suśīlā hatā, hantā tu dhanavān nirguṇaḥ kṛtānekaniṣedhātikramas tasya dānasahitaṃ tapaḥ | nirdhanasya tu tapa eva | dhanavatas tapasy asamarthasya dānam eva | asminn eva viṣaye yadi hantādhyayanādiguṇavān ayam eva ca prathamo vyatikramaḥ | tadā tasya śūdrahatyāvrataṃ dānasahitam, tapasy asamarthasya dānam eva |
yadā tūktalakṣaṇām uktalakṣaṇasya svāmino gām ajñānād akāmato hanti tatra vasiṣṭha āha: "gāṃ ced dhanyāt tasyāś carmaṇārdreṇa pariveṣṭitaḥ ṣaṇmāsān kṛcchraṃ taptakṛcchraṃ cātitiṣṭheta | vṛṣabhavehatau dadyātām" iti | dvivacananirdeśād dvayor hantroḥ prāyaścittopadeśo 'yam iti gamyate | tatra balīvardahantuḥ prājāpatyakṛcchānuṣṭhānaṃ ṣāṇmāsikaṃ vṛṣabhadānasahitaṃ vidhīyate | strīgavīhantus tu tadvat taptakṛcchrānuṣṭhānaṃ vehaddānaṃ ca, vṛṣabhaḥ sektā gaur gaur eva garbhaghātinī vehat | yadā tūktaguṇarahitasya svāmina uktaguṇarahitāṃ gāṃ kāmato hanti tatra manur āha |
[1101]        upapātakasaṃyukto goghno māsaṃ yavān pibet |
kṛtavāpo vased goṣṭhe varmaṇā tena saṃvṛtaḥ ||
caturthakālam aśnīyād akṣāralavaṇaṃ mitam |
gomūtreṇācaret snānaṃ dvau māsau niyatendriyaḥ ||
divānugacchet tā gās tu tiṣṭhann ūrdhvaṃ rajaḥ pibet |
śuśrūṣitvā namas kṛtya rātrau vīrāsanaṃ vaset ||
tiṣṭhantiṣv anutiṣṭhet tu vrajantīṣv apy anuvrajet |
āsīnāsu tathāsīta niyato vītamatsaraḥ ||
āturām abhiṣaktāṃ vā cauravyāghrādibhir bhayaiḥ |
patitāṃ paṅklagnāṃ vā sarvaprāṇair vimokṣayet ||
tathā varṣati śīte vā mārute vāti vā bhṛśam |
na kurvītātmanas trāṇaṃ gor akṛtvā tu śaktitaḥ ||
ātmano yadi vānyeṣāṃ gṛhe kṣetre khale 'tha vā |
bhakṣayantīṃ na kathayet pibantaṃ naiva vatsakam ||
anena vidhinā yas tu goghno gā anugacchati |
sa gohatyākṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tribhir māsair vyapohati ||
vṛṣabhaikādaśā gāś ca dadyāt sucaritavrataḥ |
avidyamāne sarvasvaṃ vedavidbhyo nivedayet || iti |
yavān yavāgūkṛtān pibet | caturthakālam aśnīyād ity upoṣito rātrau pāraṇaṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ | mitam atṛptiparyantam | etac ca prathamamāse pratyahaṃ yāvakaṃ pītvā māsadvayaṃ kāryam | dharmāntarāṇi tu sarvāṇi traimāsikāni | stambhabhittyādyavaṣṭambharahitam upaveśanaṃ vīrāsanam | sthānayānāsanānāṃ madhye yad bahvībhiḥ kriyate tad anukuryāt | parasamṛddhyasahanaṃ matsaraḥ | āturā vyādhitā | abhiṣiktā saṃbaddhā, caurādibhir bhayaiḥ | evaṃvidhāṃ gāṃ sarvaprāṇair vimokṣayet | svaprāṇān anapekṣamāṇa ity arthaḥ | atraiva yamena ṣaṣṭhānnakāle bhojanaṃ pratyahaṃ daśādhikasahasrasaṃkhyāko gāyatrījapo vedajapaḥ praṇavajapo gomatijapaś ceti viśeṣa uktaḥ | [gāvaḥ surabhayo nityam ityādiś ca gomatī tenaivoktā | tathā hi ] — [1102]
gāvaḥ surabhayo nityaṃ gāvo guggulugandhikāḥ |
gāvaḥ pratiṣṭhā bhūtānāṃ gāvaḥ svastyayanaṃ mahat ||
annam eva paraṃ gāvo devānāṃ paramaṃ haviḥ |
pāvanaṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ rakṣanti ca vahanti ca ||
haviṣā mantrapūtena tarpayanty amarān divi |
ṛṣīṇām agnihotreṣu gāvo homapravartikāḥ ||
sarveṣām eva bhūtānāṃ gāvaḥ śaraṇam uttamam |
gāvaḥ pavitraṃ paramaṃ gāvo maṅgalam uttamam ||
gāvaḥ svargasya sopānaṃ gāvo dhanyāḥ sanātanāḥ |
namo gobhyaḥ śrīmatībhyaḥ saurabheyībhya eva ca ||
namo brahmasutābhyaś ca pavitrābhyo namo namaḥ || iti |
eṣa ca viśeṣo mānave 'pi vrate kāryaḥ sarvaśākhāpratyayanyāyena | evaṃ smṛtyantaroktā apy aviruddhā viśeṣā grāhyāḥ | viruddhās tu pāpānubandhabhūyastve guravaḥ kalpāḥ kāryāḥ | tallāghave tu laghavaḥ | aṅgirasā ca snānādyācamanakriyā jalenaiva kāryety uktam | [pādaśaucagrahaṇaṃ tatratyasakalajalasādhyakriyopalakṣaṇārtham| ] tathā ca brahmacārivad daṇḍadhāraṇaṃ viśeṣa uktaḥ | yat tu śaṅkhenoktam,
pādonaṃ śūdrahatyāyām udakyāgrahaṇe tathā |
govadhe tu tathā kuryāt parastrīgamane tathā ||
iti, tat prayojayitur anumantuś ca draṣṭavyam | baudhāyanaḥ: "śūdravadhena strīvadho govadhaś ca vyākhyātaḥ | anyatrātreyyāḥ | dhenvanḍuhoś ca | dhenvanaḍuhor ante cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret" | anye prāyaścittānte | dhenur navaprasūtā gauḥ | viśvāmitraḥ |
kṛcchrāṃs tu caturaḥ kuryād govadhe buddhipūrvake |
amatyā tu dvayaṃ kāryaṃ tadardhaṃ vṛddhabālayoḥ ||
strīśūdrayor evam eva tadvadhe ca na saṃśayaḥ |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ vā sarveṣu nimitteṣu vidhīyate || [1103]
kṛcchrāś ca prājāpatyādyā vakṣyamāṇalakṣaṇāḥ | na punaḥ prājāpatya evābhasanīyaḥ | saṃkhyayābhinnaviṣayatvāt | etac ca mānavavratāsamarthaviṣayam | cyavanaḥ: "prājāpatyadvayaṃ gohatyāprāyaścittaṃ rodhanabandhanayoktravadhe pādavṛddhiḥ | nakhāni romāṇi śikhāvarjaṃ vapanaṃ saśikhaṃ triṣavaṇaṃ gavām anugamanaṃ saha śayanaṃ sumahattṛṇāni ca rathyāsu cārayed vratānte brāhmaṇabhojanaṃ daśa gā vṛṣabhaikādaśā dadyāt | rodhane vikartanam, bandhane romavapanam, vadhe saśikhaṃ vapanam" | yamaḥ |
yaṣṭyā ūrdhvaṃ prahāreṇa yadi gā vinipātayet |
dviguṇaṃ tu caret tatra prāyaścittam iti sthitiḥ ||
parāśaraḥ |
adakṣiṇaṃ vrataṃ nāsti hiṃsāyāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ |
na strīṇāṃ keśavapanaṃ na durāc chayanaṃ tathā ||
na ca goṣṭhe vased ratrau na divā samanuvrajet |
nadīṣu saṃgame caiva parvateṣu viśeṣataḥ ||
na strīṇāṃ vijane vāso vratam evaṃ samādiśet |
sarvān keśān samuddhṛtya chedayed aṅguladvayam ||
evam eva tu nārīṇāṃ śiraso muṇḍanaṃ smṛtam ||
saṃvartaḥ |
pāde 'ṅgaromavapanaṃ dvipāde śmaśrukartanam |
tripāde tu śikhā dhāryā saśikhaṃ tu nipātane ||
pāde vastradvayaṃ dadyād dvipāde kāṃsyabhājanam |
pādahīne tu gām ekāṃ mithunaṃ tu nipātane ||
bhuktavatsu ca vipreṣu gāṃ pradadyād vicakṣaṇaḥ |
gor abhāve tu dātavyaṃ gomūlyaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
nṛpo govṛṣabhaṃ datvā keśārthaṃ prārthayed dvijān |
rodhe gomithunaṃ dadyād bandhe govṛṣabhaṃ tathā ||
vastrāṇi yojane dadyād vṛṣabhaikādaśā vadhe |
gāvaḥ sāṃtapane pañca prājāpatye tu gotrayam ||
taptakṛcchre tathaivāṣṭāv atikṛcchre trayodaśa || [1104]
śaṅkhaḥ |
auṣadhaṃ sneham āhāraṃ dadyād gobrāhmaṇeṣu ca |
prāṇināṃ prāṇavṛttyarthaṃ prāyaścittaṃ na vidyate ||
sumantuḥ: "snehāhārā(ra)bhiṣakkriyātyayeṣu sarvaprāṇināṃ sadoṣaḥ" | vyāsaḥ |
auṣadhaṃ lavaṇaṃ snehaṃ piṇyākaṃ bhojanaṃ tathā |
atiriktaṃ na dātavyaṃ keśe tulyaṃ tu dāpayet ||
atiriktavipannānāṃ kṛcchrapādo vidhīyate | ]
sneham aṣṭapalaṃ dadyāt kṣīraṃ dadyāt tu ṣoḍaśa ||
dvātriṃśac caiva pānīyaṃ saiṣā mātrā vidhīyate |
mātropetaṃ tu dātavyaṃ nātiriktaṃ kadācana ||
atiriktapradānena prāyaścittaṃ vidhīyate ||
yamaḥ |
niśi bandhaniruddheṣu vyāghrasarpahateṣu ca |
agnividyudvipanneṣu prāyaścittaṃ na vidyate ||
etac ca yathāśāstrabandhanādau draṣṭavyam |
atha tatra bhavec chaṅkā sāyaṃ saṃyopite mṛte |
gomūtreṇa tu saṃmiśraṃ trirātraṃ yāvakaṃ pibet ||
asthibhaṅgaṃ gavāṃ kṛtvā kaṭibhaṅgaṃ tathaiva ca |
yadi jīvati ṣaṇmāsān prāyaścittaṃ na vidyate ||
[asthibhaṅgādinimittaṃ prāyaścittaṃ bhavaty eva | na govadhaprāyaścitaam ity arthaḥ]
yantraṇe gocikitsārthe gūḍhagarbhavimocane |
yatne kṛte vipattiḥ syāt prāyaścittaṃ na vidyate ||
dāhacchedaśirābhedaprayatnair upakurvatām |
dvijānāṃ gohitārthāya prāyaścittaṃ na vidyate ||
kūpakhāte bhramann eva gṛhadāhe vipadyate |
grāmakhāte tathā cauraiḥ prāyaścittaṃ na vidyate ||
auṣadhe tu na doṣo 'sti svecchayā yadi tat pibet |
anyathā dīyamāne tu prāyaścittaṃ vidhīyate ||
asamyagvaidya ity arthaḥ | [1105] parāśaraḥ |
tailabheṣajapānena bhojanena dvijanmanām |
viśalyena ca garbhāṇāṃ vipanne nāsti pātakam ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
śastrādinā tu hatvā gāṃ mānavaṃ vratam ācaret |
rodhādinā cāṅgirasam āpastamboktam eva vā ||
pādaṃ cared rodhavadhe kṛcchrārdhaṃ bandhaghātane |
pādaṃ cared rodhavadhe kṛcchrārdhaṃ bandhaghātane ||
ativāhyeṣu pādonaṃ kṛcchram ajñānatāḍane || 3.264, 265 ||
anyeṣūpapatakeṣu prāyaścittāny upadeśātideśābhyām āha |
upapātakaśuddhiḥ syād evaṃ cāndrāyanena vā |
payasā vāpi māsena parākeṇātha vā punaḥ || 3.266 ||
upapātakānāṃ vrātyatādīnām evaṃ govadhaśuddhiprakāreṇa śuddhiḥ syāt | yad vā cāndrāyaṇena māsaparimitena payovratena vā | atha vā parākeṇa | cāndrāyaṇaparākayoḥ svarūpaṃ vakṣyate | pañcagavyapānādeḥ prāyaścittacatuṣṭayasya govadhaśuddhāv eva vopadeśo nopapātakāntaraśuddhau kiṃ tu tatrātideśaḥ | yadi hi sarvatropadeśaḥ syāt tadā gocarmasaṃvaraṇagoṣṭhaśayanagavānugamanādayo dharmā na bādhyeran } na hy upadiṣṭaṃ bādhyate | atidiṣṭatve govadhaśuddhisvarūpayuktānām anyatra bādhaḥ | yathā pārvaṇahomayor darśapūrṇamāsasvarūpayuktayos tadvikṛtiṣu ca bādhaḥ | ayaṃ cākāmakṛta upapātake prāyaścittātideśo draṣṭavyaḥ | kāmakṛte tu mānavaḥ |
etad eva vrataṃ kuryur upapātakino dvijāḥ |
avakīrṇavarjaṃ śuddhyarthaṃ cāndrāyaṇam athāpi vā || iti |
tat traimāsikaṃ hy atrātide(di)śyate | yeṣv api copapātakeṣu prāyaścittaviśeṣa upadiśyate teṣv api
upapātakaśuddhiḥ syād evaṃ cāndrāyaṇena vā |
ityādinā sāmānyapravṛttenāpi vākyena govadhaprāyaścittādivratagaṇaḥ prāpyate | tatra sāmānyaviśeṣanyāyena bādhyabādhakabhāvo grāhyaḥ | tathā sati teṣām upapātakagaṇe pāṭho 'narthaḥ syāt | tasmāt sāmānyavihitāni viśeṣavihitāni ca kalpyante | ete pañcagavyapānādayaḥ prāyaścittakalpāḥ kartṛsāmānyānusāreṇa vyavasthāpanīyāḥ || 3.266 ||
uktaṃ sādhāraṇam upapātakaprāyaścittam | idānīm upapātakaviśeṣeṣu [1106] kṣatriyavaiśyaśūdrastrīvadhātmakeṣu prāyaścittaveśeṣān āha |
vṛṣabhaikasahasrā gā dadyāt kṣatravadhe pumān |
brahmahatyāvrataṃ vāpi vatsaratritayaṃ caret || 3.267 ||
vaiśyahābdaṃ cared etad dadyād vaikaśataṃ gavām |
ṣaṇmāsāñ śūdrahāpy etad dhenūr dadyād daśāpi vā || 3.268 ||
ekam adhikaṃ yasmin sahasre tad ekasahasram | ekasahasrasya pūraṇa ekasahasraḥ | vṛṣabha ekasahasro yāsāṃ tā vṛṣabhaikasahasrā gāvaḥ | tāḥ kṣatriyavadhe pumān dadyāt | brahmahatyāvrataṃ vā trīn vatsarān kuryāt | vaiśyaghātakaḥ punar abdaṃ brahmahatyāvrataṃ caret | gavām ekaśataṃ vā dadyāt | śūdrasya tu hantā ṣaṇmāsān brahmahavrataṃ kuryāt | daśa vā dhenūr dadyāt | etad akāmato brāhmaṇasya vṛttasthamātrakṣatriyavadhakāriṇo draṣṭavyam | tathā ca manuḥ |
turīyo brahmahatyāyāḥ kṣatriyasya vadhe smṛtaḥ |
vaiśye 'ṣṭamo 'ṃśo vṛttasthe śūdre jñeyas tu ṣoḍaśaḥ ||
akāmatas tu rājanyaṃ vinipātya dvijottamaḥ |
pramāpya vaiśyaṃ vṛttasthaṃ dadyād vaikaśataṃ gavām ||
etad eva vrataṃ kṛtsnaṃ ṣaṇmāsāñ śūdrahā caret |
vṛṣabhaikādaśā vāpi dadyād viprāya gāḥ sitāḥ || iti |
turīyaś caturthaḥ | vṛttastha iti kṣatriyādinā sarveṇa saṃbadhyate | vṛttaṃ ca smṛtyartara uktam |
gurupūjā ghṛṇā śaucaṃ satyamindriyanigrahaḥ |
pravartate hitānāṃ ca tat sarvaṃ vratam(vṛttam) ucyate ||
kāmakṛtakṣatriyādivadhaviṣaye gautama āha: "rājanyavadhe ṣaḍvārṣikaṃ prākṛtaṃ brahmacaryaṃ vṛṣabhaikasahasrā gā dadyāt" iti | yat tv āhatuḥ śaṅkhalikhitau: "amatipūrvaṃ caturṣu varṇeṣu pramāpya dvādaśasaṃvatsarān ṣaṭ trīn ekaṃ ca vratāny ādiśet teṣām ante gosahasraṃ tato 'rdhaṃ tasyārdham ardhaṃ ca dadyāt | sarveṣām ānupūrvyeṇa" iti, tad guṇavattaravarṇavadhe draṣṭavyam | yat tu vṛddhahārītenoktam, [1107]
trīyonaṃ kṣatriyasya vadhe brahmahaṇi vratam |
ardhaṃ vaiśyavadhe kuryāt turīyaṃ vṛṣalasya tu ||
iti, tad brāhmaṇasyātuguṇavatkṣatriyādiviṣayam | etasminn eva viṣaye vasiṣṭho 'py āha: "evaṃ rājanyaṃ hatvāṣṭau varṣāṇi, ṣaḍ vaiśyaṃ trīṇi śūdram" iti | evam iti brahmahavratātideśārtham | kṣatriyavarṇe hantur guṇavattvaṃ cātra kalpyam | yad vā jātimātrakṣatriyādiviṣayam evedaṃ hārītavasiṣṭhoktaṃ dānanirapekṣaṃ vratadvayam | viṣṇuḥ: "nṛpativadhe mahāvratam etaddviguṇaṃ kāryam | pādonaṃ kṣatriyavadhe, ardhaṃ vaiśyavadhe, sarveṣu śavaśirodhvajī syāt" | nṛpatir janapadagoptā kṣatriyaḥ | mahāvrataṃ dvādaśavārṣikaṃ brahmahavratam | saṃvartaḥ |
pramāpya kṣatriyaṃ tv eva tribhiḥ kṛcchair viśudhyati |
kuryāc caivānapūrvyeṇa trīn kṛcchrān susamāhitaḥ ||
vaiśyahatyāṃ tu saṃprāpya kathaṃcit kāmamohitaḥ |
kṛcchrātikṛcchrau kurvīta sa naro vaiśyaghātakaḥ ||
kuryāc chūdravadhaṃ prāpya taptakṛcchraṃ samāhitaḥ ||
ānupūrvyaṃ tu kṛcchro 'tikṛcchras taptakṛcchra iti | etac ca guṇavattare hantary amatipūrvake ca vadhe draṣṭavyam | jātimātrakṣatriyādivadhe tūpapātakasāmānyaprāyaścittaṃ kāryam |
upapātakāntareṣv api vaiśeṣikaprāyaścittāni smṛtyantaroktāni pradarśyante | tatra manuḥ |
yeṣāṃ dvijānāṃ sāvitrī nānūcyeta yathāvidhi |
tāṃś cārayitvā trīn kṛcchrān yathāvidhy upanāyayet ||
trīn kṛcchrān iti prājāpatyakṛcchrātikṛcchrān ity arthaḥ |
prāyaścittaṃ cikīrṣanti vikarmasthāś ca ye dvijāḥ |
brahmaṇā ca parityaktās teṣām apy etad ādiśet ||
yamaḥ |
sāvitrī patitā yasya daśa varṣāṇi pañca ca |
saśikhaṃ vapanaṃ kṛtvā vrataṃ kuryāt samāhitaḥ ||
ekaviṃśatirātraṃ ca pibet prasṛtiyāvakam |
haviṣā bhojayec caiva brahmaṇān pañca sapta vā || [1108]
tato yāvakaśuddhasya tasyopanayanaṃ smṛtam ||
etad ubhayam api samānaviṣayam | yat tu vasiṣṭhenoktam: "patitasāvitrīka uddālakavrataṃ caret | dvau māsau yāvakena vartayet | māsaṃ payasā, ardhamāsam āmikṣayā, aṣṭarātraṃ ghṛtena, ṣaḍrātram ayācitena, trirātram abbhakṣo 'horātram upavased aśvamedhāvabhṛthaṃ vā gacched vrātyastomena vā yajeta" iti, tad anāpadi buddhipūrvakaṃ cirakālaṃ vrātyatayāvasthitasya draṣṭavyam | anāpady anupanītasya ṣoḍaśavarṣād ūrdhvaṃ cirakālāvasthānarahitasya mānavaṃ traimāsikam upapātakasāmānyavratam | tadasamarthasya yāmyaṃ yāvakāśanam | prājāpatyādikṛcchratrayaṃ mānavam | tatrāpy asamarthasya cāndrāyaṇaṃ sarvopapātakasādhāraṇam | anuśāsakapitrādikavirahe tv amatipūrvake sāvitryatikrame parāko māsikaṃ payovrataṃ vā | āpastambaḥ: "atikrānte sāvitryāḥ kāla ṛtuṃ traividyakaṃ brahmavaryaṃ cared athopanayanaṃ tataḥ saṃvatsaram udakopasparśanam athādhyāpyaḥ | atha yasya[pitṛ]pitāmahāv anupanītau syātāṃ te brahmahasaṃstutās teṣām abhigamanaṃ bhojanaṃ vivāham ativarjayet teṣām icchatāṃ prāyaścittam | yathā prathame 'tikrama ṛtur eva saṃvatsareṇopanayanam | tatrodakopasparśanaṃ pratipuruṣaṃ saṃkhyāya saṃvatsarān yāvato 'nupanītāḥ syuḥ saptabhiḥ pāvamānībhir  ya(va?)danti yac ca dūraka ity etābhir jayuṣpavitreṇa sāmapavitreṇāṅgirasenety api vyāhṛtibhir evāthādhyāpyaḥ | yasya prapitāmahāder nānusmaryata upanayanaṃ te śmaśānasaṃbhūtās teṣām abhyāgamanaṃ bhojanaṃ vivāham ativarjayet teṣām icchatāṃ prāyaścittaṃ dvādaś varṣāṇi brahmacaryaṃ caret | athopanayanam | tatrodakopasparśanaṃ pāvamānyādibhir atha gṛhamedhopadeśanaṃ tasmād yo nivartate tasya saṃskāro yathā prathame sāvitrīkālātikrame tata ūrdhvaṃ prakṛtavat" | [1109] asyārthaḥ — atikrānte sāvitryāḥ kāla upanayanakāle viprādīnāṃ ṣoḍaśadvāviṃśativarṣātmaka ṛtuṃ māsadvayaṃ yāvat traividyakaṃ vedatrayasaṃbandhi brahmacaryaṃ bhaikṣāśanaguruśuśrūṣādikaṃ caret | vedatrayārtham ṛtutrayaṃ brahmacaryaṃ cared iti | atha ṛtutrayabrahmacaryānantaram upanayanam | tataḥ saṃvatsaraṃ yāvad udakopasparśanam | pratyahaṃ snānaṃ tadanantaram adhyāpyaḥ | yasya tu vrātyasya [pitṛ]pitāmahau syātāṃ vrātyau te trayo 'pi brahmahaśabdavācyāḥ | teṣāṃ gamanaṃ tān prati jñātikāryasiddhyarthaṃ gamanaṃ bhojanavivāhau ca varjayet | teṣāṃ śuddhim icchatāṃ vakṣyamāṇaṃ prāyaścittam | ṛtoḥ snāne saṃvatsaraḥ | tena pratipuruṣaṃ vatsaratrayaṃ brahmacaryaṃ kāryam | athopanayanaṃ tataḥ saṃvatsaram udakopasparśanam, tatra mantrāḥ: "yadanti yac ca dūrake" iti sapta, "yena devāḥ pavitreṇa" iti yajuṣpavitram | "kayā naś citra ābhuvat" iti sāmapavitram | "haṃsaḥ śuciṣat" ityāṅgirasam, etaiḥ pratyahaṃ saṃvatsaraṃ yāvad udakopasparśanaṃ vyāhṛtibhir vā | ete ca mantrāḥ prāyaścittārtham adhyetavyāḥ | kṛtaprāyaścitto 'dhyāpyaḥ | yasya tu prapitāmahāder upanayanaṃ na smaryate, tatrārthād eteṣām api puruṣāṇām anupanītatvaṃ te sarve śmaśānavad aśucayaḥ | teṣv āgateṣv abhyutthānaṃ bhojanaṃ ca varjayed āpady api na kuryād ity arthaḥ | teṣāṃ svayam eva śuddhim icchatāṃ prāyaścittam upadeśyaṃ dvādaśavārṣikaṃ traividhyaṃ(vidyaṃ) brahmacaryam iti | prāyaścittānantaram upanayanaṃ tadanantaraṃ saṃvatsaram udakopasparśanaṃ pūrvoktair mantrais tato nādhyāpanaṃ kiṃ tu gārhasthyopadeśanam, tata upadiṣṭagārhasthyāt puruṣād yo nirvartate jāyate tasya saṃskāro yathā prathame sāvitrīkālātikrame | tata ūrdhvaṃ prakṛtivad anatikrāntasāvitrīkālavat saṃvyavahāryatā | aṅgirāḥ |
vrātyastomair yājayitvā dvijātīn sarva eva hi |
niṣkṛtyartham idaṃ kṛtvā yathāvidhy upanāyayet ||
atha streyopapātake viśeṣaprāyaścittāni, tatra manuḥ |
dhānyānnadhanacauryāṇi kṛtvā kāmād dvijottamaḥ |
svajātīyagṛhād eva kṛcchrābdena viśudhyati ||
dvijottamo vipras tasya vipra eva svajātīyaḥ | tataś ca viprasya viprasaṃbandhidhānyādyapahartuḥ kṛcchrābdaḥ | tatra ca dhanaśabdena suvarṇād anyat sāradravyam ucyate, dhānyaṃ ca daśakumbhaparimitaṃ jñeyam, prāyaścittamahattvāt | kumbhaḥ pañcasahasrapalaparimāṇaḥ | kṣatriyāder api svajātisaṃbandhidhānyādyapahāre navaṣaṭtrimāsaparimitā [1110] kṛcchracaryā vidheyā | aśaktasyānubandhādyapekṣayā tūpapātakasādhāraṇatraimāsikādy api kalpyam | tathā |
manuṣyāṇāṃ ca haraṇe strīṇāṃ kṣetragṛhasya ca |
kūpavāpījalānāṃ ca śuddhiś cāndrayaṇena tu ||
manuṣyāṇām iti grahaṇe saty api strīṇām iti pṛthagupādānaṃ puruṣaharaṇena strīharaṇasya tulyadoṣatvakhyāpanārtham | anyathā hi hiṃsāvad dharaṇe 'pi vaiṣamyaṃ syāt | kūpavāpībhyāṃ jalaviśeṣaṇaṃ ghaṭādijalavyāvṛttyartham | tena sakalavāpīkūpāpahāre prāyaścittam idam | kṣatriyādisaṃbandhimanuṣyādyapahāra etad draṣṭavyam | brāhmaṇasaṃbandhinaś ca manuṣyāder apahāro rukmasteyasamatvenoktaḥ,
nikṣepasyāpaharaṇaṃ narāśvarajatasya ca |
bhūmivajramaṇīnāṃ ca rukmasteyasamaṃ smṛtam ||
ity atra | tathā |
pañca bhojyāpaharaṇe yānaśayyāsanasya ca |
puṣpamūlaphalānāṃ ca pañcagavyaṃ viśodhanam ||
[pañcagavyam āhārāntaram akurvato viśodhanam|] paiṭhīnasiḥ: "bhakṣyabhojyānnasyodarapūraṇamātraharaṇe trirātram ekarātraṃ vā pañcagavāhāratā" | haraṇīyadravyālpatvabhūyastvābhyām ekarātratrirātraviṣayaḥ pañcagavyāhāravidhir draṣṭavyaḥ | rātriśabdaś cāhorātropalakṣaṇārthaḥ | dhānyānnadhanacauryāṇīty atrānnaśabdo bahutarānnaviṣayaḥ prāyaścittabahutvāt | manuḥ |
tṛṇakāṣṭhadrumāṇāṃ ca śuṣkānnasya guḍasya ca |
tailacarmāmiṣāṇāṃ ca trirātraṃ syād abhojanam ||
puruṣāhāratrayaparimitamūlalabhyatṛṇādyapahāra etat | tathā |
maṇimuktāpravālānāṃ tāmrasya rajatasya ca |
ayaskāṃsyopalānāṃ ca dvādaśāhaṃ kaṇānnatā || [1111]
maṇyādīnāṃ brāhmaṇasaṃbandhinām alpamūlyānām apahāre dvādaśāhaṃ taṇḍulānnatvavidhiḥ | tathā |
kārpāsakīṭajīrṇānāṃ dviśaphaikaśaphasya ca |
pakṣigandhauṣadhīnāṃ ca rajjvāś caiva tryahaṃ payaḥ ||
viṣṇuḥ: "pakṣigandhauṣadhirajjuvidalānām apahāre dinam upavaset" | jābālaḥ |
aśvagobhūmikanyāś ca hṛtvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret |
pakvānnm auṣadhaṃ tailaṃ śayyāṃ vāsa upānahau ||
kāṃsyāyastāmrasīmaṃ vā abjaṃ kṛcchrārdhm ācaret |
udake phalamūle ca puṣpaparṇasugandhiṣu |
mṛdbhāṇḍamadhumāṃseṣu kṛcchrapādo vidhīyate ||
abjaṃ śaṅkhaśuktyādi | śaṅkhaḥ |
yasya yasya tu varṇasy vṛtticchedaṃ samācaret |
tasya tasya vadhe proktaṃ prayaścittaṃ samācaret ||
apahṛtya tu varṇānāṃ bhūmiḥ vipraḥ pramādataḥ |
pāyaścittaṃ vadhaṃ proktaṃ brāhmaṇāumate caret ||
gajāśvasyāpaharaṇe maṇīnāṃ rajatasya ca |
dhanāpaharaṇe caiva kuryāt saṃvatsaravratam ||
tiladhānyānnavastrāṇāṃ śayyānām āmiṣasya ca |
saṃvatsarārdhaṃ kurvīta vratam etat samāhitaḥ ||
kṣudrān paśūṃś cāpahrtya prājāpatyaṃ samācaret |
guḍakārpāsadhānyāni sarpir lavaṇam eva ca ||
sarvatra śaṅkhavākyena vrataśabdo gomūtrayāvakāhāravacanaḥ | hārītaḥ: "rajatastainye cāndrāyaṇam atikṛcchram, tāmre kārṣnāyase prājāpatyam" | sarvam eva steyaprāyaścittam apahṛtaṃ dravyaṃ tatsvāmine pradāyaiva kāryam ity āha viṣṇuḥ | [1112]
datvaivāpahṛtaṃ dravyaṃ dhanikasyābhyupāyataḥ |
prāyaścittaṃ tataḥ kuryāt kaśmalasyāpanuttaye ||
sīsakaṃ dviguṇaṃ vāpi śaktyā vāpi caturguṇam |
datvā steyī caret kṛcchraṃ prājāpatyaṃ viśuddhaye ||
prājāpatyagrahaṇam uktaprāyaścittopalakṣaṇārtham |
yad vā tad vā paradravyam apahṛtyātha kilbiṣī |
ṛṇād dviguṇam utsṛjya kṛcchrārdhāc chuddhim āpnuyāt ||
ajāvibarhiḥkārpāsatāmradhānyatilādikam |
caturguṇaṃ dhanaṃ datvā prājāpatyaṃ samācaret ||
gohartus taptakṛcchraṃ syād ardhaṃ hastyaśvahārake |
suvarṇarajate dānaṃ śaktasyaikādaśottaram ||
cāndrāyaṇaṃ ca śuddhyarthaṃ dānaṃ deyaṃ ca śaktitaḥ |
kāṃsyarītyāyase tāmre dviguṇaṃ steyamokṣaṇam ||
prājāpatyārdhabhāgaś ca prāyaścittaṃ viśodhanam |
iti steyaprāyaścittāni |
atha ṛṇānapa(pā)kriyāprāyaścittāni | tatra ṛṇaprastutayajñaprajādhyayanānām akaraṇe tathā "putrapautrair ṛṇaṃ deyam" ityādidharmaśāstravihitam ṛṇāpa(pā)karaṇam, tasya cānapākaraṇe śaktyanusāreṇa govadhaprāyaścittacāndrāyaṇādīnāṃ sādhāraṇopapātakaprāyaścittānām ekaṃ kāryam | tatra vyatikramakālālpatvabhūyastvādyapekṣayā tadviśeṣo 'dhyavaseyaḥ | evam ādhānādhikāre sati tadakaraṇe sādhāraṇam upapātakaprāyaścittaṃ kāryam | tatrādhikāritve saty agnyādheyam akurvataḥ saṃvatsarād ūrdhvam upapātakavratam | apūrṇe vatsare kārṣṇājinir viśeṣam āha |
kāle tv ādhāya karmaṇi kuryād vipro vidhānataḥ |
tad akurvaṃs trirātreṇa māsi māsi viśudhyati ||
anāhitāgnau pitrādau yakṣyamāṇaḥ suto yadi |
sa hi vrātyena vidhinā yajet tanniṣkrayāya tu (niṣkriyārthakaḥ) ||
āvasathyānādhāne 'pi sa evāha |
kṛtadāro gṛhe jyeṣṭho yo nādadyād dhutāśanam |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ cared varṣaṃ pratimāsam aho 'pi vā || [1113]
varṣātikrame cāndrāyaṇam | māsātikrame 'har upavāsaḥ | athāpaṇyavikrayaprāyaścittāni | tatra hārītaḥ: "guḍatilamūlaphalapakvānnavikraye somāyanam | lākṣālavaṇamadhumāṃsatailakṣīra-dadhighṛtatakragandhacandanakuvāsasām ekatamavikraye cāndrāyaṇam | tathorṇikeśikesari-bhūdhenvanaḍudveśmaśastravikraye ca | bhakṣyamāṃsasnāyvasthiśṛṅganakhaśuktivikraye taptakṛcchraḥ | hiṅguguggulasajjarasaharitālamanaḥśilāñjanagairikakṣāralavaṇamaṇimauktika-pravāladantaśaṅkhavetravaiṇavamṛnmayavikrayeṣu ca | ārāmataḍāgodapānapuṣkariṇīpūrta-svakṛtavikrayeṣu ca triṣavaṇasnāyy adhaḥśāyī caturthakālabhuk saṃvatsareṇa pūto bhavati | hīnamānonmānamāpane saṃkīrṇavikraye ca" iti | somāyanasvarūpaṃ vakṣyate | ūrṇino meṣādyāḥ | keśinaś camarādyāḥ | kesariṇo 'śvādyāḥ | mānaṃ prasthādi | unmānaṃ tulādi | tair dhānyādisaṃkīrṇaṃ miśritam | prājāpatyaṃ cared ity anuvṛttau śaṅkhalikhitau: "śastrabandhanabhūmyekaśaphavikraye caitad eva | gṛhapatiśrayodyānārāmasabhāprapā-taḍākpūjyasetuvikrayaṃ kṛtvā taptakṛcchram ācaret" | bandhanaṃ nigaḍādi | udyānāgameti pāṭha āgamo vedādiḥ | tathā: "na vikrīṇīyād avikreyāṇi | tilatailadadhikṣaudralavaṇadrākṣāmadya-māṃsakṛtānnastrīpuruṣahastyaśvagovṛṣabhagandharasakṣaumakṛṣṇājinasomodakanīlīvikrayāt sadyaḥ patati brāhmaṇaḥ | tatra prāyaścittam akāmāvāptau | kāmakṛte 'py eke, patito hy ātmānam uddharet saṃvatsaraṃ taptakṛcchram ācaret | kṛtavapanaḥ śucir apo 'bhyupeyāt triḥ | ekārdravāsāḥ kāṣṭhamaunī vīrāsanam āsīta rātrau | divā tiṣṭhed yukto 'gnikāryaparaḥ sāvitrīṃ savanānugāṃ japet | svayaṃ vratam abhyupagamya kuśacīravāsā mauñjī medhalī daṇḍī pavitrapāṇir haviṣyaṃ vā śrapayec caruṃ bhaikṣam aduṣṭaṃ vā yātrikam aśnīyāt | dvādaśarātraṃ copavāsaḥ | brāhmaṇatarpaṇaṃ [1114] gavāhnikam ity evaṃ vyavahāryaḥ | anyathā patitaḥ śiṣṭair asaṃbhāṣyaḥ śūdradharmā kāṣṭhamaunī hastasaṃjñābhir api vākkāyaṃ na kuryāt" | tathā: "ārdrauṣadhipuṣpamūlaphalaśākacarmavetravidalatuṣakapālakeśabhasmāsthikilāṭakūrcikā-kṣīravikāraviśeṣāḥ" | caturviṃśatimatāt |
surāyā vikrayaṃ kṛtvā caret saumyacatuṣṭayam |
lākṣālavaṇamāṃsānāṃ carec cāndrāyaṇadvayam ||
payaḥpāyasapūpānāṃ carec cāndrāyaṇaṃ vratam |
dadhnaś cekṣurasasyaiva guḍatakrādivikraye ||
sarveṣāṃ snehapakvānāṃ parākaṃ tu vinirdiśet |
kadalaṃ nārikelaṃ ca nāgaraṃ bījapūrakam ||
eteṣāṃ pādakṛcchraṃ syāj jambīrādeś tathaiva ca |
kastūrikādigandhānāṃ vikraye kṛcchram ācaret ||
karpūrādes tadardhaṃ syād dinaṃ hiṅgvādivikraye |
tilānāṃ vikrayaṃ kṛtvā prājāpatyaṃ samācaret ||
dharmārthaṃ kṛmijātāṃś ca yajñārthaṃ vikrayaṃ tyajet |
raktapītādivastrāṇi kṛṣṇādīni tathaiva ca ||
vikraye tryaham eteṣāṃ gargasya vacanaṃ yathā |
govikrayaṃ naraḥ kṛtvā lābhārthaṃ dhanamohitaḥ ||
prājāpatyaṃ prakurvīta gajānām aindavaṃ smṛtam |
kharāśvatarāṇāṃ ca karabhāṇāṃ ca vikraye ||
parākaṃ tatra kurvīta sutānāṃ dviguṇaṃ caret |
nārīṇāṃ vikrayaṃ kṛtvā carec cāndrāyaṇaṃ vratam ||
dviguṇaṃ puruṣasyaiva vratam āhur manīṣiṇaḥ |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ prakurvīta ekāhād vedavikraye ||
aṅgānāṃ tu parākaṃ syāt smṛtīnāṃ kṛcchram ācaret |
itihāsapurāṇānāṃ caret sāṃtapanaṃ dvijaḥ ||
rahasyapāñcarātrāṇāṃ kṛcchram evaṃ samācaret |
gāthānāṃ nītiśāstrāṇāṃ prākṛtānāṃ tathaiva ca || [1115]
sarvāsām eva vidyānāṃ parṇakṛcchraṃ samācaret ||
viṣṇuḥ: "prāṇibhūpuṇyasomavikrayī taptakṛcchraṃ kuryāt | ārdrauṣadhimūlaphalacarmavetra-vidalatuṣakapālakeśabhasmāsthigorasapiṇyākatilatailavikrayī prājāpatyam | śleṣmajatumadhūcchiṣṭaśaṅkhaśuktitrapusīsakṛṣṇalohoḍa(du)mbarakhaḍgapātravikraye mahāsāṃtapanam" | śleṣmā carmavikāraḥ, loke dṛḍhaka it prasiddhaḥ | "māṃsalavaṇalākṣā-kṣīravikraye cāndrāyaṇaṃ kuryāt | taṃ ca bhūyaś copanayet" | śaṅkhaḥ |
dhārayitvā tulāṃ vakrāṃ viṣamaṃ kārayan vaṇik |
surālavaṇamadyānāṃ kṛtvā kṣīrasya vikrayam ||
lākṣāyāś caiva māṃsasya kuryād ardhaṃ mahāvratam |
vikretā prāṇinām abdaṃ tilasya ca tathā caret ||
bhūyo'bhyāsaviṣayam etat | mahāvrataṃ dvādaśavārṣikam | etāni viśeṣaprāyaścittāni sarvopapātakasādhāraṇaprāyaścittair vikalpyamānāni jātiguṇānubandhādyapekṣayā vyavasthāpanīyāni |
atha parivedanaprāyaścittāni, tatra vasiṣṭhaḥ: "parivittiḥ kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caritvā nirviśeta tāṃ copayacchet" | akṛtavivāhe 'grajo 'nujena prāg ātmano vivāhaṃ kurvatātikrāntaḥ sa parivittiḥ | sa ca kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ prājāpatyaṃ caritvā nirviśetodvaheta, tāṃ ca kanīyasā pūrvapariṇītāṃ tena dattām upayaccheta, svī kuryād iti yāvat | tasyaiva sā bhāryā nānujasya | aśāstrīyatvāt tadvivāhasya, bhāryātvaṃ ca śāstrīyavivāhajanyam | kiṃ ca: "parividānaḥ krcchātikṛcchrau caritvā tasmai datvā punar niveśeta" | ayam arthaḥ — anujo vyutkrameṇa vivāham ācaran parividāna ucyate, sa kṛcchrātikṛcchrau vakṣyamānalakṣaṇau caritvā jyeṣṭhāya tasmai vyutkramoḍhāṃ datvā punar niveśetānyām udvahed iti | etac ca deśāntaravyavasthite jyeṣṭhe draṣṭavyam | alpatvāt prāyaścittasya | yatra guṇavati(tā) jyeṣṭhena vāryamāṇaḥ karoti, tatrāha hārītaḥ: [1116] "jyeṣṭhe 'nirviṣṭe kanīyān nirviśamānaḥ parivettā, parivitto jyeṣṭhaḥ, parivedanī kanyā, paridāyī dātā, parikartā yājakaḥ, sarve te patitāḥ saṃvatsaraṃ prājāpatyena kṛcchreṇa pārayeyuḥ | samāpte 'bde tāṃ kanyāṃ jyeṣṭhāyopapādayeyuḥ | tām anupanayan kanīyān anyathā nirviśeta, evaṃ dharma na lupyate" iti | nirguṇe jyeṣṭhe buddhipūrvake parivedane śaṅkha āha |
parivittiḥ parivettā yayā ca parividyate |
taddātā yājakaś caiva kuryāc cāndrāyaṇaṃ vratam || iti |
yamaḥ |
dve kṛcchre parivittes tu kanyāyāḥ kṛcchram eva tat |
atikṛcchraṃ cared dātā hotā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret ||
