yat tu śaṅkhenoktam: "parivittiḥ parivettā ca saṃvatsaraṃ brāhmaṇagṛheṣu bhaikṣaṃ careyātām" iti, tad dāraparivedane 'gniparivedane ca sati draṣṭavyam | bhaikṣāśanaṃ ca tṛptiparyantaṃ na kāryam | tapastvāt | yat tu sumantunoktam: "parivittiparivetttṛkanyādātṛyājakānāṃ dvādaśarātraṃ saktupānaṃ brāhmaṇatarpaṇaṃ ca tāṃ punarbhuvam ity ācakṣate, bhūyaś cainām abhigacchet" iti, tat kṣatriyādyatyantabālaviṣayam | pracetāḥ: "parivittiḥ parivettṛparyāhita-paryādhātragredidhiṣūpatīnāṃ prākṛtaṃ saṃvatsaraṃ brahmacaryaṃ brahmahavratam" | vasiṣṭhaḥ: "didhiṣūpatiḥ kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caritvā nirviśetodvahet" | katham ity apekṣita āha: "tāṃ caivopayaccheta" iti | tāṃ kaniṣṭhām agredidhiṣūsaṃjñitāṃ tatpatinā dattām upayaccheta pariṇayed iti | agredidhiṣūpatiḥ kṛcchrātikṛcchrau caritvā tasmai datvā punar nirviśetānyāṃ pariṇayed ity arthaḥ | atyantāsamarthasyājñānādinimitte 'parādha etat |
atha bhṛtād adhyayanādeḥ prāyaścittam | tatra brahmasuvarcalāṃ pibed ity anuvṛttau viṣṇuḥ | [1117]
bhṛtakādhyāpanaṃ kṛtvā bhṛtakādhyāpitas tathā |
anuyogapradānena trīn pakṣān niyataḥ pibet ||
anuyogo yaśo'rthaṃ dhanārthaṃ vā samyagadhīyānasya na samyagadhīṣa ity ākṣepaḥ | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
kṛtvānuyogān adhyetuḥ patitān manur abravīt | iti |
śātātapaḥ: "brahmavikrayānuyoganiyogeṣu caturviṃśatiṃ brahmarūpāṇi dadyāt" | brahmarūpaṃ vedapārāyaṇam | asyāpi sāmānyaprāyaścittaiḥ śaktyādyapekṣo vikalpaḥ | prasaṅgād anyad api kathyate | tatra vasiṣṭhaḥ: "patitacaṇḍālaśavaśravaṇe trirātraṃ vāgyatā anaśnanta āsīran | sahasraparamaṃ vā tad abhyasyantaḥ pūtā bhavantīti vijñāyate" | śavaśravaṇaṃ śavasaṃnidhāv adhyayanam | yāvad brahma patitādisaṃnidhāv adhītaṃ tāvat sahasraparamaṃ sahasrasaṃkhyā parmā yathā bhavati tathābhyasyantaḥ pūyanta ity arthaḥ |
atha mahāpātakatadatideśakatatsamavyatiriktaparadāragamanaprāyaścittāny ucyante | tatra śaṅkhaḥ: "vaiśyāyām avakīrṇaḥ saṃvatsaraṃ brahmacaryaṃ triṣavaṇam anutiṣṭheta, kṣatriyāyāṃ dve varṣe, trīṇi brāhmaṇyām, vaiśyāvac chūdrāyām" | atra brahmacaryaṃ brahmahatyāvratātmakam | tathā ca āpastambaḥ: "savarṇāyām ananyapūrvāyāṃ sakṛnnipāte pādaḥ patatīty apadiśanty evam abhyāse pādaś caturthe sarvam" iti | atra ca brahmahavrataṃ prakṛtam | atideśād eva triṣavaṇaprāptau punas triṣavaṇavidhānam araṇyavāsādidharmāntaranivṛttyartham | traivārṣikaṃ ca śrotriyabrāhmaṇasaṃbandhi-patnyabhigamane draṣṭavyam | yad āha gautamaḥ: "dve paradāre, trīṇi śrotriyasya, dravyalābhe cotsargo yathāsthānaṃ gamayet" iti | tathā: "dattaṃ dravyam utsraṣṭavyam | yadīyaṃ tatra gamayitavyam | evaṃvidham eva kṣatriyāṃ kṣatriyasya gacchatas trīṇi, brāhmaṇasya dve, vaiśyāṃ vaiśyasya trīṇi, kṣatriyasya dve, brāhmaṇasya tv ekaṃ kalpyam" | [1118] saṃvartaḥ |
mātṛṣvaseyīṃ bhaginīṃ svasrīyāṃ pitur eva ca |
mātulasya sutāṃ gatvā carec cāndrāyaṇaṃ vratam ||
matipūrvakābhyāsa etat | amatipūrvake tu sakṛdgamane manuḥ |
paitṛṣvaseyīṃ bhaginīṃ svastrīyāṃ mātur eva ca |
mātuś ca bhrātur āptāṃ ca gatvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || iti |
bhaginīśabdaḥ paitṛśvaseyyādisamānādhikaraṇaḥ | yato 'nantaram āha |
etās tisras tu bhāryārthe nopayaccheta buddhimān |
jñātitvenānupeyās tāḥ patati hy upayann adhaḥ || iti |
jñātitvaṃ sāpiṇḍyaṃ tac ca paitṛṣvaseyīprabhṛtīnāṃ yathā bhavati tathoktaṃ vivāhaprakaraṇe | saṃvartaḥ |
mātulānīṃ sanābhiṃ ca snuṣāṃ mātuḥ sanābhikīm |
etā gatvā striyo mohāt parākeṇa viśudhyati ||
etad amatipūrvakam atyantaguṇavatas tadgamane vīryotsargāt prāṅnivṛttau draṣṭavyam | sanābhir bhaginī | viṣayāntare tu gurutalpavrataṃ manur āha |
gurutalpavrataṃ kuryāt retaḥ siktvā svayoniṣu |
sakhyuḥ putrasya ca strīṣu kumārīṣv antyajāsu ca || iti |
yamo 'pi |
pitṛṣvasā snuṣā mātā sakhī mātṛṣvasā sutā |
mātulānīsutā śvaśrūr gatvā sadyaḥ pated dvijaḥ ||
etāś cānyāś ca satataṃ niyataḥ parivarjayet |
etair agamyāgamanaiḥ sadyaḥ patati vai dvijaḥ ||
tathā skandati yo mohād gurutalpena yujyate |
śiśnasyotkartanaṃ kṛtvā prāṇatyāgena śudhyati ||
[gurutalpānuvṛttau ca śaṅkhalikhitau: "mātulānīmātṛṣvasṛpitṛṣvasṛsnuṣāduhitṛgamena | ācāryabhāryāduhitari ca" | gurutalpānuvṛttau] vasiṣṭhaḥ: "gurvīṃ sakhīṃ gurusakhīṃ navapātrāṃ patitāṃ ca gatvā kṛcchrābdapādaṃ caret" | [1119] kṛcchrābdapādavidhir amatipūrvake hīnavarṇapuṃścalītvādiduṣṭastrīgamane draṣṭavyaḥ | gurvī gurubhāryā | navapātrā kumārī | baudhāyanaḥ: "mātṛṣvasā pitṛṣvasā bhaginī bhāgineyī snuṣā mātulānī sakhīty agamyāḥ | agamyāgamane kṛcchrātikṛcchrau cāndrāyaṇam iti sarvaprāyaścittiḥ | agamyāgamane mahāvratavidhānenābdaṃ cīravāsā vane prājāpatyaṃ vā caret | sarvaprāyaścittir amatipūrvake sakṛdgamane, matipūrvake tu sakṛdgamane kṛcchrābdaṃ tasminn eva sābhyāse maraṇāntikaṃ vacanāntaravihitaṃ mahāvrataṃ brahmahatyāvratam" | saṃvartaḥ |
guror duhitaraṃ gatvā svasāraṃ pitur eva ca |
mātulasya sutāṃ caiva carec cāndrāyaṇaṃ vratam ||
gurur atraupacārika upādhyāyādiḥ | pitṛṣvasā hīnavarṇā | tathā |
etat pitṛvyabhrātror upanetṛtve sati draṣṭavyam, "yady ete copanetāraḥ" iti smṛtyantarāt | vasiṣṭhaḥ: "brāhmaṇaś ced brāhmaṇadārān abhiprekṣāpūrvakam abhigacchen nivṛttadharmakarmaṇaḥ kṛcchraḥ | anivṛttadharmakarmaṇo 'tikṛcchraḥ" | nivṛttaṃ dharmakarmāgnihotraṃ yasmāt sa nivṛttadharmakarmā tasya dārān ity arthaḥ | saṃvartaḥ |
sakhibhāryāṃ samāruhya śvaśrūṃ vā śyālikāṃ tathā |
ahorātroṣito bhūtvā taptakṛcchraṃ samācaret ||
abuddhipūrvakasakṛdgamana etat | śyālikāṃ bhāryābhaginīm |
pitṛbhāryāṃ samāruhya mātṛvarjaṃ dvijādhamaḥ |
bhaginīṃ mātur āptāṃ ca svasāraṃ cānyamātṛjām ||
etās tisraḥ striyo gatvā taptakṛcchraṃ samācaret |
kumārīgamane caiva vratam etad vinirdiśet ||
abuddhipūrvakaṃ sakṛdgamana etat | pitṛbhāryā mātṛhīnavarṇā viplutā ca | āptā pratyāsannā | svasāraṃ cānyamātṛjāṃ hīnavarṇāṃ ceti draṣṭavyam, āruhyeti vacanāt | vīryotsargāt prāṅnivṛttāv etat | [1120] sumantuḥ: "samānārṣeyīgamane kūṣmāṇḍair homaḥ | trirātram upavāsaś ca" | baudhāyanaḥ |
pitṛvyabhrātṛbhāryāś ca bhaginīṃ mātur eva ca |
śvaśrūm āruhya dhātrīṃ ca taptakṛcchradvayaṃ caret ||
niranubandhasyaitat | tathā |
bhrātur eva kaniṣṭhasya bhāryāṃ gatvā tu kāmataḥ |
kuryāt sāṃtapanaṃ kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇam athāpi vā ||
vṛṣalīviṣayam etat | tathā |
gatvā tu mātuḥ svasreyīṃ pitṛvyatanayāṃ tathā |
taptakṛcchraṃ prakurvīta ṣaḍrātraṃ tatsutāsu ca ||
guror duhitaraṃ gatvā parākaṃ tu samācaret |
bhāgineyīṃ dvijo gatvā carec cāndrāyaṇadvayam ||
gurur atrācāryaḥ | hārītaḥ |
caṇḍālīṃ pukkasīṃ vyādhīṃ snuṣāṃ tadbhaginīṃ sakhīm |
mātuḥ pituḥ svasāraṃ ca nikṣiptāṃ śaraṇāgatām ||
mātulānīṃ svasāraṃ ca sagotrām apy anicchatīm |
śiṣyabhāryāṃ guror bharyāṃ gatvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret ||
sakṛd amatipūrvake niranubandhake kriyāyām anirvṛttāyām etat | śātātapaḥ: "kanyādūṣī prājāpatyaṃ caret" | kanyādūṣy aṅgulyādinā kanyāyonikṣatakārī | saṃvartaḥ |
śūdrāṃ tu brāhmaṇo gatvā māsaṃ māsardham eva vā |
gomūtrayāvakāhāras tiṣṭhet tatpāpamokṣa(ca)kaḥ ||
matipūrve tu gamane gomūtrayāvakāhāro māsaṃ tiṣṭhet | amatipūrvake tu māsārdham |
kṣatriyām atha vaiśyāṃ vā gacched yaḥ kāmamohitaḥ |
tasya sāṃtapanaṃ kṛcchraṃ bhavet tatpāpamocanam ||
viprām asvajanāṃ gatvā prājāpatyaṃ samācaret | [1121]
asvajanāṃ mātṛpitṛpakṣāsaṃbandhām | etac cāśrotriyabhāryāyām agarbhaparyante sakṛdgamane draṣṭavyam |
gogame tu naraḥ kuryāt kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ tathā |
viṣṇuḥ: "brāhmaṇasya kṣatriyāgamane sānubandhe 'nicchantyāṃ goghnaprāyaścittam | vaiśyāgamane cāndrāyaṇaṃ śūdrāgamane sāṃtapanaṃ goghnaprāyaścittaṃ tu traimāsikam" | yamaḥ |
brāhmaṇo brāhmaṇīṃ gatvā dvije dadyān mṛgājinam |
kṣatriyāyāṃ dhanur dadyād vaiśyāyām āyasīṃ śilām ||
śūdrāṃ gatena vipreṇa udakumbhaḥ pradīyate |
trirātropoṣitaḥ snātvā dadyāt saṃmārjanīṃ tathā ||
idam atyantāniyatānāṃ brāhmaṇyādīnāṃ gamane | śaṅkhaḥ: "tiryagyoniṣu govarjaṃ sacailaṃ snātvaiṣadhibhāraṃ dadyād gobhyaḥ" | prāyaścittāntarāsamarthaviṣayam etat | hārītaḥ: "abhiplutānupetya paradārān adhovarṇān vedam āpaḥ pravahatety antarjale 'ṣṭaśataṃ japtvā tilāḍhakaṃ brāhmaṇāya dadyāt" | abhiplutā viplutāḥ | āpastambaḥ: "dāravyatikrame kharājinaṃ bahirloma paridhāya dāravyatikramaṇe bhikṣāśine bhikṣāṃ dehīti saptāgārāṇi caret sā vṛttiḥ ṣaṇmāsān" | striyās tu bhartṛvyatikrame kṛcchradvādaśarātrābhyāsas tāvantaṃ kālaṃ sānubandhe sati pūrve 'bhyāse prāyaścittadvayam idam | caturviṃśatimatāt: "vṛṣalyām abhijātas trīṇi varṣāṇi caturthasamaye naktaṃ caret" | vṛṣalyām abhijāto vṛṣalyāṃ janitāpatyaḥ | vṛṣalī cātra pariṇītā śūdrā | manuḥ |
caṇḍālāntyastriyo gatvā bhuktvā ca pratigṛhya ca |
pataty ajñānato vipro jñānāt sāmyaṃ tu gacchati ||
sāmye ca sati prāṇāntikam eva prāyaścittam | āha ca uśanā | [1122]
antyajena tu saṃparke bhojane maithune kṛte |
praviśet saṃpradīpte 'gnau mṛtyunā sa viśudhyati || iti |
yat tu tenaivoktam,
yat karoty ekarātreṇa vṛṣalīsevanād dvijaḥ |
tad bhaikṣabhug japan nityaṃ tribhir varṣair vyapohati ||
tajjñānād ekarātriparimite 'bhyāse tataś ca jñānāt sāmyaṃ tu gacchatīty anekarātriparicchinnābhyāsaviṣayam | vṛṣaly atra caṇḍālī | asminn eva viṣaye gautamo 'py āha: "antyāvasāyinīgamane kṛcchrābdaḥ" | yat tu "amatyā dvādaśarātraḥ" iti tenaivoktaṃ tad anivṛtte kriyāphale draṣṭavyam | ajñānataḥ sakṛn nivṛttau tu vasiṣṭhaḥ: "dvādaśarātram abbhakṣo dvādaśarātram upavased aśvamedhāvabhṛthaṃ vā gacchet | etenaiva caṇḍālīvivāho vyākhyātaḥ" | viṣṇuḥ: "caṇḍālīgamane tatsāmyam āpnuyāt | ajñānataś cāndrāyaṇaṃ kuryāt" | pūrveṇa samānaviṣayam | atraiva viṣaye saṃvarta āha |
caṇḍālīṃ tu dvijo gacchet kathaṃcit kāmamohitaḥ |
tribhiḥ kṛcchrair viśudhyet tu prājāpatyānupūrvakaiḥ ||
pukkasīgamane śreṣṭhaḥ kāmato 'kāmato 'pi vā |
kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ caiva prakuryāt pāpamocanam ||
akāmataḥ kṛcchraḥ | kāmataś cāndrāyaṇam | kṛcchrātikṛcchracāndrāyaṇānuvṛttau baudhāyanaḥ: "etena caṇḍālīvyavāyo vyākhyātaḥ" | abuddhipūrvakābhyāsaviṣayam etat | saṃvartaḥ |
naṭīṃ śaulūṣikīṃ caiva rajakīṃ veṇujīvinīm |
gatvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ kuryāt tathā carmopajīvinīm ||
akāmato'bhyāsaviṣayam etat | [1123]
rajakavyādhaśailuṣaveṇucarmopajīvinīm |
kāmatas tu yadā gacchet kuryāc cāndrāyaṇadvayam ||
atriḥ: "naṭanartakagāndharvagāyanagāndhikāsūtamacūcupamadgavenakuśīlavāndhradramiḍha-
śakakāmbhojatukkhāravāhvīkakhaśotragotrādīnām abhojyānām apratigrāhyāṇāṃ bhuktvā pratigṛhya ca strīgamane rahasye rahasyaṃ prakāśe prakāśaṃ caret |
rahasye taptakṛcchraṃ tu cared vipraḥ samāhitaḥ |
prakāśe caindavaṃ kuryāt sakṛd gatvā dvijottamaḥ ||
rahaḥpāpe tv avijñāte caret sāṃtapanaṃ naraḥ |
vijñāte taptakṛcchraṃ vā eṣa eva vidhiḥ sadā ||
garbhaṃ kṛtvāntyajātānāṃ carec cāndrāyaṇaṃ vratam |
caṇḍālyāṃ garbham āropya gurutalpavrataṃ caret” ||
ajñātaviṣayam etat |
rajakaś carmakāraś ca naṭo buruḍa eva ca |
kaivartamedabhigvā(llā)dyāḥ saptaite antyajāḥ smṛtāḥ ||
eṣāṃ bhuktvā striyo gatvā pītvāpaḥ pratigṛhya ca |
jñānāt kṛcchrārdham uddiṣṭam ajñānād aindavadvayam ||
saṃvatsarāt prāg atyantābhyās etat | āpastambaḥ |
mlecchī naṭī carmakartrī rajakī baruḍī tathā |
etāsāṃ gamanaṃ kṛtvā carec cāndrāyaṇavratam ||
kāmato 'bhyāsa etat | hārītaḥ |
kaivartī dhvajinī caiva yāś cānyā antyasaṃbhavāḥ |
kāmatas tu vasan vipra etān eva samācaret ||
dvau māsau bhaikṣabhkṣyeṇa dvau māsau yavayāvakaiḥ |
dvau māsau pañcagavyena ṣaṇmāsāṃś caritavrataḥ ||
evaṃ śuddhim avāpnoti prāyaścittānurodhataḥ ||
saṃvatsarāt prāg atyantābhyāsa etat | ūrdhvaṃ tu maraṇāntikam | [1124] āpastambaḥ |
caṇḍālamedaśvapacakapālavratacāriṇām |
akāmataḥ striyo gatvā parākavratam ācaret ||
kāmatas tu prasūtau vā tatsamo nātra saṃśayaḥ |
akāmataḥ prasavāt kāmato gamanāt sābhyāsāt tatsamaḥ |
kāmato yo 'bhigacchet tu brāhmaṇīṃ śūdrasaṃmatām ||
prāyaścittaṃ kathaṃ tasya kṛcchraṃ traivārṣikaṃ smṛtam |
bhūyobhyāse gamane caitat |
hīnavarṇagatāṃ viprāṃ brāhmaṇo yo 'bhigacchati |
sa kuryāt tu viśuddhyarthaṃ kṛcchraṃ saṃvatsaraṃ dvijaḥ ||
idaṃ kāmato 'bhyāse | uśanā |
gamane tu vrataṃ kuryād garbhe taddviguṇaṃ caret |
atha strīṇāṃ vyabhicāraprāyaścittāni | tatra manuḥ |
vipraduṣṭāṃ striaṃ bhartā nirundhyād ekaveśmani |
yat puṃsaḥ paradāreṣu tac caināṃ cāreyed vratam ||
atra ca |
prāyaścittārdhm arhanti striyo rogiṇa eva ca |
[iti] paribhāṣā nādaraṇīyā tasyāḥ strīpuruṣasādhāraṇavratopadeśaviṣayatvāt | ayaṃ punar asādhāraṇātideśaḥ | ata evāha bṛhaspatiḥ |
yat puṃsaḥ paradāreṣu samāneṣu vrataṃ caret |
vyabhicārāt tu bhartuḥ strī tad aśeṣaṃ samācaret || iti |
samāneṣu savarṇeṣu | icchāpūrvakavyabhicāra etat | anicchāpūrvake tu sa evāha |
anicchantīṃ tu yo bhartā guptāṃ tāṃ dhārayed gṛhe |
malināṅgīm adhaḥśayyāṃ piṇḍamātropajīvinīm ||
kārayen niṣkṛtiṃ kṛcchraṃ parākaṃ vā samāgatām |
sāmarthyāpekṣo vikalpaḥ | samena savarṇenāgatāṃ samāgātām ity arthaḥ |
hīnavarṇopabhuktā yā tyājyā vāpy atha (vadhyātha) vā bhavet |
buddhipūrvake tu garbhaparyante kṣatriyavaiśyagamane tyājyā | evaṃ śūdragamane vadhyā vadho 'tra nāsādikartanam | [1125] viṣṇuḥ: "sakṛd duṣṭā strī yat puruṣasya paradāre vrataṃ tat kuryāt" | manuḥ |
sā cet punaḥ praduṣyet tu sadṛśenopamantritam |
kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ caiva tad asyāḥ pāvanaṃ smṛtam ||
ṣaṇmāsānuvṛttāv āpastambaḥ: "striyās tu bhartur vyatikrame kṛcchradvādaśarātrābhyāsas tāvantaṃ kālam" | kāmato 'tyantābhyāsaviṣayam etat | uśanā: "vyabhicāriṇīṃ bhāryāṃ kucailavṛttāṃ nivṛttādhikārāṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ prājāpatyaṃ vā cārayet" | manūktakṛcchrasahitacāndrāyaṇāsāmarthye cāndrāyaṇamātram | balātkāra[ta] upabhuktāviṣayaḥ prājāpatyaḥ | tathā ca saṃvartaḥ |
balāt pramathya bhuktā ced dahyamānena cetasā |
prājāpatyena śuddhiḥ syān na tasyāḥ pāvanaṃ param ||
atrāsāmarthya ṛṣyaśṛṅga āha |
balena kāmitā nārī savarṇena kathaṃcana |
prāyaścittaṃ trirātraṃ vai tasyāḥ śuddhiṃ samādiśet ||
prākṛtaṃ brahmacaryaṃ varṣaṃ cared ity adhikāre gautamaḥ: "strī cābhicāriṇī guptā piṇḍaṃ tu labheta" | "sā cet punaḥ praduṣyet" ity anena mānavena samānaviṣayam etat | saṃvartaḥ |
yadi vai brāhmaṇī gacchet kṣatriyaṃ vaiśyam eva vā |
gomūtrayāvakair māsāt tadardhāc ca viśudhyati ||
vaiśyagamane māsāt kṣatriyagamane māsārdhāt |
brāhmanyāḥ śūdrasaṃparke kathaṃcit samupāgate |
cāndrāyaṇena śuddhiḥ syāt tad asyāḥ pāvanaṃ param ||
balātkāraviṣayam etat | bṛhatpracetāḥ |
viprā śūdreṇa saṃgamya vaset tasmāt prasūyate |
prāyaścittaṃ smṛtaṃ tasyāḥ kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇatrayam ||
cāndrāyaṇe dve kṛcchre ca viprāyā vaiśyasaṃgame |
kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ caiva viprāyāḥ kṣatrasaṃgame ||
śūdraṃ gatvā kṣatriyā tu kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇadvayam |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ sakṛcchraṃ ca cared vaiśyena saṃgame || [1126]
śūdraṃ gatvā cared vaiśyā kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇottaram |
ānulomyena vihitaṃ kṛcchraṃ pādāvaropitam ||
pādāvaropaṇaṃ pādahrāsaḥ | tataś ca kṣatriyāyā vipragamane kṛcchraḥ | vaiśyāyāḥ pādadvayam | śūdrāyāḥ pādaḥ | vaiśyāyāḥ kṣatriyagamane kṛcchraḥ | evaṃ śūdrāyāḥ kṣatriyagamane vaiśyagamane ca kalpyam |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ sajātīye paunaḥpunye sakṛcchrakam |
aṅguṣṭhagame kalpyaṃ tathaivājñānasaṃgame ||
paunaḥpunyam abhyāsaḥ | tatra buddhipūrvake sakṛcchram | aṅguṣṭhenābhicāriṇīṃ praty abuddhipūrvake gamane kalpyaṃ pariṣadā prāyaścittam ity arthaḥ | ṛṣyaśṛṅgaḥ |
kāmitā syād athāntyair yā sā kṛcchrābdaṃ samācaret |
antarvatnī ca yuvatiḥ kāmitā cāntyayoninā ||
prāyaścittaṃ naiva kuryād yāvad garbho na niḥsṛtaḥ |
na pracāraṃ gṛhe kuryān na cāṅgādiprasādhanam ||
na śayīta samaṃ bhartrā na vā bhuñjīta bāndhavaiḥ |
prāyaścittaṃ sṛte garbhe vidhiṃ kṛcchābdikaṃ caret ||
hiraṇyam atha vā dhenuṃ dadyād viprāya dakṣiṇām ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ: "śūdraś ced brāhmaṇīm abhigacched ve(vī)raṇair veṣṭayitvā śūdram agnau prāsyet | brāhmaṇyāś ca śirasi vapanaṃ kārayitva sarpiṣābhyajya nagnāṃ kharam āropya mahāpatham anusaṃvrājayet | pūtā bhavatīti vijñāyate | vaiśyaś ced brāhmaṇīm abhigacchel lohitadarbhair veṣṭayitvā vaiśyam agnau prāsyet | brāhmaṇyāḥ śirasi vapanaṃ kārayitvā sarpiṣābhyajya nagnāṃ gaurakharam āropya mahāpatham anusaṃvrājayet | pūtā bhavatīti vijñāyate | rājyaś ced brāhmaṇīm abhigacchec charapatrair veṣṭayitvā rājanyam agnau prāsyet | brāhmaṇyāḥ śirasi vapanaṃ kārayitvā sarpiṣābhyajya nagnāṃ kṛṣṇakharam āropya mahāpatham anusaṃvrājayet | pūtā bhavatīti vijñāyate" |
evaṃ vaiśyo rājanyāṃ śūdraś ca vaiśyarājanyayoḥ | pūtā bhavati, prāyaścittayogyā bhavatīty arthaḥ | [1127]
"manasā bhartur abhicāre trirātraṃ yāvakaṃ kṣīraudanaṃ vā bhuñjānādhaḥ śayīta | ūrdhvaṃ trirātrād apsu nimagnāyāḥ sāvitryaṣṭaśatena śirobhir juhuyāt pūtā bhavatīti vijñāyate | vāksaṃbandhe caitad eva māsaṃ caritvordhvaṃ māsād apsu nimagnāyāḥ sāvitryaṣṭasahasreṇa śirobhir juhuyāt | pūtā bhavatīti vijñāyate | vyavāye tu saṃvatsaraṃ ghṛtapaṭṭaṃ dhārayet | gomayagarte kuśaprastare vā bhuñjānādhaḥ śayīta | ūrdhvaṃ saṃvatsarād apsu nimagnāyāḥ sāvitryaṣṭasahasreṇa śirobhir juhuyāt pūtā bhavatīti vijñāyate | vyavāye tīrthagamane dharmebhyaś ca nivartate" | dharmebhyo dharmādhikārebhyaḥ strī nivartate, cakārāt prāyaścittad api | saṃvartaḥ |
caṇḍālaṃ pukkasaṃ mlecchaṃ śvapākaṃ patitaṃ tathā |
etān gatvā striyaḥ śreṣṭhāḥ kuryuś cāndrāyaṇatrayam ||
śreṣṭhā varṇastriyaḥ | caturviṃśatimatāt |
kṛcchrārdhaṃ brāhmaṇī kuryād viprasya gamane sati |
kṣatriyasya caret kṛcchraṃ viśaḥ sāṃtapanaṃ smṛtam ||
śūdrasya gamane caiva parākaṃ ca samācaret |
ardhaṃ tv eṣu samāpteṣu pāpeṣūktaṃ manīṣibhiḥ ||
vīryakṣepāt prāgnivṛttau satyāṃ śūdrādisaṃgatānāṃ brāhmaṇyādīnāṃ kṛcchrārdhādiprāyaścittacatuṣṭayād ardhaṃ prāyaścittam ity arthaḥ | tathā |
vipragarbhe parākaḥ syāt kṣatriyasya tathaindavam |
viśas tad eva kartavyaṃ parākeṇa samanvitam ||
śūdragarbhe bhavet tyāgas tatra caṇḍāladarśanāt |
vārdhuṣyalavaṇakriyayos tu sādhāraṇopapātakaprāyaścittāny eva, viśeṣavihitānām abhāvāt || 3.267, 268 ||
idānīṃ strīvadhe vaiśeṣikaṃ prāyaścittam āha |
durvṛttā brahmanṛpaśūdrayoṣāḥ pramāpya tu |
dṛtiṃ dhanur bastam aviṃ kramād dadyād viśuddhaye || 3.269 ||
[1128]
durvṛttāḥ svairiṇīr brāhmaṇādiyoṣito hatvā yathāvarṇakramaṃ dṛtyādikaṃ brāhmaṇāya dadyāt | dṛtiś carmādimayaṃ jalabhāṇḍam | dhanuḥ prasiddham | bastaś chāgaḥ | dṛtyādidātrā dakṣiṇātvena suvarṇam api deyam | tatparimāṇaṃ cānubandhādyapekṣayā kalpyam | manur apy āha |
jīrṇakārmukabastāvīḥ pṛthag dadyād viśuddhaye |
caturṇām api varṇānāṃ nārīr hatvānavasthitāḥ ||
anavasthitā bhartary anavasthitāḥ | bhartṛvyabhicāriṇīr iti yāvat | aṅgirāḥ |
kośaṃ kūpe 'tha vipre vā brāhmaṇyāḥ pratipādayet |
vadhe dhanuḥ kṣatriyāyā basto vaiśyāvadhe smṛtaḥ ||
śūdrāyā āvikaṃ caiva veśyāṃ hatvā jalaṃ spṛśet |
asyārthaḥ — brāhmaṇyā vadhe kośaṃ carmamayaṃ jalapātraṃ kūpe paropakārārthaṃ brāhmaṇāya vā dadyāt | veśyāyāṃ tu brāhmaṇā(ṇyā)dikāyāṃ hatāyāṃ na kiṃcid dadyāt | kiṃ tu jalaṃ spṛśet || 3.269 ||
apraduṣṭāṃ striyaṃ hatvā śūdrahatyāvrataṃ caret |
prakarṣeṇa duṣṭā praduṣṭā [sā] na bhavatīty apraduṣṭā | īṣadduṣṭeti yāvat | tāṃ brāhmaṇādistriyaṃ hatvā śūdrahatyāvrataṃ ṣāṇmāsikaṃ vrataṃ kuryāt | daśa dhenūr vā dadyāt | aduṣṭāvadhe ca śātātapa āha: "ṣaṇmāsān strīghāte prājāpatyaṃ caret | amatipūrvake tu cāndrāyaṇam" | vyāsaḥ |
akāmataḥ striyaṃ hatvā brāhmaṇīṃ vaiśyavac caret |
kāmato dviguṇaṃ proktaṃ praduṣṭāyāṃ na kiṃcana ||
puṇyāṃl lokān avāpnoti śūdragāṃ yas tu pātayet |
patighnīm atha garbhaghnīṃ tā hi saṃkarakārikāḥ ||
yat tu prācetasam: "anartumatīṃ brāhmaṇīṃ hatvā kṛcchrābdaṃ ṣaṇmāsān vā, kṣatriyāṃ hatvā ṣaṇmāsān māsatrayaṃ vā, vaiśyāṃ hatvā māsatrayaṃ sārdhamāsaṃ vā, śūdrāṃ hatvā sārdhamāsaṃ dvāviṃśatidināni vā" iti, tan nirguṇahantṛviṣayam | atra ca sarveṣu vikalpeṣu pūrvavat kalpaḥ kāmakāraviṣaye | uttaras tadviparītaviṣayaḥ | yat tu vasiṣṭhenoktam [1129] "anātreyīṃ rājanyahiṃsāyām, rājanyāṃ vaiśyahiṃsāyāṃ vaiśyāṃ śūdrahiṃsāyām, śūdrāṃ saṃvatsaram" iti, tat sahadharmacāriṇīviṣayam | atra ca śūdrāṃ hatvā saṃvatsaram ity asya bhrūṇahatyāprāyaścittam iti viśeṣaḥ | atraiva viṣaye hārīto 'py āha: "ṣaḍvarṣāṇi rājanye prākṛtaṃ brahmacaryam, trīṇi vaiśye, sardhaṃ śūdre, kṣatriyavad brāhmaṇīṣu | vaiśyavat kṣatriyāyām, śūdravac ca vaiśyāyām, śūdraṃ hatvā nava māsān" iti | aṅgirās tu viṣayaviśeṣe vratātirekam āha |
āhitāgner dvijāgryasya hatvā patnīm aninditām |
brahmahatyāvrataṃ kuryād ātreyīghnas tathaiva ca ||
upapātakaprāyaścittaviṣaya[m ida]m | strīvadhaviśeṣe sa evāha |
anāhitāgnipatnīnāṃ tathā viṭśūdrayoṣitām |
goghātakavrataṃ kuryāc chūdrāyāṃ caiva nityaśaḥ ||
paiṭhīnasiḥ: "trivarṣaṃ sthānāsanābhyāṃ naktam aśnīyād goghātakaḥ śudhyati saṃvatsaraṃ prājāpatyena vā" |
prāsaṅgikaṃ hiṃsāntare 'pi prāyaścittam āha |
asthimatāṃ sahasraṃ vā tathānasthimatām anaḥ || 3.270 ||
hatvā śūdrahatyāvrataṃ cared ity anuvartate | asthimatāṃ sahasram anasthimatām anaḥ śakaṭaṃ hatvā śūdrahatyāvrataṃ caret | anaś cātra pariṇāmatvena vivakṣitam | tena śakaṭaparipūrtiparimitānāṃ vadha etat | uktaparimāṇātiriktavadhe prāyaścittātirekaḥ kalpyaḥ | alpavadhe tu vakṣyati | yat tu manunoktam,
krimikīṭavayohatyā madyānugatabhojanam |
phalaidhaḥkusumasteyam adhairyaṃ ca malāvaham ||
malinīkaraṇīyeṣu taptaḥ syād yāvakas tryaham |
tad uktaparimāṇāpekṣayā nyūnaparimāṇeṣu krimiprabhṛtiṣv anasthikeṣu draṣṭavyam | asthimantaś ca viśeṣavihitaprāyaścittarahitā atra grāhyāḥ | śūdrahatyāvrataṃ ca ṣāṇmāsikaṃ brahmahavratam | daśadhenudānaṃ vā | anasthikānāṃ ca keṣāṃcit sahasrasaṃkhyākānāṃ vadhe śūdravadhaprāyaścittam āha yamaḥ: [1130] "ata ūrdhvaṃ krimikīṭapataṅgapipīlikabhramara-daṃśamaśakamakṣikāṇāṃ sahasraṃ hatvā śūdravadhaḥ | kokilaśukakapotakapiñjalakukkuṭa-ṭiṭṭibhakharjūrakhañjarīṭādīnāṃ puruṣabhāravadhe śūdravadhaḥ" | vaiśvavad ity anuvṛttau gautamaḥ: "asthimatāṃ sahasraṃ hatvānasthimatām anuḍudbhāre ca" | sapratyayasānubandhaviṣayam etat | vasiṣṭhaḥ: "anasthimatāṃ ca sattvānāṃ gotraṃ rāśim, hatvā kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caret | kiṃcic ca dadyāt" | [asthimatāṃ caikaikam || 3.270 ||]
asthimatām ekaikasya vadhe kiṃcid deyam iti vakṣyati, tasyāpavādam āha |
mārjāragodhānakulamaṇḍūkāṃś ca patatriṇaḥ |
hatvā tryahaṃ pibet kṣīraṃ kṛcchraṃ vā pādikaṃ caret || 3.271 ||
mārjārādīnām anyatamavadhe trirātraṃ kṣīraṃ pibet | vakṣyamāṇaṃ pādakṛcchraṃ vā caret | patatriṇo 'tra kākolūkādaya eva vakṣyamāṇam anuvākyavaśāt | amatipūrvake sakṛd dhanana etat | yat tu viṣṇūktam: "śvānaṃ hatvā trirātram upavaset | hatvā mārjāranakulamaṇḍūkaḍuṇḍubhājagarāṇām anyatamam upoṣitaḥ kṛsaraṃ bhojayitvā lohadaṇḍaṃ ca dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt | godholūkakākacāṣavadhe trirātram upavaset" iti, tat svalpāparādhanirdhanapuruṣaviṣayam | yat tu mānavam,
mārjāranakulau hatvā cāṣaṃ maṇḍūkam eva ca |
śvagodholūkakākāṃś ca śūdrahatyāvrataṃ caret ||
iti, tat kāmato 'tyantābhyāsaviṣayam | yamaḥ: "śvanakulasarpamārjāramaṇḍūkabimbika-kikīdivikagodhāgṛdhrolūkavāyasamayūragrāmacarakekarasṛgālamūṣakān hatvaikaikasya vadhe śūdravadhaḥ" | [1131] paiṭhīnasiḥ: "kākolūkakṛkalāsakaṅkavṛkakharaśṛgālaśaśabarhiṇamūṣaka-cakravākahaṃsapraveṇīnakulamaṇḍūkabiḍālaśvavadha eteṣām ekaikasmiñ śūdravad vihitaḥ kalpaḥ" | ubhayam apy etan mānavena samānaviṣayam | yat tu vāsiṣṭham: "śvamārjāramaṇḍūkanaulasarpadaharamūṣakān hatvā kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caret | kiṃcic ca dadyāt" iti, tad amatipūrvakābhyāsaviṣayam | daharaḥ kṣudraḥ | yat tu mārjāravadha eva manunoktam,
payaḥ pibet trirātraṃ vā yojanaṃ vādhvano vrajet |
upaspṛśet sravantyāṃ vā sūktaṃ vābdaivataṃ japet ||
iti, tad yājñavalkīyena samānaviṣayam | trirātram iti [sarvatra] saṃbadhyate | laghuviṣṇuḥ |
mārjārasarpanakulaśvaśṛgālamṛgeṣu ca |
pramādād dhanane kāryaḥ kṛcchrapādo viśuddhaye ||
kaśyapaḥ: "śvabiḍālajātyantasthāve gardabhoṣṭrāśvastrīvadhe prāyaścittaṃ brāhmaṇebhyo nivedya ṣaḍrātropoṣitaś cīrṇānte tilān dadyāt" | hārītaḥ: "markaṭaśvabhāsamārjārajālapādabarhiṇām ekatamavadhe gāṃ brāhmaṇāya dadyāt" | aṅgirāḥ |
kāke bhāse ca gṛdhre ca ṭiṭṭibhe khañjarīṭake |
yathā gavi tathā hatyāṃ bhagavān manur abravīt ||
sakṛd amatipūrvake hanana etat || 3.271 ||
gaje nīlavṛṣāḥ pañca śuke vatso dvihāyanaḥ |
kharājameṣeṣu vṛṣo deyaḥ krauñce trihāyanaḥ || 3.272 ||
gajaṃ hatvā nīlavarṇā vṛṣāḥ śauṇḍāḥ pañca brāhmaṇebhyo deyāḥ, śuke pakṣiviśeṣe hate dvivarṣo vatso deyaḥ | gardabhacchāgameṣāṇām anyatamavadhe vṛḍo 'nadvān deyaḥ | krauñcavadhe tu trivarṣo vṛṣaḥ | anaḍvāñ śakaṭasya voḍhā gauḥ || 3.272 ||
[1132]
haṃsaśyenakapikravyājjalasthalaśikhaṇḍinaḥ |
bhāsaṃ ca hatvā dadyād grāmakravyāde tu vatsikām || 3.273 ||
haṃsādīn hatvā gāṃ viprāya dadyāt | kravyam apakvaṃ māṃsaṃ tad yo 'tti sa kravyād vyāghraśṛgālādiḥ | jalathalaśabdābhyāṃ taccāriṇaḥ pakṣino lakṣyante | śikhandī mayūraḥ |
manuḥ |
hatvā haṃsaṃ balākāṃ ca bakaṃ barhiṇam eva ca |
vānaraṃ śyenabhāsau ca sparṣed brāhmaṇāya gām ||
kravyādas tu mṛgān hatvā dhenuṃ dadyāt payasvinīm |
akravyādo vatsatarīm uṣṭraṃ hatvā tu kṣṇalām ||
caturviṃśatimatāt |
siṃhavyāghavṛkādīṃś ca duṣṭān hatvā ca daṃṣṭriṇaḥ |
hatvā sarīsṛpādīṃś ca dinam ekam abhojanam ||
matsyaṃ ca kṛki(ka)lāsaṃ ca hatvā maṇḍūkam eva ca |
upoṣya saptarātraṃ ca pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
haṃsaṃ bakaṃ balākāṃ ca śārikaṃ śukatittiram |
sārasaṃ cāṣabhāsādi hatvā kṛcchraṃ samācaret ||
kapotabakakānānāṃ gṛdhrāṇāṃ hanane dvijaḥ |
sarveṣāṃ pakṣiṇāṃ caiva dinam ekam abhojanam || 3.273 ||
urageṣv āyaso daṇḍaḥ paṇḍake trapusīsakam |
koke ghṛtaghaṭo deya uṣṭre guñjā haye 'ṃśukam || 3.274 ||
urageṣu sarīsṛpeṣu hateṣv āyaso lohamayaḥ khananasamartho daṇḍo deyaḥ | paṇḍake napuṃsake trapu sīsakaṃ ca māṣaparimāṇaṃ deyam | kole varāhe ghṛtapūrṇo ghaṭo deyaḥ | uṣṭrake tu guñjāparimāṇaṃ suvarṇaṃ deyam | haye bandhūkavastram | manuḥ |
abhrīṃ kārṣṇayasīṃ dadyāt sarpaṃ hatvā dvijottamaḥ |
palālabhārakaṃ śaṇḍhe māṣakaṃ caiva sīsakam ||
palālam aghṛtadhānaṃ tṛṇam || 3.274||
tittirau ca tiladroṇaṃ gajādinām aśaknuvan |
dānaṃ dātuṃ caret kṛcchram ekaikasya viśuddhaye || 3.275 ||
[1133]
tittirau pakṣiviśeṣe hate tiladroṇaṃ dadyāt |
aṣṭamuṣṭi bhavet kiṃcit kiṃcic catvāri puṣkalam |
puṣkalāni ca catvāri āḍhakaḥ parikīrtitaḥ ||
caturāḍhako bhaved droṇaḥ khārī droṇas tu ṣoḍaśa |
kathitaṃ droṇasya lavaṇam iti | yas tu nirdhanatvena gajādivadhaśuddhyarthaṃ dānaṃ na śaknuyāt kartuṃ sa gajādivadhapāpakṣayārthaṃ pratinimittaṃ kṛcchraṃ kuryāt | kṛcchrasaṃjñaka(śabda)ś ca prājāpatyasaṃjñake tapoviśeṣe rūḍhaḥ | tatra gajādīnām adhikadānasādhyo vadhapāpakṣaya iti prājāptyābhyāsaḥ kalpyaḥ | na tv eka eva prājāpatyaḥ | viṣamasamīkaraṇāpatteḥ | atha vā kṛcchraśabdas tapomātre vartate, tac ca nimittasya gurulaghubhāvānusāreṇa guru laghu vā kalpanīyam | ata eva caturviṃśatimate |
hastinaṃ turagaṃ kaḍgaṃ mahiṣoṣṭrakharaṃ mṛgam |
rohyaṃ ca sa(śa)mbaraṃ hatvā caret sāṃtapanaṃ dvijaḥ ||
saṃvartaḥ |
hastinaṃ turagaṃ hatvā mahiṣoṣṭraṃ kapiṃ tathā |
eṣa sarveṣu kurvīta saptarātram abhojanam ||
vyāghraṃ śvānaṃ kharaṃ siṃhaṃ rohyaṃ sūkram eva ca |
etān hatvā dvijaḥ kuryād brāhmaṇānāṃ ca bhojanam ||
sarvāsām eva jātīnaṃ mṛgāṇāṃ vanacāriṇām |
ahorātroṣitas tiṣṭhej japan vai jātavedasam ||
hatvā haṃsaṃ balākāṃ ca śvāvikāraṇḍabarhiṇaḥ |
vānaraṃ śyenabhāsau ca hatvā tiṣṭhet tryahaṃ dvijaḥ ||
ṭiṭṭibaṃ jālapādaṃ ca madguṃ kukkuṭam eva ca |
etān hatvā dvijaḥ kuryāt saptarātram abhojanam ||
jābālaḥ |
hastinaṃ turagaṃ hatvā mahiṣoṣṭraṃ kapiṃ tathā |
mahāsāṃtapanaṃ kuryād gobhūkanyānṛte tathā ||
hiraṇyaṃ gāṃ ceti dadad iti śeṣaḥ | parāśaraḥ |
krauñcasārasahaṃsānāṃ bakakukkuṭayos tathā |
mayūrameṣayor ghāte ekabhaktena śudhyati ||
madgūnāṃ ṭiṭṭibhānāṃ ca śukapārāvatasya ca |
āṭīva(ba)kasya ghāte tu śudhyed vai nakabhojanāt ||
cāṣakākakapotānāṃ śārītitiiraghātakaḥ |
antarjala ubhe madhye prāṇāyāmena śudhyati || [1134]
gṛdhraśyenavihaṃgānām ulūkānāṃ ca ghātane |
apakvāśī dinaṃ tiṣṭhed dvikāle mārutāśanaḥ ||
dvikālaḥ sāyaṃkālabhojanakālaḥ | hārītaḥ: "vājirāsabhavadhe kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret | vṛthā paśuvadhe prājāpatyaṃ trirātropoṣitaḥ | markaṭamārjārajālapādabarhiṇām ekatamavadhe brāhmaṇāya gāṃ dadyāt" | viṣṇuḥ: "anuktamṛgavadhe trirātram upavaset" | śaṅkhaḥ |
paśūn hatvā tathā grāmyān māsaṃ kuryād vicakṣaṇaḥ |
adattānāṃ tathā dāne tadardhaṃ tu vidhīyate ||
hatvā dvijas tathā sapaṃ jaleśayabileśayam |
māsaṃ kuryād brāhmaṇo vratam ity anvayaḥ || 3.275 ||
api ca |
kiṃcit sāsthivadhe deyaṃ prāṇāyāmas tv anasthike |
vṛkṣagulamalatānāṃ ca chedane japyam ṛkśatam || 3.276 ||
anuktaniṣkṛtikasya sāsthikasya vadhe kiṃcit kārṣāpaṇādi deyam | nirathikavadhe tu prāṇāyāmaḥ kāryaḥ | atra sumantuḥ: "yūkāmaṣa(śa)kamakṣikāmatkuṇapūtikīṭamātṛvāhakajalauka-gaṇḍūpādīnām anyeṣāṃ vānasthimatāṃ vadhe prāṇāyāmaḥ | athimatāṃ vadhe paṇo deyaḥ | chedana ṛcāṃ śataṃ japyam" | gulmāḥ kubjādayaḥ | latā atimuktakādayaḥ | atra manuḥ |
phaladānāṃ tu vṛkṣāṇāṃ chedane japyam ṛkśatam |
gulmavallīlatānāṃ ca puṣipitānāṃ ca vīrudhām ||
puṣpitānām iti gulmādibhiḥ saṃbadhyate | aparigṛhīteṣu vṛkṣādiṣv etat,
tṛṇaṃ vā yadi vā kāṣṭhaṃ puṣpaṃ vā yadi vā phalam |
apṛṣṭvā parigṛhṇīta(hṇaṃs tu) hastacchedanam arhati || [1135]
iti daṇḍādhikyadarśanāt | parigṛhīte prāyaścittātirekaḥ kalpyate | karṣaṇād vṛkṣacchedane na doṣaḥ | yathāh vasiṣṭhaḥ: "puṣpaphalopagān pādapān na hiṃsyāt | karṣaṇakāraṇārtham copahanyāt" iti | yamaḥ: vṛkṣalatāgulmatṛṇacchedane vṛddhakṛcchrakaḥ phalavatāṃ prājāpatyam" |
nīlīvastraṃ paridhāya bhuktvā snānārha eva ca |
trirātraṃ tu vrataṃ kuryāc chittvā vṛkṣaṃ phalapradam ||
gumalatās tathā (?) | śaṅkhaḥ |
kṣatriyas tu raṇe naṣṭaḥ prauḍhaḥ prāṇaparāyaṇaḥ |
saṃvatsaraṃ vrataṃ kurāc chittvā vṛkṣaṃ phalapradam ||
naṣṭaḥ palāyitaḥ | vratam atra gomūtrayāvakam, śaṅkhoktatvāt | chittvā vṛkṣam iti sātiṣayopabhogavṛkṣacchedanaviṣayam || 3.276 ||
puṃścalīvānarakharair daṣṭaś coṣṭrādivāyasaiḥ |
prāṇāyāmaṃ jale kṛtvā ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati || 3.277 ||
puṃścalyādibhir daṣṭaḥ snātvā jala eva sthitvā prāṇāyamaṃ kṛtvā ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati | ghṛtaprāśas tv āhārāntaranivartakaḥ, tapastvāt | ādigrahaṇāt sṛgālādayo gṛhyante | yathāha
manuḥ |
śvasṛgālakharair daṣṭo grāmyaiḥ kravyādbhir eva ca |
narāśvoṣṭravarāhaiś ca prāṇāyāmena śudhyati ||
tatra sumantuḥ: "śvasṛgālamṛgamahiṣājāvikakarakarabhanakulamārjāramūṣakaplavabaka-
puruṣadaṣṭānām āpohiṣṭhīyābhiḥ snānaṃ prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ ca" | etac copvāsāsamarthaviṣayam | aṅgirāḥ |
brahmacārī śunā daṣṭas tryahaṃ sārdhaṃ pibet payaḥ |
gṛhasthaś ced dvirātraṃ ca ekāhaṃ hy agnihotravān ||
nābher ūrdhvaṃ tu daṣṭasya tad eva dviguṇaṃ bhavet |
syād etat triguṇaṃ vaktre mastake tu caturguṇam ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
brāhmaṇas tu śunā daṣṭo nadīṃ gatvā samudragām |
prāṇāyāmaśataṃ kṛtvā ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati || [1136]
śirasi daṣṭasyaitat | parāśaraḥ |
brāhmaṇī tu śunā daṣṭā jambukena vṛkeṇa vā |
uditaṃ grahanakṣatraṃ dṛṣṭvā sadyaḥ śucir bhavet ||
jambukaḥ sṛgālaḥ | tasyāṃ savratāyāṃ sa evāha |
trirātram evopavasec chunā daṣṭā tu savratā |
saghṛtaṃ yāvakaṃ bhuktvā vrataśeṣaṃ samāpayet ||
pulastyaḥ |
rajasvalā yadā daṣṭā śunā jambukarāsabhaiḥ |
pañcarātraṃ nirāhārā pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
prāṇāyāmāsamarthaṃ praty āha marīciḥ |
tarakṣeṇa yadā daṣṭaḥ kharoṣṭranaravānaraiḥ |
agnīn dṛṣṭvā hūyamānān sadya eva śucir bhavet ||
tarakṣo vyāghraviśeṣaḥ | evaṃ ca divādaṣṭasyāsāyaṃkālād abhojanam | prātarhomakālaṃ yāvad rātridaṣṭasyeti gamyate | śaṅkhaḥ |
trirātraṃ tu vrataṃ kuryāt puṃścalīdaśanakṣataḥ |
pulastyaḥ |
vratasthaṃ tu śunā daṣṭaṃ trirātram upavāsayet |
saghṛtaṃ yāvakaṃ bhuktvā vrataśeṣaṃ samāpayet ||
avrataḥ savrato vāpi śunā daṣṭo dvijottamaḥ |
gavāṃ śṛṅgodakasnāto mahānadyāś ca saṃgame ||
samudrasparśanād vāpi śunā daṣṭaḥ śucir bhavet |
brāhmaṇai rahite grāme śunā daṣṭas tu brāhmaṇaḥ ||
vṛṣaṃ pradakṣiṇikṛtya prāṇāyāmena śudhyati |
śvasṛgālakharair daṣṭo grāmyair govāyasais tathā ||
pravāhebhyo mahānadyāḥ prāṇāyāmaśataṃ bhavet |
ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyet tu trirātropoṣaṇena vā ||
suvarṇarajatābhyāṃ vā gavāṃ śṛṅgodakena vā |
navair vā kalaśaiḥ snātvā caturbhis tu viśudhyati ||
brāhmaṇī tu śunā daṣṭā some dṛṣṭiṃ nipātayet |
vedavidyāvratsnātaḥ śunā daṣṭas tu brāhmaṇaḥ ||
śataparyāyam āvartya gayatrīṃ śuddhim āpnuyāt |
hiraṇyodakamiśraṃ vā ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati || [1137]
śaṅkhaḥ |
nīlīkāṣṭhakṣato vipraḥ śunā daṣṭas tathaiva ca |
trirātraṃ tu vrataṃ kuryāt puṃścalīdaśanahataḥ ||
yamaḥ |
sṛgālaśūkarakharair daṣṭaḥ śvanarakuñjaraiḥ |
etais tu brāhmaṇo daṣṭas trir ahnaḥ samupaspṛśet ||
haviṣyaṃ bhojayed annaṃbrāhmaṇān pañca sapta vā |
brahmacāryadhikāre hārītaḥ: "śunā daṣṭas tryaham ekāhāraḥ samudragāṃ nadīṃ gatvā prāṇāyāmaśataṃ kṛtvā ghṛtaṃ prāśya tataḥ śuciḥ | evam eva gomāyumārjārasarpanakulamūṣakair daṣṭānām" | devalaḥ: "brāhmaṇas tu śunā daṣṭo gāyatryaṣṭasahsrābhimantritaṃ kṛtvā tīrthodakena navabhiś caturbhir vā kalaśaiḥ snātaḥ śudhyati | nadīsaṃgame vā bālakasyājñasyāśaktasya vā tatra pitāsyānudhyāyan manasā sarvakāryāṇi kurvīta, pitur abhāve saty ācāryaḥ" | abhimantritaṃ kṛtvā, daṃśasthānam iti śeṣaḥ | anudhyāyan doṣanirghātaṃ cintayan | paiṭhīnasiḥ: "śunā daṣṭasya trirātropavāsaḥ | vipragṛhe vāsaś ca" | bhānuḥ |
viprasya ced vraṇadvāre pūyaśoṇitasaṃbhave |
krimir utpadyate somya niṣkṛtiṃ vacmi tasya tu ||
gavāṃ mūtrapurīṣeṇa trisaṃdhyaṃ snānam ācaret |
dadhikṣīraghṛtaṃ pītvā krimidaṣṭo viśudhyati ||
adho nābhyāṃ tu daṣṭasya ā pādād vinatātmaja |
etad vinirdiśet prāyaḥ prāyaścittaṃ khagādhipa ||
nābhikaṇṭhāntare vīra yadā copadyate krimiḥ |
ṣaḍrātraṃ tu tadā proktaṃ prāyaścittaṃ manīṣibhiḥ ||
yadā daśanti śirasi krimayo vinatātmaja |
kṛcchraṃ tadācaret prājñaḥ śuddhaye vinatātmaja ||
parāśaraḥ |
gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ kṣīraṃ dadhi sarpiḥ kuśodakam |
tryahaṃ snātvā ca pītvā ca krimidaṣṭo viśudhyati ||
snātvā pītvā hutvā ceti kecit paṭhanti | [1138]
krimibhir vraṇabhūte 'ṅge makṣikābhis tu ghātite |
kṛcchārdhaṃ saṃprakurvīta śaktyā dadyāc dakṣiṇām ||
aṅgirāḥ: "krimivraṇadarśane sāṃtapanam | vṛṣabho dakṣiṇā |
śūdrāṇāṃ copavāsena śuddhir dānena caiva hi |
gāṃ pradadyād vṛṣaṃ caikaṃ tathā vipreṣu dakṣiṇām" || 3.277 ||
phalapuṣpānnarasajasattvaghāte ghṛtāśanaḥ |
syād oṣadhivṛthāchede kṣīrāśī gonugo dinam || 3.278 ||
yāni phaleṣūdumbaraprabhṛtiṣu, puṣpeṣu madhukādiṣu, anny caudanādau jāyante sattvāni teṣāṃ ghāte vadhe dinam ekaṃ ghṛtāśanaḥ syāt | ghṛtāśanaṃ caitad bhojanakārye tapastvāt | tathauṣadhīnāṃ grāmyāraṇyānāṃ vṛthā śāstrīyaprayojanam antareṇa chedane dinam ekaṃ gavām anugataḥ payovrataḥ syāt | manuḥ |
annādyajānāṃ sattvānām rasajānāṃ ca sarvaśaḥ |
phalapuṣpodbhavānāṃ ca ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati ||
ghāta ity anuvartate | tathā |
kṛṣṭajānām oṣadhīnāṃ jātānāṃ ca svayaṃ vane |
vṛthārambhe 'nugacched gāṃ dinam ekaṃ payovrataḥ ||
vṛthārambho niṣprayojanaṃ chedanam || 3.278 ||
kiṃ ca |
yan me 'dya reta etābhyāṃ skannaṃ reto 'numantrayet |
stanāntaraṃ bhruvor vāpi tathā(tenā)nāmikayā spṛśet || 3.279 ||
strīsaṃbhogam antareṇa skannaṃ reto yan me 'dya reta ityābhyāṃ mantrābhyām anumantrayet | tato 'nāmikayopakaniṣṭhikayā tena retasā stanayor bhruvor vāntaraṃ spṛśet | tataḥ śaucārthaṃ snānādi kuryāt | vihitatvād vā nāśucitvam astīti kalpyam | etad gṛhasthaviṣayam | anyāśramiṇāṃ prāyaścittāntaravidhānāt | kāmatas tu retaskandane yama āha |
gṛhasthaḥ kāmataḥ kuryād retasaḥ skandanaṃ bhuvi |
sahasraṃ tu japed devyāḥ prāṇāyāmais tribhiḥ saha || iti || 3.279 ||
[1139]
kiṃ ca |
mayi taja iti chāyāṃ svāṃ dṛṣṭvāmbuni vai japet |
sāvitrīm aśucau dṛṣṭe cāpale vānṛte 'pi ca || 3.280 ||
ātmīyāṃ chāyāṃ pratibimbam ambuni jale dṛṣṭvā "mayi tejaḥ" ityādikaṃ mantraṃ vājasaneye prasiddhaṃ japet | aśucau tu mūtrapurīṣādau (?) dṛṣṭe pāṇipādādicāpale 'nṛtabhāṣaṇe vā kṛte, sāvitrīṃ savitṛdai(de)vatyāṃ prasiddhām ṛcaṃ japet | manur asatyavacane saty ācamanam āha |
suptvā bhuktvā ca kṣutvā ca niṣṭhīvyoktvānṛtāni ca |
pītvāpo 'dhyeṣyamāṇaś ca ācāmet prayato 'pi san || iti |
etad gāyatrījapena samuccīyate kāryabhedāt | ācamanasya hi śucitvaṃ kāryaṃ gāyatrījapasya ca pāpakṣayaḥ | yat tu saṃvartenoktam,
kṣute niṣṭhīvite caiva dantocchiṣṭe tathānṛte |
patitānāṃ ca saṃbhāṣe dakṣiṇe śravaṇaṃ spṛśet ||
iti, tad asāmarthyāj jalābhāvād vācamanāsaṃbhave sati draṣṭavyam | evaṃ tāvat strīśūdraviṭkṣatralakṣaṇeṣūpapātakaviśeṣeṣu prāyaścittā[ni] uktāḥ(uktāni) | tatra prasaṅgād vidhyantareṣv api tadantaram | vṛkṣādichedane 'pi tatprasaṅgāt prāyaścittāntarāṇy uktāni ninditādyopajīvanalakṣaṇa upapātake sāmānyopadiṣṭam eva prāyaścittam | nāstike tu yady api prāyaścittaviśeṣo noktas tathāpi śāstrāntaroktaḥ saṃpradarśyate | tatra vasiṣṭhaḥ: "nāstikaḥ kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caritvā viramen nāstikyāt | tathā nāstikyavṛttes tv atikṛcchaḥ" | nāstikyena vṛttir jīvanaṃ yasya sa nāstikyavṛttiḥ | "kṛtaghnaḥ kūṭavyavahārī mithyāśaṃsīty ete pañca saṃvatsaraṃ brāhmaṇagṛhe bhaikṣaṃ careyuḥ" iti | pañca nāstikādayaḥ saṃvatsaraṃ yāvad vipragṛhe bhaikṣaṃ careyur ity arthaḥ | nāstiko nāstikyavṛttir ity anuvṛttau hārītaḥ: "pañcatapobhrāvakāśajalaśayanāny anutiṣṭheyuḥ | grīṣmavarṣāhemanteṣu" iti | ṛtutrayātmakaṃ saṃvatsarābhiprāyeṇaitat sāṃvatsarikam eva vratam || 3.280 ||
[1140]
saṃprati vratalopākhyāvakīrṇitānirūpaṇapūrvakaṃ tatprāyaścittam āha |
avakīrṇī bhaved gatvā brahmacārī tu yoṣitam |
gardabhaṃ paśum ālabhya nairṛtaṃ sa viśudhyati || 3.281 ||
brahmacārī naiṣṭhika itaro vā yoṣitaṃ striyaṃ gatvāvakīrṇisaṃjño bhavati | tatra ca gatveti ktvāpratyayāt kriyānivṛttāv eva satyām avakīrṇīti gamayati | so 'vakīrṇī nairṛtadevatākaṃ gardabhaṃ paśum ālabhya śudhyati pāpān mucyate | gardabhena mṛtyum iṣṭvā śudhyatīty arthaḥ | paśuśabdaprayogo gṛhyoktapaśukalpaprāptyarthaḥ | atra ca manuḥ |
avakīrṇī tu kāṇena gardabhena catuṣpathe |
pākayajñavidhānena nairṛtaṃ yajate niśi ||
hutvāgnau vidhivad dhomān antataś ca sam ity ṛcā |
vātendraguruvahnīnāṃ juhuyāt sarpiṣāhutīḥ ||
kāmato retasaḥ sekaṃ vratasthasya dvijanmanaḥ |
atikramaṃ vratasyāhur dharmajñā brahmavādinaḥ ||
atra gardabhasya kāṇatvaṃ viśeṣa uktaḥ | tathā pākayajñavidhānākhyā(khya)gṛhyoktetikartavyatāprayogaḥ pradhānāhutayaś ca | pūrvaṃ vātāya svāhetyādibhiś caturbhir mantrair ājyahomaḥ | rātriś ca kālaḥ sa cāmāvāsyāyām | uktaṃ hi taittirīyake: "yo brahmacāry avakired amāvāsyāyāṃ rātrāv agniṃ praṇīyopasamādhāya" ityādi | kāmato retasaḥ sekaṃ yoṣatīti vyākhyeyam, yājñavalkyavacanāt | sarvaṃ caitat karmāraṇyacatuṣpathe laukike cāgnau kāryam | tad āha vasiṣṭhaḥ: "brahmacārī cet striyam upeyād araṇye catuṣpathe laukikāgau rakṣodaivataṃ gardabhaṃ paśum ālabheta, nairṛtaṃ vā caruṃ nirvapet tasya juhuyāt | kāmāya svāhā, kāmakāmāya svāhā, nirṛtyai vāhā, rakṣodevatābhyaḥ svāhā" iti | paśor abhāve caruḥ kāryaḥ | tasya caror ekadeśaṃ kāmāya svāhetyādibhiś caturbhir mantraiḥ pratyekaṃ juhuyāt | tad evātidiśati: "etad eva retasaḥ prayatnotsarge divā svapne vrāntareṣu vāsamāvartanāt" iti | etad evety evakāro homena samuccīyamānasya sāṃvatsarikasya nivāraṇārthaḥ | tataś ca yoṣiti reto 'vakirato brahmacāriṇaḥ sāṃvatsarikaṃ tapo 'pi prāyaścittaṃ bhavati | tad āha manuḥ | [1141]
etasminn enasi prāpte vasitvā gardabhājinam |
saptāgāraṃ cared bhaikṣaṃ svakarma parikīrtayan ||
tebhyo labdhena bhaikṣeṇa vartayen naikakālikam |
upaspṛśaṃs triṣavaṇam abdena sa viśudhyati ||
svapne tu retaḥsektṝn manur evāha |
svapne siktvā brahmacārī dvijaḥ śukram akāmataḥ |
snātvārkam arcayitvā triḥ punar mām ity ṛcaṃ japet ||
yamaḥ |
prakīrya retaḥ svapne tu brahmacāry apy akāmataḥ |
snātvārkam īkṣya prayato gāyatryaṣṭaśataṃ japet ||
tathā |
brahmacārī tu yo gacchet striyaṃ kāmaprapīḍitaḥ |
prājāpatyaṃ caret kṛcchram abdam ekaṃ suyantritaḥ ||
bhūyo'bhyāsaviṣayam etat | vyāsaḥ |
brahmacārī tu yaḥ skandet kāmataḥ śukram ātmanaḥ |
avakīrṇivrataṃ kuryāt snātvā śuddhyed akāmataḥ ||
gautamaḥ: "retaḥskandane bhaye roge svapne 'gnīndhanabhaikṣacaraṇāni saptarātram akṛtvājyahomaḥ samidho vā retasyābhyām" | bhavadamīka(punarmām aity punar mana) iti dve ṛcau retasye || 3.281 ||
prasaṅgād brahmacāriṇaṃ prati prāyaścittāntaram āha |
bhaikṣāgnikārye tyaktvā tu saptarātram anāturaḥ |
kāmāvakīrṇa ity ābhyāṃ hutvā cājyāhutidvayam || 3.282 ||
upasthānadvayaṃ kuryāt samāsiñcatv anena tu |
anāturo vyādhirahito brahmacārī saptāhorātrāṇi yāvad yadi bhaikṣād anyad aśnāti, agnikāryaṃ vā na karoti, tadā kāmāvakīrṇo 'smy avakīrṇo 'smi kāmāya svāhā, kāmāvapanno 'smy avapanno 'smi kāmakāmāya svāyety etābhyāṃ mantrābhyām āhutidvayaṃ hutvā saṃ mā siñcantu maruta ity anenāgnim upatiṣṭheta | etac ca guruśuśrūṣādikāryāntaravyagratve sati draṣṭavyam | tadadbhāve tu manur āha | [1142]
akṛtvā bhaikṣacaraṇam asamidhya ca pāvakam |
anāturaḥ saptarātram avakīrṇivrataṃ caret || iti |
atraiva bṛhaspatiḥ |
saṃdhyopāsanam uddiṣṭaṃ prathamaṃ brahmacāriṇaḥ |
agnīndhanaṃ bhakṣacaryā ceti kurvīta pratyaham ||
asya trayasyākaraṇād vrati rogavivarjitaḥ |
avakīrṇivrataṃ kuryāt saptarātraṃ na saṃśayaḥ ||
hārītaḥ: "brahmacārī saṃdhyām upāsyotthitaḥ sāvitryāḥ sahasreṇādityam upatiṣṭheta, utkramyāgnikāryaṃ vrātapatyāhutyātītaṃ saṃpādyopasthitaṃ kuryāt | ahany atīte punar manovrātapatībhyāṃ mekhalādaṇḍājinayajñopavītakamaṇḍalunāśe ca trayyāhutyā yathārthaṃ pratīyāt" | praṇavo vyāhṛtayaḥ sāvitrīti trayī | "asadbhaikṣabhojanābhyuditanirmuktavānta-divāsvapnaskanditanagnastrīdarśaneṣu śmaśānam ākramya hayādīṃś cāruhya pūjyātikrame caitābhir eva juhuyāt | evaheṣanamiti kūṣmāṇḍībhir ājyaṃ juhuyāt | maṇivāsogavādīnāṃ pratigrahe sāvitryaṣṭasahasraṃ japet" iti | paiṭhīnasiḥ: "naṣṭe daṇḍakāṣṭhe bhakṣaṃ datvā brāhmaṇāya tad ahar upavaset | kamaṇḍalāv apy evam eva naṣṭāyāṃ mekhalāyāṃ dve ṛcāv uddhared iyaṃ durkteti" | saṃvartaḥ |
bhikṣāṭanam akṛtvā yaḥ svastho hy eko 'nnam aśnute |
asnātvā caiva yo bhuṅkte gāyatryaṣṭaśataṃ japet ||
upāsīta na cat saṃdyām agnikāryaṃ na cet kṛtam |
snātvā sūryaṃ samabhyarcya prāṇāyāmena śudhyati || [1143]
divā svapiti cet svastho brahmacārī kathaṃcana |
snātvā sūryaṃ samabhyarcya gāyatryaṣṭaśataṃ japet ||
brahmasūtraṃ vinā yena saṃdhyopāstir yadā kṛtā |
gāyatryaṣṭasahasraṃ tu japet snātvā samāhitaḥ ||
brahmasūtraṃ vinā yas tu bhojanaṃ kurute dvijaḥ |
gāyatryaṣṭaśatenaiva prāṇāyāmena śudhyati ||
yamaḥ |
sūryodaye tu yaḥ śete sa sūryodita ucyate |
astaṃgate tu yaḥ śete sūryanirmukta eva saḥ |
brahmastenāv ubhau samyag ahorātroṣitau śucī |
gāyatryā daśasāhasraṃ kuryātām āhnikaṃ pṛthak ||
sūryodito 'har upoṣitaḥ | sūryanirmukto rātim upoṣitaḥ | evam ubhāv ahorātropoṣitau gāyatryā daśādhikaṃ sā(sa)hasrajapam ahani kuryātām | vasiṣṭhaḥ: "sūryābhyuditaḥ sann ahas tiṣṭhet sāvitrīṃ japet | evaṃ sūryābhinirmukto rātrāv āsīta" | viṣṇuḥ: "sūryābhyudito nirmuktaḥ sacailasnātaḥ sāvitryaṣṭaśatam āvartayet" | saṃdhyādhikāre baudhāyanaḥ: "tatra sāyam atikrame rātryupavāsaḥ, prātaratikrame 'harupavāsaḥ" | āpastambaḥ: "svapann abhinirmukto 'naśnan vāgyato rātrim āsīta, śvobhūta udakam upaspṛśya vācaṃ visṛjet | svayam abhyudito 'naśnan vāgyato 'has tiṣṭhed ātamitaḥ prāṇān āyacched ity eke" | anaśnann abhuñjānaḥ | ātamita āklāntaḥ | astagamanābhiprāyaṃ vā || 3.282 ||
kiṃ ca |
madhumāṃsāśane kāryaḥ kṛcchraḥ śeṣo vratāni ca || 3.283 ||
brahmacāriṇā madhubhakṣaṇe śiṣṭabhojanīyamāṃsabhakṣaṇe ca prājāpatyaḥ kṛcchraḥ kāryaḥ | upakrāntasya brahmacaryasya vedavratānāṃ vānyatamasya śeṣaḥ kāryaḥ | punar brahmacaryārambhaśaṅkānivṛttyartho 'yaṃ vrataśeṣakartavyatāvidhiḥ | tadāśaṅkā ca punar upanayanavidhānāt | tathā ca saṃvartaḥ |
brahmacārī tu yo 'śnīyān madhmaṃśe kathaṃcana |
sa kṛtvā prākṛtaṃ kṛcchraṃ mauñjīhomena śudhyati || iti |
māṃsasya ca śiṣṭabhojanīyasya bhakṣaṇa etat | yad āha vasiṣṭhaḥ: [1144] "brahmacārī cen māṃsam aśnīyāc chiṣṭabhojanīyaṃ kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caritvā vrataśeṣaṃ samāpayet" iti | etac cāvyādhitasya | vyādhitasya tu madhumāṃsabhakṣaṇe 'pi na doṣa ity āha sa eva: "sa ced vyādhīyīta(dhitaḥ) kāmaṃ guror ucchiṣṭaṃ bheṣajārthaṃ sarvaṃ prāśnīyāt" iti | madhumāṃsādy avipratiṣiddham | akāmopanate ca madhubhakṣaṇe 'pi na doṣa ity āha sa eva: "akāmopanataṃ madhu vājasaneyake na duṣyatīti vijñāyate" | vājasaneyaṃ yajuḥśākhāntaram | brahmacāryadhikāre paiṭhīnasiḥ: "madhumāṃsaprāśane trirātraṃ punar upanayanaṃ ca" | prājāpatyāsamarthaviṣayam etat | manuḥ |
māsikānnaṃ tu yo 'śnīyād asamāvṛttako dvijaḥ |
sa trīṇy ahāny upavased ekāhaṃ codake vaset ||
māsikam atra pratimāsaṃ mṛtāhanimittaṃ yad vihitaṃ tad grāhyam, na punar āmāvāsyam,
sūtikānnaṃ navaśrāddhaṃ māsikānnaṃ tathaiva ca |
brahmacārī tu yo 'śnīyāt trirātreṇa sa śudhyati ||
iti saṃvartavākyena navaśrāddhasāhacaryāt | baudhāyanaḥ: "sa ced vyādhīyīta(dhitaḥ) kamaṃ guror ucchiṣṭaṃ bheṣajārthaṃ sarvam aśnīyāt | yenecchet tena viciktset | sa yadāgado bhavati tadotthāyādityam upatiṣṭheta, haṃsaḥ śuciṣad ity etayarcā" | agado rogarahitaḥ | vṛddhayājñavalkyaḥ |
brahmacārī vratī caiva brāhmaṇī vidhavā ca yā |
aśitvā madhumāṃsāni trirātreṇaiva śudhyati || iti |
yamaḥ |
madhu māṃsaṃ ca yo 'śnīyāc chrāddhaṃ sūtakam eva ca |
prājāpatyaṃ caret kṛcchraṃ vrataśeṣaṃ samāpayet ||
chardanābhāva etat | śātātapaḥ |
sūtake tu yadā vipro brahmacārī viśeṣataḥ |
pibet pānīyam ajñānāt samaśnīyāt spṛśeta vā || [1145]
pānīyapāne kurvīta pañcagavyasya bhakṣaṇam |
trirātraṃ bhojane proktaṃ spṛṣṭvā snānaṃ vidhīyate ||
viṣṇuḥ: "brahmacāry āmaśrāddhāśane trirātram upavaset" | hārītaḥ |
brahmacārī tu yo bhuṅkte śūdrānnaṃ jñānadurbalaḥ |
parākeṇa viśuddhiḥ syān niṣkṛtir nānyathā bhavet ||
ucchiṣṭaṃ kṣatriyaviśoḥ śūdrocchiṣṭam athāpi vā |
brahmacārī yadāśnīyāt tasya śuddhiḥ kathaṃ bhavet ||
snānaṃ tathaiva kṛtvā tu gāyatryaṣṭaśataṃ japet |
upoṣya dvādaśadinam evaṃ śuddhiḥ pṛthak pṛthak ||
evam eva gṛhasthasya pādonam iti niścayaḥ ||
brāhmaṇakṣatriyaviśāṃ yathākramaṃ kṣatriyavaiśyaśūdrocchiṣṭaprāśana etat | etac ca brāhmaṇasya vaiśyaśūdrocchiṣṭaṃ kṣatriyasya ca śūdrocchiṣṭam aśnataḥ kiṃcid adhikaṃ kalpyam | devalaḥ |
sūtānnaṃ madhu māṃsaṃ ca yady aśnāti vratī kvacit |
trirātropoṣitaḥ samyag rātrim ekāṃ jale vaset ||
anena rātrau jalavāso vihitaḥ | manunā tv ahani, tato vikalpaḥ | doṣabhūyastve tu samuccayaḥ || 3.283||
brahmacāriprasaṅgād guruviṣayaṃ kiṃcid āha |
kṛcchratrayaṃ guruḥ kuryāt prahito mriyate yadi |
brahmacārī guruṇā prahitaḥ preṣitaḥ śakyam ānārthaviṣaye yadi mriyate tadā guruḥ prājāpatyapramukhāṃs trīn kṛcchrān ācaret | atra pūrvavākyād brahmacārīti śabdo 'nuvartate | ata eva brahmacāriprāyaścitte prakṛte prasaṅgād guroḥ prāyaścittam upadiśyate |
pratikūlaṃ guroḥ kṛtvā prsādyaiva viśudhyati || 3.284 ||
guroḥ pratikūlaṃ duḥkhakaraṃ kṛtvā taṃ prasādya vyapagataduḥkam akopaṃ ca kṛtvā viśudhyaty apāpo bhavati | atra vasiṣṭhaḥ: "guroś cālīkanirbandhe sacailasnāto guruṃ prasādayet pūto bhavatīti vijñāyate" | [1146] etad amatipūrvakaṃ sakṛd alpāparādhaviṣayam | atyantasānubandhamatipūrvakamahāparādhe tu guruprātikūlye dvādaśavārṣikabrahmahavrataprāptyartham uktaṃ manunā "parirabhya guruṃ tathā" iti | yad vā pitṛviṣayaṃ mānavaṃ vāsiṣṭhaṃ vā, yājñavalkīyaṃ caupacārikaguruviṣayam | viṣṇuḥ: "samutkarṣānṛte guroś cālīkanirbandhe tadākṣāraṇe ca māsaṃ payasā varteta" | svalpābhyāsaviṣayam idam || 3.284 ||
sāmānyataḥ prāpte prāyaścittanimittatve kvacid viśeṣe tadapavādam āha |
kriyamāṇopakāre tu mṛte vipre na pātakam |
upakāram uddiśya cikitsādau vā kriyamāṇe yady upakartavyo viprādiḥ prāṇī mriyate, tadauṣadhādikartur hiṃsānimittaṃ na pātakaṃ bhavatīti cikitsādi ca samyag jñātvā yatnena ca kurvatāṃ pātakābhāvaḥ | ajñānād anādarād vā tatkriyānimitte prāṇimaraṇe bhavaty eva tatkartuḥ pāpayogaḥ | ata eva yatnagrahaṇaṃ saṃvartaḥ karoti |
yatnena ca cikitsārthe gūḍhagarbhavimocane |
yatne kṛte vipattiḥ syāt prāyaścittaṃ na vidyate || iti |
vipragrahaṇaṃ prāṇimātrapradarśanārtham |
mithyābhiśāpakartuḥ prāyaścittam āha |
mahāpāpopapāpābhyāṃ yo 'bhiśaṃsen mṛṣā param || 3.285 ||
abbhakṣo māsam āsīta sa jāpī niyatendriyaḥ |
mahāpāpena brahmahatyādinopapāpena govadhādinā vā yo mṛṣā mithyā parama abhiśaṃsed anenedaṃ pāpaṃ kṛtam iti saṃvyavahartṝn prati brūyāt, asau māsam udakabhakṣaḥ saṃyatendriyo japann āsīta | mahāpatakaśaṃsane sakṛt kṛte, upapātakaśaṃsane cāsakṛt kṛte prāyaścittam idaṃ draṣṭavyam | mahāpāpopapāpābhyām iti viśeṣaṇe tṛtīyā | yaj japanīyaṃ tad āha vasiṣṭhaḥ: [1147] "brahmahatyām anṛtenābhiśasya patanīyenopapatanīyena vā māsam abbhakṣaḥ śuddhaḥ śuddhavatīr āvartayed aśvamedhāvabhṛthaṃ vā gacched etenaivābhiśasto vyākhyātaḥ" | mithyābhiśasto 'py evam eva prāyaścittaṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ | śaṅkhalikhitau: "nāstikaḥ kṛaghnaḥ kūṭavyavahārī brāhmaṇavṛttighno mithyābhiśaṃsī cety ete ṣaḍvarṣāṇi brāhmaṇagṛheṣu bhaikṣaṃ careyuḥ | saṃvatsaraṃ vā dhautabhaikṣaṃ aśnīyuḥ | ṣaṇmāsān vā gā anugaccheyuḥ" iti | etad atyantābhyāse sānubandhe tadabhiśaṃsananimitte ca brāhmaṇasyāṅkananirvāsanādidaṇḍaprāptau satyāṃ veditavyam | brāhmaṇam abhiśaṃsato yāny uktāni prāyaścittāni tatrānulomye tu varṇānām abhiśāpe prāyaścittahrāsaḥ, prātilomye tu tadvṛddhiḥ kalpyā | ata eva,
prātilomyāpavāde tu dviguṇatriguṇā damāḥ |
varṇānām ānulomyena tasmād ardhārdhahānitaḥ ||
iti prātilomyānulomyavarṇābhiśape vṛddhihrāsau daṇḍasyoktau | yamaḥ |
krodhād uktvā tv agamyātvaṃ bhāryāyāṃ hi naraḥ kvacit |
prāyaścittaṃ caret kṛcchraṃ tasya pāpasya śuddhaye ||
prājāpatyaṃ cared vipraḥ kṣatriyo divasān nava |
ṣaḍrātraṃ tu cared vaiśyas trirātraṃ śūdra ācaret ||
suvarcalādhikāre hārītaḥ |
anṛtābhiśaṃsanākrośe gurūṇāṃ paiśuneṣu ca |
ekaviṃśatirātraṃ tu pibet tām eva yatnataḥ ||
tām eva suvarcalām ity arthaḥ | śātātapaḥ: "devagurubrāhmaṇātikrame trisāhasro japo gāyatryāḥ" | āpastambaḥ: "anākrośyam ākrośyānṛtaṃ coktvā trirātram akṣāralavaṇaṃ bhojanaṃ śūdrasya saptarātram abhojanaṃ strīṇāṃ caitat" | viṣṇuḥ |
viprasya vadhasaṃyuktaṃ kṛtvā tu śapathaṃ mṛṣā |
brāhmaṇo yāvakānnena tīvraṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret ||
kṣatriyasya parākaṃ syāt prājāpatyaṃ tathā viśaḥ |
vṛṣalasya trirātraṃ tu vrataṃ brāhmaṇavac caret || [1148]
śaṅkhaḥ: "ākrośānṛtavādeṣu ekarātram upavāsaḥ" || 3.285 ||
abhiśaṃsakaprāyaścittaprasaṅgenābhiśastaprāyaścittam āha |
abhiśasto mṛṣā kṛcchraṃ cared āgneyam eva ca || 3.286 ||
nirvapeta puroḍāśaṃ vāyavyaṃ carum eva vā |
mithyābhiśastaḥ prāyaścittaṃ caret | āgneyam agnidai(de)vatyaṃ caruṃ puroḍāśaṃ vā yad vā vāyavyaṃ caruṃ puroḍāśaṃ nirvapeta | ete ca pakṣā yena pāpenābhiśatās tadgauravalāghavavaśena śaktyanubandhādivaśena vā vyavasthāpyāḥ | yat tu vasiṣṭhena mithyābhiśāpena māsaṃ yāvad abbhakṣatāyāṃ śuddhavatījapaṃ ca vidhāyoktam: "etenaivābhiśasto vyākhyātaḥ" iti, tan mahāpāpenābhiśastasya draṣṭavyam | abhiśastasya cānaparādhino 'pi parivitter iva vacanāt prāyaścittam | yac ca paiṭhīnasinoktam: "anṛtenābhiśasyamānaḥ kṛcchraṃ caren māsaṃ pātakeṣu dvimāsam" iti, tad bahubhiḥ pātakair abhiśastasya veditavyam | pātakeṣu mahāpātakeṣv iti yāvat | baudhāyanaḥ: "pātakābhiśaṃsane kṛcchraḥ | tadardham abhiśaste" | alpataradoṣābhiśāpaviṣayam etat || 3.286 ||
kiṃ ca |
aniyukto bhrātṛjāyāṃ gacchaṃś cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || 3.287 ||
śāstrīyaviniyogam antareṇa jyeṣṭhasya kaniṣṭhasya vā bhrātur jāyāṃ gacchaṃś cāndrāyaṇaṃ vakṣyamāṇam ācaret | etac ca sakṛd amatipūrvake gamane matipūrvake vā prākkriyāsiddher nivṛttau viplutabhrātṛjāyāviṣayaṃ vā | abhyāse tv amatipūrvake śaṅkha āha: "parivittiḥ parivettā ca saṃvatsaraṃ brāhmaṇagṛheṣu bhaikṣaṃ careyātām | jyeṣṭhabhāryāyām aniyukto gacchaṃs tad eva kaniṣṭhabhāryāṃ vā" iti | matipūrvake sābhyāse tu gurutalpavratātideśaḥ || 3.287 ||
kiṃ ca |
trirātrānte ghṛtaṃ prāśya gatvodakyāṃ viśudhyati |
[1149]
udakyāṃ rajasvalāṃ svabhāryāṃ gatvā trirātram upoṣya tadante ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati, pāpād asmād vimucyate | etac ca svabhāryāyāṃ matipūrvake sakṛd gamane | amatipūrvake tu sakṛd gamane śātātapa āha: "anudakamūtrapurīṣakaraṇāt sacailasnānaṃ mahāvyāhṛtibhir homaś ca, rajasvalābhigamane caitad eva | amānuṣīṣu govarjam" iti | paradāreṣu matipūrvake sakṛd gamane vasiṣṭha āha: "rajasvalāvyavāye kṛṣṇaṃ vṛṣabhaṃ dadyāc chuklaliṅgam" iti | vyavāyo maithunam, liṅgaṃ lāṅchanam | yat tu manunoktam,
amānuṣīṣu puruṣa udakyāyām ayoniṣu |
retaḥ siktvā jale caiva kṛcchraṃ sāṃtapanaṃ caret |
iti, tan matipūrvake sābhyāse ca parastrīgamane | asminn eva viṣaye 'nicchantīṃ gacchantaṃ prati saṃvartaḥ |
rajasvalāṃ tu yo gacched garbhiṇīṃ patitāṃ tathā |
tasya pāpaviśuddhyartham atikṛcchraṃ viśodhanam ||
śaṅkhalikhitau: "rajasvalāvadhūtābhigamane trirātram upavāso ghṛtaprāśanaṃ ca" | avadhūtā bandhakī | tadgamanaṃ ca pṛthaṅnimittam | kāmato 'tyantābhyāse śrotriyabhāryāyāṃ garbhaparyante gamane dvādaśavārṣikaṃ prakṛtya śaṅkha āha: "pādaṃ tu śūdrahatyāyām udakyāgamane tathā" | śūdrahatyāyām udakyāgamane ca nimitte dvādaśavārṣikasya pādaṃ traivārṣikaṃ vrataṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ | asminn eva garbhābhāve śrotriyapatnītvābhāve vā bṛhaspatir āha |
akāmāt pukkasīṃ gatvā kāmād gatvā rajasvalām |
śiṣyabhāryāṃ naro gatvā parākeṇa viśudhyati ||
śātātapaḥ: "rajasvalāgamane saptarātram" | mānavena samānaviṣya[m ida]m | iha sarvatra parabhāryārajasvalāgamane pāradāryaprāyaścittaṃ pṛthakkāryam anyonyanirapekṣanimittabhedāt | yatra tu nimittāntarāvinābhūtaṃ tatraikam eva naimittikaṃ prāyaścittam | yathā gurutalpagamane | pāradāryāvinābhāvi hi gurutalpagamanam | tatra hi rajasvalāgamanaṃ pṛthaṅnimittam | avinābhāvavirahāt | evaṃ divāgamanaṃ parvagamanaṃ ca | brahmahatyāṃ ca kurvataḥ śastrodyamanādinimittaṃ pṛthakkāryam eva, tena vināpi hiṃsā bhavaty eva | yathā vastrādinā mukhanāsikāpidhānād viṣadānād bhṛgupātanād iti ||
[1150]
asatpratigrahaprāyaścittam āha |
goṣṭhe vasan brahmacārī māsam ekaṃ payovrataḥ || 3.288 ||
gāyatrījapyanirato mucyate 'satparigrahāt |
goṣṭhe gonivāsasthāna eva vasan brahmacārī striyam anugacchan paya eva vratayan piban sāvitrījapodyataḥ | evaṃ māsam ekaṃ sātatyena vartamāno 'satparigrahān mucyate | asato ninditāt pratigraho 'satpratigrahaḥ | na punar asataḥ pratiṣiddhapratigrahasya pratigraho 'satpratigrahaḥ | na hi tad upapātakeṣu paṭhitam | ninditebhyo dhanādānaṃ tu manunopapātakam uktam | goṣṭhe vāsādy apy etat prāyaścittatvena sa evāha |
japitvā trīṇi sāvitryāḥ sahasrāṇi samāhitaḥ |
māsaṃ goṣṭhe payaḥ pītvā mucyate 'satpratigrahāt || iti |
pratigṛhītaṃ dhanaṃ tyaktvaiva prāyaścittaṃ kāryam | tad āha manuḥ |
yad garhitenārjayanti karmaṇā brāhmaṇā dhanam |
tad utsargeṇa śudhyanti japena tapasaiva ca || iti |
kaśyapaḥ |
asatpratigrahe māsaṃ vasan goṣṭhe payaḥ pibet |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ vā goghnasya yathoktaṃ vratam ācaret ||
cāndrāyaṇam ācaret | goghnasya vā traimāsikaṃ vā vratam ācared ity arthaḥ | caturviṃśatimatāt |
bhikṣāmātre gṛhīte tu puṇyaṃ mantram udīrayet |
pratigraheṣu sarveṣu ṣaṣṭhāṃśaṃ parikalpayet ||
japahomādikaṃ kuryāj jñātvā yajñapratigrahau |
pavitreṣṭyā viśudhyanti sarve ghorāḥ pratigrahaḥ ||
aindavena mṛgāreṣṭyā kadacin mitravindayā |
devyā lakṣajapenaiva mucyante duṣpratigrahāt ||
pratikalpayed viprebhyo dadyād ity arthaḥ | devī gāyatrī | uśanā |
patitād dravyam ādatte bhuṅkte vā brāhmaṇo yadi |
kṛtvā tasya samutsargam atikṛcchraṃ cared dvijaḥ || [1151]
hārītaḥ: "maṇivāsogavādīnāṃ pratigrahe sāvitryāḥ sahasraṃ japet | pañca madhyame daśottame dvādaśarātraṃ payovrataḥ | śatasahasram atipratigraheṣu" | madhyamapratigraheṣu pañca sahasrāṇi japet | pratigrāhyadravyamadhyamatvena ca pratigrahasya madhyamatvam | evam uttamapratigrahe daśa dahasrāṇi | atra devalaḥ |
navottamāni catvāri madhyamāni nidhānataḥ |
adhamānīti śeṣāṇi trividhatvam idaṃ viduḥ ||
annaṃ dadhi madhu trāṇaṃ gobhūrukmāśvahastinaḥ |
dānāny uttamadānāni uttamadravyadānataḥ ||
vidyād ācchādanāvāsaparibhogauṣadhāni ca |
dānāni madhyamānīti madhyamadravyadānataḥ ||
upānatpreṅkhayānāni chatrapātrāsanāni ca |
dīpakāṣṭhaphalādīni caramaṃ bahuvārṣikam ||
bahutvād arthajātānāṃ saṃkhyā śeṣeṣu neṣyate |
adhamāny avaśiṣṭāni sarvadānāny ato viduḥ ||
atidānaṃ chāndogye 'bhihitam |
trīṇy āhur atidānāni gāvaḥ pṛthvī sarasvatī | iti |
atra gāva iti bahuvacanama[pi] vivakṣitam | tena tadbahutvam uttamaḥ pratigrahaḥ | tathā |
rājñaḥ pratigrahaṃ kṛtvā māsam apsu sadā vaset |
ṣaṣṭhe kāle payo 'śnaṃs tu pūrṇe māse pramucyate ||
tarpayitvā dvijān kāmaiḥ satataṃ niyatavrataḥ |
tapasaivejyayā vāpi rājapāpāt pramucyate ||
sumantuḥ: "śūdrayājakaḥ sarvadravyaparityāgāt pūto bhavati" | baudhāyanaḥ: "bahupratigrāhyasya vā pratigṛhyānāśyasya vānnam aśitvāyājyaṃ vā yājayitvā pratigrāhyasya vātipratigṛhya taratsamandīyaṃ japet" || 3.288 ||
kiṃ ca |
trīn kṛcchrān ācared vrātyayājako 'bhicarann api || 3.289 ||
vedaplāvī yavāśy abdaṃ tyaktvā ca śaraṇāgatam |
vrātyānāṃ sāvitrīpatitānāṃ yājaka ṛtvikprājāpatyapramukhāṃs trīn [1152] vakṣyamāṇān kṛcchrān ācaret | etad evābhicāram api kurvann ācaret | abhicāraś ca baudhāyanena "ṣaṭsv abhicaran patati" ity agnidaprabhṛtiṣv ātatāyiṣu ṣaṭsu vihitaḥ | tadanya eva prāyaścittanimittam | manuḥ |
vrātyānāṃ yājanaṃ kṛtvā pareṣām antyakarma ca |
abhicāram ahīnaṃ ca tribhiḥ kṛcchrair viśudhyati ||
antyakarmāntyeṣṭiḥ | mūlyāya kurvata etan na dharmāya | kṣatriyādīnām antyakarma kurvato brāhmaṇasyaivaitat | dvirātrādir dvādaśarātrānto 'hargaṇo 'hīnaḥ | śātātapaḥ: "patitasāvitrīkān nopanayen nādhyāpayed ya etān upanayed adhyāpayed vā sa uddālakavrataṃ caret" | kāmato 'bhyāsaviṣayam etat | uddālakavratam uktam | śūdrapurohitayājakādhyāpakānuvṛttau yamaḥ |
teṣām api ca sarveṣāṃ pratyāpattiṃ ca yācatām |
bhaikṣānnam upayuñjāno dvijaś cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret ||
phalaṃ nāgahrade toye plāvayed avicāritam |
arthotsargeṇa yatnānte tasmāt pāpāt pramucyate ||
akṣāralavaṇāṃ rūkṣāṃ pītvā brahmasuvarcalām |
tasyopanayanaṃ kṛtvā vyavahāryas tato bhavet ||
phalaṃ śūdrād avāpto 'rthaḥ | yatnaḥ prāyaścittam | "trirātraṃ vāgyatā anaśnanta āsīran sahasraparamaṃ vā atd abhyasyantaḥ pūtā bhavantīti vijñāyate" iti anuvṛttau vasiṣṭhaḥ: "etenaiva garhitādhyāpakā vyākhyātāḥ | dakṣiṇātyāgāc ca pūtā bhavantīti vijñāyate" | tathā — vedaplāvī vedaviplāvakaḥ | pratiṣiddhe deśe kāle vā svayam aśuciś ca yo 'dhīte yac (yaś) ca pratiṣiddham adhyāpayati pratiṣiddhād adhīte cetyevamādir vedaviplāvakaḥ | tathā ca śaraṇāgatam ayaṃ me prāṇāṃs  trāsyata iti paribhāvyāgataṃ paritrāṇasamartho 'pi yaḥ parityajati tāv ubhau saṃvatsaraṃ tāvad yāv āśinau syātām | tathā ca manuḥ |
śaraṇāgataṃ parityajya vedaṃ viplāvya vā dvijaḥ |
saṃvatsaraṃ yavāhāras tat pāpam apakarṣati || [1153]
pitṛmātṛtyāgino 'pāṅktya(kta)madhyavartitayā prāyaścittaviśeṣo manunoktaḥ |
ṣaṣṭhānnakālatā māsaṃ saṃhitājapa eva vā |
homāś ca śākalā nityam apāṅktyā(ktā)nāṃ viśodhanam ||
apāṅktyā(ktā)ś ca śrāddhakalpe tenaiva darśitaḥ,
ye stenapatitaklībā ye ca nāstikavṛttayaḥ |
ityādinā | sutatyāgādiṣu sādhāraṇāny evopapātakaprāyaścittāni yathāśakti yathānubandham abhyāsapratyayāpratyayapūrvakaṃ cāpekṣya kalpyāni | atra hārītaḥ: "kanyādūṣī somavikrayī vṛṣalīpatiḥ kaumāradāratyāgī surāmadyapaḥ śūdrayājako guroḥ pratikartā nāstiko nāstikavṛttiḥ kṛtaghnaḥ kūṭavyavahārī brāhmaṇavṛttighno mithyābhiśaṃsī patitasaṃvyavahārī mitradhruk śaraṇāgataghātī pratirūpakavṛttir ity ete pañcatapobhrāvakāśajalaśayanāny anutiṣṭheyuḥ | grīṣmavarṣāhemanteṣu māsaṃ gomūtrayāvakam aśnīyuḥ" iti | atra ca kanyādūṣītiśabdena kanyādūṣakatvamātraṃ na vivakṣitaṃ kiṃ tu prātilomyena yaḥ kanyādūṣakaḥ sa eva, prāyaścittabhūyastvāt | atraiva vihitaṃ daṇḍabhūyastvaṃ smarati: "sakāmāsv ānulomyena na doṣas tv anyathā vadhaḥ" iti | vadatas (?) tatra savarṇakanyādūṣaṇe traimāsikaṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ vā doṣānusāreṇa kalpyam | prātilomyadūṣaṇe tu kṛcchrātikṛcchracaryā māsaṃ pañcagavyāśanam ityādi kalpyam | yat tu śaṅkhenoktam: "kanyādūṣī somavikrayī kṛcchram abdaṃ caretām" iti, tad api pratilomakanyādūṣaṇaviṣayam | prātilomye 'pi śūdrasya vadha eva, dūṣaṇe tu karacchedaḥ, "uttamāyāṃ vadhas tathā" iti daṇḍadarśanāt | svādhyāyatyāge prāyaścittaviśeṣam āha vasiṣṭhaḥ: "brahmojjhaḥ kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caritvā punar upayuñjīta vedam ācāryāt" | atra ca yāvanto dvādaśarātrasādhyāḥ kṛcchrās te sarve vidhīyante viśeṣavacanāt | tatrātyantāpadi svalpakāle ca vedatyāge prājāptyaḥ | adhikakāle [1154] tv atikṛcchraḥ | tato 'pi kālabhūyastve parāka ity anayā diśā kalpyam | yadā tu dhanārjanavyagratayā cirakālaṃ vedādhyayanaṃ tyajati, tadā sādhārāṇānām upapātakaprāyaścittānām ekaṃ prāyaścittaṃ kalpyam | tatra mandatarāyām āpadi brahmojjhasya traimāsikaṃ goghnaprāyaścittaṃ kalpyam | mandāyāṃ cāndrāyaṇam ity eṣa kalpanāprakāraḥ | yadi nāstikyād brahmojjho bhavati, tasya brahmahatyāsamaṃ jñeyam, "adhītasya ca nāśanam" iti tatsamatvavidheḥ | agnityāge 'pi vasiṣṭhenoktam: "yo 'gnīn apavidhyet sa kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caritvā punar ādhānaṃ kārayet" iti | atrāpi pūrva[vad?] dvādaśarātrasādhyānekakṛcchraprāptāv agnityāgakāraṇaṃ tattyāgakālālpatvaṃ bhūyastvaṃ cāpekṣya laghugurukṛcchradvādaśarātratvaṃ kalpyam | manuḥ |
agnihotry apavidhyāgnīn brāhmaṇaḥ kāmakārataḥ |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ caren māsaṃ vīrahatyāsamaṃ hi tat ||
māsam agnīn apavidhyety anvayaḥ | vīro yajamānaḥ | adhikakāle tu tattyāge traimāsikaṃ goghnaprāyaścittam | yat tu hārītenoktam: "saṃvatsarotsanne 'gnihotre cāndrāyaṇaṃ kṛtvā punar ādadyāt | dvivarṣotsanne somāyanacāndrāyaṇe kuryāt | trivarṣotsanne saṃvatsaraṃ kṛcchram abhyasya punar ādadyāt" iti, tad akāmato 'gnyutsāde | somāyanaṃ cāgre vakṣyate | śaṅkhaḥ: "agnyutsādī saṃvatsaraṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ cared gāṃ ca dadyāt" | saṃvatsaratrayotsannaviṣayam etat | saṃvatsaraṃ prākṛtaṃ brahmacaryaṃ cared ity anuvṛttau gautamaḥ: "agnyutsādinirākṛtyupapātakeṣu" | kṛcchrābdenordhvaṃ tulyam etat (?) | paiṭhīnasiḥ: "agnyutsādī taptakṛcchreṇa" | prāṅ māsād ādhānasyaitat | śaṅkhalikhitau: "agnyutsādī mithyādhītī ca saṃvatsaraṃ brāhmaṇagṛheṣu bhaikṣaṃ caretām" | prākṛtabrahmacaryātmīyena tulyam etat | mithyādhītī vedaviplāvakaḥ | atraiva viṣṇunokto viśeṣaḥ: "vedāgnyutsādī triṣavaṇasnāyī adhaḥśāyī saṃvatsaraṃ bhakṣeṇa vartayet" iti | bharadvājena tu yad uktam: [1155] "prāṇāyāmaś caturviṃśatirātrād upavāsaś ca triṃśadrātrāt tisro rātrīḥ ṣaṣṭirātrād ata ūrdhvam ā saṃvatsarāt prājāpatyam ata ūrdhvaṃ kālabahutve doṣabahutve doṣagurutvam" iti, tad gṛhyāgnyutsādiviṣayam | "dvādaśātikrame tryaham upavāso māsātikrame dvādaśāhaṃ saṃvatsarātikrame māsopavāsaḥ payobhakṣaṇaṃ vā" iti, tad api kāmato gṛhyāgnyutsādiviṣayam eva | sutatyāgo yadi vikrayātmakas tatprāyaścittam āha pracetāḥ: "anṛtavāk taskaro rājabhṛtyo vṛkṣāropakavṛttir garado 'gnido 'śvagajarathārohavṛttī raṅgopajīvī śvāgaṇikaḥ śūdropādhyāyo bhāṇḍiko nakṣatropajīvī śvavṛttir brahmajīvī cikitsako devalakaḥ purohitaḥ kitavo madyapaḥ kūṭakārako 'patyavikrayī manuṣyapaśuvikretā ceti | tān uddhareyuḥ sametya nyāyato brāhmaṇā vyavasthayā sarvatyāgakāle caturthakālāhārāḥ saṃvatsaraṃ triṣavaṇam upaspṛśeyuḥ | tasyānte devapitṛtarpaṇaṃ gavāhnikaṃ cety evaṃ vyavahāryāḥ" iti | etac ca kāmato 'tyantābhyāsaviṣayam | atra ca śrāddhaprakaraṇoddiṣṭā apāṅktā uktās teṣām idaṃ vā manūktaṃ vā "ṣaṣṭhānnakālatā māsam" ityādinoktaṃ prāyaścittaṃ bhavati | ye cātropapātakinaḥ kecid viśeṣato na saṃveśitās teṣāṃ sāmānyavihitānām upapātakaprāyaścittānām anyatamaṃ prāpnoti | tasminn eva viṣaye baudhāyano 'py āha: "athāśucikaraṇāni dyūtam abhicāram anāhitāgner uñchavṛttiḥ samāvṛttasya bhaikṣacaryā tasya gurukule vāsa ūrdhvaṃ caturbhyo māsebhyo yac (yaś) ca tam adhyāpayati nakṣatranirdeśakaś ceti | dvādaśamāsān dvādaśārdhamāsān dvādaśa dvādaśāhān dvādaśa ṣaḍahān dvādaśa tryahān dvādaśāhaṃ ṣaḍahaṃ tryaham ekāham ity aśucikaranirveśaḥ" iti | [1156] śvāgaṇiko yaḥ śvagaṇena jīvati | bhāṇḍiko 'sabhārhavākyaḥ | nirveśaḥ prāyaścittam | tathā: "samudrayānaṃ brāhmaṇanyāsāpaharaṇaṃ sarvapaṇyair vyavaharaṇaṃ bhūmyanṛtaṃ śūdrasevā yaś ca śūdrāyām api jāyate tadapatyaś ca bhavati teṣāṃ tu nirveśaḥ | caturthakālāmitabhojinaḥ syur apo 'bhyupeyuḥ savanānukalpam | sthānāsanābhyāṃ viharanta ete tribhir varṣaiḥ svakīyaṃ pāpaṃ ghnanti" iti | etac ca bhūyobhyāsaviṣayam | hīnayoniniṣevaṇe śātātapaḥ prāyaścittaviśeṣam āha: "brāhmaṇo rānyāpūrvī kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caritvā nirviśeta tāṃ copayaccheta | vaiśyāpūrvī taptakṛcchraṃ śūdrāpūrvī kṛcchrātikṛcchraṃ rājanyaś ced vaiśyāpūrvī kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caritvā nirviśeta tāṃ copayaccheta" iti | rājanyaiva pūrvā prathamabhāryā yasya sa rājanyāpūrvī sa savarṇām udvaheta | tāṃ ca rājakanyām upayaccheta, svī kuryāt | evam anyatrāpi | etad asamarthaviṣayam | samarthasya tu sādhāraṇaṃ traimāsikaṃ cāndrāyaṇādi bhavati | hīnayoniniṣevaṇena hīnavarṇastrīgamanaṃ vivakṣitam | tac ca yathāvarṇakrameṇa pariṇītāyā gamane nopapātakam, nāpi paradāratve sati śakyaṃ vaktum, tasya pāradāryaśabdenaiva gṛhītatvāt | tasmād vyutkramapariṇītāgamanaviṣayo veśyāgamanaviṣayo vā hīnayoniniṣevaṇaśabdo vyākhyeyaḥ | yad vāntyajātistrīgamanaviṣayaḥ | tatra saṃvartenokto veśyāgamane prāyaścittaviśeṣaḥ "paśuveśyāgamane prājāpatyo vidheyaḥ" iti | sakṛd amatipūrvakagamanaviṣayaṃ caitat | yat tu yamenoktam,
veśyāgamanajaṃ pāpaṃ vyapohanti dvijātayaḥ |
pītvā sakṛt sakṛt taptaṃ saptarātraṃ kuśodakam ||
iti, tad akāmato dvis trir abhyāse kāmato vā sakṛdgamane draṣṭavyam | yat tu yamenaiva veśyāgamane gurutalpavratādikam uktam, yathā [1157]
gurutalpavrataṃ kecit kecic cāndrāyaṇaṃ vratam |
goghanasyecchanti kecic ca kecid apy avakīrṇinaḥ ||
iti, tan matipūrvakābhyāsaviṣayatayā yojyam | anāśramavāse hārīta āha: "anāśramī saṃvatsaraṃ prājāpatyaṃ caritvāśramam upeyāt | dvitīye kṛcchraṃ tṛtīye kṛcchrātikṛcchram ata ūrdhvaṃ cāndrāyaṇam" iti | āpadviṣayam etat | anāpadi traimāsikaṃ sādhāraṇam upapātakaprāyaścittam | ninditānnādanaṃ manunopapātakam uktam | annasya ca ninditatvaṃ svabhāvakālasaṃparkakriyābhāvaparigrahaiḥ ṣoḍhā bhavati | tatra svabhāvāl laśunādeḥ | kālataḥ śuktādeḥ | saṃparkāc chvaspṛṣṭādeḥ | kriyāto hastadattavyañjanādeḥ | bhāvād ikṣurasādeḥ | bhavati hi kasyacid ikṣurase sureyam iti bhāvaḥ | parigrahāt tu patitānnādeḥ |
abhakṣyabakṣaṇaprāyaścitte vyavasthām āha viṣṇuḥ |
vipre tu sakalaṃ deyaṃ pādonaṃ kṣatriya matam |
vaiśye 'rthaṃ pādam ekaṃ vā śūdrajātiṣu śasyate || iti |
atra manuḥ |
chatrākaṃ viḍvarāhaṃ ca laśunaṃ grāmakukkuṭam |
palṇḍuṃ gṛñjanaṃ caiva matyā jagdhvā pated dvijaḥ ||
amatyaitāni ṣaḍ jagdhvā kṛcchraṃ sāṃtapanaṃ caret |
yaticāndrāyaṇaṃ vāpi śeṣeṣūpavased ahaḥ ||
matipūrvake chatrākādibhakṣaṇe pātityaṃ dvijātikarmahānilakṣaṇam | prāyaścittaṃ tatra surāpavratātmakam eva |
brahmojjhaṃ vedanindā ca kūṭasākṣyaṃ suhṛdvadhaḥ |
garhitānnādyayor jagdhiḥ surāpānasamāni ṣaṭ ||
iti surāpānasamatvaṃ manunaivoktam | amatyā sābhyāsabhakṣaṇe yaticāndrāyaṇam | śeṣeṣv abhakṣyeṣu bhakṣiteṣv ahar upavāsaḥ | caturviṃśatimatāt |
laśunaṃ gṛñjanaṃ caiva tṛṇarājaphalaṃ tathā |
nīlīṃ caiva dvijo bhuktvā carec cāndrāyaṇaṃ vratam ||
akāmato 'bhyāse kāmato vā sakṛdbhakṣaṇa etat | tṛṇarājas tālaḥ | [1158]
alābu kṣudravṛntākaṃ kusumbhakam akaṇṭakam |
nālikāśaṇapuṣpaṃ ca jagdhvā dinam abhojanam ||
kandamūlaphalādīni saṃbhrāntāni na bhakṣayet |
upavāso bhavet tatra śātātapavaco yathā ||
saṃbhrāntāni bhakṣyābhakṣyatvena saṃdigdhāni |
śaṇapuṣpaṃ śālmalaṃ ca karanirmathitaṃ dadhi |
bahirvedi puroḍāśaṃ jagdhvā nādyād aharniśam ||
laśunādiṣu ye tulyā gandhavarṇarasādibhiḥ |
abhakṣyās te dvijātīnāṃ bhuktvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret ||
śaṅkhaḥ: "laśunapalāṇḍugṛñjanacchatrākanāliṅgaviḍvarāhagrāmakukkuṭanakharomamāṃsa-
bhakṣaṇe cāndrāyaṇaṃ kuryāt" | gṛñjanaṃ laśunānukāri |
viṣadigdhena śastreṇa yo mṛgaḥ parihanyate |
abhakṣyaṃ tasya tanmāṃsaṃ tad dhi gṛñjanam iṣyate ||
iti kecit paṭhanti | śātātapaḥ: "laśunapalāṇḍugṛñjanabhakṣaṇe taptakṛcchraḥ | matipūrvakābhyāsagomāṃsabhakṣaṇe ca" | cāndrāyaṇānuvṛttau viṣṇuḥ: "laśunapalāṇḍugṛñjana-picchagandhirviḍvarāhagrāmakukkuṭanaragomāṃsabhakṣaṇe ca sarveṣv eteṣu dvijātīnāṃ prāyaścittānte punaḥsaṃskāraṃ kuryāt" | sumantuḥ: "laśunapalāṇḍugṛñjanakumbhīkaśrāddha-sūtikābhojyānnamadhumāṃsamūtraretomaṃdhyābhakṣyabhakṣaṇe sāvitryaṣṭasahasraṃ mūrdhni saṃpātān avanayet | upavāsaś ca | etāny eva vyādhitasya bhiṣakkriyāyām apratiṣiddhāni bhavanti | yāni caivaṃprakārāṇi teṣv apy adoṣaḥ" | saṃpātāvanayopavāsāv anupnītabālaviṣayau | pracetāḥ |
pīyūṣaṃ śvetalaśunaṃ vṛntākaphalagṛñjanam |
palāṇḍuṃ vṛkṣaniryāsaṃ devasvaṃ kavukāni ca || [1159]
uṣṭrīkṣīram avikṣīraṃ yas tv ajñānāt pibed dvijaḥ |
trirātram upavāsaś ca pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
pīyūṣaṃ navaprasūtāyā goḥ kṣīram | asamarthasya tadānīm eva praccharditavata etat | yamaḥ |
taṇḍulīyakakumbhīkavraścanaprabhavāṃs tathā |
nālikā nālikerī ca śleṣmāntakaphalāni ca ||
bhūstṛṇaṃ śigrukaṃ caiva khukhuḍaṃ kṣavakaṃ tathā |
eteṣāṃ bhakṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā prājāpatyaṃ cared vratam ||
nālikerī śākaviśeṣaḥ | kṣavakaṃ rājasarṣapaḥ | āpastambaḥ |
bhakṣayed yadi nīlīṃ tu pramādād brāhmaṇaḥ kvacit |
cāndrāyaṇena śuddhiḥ syād āpastambo 'bravīn muniḥ ||
saṃvartaḥ |
avatsaikaśaphāstrīṇāṃ kṣīraṃ prāśya dvijottamaḥ |
anirdaśāyā goś caiva trirātraṃ yāvakaṃ pibet ||
yāvakasya ca pānamātraṃ kāryam, na tu tena tṛpyet | tathā |
strīkṣīram āvikaṃ caiva saṃdhinyāś caiva goḥ payaḥ |
prāśya śudhyet trirātreṇa viḍjānāṃ bhakṣaṇe tathā ||
etad amatipūrvake sakṛdbhakṣaṇe | viṣṇuḥ: "uṣṭrīkharīmānuṣīkṣīrabhakṣaṇe taptakṛcchram" | etad amatipūrvake sakṛdbhakṣaṇe ca | śaṅkhaḥ |
māhiṣyaṃ gavyam ājaṃ ca bhakṣyaṃ kṣīreṣu nirdiśet |
bhuktvā parasya tu kṣīraṃ māsaṃ kuryād vrataṃ budhaḥ ||
anirdaśāhaṃ gokṣīraṃ māhiṣaṃ cājam eva ca |
goś ca kṣīraṃ vivatsāyāḥ saṃdhinyāś ca tathā payaḥ ||
saṃdhinyamedhyabhakṣāyā bhuktvā pakṣavrataṃ caret |
kṣīrāṇi yāny abhakṣyāṇi tadvikārāśane budhaḥ ||
saptarātraṃ vrataṃ kuryād yad etat parikīrtitam ||
vrataṃ gomūtrayāvakam | anyatrāpi śaṅkhavākye vrataśabdenedam evābhidhīyate | caturviṃśatimatāt | [1160]
striyāḥ kṣīraṃ dvijaḥ pītvā kathaṃcit kāmamohitaḥ |
punaḥ saṃskṛtya cātmānaṃ prājāpatyaṃ caret tataḥ ||
avyuṣṭrīsaṃdhinīkṣīraṃ mṛgāṇāṃ vanacāriṇām |
anirdaśāyā goś caiva pītvā dinam abhojanam ||
amatyā sakṛt samanantarapracchardane strībālāsamarthaviṣayam etat | smṛtyantaram |
tvaṅ māṃsam ūrṇayā sārdham āvikaṃ tritayaṃ śuci |
purīṣamūtre sakṣīre apavitraṃ trayaṃ punaḥ ||
pramādād aśane tasya dinam ekaṃ vrataṃ caret ||
tasyāpavitratrayasya vratam abhojanam | pūrvoktaviṣayam etat | śātātapaḥ |
uṣṭrīkṣīram avikṣīram annaṃ mṛtakasūtake |
caurasyānnaṃ navaśrāddhaṃ bhuktvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret ||
kāmato 'bhyāsaviṣayam etat | paiṭhīnasiḥ: "avikharyuṣṭrīmānuṣīdugdhaprāśane punar upanayanaṃ prājāpatyaṃ ca" | jābālaḥ |
anirdaśāyā goḥ kṣīraṃ mānuṣaṃ cauṣṭram eva ca |
madirāṃ ca navaśrāddhaṃ bhuktvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret ||
ajñānataḥ sakṛd bhojana etat | madirā madyaṃ surāvyatiriktaṃ dravyāntaratirohitarasarūpam | aṅgirāḥ |
māhiṣyaṃ(ṣaṃ) gavyam ājaṃ ca bhakṣyaṃ kṣīreṣu nirdiśet |
bhuktvā paramataḥ kṣīraṃ kṛchhrapādaṃ samācaret ||
hārītaḥ: "asurāmadyapāne 'lpake caurye śleṣmātakaprāsane māsa(ṣa)masūrakavamane maricabhakṣaṇe 'medhyāvapannānām apāṃ pāne ca sāṃtapanam eva caret" | kṛcchrātikṛcchrau ghṛtaprāsaḥ punaḥ saṃskāraś cety anuvṛttau vasiṣṭhaḥ: "mūtraśakṛcchukrābhyavahāreṣu caivam" | bṛhaspatiḥ |
pītvā pramādato madyam atikṛcchraṃ cared dvijaḥ |
saṃskāraṃ tu punaḥ kuryāc chaktyā viprāṃś ca bhojayet ||
madyaṃ khārjūrapānasādi na tu surā | dvijo vipraḥ | manuḥ |
apaḥ surābhājasthā madyabhāṇḍasthitās tathā |
pañcarātraṃ pibet pītvā śaṅkhapuṣpīśṛtaṃ payaḥ || [1161]
payaḥ kṣīram | apariśuṣkasurābhājana ārdre ca madyabhāṇḍe cānupalabhyamānamadyarasodake pānāntarapracchardane ca sati strībālāder etat | śaṅkhalikhitau: "surābhāṇḍodakapāne ṣaḍrātram abhojanaṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ kāmataḥ" | ārdrasurābhāṇḍodake pīte jīrṇe caitat | śaṅkhaḥ |
apaḥ surābhājanasthāḥ pītvā pakṣaṃ vratī bhavet |
madyabhāṇḍasthitāḥ pītvā saptarātraṃ vratī bhavet ||
trirātropavāsaghṛtaprāśanānuvṛttau śaṅkhalikhitau: "madhvāsavaduṣṭabhāṇḍodakapāne striyā saha bhojane caitad eva" | viṣṇuḥ |
surāsaṃspṛṣṭabhāṇḍe tu retomūtrādidūṣite |
jalakṣīrādike pīte taptakṛcchraṃ samācaret ||
sakṛtpāne chardane ca sati dvirātrasādhyas taptakṛcchraḥ | chardanābhāve saptarātrasādhyaḥ | kāśyapaḥ |
pramādān madyam asuraṃ sakṛt pītvā dvijottamaḥ |
gomūtrayāvakāhāro daśarātreṇa śudhyati ||
buddhivirahaḥ pramādaḥ | "pramādo 'navadhānatā" ity amaraḥ | asuraṃ surāvyatiriktam | vasiṣṭhaḥ: "madyabhāṇḍasthitaṃ toyaṃ yadi kaścit pibed dvijaḥ | padmodumbarabilvapalāśakuśānām udakaṃ pītvā trirātreṇaiva śudhyati | surāvyatiriktamadyabhāṇḍasthitodakapānaviṣayam etat | jābālaḥ |
surābhāṇḍodakaṃ pītvā saptarātraṃ yavān pibet |
surā paiṣṭī | nirlepasya tadbhāṇḍasya sakṛd amatipūrvake ca pāna etat | śātātapaḥ: "madyabhāṇḍodakapāne chardanaṃ ghṛtaprāśanam ahorātraṃ ca" | ghṛtaprāśanād āhārāntaranivṛttiḥ | ahorātropavāsaś cāparaḥ | samarthaviṣayam etat | yamaḥ |
śunocchiṣṭāḥ sthitā āpo yadi kaścid dvijaḥ pibet |
śaṅkhapuṣpīvipakvena tryahaṃ kṣīreṇa śudhyati ||
madyabhāṇḍasthitā āpo yadi kaścid dvijaḥ pibet |
dvādaśāhaṃ tu payasā pibed brahmasuvarcalām ||
gāyatryaṣṭasahasraṃ vā japyaṃ kurvīta mānasam |
brahmasuvarcalāṃ pratyahaṃ pibet | tajjapyaṃ vā pratyahaṃ kurvītety arthaḥ | [1162] hārītaḥ |
striyocchiṣṭāḥ sthitā āpo yadi kaścit pibed dvijaḥ |
śaṅkhapuṣpīvipakvena tryahaṃ kṣīreṇa śudhyati ||
śūdrocchiṣṭāḥ sthitā āpo yadi kaścit pibed dvijaḥ |
kuśamūlavipakvena tryahaṃ kṣīreṇa vartayet ||
parāśaraḥ |
bhāṇḍe sthitam abhojyānnam apaḥ pītvā payo dadhi |
brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyo vaiśyaḥ śūdraś caivopasajjati ||
brahmakūrcopavāsaś ca yojyaṃ karmāsya niṣkṛtiḥ |
śūdrasya nopavāsaś ca dānenaikena śudhyati ||
uapasajjati prāyaścittārtham āgacchati | brahmakūrcasvarūpaṃ vakṣyate | vṛddhaśātātapaḥ |
pibataḥ patitaṃ toyaṃ bhājane sukhaniḥsṛtam |
abhojyaṃ tad bhaved annaṃ bhuktvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret ||
śaṅkhaḥ |
pītvāvaśeṣitaṃ pītvā pānīyaṃ brāhmaṇaḥ kvacit |
trirātraṃ tu vrataṃ kuryād vāmahastena vā punaḥ ||
parāśaraḥ |
kūpe tu patitaṃ dṛṣṭvā śvaśṛgālaṃ ca markaṭam |
asthicarmādipatanāt pītvāmedhyā hy apo dvijaḥ ||
nāraṃ tu kuṇapaṃ kākaṃ viḍvarāhaṃ kharoṣṭrayoḥ |
gāvayaṃ saupratīkaṃ ca vāggudaṃ tv ākhujaṃ tathā ||
vaiyāghraṃ mārgaṃ saiṃhaṃ vā kūpe yady asthi majjati |
taḍākasyaiva duṣṭasya pītaṃ syād udakaṃ yadi ||
prāyaścittaṃ bhavet tasya krameṇaitena sarvaśaḥ |
vipraḥ śudhyet trirātreṇa kṣatriyaś ca dinadvayāt ||
ekāhena ca vaiśyas tu śūdro naktena śudhyati ||
supratīko 'tra gajaḥ | tasyedaṃ saupratīkam | taḍākodakopa[yoga]viṣayam etat | kūpodakopayoge tv adhikaṃ kalpyam | viṣṇuḥ: "mṛtapañcanakhāt kūpād atyantopahatād vodakaṃ pītvā brāhmaṇas trirātram upavaset | dvyahaṃ rājanyaḥ | ekāhaṃ vaiśyaḥ | śūdro naktam, sarve ca vratasyānte pañcagavyaṃ pibeyuḥ" | āpastambaḥ |
antaḥ sthiteṣu klinneṣu sarvapañcanakheṣu ca |
pibed apo yas tv ajñānāt ṣaḍrātreṇa viśudhyati || [1163]
aklinneṣu trirātraṃ syāc chūdrāṇāṃ dinam eva tu |
aṅgirāḥ |
caṇḍālaparigṛhītaṃ tu yo 'jñānād udakaṃ pibet |
tasya śuddhiṃ vijānīyāt prājāpatyena nityaśaḥ ||
kūpaikapānaduṣṭānāṃ kuryāt saṃparkadūṣaṇam |
sarva evopavāsena pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
yas tu caṇḍālasaṃspṛṣṭaṃ pibet kiṃcid akāmataḥ |
sa tu sāṃtapanaṃ kṛcchraṃ carec chudhyartham ātmanaḥ ||
laghuviṣṇuḥ |
apo mūtrapurīṣādyair dūṣitāḥ prāśayed yadi |
tadā sāṃtapanaṃ proktaṃ vrataṃ kāyaviśuddhaye ||
laghuhārītaḥ |
yas tu kūpāt pibet toyaṃ brāhmaṇaḥ śavadūṣitāt |
upavāsatrayaṃ kṛtvā pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
klinne bhinne śave caiva tatrasthe yadi tat pibet |
śuddhyai cāndrāyaṇaṃ kuryāt taptakṛcchram athāpi vā ||
śaktikṛto vikalpo jñānājñānakṛto vā |
yadi kaścit tataḥ snāyāt pramādena dvijottamaḥ |
japtvā triṣavaṇasnāyī ahorātreṇa śudhyati ||
pānarahitaṃ snānaṃ kṛtavata etat | devalaḥ |
klinno bhinnaḥ śavaś caiva kūpastho yadi dṛśyate |
payaḥ pibaṃs trirātraṃ ca mānuṣe dviguṇaṃ bhavet ||
dviguṇaṃ ṣaḍrātram ity arthaḥ | uśanā |
viṇmūtrotsargakūpasthā apaḥ prāśya dvijottamaḥ |
trirātreṇa viśudhyet tu kumbhāt sāṃtapanaṃ smṛtam ||
āpastambaḥ |
caṇḍālakhātavāpīṣu ajñānād yaḥ pibej jalam |
upoṣya rajanīm ekāṃ pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
caṇḍālabhāṇḍatoyaṃ tu dvijaḥ pītvā vimohitaḥ |
kṛcchratrayaṃ prakurvāṇas tataḥ pāpāt pramucyate ||
caṇḍālena tu saṃspṛṣṭā apo yaḥ pibati dvijaḥ |
kṛcchrapādena śudhyet tu punaḥsaṃskāram arhati ||
atra yaḥ punaḥsaṃskāraḥ sa pūrvatāpi draṣṭavyaḥ | [1164] atriḥ |
mlecchādīnāṃ jalaṃ pītvā puṣkariṇyāṃ hrade 'pi vā |
jānudadhnaṃ śuci jñeyam adhastād āsuci smṛtam ||
tat toyaṃ yaḥ pibed vipraḥ kāmato 'kāmato 'pi vā |
akāmān naktabhojī syād ahorātraṃ tu kāmataḥ ||
śātātapaḥ |
caṇḍālodakabhāṇḍeṣu yaḥ pibet tṛṣito jalam |
tatkṣaṇāt kṣipate tac cet prājāpatyena śudhyati ||
yadi na kṣipate toyaṃ cireṇaivāsya jīryate |
prājāpatyaṃ tu kartavyaṃ kṛcchraṃ sāṃtapanaṃ caret ||
manuḥ |
spṛṣṭvā datvā tu madirāṃ vidhivat pratigṛhya ca |
śūdrocchiṣṭās tu pītvāpaḥ kuśavāri pibet tryaham ||
ajñānāt prāśya viṇmūtraṃ surāsaṃspṛṣṭam eva ca |
punaḥsaṃskāram arhanti trayo varṇā dvijātayaḥ ||
brāhmaṇas tu surāpasya gandham āghrāya somapaḥ |
prāṇān apsu trir āyamya ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati ||
sumantuḥ: "surāpasyāghrāya gandhaṃ somapa udakamagnas trir aghamarṣaṇaṃ japtvā ghṛtaprāśanam ācaret" | bṛhaspatiḥ |
surāpalāṇḍulaśunasparśe kāmakṛte dvijaḥ |
tryahaṃ pibet kuśajalaṃ gāyatrīṃ ca japet tathā ||
palāṇḍulaśunasparśe snātvā naktaṃ samācaret |
kṛtoccāras tv ahorātram ucchiṣṭo dvyaham eva ca ||
akāmata etat | manuḥ |
viḍvarāhakharoṣṭrāṇāṃ gomāyoḥ kapikākayoḥ |
prāśya mūtrapurīṣāṇi dvijaś cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret ||
aṅgirāḥ |
prājāpatyaṃ cared vaiśyo 'tikṛcchraṃ kṣatriyaś caret |
kṛcchrātikṛcchraṃ kurvīta vipro viṇmūtrabhakṣaṇe ||
śūdrasya tu kathaṃ kuryād viṇmūtrasya tu bhakṣaṇe |
trirātropoṣitaḥ snātvā tarpayet pitṛdevatāḥ ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
alehyānām apeyānām abhojyānāṃ ca bhakṣaṇe |
retomūtrapurīṣāṇāṃ śuddhiś cāndrāyaṇaṃ smṛtam || [1165]
kāmakāra etat | devalaḥ |
śaṅkhapuṣpīṃ ghṛtaṃ kṣīraṃ padmanālaṃ sakesaram |
pītvā puṣkaraparṇaṃ ca mucyate vārisaṃkarāt ||
śaṅkhalikhitau: "keśamakṣikarudhiraprāśana āmamāṃsabhūmibhakṣaṇe rudhiracchardane rajasvalāvadhutābhigamane trirātropavāsaṃ ghṛtaprāśanaṃ ca kuryāt" | sumantuḥ: "nakhakeśarudhiraprāśane sadyaḥ snānaṃ ghṛtakuśahiraṇyodakaprāśanaṃ ca" | gautamaḥ: "abhojyabhojane niṣpurīṣībhāvas trirātrāvasaram abhojanaṃ saptarātraṃ vā svayaṃśīrṇāny upayuñjānaḥ phalāny anatikrāman" | svayaṃśīrṇāni svayaṃ patitāni | anatikrāmann apra(pra)thamalabdhāni phalāni nirguṇāny api aparityajann ity arthaḥ | saṃvartaḥ |
abhojyabhojanaṃ kṛtvā brahmakṣatraviśāṃ gaṇaḥ |
gomūtrayāvakāhāraḥ saptarātreṇa śudhyati ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
pītvā śuktakaṣāyāṇi bhuktvā cānnaṃ vigarhitam |
bhaved aprayato vipraḥ karmaṇyaḥ syād adhogate ||
adhogate tasminn anne niṣpurīṣatāyāṃ satyām ity arthaḥ |
atha māṃsabhakṣaṇaprāyaścittāni | tatra saṃvartaḥ |
gomāṃsaṃ mānuṣaṃ caiva sūnihastāt samāhṛtam |
abhakṣyaṃ tad bhavet sarvaṃ bhuktvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret ||
śaṅkhalikhitau: "bakabalākāhaṃasplavakhañjanakakāraṇḍavacakravākakaṭabhāragṛhakapota-caṭakapārāvataśukaśārikāsārasaṭiṭṭibholūkakākolaraktapādajālapādavāggudacāṣabhāsavāyasa-kokilaśādvalikukkuṭahārītabhakṣaṇe daśarātram anāhāraḥ pibed vā gomūtrayāvakam" | kākolaḥ śyenaḥ | eṣām anyatamasyātyantabhakṣaṇābhyāse pratyekaṃ bhakṣaṇasamudāye vaitat |
śaṅkhaḥ |
madguhaṃsaṃ bakaṃ kākaṃ kokilaṃ khañjarīṭakam |
matsyādāṃś ca tathā matsyān balākāḥ śukaśārike || [1166]
cakravākaṃ plavaṃ caiva maṇḍūkaṃ bhujagaṃ tathā |
māsam ekaṃ vrataṃ kuryād bhūyaś caiva na bhakṣayet ||
madgur jalavāyasaḥ |
jalecarāṃś ca jalajān pratudān nakhaviṣkirān |
raktapādāñ jālapādān saptarātraṃ vrataṃ caret ||
pratudya prāṇinaṃ bhakṣayantīti pratudaḥ | nakhair vikīrya bhakṣayantīti nakhaviṣkirāḥ | jālākārāḥ pādā yeṣāṃ te jālapādāḥ | ta eva raktavarṇāḥ pādā yeṣāṃ te raktapādāḥ |
bhuktvā cobhayatodantāṃs tathā caikaśaphān api |
daṃṣṭriṇaś ca tathā bhuktvā ṣaṇmāsān vratam ācaret ||
sūnāmāṃsaṃ śuṣkamāṃsam ātmano 'rthe tathā kṛtam |
bhuktvā māsaṃ vrataṃ kuryāt prayataḥ susamāhitaḥ ||
yatra phalake māṃsaṃ khaṇḍyate sā sūnā |
ṛbhīkapakvaṃ māṃsaṃ ca sarvaṃ yatnena varjayet |
saṃvatsaraṃ vrataṃ kuryāt saṃprāśyājñānatas tu tat ||
ṛbhīkaṃ nāma māṃsapākasthānaviśeṣaḥ | tatra pakvaṃ sarvaṃ śiṣṭaṃ bhojanīyam api māṃsaṃ na bhakṣaṇīyam | taptakṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ cared ity anuvṛttau manuḥ |
śuṣkāni bhuktvā māṃsāni bhaumāni kavakāni ca |
ajñātaṃ caiva sūnāsthaṃ māṃsam etad vrataṃ caret ||
kavakāni chatrākāṇi teṣāṃ bhūmirūḍhānāṃ bhakṣaṇa etat prāyaścittaṃ na vṛkṣādirūḍhānām |
kravyād viṭsūkaroṣṭrāṇāṃ kukkuṭānāṃ ca bhakṣaṇe |
narakākakharāṇāṃ ca taptakṛcchro viśodhanam ||
sakṛd amatyā bhakṣaṇa etat | kravyam apakvaṃ māṃsaṃ tad adantīti kravyādā vyāghrādayaḥ | śaṅkhalikhitau: "śvasṛgālakākakukkuṭagrāmyasūkarakaṅkagṛdhrabhāsavāyasapārāvatamānuṣa-
kākolukakānāṃ māṃsādane saptarātram upavāso [1167] niṣpurīṣībhāvo ghṛtaprāśaḥ punaḥsaṃskāraś ca" | viṣṇuḥ: "kalaviṅkahaṃsacakravākarajjudālakasārasadātyūhaśukaśārikā-
balākākokilakhañjarīṭāśane trirātram upavaset | ekaśaphobhayatodantāśane ca tittirakapiñjala-
lāvakavartiāmayūravarjaṃ sarvapakṣimāṃsāśane tv ahorātram | kīṭāśane dinam ekaṃ brāhmīṃ suvarcalāṃ pibet | pṛṣṭhamāṃsāśane sūnāmāṃsāśane ca" | kalaviṅko gṛhacaṭakaḥ | sarvam etatd amatipūrvake sakṛd bhakṣaṇe vā draṣṭavyam | chāgaleyaḥ |
vṛthāmāṃsaṃ na bhoktavyaṃ bhoktavyaṃ śrāddhakarmaṇi |
anyathā bhakṣayan vipraḥ prājāpatyaṃ samācaret ||
śiṣṭabhojaniyamāṃsaviṣayam etat | śaṅkhaḥ |
bhakṣyāḥ pañcanakhāś caiva godhākacchapaśalyakāḥ |
khaḍgaś ca śaśakaś caiva tān bhuktvā nācared vratam ||
tathā |
tittiraṃ ca mayūraṃ ca lāvakaṃ ca kapiñjalam |
vādhrīṇasaṃ vartakaṃ ca bhakṣyān āha sadā yamaḥ ||
tathā |
māhiṣaṃ tv ājam aurabhraṃ rauravaṃ mārgam eva ca |
bhakṣyaṃ māṃsaṃ samuddiṣṭaṃ yaś caiva pārṣataṃ bhavet ||
rurur bahuśākhaśṛṅgo mṛgaviśeṣaḥ | viṣṇuḥ: "śaśakaśalyakagodhākhaḍgakūrmavarjaṃ pañcanakhamāṃsāśane saptarātram upavaset" | parāśaraḥ |
agamyāgamane caiva madyagomāṃsabhakṣaṇe |
śuddhyai cāndrāyaṇaṃ kuryān nadīṃ gatvā samudragām ||
cāndrāyaṇe tataś cīrṇe kuryād brāhmaṇabhojanam |
anaḍutsahitāṃ gāṃ ca dadyād vipreṣu dakṣiṇām ||
manuḥ |
abhojyānāṃ tu bhuktvānnaṃ strīśūdrocchiṣṭam eva ca |
jagdhvā māṃsam abhakṣyaṃ ca saptarātraṃ yavān pibet ||
biḍālakākākhūcchiṣṭaṃ jagdhvā śvanakulasya ca |
keśakīṭāvapannaṃ ca pibed brahmasuvarcalām ||
ākhur mūṣakaḥ | [1168] viṣṇuḥ: "gor ucchiṣṭāśane dinam ekam upoṣitaḥ pañcagavyaṃ pibet" | saṃvartaḥ |
viṇmūtrabhakṣaṇe vipraḥ prājāpatyaṃ samācaret |
śvakākagobhir ucchiṣṭabhakṣaṇe tu dinatrayam ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ: "śvakākāvalīḍhaśūdroccheṣaṇasaṃbhojaneṣv atikṛcchraḥ kṛcchra itareṣv anyatra madhumāṃsaphalavikāreṣu" | matipūrvakābhyāsa etat | śaṅkhaḥ |
śūnocchiṣṭaṃ tu bhuktvānnaṃ māsam ekaṃ vrataṃ caret |
kākocchiṣṭaṃ gavāghrātaṃ bhuktvā pakṣaṃ vratī bhavet ||
dūṣitaṃ keśakīṭaiś ca mārjārair dūṣikais tathā |
makṣikāmaṣa(śa)kaiś caiva trirātraṃ tu vrataṃ caret ||
vṛthā kṛsarasaṃyāvapāyasāpūpaśaṣkulīḥ |
bhuktvā trirātraṃ kurvīta vratam etat samāhitaḥ ||
