etad amatipūrvābhyāse veditavyam | śvakākagavāghrāte bhojane kāmataḥ sābhyāsa etat | vrataṃ gomūtrayāvakāśanam | kāmataḥ sakṛdbhojanaviṣaye tv āha pracetāḥ |
pakṣigodhātha khadyotaḥ śataspṛṣṭo 'tha loṣṭakṛt |
ṣaḍbindur atha durgaś ca saptaite duṣṭakīṭakāḥ ||
etaiḥ kākādibhiś caiva yad annaṃ dūṣitaṃ bhavet |
tad annaṃ kāmato bhuktvā kṛcchraṃ sāṃtapanaṃ caret ||
abuddhipūrve tu sakṛdbhakṣaṇe jābāla āha |
paryuṣitaṃ punaḥ siddham abhojyaṃ laśunādikam |
śūdrakākagavocchiṣṭaṃ bhuktvā tu tryaham ācaret ||
keśakīṭāvapannaṃ ca strībhiḥ khātaṃ tathaiva ca |
śvodakyāśūdrasaṃspṛṣṭaṃ pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
śaṅkhalikhitau: "vṛthākṛsarapāyasāpūpamāṃsabhakṣaṇam āhitāgniḥ kṛtvā prājāpatyaṃ caret" | yamaḥ |
mākṣikaṃ phāṇitaṃ śākaṃ gorasaṃ lavaṇaṃ ghṛtam |
hastadattāni bhuktvā tu bhoktā sāṃtapanaṃ caret ||
abhyāsaviṣayam etat | phāṇitam ikṣurasavikāraviśeṣaḥ | [1169] parāśaraḥ |
ekapaṅktyupaviṣṭānāṃ viprāṇāṃ sahabhojane |
yady eko 'pi tyajet pātraṃ śeṣam annaṃ na bhojayet ||
homād bhuñjīta yas tv annaṃ paṅktyām ucchiṣṭabhojanaḥ |
prāyaścittaṃ cared vipraḥ kṛcchraṃ sāṃtapanaṃ tathā ||
sumantuḥ |
śūdrocchiṣṭabhojane trirātram aghamarṣaṇaṃ japet |
yamaḥ |
vipreṇa saha bhuktvānnaṃ prājāpatyena śudhyati |
bhūbhujā saha bhuktvānnaṃ taptakṛcchreṇa śudhyati ||
śūdreṇa saha bhuktvānnaṃ cāndrāyaṇam athācaret |
bastikarmanirūhaiś ca pracchardanavirecanaiḥ ||
śiśukṛcchreṇa śudhyanti tasmāt pāpān na saṃśayaḥ |
kāmakāraviṣayam etat | śaṅkhalikhitau: "brāhmaṇocchiṣṭabhojane mahāvyāhṛtibhir abhimantryāpaḥ pibet | kṣatriyocchiṣṭabhojane brāhmīrasavipakvena tryahaṃ kṣīreṇa vartayet | vaiśyocchiṣṭabhojane trirātropoṣito brahmasuvarcalāṃ pibet | śūdrocchiṣṭabhojane surābhāṇḍodakapāne ca saptarātram abhojanaṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ vā" | viṣṇuḥ: "āmaśrāddhāśane trirātraṃ payasā varteta brāhmaṇaḥ | śūdrocchiṣṭāśane vamanaṃ kṛtvā saptarātraṃ vaiśyocchiṣṭāśane pañcarātram, rājanyocchiṣṭāśane trirātram, rājanyaḥ śūdrocchiṣṭāśī pañcarātram | vaiśyocchiṣṭāśī trirātraṃ vaiśyaḥ śūdrocchiṣṭāśī ca |
śūdrocchiṣṭāśane māsaṃ pakṣam ekaṃ tathā viśaḥ |
kṣatriyasya tu saptāhaṃ brāhmaṇasya tathā dinam ||
āmaśrāddhāśane vidvān māsam ekaṃ vrataṃ caret" ||
navaśrāddhaviṣayam etat | śātātapaḥ: "ucchiṣṭam aguror abhojyaṃ svam ucchiṣṭam ucchiṣṭopahataṃ ca tadbhojane kṛcchram" | [1170] śaṅkhaḥ: "amedhyapatitacaṇḍālapukkasa-rajasvalākuṇikuṣṭhikunakhispṛṣṭaṃ ca bhuktvā kṛcchram ācaret" | kuṇiḥ kuṇapam | yamaḥ |
masūramāṣamāṃsāni bhuktvā yo vasati dvijaḥ |
trirātram upavāso 'sya prāyaścittaṃ vidhīyate ||
prāṇāyāmais tribhiḥ snātvā ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati ||
viṣṇuḥ |
śūdrajuṣṭaṃ śunā vāpi saṃspṛṣṭaṃ vāpi bhojanam |
taptakṛcchreṇa śudyet tu prājāpatyena vā punaḥ ||
śaktyā parāko dātavyaḥ iti dharmasya niścayaḥ |
kṣatravaiśyadibhir juṣṭaṃ gardabhaiḥ sūkarais tathā |
pādahanyā bhavet kṛcchraṃ śaktyā sarvaṃ cared dvijaḥ ||
juṣṭaṃ bhuktam | śaṅkhaḥ |
vāgduṣṭaṃ bhāvaduṣṭaṃ ca bhājane bhāvadūṣite |
bhuktvānnaṃ brāhmaṇaḥ paścāt trirātraṃ tu vratī bhavet ||
parāśaraḥ |
bhinnabhāṇḍe tu bhuñjāno hy ajñānena dvijottamaḥ |
suvarṇodakasaṃspṛṣṭaṃ ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati ||
ghṛtaprāśanād āhārāntaranivṛttiḥ | āpastambaḥ |
vicchardane dvijātīnāṃ bhinnabhānḍe ca bhojane |
pañcagavyena śuddhiḥ syād iti śātātapo 'bravīt ||
saṃvartaḥ |
śūdrāṇāṃ bhājane bhuktvā bhuktvā vā bhinnabhājane |
ahorātroṣito bhūtvā pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
bhinnabhāṅḍadoṣaḥ kāṃsyasyaiva, yad āha baudhāyanaḥ |
bhinnakāṃsye tu yo bhuṅkte nadyāṃ gatvā japan budhaḥ |
gāyatryaṣṭasahasraṃ tu ekabhaktas tataḥ śuciḥ || iti |
saṃvartaḥ |
anācāntaḥ pibed yas tu bhakṣayed vāpi kiṃcana |
gāyatryaṣṭasahasraṃ tu japaṃ kṛtvā viśudhyati ||
a(ā)pośā(śa)nam akṛtvā tu yo bhuṅkte 'nāpadi dvijaḥ |
bhuñjānas tu yadā brūyād gāyatryaṣṭaśataṃ japet || [1171]
laghuhārītaḥ:
vinā yajñopavītena bhuṅkte tu brāhmaṇo yadi |
snānaṃ kṛtvā japaṃ caiva upavāsena śudhyati ||
vṛddhaśātātapaḥ |
yadā bhojanakāle ca aśucir bhavati dvijaḥ |
bhūmau nikṣipya taṃ grāsaṃ snātvā vipro viśudhyati ||
bhakṣayitvā tu taṃ grāsam ahorātreṇa śudhyati |
aśitvā sarvam evānnaṃ trirātreṇa viśudhyati ||
mūtroccāre śakṛtsarge mohād bhuṅkte 'tha vā pibet |
trirātraṃ tatra kurvīta iti śātātapo 'bravīt ||
mūtroccāre bhuñjānasya purīṣotsarge ca pibata etat |
mūtrayitvā vrajan mārge smṛtibhraṃśāj jalaṃ pibet |
ahorātroṣitaḥ snātvā pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
nigiran yadi meheta bhuṅkte vā mehane kṛte |
ahorātroṣitaḥ snātvā juhuyāt sarpiṣāhutīḥ ||
vyāhṛtibhir iti śeṣaḥ | bṛhatpracetāḥ |
āranālakṛte śauce pramādād bhojane kṛte |
ṣaḍrātraṃ yāvakaṃ pītvā śudhyed vipraḥ samāhitaḥ ||
trirātreṇa viśudhyet tu dvijaś cācamane kṛte |
vidhāya śuddhiṃ nīre ca yaś cācāmati kāñjikam ||
upoṣya rajanīm ekāṃ pañcagavyena śudhyati |
āranālaṃ kāñjikam |
samutpanne dvijaḥ snāne bhuñjītātha pibeta vā |
gāyatryaṣṭasahasraṃ tu japet snātvā samāhitaḥ ||
patitādisparśanimittāt snānāntara etat | tannimitte tu paiṭhīnasir āha |
mahāpātakisaṃsparśe snānam eva vidhīyate |
saṃspṛṣṭas tu yadā bhuṅkte taptakṛcchraṃ tadācaret ||
vṛddhayājñavalkyaḥ |
asnātvā yadi vāśnīyāc caṇḍālasparśane dvijaḥ |
tryahaṃ copavaset snātvā pītvā copavased ahaḥ ||
akāmakāraviṣayam etat |
bhuñjānasya tu viprasya viṣṭhā cen mastake patet |
annaṃ tyaktvā nadīsnātaḥ prāṇāyāmaṃ trir abhyaset || [1172]
āsanārūḍhapādo yo vastrasyārdhapaṭīkṛtam |
mukhena dhamitaṃ kṛtvā kṛcchraṃ sāṃtapanaṃ caret ||
ardhapaṭīkṛtaṃ paridhānaikadeśīkṛtam ity arthaḥ | dhamitaṃ kṛtvety annādi bhuṅkte yadīti śeṣaḥ |
keśakīṭanakhaṃ prāśya matsyakaṇṭakam eva ca |
hemataptaṃ ghṛtaṃ prāśya tatkṣaṇād eva śudhyati ||
asthikaṇṭakaśālūkaśaṅkhaśuktikapardikāḥ |
pītvā navodakaṃ caiva pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
śālūkaḥ kīṭabhedaḥ | kapardikā varāṭikā | grāme 'lpakulyādigataughavāri avihitaśuddhikābhinavakūpādivāri vā navodakam |
śūdrahastena yo 'śnīyāt pānīyaṃ vā pibet kvacit |
ahorātroṣito bhūtvā pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
bhāryayā sodarair vāpi bhuktvā tūpavased dinam |
jñātibhiḥ syād ahorātraṃ trirātram itaraiḥ saha ||
tathā |
pibato grasato mūtraṃ nirgacchec chardikarmaṇi |
bahirantarmukhe lepān prakṣālya snānam ācaret ||
mūtranirgame chardau ca samanantaram eva bhojanatyāga etat |
aśnato 'nāturasyātha viprasya syād gudasravaḥ |
ucchiṣṭam aśucitvaṃ ca prāyaścittaṃ kathaṃ bhavet ||
ādau tu kṛtvā śaucaṃ vai paścāt tapta upaspṛśet |
ahorātroṣito bhūtvā pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
kratubhūmiṃ tu yaḥ prāpto dvijo bhuṅkte pramādataḥ |
gomūtrayāvakāhāraḥ pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
mūtroccāraṃ naraḥ kṛtvā hy akṛtvā śaucam ātmani |
mohāt pītvā trirātraṃ syād yavān pītvā viśudhyati ||
snānārhas tu ca yaḥ kaścit pibet khādeta vā dvijaḥ |
gāyatryās trisahasreṇa śudhyate nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
prāyaścittapūrvam evaitat | kāmakāraviṣayam etat | [1173] hārītaḥ: "lomnāṃ khādane hastadattabhojane 'prakṣālitapāṇipādasya bhojane tv adhikabhojane paṅktigatadūṣaṇe paṅktyagrato bhojane tyaktamūtrapurīṣakaraṇe mṛtakasūtakaśūdrānnabhojane śūdraiḥ svapne trirātram abhojanam | loṣṭamṛttikādane 'horātrābhojanāc chuddhiḥ" | saṃvartaḥ |
anācāntaḥ pibed yas tu api vā bhakṣayed dvijaḥ |
gāyatryaṣṭaśataṃ tasya prāyaścittaṃ vinirdiśet ||
śūdrādisparśanimittasnānaviṣayam etat | caṇḍālādisparśanimittasnānātikrame prāyaścittātirekaḥ |
vinā yajñopavītena bhuṅkte tu brāhmaṇo yadi |
snānaṃ kṛtvā japaṃ kurvann upavāsena śudhyati ||
hārītaḥ |
caṇḍālādīn dvijo 'śnaṃs tu dṛṣṭvā grāsaṃ parityajet |
antarbhāhyān mukhālepān prakṣālya snānam ācaret ||
bhuktvā trirātraṃ nāśnīyāt pītvā toyam aharniśam |
śvānaṃ dṛṣṭvā tathācāmed bhuktvā pītvā tadardhikam ||
śvānaṃ dṛṣṭvā tathācāmed eva, na tu snāyāt | yadi bhuktvā tv ahorātram upoṣya naktam aśnīyāt | pibec cen naktam eva bhuñjīta (?) | caturviṃśatimatāt |
uṣite chardite caiva bhinnabhājanabhojane |
ahorātroṣitaḥ snātvā pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
uṣite chardite pūrvedyur bhuktasya vamana ity arthaḥ |
ghṛtahīnaṃ tu yo bhuṅkte naras tv āhutipañcakam |
paścād ghṛtena yo bhuṅkte bhuktvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret ||
bhuñjānas tu yadā paśyec chvānam antyajagardabham |
ācamya prayato bhūtvā prāṇāyāmaṃ trir āyamet ||
dṛṣṭvā caiva tu yo bhuṅkte loluptvād dvijottamaḥ |
ahorātroṣito bhūtvā pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
ekapaṅktyāṃ tu bhuñjāno brāhmaṇaṃ tu yadā spṛśet |
tadannaśiṣṭaṃ bhuktvā vai gāyatryaṣṭaśataṃ japet || [1174]
ṣaṭtriṃśanmatāt |
samucchiṣṭas tu yo bhuṅkte bhuṅkte vā muktabhojanaḥ |
evaṃ vaivasvataḥ prāha bhuktvā sāṃtapanaṃ caret ||
jñānto 'bhyāsaviṣayam etat | vyāsaḥ |
sūtikādyais tu yad bhāṇḍaṃ spṛṣṭaṃ tatspṛṣṭinā tathā |
tatsthaṃ bhuktvā dvijo mohāt trirātreṇa viśudhyati ||
kṛtamūtrapurīṣasya yadā naivodakaṃ bhavet |
snānaṃ śaucaṃ ca daśabhiḥ prāṇāyāmair viśudhyati ||
snānaṃ śaucaṃ cety atra kṛtveti śeṣaḥ |
tailābhyaktas tathā vāntaḥ śmaśrukarmaṇi maithune |
mūtroccāraṃ yadā kuryāt trirātreṇai śudhyati ||
parāśaraḥ |
dīpocchiṣṭaṃ tu yat tailaṃ rātrau rathyāhṛtaṃ ca yat |
abhyaṅgāc caiva yac chiṣṭaṃ jagdhvā naktena śudhyati ||
atha parigrahaduṣṭānnabhojane tatrabhavatā yājñavalkyena "agnihīnasya nānnam aśnīyād anāpadi" ityādinā granthena svāmidoṣavaśād duṣṭam annaṃ pratipāditam | tatra manuḥ |
nāśrotriyatate yajñe grāmayājihute tathā |
striyā klībena ca hute bhuñjīta brāhmaṇaḥ kvacit ||
aślīlam etat sādhūnāṃ yatra juhvaty amī haviḥ |
pratīpam etad devānāṃ tasmāt tat parivarjayet ||
aślīlaṃ lajjākaram, pratīpaṃ pratikūlam |
mattakruddhāturāṇāṃ ca na bhuñjīta kadācana |
keśakīṭāvapannaṃ ca padā spṛṣṭaṃ ca kāmataḥ ||
bhrūṇaghāvekṣitaṃ caiva saṃspṛṣṭaṃ cāpy udakyayā |
patatriṇāvalīḍhaṃ ca śunā saṃspṛṣṭam eva ca ||
gavā cānnam upaghrātaṃ ghuṣṭānnaṃ ca viśeṣitaḥ |
gaṇānnaṃ gaṇikānnaṃ ca viduṣāṃ ca jugupsitam ||
stenagāyanayoś caiva takṣṇo vārdhuṣikasya ca |
abhiśastasya śaṇḍasya puṃścalyā dāmbhikasya ca ||
śuktaṃ paryuṣitaṃ caiva śūdrasyocchiṣṭam eva ca |
cikitsakasya mṛgayoḥ krūrasyocchiṣṭabhojinaḥ || [1175]
ugrānnaṃ sūtikānnaṃ ca paryāyānnam anirdaśam |
anarcitaṃ vṛthāmāṃsam avīrāyāś ca yoṣitaḥ ||
dviṣadannaṃ nagaryannaṃ patitānnam avakṣutam |
piśunānṛtinoś cānnaṃ kratuvikrayiṇas tathā ||
śailūṣatantuvāyānnaṃ kṛtaghnasyānnam eva ca |
karmārasya niṣādasya raṅgāvatarakasya ca ||
suvarṇakartur venasya śastravikrayiṇas tathā |
śvavatāṃ śauṇḍikānāṃ ca cailanirṇejakasya ca ||
rajakasya nṛśaṃsasya yasya copapatir gṛhe |
mṛṣyanti ye copapatiṃ strījitānāṃ ca sarvaśaḥ ||
anirdaśaṃ ca pretānnam atuṣṭikaram eva ca |
abhakṣyaprakaraṇe padārthā vyākhyātāḥ |
bhuktvāto 'nyatamasyānnam amatyā kṣapaṇaṃ tryaham |
matyā bhuktvā caret kṛcchraṃ reto viṇmūtram eva ca ||
abhojyānnaṃ ca bhuktvānnaṃ strīśūdrocchiṣṭam eva ca |
jagdhvā māṃsam abhakṣyaṃ ca saptarātraṃ yavān pibet ||
paiṭhīnasiḥ: "kunakhī śyāvadantaḥ pitrā vivadamānaḥ strījitaḥ kuṣṭhī piśunaḥ somavikrayī vāṇijako grāmayājako 'bhiśasto vṛṣalyām abhijātaḥ parivittiḥ parivividāno didhiṣūpatiḥ punarbhūputraś cauraḥ kāṇḍapṛṣṭhaḥ sevakaś cety abhojyānnā apāṅktyā(ktā) aśrāddhārhā eṣāṃ bhuktvā datvā cāvijñānāt trirātram" | śaṅkhaḥ: "abhiśastarajakapatitacākrikatailikagrāmayājaka-
śūdrasāṃvatsarikakulikasuvarṇakārakarmakārakarmāracitravṛttighoṣakatantuvāyaraṅgāvatārika-kūṭamānakṛcchauṇḍikavadhajīvinṛśaṃsātmavikrayivārdhuṣikatārikabhagavṛttivrātyataskara-
gaṇānnabhojaneṣv atikṛcchraṃ kuryāt" | [1176] kuliko devalakaḥ | ghoṣako mūlyena pāṭhakaḥ | tāriko nadyādisaṃtāraṇajīvī | abhyāsaviṣayam etat | viṣṇuḥ: "gaṇagaṇistenagāyanakānnāni bhuktvā saptarātraṃ payasā varteta" | sumantuḥ: "abhiśatapatitapaunarbhavabhrūṇaha-
puṃścalyaśuciśastrakāratailikacākrikadhvajikasuvarṇakārālekhyakapaṇḍabandhakīgaṇa-
gaṇikānnāni cābhojyāni | saukarivyādhaniṣādavaruṭa(ḍa)carmakārā abhojyānnā apratigrāhās tadaśanapratigrahayoś cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret" | sūkareṇa dīvyanti jīvati veti vaktavyādiṇī(ṇ?) | varuṭo(ḍo) veṇujīvī | buddhipūrvakabhūyobhyāsaviṣayam etat | hārītaḥ: "cākrikalohakārābhakṣyānnabhojane trirātram upavaset | gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ ca prāśnīyāt | dvajivaṇikkirāṭivārdhuṣikānnabhojane pañcarātraṃ kṛcchraṃ caret" | abhyāsaviṣayam etat | "puṃścalīveśyānnabhojane saptarātram | dīkṣitapravrajitagaṇoddhrāvaruddhānnabhojane cāndrāyaṇam | mṛtake sūtake daśarātram | brāhmaṇocchiṣṭānnamṛtakasūtakaikoddiṣṭabhojane trirātram ekarātraṃ vā | kṣatriyocchiṣṭānnamṛtasūtakaikoddiṣṭabhojane kṛcchram | vaiśyocchiṣṭānnasūtakamṛtakaikoddiṣṭabhojane kṛcchrātikṛcchram, śūdrocchiṣṭānnasūtaka-mṛtakaikoddiṣṭabhojane cāndrāyaṇam, mlecchānnabhojane ca tāmrakāṃsyasīsakāravyādhita-taskarānnabhojane ca taptakṛcchram | brāhmaṇavṛttighnavadhabandhopaghātavṛttyannabhojane paunarbhavasaukhasaṃkīrṇānnabhojane ca" | ekoddiṣṭaṃ navaśrāddhād anyat | kirāṭī vaṇigviśeṣaḥ | aṅgirāḥ |
abhojyānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ bhuktvā cānnam upaskṛtam |
antyāvasāyino bhuktvā kṛcchrapādena śudhyati ||
sakṛdamatipūrvake sāmarthyābhāve ca saty etat | [1177] śātātapaḥ |
yatra nāśnanti devāś ca pitaraś ca tathātithiḥ |
vṛthāpākaḥ sa vijñeyas tatra nādyāt kathaṃcana ||
yo gṛhītvā vihāyāgniṃ gṛhastha iti manyate |
annaṃ tasya na bhoktavyaṃ vṛthāpāko hi sa smṛtaḥ ||
vṛthāpākasya bhuñjānaḥ prāyaścittaṃ cared dvijaḥ |
prāṇāyāmaṃ trir abhyasya ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati ||
tathā |
parapākanivṛttasya parapākaratasya ca |
apacasya ca bhuktvānnaṃ dvijaś cāṇdrāyaṇaṃ caret ||
aninditapākād api nivṛttaḥ | apaco bhikṣuḥ | parāśaraḥ |
āpatkāle tu vipreṇa bhuktaṃ śūdragrahe yadi |
manastāpena śudhyet tu drupadānāṃ śataṃ japet ||
brahmapurāṇe |
yaś copanane(naṃ) cakre bahūnāṃ bahuyājakaḥ |
yajñārthaṃ krītasomasya dīkṣitasya mahādhvare ||
yatra cāśrotriyas tv anyas tv atha vā grāmayājakaḥ |
hotā klībo 'tha nārī vā nāśnīyād yatra kutracit ||
tapoyajñaphalānāṃ ca yaḥ kretā yaś ca vikrayī |
puraḥ purohitaṃ kālam uddiśya nṛpater hitam ||
karoti yaḥ sa kathitas tv abhojyānnaḥ purohitaḥ |
mṛgavyādho vyālajīvī madyapaḥ śauṇḍiko dhvajī ||
tilaniṣpīḍakaś cakrī rajako vastraśaucakṛt |
celanirṇejako nīlaraktapītopagāgakṛt ||
nṛśaṃsas tu bhaved dvaṃdvī nṛṇāṃ śaṃsāṃ karoti yaḥ|
nigrahādhikṛto yas tu vijñeyaḥ sa hi dāṇḍikaḥ ||
baddhānāṃ rakṣitā yas tu sa ca bandhanako bhavet |
avijñātakulācāro vijñeyo 'tha videśagaḥ ||
kitavo dyūtakāraś ca hemacauraḥ suvarṇakṛt |
dārāḥ paṇyaṃ ca yasyeha sa ca dāropajīvakaḥ ||
bhaved athopapatiko jāyājāraparas tu yaḥ |
bhāṇḍakartā kulālas tu citrakṛc citrakarmakṛt ||
vṛddhiṃ paṇyeṣu yo bhuṅkte sa tu vārdhuṣiko 'dhamaḥ |
carmakṛc carmaṇāṃ chettā saunikaḥ paśumārakaḥ || [1178]
rogabhṛtyupajīvī ca śalākī ca bhiṣaṅnaraḥ |
sūcī syāt tunnavāyas tu vastrakṛt tantuvāyakaḥ ||
vṛttyarthaṃ savikāras tu raṅgāvatarako naṭaḥ |
vṛttyanveṣī naṭānāṃ tu sa tu śaulūṣakaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
śaṇḍo mukhebhagaś cātha durlomā duṣṭalomavān |
vaino dhanurdaṇḍakartā karmāraḥ śaraghāṭakaḥ ||
niṣādo matsyajīvī ca tathānyau kuṇḍagolakau |
ābālyādyas tv adaśanas tv ajadaṃśaḥ sa ucyate ||
śayyāyāṃ vyādhinā kṣīṇas tv avasannaś ca tādṛśaḥ |
svajātivihitād dharmāt pracyutaḥ patito bhavet ||
yasyopanayanāt kālo gataḥ sa vrātya eva hi |
bahūnāṃ dīptimān ekaḥ sa tu rājā mahāyaśāḥ ||
ugraṃ daṇḍaṃ pātayate sa tu tīkṣṇogra ucyate |
jahāti mitraṃ yaś cānyaṃ sa kṛtaghnas tu kīrtyate ||
hatvā parān yas tu jīved vadhajīvī sa kīrtitaḥ |
prakāśadharmo guptāghaḥ sa tu dāmbhika eva hi ||
dharmaṃ tv adharmarūpeṇa yaḥ karoti sa cākrikaḥ |
pareṣāṃ maraṇārthāya śastrakṛc chastravikrayī ||
eteṣāṃ ca na bhoktavyaṃ dīnasya rudatas tathā |
karuṇākranditasyātha chadmanā garadasya ca ||
yatīnāṃ liṅgināṃ caiva tathā pāṣaṇḍinām api |
strījitasya ca nāśnīyād annam asvāmikasya ca ||
duhituś cāprasūtāyās tathākalpasya vā kvacit |
asaṃmānaṃ tu guṇināṃ saṃmānaṃ pāpakarmiṇām ||
samaṃ vā kurute yas tu tadannaṃ naiva bhakṣayet |
śūdrānumantritaṃ yac ca brāhmaṇānnaṃ ca vā kvacit ||
na bhakṣayeta śūdrānnaṃ brāhmaṇenānumantritam |
mattonmattāturāṇāṃ ca mumūrṣor vā kadācana ||
nigaḍair atha baddhasya caurasya vṛjinasya ca |
abhiśastasya takṣṇaś ca kadaryasya ca jālminaḥ || [1179]
cikitsikasya baddhasya krūrasyocchiṣṭabhojinaḥ |
putrabhartṛvirodhinyāḥ striyāḥ paṇyastriyās tathā ||
nagarādhipateḥ śatroḥ piśunānṛtinos tathā |
sāvajñasyātha mūrkhasyāsatkārasyātha durmateḥ ||
asaṃskṛtam avijñātaṃ saroṣaṃ vismayānvitam |
bhakṣyaṃ tv abhakṣyavākyena(ṇa) yo dadyād roṣamānasaḥ ||
guror api na bhoktavyaṃ vāgduṣṭaṃ tan mahāghadam |
asacchūdrāc ca pakvānnaṃ nādadyād aśraddhayānvitam ||
yasyāgnikāryaṃ na gṛhe nāgrabhug brāhmaṇas tathā|
tadgṛhe nānnam aśnīyāt pravāsiny agnihotriṇi ||
kaḥ kṣudhārtaś ca ko bhuṅkte mamakaṃ tv annam eva hi |
yaśo'rthe yas tu kathayet tad vāgduṣṭaṃ na bhakṣayet ||
eteṣāṃ tu gṛhe bhuktvā vane cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret |
kāmato bhūyo'bhyāsaviṣayam etat | tathā |
yogo maghātrayodaśyoḥ kuñjaracchāyasaṃjñakaḥ |
bhaven maghāyāṃ saṃsthendau haṃse caiva kare sthite ||
mṛtake sūtake cātha grastayoś candrasūrayoḥ |
chāyāyāṃ kuñjarasyātha bhuktvā tu narakaṃ vrajet ||
bhuktvā pramādād vipras tu samyak cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret |
baudhāyanaḥ |
brahmaudane ca some ca sīmantonnayane tathā |
jātaśrāddhe navaśrāddhe bhuktvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret ||
bharadvājaḥ |
pakṣe vā yadi vā māse na yasyāśnāti vai dvijaḥ |
bhuktvā durātmanas tasya dvijaś cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret ||
vṛddhayājñavalkyaḥ |
yatiś ca brahmacārī ca pakvānnasvāmināv ubhau |
tayor annaṃ na bhoktavyaṃ bhuktvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret ||
caturviṃśatimatāt |
nirācārasya viprasya niṣiddhācaraṇasya ca |
annaṃ bhuktvā naraḥ kuryād dinam ekam abhojanam ||
ācāryāgnigurudveṣṭur dharmaśāstradviṣas tathā |
gobrāhmaṇadviṣaś caiva tad eva vratam ācaret || [1180]
upapātakayuktasya cābdam ekaṃ nirantaram |
annaṃ bhuktvā naraḥ kuryāt parākaṃ tu viśodhanam ||
yaḥ samagraṃ varṣam upapātakayuktas tasyānnam abhyāsato matyā cāsakṛd bhuktavata etat | hārītaḥ |
patitānnaṃ yadā bhuṅkte vipraś caṇḍālaveśmani |
māsārdhaṃ tu pibed vāri māsaṃ kāmakṛte tathā ||
caṇḍālaveśmanīti caṇḍālānnopalakṣaṇam | chāgaleyaḥ |
ajñānād bhuñjate viprāḥ sūtake mṛtake 'pi vā |
prāṇāyāmaśataṃ kṛtvā śudhyante śūdrasūtake ||
vaiśye ṣaṣṭir bhaved rājñi viṃśatir brāhmaṇe daśa |
ekāhaṃ ca tryahaṃ pañca saptarātram abhojanam ||
tataḥ śucir bhaved vipraḥ pañcagavyaṃ pibet tataḥ |
brāhmaṇādikrameṇaikāhādayaḥ prāṇāyāmaiḥ samuditā jñeyāḥ | āpadi sakṛd bhojana etat | mārkaṇḍeyaḥ |
bhuktvā tu kṣatriyāśauce taptakṛcchro vidhīyate |
vaiśyāśauce tathā bhuktvā mahāsāṃtapanaṃ caret ||
śūdrasyaiva tathā bhuktvā dvijaś cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret |
sakṛtkāmakāraviṣayam etat | śaṅkhaḥ |
śūdrasya sūtake bhuktvā ṣaṇmāsān vratam ācaret |
vaiśyāśauce tathā bhuktvā trīn māsān vratam ācaret ||
brāhmaṇasya tathāśauce māsam ekaṃ vrati bhavet |
kāmato bhūyo'bhyāsaviṣayam etat | vrataṃ gomūtrayāvakāśanam | parāśaraḥ |
ajñānād bhuñjate viprāḥ sūtake mṛtake 'pi vā |
prāyaścittaṃ kathaṃ teṣāṃ varṇe varṇe vinirdiśet ||
gāyatryaṣṭasahasreṇa śuddhiḥ syāc chūdrasūtake |
viśaḥ pañcaśatenaiva kṣatriyasya śatena tu ||
brāhmaṇasya yadā bhuṅkte prāṇāyāmena śudhyati |
atha vā vāmadevyena sāmnā caikena śudhyati || [1181]
upavādādyasamarthasyāpadi sakṛd ajñānād bhuñjata etat | śātātapaḥ |
brahmakṣatraviśāṃ bhuktvā na doṣaś cāgnihotriṇām |
sūtake śāvaśauce tu asthisaṃcayanāt param ||
āpadi brāhmaṇādīnām agnihotriṇāṃ sūtake śāvaśauce 'nnasya bhoktur adoṣaḥ | tathaiva teṣāṃ mṛtakasūtake 'sthisaṃcayanād ūrdhvam |
annasatrapravṛttānām āmam annam agarhitam |
bhuktvā pakvānnam eteṣāṃ trirātraṃ tu payaḥ pibet ||
aśaucānnabhoktṛbhir aśaucavyapagame vā prāyaścittaṃ kāryam ity āha viṣṇuḥ: "brāhmaṇādīnāṃ yaḥ sakṛd evānnam aśnāti tasya tāvad evāśaucaṃ yāvat teṣām aśaucavyapagame prāyaścittaṃ kuryāt | savarṇasyāśauce dvijo bhuktvā sravantīm āsādya tannimagnas trir aghamarṣaṇaṃ japtvottīrya gāyatryaṣṭasahasraṃ japet | kṣatriyāśauce brāhmaṇas trirātropoṣitaḥ kṛtvā śudhyati | vaiśyāśauce brāhmaṇas trirātropoṣitaś ca, brāhmaṇāśauce rājanyaḥ kṣatriyāśauce vaiśyaḥ sravantīm āsādya gāyatrīśatapañcakaṃ japet | vaiśyaś ca brāhmaṇāśauce gāyatryaṣṭaśataṃ japet | śūdrāśauce dvijo bhuktvā prājāpatyaṃ samācaret | śūdraś ca dvijāśauce snānam ācaret | śūdrāśauce śūdraḥ snātvā pañcagavyaṃ pibet" | aṣṭasahasram aṣṭābhir adhikaṃ sahasram | saṃvartaḥ |
viprānnaṃ sūtake vipro bhuktvā nādyād dinatrayam |
pītvā pānīyam ajñānāt pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
"anirdaśāhe maraṇe niyogād bhuktavān dvijaḥ |
krimir bhūtvā sa dehānte tāṃ viṣṭhāṃ samupāśnute ||
dvādaśa māsān dvādaśārdhamāsān vānaśnan saṃhitādhyayanam adhīyānaḥ pūto bhavatīti vijñāyate" | kāmato dvādaś māsā akāmatas tadardham | ubhayaṃ caitad atyantābhyāse | anaśnan prāg bhojanād ity arthaḥ | [1182] śaṅkhaḥ |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ navaśrāddhe parāko māsike smṛtaḥ |
pakṣatraye 'tikṛcchraḥ syāt ṣaṇmāse kṛcchra eva tu ||
ābdike pādakṛcchraḥ syād ekāhaḥ punar ābdike |
ata ūrdhvaṃ na doṣaḥ syāc chaṅkasya vacanaṃ yathā ||
kāmata etat | caturviṃśatimatāt |
prājāpatyaṃ navaśrāddhe pādonaṃ māsike tathā |
pādonaṃ kṛcchram uddiṣṭaṃ ṣaṇmāse 'tha tathābdike ||
trirātraṃ cānyamāseṣu pratyabdaṃ ced ahaḥ smṛtam |
amatipūrvake sakṛd bhojana etat | matipūrvake 'py āpadi vā | tathā |
śūdrasya tu navaśrāddhe carec cāndrāyanatrayam |
cāndrāyaṇadvayaṃ māse tripakṣe tv aindavaṃ smṛtam ||
āmaśrāddhe bhaved ardhaṃ prājāpatyaṃ tu sarvadā |
sakṛt kāmata etat | ṣaṭtriṃśanmatāt |
prājāpatyaṃ navaśrāddhe pādahīnaṃ tu misrake |
traipakṣike tadardhaṃ tu pañcagavyaṃ dvimāsike ||
asamarthasyāpadi sakṛd āmaśrāddhaṃ gṛhītavata etat | miśrakaṃ prathamasaṃvatsarāntargataśrāddham | viṣṇuḥ: "āmaśrāddhāśane trirātraṃ payaśā varteta" | pratiṣiddhaśrāddhaviṣayam etat | śūdraśrāddhaviṣayaṃ vā | bharadvājaḥ |
apāṅkteyān samuddiśya śrāddham ekādaśe 'hani |
brāhmaṇas tv atra bhuktvānnaṃ śiśucāndrāyaṇaṃ caret ||
apāṅkteyāḥ śrāddhaprakaraṇa uktāḥ | taddevate tathaikādaśāhike śrāddhe bhuktvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ cared ity arthaḥ | mārkaṇḍeyaḥ |
āmaśrāddhe tathā bhuktvā taptakṛcchreṇa śudhyati |
saṃkalpite tathā bhuktvā trirātraṃ kṣapaṇaṃ bhavet ||
śūdrānnaviṣayam etat | aṅgirāḥ |
daśakṛtvaḥ pibed āpo (ec cāpo) gāyatryā cābhimantritāḥ |
saṃdhyātrayam upāsīta japec ca juhuyāt tataḥ || [1183]
daśakṛtva iti jalapānābhyāso vyākhyeyaḥ | samabhivyāhṛtakriyābhyāvṛttigaṇane kṛtvasuc pratyayavidhānāt | gayatry api sakṛd eva vācyā | ekadravyasābhyāsapānaprayogāṅgatvāt | likhitaḥ |
yasya cāgnau na kriyate yasya cānnaṃ na dīyate |
na tad bhojyaṃ dvijātīnāṃ bhuktvā copavased ahaḥ ||
bharadvājaḥ |
śiṣṭā nāśnanti yat kiṃcid annaṃ mūlaphalādikam |
tad bhuktvā brāhmaṇaḥ kuryāt prāyaścittam abhojanam ||
ity upapātakaprāyaścittani |
atha jātibhraṃśakarādiprāyaścittāni vakṣyante | tatra manuḥ |
brāhmaṇasya rujaḥ kṛtyaṃ ghrātir aghreyamadyayoḥ |
jaihmyaṃ puṃsi ca maithunyaṃ jātibhraṃśakaraṃ smṛtam ||
rujaḥ kṛtyaṃ rogotpādanam | aghreyaṃ pūtigandhi laśunaprabhṛti |
jātibhraṃśakaraṃ karma kṛtvānyatamam icchayā |
caret sāṃtapanaṃ kṛcchraṃ prājāpatyam anicchayā ||
kharoṣṭramṛgabāṇānām ajāvikavadhas tathā |
saṃkarīkaraṇaṃ jñeyaṃ mīnāhimahiṣasya ca ||
tathā |
ninditebhyo dhanādānaṃ vāṇijyaṃ śūdrasevanam |
apātrīkaraṇaṃ jñeyam asabhyasya ca bhāṣaṇam ||
asabhyaṃ sabhānarhaṃ maithunatatkārakādivacanam |
saṃkarāpātrakṛtyāsu māsaḥ śodhanam aindavaḥ |
tathā |
kṛmikīṭavayohatyā maghānugatabhojanam |
phalaidhakusumasteyam adhairyaṃ ca malāvaham ||
malinīkaraṇīyeṣu taptaḥ syād yāvakas tryaham |
śītakṛcchreṇa vā śuddhir mohāt sāṃtapanena vā ||
malinīkaraṇīyeṣu taptakṛcchraṃ viśodhanam |
saṃkarīkaraṇaṃ kṛtvā māsam aśnīta yāvakam ||
kṛcchātikṛcchram atha vā pājāpatyam athāpi vā |
apātrīkaraṇaṃ kṛtvā taptakṛcchreṇa śudhyati ||
abhyāsānubandhatāratamyāpekṣayā matipūrvakeṣu saṃkarīkaraṇāpātrīkaraṇamalinīkaraṇīyeṣ [1184] sarveṣu vā kṛteṣv etāni | mānavaṃ tu sakṛd amatyā pratyekakaraṇe | atra bārhaspatyo viśeṣaḥ |
brāhmaṇasya rujaḥ kṛtvā gardabhādipramāpaṇam |
ninditebhyo dhanādānaṃ kṛcchrārdhaṃ vratam ācaret ||
jātibhraṃśakarādigaṇamadhyapaṭhitānāṃ pāpānāṃ yāni manunaiva prāyaścittāntarāṇy uktāni tair eṣāṃ vikalpaḥ | tasya ca yathāsāmārthyaṃ vyavasthā || 3.289 ||
athānyāni prāyaścittāny āha |
prāṇāyāmaṃ jale kṛtvā kharayānoṣṭrayānagaḥ || 3.290 ||
nagnaḥ snātvā ca bhuktā ca gatvā caiva divā striyam |
kharayuktaṃ yānaṃ kharayānam | evam uṣṭrayānam, tadgas tadārohī nagno vivasanaḥ snātvā bhuktvā vāhni svadārān upetya vā bhūmiṣṭho jale snātvā prāṇāyāmam ācaret | kāmata etat | tad āha manuḥ |
uṣṭrayānaṃ samāruhya kharayānaṃ ca kāmataḥ |
vivāsā jalam āplutya prāṇāyāmena śudhyati ||
kāmata iti vacanād akāmataḥ snānamātram | viṣṇuḥ: "uṣṭreṇa khareṇa vā gatvā nagnaḥ snātvā suptvā bhuktvā vā prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kuryāt" | abhyāsaviṣayam etat | atyantābhyāse tv āha yamaḥ |
kharayānam uṣṭrayānaṃ yo 'dhirohed dvijottamaḥ |
apo vā niviśen nagnas trirātraṃ kṣapaṇaṃ bhavet ||
śaṅkhaḥ |
divā tu maithunaṃ kṛtvā nagnaḥ snatvā tathāmbhasi |
nagnāṃ parastriyaṃ dṛṣṭvā dinam ekaṃ vrataṃ caret || 3.290 ||
guruṃ tvaṃkṛtya huṃkṛtya vipraṃ nirjitya vādataḥ |
baddhvā vā vāsasā kṣipraṃ prasādyopavased dinam || 3.291 ||
guruṃ garīyāṃsaṃ tvaṃkṛtya bhartsanārthaṃ tvamādiśabdaṃ guruṃ prati prayujya huṃkāraś ca yo hīnapuruṣaṃ prati bhartsanāya prayujyate taṃ vā kṛtvā vipraṃ vādādikathayā nijitva vasasāpi baddhvā kṣipraṃ śīghraṃ prasādya vigatakopaṃ kṛtvā naktaṃ bhuñjītety arthaḥ | [1185] manuḥ |
huṃkāraṃ brāhmaṇasyoktvā tvaṃkaraṃ ca garīyasaḥ |
snātvānaśnann ahaḥśeṣam abhivādya prasādayet ||
tāḍayitvā tṛṇenāpi kaṇṭhe vābadhya vāsasā |
vivāde vā vinirjitya praṇipatya prasādayet ||
yamaḥ |
pādena brāhmaṇaṃ spṛṣṭvā prāyaścittaṃ vidhīyate |
divasopoṣitaḥ snātvā abhivādya prasādayet ||
sānbandha etat | tathā |
avācyaṃ brāhmaṇasyoktvā prāyaścittaṃ vidhīyate |
kṛcchrātikṛcchraṃ kṛtvā tu praṇipatya prasādayet ||
āveṣṭya brāhmaṇaṃ kaṇṭhe prāyaścittaṃ vidhīyate |
trirātropoṣitaṃ snātvā praṇipatya prasādayet ||
pīḍātiśaye 'nubandhātiśaye caitat |
ākrośam anṛtaṃ hiṃsām anurūpāṃ samācaret |
ekarātraṃ trirātraṃ vā ṣaḍrātraṃ vā vidhīyate || 3.291 ||
kiṃ ca |
vipradaṇḍodyame kṛcchras tv atikṛcchro nipātane |
kṛcchrātikṛcchro 'sṛkpāte kṛcchro 'nyantaraśoṇite || 3.292 ||
vipraṃ prati prahārecchayā daṇḍodyame kṛcchraḥ prāyaścittam, daṇḍanipātane tv atikṛcchraḥ, phṛtād vipraśarīrād asṛjo rudirasya pāte srāve kṛcchrātikṛcchraḥ | tvaco 'bhyantare śoṇitotpādane prājāpatyaḥ kṛcchraḥ | bṛhaspatiḥ |
kāṣṭādinā tāḍayitvā tvagbhede kṛcchram ācaret |
asthibhede tu kṛcchraṃ tu parākaṃ cāṅgakartane ||
yamaḥ |
avagūrya cared kṛcchram atikṛcchraṃ nipātane |
kṛcchrātikṛcchraṃ kurvīta cāndrāyaṇam athāpi vā ||
daśasaṃkhyāś ca gā dadyād aṅgacchedo yadā bhavet |
viṣṇuḥ |
viprasya cadhasaṃyuktaṃ śapathaṃ kārayan mṛṣā |
brahmahā yavakānnena tīvraṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret ||
kṣatriyasya parākaṃ syāt prājāpatyaṃ tathā viśaḥ |
vṛṣalasya trirātraṃ tu vrataṃ śūdrahaṇaś caret || [1186]
anyāny api prakīrṇaprāyaścittāni(tta)vidhāyakāni smṛtyantarāṇi kānicit pradarśyante | tatra bhaviṣyatpurāṇam |
nīlīmadhye yadā gacchet pramādād brāhmaṇaḥ kvacit |
ahorātroṣito bhūtvā pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
kuryād ajñānato yas tu nīlījaṃ dantadhāvanam |
ekarātraṃ nirāhāraḥ pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
romakūpe yadā gacched rajo nīlyās tu kasyacit |
triṣu varṇeṣu sāmānyaṃ taptakṛcchraṃ viśodhanam ||
pālanaṃ tu vyayaś caiva tadvṛddher upajīvanam |
pātityaṃ tu bhaved vipre tribhiḥ kṛcchrair viśodhanam ||
nīlīdāru yadā bhindyād brāhmaṇasya śarīrataḥ |
śoṇitaṃ dṛśyate tatra dvijaś cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret ||
śarīrata iti dvitīyārthe tasiḥ | śaṅkhaḥ |
adhyāsya śayanaṃ yānam āsanaṃ pāduke tathā |
palāśasya dvijaśreṣṭhas trirātraṃ tu vratī bhavet ||
kṣiptvāgnāv aśucidravyaṃ tad evāmbhasi mānavaḥ |
māsam ekaṃ vrataṃ kuryād upākruśya tathā gurum ||
upākrośyetipāṭhe ādhikyenākramya |
dvau viprau brāhmaṇāgnī ca dampatī tau dvijottamau |
antareṇa yadā gacchet kṛcchraṃ sāmtapanaṃ caret ||
homakāle tathā dohe svādhyāye dārasaṃgrahe |
antareṇa yadā gacched dvijaś cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret ||
dārānnadhanalābhānāṃ buddhvā vighnaṃ samācaret |
pratyekaṃ prākṛtaṃ kuryād brahmacaryaṃ tu vārṣikam ||
bhūmikanyānṛteṣu gomūtrayāvakaṃ samaśnīyāt | duḥsvapnāriṣṭadarśane ca ghṛtaṃ hiraṇyaṃ ca dadyāt | atriḥ |
āhitāgnir upasthānaṃ na kuryād yad tu parvaṇi |
ṛtau na gacched yo bhāryāṃ so 'pi kṛcchrārdham ācaret || [1187]
upasthānam agnisamīpe sthānam | manuḥ |
vinādbhir apsu vāpy antaḥ śārīraṃ saṃniveśya tu |
sacailaṃ bahir āplutya gām ālabhya viśudhyati ||
śārīraṃ mūtrādyutsargaḥ | yamaḥ |
aṭavyām aṭamānasya brāhmaṇasya viśeṣataḥ |
pranaṣṭasalile deśe kathaṃ śuddhir vidhīyate ||
apo dṛṣṭvaiva vipras tu kuryāc chaucaṃ sacailakam |
gāyatryaṣṭaśataṃ japyaṃ snānam etat paraṃ bhavet ||
deśaṃ kālaṃ samāsādya avasthām ātmanas tathā |
dharmaśauce 'vatiṣṭheta na kuryād vegadhāraṇam ||
vego malavegaḥ | vasiṣṭhaḥ: "daṇḍyotsarge rājaikarātram upavaset | trirātraṃ purohitaḥ | kṛcchram adaṇḍyadaṇḍane purohitaḥ | trirātraṃ rājā" | hārītaḥ: "grāmyopayogaṃ vānaprasthānāṃ cāndrāyaṇam | svadharmācāraniyamātikrame phalacāndrāyaṇam" | vasiṣṭhaḥ: "vānaprastho dīkṣābhede kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caritvā mahākakṣaṃ vardhayet" | dīkṣābhedo brahmacaryavyatiriktanijadharmavyatikramaḥ | brahmacaryātikrame tu bhikṣoḥ ṣāṇmāsikaṃ kṛcchrācaraṇaṃ vakṣyate | vānaprasthasyāpi tad eva, bhikṣutulyatvāt | mahākakṣaṃ mahāvanam | tasya vṛddhir jalasekādinā | sa evāha: "bhikṣur vānaprasthavat somavṛddhiṃ varjayet" | bhikṣur yatiḥ | svadharmātikrame vānaprasthavad vrataṃ kuryāt | somavṛddhiṃ kakṣavṛddhiṃ varjayet | śaṅkhaḥ |
pādapratāpanaṃ kṛtvā kṛtvā vahniṃ tathāpy adhaḥ |
kuśaiḥ pramārjya pādau tu dinam ekaṃ bhaved vratī ||
snātakavratādhikāre kratuḥ: "eteṣām ācārāṇām ekaikasya vyabhicāre gāyatryaṣṭaśataṃ japyaṃ kṛtvā pūto bhavati" | [1188] manuḥ |
vedoditānāṃ nityānāṃ karmaṇāṃ samatikrame |
snātakavratalope ca prāyaścittam abhojanam ||
abhojanam upavāsaḥ | anuktaprāyaścittaviśeṣātikrama etat | hārītaḥ: "śayyārūḍhapādukopānad-āropitapādocchiṣṭāndhakārasthāśuddhaprāptakṛcchradevapūjāniratetyevamādīnām abhivādane dvirātram upavāsaḥ | nimantrite tadanyatra bhojane trirātram" | kratuḥ |
śudhyaty āhnikavicchede trirātram upavāsataḥ |
bahudaivasikabhraṃśe tad eva dviguṇaṃ bhavet ||
bahudivasiko bhraṃśo bahudaivasikaḥ | hārītaḥ: "ucchiṣṭasya gamana ekāham upavāsaḥ | na gṛhī niyamātikrame 'śnīyāt" | aṅgirāḥ |
anāpadi cared yas tu siddhabhikṣāṃ gṛhe vasan |
daśarātraṃ pibed vajram āpatkāle tryahaṃ dvijaḥ ||
pratimāsam idaṃ proktam abhakṣyaṃ pratiśaṅkayā |
pratisaṃvatsaraṃ caiva śrotriyasya vidhīyate ||
vahan kamaṇḍaluṃ riktam asnāto 'śnaṃś ca bhojanam |
ahorātreṇa śuddhiḥ syād dinajapyena vā śuciḥ ||
ṛṣyaśṛṅgaḥ |
indracāpaṃ ca śālāgniṃ yady anyasya pradarśayet |
prāyaścittam ahorātraṃ dhanur daṇḍaś ca dakṣiṇā ||
yamaḥ |
ketanaṃ karayitvā tu yo 'tipātayate dvijaḥ |
brahmahatyām avāpnoti śūdrayonau ca jāyate ||
etasminn enasi prāpte brāhmaṇo niyatavrataḥ |
yaticāndrāyaṇaṃ cīrtvā tasmāt pāpāt pramucyate ||
ketanaṃ nimantraṇam | atipātanam atikramaḥ | nimantritāya bhojanāpradānam it yāvat | tathā |
nadīsaṃgamahartuś ca kanyāvighnakarasya ca |
same viṣamakartuś ca niṣkṛtir nopapadyate || [1189]
trayāṇām api caiteṣāṃ pratyāpattiṃtu mārgatām |
bhaikṣeṇa ca vrataṃ māsaṃ suddhyau cāndrāyaṇaṃ smṛtam ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
anirvartya mahāyajñān yo bhuṅkte pratyahaṃ gṛhī |
anāturaḥ sati dhane sa tu kṛcchrārdham ācaret ||
śaṅkhaḥ |
pretasya pretakāryāṇi akṛtvā dhanahārakaḥ |
varṇānāṃ yad vadhe proktaṃ tadardhaṃ prayataś caret ||
atimānād atikrodhād bhayād ajñānato 'pi vā |
tad badhnīyāt strī pumān vā gatir yeṣāṃ na vidyate ||
pūyaśoṇitasaṃpūrṇe tamasy andhe sudāruṇe |
ṣaṣṭiṃ varṣasahasrāṇi narakaṃ paryupāsate ||
gobhir hataṃ tathodbaddhaṃ brāhmaṇena ca ghātitam |
saṃspṛśanti tu ye viprā agnidā garadāś ca ye ||
anuyātāro 'pi cānye ye cānye pāśachedakāḥ |
sarve te pāpasaṃyuktās teṣāṃ vakṣyāmi niṣkṛtim ||
taptakṛcchreṇa śudhyet tu kuryād brāhmaṇabhojanam |
anaḍutsahitāṃ gāṃ ca dadyād viprāya dakṣiṇām ||
saṃvartaḥ |
gobhir hate tathā vipre tathā caivātmaghātini |
naivāśrupātanaṃ kāryaṃ sadbhiḥ śreyo'bhikāṅkṣibhiḥ ||
eṣām anyatamaṃ pretaṃ yo vaheta daheta vā |
kaṭodakakriyāṃ kṛtvā taptakṛcchraṃ samācaret ||
tacchavaṃ kevalaṃ spṛṣṭam asru vā patitaṃ yadi |
pūrvoktānām akartā ced ekarātram abhojanam ||
ya ātmatyāginaḥ kuryāt senhāt pretakriyāṃ naraḥ |
sa taptakṛcchrasahitaṃ carec cāndrāyaṇaṃ vratam ||
buddhipūrvakam etat | yamaḥ |
nāśaucaṃ nodakaṃ nāsru nāpavādānukampane |
brahmadaṇḍahatānāṃ ca na karyaṃ kaṭadhāraṇam ||
snehakāryabhayādibhyo yas tv etāni samācaret |
gomūtrayāvakāhāraḥ sa tu kṛcchreṇa śudhyati ||
etat samuditānāṃ ca karmaṇāṃ matipūrvake | [1190]
saṃvartaḥ |
voḍhṝṇām agnidātṝṇāṃ saṃvidhānavidhāyinām |
taptakṛcchradvayāc chuddhir ekam evānuyāyinām ||
etad api samuditakaraṇe | uśanā |
prāyānāśakaśastrāgniviṣodbandhabhṛgūdakaiḥ |
kāṣṭhādaiś cātmano hantur nṛpabrahmasarīsṛpaiḥ ||
śṛṅgidaṃṣṭrinakhivyālavidyutābhihatasya ca |
tathā saṃkarajātasya nāśaucodakavahnayaḥ ||
tatsparśe yadi cākrośe dinam ekam abhojanam |
ajñānād vahanādau tu kṛcchraṃ sāṃtapanaṃ smṛtam ||
buddhipūrve punas tasmin kṛcchraṃ gomūtrayāvakaḥ |
taptakṛcchro 'py aśaktau tu māsaṃ bhikṣāśano 'pi vā ||
kṛtvā ca vahanādīni prāyaścittam akurvatām |
taptakṛcchradvayāc chuddhir ekam evānuyāyinām ||
yas tu śeṣāḥ kriyāḥ kuryāt snehān mūlyena vā punaḥ |
bhavet tasya punas taptakṛcchracāndrāyaṇottaraḥ ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
viṣodbandhanaśastreṇa yas tv ātmānaṃ pramāpayet |
mṛto medhyena leptavyo nānyaṃ saṃskāram arhati ||
pāśacchettā tu yas tasya voḍhā vahnipradas tathā |
so 'pi kṛcchreṇa śudhyet tu ghātako 'pi narādhamaḥ ||
dakṣaḥ |
ārūḍhapatitaṃ vipraṃ caṇḍālāśvaviniḥsṛtam |
udbandhanamṛtaṃ caiva spṛṣṭvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret ||
sumantuḥ: "udbandhanapāśacchedanavahaneṣu māsaṃ bhaikṣabhakṣaṇaṃ triṣavaṇaṃ syāt" | cyavanaḥ: "ātmaghātakasya sparśanvahanadahane taptakṛcchraṃ caret | viṃśatigāvo dakṣiṇā brāhmaṇeṣu" | ādipurāṇam |
śṛṅgidaṃṣṭrinakhivyālaviṣavahnimahājalaiḥ |
sudūrāt parihartavyaḥ kurvan krīḍāṃ mṛtas tu yaḥ ||
nāgānāṃ vipriyaṃ kurvan dagdhaś cāpy atha vidyutā |
nigṛhītās tu ye rājñā cauryadoṣeṇa kutracit || [1191]
paradārān harantaś ca roṣāt tatpatibhir hatāḥ |
asamānais tu saṃkīrṇaiś caṇḍālādyais tathā hatāḥ ||
caurāgniviṣadāś caiva pāṣāṇḍāḥ krūrabuddhayaḥ |
krodhāt prāyaṃ viṣaṃ vahniṃ śastram udbandhanaṃ jalam ||
girivṛkṣaprapātān vā ye kurvanti narādhamāḥ |
kuśilpajīvino ye ca ye cālaṃkārakāriṇaḥ ||
mukhebhagās tu ye kecit klībaprāyā napuṃsakāḥ |
brahmadaṇḍahatā ye ca ye ca vā brāhmaṇair hatāḥ ||
mahāpātakino ye ca patitās te prakīrtitāḥ |
patitānāṃ na dāhaḥ syān nāntyeṣṭir nāsthisaṃcayaḥ ||
na cāsrupātaḥ piṇḍo vā kāryaṃ śrāddhādikaṃ kvacit |
etāni patitānām tu yaḥ karoti vimohitaḥ ||
taptakṛcchradvayenaiva tasya śuddhir na cānyathā ||
parāśaraḥ |
caṇḍālena śvapākena gobhir vipro hato yadi |
āhitāgnir mṛto vipro viṣeṇātmahato 'pi vā ||
lokāgninā sa dagdhavyo mantrasaṃskāravarjitaḥ |
spraṣṭā dagdhā ca voḍhā ca sapiṇḍeṣu ca sarvaśaḥ ||
prājāpatyaṃ caret paścād viprāṇām anuśāsanam |
dagdhvāsthīni punar gṛhya kṣīre prakṣālayed budhaḥ ||
svenāgninā punar dāhaḥ svamantreṇa pṛthak pṛthak ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ: "jīvann ātmatyāgāt kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caret trirātraṃ copavasen nityaklinnena vāsasā prāṇān ātmani cāyamya triḥ paṭhed aghamarṣaṇam |
atha vaitena kalpena gāyatrīṃ parivartayet |
api vāgniṃ samādhāya kūṣmāṇḍair juhuyād ghṛtam ||
yad anyan mahāpātakebhyaḥ sarvam etena pūyate | athācāmet | agniś ca mā manyuś ca manyupatayaś ca manyukṛtebhyaḥ | pāpebhyo rakṣantām | yad ahnā pāpam akārṣam | manasā vācā hastābhyām | padbhyām udareṇa śiśnā | ahas tad avalumpatu | [1192] yat kiṃ ca duritaṃ mayi | idam ahaṃ mām amṛtayonau | satye jyotiṣi juhomi svāhā | sūryaś ca mā manyuś ca manyupatayaś ca manyukṛtebhyaḥ | pāpebhyo rakṣantām | yad rātriyā pāpam akārṣam | manasā vācā hastābhyām | padbhyām udareṇa śiśnā | rātris tad avalumpatu | yat iṃ ca duritaṃ mayi | idam ahaṃ mām amṛtayonau | sūrye jyotiṣi juhomi svāhā" iti | viṣṇuḥ: "udbandhanamṛtasya yaḥ pāśaṃ chindyāt sa taptakṛcchreṇa śudhyati | ātmanas tyāgināṃ ca saṃskartā tad asrupātakārī ca sarvasyaiva pretasya tadbāndhavaiḥ sahāsrupātanaṃ kṛtvā snānena vā kṛte 'sthisaṃcaye sacailasnānāt | dvijaḥ śūdrapretānugamanaṃ kṛtvā sravantīm āsādya gāyatryaṣṭasahasraṃ japet | dvijapretasyāṣṭaśatam | śūdraḥ pretānugamanaṃ kṛtvā snānamn ācaret" | athānāśakādipracyutaprāyaścittāni | tatra mārkaṇḍeyaḥ |
ye pratyavasitā viprāḥ pravrajyāgnijalāditaḥ |
anāśakān nivṛttā ye vāñchanti gṛhamedhitām ||
tāṃś cārayitvā trīn kṛcchrāṃs trīṇi cāndrāyaṇāni vā |
jātakarmādisaṃskāraiḥ saṃskṛtāḥ śuddhibhājanāḥ ||
parāśaraḥ |
anāśakān nivṛttas tu cāturvarṇye vyavasthitaḥ |
caṇḍālaḥ sa tu vijñeyo varjanīyaḥ prayatnataḥ ||
saṃvartaḥ |
saṃnyasya durmatiḥ kaścit pratyāpattiṃ bhajeta yaḥ |
sa kuryāt kṛcchram aśrāntaḥ ṣaṇmāsān pratyanantaram ||
parāśaraḥ |
jalāgnipatane caiva pravrajyānaśane tathā |
adhyavasya nivṛttānāṃ prāyaścittaṃ kathaṃ bhavet ||
brāhmaṇānāṃ prasādena tīrthānugamanena ca |
gavāṃ śatapradānena varṇāḥ śudhyanti vai trayaḥ ||
yamaḥ |
jalāgnyudbandhanabhraṣṭāḥ pravrajyānāśakacyutāḥ |
viṣaprapatanaprāyaśastraghātāc ca ye cyutāḥ || [1193]
sarve te pratyavasitāḥ sarvalokavigarhitāḥ |
cāndrāyaṇena śudhyeyus taptakṛcchradvayena vā ||
asamarthaviṣayam etat | aṅgirāḥ |
yaḥ pratyavasito vipraḥ pravrajyāgnijalāditaḥ |
anāśananivṛttas tu gṛhasthatvaṃ cikīrṣati ||
cāreyet trīṇi kṛcchrāṇi trīṇi cāndrāyaṇāni vā |
jatakarmāditaḥ proktaṃ punaḥsaṃskāram arhati ||
cirakālaṃ prāyaścittam akurvato 'vasthāna etat | āpastambaḥ |
citibhraṣṭā tu yā nārī mohād vicalitā tataḥ |
prājāpatyena śudhyet tu tasmād vai pāpakarmaṇaḥ ||
bhaviṣyatpurāṇam |
ārūḍho naiṣṭhikaṃ dharmaṃ pratyāpattiṃ vrajet tu yaḥ |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ caren māsam iti viddhi khagādhipa ||
mānasyāṃ pratyāpattāv etat |
atha sparśaprāyaścittāni | dakṣaḥ |
pāne maithunasaṃsarge tathā mūtrapurīṣayoḥ |
saṃsargaṃ yadi gaccheta śavodakyāntyajaiḥ saha ||
dinam ekaṃ caren mūtre purīṣe tu dinadvayam |
dinatrayaṃ maithune syāt pāne ca syāc catuṣṭayam ||
āpastambaḥ |
bhuktvocchiṣṭas tv anācāntaś caṇḍālaiḥ śvapacena vā |
pramādāt sparśanaṃ gacchet tatra kuryād viśodhanam ||
gāyatryaṣṭasahasraṃ tu drupadānāṃ śataṃ tathā |
trirātropoṣito bhūtvā pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
śātātapaḥ |
ucchiṣṭas tu spṛśed vipraś caṇḍālaṃ cet kathaṃcana |
ūrdhvocchiṣṭas tu saṃspṛśya dvijaḥ sāṃtapanaṃ caret ||
adhocciṣṭas trirātrānte pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
bhuktocchiṣṭa ūrdhvocchiṣṭaḥ | utsṛṣṭamūtrapurīṣo 'dhaucchiṣṭaḥ | uśanā |
caṇḍālaśvapacaspṛṣṭo viṇmūtre tu kṛte dvijaḥ |
trirātreṇa viśudhyet tu bhuktocchiṣṭaḥ ṣaḍ ācaret || [1194]
vyāghraḥ |
caṇḍālodakasaṃspṛṣṭaḥ snānena sa viśudhyati |
ucchiṣṭas tena saṃspṛṣṭas trirātreṇa viśudhyati ||
sumantuḥ |
caṇḍālān patitāṃś caiva tathā nārīṃ rajasvalām |
ucchiṣṭas tu dvijaḥ spṛṣṭvā prājāpatyena śudhyati ||
kaśyapaḥ |
śvasūkarāntyacaṇḍālamadyabhāṇḍarajasvalāḥ |
yady ucchiṣṭaḥ spṛśet tatra kṛcchraṃ sāṃtapanaṃ caret ||
āpastambaḥ |
bhuktocchiṣṭo 'ntyajaiḥ spṛṣṭaḥ prājāptyaṃ samācaret |
ardhocchiṣṭaṃ smṛtaḥ pādaḥ pada ācamane tathā ||
ardhocchiṣṭo vartamānabhojanaḥ |
ekavṛkṣe samārūḍhau caṇḍālabrāhmaṇau yadi |
phalaṃ bhakṣayatas tatra prāyaścittaṃ kathaṃ bhavet ||
brāhmaṇān samanujñāpya savāsāḥ snānam ācaret |
ahorātroṣito bhūtvā pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
ekaśākhāṃ samārūḍhaḥ phalāny aśnāty asau yadā |
prāyaścittaṃ trirātraṃ syāt pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
caṇḍālena gṛhītaṃ yas tv ajñānād udakaṃ pibet |
tatra śuddhiṃ vijānīyāt prājāpatyena nityaśaḥ ||
bhuktocchiṣṭas tv anācānto hy amedhyaṃ yadi saṃspṛśet |
ahorātroṣito bhūtvā pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
ucchiṣṭocchiṣṭasaṃspṛṣṭaḥ śunā śūdreṇa vā dvijaḥ |
kṛtvopavāsaṃ naktaṃ ca pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
kāmakāraviṣayam idam | akāmatas tu chāgaleyoditam |
ucchiṣṭocchiṣṭasaṃspṛṣṭaḥ snānaṃ yeṣu vidhīyate |
tenaivocchiṣṭasaṃspṛṣṭaḥ prājāpatyaṃ samācaret ||
karmārarajakaṃ venaṃ(na)dhīvaraṃ naṭam eva ca |
ebhiḥ spṛṣṭas tathocchiṣṭa ekarātraṃ payaḥ pibet ||
tair ucchiṣṭais trirātraṃ syād ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati |
bhuñjānena tu vipreṇa spṛṣṭā yadi rajasvalā ||
śiśukṛcchreṇa śudhyet tu prāṇāyāmaśatena ca ||
āpastambaḥ |
ucchiṣṭena tu saṃspṛṣṭo viśaucas tu dvijottamaḥ |
upoṣya rajanīm ekāṃ pañcagavyena śudhyati || [1195]
caṇḍālādiviṣayam idam | hārītaḥ |
mahāpātakisaṃsparśe snānam eva vidhīyate |
saṃspṛṣṭas tu yadā spṛṣṭo brahmakūrcena śudhyati ||
trirātram ekarātraṃ vā yo 'na('pya)śnan pañcagavyakam |
japaṃś ca praṇavaṃ samyag evaṃ śuddhim avāpnuyāt ||
kṛte mūtre purīṣe vā bhuktocchiṣṭo 'tha vā dvijaḥ |
śvādispṛṣṭo japed devyāḥ sahasraṃ snānapūrvakam ||
āpastambaḥ |
vipro vipreṇa saṃspṛṣṭa ucchiṣṭena kathaṃcana |
ācamyaiva tu śuddhaḥ syād ity āṅgirasabhāṣitam ||
udakyāspṛṣṭa ucchiṣṭo viḍvarāhaś ca kukkuṭaiḥ |
kākamārjārakravyādbhir upavāsena śudhyati ||
yena kenacid ucchiṣṭo hy amedhyaṃ yadi saṃspṛśet |
ahorātroṣito bhūtvā pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
chāgaleyaḥ |
ucchiṣṭaḥ saṃspṛśed vipro madyaśūdraśuno 'śucīn |
ahorātroṣitaḥ snātvā pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
ucchiṣṭaḥ spṛṣṭa ācāmed ucchiṣṭena svajātinā |
naktena copavāsena kṣatraviṭsparśane kramāt ||
śātātapaḥ |
yas tu chāyāṃ śvapākasya brāhmaṇo 'py adhigacchati |
tatra snānaṃ tu tasyoktaṃ ghṛtaprāśo viśodhanam ||
tathā |
antyajair hastakāṣṭhena vāsasā spṛṣṭa eva vā |
prakṣālyāṅgaṃ samācāmed ucchiṣṭas tu niśāṃ kṣipet ||
aupakāyanaḥ |
aspṛśyena sahaikāntī taran nausaṃkramādibhiḥ |
nidadhyād apsu pānyādi na duṣyet tena cāspṛśan ||
śātātapaḥ |
kāpālikānāṃ saṃsparśe snānaṃ kṛtvā yathāvidhi |
prāṇāyāmaśataṃ kṛtvā ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati ||
vṛddhaśātātapaḥ |
caṇḍālaṃ patitaṃ vyaṅgam unmattaṃ śavam antyajam |
sūtikāṃ sūyikānārīṃ rajasā ca pariplutām ||
śvakukkuṭavarāhāṃś ca grāmyān saṃspṛśya mānavaḥ |
sacailaḥ saśiraḥ snātvā tadānīm eva śudhyati || [1196]
prasavaṃ kārayati yā sā sūyikā |
aśuddhān svayam apy etān aśuddho yadi saṃspṛśet |
viśudhyaty upavāsena trirātreṇottareṇa ca ||
uttareṇa bhuktocchiṣṭenety arthaḥ | gārgyaḥ |
kravyādaśvakharoṣṭraiś ca sparśe vyavahite dvijaḥ |
acailaṃ vā sacailaṃ vā snānaṃ kṛtvā viśudhyati ||
sacailaṃ matipūrvake | acailam anyatra |
śūdraṃ spṛṣṭvā niṣādaṃ ca śudhyed ācamanād dvijaḥ |
taddhīnasparśanāt snānaprāṇāyāmais tapobalāt ||
snānāsamarthasya śūdrasparśiṇa ācamanam, samarthasya tu snānam eva | ata eva smṛtyantaram |
eḍakaṃ kukkuṭaṃ kākaṃ śvaśūdrāntyāvasāyinaḥ |
dṛṣṭvaitān ācaret karma spṛṣṭvaitān snānam ācaret ||
yad vā sacchūdrasparśa ācamanam asacchūdrasparśe snānam | vṛddhayājñavalkyaḥ |
caṇḍālapukkasamlecchabhillukāpālidārakān |
upapātakinaṃ caiva spṛṣṭvā snānaṃ samācaret ||
saṃvartaḥ |
kaivartamṛgayuvyādhasainiśākunikān api |
rajakaṃ ca tathā spṛṣṭvā snātvaivāśanam ācaret ||
snātvaiva bhuñjītety arthaḥ | evaṃ ca yad rajakādisparśe 'śanaṃ tad vyādhitādiviṣayaṃ draṣṭavyam | ṣaṭtriṃśanmatāt: "caṇḍālaśavasparśane kṛcchraṃ kuryāt | yānaśayyāsaneṣu trirātram" | caṇḍāla[śava]sparśana iti | caṇḍālasya śavatvam āpannasya sparśana ity arthaḥ | jīvatā caṇḍālena saha yānādiṣu trirātram iti | tathā: "vraṇabandhanābhyañjanavisrāvaṇa-rudhirotpādaneṣu kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caret" | vraṇabandhādīnāṃ caṇḍālaṃ prati karaṇe caṇḍālena vātmani karaṇa etat | āpastambaḥ |
yena kenacid abhyaktaś caṇḍālaṃ yadi saṃspṛśet |
upavāsena caikena pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
tailābhyaktas tathācāntaḥ śmaśrukarmaṇi maithune |
mūtroccāraṃ yadā kuryād ahorātreṇa śudhyati || [1197]
pracetāḥ: "svakāye caṇḍālādikāyābhisparśane dvirātrābhojanāc chuddhiḥ" | idaṃ pariṣvaṅgaviṣayam |
caṇḍālo yadi kāyasya raktam utpādayet kvacit |
trirātreṇa viśuddhiḥ syād ekarātreṇa cottare ||
uttare kṣatriyādau | trirātrād ekaikasyāhorātrasya hrāsaḥ | kratuḥ: "caṇḍālasyocchiṣṭadāne caṇḍālanṛttadarśane gītavāditraśravaṇe bhaiṣajyakriyāyāṃ dvirātrābhojanena śuddhiḥ | aśuciṃ dṛṣṭvādityam īkṣeta, prāṇāyāmaṃ kṛtvā pañcadaśamātrikam" | aśuciś caṇḍālādiḥ | parāśaraḥ |
śvapākaḍombacaṇḍālān mithaḥ saṃbhāṣate yadi |
dvijasaṃbhāṣaṇaṃ kuryāt sāvitrīṃ vā sakṛj japet ||
caṇḍālena samaṃ suptvā trirātreṇa viśudhyati |
caṇḍālaikamayīṃ gatvā sāvitrīsmaraṇāc chuciḥ ||
mitho brāhmaṇaiḥ saha saṃbhāṣetety arthaḥ | pracetāḥ: "caṇḍālagṛhapraveśane caṇḍālenaiva gṛhe vṛkṣacchāyāyāṃ vā mahāvasthāne caṇḍāla eva syāt | brāhmaṇānuddiṣṭaṃ ṣāṇmāsikaṃ prāyaścittaṃ kṛcchraṃ vā brāhmaṇasya catustridvyekamāsāḥ śeṣāṇām" | śeṣāḥ kṣatriyo śūdraḥ kaivartādiś ca | eṣāṃ yathāsaṃkhyaṃ catustridvyekamāsāḥ kṛcchrāḥ | uśanā: "aniṣṭagandhopaghrāṇaśravanadarśanodāharaneṣv ādityadarśanāc chaucam" | aniṣṭānāṃ gandhaśabdarūpavākyānā(ṇā)m upaghrāṇaśravaṇadarśanodāharaṇeṣu ādityadarśanāc chuddhir ity arthaḥ |
caṇḍāladharmasaṃyoge prājāpatyaṃ samācaret |
caret trirātraṃ caṇḍālakūpatīrthaniṣevaṇāt ||
dharmasya saṃyoga upadeśaḥ | āpastambaḥ |
dṛṣṭvā caṇḍalapatitau saṃdhyākāla upasthite |
īkṣetādityam udyantaṃ tathodakyāṃ malāni ca || [1198]
manuḥ |
ācamya prayato nityaṃ japed aśucidarśane |
saurān mantrān yathotsāhaṃ pāvamāṇīś ca śaktitaḥ ||
parāśaraḥ |
avijñātas tu caṇḍālo nivased yasya veśmani |
vijñāte rūpam annasya (vijñātur upasannasya) dvijāḥ kuryur anugraham ||
ṛṣivaktracyutān dharmāṇ gāyanto dharmapāṭhakāḥ |
patantam uddhareyus taṃ śāstradṛṣṭena karmaṇā ||
dadhnā ghṛtena kṣīreṇa kṛcchraṃ gomūtrayāvakam |
bhuñjīta sahito bhṛtyais trisaṃdhyam avagāhanam ||
tryahaṃ tu dadhnā bhuñjīta sarpiṣā tu tryahaṃ tataḥ |
kṣīreṇa tu tryahaṃ bhojyam ekaikena punas tryaham ||
bāvaduṣṭaṃ na bhuñjīta bhoktavyaṃ gorasaplutam |
tiṣṭhed dināni yāvanti tāvanty eva samācaret ||
tripalaṃ tu dadhi kṣīraṃ palam ekaṃ tu sarpiṣaḥ |
ākare tu bhavec chuddhir ārakūṭe sakāṃśyake ||
ākara utpattisthānam | ārakūṭo rīrikam |
jalaśaucena vastrāṇi parityāgena mṛnmayam |
kusumbhaguḍakarpāsalavaṇaṃ madhusarpiṣī ||
dvāri kurvīta dhānyāni dadyād veśmani pāvakam |
hutāśajvālāsaṃspṛṣṭaṃ śuci tan manur abravīt ||
saputraḥ sahabhṛtyaiś ca kuryād brāhmaṇabhojanam |
goviṃśatiṃ vṛṣaṃ caikaṃ dadyād vipreṣu dakṣiṇām ||
punar lepena khātena homajapyena caiva hi |
avadhāraṇena viprāṇāṃ tatra doṣo na vidyate ||
svalpakālasaṃparka etat | saṃvartaḥ |
antyajaḥ patito vāpi nigūḍho yatra tiṣṭhati |
samyag jñātvā tu kālena tataḥ kuryād viśodhanam ||
cāndrāyaṇaṃ parāko vā dvijātīnāṃ viśodhanam |
prājāpatyaṃ tu śūdrāṇāṃ śeṣāṇām idam ucyate |
yais tatra bhuktaṃ pakvānnaṃ teṣām ukto vidhikramaḥ || [1199]
prājāpatya ity arthaḥ |
teṣām api tu yair bhuktaṃ kṛcchrapādo vidhīyate |
kūpaikapānaduṣṭā ye tathā saṃsargadūṣitāḥ ||
sarvān evopavāsena pañcagavyena śodhayet |
bālāpatyā tathā rogī garbhiṇī vṛddha eva vā ||
teṣāṃ naktaṃ pradātavyaṃ bālānāṃ praharadvayam |
atha vā kriyamāṇeṣu yeṣām ārtiḥ pradṛśyate ||
śeṣaṃ saṃpādayet teṣāṃ vipattir na bhaved yathā |
antyajo 'tra caṇḍālaḥ | vasiṣṭhaḥ |
caṇḍālo nivased yatra gṛhe tv ajñāta eva vā |
tasya tv annaṃ dvijo bhuktvā prājāpatyaṃ samācaret ||
akāmataḥ sakṛd bhuktvā kuryād etad dvijottamaḥ |
kāmāc chuddhiḥ parākeṇa mahāsāṃtapanena vā ||
cāndrāyaṇaṃ parāko vā dvijātīnāṃ viśodhanam |
prājāpatyaṃ tu śūdrāṇāṃ śeṣāṇām idam ucyate ||
yo 'nyo 'pi bhuṅkte pakvānnaṃ kṛcchraṃ syāt tasya śodhanam |
śuṣkānnabhojane pādam ity āha bhagavān manuḥ ||
tathā |
taiḥ spṛṣṭo yadi bhuṅkte 'nnam asnātvā vidhivac jale |
vihito bhojane kṛcchraḥ pāne tatpāda eva tu ||
caṇḍālena tu saṃspṛṣṭaṃ kāṃśyabhāṇḍaṃ samṛnmayam |
ajñānāt kāṃsyabhojī ca mṛnmaye. jalapānakṛt ||
kāṃsye bhuktvā caret kṛcchaṃ jalapāne tu kaṛcchrakam ||
kṛcchrakaḥ kṛcchrapādaḥ | cyavanaḥ: "caṇḍālasaṃkare svabhavanadahanam | sarvamṛdbhāṇḍabhedanam | dāravāṇāṃ tu takṣaṇam | kāṃsyatāmrāṇām ākare śuddhiḥ | sauvīrapayodadhitakrāṇāṃ parityāgaḥ | śeṣarasavad yavasadravyarakṣaṇam gomūtrayāvakāhāro māsaṃ kṣipet | bālavṛddhastrīṇām ardhaprāyaścittam" | [1200] ā ṣoḍaśād bālaḥ | saptatyūrdhvaṃ vṛddhaḥ | cīrṇe prāyaścitte brāhmaṇabhojanaṃ gośataṃ dadyāt | tadabhāve sarvasvam | baudhāyanaḥ: "caṇḍāladarśane jyotiṣāṃ darśanam | saṃbhāṣaṇe brāhmaṇasaṃbhāṣaṇam | sparśane snānam | ucchiṣṭadarśana ekarātram upavaset | saṃbhāṣaṇe dvirātram | sparśane trirātram | caṇḍālena sahādhvagamane sacailaṃ snānam" | āpastambaḥ |
balād dāsīkṛtā ye tu mlecchacaṇḍāladasyubhiḥ |
aśubhaṃ kāritāḥ karma gavādiprāṇihiṃsanam ||
ucchiṣṭamārjanaṃ caiva tathā tasyaiva bhojanam |
kharoṣṭraviḍvarāhāṇām āmiṣasya ca bhakṣaṇam ||
tatstrīṇāṃ ca tathā saṅgas tābhiś ca saha bhojanam |
māsoṣite dvijātau tu prājāpatyaṃ viśodhanam ||
cāndrāyaṇaṃ tv āhitāgneḥ parākas tv atha vā bhavet |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ parākaṃ ca caret saṃvatsaroṣitaḥ ||
saṃvatsaroṣitaḥ śūdro māsārdhaṃ yāvakaṃ pibet |
māsamātroṣitaḥ śūdraḥ kṛcchrapādena śudhyati ||
ūrdhvaṃ saṃvatsarāt kalpyaṃ prāyaścittaṃ dvijottamaiḥ |
tribhiḥ saṃvatsaraiś cāpi tadbhāvaṃ sa nigacchati ||
sarvāṇy etāni prāyaścittāni yathāśakti yathānubandhaṃ pratyayābhyāsāpekṣayā vyavasthāpanīyāni |
hīnavarṇas tu yaḥ kaścid antyajaiḥ saha saṃvaset |
saśikhaṃ pavanaṃ kṛtvā māsam ekaṃ yavān pibet ||
rajakī carmakārī ca lubdhakī pukkasī tathā |
cāturvarṇyasya gehe tu ajñānād yadi tiṣṭhati ||
jñātvā tu niṣkṛtiṃ kuryāt pūrvoktasyārdham eva ca |
gṛhadānaṃ na kurvīta śeṣaṃ sarvaṃ tu kārayet ||
gṛhasyābhyantaraṃ gacchec caṇḍālo yadi kasyacit |
tasmād gehād viniḥsārya mṛdbhāṇḍaṃ tu vivarjayet || [1201]
rasapūrṇaṃ tu mṛbhāṇḍaṃ na tyajet tu kadācana |
gomayodakasaṃmiśraiḥ prokṣayet tu gṛhaṃ tathā ||
caṇḍālśvapacānāṃ ca saṃkare samupasthite |
ahorātraṃ dvirātraṃ syāt trirātraṃ ṣaḍahaḥ smṛtam ||
avijñātas tu caṇḍālaḥ saptāhaṃ nivased yadi |
tasmāj jñātvopasannasya viprāḥ kuryur anugraham ||
dadhikṣīraghṛtair yuktaṃ kṛcchraṃ gomūtrayāvakam |
prāśayet saha bhṛtyais tu māsam ekaṃ nirantaram ||
atha rajasvalāsparśaprāyaścittāni |
vṛddhaśātātapaḥ |
udakyā sūtikā vāpi śavāntyau saṃspṛśed yadi |
dvirātreṇaiva śudhyet tu iti śātātapo 'bravīt ||
śātātapaḥ |
caṇḍālaiḥ śvapacair vāpi ātreyī spṛśyate yadi |
trirātropoṣitā bhūtvā pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
ātreyī rajasvalā | kaśyapaḥ |
caṇḍālena tu saṃspṛṣṭā kadācit strī rajasvalā |
tāny ahāni vyatikramya prāyaścittaṃ samācaret ||
trirātram upavāsaḥ syāt pañcagavyena śodhanam |
tāṃ niśāṃ tu vyatikramya ajāghrātāṃ tu kārayet ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
patitena śvapākena saṃspṣṭā ced rajasvalā |
ahorātraṃ tṛtīye 'hani parato naktam ācaret ||
vṛddhaśātātapaḥ |
rajasvalā yadi spṛṣṭā caṇḍālāntyaśvavāyasaiḥ |
tāvat tiṣṭhen nirāhārā snātvā kālena śudhyati ||
asāmarthye saty etat | parāśaraḥ |
ekavṛkṣasamārūḍhau caṇḍālo 'tha rajasvalā |
ahorātroṣitā bhūtvā pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
rajasvalā tu bhuñjānā caṇḍālādīn spṛśed yadi |
gomūtrayvakāhārā ṣaḍrātreṇaiva śudhyati ||
aśaktā kāñcanaṃ dadyād viprebhyo vāpi bhojanam ||
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ |
udakyā tu savarṇā yā spṛṣṭā cet syād udakyayā |
tasminn evāhani snātvā śuddhim āpnoty asaṃśayam || [1202]
etat sagotrāviṣayam | tathā ca vṛddhavasiṣṭhaḥ |
spṛṣṭe rajasvale 'nyonyaṃ sagotre tv ekasūtake |
kāmato 'kāmato vāpi sadyaḥ snānena śudhyataḥ ||
kaśyapaḥ |
rajasvalā tu saṃspṛṣṭā brāhmaṇyā brāhmaṇī yadi |
ekarātraṃ nirāhārā pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
rajasvalā tu saṃspṛṣṭā kṣatrajātyā dvijottamā |
trirātreṇa viśudhyet tu vyāghrasya vacanaṃ yathā ||
rajasvalā tu saṃspṛṣṭā vaiśyayā brāhmaṇī yadi |
pañcarātraṃ nirāhārā pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
rajasvalā tu saṃspṛṣṭā śūdrayā brāhmaṇī yadi |
ṣaḍrātreṇa viśudhyet tu brāhmaṇī kāmakārataḥ ||
akāmataś cared eva brāhmaṇī sarvajātiṣu |
viṣṇuḥ: "rajasvalā hīnavarṇāṃ rajasvalāṃ spṛṣṭvānnaṃ na tāvad aśnīyād yāvan na śuddhā | savarṇām adhikavarṇāṃ ca spṛṣṭvā snātvāśnīyāt" | atriḥ |
rajasvale tu ye nāryāv anyonyaṃ spṛśato yadi |
savarṇe pañcagavyaṃ syāt trirātram asavarṇake ||
asavarṇe hīnavarṇe |
spṛṣṭe rajasvale 'nyonyaṃ śūdrā ca brāhmaṇī tathā |
kṛcchreṇa śudhyate viprā śūdrā dānena śudhyati ||
dānena niṣkacaturthāṃśadānenety arthaḥ | mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe |
ucchiṣṭena tu saṃspṛṣṭā kadācit strī rajasvalā |
yāvan na śuddhim āpnoti nāśnīyāt tāvad eva tu ||
rajonimittāśaucanivṛttiḥ śuddhiḥ |
ucchiṣṭāṃś ca dvijātīṃs tu rajaḥstrī yadi saṃspṛśet |
srāvocchiṣṭe tv ahorātram ūrdhvocchiṣṭe tryahaṃ kṣipet ||
srāvo mutrādyutsargaḥ | tatkṛtam āśaucaṃ taducchiṣṭam | vyāghraḥ |
rajasvalā tu saṃspṛṣṭā śvajabukakharaiḥ kvacit |
nirāhārā bhavet tāvad yāvat snānena śudhyati ||
idaṃ samarthaviṣayam | atrāpy asāmarthye baudhāyana āha | [1203]
rajasvalā tu saṃspṛṣṭā grāmyakukkuṭasūkaraiḥ |
śvabhiḥ snātvā kṣipet tāvad yāvac candrasya darśanam || iti |
pracetāḥ |
rajasvalā spṛśec chvānaṃ pramādād yadi karhicit |
upoṣya rajanīm ekāṃ pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
baudhāyanaḥ |
rajasvalā tu saṃspṛṣṭā śunā caṇḍālarāsabhaiḥ |
ahorātraṃ tṛtīye 'hni parato naktam ācaret || 3.292 ||
viruddhaprāyaścittasaṃnipātaprasaṅge tadvirodhaparihārārthaṃ yat kalpyaṃ yac cānuktaniṣkṛtikeṣu pāpeṣu kalpanīyam, tad ubhayam āha |
deśaṃ kālaṃ vayaḥ śaktiṃ pāpaṃ cāpekṣya yatnataḥ |
prāyaścittaṃ prakalpyaṃ syād yatra noktā ca niṣkṛtiḥ || 3.293 ||
ekasmin nimitte gurulaghurūpānekavaikalpikaprāyaścittaprāptau deśādikam apekṣya pariṣadā tadvyavasthāḥ kalpyāḥ | yaḥ prāyaścittaviśeṣo yatra deśe kāle vā prayujyamānaḥ kartāraṃ nāśayatīti śaṅkyate sa tatra nopadeśyaḥ | yathā — jalopavāso himadeśe mahāśiśire ca kāle | tathā vayo 'py apekṣyam | anyathā yady ūnā śakyaṃ tad bālavṛddhayoḥ kalpyamānaṃ tau nipātayet | tathā kartuḥ śaktir api vyavasthāpikā, yato yuvāpi kaścit pittodrekādinā pratibaddhatapaḥśaktiḥ parākādy anutiṣṭhan vyāpadyate | ata evāha hārītaḥ |
yathāvayo yathākālaṃ yathāprāṇaṃ ca brāhmaṇaiḥ |
prāyaścittaṃ pradātavyaṃ vṛddhānāṃ rogiṇāṃ tathā ||
na tena śuddhim āpnoti yena prāṇair viyujyate |
ārtiṃ vā mahatīṃ yāti na vai tad vratam ādiśet ||
tasmāt kṛcchraṃ athāpy ardhaṃ pādaṃ vāpi vidhānataḥ |
jñātvā balābalaṃ kālaṃ prāyaścittaṃ vidhīyate ||
viṣṇuḥ |
aśītir yasya varṣāṇi bālo vāpy ūnaṣoḍaśaḥ |
prāyaścittārdham arhanti striyo rogiṇa eva ca || [1204]
aṅgirāḥ |
ūnaikādaśavarṣasya pañcavarṣāt parasya ca |
cared guruḥ suhṛd vāpi prāyaścittaṃ viśūddhaye || iti |
prāyaścittapāpaṃ laghu guru vety avekṣya tadanusāreṇa prāyaścittam api laghu guru vā kalpyam | tathoktaṃ gautamena: "enasi guruṇi gurūṇi laghuni laghūni" iti | pāpasya ca gurutvaṃ kāmataḥ sapratyayaṃ sābhyāsam anuṣṭhitasya jñāyate, tadā daṇḍagauravāt sātiśayāc ca nindārthavādāl lokagarhātiśayāc ca | etac ca yatnata ācārāt tadviṣayasakalasmṛtiśāstrāvadhāraṇarūpāt kalpyam | tatra nimittasaṃbandhitvena kalpayitavyasya niṣkṛtiśeṣasya svarūpaṃ sāmānyataḥ pradarśyate | tatra gautamaḥ: "tasya niṣkrayaṇāni japas tapo homa upavāso dānam" iti | japyaṃ ca tenaivoktam: "upaniṣado vedādayo vedāntāḥ sarvacchandaḥsu saṃhitā madhūny aghamarṣaṇam atharvaśiro rudrāḥ puruṣasūktaṃ rājanarauhiṇe samanī bṛhadrathantaraṃ puruṣagatir mahānāmnyo mahāvairājī(jaṃ) mahādinā(vā)kīrtyaṃ jyeṣṭhasāmnām anyatamaṃ bahiṣpavamānaṃ kūṣmāṇḍāni pāvamānyaḥ savitrī ceti pāvanāni" | tathā | "payovratatā śakabhakṣatā phalabhakṣatā prasṛtiyāvako hiraṇyaprāśanaṃ somapānam iti medhyāni pavitrāṇi" | tathā | "sarve śiloccayāḥ sarvāḥ sravantyaḥ puṇyahradād tīrthāni ṛṣinivāsā goṣṭhapariskandhā iti deśaḥ" | goṣṭhaṃ gosthānam, tadgamanamārgaḥ pariskandhaḥ | tathā | "satyavacanaṃ brahmacaryaṃ savaneṣūdakopasparśanam ārdravastratānāśaka iti tapāṃsi, hiraṇyaṃ gaurvāso bhumir aśvas tilā ghṛtam annam iti deyāni" | tathā | "saṃvatsaraḥ ṣaṇmāsāś catvāras trayo dvāv ekaś caturviṃśty aho dvādaśāhaḥ ṣaḍahas tryaho (?) 'horātra iti kālaḥ | etāny evānādeśe vikalpena [1205] kriyerann enasi guruni gurūṇi laghuni laghūni kṛcchrātikṛcchracāndrāyaṇam iti prāyaścittam" iti || 3.293 ||
uktāni patitānāṃ prāyaścittāni, saṃprati tatsaṃbandhibhis tān prati yat karyaṃ tad āha |
dāsī kumbhaṃ bahir grāmān ninayeyuḥ svabāndhavāḥ |
patitasya bahiḥ kuryuḥ sarvakāryeṣu caiva tam || 3.294 ||
dāsī bāndhavāś ca patitasya kumbhaṃ jalapūrṇaṃ grāmād bahir ninayeyuḥ | tataḥ prabhṛti taṃ patitaṃ sarvakāryeṣu laukikavaidikeṣu bahiḥ kuryuḥ | tena saha na saṃvyavahareyuḥ | bāndhavagrahaṇaṃ saṃbandhimātraparigrahārtham | tena vidyāyonisaṃbandhāś ca sarve ninayeyuḥ | tathā ca gautamaḥ: "tasya vidyā[guru]yonisaṃbandhāś ca saṃnipatya sarvāṇy udakā[dī]ni pretakāryāṇi kuryuḥ | pātraṃ cāsya viparyasyeyuḥ | dāsaḥ karmakaro vāvakarāt pātram ānīya dāsīghaṭāt pūrayitvā dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ padā vinya(parya)syed amum anudakaṃ karomīti nāmagrāhaṃ te sarve 'nvālabheran prācīnāvītino muktaśikhā vidyāguravo yonisaṃbandhāś ca vīkṣeran | apa upaspṛśya grāmaṃ praviśanti | ata ūrdhvaṃ tena saṃbhāṣya tiṣṭhed ekarātraṃ japet sāvitrīṃ jñānapūrvaṃ cet trirātram" iti | asyārthaḥ — yo rājaghātakatvādinā hetunā tyājya uktas tasya vidyāgurūn ācāryādīn yonisaṃbaddhān mātāmahamātulādīṃś ca putrādayaḥ sapiṇḍāḥ sarvāṇi piṇḍodakaśrāddhādīni jīvantam eva tam uddiśya kṛtvā pātraṃ cāsya tyājyasya kṛte viparyasyeyuḥ parāsyeyuḥ | katham ity apekṣita āha — dāsaḥ karmakaro vetyādi | dāsa uktalakṣaṇaḥ | karmakaro bhṛtyaḥ | tayor yaḥ saṃbhavati so 'vakarāt purīṣasthānād aśucipātram ānīya dāsīghaṭāt tat pātram udakena pūrayati | tad itaraḥ pādena dakṣiṇāmukho 'muṃ devadattam anudakaṃ karomīti paryasyet | taṃ paryasyantaṃ tyājyasya [1206] sapiṇḍāḥ putrādayaḥ prācīnāvītino muktaśikhā anvālabheran | vidyāguravo yonisaṃbandhāś cānvīkṣeran | tataḥ snātvā grāmaṃ praviśati | ata ūrdhvam ajñānāt tena saha saṃbhāṣya sāvitrīṃ japann ahorātraṃ tiṣṭhet | jñānāt tu trirātram iti | vasiṣṭhaḥ: "vedaviplāvakaśūdrayājakottamavarṇavargapatitās teṣāṃ pātraninayanam apātrasaṃkarād akṛtsnaṃ pātram ādāya dāso 'savarṇāputro vā bandhur asadṛśo vā guṇahīnaḥ savyena pādenāpavṛttāgrān darbhāṃl lohitān upastīryāsaṃpūrṇapadāsmai pātraṃ ninayet | ninetāraṃ cāsya prakīrya keśāñ jñātayo 'nvālabherann apasavyaṃ kṛtvā gṛheṣu svairam āpadyeran" | asyārthaḥ — ayathāvidhi yo vedam adhīte 'dhyāpayati vā sa vedaviplāvakaḥ | śūdrayājakaḥ prasiddhaḥ | uttamavarṇā brāhmaṇās teṣāṃ vargaḥ samūhas tataḥ patitā brahmahādayas teṣāṃ tān uddiśya pātrasya ninayanaṃ nyubjīkaraṇaṃ kāryam | apātrasaṃkarāt kutsitapātrasamūhād akṛtsnaṃ khaṇḍaṃ pātram ādāya dāso 'savarṇāyāḥ śūdrāyāḥ putro vānyo vā bandhur asadṛśo 'savarṇo guṇahīno 'pavṛttāgrāṃś chinnāgrāṃl lohitān dhātvādiraktān darbhān āstīrya savyena pādena jalapūrṇaṃ pātram asmai patitāya ninayet | ninetāraṃ ca patitajñātayaḥ kṛtāpasavyā ninayanakāle spṛśeyuḥ | tataḥ svagṛhasthitāḥ svairaṃ yathecchaṃ dharmādikāryaṃ pratipadyerann iti | gṛheṣu svairam āpadyerann iti vacanasāmarthyāt pātraninayāt purā pati[ta]jñātīnāṃ dharmādiṣu svairāpattir nāstīti gamyate | manuḥ |
patitasyodakaṃ kāryaṃ sapiṇḍair bāndhavair bahiḥ |
nindite 'hani sāyāhne jñātartviggurusaṃnidhau ||
śaṅkhalikhitau: "patitābhiśastaduṣṭātmakulakṣayakarasya vā tasya guror bāndhavānām rājñaś ca samakṣaṃ doṣam anukhyāpya tam abhibhāṣya punaḥ punar avasthāṃ labhaveti | sa yady evam apy anavasthitamatiḥ syāt tato bhinnapātram ādāya pūrṇam apāṃ pāsūnām apasavyaṃ kṛtvā vāmapādena dakṣiṇāṃ diśam abhimukhaṃ nyubjayet pātram apasavyaṃ kṛtvā pavitrodakaṃ śaucam āsīran | apapātritasya rikthapiṇḍodakāni vyāvartante | saṃbhāṣaṇasahāsanavyavahārāṇ na tena saha kuryāt | kṛtvā vasanānugāṃ gāyatrīṃ japet" || 3.294 ||
[1207]
kiṃ ca |
caritavrata āyāte ninayeran navaṃ ghaṭam |
jugupseran na vāpy enaṃ saṃpibeyuś ca sarvaśaḥ || 3.295 ||
tasmimś caritavrate kṛtaprāyaścitte samāgate sarve sapiṇḍā navaṃ ghaṭam adbhiḥ pūrṇaṃ śubhe jalāśaye ninayern prakṣipeyuḥ | ata ūrdhvaṃ na jugupseyur na garheran | pratyuta sarvaśaḥ sarveṣu kāryeṣu saṃpibeyuḥ saṃvyavahareyuḥ | vasiṣṭhaḥ: "patitānāṃ tu caritavratānāṃ pratyuddhāro 'thāpy udāharanti —
agne 'bhyuddharatāṃ gacchet krīḍann iva hasann iva |
paścāt pātayitā gacchec chocann iva rudann iva ||
ācāryamātṛhantāras tatprasādād apayāvyā eteṣāṃ pratyāpattiḥ pūrṇād dhradāt pravṛttād vā kāñcanaṃ pātraṃ ma(mā)heyaṃ vādbhiḥ pūrayitvāpohiṣṭhīyābhir ṛgbhir abhiṣiñceyuḥ | sarva evābhiṣiktasyābhyuddhāraḥ putrajanmanā vyākhyātaḥ" | ayam arthaḥ — patitānāṃ tu śabdād apatitānāṃ ca kṛtaprāyaścittānāṃ pratyuddhāraḥ parigrahaḥ | tatra coddharatām agresaro harṣavāṃś coddhartā syāt | pātayitā tu jaghanyo bhaved viṣādavāṃś ca | ācāryādīnāṃ hantāraḥ pīḍākartāras teṣāṃ prasādād evāpagatayāvyā bhavanti | yāvyaṃ pāpam | tataś ca teṣāṃ patitavat pratyuddhāro na kāryaḥ | asati punar ācāryādiprasāde cīṇavratānāṃ paribhāvapratyuddhāraḥ kārya eva | eteṣāṃ patitādīnāṃ prayāpattiḥ prayānayanaṃ jalapūrṇād dhradāt pravṛttasrotaso vā kāñcanapātram api vā mānti(rti)kam adbhiḥ pūrayitvā "āpo hi ṣṭhā" ity evamādibhir ṛgbhir enam uddharaṇīyam abhiṣiñceyuḥ | tathābhiṣiktasya pratyuddhāraḥ putrajanmanā vyākhyātaḥ | jātakarmādayaḥ saṃskārā yathā putrasya kriyante tathāsya bandhubhiḥ kāryās te caikadeśakāle dṛśyatvāt tantreṇa syur iti | gautamaḥ: "yas tu prāyaścittena śudhyet tasmiñ śuddhe śātakaumbamayaṃ pātraṃ puṇyatamād dhradāt prasravantībhyo vā pūrayitvā tataḥ param etaṃ snapayeyuḥ | athāsmai tat pātraṃ dadyus tat pratigṛhya japec chāntā dyauḥ śāntā pṛthivī śāntam antarikṣaṃ śivam antarikṣaṃ yo vai [1208] retas tam iha gṛhṇīyām ity etair yajurbhis taratsamandībhiḥ pāvamānībhiḥ kūṣmāṇḍaiś cājyaṃ juhuyāt | hiraṇyaṃ brāhmaṇāya dadyāt | gāṃ cācāryāya | yasya tu prāṇāntikaṃ prāyaścittaṃ sa mṛtaḥ śudhyet | sarvāṇy eva tasminn udakādipretakarmāṇi kuryuḥ | etad eva śāntyudakaṃ sarveṣūpapātakeṣu" iti || 3.295 ||
puṃsām uktaṃ vidhiṃ strīṣv atidiśann āha |
patitānām eṣa eva vidhiḥ strīṇāṃ prakīrtitaḥ |
vāso gṛhāntike deyam annaṃ vāsaḥ sarakṣaṇam || 3.296 ||
strīṇām api patitānām eṣa eva pūrvokta eva parityāgapratyuddhāravidhiḥ | vidhīyata iti vidhiḥ kriyākalāpaḥ kāryaḥ | iyāṃs tu viśeṣaḥ — parityaktānām api strīṇāṃ gṛhasaṃnidhau nivāsa ālayo deyaḥ | tathā tābhyo jīvanāyānnaṃ paridhānaṃ ca rakṣaṇashitam | atrāpavādam āha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
catasras tu parityājyāḥ śiṣyagā gurugā ca yā |
patighnī ca viśeṣeṇa juṅgitopagatā ca yā ||
bhartṛśiṣyagā gurugā pitṛgā śvaśurgā | juṅgitā pratilomajaḥ || 2.296 ||
uktā brahmahatyādayaḥ puṃsāṃ strīṇāṃ ca ye pātityahetavaḥ | saṃprati strīṇām eva ye pātityahetavas tān āha |
nīcābhigamanaṃ garbhapātanaṃ bhartṛhiṃsanam |
viśeṣapatanīyāni strīṇām etāny api dhruvam || 3.297 ||
nīco hīnavarṇaḥ śūdraḥ | tasyābhimukhyena kāmato brāhmaṇyādīnāṃ gamanaṃ nīcābhigamanam, svasya ca garbhasya brāhmaṇasyāpi rāgāt pātanaṃ svabhartuś ca brāhmaṇasyāpi hiṃsanam | ity etāni strīṇāṃ viśeṣataḥ patanīyāni | strīṇām evaitāni pātityakāraṇānīty arthaḥ | na caitāny eva kiṃ tu brahmahatyādīny api [iti] vaktum apīty yuktam | dhruvaṃ prāmāṇikam etad ity arthaḥ | yat tu vasiṣṭhenoktam,
trīṇi striyāḥ pātakāni loke dharmavido viduḥ |
bhartur vadho bhrūṇahatyā svasya garbhasya pātanam ||
iti, tatra bhrūṇahatyāśabdaḥ sakalapātityakāranopalakṣaṇārthaḥ || 3.297 ||
"jugupseyur na capy enaṃ saṃpibeyuś ca sarvaśaḥ" ity asyāpavādam āha |
śaraṇāgatabālastrīhiṃsakān saṃpiben na tu |
cīrṇavratān api sadā kṛtaghnasahitān imān || 3.298 ||
śaraṇāgatādīnāṃ hantṝn ghātakān kṛtaghnāṃś ca kṛtaprāyaścittān api na saṃpiben na saṃvyavaharet | na cāsaṃvyavahāre prāyaścittānarthakyam, pāpakṣayakaratvena tasyārthavattvāt | yat tu kāmato vyapahāryas tu vacanād iha jāyata ity uktaṃ tad etad vyatiriktaviṣayam | manuḥ |
bālaghnāṃś ca kṛtaghnāṃś ca viśuddhān api dharmataḥ |
śaraṇāgatahantṝṃś ca strīhantṝṃś ca na saṃvaset ||
"viśuddhān api dharmataḥ" iti vacanāt pāpakṣayaprayojanatāṃ strīvadhādiprāyaścittasya darśayati || 3.298 ||
evaṃ prāsaṅgikam abhidhāya prakṛtasya "jugupseran na cāpy enam" ityāder vacanasyārthaśeṣam āha |
ghaṭe 'pavarjit jñātimadhyastho yavasaṃ gavām |
pradadyāt prathamaṃ gobhiḥ satkṛtasya saha kriyā || 3.299 ||
"caritavrata āyāte" ity atra ghaṭāpavarjanam uktam | tasminn apavarjane kṛte caritavrato jñātimadhyastho gavāṃ yavasaṃ grāsaṃ pradadyāt | tad yavasaṃ caritavratadattaṃ yadi gāvo 'śnanti tato 'sau gobhiḥ satkṛtaḥ kṣīṇapāpatayā pratipādito bhavati | tasyaivaṃ gobhiḥ satkṛtasya bandhubhiḥ saha kriyāsaṃvyavahāro yujyate | anyathā tu prāyaścittakriyaiva punaḥ kāryā | ata eva hārītaḥ: "svaśirasā yavasam ādāya gobhyo dadyād yadi tāḥ pratigṛhṇīyur athainaṃ pravartayeyuḥ" iti | itareṣāṃ nety arthād gamyate || 3.299 ||
yāny uktāni brahmahatyādiṣu prāyaścittāni tāni pariṣadanumatāni santi kāryāṇi nānyathety āha |
vikhyātadoṣaḥ kurvīta parṣado 'numataṃ vratam |
[1210]
vikhyāto 'nekasiddho doṣo yasya sa vikhyātadoṣaḥ | sa uktalakṣanāyāḥ pariṣado yad anumataṃ saṃmataṃ vrataṃ prāyaścittaṃ tat kurvīta, na punaḥ śāstrārthanirṇayanipuṇatayā svātantryeṇa | atra ca yenāvidito 'pi yo doṣaḥ sidhyati, sa tena vijñāto vyākhyāta ity ucyate | tataś ca viduṣāpi vikhyātadoṣeṇa pariṣadupasthānaṃ kāryam, yasya tu prāyaścittasya nirdeśaḥ pariṣadā pratiṣiddho yathā "trīṇi prathamāny anirdeśyāni" iti | tathā manur api |
matipūrvam anirdeśyaṃ prāṇāntikam iti sthitiḥ |
iti, tatra vikhyātadoṣeṇāpi viduṣā svātantryeṇa vratam atyū(tyu)hya kāryam | yad āha "patito hy ātmānam uddhariṣyan" it prakramya śaṅkhaḥ: "svayaṃ vratam abhyupagamya cīravalkalavāsā adhaḥśāyī" ityādi | yatra vratādeśo na pratiśiddhaḥ, tatra viduṣāpi pariṣadupasthānaṃ kāryam eva | atra aṅgirāḥ |
kṛte niḥsaṃśaye pāpe na bhuñjītānupasthitaḥ |
bhuñjāno vardhayet pāpaṃ yāvan nākhyāti parṣadi ||
sacailaṃ vāgyataḥ snātvā klinnavāsāḥ samāhitaḥ |
parṣadānumatas tattvaṃ sarvaṃ vikhyāpayen naraḥ ||
upasthāya tataḥ śīghram ārtimān dharaṇīṃ vrajet |
gātraiś ca śirasā caiva na ca kiṃcid udāharet ||
tatas te praṇipātena dṛṣṭvā taṃ samupasthitam |
viprāḥ pṛcchanti kiṃ kāryam upaviśyāgrataḥ sthitam ||
kiṃ te kāryaṃ kim arthaṃ vā bhṛśaṃ mṛgayase dvija |
tattvato brūhi tat sarvaṃ satyaṃ hi gatir ātmani ||
yadi vakṣyasi satyaṃ tvaṃ niyataṃ prāpsyase śubham |
yady āgato 'sy asatyena na te śuddhis tu karhicit ||
tasmān na vācyam anṛtaṃ sadbhir dharmavicakṣaṇaiḥ |
tasmin nivedite kārya utsāryo hi sa kāryavān ||
tasminn utsārite vipre ye 'pi syur dharmapāṭhakāḥ |
te tathā tatra jalpeyur vimarśantaḥ parasparam ||
yathā śāstreṣu yac coktaṃ yac ca sānugrahaṃ bhavet |
vimṛśya pariṣat sarvaṃ prāyaścittaṃ prakalpayet || [1211]
yathākālaṃ yathādeśaṃ yathākāryāntaraṃ bhavet |
parṣado 'numataṃ caiva kāryinaś ca balābalam ||
prakṛtaṃ pāpam āsādya daivāt pūrvakṛtena vā |
traividyam upasarpeta cāturvidyam athāpi vā ||
na dīyate vrataṃ yāvat karmaṇā manasā girā |
gobrāhmaṇahite yukto vrataṃ tad vartayet sadā ||
kṛtvā pāpaṃ na bhuñjīta trirātram ahar eva vā |
kuryād brāhmaṇaśuśrūṣām āpradānād vratasya tu ||
utthāya parṣadām ekaḥ śrāvayet tad yathāvidhi |
sarveṣāṃ niścitaṃ yac ca yac ca prāṇān na pātayet ||
kāryāntaraṃ vṛddhamātāpitṛśuśrūṣādi | devalaḥ |
pāpakṛt svakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ kṛtsnam ākhyāya saṃsadi |
tataḥ snātvaiva yāceta niṣkṛtiṃ tasya karmaṇaḥ ||
tac ca parṣado 'numataṃ vrataṃ yathā grāhyaṃ tathopariṣṭād vakṣyate | yamaḥ |
gavāhnikaṃ devapūjā vedābhyāsaḥ saritplavaḥ |
nāśayanty āśu pāpāni mahāpātakajāny api ||
śūdrāṇāṃ pāpināṃ caiva saṃvādaṃ naiva kārayet |
saṃvādas tu na kartavyaḥ strībhir vā taiḥ kadācana ||
brāhmaṇenaiva kartavya utpanne saṃśaye sati ||
saṃvādaḥ saṃbhāṣaṇam | marīciḥ |
bhasmagomayagoṣṭheṣu śayanaṃ sikatāsu ca |
patitavrātyacaṇḍālastrīśūdrānabhibhāṣaṇam ||
triḥ snānaṃ kāreyan nityaṃ dhyānaṃ japyaṃ surārcanam |
yā yasyābhimatā loke devatā tāṃ samarcayet ||
rahasyavratam āha |
avikhyāpitadoṣas tu rahasyavratam ācaret || 3.300 ||
avikhyāpito 'prakāśitaḥ svadoṣo yena so 'vikhyāpitadoṣaḥ | sa svayam [1212] eva vidvattayā rahasyaprāyaścittādhikāravihitaṃ vrataṃ kiṃcit saṃpradhāryācaret | pariṣadupasthānam iha nāsti, vikhyātadoṣaviṣayatvena tasya vihitatvāt | doṣotpādakasāmagrībahirbhūtenāparijñātatvam avikhyātatvaṃ yatra rahasyaprāyaścittavidhiḥ | tatra ca viduṣa evādhikāraḥ | tad uktaṃ caturviṃśatimate |
āhitāgner vinītasya vṛddhasya viduṣo 'pi vā |
prāyaścittaṃ rahasyoktaṃ pūrvoktam itarasya tu || iti || 3.300 ||
idānīṃ rahasyapṛāyaścittaviśeṣān āha |
trirātropoṣito japtvā brahmahā tv aghamarṣaṇam |
antarjale viśudhyet tu datvā gāṃ ca payasvinīm || 3.301 ||
trirātraṃ yāvat kṛtopavāsaś caturthe 'hni aghamarṣaṇena dṛṣṭaṃ sūktam ṛtaṃ cety etad ānuṣṭubham ṛcāṃ tritayaṃ bhāvavṛttadevatākaṃ jale nimagnasakalaśarīras trir japet | anantaraṃ gāṃ payasvinīṃ bahukṣīrāṃ viprāya pradāya brahmahā śudhyati || 3.301 ||
aparam apy āha |
lomabhyaḥ svāheti hi vā divasaṃ mārutāśanaḥ |
jale sthitvābhijuhuyāc catvāriṃśad ghṛtāhutīḥ || 3.302 ||
atha vā divasaṃ samagnaṃ mārutāśano niraśano bhūtvā rātriṃ samagrāṃ jale sthitvodita āditye jalād uttīrya lomabhyaḥ svāhety aṣṭānāṃ mantrāṇām ekaikena mantreṇa pañca pañcāhutīr brahmahatyāśuddhikāmo juhuyāt | etat prāyaścittanimitte brahmavadhe || 3.302 ||
trirātropoṣito hutvā kūṣmāṇḍībhir ghṛtaṃ śuciḥ |
surāpaḥ svarṇahārī ca rudrajāpī jale sthitaḥ || 3.303 ||
sahasraśīrṣājāpī tu mucyate gurutalpagaḥ |
gaur deyā karmaṇo 'syānte pṛthag ebhiḥ payasvinī || 3.304 ||
rahasi surāṃ paiṣṭīṃ yaḥ pibati sa trirātropoṣitaḥ snānānantaraṃ kūṣmāṇḍībhir ṛgbhiḥ "yad devā devaheḍanam" ity evamādibhir ājyaṃ laukike 'gnau hutvā [1213] śucir apāpo bhavati | brāhmaṇasvarṇahārī punas trirātropoṣito 'ntarjale sthitvā rudrān "namas te rudra manyave" ity evamādikān ekādaśānuvākāñ japitvā śuciḥ syāt | tathā gurutalpagas trirātropavāsānte snātaḥ sahasraśīrṣeti sūktasya japaśīlaḥ sa gurutalpagamanapāpān mucyate | jāpīti tācchīlyapratyayād rudrāṇāṃ puruṣasūktasya ca japo manaḥparitoṣaṃ yāvad abhyasanīyaḥ | asya ca kūṣmāṇḍahomādeḥ karmaṇo 'nte surāpādinā payasvinī gaur deyā || 3.303, 304 ||
idānīṃ mahāpatakebhyo yāny alpapātakaviṣayāṇi rahasyaprāyaścittavratāni tāny āha |
prāṇāyāmaśataṃ kāryaṃ sarvapāpāpanuttaye |
upapātakajātānām anādiṣṭasya caiva hi || 3.305 ||
uktalakṣaṇānāṃ prāṇāyāmānāṃ śataṃ sarveṣāṃ pāpānām upapātakajātānām upapātakasaṃjñakānām anādiṣṭasya ca svaśāstre prāyaścittavidhiśūnyasya ca pāpasyāpanuttaye kṣayāya kāryam | pratipāpaṃ ca prāṇāyāmaśatasyāvṛttiḥ kāryā || 3.305 ||
kiṃ ca |
oṃkārābhiṣṭutaṃ somasalilaṃ pāvanaṃ pibet |
kṛtvā tu retoviṇmūtraprāśanaṃ ca dvijottamaḥ || 3.306 ||
retoviṇmūtrāṇām anyatamaṃ bhakṣayitvā śatakṛtvaḥ praṇavenābhiṣṭutam abhimantritaṃ somasalilaṃ somarasaṃ pāvanaṃ śuddhikaraṃ pibet | sakṛd amatibhakṣaṇa etat | kāmato 'bhyāse vā smṛtyantroktaṃ draṣṭavyam | tad upariṣṭād vakṣyate || 3.306 ||
aparam apy āha |
niśāyāṃ vā divā vāpi yad ajñānakṛtaṃ bhavet |
trikālasaṃdhyākaraṇāt tat sarvaṃ saṃpraṇaśyati || 3.307 ||
yad amatipūrvakam alpakaṃ pāpaṃ rātrāv ahani vā kṛtaṃ syāt tat sarvaṃ madhaṃdinādikālatrayavihitasaṃdhyānuṣṭhānāt praṇaśyati | atra ca saṃdhyāśabdena prakaraṇāntaravihitaṃ saṃdhyākarma pratinirdiśya tasya pāpakṣayakaratvaṃ vidhīyate, na punaḥ saṃdhyākarmakasya karmāntarasya | tathā sati saṃdhyāśabdasya svārthahāniḥ syāt | na caivaṃ sati saṃdhyākarmaṇo nityatvahāniḥ śaṅkanīyā | sarvasya sarvadā [1214] pāpakṣayakāmanāyāḥ saṃbhavāt | yad vā saṃyogapṛthaktvena nityakāmyatvayoḥ saṃbhavāt || 3.307 ||
evaṃ pratipāpaṃ prāyaścittaviśeṣā uktāḥ | saṃprati sādhāraṇāny (ān) pāpakṣayopāyān āha |
śukriyāraṇyakajapo gāyatryāś ca viśeṣataḥ |
sarvapāpaharā ete rudraikādaśiṇī tathā || 3.308 ||
"viśvāni deva savitaḥ" ityādyā ṛcaḥ śukriyā vājasaneyakaprasiddhāḥ | araṇyādhyeyaṃ brahmāraṇyakaṃ tayor japo gāyatrījapaś ca viśeṣataḥ pāpaviśeṣam apekṣya saṃkhyālpatvabhūyastvayuktā ete japāḥ pratyekaṃ sarvapāpakṣayakarāḥ | tathā rudraikādaśinī rudrasaṃjñakānām ekādaśānām anuvākānāṃ samāhāro japyamānaḥ sarvapāpakṣayakaro veditavyaḥ | śukriyādijapaś ca pratipāpaṃ bhedena kāryo na tu sakṛt || 3.308 ||
api ca |
yatra yatra tu saṃkīrṇam ātmānaṃ manyate dvijaḥ |
tatra tatra tilair homo gāyatryāvartanaṃ tathā || 3.309 ||
yatra yatra viṣaye saṃkīrṇapatitādisaṃkaravantam ātmānaṃ dvijo manyate, tatra tatra tilair homaḥ karyaḥ | gāyatryāś cāvartanam abhyāsaḥ | tilahome vyāhṛtayo mantrāḥ,
na gāyatryā samaṃ japyaṃ na vyāhṛtisamaṃ hutam |
iti śāstrāntaradarśanāt | tathā ca saṃvartaḥ |
mahāvyāhṛtibhir homas tilaiḥ kāryo dvijanmanā |
upapātakaśuddhyarthaṃ sahasraparisaṃkhyayā ||
ataś copapātakasaṃkaraviṣayatāsya gamyate || 3.309 ||
kiṃ ca |
vedābhyāsarataṃ kṣāntaṃ mahāyajñakriyāparam |
na spṛśantīha pāpāni mahāpātakajāny api || 3.310 ||
[1215]
vedasya grahaṇādhyayanārthavicāraguṇanajapakhyāpanarūpe 'bhyāse rataṃ sarvadā vartamānaṃ kṣāntaṃ kṛtāparādhaṃ praty apy anapakāriṇam, pañcamahāyajñānuṣṭhānaṃ paraṃ prayojanaṃ yasya sa mahāyajñakriyāpara enaṃ mahāpātakajāny api pāpāni na spṛśanti na tatra vartante, kiṃ punar anyāni | tasmān mahāpātakavyatiriktaṃ pāpakṣayaṃ saṃkalpya vedābhyasaratena bhavitavyam iti tātparyārthaḥ || 3.310 ||
kiṃ ca |
vāyubhakṣo divā tiṣṭhed rātriṃ nītvāpsu sūryadṛk |
japtvā sahasraṃ gāyatryāḥ śuddhir brahmavadhād ṛte || 3.311 ||
vāyubhakṣo vāyor anyat kim apy abhakṣayan divā tiṣṭhen nopaviśet | rātrim apsu nītvā sūryadṛk sūryodaye sati gāyatrīsahasrajapaṃ yo 'nutiṣṭhati tasya brahmavadhavyatiriktasya pāpasya śuddhir bhavet | atha smṛtyantroktāni rahasyaprāyaścittāny anukramiṣyante | tatra yamaḥ: "rahasyakṛte rahasyam eva kartavyam" | viśvāmitraḥ |
prakāśa uktaṃ yat kiṃcid viṃśo bhāgo rahasyake |
triṃśadbhāgaḥ ṣaṣṭibhāgaḥ kalpyo jātyādyapekṣayā ||
avikhyāpitadoṣāṇāṃ pāpānāṃ mahatāṃ tathā |
sarveṣāṃ copapāpānāṃ śuddhiṃ vakṣyāmy aśeṣataḥ ||
prāṇāyāmaiḥ pavitraiś ca dānair homair japais tathā |
viniyuktaiḥ pramucyante pātakebhyo na saṃśayaḥ ||
pavitrāṇi sūktāni |
prāṇāyāmāḥ pavitrāṇi vyāhṛtīḥ praṇavaṃ tathā |
pavitrapāṇir āsīno hy abhyased brahma naityakam ||
āvartayet sadā yuktaḥ prāṇāyāmān punaḥ punaḥ |
ālomāgrān nakhāgrāṃś ca tapas tapyata uttamam ||
baudhāyanaḥ |
vidhinā śāstradṛṣṭena prāṇāyāmān samācaret |
yad upasthakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ padbhyāṃ vā yat kṛtaṃ bhavet ||
bāhubhyāṃ manasā vācā śrotratvagghrāṇacakṣuṣā ||
tat sarvaṃ nāśayed iti śeṣaḥ | [1216] tathā: "śrotratvagghrāṇamanovyatikrameṣu tribhiḥ prāṇāyāmaiḥ śudhyati | śūdrānnastrīgamanabhojaneṣu kevaleṣu pṛthak pṛthak saptāhaṃ sapta sapta prāṇāyāmān dhārayet | tathābhakṣyābhojyāpeyānādyaprāśaneṣu | tathaivāpaṇyavikrayeṣu madhumāṃsaghṛtadvādaśatailalākṣālavaṇarasānnavarjeṣu | yac cānyad apy evaṃ yuktaṃ syād ardhamāsaṃ dvādaśa dvādaśa prāṇāyāmān dhārayet | atha pātakeṣu patanīyavarjeṣu yac cānyad apy evaṃ syāt | dvādaśārdhamāsān dvādaśa dvādaśa prāṇāyāmān dhārayet" | manuḥ |
savyāhṛtikāḥ sapraṇavāḥ prāṇāyāmās tu ṣoḍaśa |
api bhrūṇahanaṃ māsāt punanty ahar ahaḥ kṛtāḥ ||
sarvamahāpātakāpanodanam etat | atra ca tatkāryāpattyā taddharmalābha iti nyāyāt prakāśaprāyaścittadharmā brahmacaryaṃ satyavacanādhaḥśayanahaviṣyādayaḥ prāpnuvanti |
kautsaṃ japtvāpa ity etad vāsiṣṭhaṃ ca tṛcaṃ prati |
sāhityaṃ śuddhavatyaś ca surāpo 'pi viśudhyati ||
kautsaṃ kutsena dṛṣṭam — "apa naḥ śośucad agham" ity etad aṣṭarcam āgneyaṃ gāyatraṃ "pratistomebhir uṣasam" ityādikaṃ vasiṣṭhadṛṣṭaṃ tṛcam auṣasyaṃ traiṣṭubhaṃ japitvā, tathā "mahitrīṇām" iti tṛcaṃ satyadhṛtivāruṇidṛṣṭam ādityaṃ gāyatram "eto nv indram" iti śuddhavatyas titra (?) aindryo 'niṣṭubha āṅgirasatiraścidṛṣṭāḥ | etāni japitvā surāpo viśudhyati | ṣoḍaśasaṃkhyākaś cātra japaḥ kāryaḥ |
sakṛj japtvāsyavāmīyaṃ śivasaṃkalpam eva ca |
apahṛtya suvarṇaṃ tu kṣaṇād bhavati nirmalaḥ ||
"asya vāmasya palitasya hotuḥ" iti sūktam auṣasyaṃ dīrghatapodṛṣṭaṃ dvipañcāśadṛkkaṃ traiṣṭubham | "pañcapādaṃ pitaram," "sākaṃjānāṃ saptatham," "yad gāyatre adhi," "saptārdhagarbhāḥ," "gaurīr mimāya" iti jagatyaḥ | "tasyāḥ samudrāḥ" iti viṣṭarapaṅktir vaiśvadevam "yaj jāgrataḥ" ityādikaṃ ṣaḍṛcaṃ manodai(de)vatyaṃ traiṣṭubham | tathā |
haviṣpāntīyam abhyasya na tamaṃha itīt ca |
japtvā ca pauruṣaṃ sūktaṃ mucyate gurutalpagaḥ ||
"haviṣpāntam ajaraṃ svarvidi" ity ekonaviṃśatis triṣṭubho vāmadevamūrdhanvaḍdṛṣṭāḥ [1217] sūryavaiśvānaradai(de)vatyāḥ | "na tamaṃho na duritam" ity aṣṭarcaṃ sūktaṃ vāmadevam am(putrāṃ)homucā dṛṣṭaṃ vaiśvadevaṃ tasyādyāḥ saptopariṣṭād bṛhatyo 'ntyā triṣṭup | "iti vā iti me manaḥ" trayodaśarcam aindraṃ lavena dṛṣṭaṃ taddai(de)vatyaṃ gāyatraṃ sūktaṃ "sahasraśīrṣā puruṣaḥ" iti puruṣasūktaṃ ṣoḍaśarcaṃ sādhyanāḍāyanadṛṣṭaṃ puruṣadai(de)vatyaṃ tasyāntyā triṣṭup | anuṣṭubho 'nyāḥ | haviṣpāṅktīyādīnāṃ japaḥ ṣoḍaśakṛtvaḥ kāryaḥ | tathā |
enasāṃ sthūlasūkṣmāṇāṃ cikīrṣann apanodanam |
avety ṛcaṃ japed abdaṃ yat kiṃcedam itīti ca ||
"ava te heḍo varuṇa namobhiḥ" iti vāruṇī | ājīgartiśunaḥśepadṛṣṭā triṣṭup | "yat kiṃcedaṃ varuṇa" iti vasiṣṭhadṛṣṭā vāruṇī triṣṭup "iti vā iti me manaḥ" iti pūrvoktam ṛṣyādi | tathā |
pratigṛhyāpratigrāhyaṃ bhuktvā cānnaṃ vigarhitam |
japaṃs taratsamandīyaṃ mānavo mucyate tryahāt ||
apratigrāhyaṃ pratiṣiddhapratigrahaṃ surādipatitādisaṃbandhi gavādy api | vigarhitam annaṃ laśunādi | "taratsamandī dhāvati" iti caturdaśarcaṃ sūktam avatsāradṛṣṭaṃ pavamānasomadai(de)vatyaṃ gāyatram | tathā |
saumāraudraṃ tu abhvenā māsam abhyasya śudhyati |
sravantyām ācaran snānam aryamyam api vāpy ṛcam ||
"somarudrā dhārayethām" iti caturdaśam āṅgirasaṃ bhāradvājadṛṣṭaṃ saumāraudradai(de)vatyaṃ traiṣṭubham | "aryamyaṃ varuṇaṃ mitryaṃ vā" iti triṣṭupā(pya)rjanyātridṛṣṭā vāruṇī | tathā |
abdārdhm indram ity etad enasvī saptakaṃ japet |
apraśastaṃ tu kṛtvāpsu māsam āsīta bhaikṣabhuk ||
"indraṃ mitraṃ caruṇam agnim ūtaye" iti saptarcaṃ sūktaṃ kutsadṛṣṭaṃ vaiśvadevaṃ jāgataṃ triṣṭubantam | apraśastaṃ mūtrapurīṣaretasām utsargam apsu kṛtvā māsaṃ bhaikṣabhug āsīta | kāryāntarāṇi parityajya vartetety arthaḥ |
mantraiḥ śākalahomīyair abdaṃ hutvā ghṛtaṃ dvijaḥ |
sugurv apy apahanty eno japtvā vā nama ity ṛcam ||
"devakṛtasyainasaḥ" ityādyāḥ śākalahomīyāḥ ṣaṇmantrāḥ | "nama id ugraṃ nama āvivāsa" iti vaiśvadevī triṣṭub āṅgirasabhāradvājadṛṣṭā | yatra maraṇāntikaṃ prāyaścittaṃ tat suguru | [1218]
mahāpātakasaṃyukto 'nugacched gāḥ samāhitaḥ |
abhyasyābdaṃ pāvamānīr bhaikṣāhāro viśudhyati ||
pāvamānyaḥ "svādiṣṭhayā" ityādayo "yat te rājañ chṛtaṃ haviḥ" ityetadantāḥ | atyantagurutaramahāpātakaviṣayaṃ caitat | tathā |
araṇye vā tir abhyasya prasanno vedasaṃhitām |
mucyate pātakaiḥ sarvaiḥ parākaiḥ śodhitas tribhiḥ ||
idam api pūrveṇa samānaviṣayam |
tryahaṃ tūpavased yuktas trirahno 'bhyupayann apaḥ |
mucyate pātakaiḥ sarvair japtvā trir aghamarṣaṇam ||
atra pakṣe pratisnānaṃ trir aghamarṣaṇajapaḥ |
ṛksaṃhitāṃ trir abhyasya yajuṣāṃ vā samāhitaḥ |
sāmnāṃ vā sarahasyānāṃ sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate ||
yathā mahāhradaṃ prāpya kṣipraṃ loṣṭo vinaśyati |
tathā duścaritaṃ sarvaṃ vede trivṛti majjati ||
ṛco yajūṃṣi cāgryāṇi sāmāni vividhāni ca |
eṣa jñeyas trivṛdvedo yo vedainaṃ sa vedavit ||
ādyaṃ yat tryakṣaraṃ brahmeti praṇavābhiprāyam | śaṅkhalikhitau: "brahmahā trirātropoṣito 'ntarjale 'ghamarṣaṇaṃ trir āvartayet | gurutalpagaḥ pavitram ṛṣabhaṃ suvarṇasteyī rudrādhyāyī surāpo madhyāhne kūṣmāṇḍair ghṛtahomaṃ kuryāt | hiraṇyastenaś catasṛmihir hiraṇyavarābhir ātmānam abhyukṣya hiraṇyam apsu nidadhyāt" | yamaḥ: "atha surāpaḥ kaṇṭhamātram udakam avatīrya sutasomasya prasṛtim ādāyoṃkāreṇābhimantrya pibet | tato 'spsu nimajya mānastokyāṃ paṭhet | tasmāt pūto bhavati" |
"mā nas toke tanaye mā na āyau" iti māsastokyā kutsadṛṣṭā raudrī jagatī |
"brāhmaṇasuvarṇasteyaṃ kṛtvā hiraṇyaṃ śālāyāṃ prakṣipya grīvāmātram udakam avatīryāpsu nimajjya catasṛbhir hiraṇyavarṇābhir ātmānam abhyukṣya mānastokyayā trīn prāṇāyāmāṇ kṛtvā tad etasmāt pāpāt pūto bhavati | atha gurutalpagamanaṃ kṛtvāghamarṣaṇam antarjale [1219] trir āvartya tad etasmāt pāpāt pūto bhavati | atha brahmahatyāṃ kṛtvā prācīṃ codīcīṃ vā diśam upaniṣkramya prabhūtenendhanenāgniṃ prajvālyāghamarṣaṇenāṣṭasahasram āhutīr juhuyāt | tad etasmāt pāpāt pūto bhavati" | ājyam atra haviḥ | "pātakopapātakamahāpātakābhi(ti)pātakānām anyatamasaṃnipāte 'ghamarṣaṇam eva japet" | baudhāyanaḥ: "athātaḥ pavitrātipavitrasyāghamarṣaṇasya kalpaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ — grāmāt prācīṃ vodīcīṃ vā diśam upaniṣkramya snātaḥ śuciḥ śucivsā udakānte sthaṇḍilam upalipya sakṛtklinnena vāsasā sakṛtpūtena pāṇināghamarṣaṇam svādhyāyam adhīyīta | prātaḥ śataṃ madhyāhne śatam aparimitaṃ coditeṣu nakṣatreṣu prasṛtiyāvakaṃ prāśnīyād ajñānakṛtebhyo jñānakṛtebhyaś copapātekebhyaḥ saptarātrāt pramucyate | dvādaśarātrād bhrūṇahananam | gurutalpaṃ suvarṇasteyaṃ surāpānam iti varjayitvaikaviṃśatirātrāt tāny api tarati sarvaṃ jayati, sarvakratuphalam āpnoti, sarveṣu vedeṣu cīrṇavrato bhavati, sarveṣu tīrtheṣu snāto bhavati, sarvedeṣu jñāto bhavati ā cakṣuṣaḥ paṅktiṃ punāti, karmārambhā asya sidhyanti" iti | baudhāyanaḥ: "upapātakebhyo yāni mahānti mahāpātakebhyaś ca yāni nyūnāni tāni sarvāṇy api dvādaśarātrād apohati | ekaviṃśatirātrān mahāhāpātakāni" | aṅgirāḥ |
ekādaśaguṇān vāpi rudrān āvartya dharmavit |
mahāpāpair upaspṛṣṭo mucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
surāpo yadi vā cauro bhrūṇahā gurutalpagaḥ |
mucyate sarvapāpais tu rudrāṃs tu satataṃ japan ||
śātātapaḥ |
madyaṃ pītvā gurudārāṃś ca gatvā steyaṃ kṛtvā brahmahatyāṃ vidhāya |
bhasmacchanno bhasmaśayyāṃ śayāno rudrādhyāyī mucyate sarvapāpaiḥ ||
[1220] yamaḥ |
maṇḍalaṃ pāvamānaṃ ca japan pakṣam upoṣitaḥ |
nāśayed brahmahatyāṃ ca vasiṣṭhavacanaṃ yathā ||
indraṃ mantram idaṃ sūktaṃ samitraṃ ma(mā)dhucchandasam |
surāpānāt pramucyeta pāvamānajapena vā ||
"agnim īḷe purohitam" ityādyam "indraṃ viśvā avīvṛdhan" ity etad vargasahitaṃ saptarcaṃ mādhuchandasam |
śaunā(naḥ)śepaṃ nāsadasyaṃ kautsaṃ steyavināśanam |
japed vāpy asyavāmīyaṃ pāvamānam athāpi vā ||
kuntāpaṃ vālakhilyāṃś ca nivitpraiṣān vṛpākapīn |
hotṝn rudrāṃs tathā japtvā mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ ||
daśapraṇavagāyatryāḥ prāṇāyāmaśatais tribhiḥ |
mucyate brahmahatyāyāḥ kiṃ punaḥ śeṣapātakaiḥ ||
ghṛtaṃ tilān vā samidho hutvā devakṛtādibhiḥ |
apsu vā niyato japtvā tad ahnaiva viśudhyati ||
yat kiṃceti vāruṇīm antarjale japan sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate |
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
sarveṣām eva pāpānāṃ saṃkare samupasthite |
abhyāso daśasāhasro gāyatryāḥ śodhanaṃ param ||
gāyatryanuvṛttau śaṅkhaḥ |
suvarṇasteyakṛd vipro brahmahā gurutalpagaḥ |
surāpaś ca viśudhyanti lakṣajāpān na saṃśayaḥ ||
hutā devī viśeṣeṇa sarvakalmaṣanāśinī |
sarvakāmapradā devī varadā bhaktavatsalā ||
ghṛtayuktais tilair vahniṃ hutvā tu susamāhitaḥ |
pāpātmā lakṣahomena pātakebhyaḥ pramucyate ||
abhīṣṭaṃ lokam āpnoti tathā pāpavivarjitaḥ ||
manuḥ |
sahasrakṛtvas tv abhyasya bahir etat trikaṃ dvijaḥ |
mahato 'py enaso māsāt tvacevāhir vimucyate ||
