bahir grāmāt | trikaṃ savyāhṛtisāvitryātmakam | hārītaḥ: "paṇavo vyāhṛtayaḥ sāvitrī ceti sāvitraṃ pavitraṃ yena sarvapāpebhyo vimucyate | śataṃ japtvā [1221] māsāt pūto bhavati | daś sahasrāṇi japtvā sarvapūtātmā bavatīty āhuḥ" | tathā "haṃsaḥ śuciṣad iti vimalaṃ pavitraṃ yena sarvapāpebhyaḥ pramucyate | apsu japed ghṛtaṃ tilān samidho vā juhuyāt | ādityaṃ vopatiṣṭheta pūto bhavati" | tathā "ā kṛṣṇenety ādityam apatiṣṭhan sarvapūtātmā bhavati" | yamaḥ |
oṃkārāyutam abhyasya tadahnaiva viśudhyati |
tathā: "atha caṇḍālakule naṭakule mauṣṭikakule dāmbhikābhiśastapatitakule 'nyasmin vā 'bhojyapāpakarmakule pratigṛhya kṣudhito bhuktvā vā grāmāt prācīm udīcīṃ vā diśam abhiniṣkramya pṛthivyāṃ śucau deśe 'psu pariplavamānas taratsamandīr japet | oṃkārapūrvikā vyāhṛtīr vā tasmāt pāpāt pūto bhavati | mātaraṃ bhaginīṃ gatvā mātṛsvasāraṃ snuṣāṃ sakhīṃ vānyad vāgamyāgamanaṃ kṛtvāghamarṣaṇam antarjale trir āvartya tad etasmāt pāpāt pūto bhavati | gohatyāṃ strīhatyāṃ rājanyavaiśyahatyāṃ nyāsāpahāraṃ kanyādūṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā grāmāt prācīṃ vodīcīṃ vā diśam abhiniṣkramya prabhūtenendhanenāgniṃ prajvālya gavyena ghṛtena vyāhṛtibhir āhutisahasraṃ juhuyāt | gāyatryaṣṭasahasram āvartyādityaṃ prekṣamāṇaś catasṛbhir hiraṇyavarṇābhir ātmānaṃ prokṣya vāmadevyaṃ trir āvartya tad etasmāt pāpāt pūto bhavati | caṇḍālīṃ pukkasīṃ mitrakalatraṃ vā gatvopaspṛśya vāmadeve(vye)na vā trīn prāṇāyāmān kṛtvā tad etasmāt pāpāt pūto bhavati | retomūtrapurīṣaprāśanaṃ kṛtvā laśunapalāṇḍugṛñjanakakumbhīkādīnām abhakṣyāṇāṃ bhakṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā haṃsagrāmakukkutaśvasūkarasṛgālamāṃsabhakṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā tataḥ kaṇṭhamātram udakam avatīrya śuddhavatībhiḥ prāṇāyāmaṃ kṛtvā vyāhṛtibhir urogam udakaṃ pītvā tad etasmāt pāpāt pūto bhavati" | atriḥ: "rajanīpādaṃ dhyānam eva samācaret | tatpūrvaṃ tadaparaṃ brahma savituḥ | abhakṣyabhakṣaṇad apeyapānād akāryakaraṇād aśeṣato mucyate | vāmadevaṃ(vyaṃ) trir āvartya mucyate brahmahatyayā | [1222] ayaṃ ta indrasometi japitvā kanyādūṣī ca śudhyati | somaṃ rājānaṃ varuṇam iti japitvā viṣadagaradāgnidāś ca mucyante | ud u tyaṃ jātavedasam iti saptakṛtva ādityopasthānād iha kṛtaiḥ pūvakṛtaiś ca pāpair mucyate" | yamaḥ: "apeyaṃ pītvābhakṣyaṃ bhakṣayitvā snātvopaspṛśyarṣabheṇa trīn prāṇāyāmān kṛtvā gāyatryaṣṭaśatam āvartya tad etasmāt pūto bhavati" | ṛṣabhaḥ sāmaviśeṣaḥ | caturviṃśatimatāt |
mahāvyāhṛtibhir homas tilaiḥ kāryo dvijanmanā |
upapātakaśuddhyarthaṃ sahasraparisaṃkhyayā ||
na tathā vedajāpena sarvapāpāny apohati |
aihikāmuṣmikaṃ pāpaṃ sarvaṃ niravaśeṣataḥ ||
pañcarātreṇa gāyatrīṃ japamāno vyapohati |
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
sarvadevapavitrāṇi vakṣyāmy aham ataḥ param |
yeṣāṃ japaiś ca homaiś ca pūyante nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
aghamarṣaṇadevakṛtaṃ śuddhavatyas taratsamāḥ |
kūṣmāṇḍyaḥ pāvamānyo 'pi virājaṃ mṛtyulāṅgalam ||
bhadrā vyāhṛtayo durgā mahādoṣavināśinī |
prāyaścittāni vakṣyāmi vikhyātāni viśeṣataḥ ||
samāhitānāṃ yuktānāṃ pramādeṣu kathaṃ bhavet ||
"ṛtaṃ ca satyaṃ cā(ce)ty aghamarṣaṇaṃ trir antarjale japan sarvasmāt pāpāt pramucyate | āyaṃ gauḥ pṛśnir akramīd ity etām ṛcaṃ trir antarjale japan sarvasmāt pāpāt pramucyate | haṃsaḥ śuciṣad ity etām ṛcaṃ trir antarjale japan sarvasmāt pāpāt pramucyate |. api vā gāyatrīṃ paccho 'rdharcaśaḥ samastām iti trir antarjale japan sarvasmāt pāpāt pramucyate" |
viṣṇuḥ: "athātaḥ sarvadevapavitrāṇi bhavanti | yeṣāṃ japaiś ca homaiś ca dvijātayaḥ pāpebhyaḥ pūyante | aghamarṣaṇaṃ devakṛtaḥ | śuddhavatyas taratsamandī dhāvati | kūṣmāṇḍyaḥ pāvamānyo durgāsāvitrī [1223] abhiṣaṅgāḥ padastomā vyāhṛtayo bhāruṇḍāni aindrasāma puruṣavrataṃ bhā(mā)sam abliṅgā bārhaspatyaṃ vāksūktaṃ gosūktam aśvasūktaṃ madhvṛcaḥ sāmanī candraśuddheḥ śatarudriyam artharvaśiras trisuparṇaṃ mahāvrataṃ nārāyaṇīyaṃ puruṣasūkaṃ trīny ājyadohāni rathaṃtaraṃ cāgnivrataṃ vāmadevyaṃ bṛhac caitāni gītāni punanti jantuṃ jātismaratvaṃ labhate ya icchet" | hārītaḥ |
hatvā lokān apīmāṃs trīṃs triḥ paṭhed aghamarṣaṇam |
yathāśvamedhāvabhṛthas tathā tan maitrir abhravīt ||
rudraikādaśinīṃ japtvā tadahnaiva viśudhyati |
ghṛtaṃ tilān samidho vā juhuyād devakṛtasya vai ||
apsu vā niyato japtvā tadahnaiva viśudhyati |
virajaṃ vimalaṃ śuddhaṃ trimadhu jyeṣṭhasāma ca ||
apratiratham aśvastomīyaṃ kayāśubhīyaṃ manobhāsūktam eva ca |
trisuparṇaṃ suparṇaṃ ca pratyaṅgaṃ mṛtyulāṅgalam ||
atharvaśiro 'tha bhaumaṃ vā vāmadevyam athāpi vā |
ādityasūktaṃ vāksūktaṃ śrīsūktaṃ sarvasaṃmitam ||
vāksūktaṃ vividhaṃ caiva sarvapāpapraṇāśanam || iti |
atha śārīraṃ pavitram | yad ahnā rātryā padbhyāṃ pāpam akārṣaṃ viṣṇur mā tasmād enaso viśvān muñcatv aṃhasaḥ | yad adhnā rātryā vopasthena pāpam akārṣaṃ prajāpatir mā tasmād enaso viśvān muñcatv aṃhasaḥ | yad ahnā rātryā vā pāyunā pāpam akārṣaṃ mitro mā tasmād enaso viśvān muñcatv aṃhasaḥ | yad ahnā rātryā vā pāṇibhyāṃ pāpam akārṣam indro mā tasmād enaso viśvān muṇcatv aṃhasaḥ | yad ahnā rātryā vā vācā pāpam akārṣam agnir mā tasmād enaso viśmān muṇcatv aṃhasaḥ | yad ahnā rātryā vā jihvayā pāpam akārṣam āpo mā tasmād enaso viśvān muṇcatv aṃhasaḥ | yad ahnā rātryā vā prāṇena pāpam akārṣaṃ pṛthivī mā tasmād enaso viśvān muṇcatv aṃhasaḥ | yad ahnā rātryā [vā?] cakṣuṣā pāpam akārṣaṃ [1224] sūryo mā tasmād enaso viśvān muñcatv aṃhasaḥ | yad ahnā rātryā vā śrotrābhyāṃ pāpam akārṣaṃ parjanyo mā tasmād enaso viśvān muṇcatv aṃhasaḥ | yad ahnā rātryā vā tvacā pāpam akārṣaṃ vāyur mā tasmād enaso viśvān muṇcatv aṃhasaḥ | yad ahnā rātryā vā manasā pāpam akārṣaṃ candro mā tasmād enaso viśvān muṇcatv aṃhasaḥ | yad ahnā rātryā vā buddhyā pāpam akārṣaṃ brahmā mā tasmād enaso viśvān muṇcatv aṃhasaḥ | yad ahnā rātryā vāhaṃkāreṇa pāpam akārṣaṃ rudro mā tasmād enaso viśvān muṇcatv aṃhasaḥ |
yan me keśeṣu daurbhāgyaṃ sīmante yac ca mūrdhani |
lalāṭe karṇayor akṣṇor agnis tan me vyapohatu ||
ya udagān mahato 'rṇavād vibhrājamānaḥ salilasya madhyāt |
sa mām ṛṣabho rohitākṣaḥ sūryo vipaścin manasā punātu ||
avadhūto vā laṅghito 'psu japet pūto bhavati | śucimarghamādāyoditamātrāya namas te 'stv aśītaraśmaye tamonude jahi me daurbhāgyaṃ saubhāgyāyodayasva ma iti | ud u tyaṃ citraṃ devānām ity etābhyāṃ hastābhyām avanīyārdhyāñjaliṃ haṃsaḥ śuciṣad ity ādityam upatiṣṭhet tadvrataṃ sārvakāmikam | akṛtāhniko gave yavasaṃ dadyāt | bhadrābhyo namo bahulābhyo namo denubhyo namaḥ sarvalokadevamātṛbhyo namaḥ | yadi gāṃ na vinden na parivaden nākrośet tadvrataṃ sārvakālikam | upahṛtam aśanaṃ sahasāgatāya brāhmaṇāya dadyāt | namo brahmabhyo bhrahmavidbhyo brāhmaṇebhyo nama iti | yad brāhmaṇaṃ na vinden na parivaden nākrośet tadvrataṃ sārvakālikam" iti |
atriḥ |
na vedabalam āśritya pāpakarmaratir bhavet |
ajñānāc ca pramādāc ca dahyate karma netarat ||
hariḥ |
suvarṇadānaṃ godānaṃ pṛthivīdānam eva ca |
etat prayacchamāno vai sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate ||
purāṇāt |
atipāpamahāpāpopapāpādīny aśeṣataḥ |
mohād vidhāya viratat svarṇaṃ tadanusārataḥ || [1225]
brāhmaṇaṃ prārthayitvādau śrotriyaṃ vedapāragam |
saṃpūjya puṣpavastrādyair mantreṇānena dāpayet ||
ajinaṃ sarvadai(de)vatyam suvarṇaṃ ca hutāśanaḥ |
śaraṇyaṃ pitṛdai(de)vatyam ajyaṃ tejo divaukasām ||
tilāṃś ca dadyād vidhivan mantreṇānena suvrata |
maharṣer gātrataḥ sṛṣṭāḥ kaśyapasya tilāḥ smṛtāḥ ||
tadmād eṣāṃ prasādena pāpasaṃghātapañjaram |
vilayaṃ yātu me sarvam āmapātram ivāmbhasi ||
tathā dadyād vidhānena dvijāya madhusarpiṣī |
tārayati akhilān pūrvān ātmānaṃ ca narādhipa ||
gobhiś catasṛbhir yuktaṃ tathā bhūmisamanvitam |
pratigrahasamarthāya viduṣe tv āhitāgnaye ||
datvājinaṃ tu kārtikyāṃ vaiśākhyāṃ vā viśeṣataḥ |
viṣuvaty ayane caiva grahaṇe candrasūryayoḥ ||
kṛcchrāt tu tamaso ghorān mucyate sarvato bhayāt |
atītān sapta puruṣāṃs tathā cānyān anāgatān |
uddhṛtya sa naro yāti brahmalokaṃ sanātanam ||
vasiṣṭhabṛhaspatī |
yat kiṃcit kurute pāpaṃ puruṣo lobhamohitaḥ |
sarvaṃ tad bhūmidānena kṣipram eva praṇāśayet ||
ādipurāṇe |
yas tu gocarmamātrā vai prayacchati vasuṃdharām |
vimuktaḥ sarvapāpebhyo viṣṇulokaṃ sa gacchati ||
vṛddhavasiṣṭhaḥ |
gavāṃ śataṃ vṛṣaś caiko yatra tiṣṭhed ayantritaḥ |
etad gocarmamātraṃ tu prāhur dharmavido janāḥ ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
daśahastena vaṃśena daśavaṃśāḥ samantataḥ |
pañca cābhyadhikās tadvad etad gocarmalakṣaṇam ||
mātsye |
daṇḍena saptahastena triṃśaddaṇḍā nivartanam |
tribhāgahīnaṃ gocarmamānam āha prajāpatiḥ ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ: "atha karmabhir ātmakṛtair gurum ātmānaṃ manyetātmārthe prasṛtiyāvakaṃ śrapayen na tato 'gnau juhuyāt | na cātra [1226] balikarma | aśṛtaṃ śrapyamāṇaṃ śṛtaṃ vābhimantrayeta
yavo 'si dhānyarājo 'so vāruṇo madhusaṃyutaḥ |
nirṇodaḥ sarvapāpānāṃ pavitram ṛṣibhiḥ ṣṭu(smṛ)tam ||
ghṛtaṃ yavā madhu yavā āpo vā amṛtaṃ yavāḥ |
sarvaṃ punīta me pāpaṃ yan me kiṃcana duṣkṛtam ||
vācā kṛtaṃ karmakṛtaṃ duḥsvapnaṃ durvicintitam |
alakṣmīṃ nāśayata yavāḥ sarvaṃ punīta me yavāḥ ||
gaṇānnaṃ gaṇikānnaṃ ca śūdrānnaṃ śūdrayājanam |
caurasyānnaṃ navaśrāddhaṃ sarvaṃ punīta me yavāḥ ||
bālavṛtttam adharmyaṃ yad rājadvārakṛtaṃ ca yat |
suvarṇasteyam āvrā(vra)tyam ayājyasya ca yājanam ||
brāhmaṇānāṃ parīvādaṃ sarvaṃ punīta me yavāḥ ||
iti śrpyamāṇe rakṣāṃ kuryāt | brahmā devānāṃ padavīḥ kavīnām iti śṛtaṃ ca laghvaśnīyāt | prayataḥ pātre niṣicya ye devā manojātā mano yujaḥ sudakṣā dakṣapitaras te naḥ pāntu te no 'vantu tebhyo namaḥ svāhety ātmani juhuyāt | atha nābhim ālabheta śāntāḥ pītā bhavata yūyam āpo 'smākam antarudare sudevāḥ | tā asmabhyam ayakṣmā anamīvā ajāgamaḥ | svadantu devīr amṛtā ṛtāvṛdha iti trirātraṃ medhārthi ṣaḍrātraṃ pāpakṛt saptarātraṃ pītvā brahmahatyāsurāpānasuvarṇasteyagurutalkān punāti | ekādaśarātraṃ pīvā pūrvapūruṣakṛtam api pāpaṃ nirṇudati | dvādaśarātraṃ pītvā tāḍanodghāṭanāñjanavaśīkaraṇāni karoti | honirhāram uktānāṃ yavānām ekaviṃśatirātraṃ pītvā vidyāḥ paśyati | vidhyādhpatīn paśyati | gaṇān paśyati | gaṇādhpatīn paśyati, ityāha bhagavān vasiṣṭhaḥ" | atraiva vṛddhavasiṣṭhenokā viśeṣāḥ: [1227] "yavānāṃ prasṛtim añjaliṃ vā śrapyamāṇaṃ śṛtaṃ vābhimantrayeta | yavo 'si dhānyarājo 'si vāruṇo madhusaṃyutaḥ | nirṇodaḥ sarvapānānāṃ pavitram ṛṣibhiḥ stuta ity anena | tathā ghṛtaṃ yavā madhu yavāḥ pavitram amṛtaṃ yavāḥ | sarvaṃ punantu me pāpaṃ vāṅmanaḥkāyasaṃbhavam ity anena vā |
agnikāryaṃ na kurvīta tena bhūtabaliṃ tathā |
nāgraṃ na bhikṣāṃ nātithyaṃ na cocchiṣṭaṃ parityajet ||
ye devā manojātā manoyujaḥ sudakṣā dakṣa kratavaḥ |
te no 'vantu te naḥ pāntu tebhyaḥ svāhety ātmani juhuyāt ||
trirātraṃ medhāvṛddhaye | pāpakṛ ṣaḍrātram, brahmahatyādiṣu dvādaśarātraṃ patitotpanna iti |
aśnīyād yāvakaṃ pakvaṃ gomūtraṃ ca śakṛdrase |
sakṣīradadhisarpiṣkaṃ mucyate kilbiṣāt kṣaṇāt ||" iti |
laugākṣiḥ: "kūṣmāṇḍair juhuyād yo 'pūta iva manyeta yad arvācīnam eno bhrūṇahatyāyās tasmān mucyetāyonau vā retaḥ siktvānyatra svapnāder eṣā vā pavitrakāmaḥ paurṇamāsyām amāvāsyāyāṃ vā śuddhapakṣasya rohiṇyāṃ vā snātaḥ śuciḥ śucivāsā vā kṛṣṇājinavāsā vā brahmacārikalpena vratam upeti, saṃvatsaraṃ māsaṃ dvādaśarātraṃ ṣaḍrātraṃ vā | na māṃsam aśnīyāt | na striyam upeyān no pāryāsīta jugupsetānṛtāt payobhakṣa iti pradhānakalpo yāvakaṃ vopabhuñjānaḥ kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ vā bhaikṣaṃ vā vadvidheṣu payo brāhmaṇasya vratam, yavāgū rājanyasyāmikṣā vaiśyasya, pūrvāhṇe 'gnim upasamādhāya caruṃ śrapayitvā yad deva devaheḍanaṃ yad adīvyann ṛṇam aham āyuṣṭe viśvato dadhad ity etaiḥ sūktaiḥ pratyṛcam ājyaṃ juhuyāt | yed devā devaheṣanam iti tisṛbhiḥ | bhadraṃ karṇebhir iti catasṛbhiḥ| punantu mā pitara iti navabhiḥ | punar mām aitv indriyam iti tisṛbhiḥ | rathe akṣeṣu iti catasṛbhiḥ | hiraṇyavarṇā iti tisṛbhiḥ | [1228] agnaye vāyave sūryāya brahmaṇe prajāpataye kūṣmarṣibhya iti vratahomaḥ | agne tvaṃ pārayeti sviṣṭakṛtaṃ vaiśvānarīyeṇ sūktena prāṅmukhaḥ prāñjalir upatiṣṭheta | japet parñci sūktāni vaiśvānaryaṃ ca japet" | vasiṣṭhaḥ |
snātvā śatena gāyatryāḥ śatam antarjale japet |
apaḥ śatena vai pītvā mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ || iti |
rahasyaprāyaścittāny api gurūṇi guruṣ laghūni laghuṣu pāpeṣu vyavasthāpanīyāni gurukalpavidhānasya vaiyarthyaparihārāya |
iti rahasyaprāyaścittāni || 3.311 ||
uktānāṃ prakāśavratānāṃ vakṣyamāṇānāṃ kṛcchrāditapasām aprāyaścittātmakānām api sādhāraṇāny aṅgāni yamaniyamākhyāni śrokadvayenāha |
brahmacaryaṃ dayā kṣāntir dānaṃ satyaṃ akalkatā |
ahiṃsāstreyamādhuryadamāś ceti yamāḥ smṛtāḥ || 3.312 ||
snānamaunopavāsejyāsvādhyāyopasthanigrahāḥ |
niyamā guruśuśrūṣā śaucākrodhāpramāditāḥ || 3.313 ||
brahmacaryādayo yamasaṃjñakāḥ prāyaścittānāṃ kṛcchrādīnāṃ cāṅgatayā kāryāḥ | puruṣārthatayā vihitānām apy eṣāṃ vratādyaṅgatvena vidhānaṃ prayujyate | atra ca brahmacaryaśabdena brahmacāridharmā madhumāṃsavarjanādayo gṛhyante | śukrotsargavaimukhyaṃ tūpasthanigrahaśabdenocyate | akalkatā dambharahitatā | prasiddham anyat |
prasaṅgād anye 'pi sādhāraṇavradharmavidhayo likhyante | tatra viṣṇuḥ |
sarvavarṇeṣu sarveṣāṃ vratānāṃ vidhipūrvakam |
grahaṇaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi prāyaścittacikīrṣayā ||
dinānte nakharomādīn pravāpya snānam ācaret |
bhasmagomayamṛdvāripañcagavyādikalpitaiḥ ||
malāpakarṣaṇaṃ kāryaṃ bāhyaśaucopasiddhaye |
dantadhāvanapūrveṇa pañcagavyena saṃyutam || [1229]
vrataṃ niṣāmukhe grāhyaṃ bahis tārakadarśane |
ācamyātaḥ paraṃ maunī dhyāyan duṣkṛtam ātmanaḥ||
manaḥsaṃtāpanaṃ tīvram udvahec chokam ātmanaḥ |
niṣkāmo nityasaṃkalpaḥ śīghraṃ mucyeta kilbiṣāt ||
nityasnāyī nirāhāro gurudevadvijārcakaḥ |
pūjayed atithīn nityaṃ vratasthān dharmabhikṣukān ||
śāstrajñatve ca viduṣo dharmaniścayapāragān |
kaivalyajñānino ye ca ye ca saṃnyāsakāriṇaḥ ||
tebhyo dānāni deyāni pūjitāni śubhāni ca ||
saṃnyāsino 'tra brahmārpaṇanyāyena karmakāriṇo gṛhasthā ucyante, na tu bhikṣukāḥ, tebhyo dānasya pratiṣiddhatvāt | śaṅkhaḥ |
nyupya keśanakhān pūrvaṃ nadyāṃ snātvā bahir niśi |
pratyekaṃ niyataṃ kālam ātmano vratam ādiśet ||
prāyaścittam upāsīno vāgyatas trir upaspṛśet |
ekavāsā ārdravāsā laghvāśī sthaṇḍileśayaḥ ||
snānaṃ vīrāsanaṃ maunaṃ mauñjī daṇḍakamaṇḍalū |
bhakṣacaryāgnikāryaṃ ca kūṣmāṇḍair juhuyād ghṛtam ||
upasparśanaṃ snānam, sthaṇḍilaṃ bhūḥ | mauñjītyādiṣu ghārayitavyetu adhyāhāraḥ kāryaḥ |
abhuktvā prātarāhāraṃ jñātvā cāsya samāhitaḥ |
sūryāya devatābhyaś ca nivedya vratam ācaret ||
vratanivedanaṃ samantraṃ vratāmba(mbha)vimocane, "agne vratapate vratam ālabhe tat te prabravīmi tac chakeyam evaṃvāyo vratapate sūrya vratapata iti vratam ālabheta, agne vratapate vratam ācāriṣaṃ tad aśakaṃ tat te prabrūmas tan no gopāya | evaṃ vāyo vratapat ityādinā vrataṃ vimuñcet" ityādiśravaṇāt | manuḥ |
na dharmasyāpadeśena pāpaṃ kṛtvā vrataṃ caret |
vratena pāpaṃ pracchādya kurvan strīśūdralambhanam ||
muṇḍas traṣavaṇasnāyī adhaḥśāyī jitendriyaḥ |
strīśūdrapatitānāṃ ca varjayed abhibhāṣaṇam || [1230]
pavitrāṇi japec chaktyā juhuyāc caiva śaktitaḥ |
ayaṃ vidhiś ca nijñeyaḥ sarvakṛcchreṣu sarvadā ||
gāyatryādijapyāni pavitrāṇi | vasiṣṭhaḥ:  "kṛcchrāṇāṃ śmaśrukeśān vāpayed akṣiromaśikhāvarjam |
mahāvyāhṛtibhir homaḥ sāvitryā vānvahaṃ svayam |
kartavyaḥ pavanaḥ samyak sarpistilayavās tathā" ||
sarpirādīni homadravyāṇi parasparaṃ vikalpyante sāvitryā ca vyāhṛtayaḥ | hārītaḥ: "māṣamasūramadhuparānnamaithunāni pratyahaṃ varjayet | vratopeto nākāle vācaṃ visṛjet | strīśūdrocchiṣṭābhibhāṣaṇa ācāmet" | sumantuḥ: "kṣārakṣaudrarasalavaṇamadhumāṃsavarjam annaṃ kṛcchrādiṣu" | baudhāyanaḥ: "triṣavaṇam udakopasparśanam adhaḥśayanam ekavastratā keśaśmaśrunakhalomavāpanaṃ cāndrāyaṇādiṣu | etad eva striyā keśavapanavarjam |
rājā vā rājaputro vā brāhmaṇo vā bahuśrutaḥ |
keśānāṃ vapanaṃ kṛtvā prāyaścittaṃ samācaret ||
keśānāṃ rakṣanārthe tu dviguṇavram ācaret |
dviguṇe vrata ācīrṇe dvigunā dakṣiṇā bhavet" ||
jābāliḥ |
ārambhe sarvakṛcchrāṇāṃ samāptau ca viśeṣataḥ |
ājyenaiva hi śālāgnau juhuyād vyāhṛtīḥ pṛthak ||
śrāddhaṃ kuryād vratāne tu gohiraṇyaṃ ca dakṣinā |
gṛhī gṛhe namaskurvan brahmaloke mahīyate ||
strīṇāṃ homo na dātavyaḥ pañcagavyaṃ tathaiva ca ||
manuḥ |
mahāvyāhṛtibhir homaḥ kartavyaḥ svayam anvaham |
ahiṃsā satyam akrodham ārjavaṃ ca samācaret ||
trir ahni trir niśāyāṃ ca savāsā jalam āviśet |
strīśūdrapatitāṃś caiva nābhibhāṣeta karhicit ||
snānāsanābhyāṃ vihared aśakto 'dhaḥ śayīta vā |
brahmacārī vratī ca syād gurudevadvijārcakaḥ || [1231]
sāvitrīṃ ca japen nityaṃ pavitrāṇi ca śaktitaḥ |
sarveṣv eva vrateṣv evaṃ prāyaścittārtham ādṛtaḥ || iti |
vacanād aprāyaścittārtheṣu kṛcchrādiṣu snānāsanābhyām ity ukter yathāśakti kuryād iti gamyate (?) |
khyāpanenānutāpena tapasādhyayanena ca |
pāpakṛn mucyate pāpāt tathā dānena vāpadi ||
yathā yathā naro 'dharmaṃ svayaṃ kṛtvānubhāvate |
tathā tathā tvacevāhis tenādharmeṇa mucyate ||
yathā yathā punas tasya duṣkṛtaṃ karma nindati |
tathā tathā śarīraṃ tat tenādharmeṇa mucyate ||
kṛtvā pāpaṃ hi saṃtapya tasmāt pāpāt pramucyate |
naitat kuryāṃ punar iti pravṛttyā pūyate hi saḥ ||
pūyate pāpanivāraṇe jāte prāyaścitte 'dhikriyata ity arthaḥ | caturviṃśatimatāt |
ajñānād yadi vā jñānāt kṛtvā karma vigarhitam |
tasmād vimuktim anvicched dvitīyaṃ na samācaret ||
kṛtapāpaviṣayaṃ vairāgyam aprakṛtiparyantaprāyaścittādhikāre hetur iti samudāyārthaḥ | śaṅkhaḥ |
taskaraśvāpadākīrṇe bahuvyālamṛge vane |
na vrataṃ brāhmaṇaḥ kuryāt prāṇabādhābhayāt sadā ||
sarvato jīvitaṃ rakṣej jīvan pāpaṃ vyapohati |
vrataiḥ kṛcchrais tathā dānair ity āha bhagavān yamaḥ ||
śarīraṃ dharmasaṃyuktaṃ rakṣaṇīyaṃ prayatnataḥ |
śarīrāt sravate dharmaḥ parvatāt salilaṃ yathā ||
yamaḥ |
paścāttāpo nivṛttiś ca snānaṃ cāṅgatayoditam |
naimittikānāṃ svātantrye tadvidhiḥ syād anarthakaḥ ||
paścāttāpādikam naimittikānāṃ prāyaścittānām aṅgatvenoktam | svātantrye 'ṅgabhāvarahitatve tadvidhiḥ prāyaścittavidhir niṣphalo 'nuṣṭhānalakṣaṇaphalaśūnyaḥ syāt | mahākleśasādhyaṃ hi prāyaścittam | alpakleśā hi paścāttāpādayaḥ | taiḥ prāyaścittakāle kṛte prāyaścittavidhir aphala iti spaṣṭam | viṣṇupurāṇam |
kṛte pāpae 'nutāpo vai yasya puṃsaḥ prajāyate |
prāyaścittaṃ tu tasyoktaṃ harisaṃsmaraṇam param || [1232]
prātar niṣi tathā saṃdhyāmadhyāhṇādiṣu saṃsmaran |
nārāyaṇam avāpnoti sadyaḥ pāpakṣayaṃ naraḥ ||
yamaḥ |
avabodhaḥ parityāgas tapo dānānukīrtane |
vidyābhyāso hy apāṃ sparśaḥ saptāṅgaṃ pāpanāśanam ||
avabodhaḥ pāpasya duḥkhahetutvaviṣayaḥ | parityāgo na punaḥ pāpakriyā | tapodāne prasiddhe | anukīrtanaṃ pāpasya | vidyā vedaḥ | apāṃ sparśaḥ snānam | parāśaraḥ |
prāyaścitteṣu sarveṣu kuryād brāhmaṇabhojanam |
śaktyā vittānusāreṇa prāyaścittānurūpataḥ ||
ā sahasrād ā śatād vā daśāntam api vā japet |
oṃkārādyaṃ tathā japtvā gāyatrīm atha vāyutam ||
brahmā |
gātrābhyaṅgaṃ śirobhyaṅgaṃ tāmbūlam anulepanam |
vratastho varjayen sarvaṃ yad vānyad balarāgakṛt ||
purāṇāt |
utthitas tu divā tiṣṭhed upaviṣṭas tathā niśi |
etad vīrāsanaṃ proktaṃ mahāpātakanāśanam ||
āpo hi ṣṭheti sūktaṃ tu śuddhavatyo 'ghamarṣaṇam |
śaṃvatyaḥ svastimatyaś ca pāvamānyas tathaiva ca ||
sarvatraiva prayoktavyāḥ kṛcchrādivratam ācaret ||
marīciḥ |
bhasmagomayagoṣṭheṣu śayanaṃ sikatāsu ca |
patitavrātyacaṇḍālastrīśūdrānabhibhāṣaṇam ||
triḥ snānaṃ vāsasā sārdhaṃ snānaṃ japyaṃ surācanam |
yā yasyābhimatā loke devatā tāṃ samarcayet ||
baudhāyanaḥ : "ahataṃ vāso vasīta sāvitrīṃ vyāhṛtīś ca japed aṣṭasahasrakṛtvaḥ |
oṃkāram āditaḥ kṛtvā rūpe rūpe tathāntataḥ |
bhūmau vīrāsane yuktaḥ kuryāj japyaṃ samāhitaḥ ||
āsīnaḥ śalyaviddho vā pibed gavyaṃ payaḥ sakṛt |
gavyasya payaso 'lābhe gavyam eva bhaved dadhi ||
dadhno 'bhāve bhavet takraṃ takrābhāve tu yāvakam |
eṣām anyatamaṃ yad yad upapadyet tat pibet || [1233]
śalyaviddho veti vāśabdaḥ upamārthaḥ || 3.312, 313 |
sāṃtapanādikṛcchrāṇāṃ prāyaścittatayā viniyuktānāṃ svarūpāny āha |
kuśodakaṃ tu gokṣīraṃ dadhimūtraśakṛdghṛtam |
prāśyāpare 'hny upavaset kṛcchraṃ sāṃtapanaṃ caret || 3.314 ||
kuśodakādi dravyaṣaṭkam ekīkṛtya prāśyāparedyur upavaset | eṣa dvirātrasādhyaḥ sāṃtapanaḥ | kuśodakādiprāśanam āhārāntaranivṛttyartham | atra parāśarokto viśeṣaḥ |
gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ kṣīraṃ dadhi sarpiḥ kuśodakam |
nirdiṣṭaṃ pañcagavyaṃtu prāyaścittaṃ viśodhanam ||
gomūtraṃ tāmravarṇāyāḥ śvetāyāś cāpi gomayam |
payaḥ kāñcanavarṇāyā nīlāyāś ca tathā dadhi ||
ghṛtaṃ ca kṛṣṇavarṇāyāḥ sarvaṃ kāpilam eva vā |
alābhe sarvavarṇānāṃ pañcagaveṣv ayaṃ vidhiḥ ||
gomūtramāṣakās tv aṣṭau gomayasya ca ṣoḍaśa |
kṣīrasya dvādaś proktā dadhnas tu daśa kīrtitāḥ ||
gomūtravad ghṛtasyoktās tadardhaṃ tu kuśodakam ||
pañcagavyānuvṛttau śaṅkaḥ |
etad eva tryahābhyastaṃ yatisāṃtapanam smṛtam || 3.314 ||
mahāsāṃtapanam āha |
pṛthaksāṃtapanadravyaiḥ ṣaḍahaḥ sopavāsakaḥ |
saptāhena tu kṛcchro 'yaṃ mahāsāṃtapanaḥ smṛtaḥ || 3.215 ||
kuśodakādīnāṃ ṣaṇṇāṃ sāṃtapanadravyāṇām ekaikam ekasminn ahani prāśya saptame 'hany upavaset | jābālaḥ |
gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ kṣīraṃ dadhi sarpiḥ kuśodakam |
ekaikaṃ kramaśo 'śnīyād ahorātram abhojanam ||
kṛcchraḥ sāṃtapano nāma sarvapāpapraṇāśanaḥ ||
etāny api prāśanāny āhārāntaranivartakāni | yamas tv anyathā sāṃtapanam āha |
tryahaṃ pibet tu gomūtraṃ tryahaṃ vai gomayaṃ pibet |
tryahaṃ dadhi tryahaṃ kṣīraṃ tryahaṃ sarpis tataḥ śuciḥ || [1234]
mahātāṃtapanaṃ hy etat sarvapāpapraṇāśanam ||
jābālaḥ puna ekaviṃśatirātrasādhyaṃ mahāsāṃtapanam āha |
ṣaṇṇām ekeikam eteṣāṃ trirātram upayojayet |
tryahaṃ copavased antyaṃ mahāsāṃtapanaṃ viduḥ || iti |
yadā kuśodakādīnāṃ sāmtapanadravyāṇām ekaikaṃ dvyaham upayujyate, tadā[ti]sāṃtapanākhyaṃ bhavati | yad āha yamaḥ |
etāny eva tu peyāni ekaikaṃ tu dyahaṃ tryaham |
atisāṃtapanaṃ nāma śvapākam api śodhayet || iti |
evaṃ pañca sāṃtapanaprakārā bhavanti | tatra dvirātrasādhyaḥ prathamaḥ | saptarātrasādho dvitīyaḥ | dvādaśarātrikas tṛtīyaḥ | pañcadaśarātasādhyaś caturathaḥ | ekaviṃśatirātrasādhyaḥ pañcamaḥ || 3.315 ||
parṇakṛccham āha |
parṇodumbararājīvabilvapatrakuśodakaiḥ |
pratyekaṃ pratyahābhyastaiḥ parṇakṛcchra udāhṛtaḥ || 3.316 ||
parṇādipatrāṇāṃ kuśānāṃ caikaikasya kvāthodakam ekaikasminn ahani pīyat ity eṣa pañcarātrasādhyaḥ parṇakṛcchraḥ | atrāpi prāśanam āhārāntaranivartakam | parṇaḥ palāśaḥ | rājīvaṃ padmam | prasiddham anyat | viṣṇus tu praṇakṛcchram āha: "kuśapalāśodumbarapadmaśaṅkhapuṣpīvaṭabrahamsuvarcalāpatraiḥ kvathitasyāmbhasaḥ pratyahaṃ pāne parṇakṛcchraḥ" iti | jābālis tv anyathāha |
palāśabilvapatrāṇāṃ patrāṇy audumbarāṇi ca |
aśvatthasya ca patrāṇi aśed ekaikaśas tathā ||
ahorātropavāsaś ca parṇakṛcchra udāhṛtaḥ |
anyajanmakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ kṛto nāśayate tu saḥ || iti |
śakhalikhitau: "padmabilvapalāśodumbarakuśodakāny ekaikam abhyastāni parṇakṛcchaḥ samastāny etāni trirātreṇopayuktāni ca parṇakṛcchraḥ" | [1235] yamaḥ |
palāśabilvaparṇāni kuśapadmāni vāgyataḥ |
ekaikaṃ tryaham aśnīyāt parṇakṛcchro vidhīyate || 3.316 ||
taptakṛcchram āha |
taptakṣīraghṛtāmbūnām ekaikaṃ pratyahaṃ pibet |
ekarātropavāsaś ca taptakṛcchras tu pāvanaḥ || 3.317 ||
uṣṇānāṃ kṣīrādīnām ekaikasya pratyahaṃ pānaṃ caturthe 'hny upavāsa iti taptakṛcchraḥ | laghupāpaviṣaye vihitas taptakṛcchro 'yam | gurupāpaviṣaye vihita[sya] svarūpam āha manuḥ |
taptakṛcchraṃ caran vipro jalakṣīraghṛtānilān |
pratitryahaṃ pibed uṣṇān sakṛt snāyī samāhitaḥ ||
uṣṇodakān nirgacchato bāṣpodakasya pānam uṣṇānilaṃ pānam |
ṣaṭpalaṃ tu pibed ambas tripalaṃ tu payaḥ pibet |
palam ekaṃ pibet sarpis taptakṛcchraṃ vidhīyate ||
viṣṇuḥ: "tryaham uṣṇāḥ pibed apaḥ | tryaham uṣṇaṃ ghṛtam | tryaham uṣṇaṃ payaḥ | tryahaṃ nāśnīyat | eṣa taptakṛcchraḥ" || 3.317 ||
pādakṛcchram āha |
ekabhaktena naktena tathaivāyācitena ca |
upavāsena caikena pādakṛcchra udāhṛtaḥ || 3.318 ||
ahorātramadhye divaikaikaṃ bhojanam ekabhaktam | evaṃ rātrāv ekaikaṃ bhojanaṃ naktam | aprārthitaṃ divā rātrau vaikarātrau caikaṃ bhojanam | ayācitae divārātram | na rātrau na divā bhojanam upavāsaḥ | evaṃ caturahaḥsādhyaḥ pādakṛcchraḥ | ekabhaktenetyādi viśeṣaṇe tṛtīyā | ekabhaktādau grāsasaṃkhyāniyamam āha parāśaraḥ |
sāyaṃ tu dvādaś grāsāḥ prātaḥ pañcadaśa smṛtāḥ |
caturviṃśatir ayācyāḥ paraṃ niraśanaṃ smṛtam || iti |
vṛddhaparāśaras tv anyathāha |
sāyaṃ dvāviṃśatir grāsāḥ prātaḥ ṣaḍviṃśatiḥ smṛtāḥ | [1236]
caturviṃśatir ayācyāḥ paraṃ niraśanaṃ smṛtam ||
kukkuṭāṇḍapramāṇāḥ syur yathā cāsye viśet sukham || iti |
anayoś grāsasaṃkhyāpramāṇavikalpayoḥ śaktyapekṣā vyavasthā | āpastambena tu pādakṛcchro 'nyathā vikalpitaḥ | yad āha |
tryahaṃ niraśanaṃ pādaḥ pādaś cāyācitas tryaham |
sāyaṃ tryahaṃ tathā pādaḥ pādaḥ prātas tathā tryaham || iti ||
eteṣāṃ catvāraḥ pādāḥ prativarṇaṃ vyavasthāpitā tenaiva |
prātaḥ pādaṃ carc chūdraḥ sāyaṃ vaiśyasya dāpayet |
ayācitaṃ tu rājanye trirātraṃ brāhmaṇe smṛtam || iti |
prātaḥ pāda ekabhaktatrayaṃ sāyaṃ pādo naktatrayam | ayācitam ayācitatrayam | trirātram upavāsatrayam ity arthaḥ | ayācitapādopavāsapādau kṛcchārdham | ardhakṛcchram ity arthaḥ | naktapādarahitaṃ pādatrayaṃ pādonaṃ kṛcchram iti | prakārāntareṇa naktakṛcchraṃ sa evāha |
sāyaṃ prātas tathaikaikaṃ dinadvayam ayācitam |
dinadvayaṃ ca nāśnīyāt kṛcchraṃ tadvad vidhīyate ||
sāyaṃ prātas tathaikaikabhojanam iti kṛcchrārdhayoś ca śaktyapekṣo vikalpaḥ | caturahātmakas tu pādakṛcchraḥ strībālavṛddhāturaviṣayo draṣṭavyaḥ | yad āha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
ahaḥ prātarahr naktam ahar ekam ayācitam |
ahaḥ paraṃ ca nāśnīyād ardhaṃ caturahaḥ param ||
anugrahārthaṃ viprāṇāṃ manur dharmabhṛtāṃ varaḥ |
bālavṛddhātureṣv evaṃ śiśukṛcchram uvāha ha || 3.318 ||
prājāptyam āha |
yathākathaṃcid triguṇaḥ prājāpatyo 'yam ucyate |
ayam eva pūrvoktaś caturahātmakaḥ pādakṛcchro yena kenacit prakāreṇa trirāvṛttaḥ prājāpatya ity ucyate | manuḥ |
tryahaṃ prātas tryahaṃ sāyaṃ tryaham adyād ayācitam |
tryahaṃ paraṃ ca nāśnīyāt prājāpatyaṃ caran dvijaḥ ||
atra gautamokto viśeṣaḥ: [1237] "athātaḥ kṛcchrān vyākhyāsyāmaḥ — haviṣyān prātarāśān bhuktvā tisro rātrīr nāśnīyāt | yathāparaṃ tryahaṃ naktaṃ bhuñjīta, paraṃ tyaham ayācitam athāparaṃ tryaham upavaset tiṣṭhed ahani rātrāv āsīta kṣiprakāmaḥ satyaṃ vaded anāryair na saṃbhāṣeta jaye nityaṃ prayuñjīta, anusavanam udakopasparśanam āpo hi ṣṭheti tisṛbhiḥ pavitravatībhir mārjayet | hiraṇyavarṇāḥ śucayaḥ pāvakā iti catasṛbhiḥ | athodakatarpaṇam | namo 'hamāya (?) namo haṃsāyordhvaretase tāpasāya punarvasave namo mauñjyāyonmāya vasuvindāya namaḥ parāya supārāya mahāpārāya pārayiṣṇave namo rudrāya paśupataye mahate devāya tryambakāyaikacarāyādhipataye harāya śarvāyeśānāyogrāya vajriṇe ghṛṇine kapardine namaḥ sūryāyādityāya namo nīlagrīvāya śitikaṇṭhāya namaḥ kṛṣṇāya piṅgalāya namo jyeṣṭhāya śreṣṭhāya vṛddhāyendrāya harikeśāyordhvaretase namaḥ satyāya pāvakāya pāvakavarṇāya kāmāya kāmarūpiṇe namo dīptāya dīptarūpiṇe namas tīkṣṇāya tīkṣṇarūpine namaḥ saumyāya puruṣāya mahāpuruṣāya madhyamapuruṣāyottamapuruṣāya brahmacāriṇe namaś candralalāṭāya kṛttivāsase nama iti | etad evādityopasthānam etā evājyāhutayo dvādaśarātrasyānte caruṃ śrapayitvā juhuyāt | agnaye svāha, agnīṣomābhyām indrāya viśebhyo devebhyo brahmaṇe prajāpataye 'gnaye sviṣṭakṛta iti | atha brāhmaṇabhojanam | etenaivātikṛcchro vyākhyātaḥ" iti | evaṃ samantrakā api kṛcchrāḥ śūdrasyāmantrakā bhavanti | aṅgirāḥ |
tasmāc chūdraṃ samāsādya sadā dharmapathe sthitam |
prāyaścittaṃ vaiśyaḥ prājāpatyaṃ tu śūdrakaḥ || iti | [1238]
śṛṅgagrāhitayā śūdrasya kṛcchrārdhavidhir bhavati | tataś ca niṣādasyeva śūdrasyāpy amantrakaṃ karma bhavati | striyo 'py amantrakam eva kuryuḥ | yatas tāsāṃ kṛcchravidhir dṛśyate |
prativarṇaṃ careyus tāḥ kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇottaram | iti |
atikṛcchram āha |
ayam evātikṛcchraḥ syāt pāṇipūrvānnabhojanaḥ || 3.319 ||
ayam eva prājāpatya evaikabhaktanaktācāciteṣu pāṇipūraṇaparimāṇānnabhojanaviśiṣṭopavāsa-trayavān ati kṛcchrasaṃjño bhavati | manuḥ |
ekaikaṃ grāsam aśnīyāt tryahāṇi trīṇi pūrvavat |
tryahaṃ copavased antyam atikṛcchraṃ caran dvijaḥ ||
pāṇipūragrāsayor vikalpaḥ śaktyapekṣaḥ pāpāpekṣo vā || 3.319 ||
kṛcchrātikṛcchraparākāv āha |
kṛcchrātikṛcchraḥ payasā divasān ekaviṃśatim |
dvādaśāhopavāsena parākaḥ parikīrtitaḥ || 3.320 ||
ekaviṃśatirātraṃ payovrataṃ kṛcchrātikṛcchraḥ | dvādaśarātram abhojanaṃ parākaḥ | gautamas tu kṛcchrātikṛcchrasya lakṣaṇāntara[m atikṛcchrāntara]m āha: "abbhakṣas tṛtīyaḥ sa kṛcchrātikṛcchraḥ" iti | atikṛcchra eva dviguṇaḥ kṛcchrātikṛcchra ity āha yamaḥ |
ekaikaṃ piṇḍam aśnīyāt tryahaṃ kālyaṃ tryahaṃ niśi |
ayācitas tryahaṃ piṇḍaṃ vāyubhakṣas tryahaṃ param ||
atikṛcchraṃ cared etat sarvapāpapraṇāśanam |
caturviṃśatirātraṃ tu niyatātmā jitendriyaḥ ||
kṛcchrātikṛcchraṃ kurvīta ekasthāne dvijottamaḥ || iti |
ekasthānavacanāc caturviṃśatirātrasādhya eṣa eva kṛcchra iti gamayati || 3.320 ||
saumyam āha |
piṇyākācāmatakrāmbusaktūnāṃ prativāsaram |
ekarātropavāsaś ca saumyaḥ kṛcchro 'yam ucyate || 3.321 ||
piṇyākādīnāṃ pañcānāṃ dravyāṇām ekaikena dravyeṇaikaikam ahar ativāhya ṣaṣṭhe 'hany upavāsaḥ kriyata ity eṣa saumyasaṃjñakaḥ kṛcchraḥ | pāvana ity anuvartate | tenātraiva [1239] vākye kṛcchrasvarūpaṃ vidhīyate | tatraiva pāpakṣayārthatvena viniyojyata iti mantavyam | piṇyākaṃ piṇḍī | ācāma odanāsrāvaḥ | prasiddham anyat | jābālas tv anyathāha |
piṇyākaṃ saktavas takraṃ caturthe 'hny abhojanam |
vāso vai dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt saumyo 'yaṃ kṛccha ucyate || iti || 3.321 ||
tulāpuruṣam āha |
eṣāṃ trirātram abhyāsād ekaikasya yathākramam |
tulāpuruṣa ity eṣa jñeyaḥ pañcadaśāhikaḥ || 3.322 ||
eṣāṃ piṇyākādīnām ekaikasya trirātropayogābhyāse pañcadaśāhikas tulāpuruṣākhyaḥ kṛcchro bhavati | yamaḥ punar ekaviṃśatirātrasādhyaṃ tulāpuruṣam āha |
ācāmam atha piṇyākaṃ takraṃ codakasaktavaḥ |
tryahaṃ tryahaṃ prayuñjāno vāyubhakṣaḥ paraṃ tryaham ||
ekaviṃśatirātras tu tulāpuruṣa ucyate || iti |
ācāmādīnāṃ pañcānām ekaikasya trirātrābhyāse pañcadaśāhāni bhavanti, trirātrikasya vāyubhakṣasya dvirabhyāse 'hānīty evam ekaviṃśtirātrikas tulāpuruṣaḥ ṣaḍrātreṇapy dṛśyate |
prathame 'hani nāśnīyāt samyak kṛcchreṣu sarvadā |
ataḥ palāni catvāni piṇyākasya tu bhakṣayet ||
nīvārataṇḍulācāmaṃ tṛtīyo 'hani tatsamam |
pibet takraṃ caturthe tu pañcame tu jalaṃ kramāt ||
gomūtrayāvakodbhūtāt prāśya saktūṃs tu tatsamān |
iti smṛtyantarānusārāt | atra hārītokto viśeṣaḥ: "athātas trinayanoktasya tulāpuruṣasya kalpaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ | atha ca —
pratibandhādibhir dharmaiḥ prasūtānāṃ viyoniṣu |
ayājyayājanaṃ kṛtvā prāśya mūtrapurīṣayoḥ ||
apratigraham ādāya yājayitvā tu ninditān |
vināyakopasṛṣṭasya mahāvyādhihatasya ca || [1240]
tulāpuruṣakaṃ kṛcchraṃ mahāpātakanāśanam |
svargadvāram idaṃ puṇyaṃ mahādevena nirmitam ||
ācāmam atha piṇyākaṃ takraṃ codakasaktukān |
tryahaṃ tryahaṃ prayuñjāno vāyubhakṣaḥ paraṃ tryaham ||
vākpādatailarajobhir hitāni nivṛttāni ca |
ācāmas tāni nirhanti navaśrāddhaṃ ca sūtakam ||
tailasya dhāraṇaṃ kiṃcit keśakīṭahataṃ ca yat |
prāśya mūtrapurīṣaṃ ca brahmahaspṛṣṭam eva ca ||
piṇyākaṃ tāni nirhanti asthi bhittvā ca yatsthitam |
ākareṣu ca ye doṣāḥ snehadoṣāś ca ye kvacit ||
kharoṣṭramukhasaṃspṛṣṭaṃ madyasaṃspṛṣṭam eva ca |
takreṇa tāni nirhanti yaś cādyāc chvāvalokitam ||
kanakaṃ ca tilā gāvo bhūmir ācchādanaṃ striyaḥ |
sarvaṃ punāti dharmātmā gūḍhaṃ codakasaktubhiḥ ||
brahmahatyāṃ bhrūṇahatyāṃ suvarṇasteyaṃ surāpānaṃ gurudārābhigamanaṃ kanyādūṣaṇaṃ klībābhigamanaṃ vā vāyubhakṣaḥ punāti tat |
gatvvośīrasamau karyau puruṣau parṇasaṃmitau |
nadīmṛttikayā vāpi puruṣau dvau tu kārayet ||
mahāpaśur yena śareṇa viddho yaś (yac) cāpi śastram ubhayor labdhalakṣyam |
tenāyasīṃ sudṛḍhāṃ kārayitvā prādeśamātram ubhayoḥ kṛttaśīrṣām ||
sauvarṇīṃ rājatīṃ vāpi tulādevīṃ tu kārayet |
api candanavṛkṣasy badarasyāpi kārayet ||
tasyālābe tulā kāryā yathā vā hy upapadyate |
yaḥ puruṣa piṅgalo babhrur halamusalaśūlavajrapāṇis tam ahaṃ puruṣaṃ piṅgalaṃ babhruhalamusalavajraśūlapāṇim āvāhayiṣyāmi svata eva sūnṛta eva hi svadhāma ehi kṛṣṇājina ehi svāgatam anusvāgataṃ bhavate tulāpuruṣāya mahādevāyedam arghyam idaṃ pādyam idam ācamanīyaṃ ca prasīda gandhamālyadhūpadīpajapyaṃ [1241] pratigṛhāṇa, yed enobhyo mama kruddhaḥ sumanā bhava | atha tulām abhimantrayet | ṛṣisatyaṃ devasatyaṃ brahmasatyaṃ yat satyaṃ etna satyena satyavādini deśānāṃ saṃpadaṃ brūhi kiṃ bījaṃ vardhyiṣyasi |
rājñāṃ ca saṃpadaṃ brūhi kaṃ nṛpaṃ vardhyiṣyasi |
asmākaṃ saṃpadaṃ brūhi kaṃ nṛpaṃ vardhayiṣyasi ||
yadā ca gurum ātmānam asakṛt tulayā dhṛtam |
paraṃ laghuṃ ca manyeta tadā karma samārabhet ||
agnaye pṛthivyadhipataye svāhā | vāyave 'ntarikṣādhipataye svāhā | sūryāya divo 'dhipataye svāhā | somāya nakṣatrādhipataye svāhā | vāsukāya nāgādhipataye svāhā | takṣakāya sarpādhipataye svāhā | kuberāya yakṣādhipataye svāhā | indrāya devādhipataye svāhā | skandāya senādhipataye svāhā | brahmaṇe trailokyādhipataye svāhā | devyai bhagādhipataye svāhā | sālakaṭaṃkaṭāy kūṣmāṇḍarājaputrāya svāhā | mahāvināyakāya vakratuṇḍāya gaṇādhipataye svāhā | dharmāya svāha | tulāpuruṣāya svāhā |prajādidevo 'yaṃ marudbhiḥ sadaiva punar āgamanāya svāhā | akṛc caritvā śuciḥ karmaṇyo bhavati | dviḥ kṛtvā gāṇapatyam avāpnoti | triḥ kṛtvā mahādevasyāvasathyaṃ prāpnoti | āṣāḍhyāṃ kārtikyāṃ phālgunyāṃ puṇye vā nakṣatra eṣa vihito dharmaḥ" iti |
anye 'pi kṛcchrā likhyante | tatra jābāliḥ |
anaśnañ jalamadhyasthas tv ahorātraṃ kṣiped budhaḥ |
saṃvatsarakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ jalakṛcchro vyapohati ||
viṣṇuḥ: "udakasaktūnāṃ māsābhyavahāreṇodakakṛcchraḥ | bisābhyavahāreṇa śītakṛcchraḥ" | śaṅkhaḥ |
śaṅgāṭādīna bisāny udakasiddhāni māsam aśnīta saṃyataḥ |
sakṛd vāsodakaṃ māsaṃ kṛcchro vāruṇa ucyate || [1242]
bilvair āmalakair vāpi padmākṣair atha vā śubhaiḥ |
māsena loke śrīkṛcchraḥ kathyate dvijasattamaiḥ || 3.322 ||
uktāḥ kṛcchrāḥ | cāndrāyaṇādīnīdānīm āha |
tithivṛddhyā caret piṇḍāñ śukle śikhyaṇḍasaṃmitān |
ekaikaṃ hrāsayet kṛṣṇe piṇḍaṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ caran || 3.323 ||
cāndrāyaṇākhyaṃ tapaḥ kurvāṇo mayūrāṇdaparimitā[n piṇḍā]ñ śuklapakṣe tithivṛddhau cared bhakṣayet | tithiṣu pratipaddvitīyādiṣu candrakalānāṃ saṃkhyāvṛddhis tithivṛddhiśabdena vivkṣitā | tasyāś ca tulyāṃ grāsasaṃkhyām āpādayantyāḥ piṇḍacaraṇe kāraṇatvaṃ pauruṣeyavākyānāṃ(ṇāṃ) mānāntarato 'vasitārthānāṃ padeṣu sāpekṣasamāsāsamarthasamāsatvādikaṃ codyam ācodyam | tathā paurṇamāsyāṃ pañcadaśa grasitvā kṛṣṇapakṣapratipatprabhṛty ekaikaṃ grāsaṃ hrāsayet | kṛṣṇacaturdaśyām ekaṃ grāsaṃ grasitvāmāvāsyāyām upavaset | etad yavamadhyaṃ cāndrāyaṇam | vasiṣṭhaḥ |
māsasya kṛṣṇapakṣādau grāsān adyāc caturdaśa |
grāsāpacayabhojī ca pakṣam ekaṃ samāpayet ||
tathaiva śuklapakṣādau grāsaṃ bhuñjīta cāparam |
grāsopacayabhojī san pakṣaśeṣaṃ samāpayet ||
gautamaḥ punar yavamadhyaṃ cāndrāyaṇam anyathāha: "athātaś cāndrāyaṇam | tasyokto vidhiḥ kṛcchre | vapanaṃ vrataṃ caret | śvobhūtāṃ paurṇamāsīm upavaset | āpyāyasva saṃ te payāṃśi navo nava ity teābhis tarpaṇam ājyahomo haviṣaś cānumantraṇam upasthānaṃ candramado yad devā devaheḍanam iti catasṛbhir ājyaṃ juhuyād devakṛtasyeti cānte samidbhiḥ | oṃ bhūḥ oṃ bhuvaḥ oṃ svaḥ oṃ mahaḥ oṃ janaḥ oṃ tapaḥ oṃ satyam | oṃ yaśaḥ oṃ śrīḥ oṃ ūrk oṃ iḍā oṃ ojaḥ oṃ tajaḥ oṃ puruṣaḥ oṃ dharmaḥ oṃ śiva ity etair grāsānumantraṇaṃ pratimantraṃ manasā namaḥ svāheti vā sarvān | grāsapramāṇam āsyāvikāreṇa | carubhaikṣasaktukaṇayāvaka-śākapayodadhighṛtamūlaphalodakāni [1243] havīṃśy uttarottarapraśastāni | paurṇamāsyāṃ pañcadaśa grāsān bhuktvaikāpacayenāparapakṣam aśnīyāt | amāvāsyāyām upoṣyaikopacayena pūrvapakṣaviparītam ekeṣām eva cāndrāyaṇo māsaḥ | etad aptvā vipāpma sarvam eno hanti | dvitīyam āptvā daśa pūrvān daśāparān ātmānaṃ caikaviśaṃ paṅktiṃ ca punāti | tṛtīyam āptvā candramasaḥ salokatām āpnoti" |
asyārthaḥ — asya cāndrāyaṇasyokto vidhir itikartavyatā kṛcchreṇa vapanaṃ vrataṃ caret | prāyaścittaṃ caret | śvobhūtāṃ paurṇamāsīm upavaset | śuklacaturdaśyām upavased ity uktaṃ bhavatīti caturdaśyām upavāsaḥ | paurṇamāsyāṃ pañcadaśagrāsagrasanaṃ prāyikam anyathā cāndrāyaṇaśabdo na syāt | grāsaś candrodaye grasanīyaḥ | yad āha hārītaḥ: "tṛtīyasavane cāndramasaṃ caruṃ śrapayitvā navo nava iti hutvā jyotsnāyāṃ caruśeṣān piṇḍān sāvitryabhimantritān prāśnīyāt" iti | grāsagrasanasamaye yā tithis tadanurūpā grāsasaṃkhyopādeyā | jyotsnāśabdena candrodaye lakṣitaḥ | tena rātrau divā vā candrodaya eva grāsā grasanīyāḥ | amāvāsyāyāṃ candrodayābhāvād abhojanam | yamaḥ |
vardhamāne yathā some grāsā vardhanti vai samāḥ |
hīyamāne tu hīyante tatkṣaye tu na bhujyate || iti |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
ekavāsāś cared dīkṣāṃ snātvā vāso na dīḍayet |
gāyatryā daśasāhasram āhnikaṃ sarvadā caret ||
yavamadhyam uktaṃ pipīlikāmadhyam api bhavati yadi kṛṣṇapratipady ārambhaḥ | tad āha manuḥ |
ekaikaṃ hrāsayet piṇḍaṃ śukle kṛṣṇe ca vardhayet |
upaspṛśaṃs triṣavaṇam etac cāndrāyaṇaṃ smṛtam ||
tathā |
aṣṭāv aṣṭau samaśnīyāt piṇḍān madhyaṃdine sthite |
niyatātmā haviṣyasya yaticāndrāyaṇaṃ caran ||
caturaḥ prātar aśnīyāt piṇḍān vipraḥ samāhitaḥ |
caturo 'stam ite sūrye śuśucāndrāyaṇaṃ smṛtam ||
bṛhadviṣṇuḥ | [1244]
trīṃs trīn piṇḍān samaśnīyān niyatātmā dṛḍhavrataḥ |
haviṣyānnasyārdhamāsam ṛṣicāndrāyaṇaṃ smṛtam ||
śaṅkhaḥ |
ārdrāmalakamātrās tu grāsā induvrate sthitāḥ |
tathaivāhutayas tatra grāsārthe caiva mṛttikāḥ || 3.323 ||
cāndrāyaṇtaram āha |
yathākathaṃcit piṇḍānāṃ catvāriṃśac chatadvayam |
māsenaivopayuñjīta cāndrāyaṇam athāparam || 3.324 ||
yathākathaṃcid divasaṃ pratyaniyatatayā grāsasaṃkhyā māsenaiva triṃśataiva tithibhir grāsānāṃ śatadvayaṃ catvāriṃśadadhikam upayuñjītādyāt | etad aparaṃ pūrvoktāc cāndrāyaṇāt | atra ca cāndrāyaṇe pūrvaprakrāntam ekādaśyupavāsādivrataṃ na lupyate, saṃkhyāviśeṣo hi māsasaṃpādyatayātra vidhīyate, na cāndrāyaṇāntaravat pratyekadivasasaṃpādyatayā | śaknoti caikādaśīm upavasann api māsasaṃpadyāṃ catvāriṃśacchatadvayātmikāṃ saṃkhyāṃ saṃpādayituṃ cāndrāyaṇānteṣu yady ekādaśīm upavaset tannimittāṃ grāsāśritām ekādaśasaṃkhyāṃ naiva saṃpādayed eva kiṃ tu vinipātayed eva | yady api grāsagrasanam avidheyaṃ rāgataḥ prāptatvāt tathāpi tadāśritāṃ saṃkhyāṃ cāndrāyaṇavidhir viṣayī karoty eva | yathā — daeśapūrṇamāsavidhivyāpārataḥ prāptyānuṣṭhānaṃ godohanaṃ paśukāmavidhiḥ | tatra yadi darśapūrṇamāsaprayuktam apāṃ praṇayanaṃ nāsti, tadā — "godohanena paśukāmasya praṇayet" ity eṣa śastrātho na sidhyati | tathaikādaśyādau grāsāgrasane tadāśritasaṃkhyāviśeṣaviṣayaś cāndrāyaṇavidhir na sidhyaty eva | atrāpi hi rāgaprāptabhojanāśritagrāsasaṃkhyāviśeṣalakṣaṇo guṇaḥ pāpakṣayādiphalārthatvena vidhīyate | tena cāndrāyaṇam athāparam ity etasmād anyeṣu cāndrāyaṇeṣu yadaikādaśyupavāsādy ādriyante na tadā cāndrāyaṇavidhisiddhir iti | yadā pañca grāsā grasyante tadā prāṇāya svāhā, apānāya svāhetyādi paṇca mantrā nityavad bhinnaviṣayatvena kriyante | yadā pañcasaṃkhyāto nyūnasaṃkhyakā grāsās tadā katham ity apekṣite baudhāyana āha: "prāśnīyāt prāṇāyeti prathamam | apānāyeti dvitīyam | vyānāyeti tṛtīyam | udānāyeti caturtham | samānāyeti [1245] pañcamam | yadā catvāras tadā dvābhyāṃ pūrvaṃ yadā trayas tadā dvābhyāṃ pūrvau yadā dvau tadā dvābhyām evottram ekaṃ sarvaiḥ" iti | asyārthaḥ — yadā caturgrāsapakṣas tadādyadvitīyābhyāṃ mantrābhyāṃ prathamaṃ grāsaṃ graset | grāsatrayapakṣa ādyābhyāṃ prathamaṃ tṛtīyacaturthābhyāṃ dvitīyaṃ pañcamena tṛtīyaṃ, grāsadvaye tu tribhiḥ prathamam antimābhyāṃ dvābhyāṃ dvitīyam ekasmin prāpte sarvair ekam iti || 3.324 ||
kṛcchracāndrāyaṇasādhāraṇām itikartavyatām āha |
kuryāt triṣavaṇasnāyī kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ tathā |
pavitrāṇi japet piṇḍān gāyatryā cābhimantrayet || 3.325 ||
triṣavaṇasnāyī prātramadhaṃdināparāhṇeṣu snānāny ācaran kṛcchrāṇi sāṃtapanādīni cāndrāyaṇaṃ ca kuryāt | pavitrāṇi gāyatryādīni ṛgyajuḥsāmasu vyavasthitāni yathāsvaśākhaṃ japet | gāyatryā ca piṇḍān grāsān abhimantrayet | gautamoktaiś ca grāsābhimantraṇamantrair gāyatrī vikapyate | yad vā "āpyāyasva same tu te" ityādikaṃ mantratrayaṃ gāyatrī tu (ca) grāsānumantraṇaviṣayā, tena(nā)bhinnaviṣayatād yuktāḥ samuccayaḥ | grāsānumantraṇe ca viśeṣa ukto yamena |
aṅgulyagrasthitaṃ piṇḍaṃ gāyatryā cābhimantritam |
prāśyācamya punaḥ kuryād anyasyāpy abhimantraṇam || iti |
"sakṛtsnāyī jitendriyaḥ" iti manuvacanena taptakṛcchratriṣavaṇasnāyitvaṃ bādhyate | yat tu kṛcchrāntareṣu,
trir ahnas trir niṣāyāṃ tu savāsā jalam āviśet |
iti, tat tu triṣavaṇasnānena vikalpyate | tatra śaktaṃ phalātirekakāmaṃ vā prati snānaṣaṭkavidhiḥ | anyaṃ prati tu triṣavaṇavidhiḥ | triṣavaṇe 'py asamarthasya snānadvayam āha vaiśaṃpāyanaḥ |
snānaṃ dvikālam eva syāt trikālaṃ vā dvijanmanaḥ | iti |
snāne tu hārītokto viśeṣaḥ: "tryavaraṃ śuddhavatībhiḥ snātvāghamarṣaṇaṃ jale japitvā dhautam ahataṃ vāsaḥ paridhāya sāmnāṃ saumyenādityam upatiṣṭheta" iti | [1246] yat tu gautamenoktam: "rauravayodhā japen nityaṃ prayuñjīta" iti, tat sāmagaviṣayam | na hi prāyaścittārthavad adhyayanam asti | na ca sāmavedina eva cāndrāyaṇe 'dhikāra iti vaktavyam | ṛgvedayajurvedavartipavitrajapyasyāpi cāndrāyaṇāṅgatvena darśanāt | ye 'pi gautamena home namo 'hamāyetyevamādayo mantrā vihitās te 'pi na nityāḥ kiṃ tu pākṣikāḥ | yato manuḥ,
mahāvyāhṛtibhir homaḥ kartavyaḥ svayam anvaham |
iti mantrāntarāṇy āha | mahāvyahṛtayaś ca bhūrādyās tisras tad āha manur eva |
oṃkārapūrvikās tisro mahāvyāhṛtayo 'vyayāḥ | iti |
mahāvyāhṛtīnāṃ ca tritvaṃ japa eva, home to vyastāḥ prayojyāḥ | samastā api prayojyāḥ śāstrāntaradarśanāt | cāndrāyaṇe ca kṛcchroktetikartavyatā sarvaiva grāhyā | tad āha gautamaḥ: "atha cāndrāyaṇaṃ tasyokto vidhiḥ" | kṛcchre 'tijape ca saṃkhyāṃ vaiśaṃpāyana āha |
ṛṣabhaṃ ca virājaṃ ca tathā caivāghamarṣaṇam |
gāyatrīṃ vā japed devīṃ pavitrāṃ vedamātaram ||
śatam aṣṭaśataṃ vāpi sahasram api vā param || iti |
cāndrāyaṇānte ca tadaṅgatvena ca dhenur deyā viprāś ca bojyāḥ, yad āha yamaḥ |
evaṃ cāndrāyaṇe cīrṇe dhenuṃ dadyāt payasvinīm |
brāhmaṇān bhojayec chaktyā tebhyo dadyāc ca dakṣiṇām || 3.325 ||
cāndrāyaṇaviniyogam āha |
anādiṣṭeṣu pāpeṣu śuddhiś cāndrāyaṇena tu |
anādiṣṭeṣv anupadiṣṭaprāyaścitteṣu pāpeṣu cāndrāyaṇena śuddhiḥ | na kevalaṃ cāndrāyaṇadvayenaiva kṛcchrātikṛcchābhyām api, yad āha vasiṣṭhaḥ: "kṛcchrātikṛcchrau cāndrāyaṇam iti sarvaprāyaścittiḥ" iti | pāpagaurave kṛcchrātikṛcchasahitaṃ cāndrāyaṇam anyatra kevalam | sarvaprāyaścittatve 'pi pratipāpaṃ bhedena kṛcchrādīnāṃ prayogaḥ | nimittabhedasya naimittikaprayogabhedahetutvāt | naimittikatayā tu kṛcchrādiṣv anuṣṭheyeṣu prakrānteṣu kartur asāmarthyopanipāte 'nugrahān smṛtikārā āhuḥ | [1247]
athāto 'nugrahān vakṣye durbalasyātmaśīlinaḥ |
yat kṛtvā mucyate pāpād uragaḥ kañcukā yathā ||
dvādaśaiva sahasrāṇi japed devīm upoṣitaḥ |
jalānte vidhivan maunī prājāptyo 'yam ucyate ||
samid ghṛtaṃ havir dhānyaṃ tilān vā mārutāśanaḥ |
hutvā dvādaśasāhasraṃ gāyatryāḥ kṛcchram āpnuyāt ||
dvādaśabhir adhikaṃ sāhasraṃ dvādaśasāhasram | sahasram eva sāhasram |
annadānahiraṇyena dvādaśa brāhmaṇāñ śucīn |
tarpayen mārutāśī ca śrotiryān kṛcchra ucyate ||
upoṣya śraddhayā yuktas tilapātrāṇi dharmataḥ |
dvādaśabrahmavādibhyaḥ prājāpatyena tatsamam ||
svayam āhṛtya yo mūrdhnā tṛṇabhārān upoṣitaḥ |
dadyād gomaṇḍale kṛcchre dvādaśaiva na saṃśayaḥ ||
prāṇāyāmaśataṃ kṛtvā dvātriṃśottaram ārtiṣu |
ahorātroṣitas tiṣṭhet prāṅmukhaḥ kṛcchra ucyate ||
namaskārasahasrāṇi dvādaśaiva dṛḍhavrataḥ |
goviprapitṛdeveṣu kuryāt kṛcchratrayaṃ bhavet ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
atha cet tvarate kartuṃ divasaṃ mārutāśanaḥ |
rātrau sthitvā jape vyuṣṭaḥ prājāptyena tatsamam ||
gomūtreṇa samāyuktaṃ yāvakaṃ copayojayet |
kṛcchram aikāhikaṃ proktaṃ dṛṣṭam aṅgirasā svayam ||
niśy āsīno divā tiṣṭhet trirātraṃ mārutāśanaḥ |
prājāpatyaṃ vijānīyāt kūṣmāṇḍair juhuyād ghṛtam ||
pratyabdaṃ ya imān kryād vasante 'nugrahān parān |
sa yāti brahasadanaṃ tejomūrtiḥ pṛthurjanaḥ ||
cāndrāyaṇe trayaḥ kṛcchāṃs triṃśatsāhasriko japaḥ ||
parāśaraḥ |
kṛcchro 'yutaṃ tu gāyatryā upavāsas tathaiva ca |
dhenupradānaṃ viprāya samam etac catuṣṭayam || [1248]
etac catuṣṭayaṃ samaṃ prājāpatyenety arthaḥ | pracetāḥ: "saṃhitādhyayanaṃ yāvantaṃ kālaṃ gāyatrīṃ japet" | caturviṃśatimatāt |
viprā dvādaśa vai bhojyāḥ pāvakeṣṭis tathaiva ca |
anyā vā pātanī kācit samam āhur manīṣiṇaḥ ||
tathā |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ mṛgārī(re)ṣṭiḥ pavitreṣṭis tathaiva ca |
mitravindā paśuś caiva kṛcchraṃ māsadvayaṃ tathā ||
nityanaimittikānāṃ ca kāmyānāṃ caiva karmaṇām |
iṣṭīnāṃ paśubandhānām abhāve caravaḥ smṛtāḥ ||
cāndrāyaṇaṃ tribhiḥ kṛcchraiḥ parākas tu dvayena ca |
etat kṛcchrapramāṇaṃ ca vidvadbhiḥ parikīrtitam ||
cāndrāyaṇam akurvāṇāḥ kuryuḥ kṛcchracatuṣṭayam ||
tathā |
cāndrāyaṇaparākābhyāṃ niṣkṛtiṃ yo 'nuśaknuyāt |
sa kuryād atmaśuddhyarthaṃ prājāpatyaṃ to pañcamam ||
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ |
prājāpatyasamā dhenus taddvayaṃ taptakṛcchrake |
parāke tu suvarṇaṃ syād dhemaśṛṅgī tathaindave ||
taptakṛcchro mahātaptakṛcchraḥ | parāke suvarṇaṃ dhenudvaysya mūlyaṃ samam | hemaśṛṅgīgrahaṇena sakalakāṃsyadohādyupaskaravatīṃ dhenuṃ lakṣayati |
aṣṭau cāndrāyaṇe deyāḥ pratyāmānāyavidhau sadā |
dhenava iti śeṣaḥ | kṛcchrāṣṭakasaṃpādyacāndrāyaṇaviṣayam etat | caturviṃśatimatāt |
prājāpatye tu gām ekāṃ dadyāt sāṃtapane dvayam |
parāke taptakṛcchre ca tisras tisras tu gās tathā ||
ṣaṭtriṃśanmatāt |
parākataptātikṛcchrasthāne kṛcchratrayaṃ caret |
sāṃtapanasya cādhyardham aśaktau vratam ācaret || [1249 |
upavāsaś ca gāyatryāḥ sahasraṃ viprabhojanam |
prāṇāyāmā daś dvau ca samam etac catuṣṭayam ||
kṛcchraṃ devyayutaṃ caiva prāṇāyāmaśatadvayam |
viprā dvādaśa vā bhojyā mukkā(mṛgā)reṣṭis tathaiva ca ||
anyā vā pāvanī kācit samāny āhur manīṣiṇaḥ |
tilahomāyutaṃ caiva parākadvayam eva ca ||
gāyatryā lakṣam ekaṃ ca samāny āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
atikṛcchre parāke ca taptakṛcchre tathaiva ca ||
prājāpatyatrayaṃ kuryāt kṛcchre gomithunaṃ bhavet ||
cāndrāyaṇasya phalāntaram āha |
dharmārthaṃ yaś cared etac candrasyaiti salokatām || 3.326 ||
dharmārtham abhyudayārtham etac cāndrāyaṇaṃ yaś cared anutiṣṭhet sa candrasya salokatāṃ samānalokatām eti || 3.326 ||
kṛcchrakṛd dharmakāmas tu mahatīṃ śriyam āpnuyāt |
yathāguru kratuphalaṃ prāpnoti ca samāhitaḥ || 3.327 ||
dharmo me bhūyād ity evaṃ kāmayamāno yaḥ kṛcchrasāṃtapanādikṛcchrakārī syāt sa mahatīṃ sakalalokaspṛhaṇīyām śriyaṃ saṃpadaṃ prāpnuyāt | yas tu gurukṛcchrakṛt syād yo guruḥ suduṣkarakṛcchras taṃ tam ādarād yathāśāstraṃ samāhitas tātparyavān kuryāt, sa kratūnāṃ jyotiṣṭomādīnāṃ phalaṃ svargādikam āpnoti labhate | viśvāmitraḥ |
kṛcchracāndrāyaṇādīni śuddhyaty (yanty) ubhayakāriṇām (?) |
prakāśe ca rahasye ca anukte saṃśaye sphuṭe ||
prājāpatyaḥ sāṃtapanaḥ śuśukṛcchraḥ parākakaḥ |
atikṛcchraḥ parṇakṛcchraḥ saumyaḥ kṛcchrātikṛcchrakaḥ ||
mahāsāmtapanaḥ śuddhyai taptakṛcchras tu pāvanaḥ |
jalopavāsakṛcchras tu brahmakṛcchras tu śodhakaḥ ||
phalakṛcchro mūlakṛcchraḥ pratyekam ekaśo 'pi ca |
pātakādiṣu sarveṣām upapāpeṣu yatnataḥ ||
kāryāś cāndrāyaṇair yuktāḥ kevalā vā viśuddhaye |
śuśucāndrāyanaṃ proktaṃ yaticāndrāyaṇaṃ tathā || [1250]
yavamadhyaṃ tathā proktaṃ tathā pipīlakākṛti |
upavāsas tadardhaṃ vā māsaḥ pakṣas tadardhakam ||
ṣaḍaho dvādaśāhādi kāryasiddhiḥ(ddhi) phalārthinī(ni) |
upapātakayuktānām anādiṣṭeṣu caiva hi ||
prakāśe ca rahasye ca abhisaṃdhyādyapekṣayā |
śaktiṃ jātiṃ guṇaṃ dṛṣṭvā sakṛd buddhikṛtaṃ tathā ||
anubandhādikaṃ dṛṣṭvā. kalpyaṃ sarvaṃ yathāgamam ||
atha brahmakūrcasvarūpaṃ kathyate | tatra parāśaraḥ |
upavāsam ahorātraṃ brahmakūrcaṃ vinirdiśet |
pañcagavyaṃ ca varṇebhyo vihitaṃ divyacakṣuṣā ||
pavitraṃ triṣu lokeṣu devatābhir adhiṣṭhitam |
varuṇaś caiva gomūtre gomaye havyavāhanaḥ ||
dadhni vāyuḥ samuddiṣṭaḥ somaḥ kṣīre ghṛte raviḥ |
payaḥ kāñcanavarṇāyāḥ śvetāyāś caiva gomayam ||
gomūtraṃ tāmravarṇāyā nīlāyāś ca tathā ghṛtam |
dadhi vai kṛṣṇavarṇāyā ity etat pañcagavyakam ||
gomūtramāṣakās tv aṣṭau gomayasya tu doṣaśa |
kṣīrasya dvādaśa proktā dadhnas tu daś kīrtitāḥ ||
gomūtravad ghṛtasyoktās tadardhaṃ tu kuśodakam |
etad dravparī(ri)māṇaṃ śudhyate kāyaśodhanam ||
gāyatryāgṛhya gomūtraṃ gandhadvāreti gomayam |
āpyāyasveti ca kṣīraṃ dadhikrāvṇaṛcā dadhi ||
tejo 'si śukram ity ājyaṃ devasya tvā kuśodakam |
āpo hi ṣṭheti cālokya praṇavena tataḥ pibet ||
madhamena palāśasya padmapatreṇa vā pibet |
atha vā tāmrapatreṇa brahmapātrāṇi tāni vai ||
yat tvagasthigataṃ pāpaṃ dehe tiṣṭhati mānave |
brahmakūrco dahet sarvaṃ śuṣkam agnir ivendhanam || iti |
atha somāyanam | tatra mārkaṇḍeyaḥ |
gokṣīraṃ saptarātraṃ tu pibet stanacatuṣṭayāt |
stanatrayāt saptarātraṃ saptarātraṃ stanadvayāt ||
stanāt tathaiva ṣaḍrātraṃ trirātraṃ vāyubhug bhavet |
etat somāyanaṃ nāma vrataṃ kalamaṣanāśanam ||
vyāsaḥ |
śrīkāmaḥ puṣṭikāmaś ca svargakāmas tathaiva ca |
devatārādhanaparas tathā kṛcchraṃ samācaret ||
rasāyanāni mantrāś ca tathā caivauṣadhāni ca |
tasya sarvāṇi sidhyanti yo naraḥ kṛcchrabug bhavet ||
vaidikāni ca sarvāṇi yāni kāmyāni kānicit |
sidhyanti sarvadā tāni kṛcchrakartur na saṃśayaḥ ||
pāpātmānas tu pāpebhyaḥ kṛcchre saṃtāpitā narāḥ |
gatapāpā divaṃ yānti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ||
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ |
ajñātaṃ yadi vā jñātaṃ kṛcchraḥ pāpaṃ viśodhayet |
kṛcchreṇa śuddhapāpānāṃ narako na vidhīyate || 3.327 ||
pūrvoktasakalaśāstrārthībhūtān varṇāśramādidharmān ākarṇya yad āhur maharṣayas tad idānīm āha |
śrutvaitān ṛṣayo dharmāṇ yājñavalkyena bhāṣitān |
idam ūcur mahātmānaṃ yogīndram amitaujasam || 3.328 ||
ya idaṃ dhārayiṣyanti dharmaśāstram atandritāḥ |
iha loke yaśaḥ  prāpya te yāsyanti triviṣṭapam || 3.329 ||
vidyārthī prāpnuyād vidyāṃ dhanakāmo dhanaṃ tathā |
āyuṣkāmas tathaivāyuḥ śrīkāmo mahatīṃ śriyam || 3.330 ||
yājñavalkyena saṃsiddhayogabalaśālinā sakalaśrutyādividyāsthānatattvārthavedinā sakalalokānugrahapravaṇaviśuddhābhisaṃdhinā bhāṣitān vividhān dharmāñ śrutvā granthato 'rthataś cāvadhārya vakṣyamāṇaṃ vaco mahātmānaṃ rāgadveṣāsisakaladoṣavinirmuktaṃ yogīndraṃ yogīśvarasaṃmitasāmarthyam ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ | tad yathā — ye dvijā atandritā analasā idaṃ bhavatā praṇītaṃ dharmaśāstraṃ dharyiṣyanti guṇanādivyāpāreṇa paṭhiṣyanti te 'traiva loke mahad anarghaṃ yaśaḥ prāpya dehatyāgottarakālaṃ [1252] triviṣṭapaṃ yāsyantīti tathānumanyatām | tathedaṃ śāstram adhīyāno yadā vidyādikāmas tadā vidyādīn prāpnuyāt | dhanaṃ suvarṇādi | śrīḥ punar anekavidhā | brahmavarcasātmikā brāhmī | aparibhavapāṭavalakṣaṇā tu kṣattrī | dhanalakṣaṇā tu vaiśyaśrīḥ | tena dhanapadopādānāt pṛthakśriyam ity upāttam || 3. 328, 329, 330 ||
etad eva vivṛṇoti |
ślokatrayam api hy asmād yaḥ śrāddhe śrāvayiṣyati |
pitṛṇāṃ tasya tṛptiḥ syād akṣayā nātra saṃśayaḥ || 3.331 ||
brāhmaṇaḥ pātratāṃ yāti kṣatriyo vijayī bhavet |
vaiśyas tu dhānyadhanavān asya śāstrasya dhāraṇāt || 3.332 ||
ya idaṃ śrāvayet sarvān dvijān parvasu parvasu |
aśvamedhaphalaṃ tasya tad bhavān anumanyatām || 3.333 ||
parva pañcadaśī | prasiddham anyat || 3.331, 332, 333 ||
śrutvaitad yājñavalkyo 'pi prītātmā munibhāṣitam |
evam astv iti hovāca namaskṛtya svayaṃbhuve || 3.334 ||
svayaṃbhuve brahmaṇe | atirohitam anyat || 3.334 ||
akhaṇḍabhūmaṇḍalalīnam āvilaṃ nivārya sadvṛkpratirodhakṛttamaḥ (?) |
doṣodbhavād viprakṛtiṃ parāṃ gataṃ pūṣaiva lokaṃ nayati prakāśatām || 1 ||
rāṣṭraṃ yasya niraṅkuśā vasumatī kośaḥ samṛddhaḥ suhṛcchakto
durgamanāgamaṃ kṣataparā senā hitā mantriṇaḥ|
śāstrārthāmṛtacarvaṇārpitamatir viśvaikanātho 'py asau
śauryaudāryayaśodhano 'mum aparādityo nibandhaṃ vyadhāt || 2 ||
iti śrīvidyādharavaṃśaprabhavaśrīśilāhāranarendrajīmūtavāhanānvayaprasūta-
śrīmadaparādityadevaviracite yājñavalkyadharmāstranibandhe 'parārke
prāyaścittaprakaraṇam || 5 ||
samāpto 'yaṃ prāyaścittādhyāyas tṛtīyaḥ || 3 ||
samāpto 'yaṃ saṭīkā yājñavalkyasmṛtiḥ ||
